




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

majjhimanikāyai


uparipaṇṇāsapāḷi


1. daivadahavaggau

1. daivadahasuttaṃ

1. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati daivadahaṃ nāma sakyānaṃ nigamau| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘yaṃ kiñ‍cāyaṃ purisapuggalau paṭisaṃvaidaiti sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā, sabbaṃ taṃ pubbaikatahaitu| iti purāṇānaṃ kammānaṃ tapasā byantībhāvā, navānaṃ kammānaṃ akaraṇā, āyatiṃ anavassavau; āyatiṃ anavassavā kammakkhayau; kammakkhayā  dukkhakkhayau; dukkhakkhayā vaidanākkhayau; vaidanākkhayā sabbaṃ dukkhaṃ nij‍jiṇṇaṃ bhavissatī’ti| aivaṃvādinau, bhikkhavai, nigaṇṭhā|

‘‘aivaṃvādāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, nigaṇṭhai upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, āvusau nigaṇṭhā, aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – yaṃ kiñ‍cāyaṃ purisapuggalau paṭisaṃvaidaiti sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā, sabbaṃ taṃ pubbaikatahaitu| iti purāṇānaṃ kammānaṃ tapasā byantībhāvā, navānaṃ kammānaṃ akaraṇā, āyatiṃ anavassavau; āyatiṃ anavassavā kammakkhayau; kammakkhayā dukkhakkhayau; dukkhakkhayā vaidanākkhayau; vaidanākkhayā sabbaṃ dukkhaṃ nij‍jiṇṇaṃ bhavissatī’ti? tai ca mai, bhikkhavai, nigaṇṭhā aivaṃ puṭṭhā ‘āmā’ti paṭijānanti|

‘‘tyāhaṃ  aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘kiṃ pana tumhai, āvusau nigaṇṭhā, jānātha – ahuvamhaiva mayaṃ pubbai, na nāhuvamhā’ti? ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’|

‘‘‘kiṃ pana tumhai, āvusau nigaṇṭhā, jānātha – akaramhaiva mayaṃ pubbai pāpakammaṃ, na nākaramhā’ti? ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’|

‘‘‘kiṃ pana tumhai, āvusau nigaṇṭhā, jānātha – aivarūpaṃ vā aivarūpaṃ vā pāpakammaṃ akaramhā’ti? ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’|

‘‘‘kiṃ pana tumhai, āvusau nigaṇṭhā, jānātha – aittakaṃ vā dukkhaṃ nij‍jiṇṇaṃ, aittakaṃ vā dukkhaṃ nij‍jīraitabbaṃ, aittakamhi vā dukkhai nij‍jiṇṇai sabbaṃ dukkhaṃ nij‍jiṇṇaṃ bhavissatī’ti? ‘nau  hidaṃ, āvusau’|

‘‘‘kiṃ pana tumhai, āvusau nigaṇṭhā, jānātha – diṭṭhaiva dhammai akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānaṃ, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampada’nti? ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’|

2. ‘‘iti kira tumhai, āvusau nigaṇṭhā, na jānātha – ahuvamhaiva mayaṃ  pubbai, na nāhuvamhāti, na jānātha – akaramhaiva mayaṃ pubbai pāpakammaṃ, na nākaramhāti, na jānātha – aivarūpaṃ vā aivarūpaṃ vā pāpakammaṃ akaramhāti, na jānātha – aittakaṃ vā dukkhaṃ nij‍jiṇṇaṃ, aittakaṃ vā dukkhaṃ nij‍jīraitabbaṃ, aittakamhi vā dukkhai nij‍jiṇṇai sabbaṃ dukkhaṃ nij‍jiṇṇaṃ bhavissatīti, na jānātha – diṭṭhaiva dhammai akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānaṃ, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadaṃ; aivaṃ santai āyasmantānaṃ nigaṇṭhānaṃ na kal‍lamassa vaiyyākaraṇāya – yaṃ kiñ‍cāyaṃ purisapuggalau paṭisaṃvaidaiti sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā, sabbaṃ taṃ pubbaikatahaitu| iti purāṇānaṃ kammānaṃ tapasā byantībhāvā, navānaṃ kammānaṃ akaraṇā, āyatiṃ anavassavau; āyatiṃ anavassavā kammakkhayau; kammakkhayā dukkhakkhayau; dukkhakkhayā vaidanākkhayau; vaidanākkhayā sabbaṃ dukkhaṃ nij‍jiṇṇaṃ bhavissatī’’ti|

‘‘sacai pana tumhai, āvusau nigaṇṭhā, jānaiyyātha – ahuvamhaiva mayaṃ pubbai, na nāhuvamhāti, jānaiyyātha – akaramhaiva mayaṃ pubbai pāpakammaṃ, na nākaramhāti, jānaiyyātha – aivarūpaṃ vā aivarūpaṃ vā pāpakammaṃ akaramhāti, jānaiyyātha – aittakaṃ vā dukkhaṃ nij‍jiṇṇaṃ, aittakaṃ vā dukkhaṃ nij‍jīraitabbaṃ, aittakamhi vā dukkhai nij‍jiṇṇai sabbaṃ dukkhaṃ nij‍jiṇṇaṃ bhavissatīti, jānaiyyātha – diṭṭhaiva dhammai akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānaṃ, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ  upasampadaṃ; aivaṃ santai āyasmantānaṃ nigaṇṭhānaṃ kal‍lamassa vaiyyākaraṇāya – yaṃ  kiñ‍cāyaṃ purisapuggalau paṭisaṃvaidaiti sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā, sabbaṃ taṃ pubbaikatahaitu| iti purāṇānaṃ kammānaṃ  tapasā byantībhāvā, navānaṃ kammānaṃ akaraṇā, āyatiṃ anavassavau; āyatiṃ anavassavā kammakkhayau; kammakkhayā dukkhakkhayau; dukkhakkhayā vaidanākkhayau; vaidanākkhayā sabbaṃ dukkhaṃ nij‍jiṇṇaṃ bhavissatī’’ti|

3. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, āvusau nigaṇṭhā, purisau sal‍laina viddhau assa savisaina gāḷhūpalaipanaina gāḷhapalaipanaina (ka॰); sau sal‍lassapi vaidhanahaitu vaidanāhaitu (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) dukkhā tibbā tippā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) kaṭukā vaidanā vaidiyaiyya| tassa mittāmac‍cā ñātisālauhitā bhisak‍kaṃ sal‍lakattaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiyyuṃ| tassa sau bhisak‍kau sal‍lakattau satthaina vaṇamukhaṃ parikantaiyya; sau satthainapi vaṇamukhassa parikantanahaitu dukkhā tibbā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidiyaiyya| tassa sau bhisak‍kau sal‍lakattau aisaniyā sal‍laṃ aisaiyya; sau aisaniyāpi sal‍lassa aisanāhaitu dukkhā tibbā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidiyaiyya| tassa sau bhisak‍kau sal‍lakattau sal‍laṃ abbuhaiyya abbuyhaiyya (sī॰), abbhūṇhaiyya (syā॰ kaṃ॰); sau sal‍lassapi abbuhanahaitu abbuyhanahaitu (sī॰), abbhūṇhanahaitu (syā॰ kaṃ॰) dukkhā tibbā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidiyaiyya| tassa sau bhisak‍kau sal‍lakattau agadaṅgāraṃ vaṇamukhai audahaiyya; sau agadaṅgārassapi vaṇamukhai audahanahaitu dukkhā tibbā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidiyaiyya| sau aparaina samayaina rūḷhaina vaṇaina sañchavinā araugau assa sukhī sairī sayaṃvasī yaina kāmaṅgamau| tassa aivamassa – ahaṃ khau pubbai sal‍laina viddhau ahausiṃ savisaina gāḷhūpalaipanaina| sauhaṃ  sal‍lassapi vaidhanahaitu dukkhā tibbā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidiyiṃ| tassa mai mittāmac‍cā ñātisālauhitā bhisak‍kaṃ sal‍lakattaṃ upaṭṭhapaisuṃ| tassa mai sau bhisak‍kau sal‍lakattau satthaina vaṇamukhaṃ parikanti; sauhaṃ satthainapi vaṇamukhassa parikantanahaitu dukkhā tibbā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidiyiṃ| tassa mai sau bhisak‍kau sal‍lakattau aisaniyā sal‍laṃ aisi; sau ahaṃ aisaniyāpi sal‍lassa aisanāhaitu dukkhā tibbā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidiyiṃ| tassa mai sau bhisak‍kau  sal‍lakattau sal‍laṃ abbuhi abbuyhi (sī॰), abbhūṇhi (syā॰ kaṃ॰); sauhaṃ sal‍lassapi abbuhanahaitu dukkhā tibbā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidiyiṃ| tassa mai sau bhisak‍kau sal‍lakattau agadaṅgāraṃ vaṇamukhai audahi; sauhaṃ agadaṅgārassapi vaṇamukhai audahanahaitu dukkhā tibbā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidiyiṃ| saumhi  aitarahi rūḷhaina vaṇaina sañchavinā araugau sukhī sairī sayaṃvasī yaina kāmaṅgamau’’ti|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, āvusau nigaṇṭhā, sacai tumhai jānaiyyātha – ahuvamhaiva mayaṃ pubbai, na nāhuvamhāti, jānaiyyātha – akaramhaiva mayaṃ pubbai pāpakammaṃ, na nākaramhāti, jānaiyyātha – aivarūpaṃ vā aivarūpaṃ vā pāpakammaṃ akaramhāti, jānaiyyātha – aittakaṃ vā dukkhaṃ nij‍jiṇṇaṃ, aittakaṃ vā dukkhaṃ nij‍jīraitabbaṃ, aittakamhi vā dukkhai nij‍jiṇṇai sabbaṃ dukkhaṃ nij‍jiṇṇaṃ bhavissatīti, jānaiyyātha – diṭṭhaiva dhammai akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānaṃ, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadaṃ; aivaṃ santai āyasmantānaṃ nigaṇṭhānaṃ kal‍lamassa vaiyyākaraṇāya – yaṃ  kiñ‍cāyaṃ purisapuggalau paṭisaṃvaidaiti sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā, sabbaṃ taṃ pubbaikatahaitu| iti purāṇānaṃ kammānaṃ tapasā byantībhāvā, navānaṃ kammānaṃ akaraṇā, āyatiṃ anavassavau; āyatiṃ anavassavā kammakkhayau; kammakkhayā dukkhakkhayau; dukkhakkhayā vaidanākkhayau; vaidanākkhayā sabbaṃ dukkhaṃ nij‍jiṇṇaṃ bhavissatī’’ti|

‘‘yasmā ca khau tumhai, āvusau nigaṇṭhā, na jānātha – ahuvamhaiva mayaṃ pubbai, na nāhuvamhāti, na jānātha – akaramhaiva mayaṃ pubbai pāpakammaṃ, na nākaramhāti, na jānātha – aivarūpaṃ vā aivarūpaṃ vā pāpakammaṃ akaramhāti, na jānātha – aittakaṃ vā dukkhaṃ nij‍jiṇṇaṃ, aittakaṃ  vā dukkhaṃ nij‍jīraitabbaṃ, aittakamhi vā dukkhai nij‍jiṇṇai sabbaṃ dukkhaṃ nij‍jiṇṇaṃ bhavissatīti, na jānātha – diṭṭhaiva dhammai akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānaṃ, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadaṃ; tasmā āyasmantānaṃ nigaṇṭhānaṃ na kal‍lamassa vaiyyākaraṇāya – yaṃ kiñ‍cāyaṃ purisapuggalau paṭisaṃvaidaiti sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā, sabbaṃ taṃ pubbaikatahaitu| iti purāṇānaṃ kammānaṃ tapasā byantībhāvā, navānaṃ kammānaṃ akaraṇā, āyatiṃ anavassavau; āyatiṃ anavassavā kammakkhayau; kammakkhayā dukkhakkhayau; dukkhakkhayā vaidanākkhayau; vaidanākkhayā sabbaṃ dukkhaṃ nij‍jiṇṇaṃ bhavissatī’’ti|

4. ‘‘aivaṃ  vuttai, bhikkhavai, tai nigaṇṭhā maṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘nigaṇṭhau, āvusau, nāṭaputtau nāthaputtau (sī॰) sabbañ‍ñū sabbadassāvī, aparisaisaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ paṭijānāti| caratau ca mai tiṭṭhatau ca suttassa ca jāgarassa ca satataṃ samitaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhita’nti| sau aivamāha – ‘atthi khau vau, āvusau nigaṇṭhā, pubbaiva pāpakammaṃ kataṃ, taṃ imāya kaṭukāya duk‍karakārikāya nij‍jīraitha, yaṃ panaittha aitarahi kāyaina saṃvutā vācāya saṃvutā manasā saṃvutā taṃ āyatiṃ pāpakammassa akaraṇaṃ| iti purāṇānaṃ kammānaṃ tapasā byantībhāvā, navānaṃ kammānaṃ akaraṇā, āyatiṃ anavassavau; āyatiṃ anavassavā kammakkhayau; kammakkhayā dukkhakkhayau; dukkhakkhayā vaidanākkhayau; vaidanākkhayā sabbaṃ dukkhaṃ nij‍jiṇṇaṃ bhavissatī’ti| tañ‍ca panamhākaṃ ruc‍cati caiva khamati ca, taina camhā attamanā’’ti|

5. ‘‘aivaṃ vuttai ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, tai nigaṇṭhai aitadavaucaṃ – ‘pañ‍ca khau imai, āvusau nigaṇṭhā, dhammā diṭṭhaiva dhammai dvidhāvipākā| katamai pañ‍ca? saddhā, ruci, anussavau, ākāraparivitak‍kau, diṭṭhinijjhānakkhanti – imai khau, āvusau nigaṇṭhā, pañ‍ca dhammā diṭṭhaiva dhammai dvidhāvipākā| tatrāyasmantānaṃ nigaṇṭhānaṃ kā atītaṃsai satthari saddhā, kā ruci, kau anussavau, kau ākāraparivitak‍kau, kā diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantī’ti| aivaṃvādī aivaṃvādīsu (ka॰) khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, nigaṇṭhaisu na kañ‍ci kiñ‍ci (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) sahadhammikaṃ vādapaṭihāraṃ samanupassāmi|

‘‘puna caparāhaṃ puna ca panāhaṃ (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰), bhikkhavai, tai nigaṇṭhai aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, āvusau nigaṇṭhā| yasmiṃ vau samayai tibbau tippau (pī॰) upak‍kamau hauti  tibbaṃ padhānaṃ, tibbā tasmiṃ samayai aupak‍kamikā dukkhā tibbā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidiyaitha; yasmiṃ pana vau samayai na tibbau upak‍kamau  hauti na tibbaṃ padhānaṃ, na tibbā tasmiṃ samayai aupak‍kamikā dukkhā tibbā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidiyaithā’ti? ‘yasmiṃ nau, āvusau gautama, samayai tibbau upak‍kamau hauti tibbaṃ padhānaṃ, tibbā tasmiṃ samayai aupak‍kamikā dukkhā tibbā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidiyāma; yasmiṃ pana nau samayai  na tibbau upak‍kamau hauti na tibbaṃ padhānaṃ, na tibbā tasmiṃ samayai aupak‍kamikā dukkhā tibbā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidiyāmā’’’ti|

6. ‘‘iti  kira, āvusau nigaṇṭhā, yasmiṃ vau samayai tibbau upak‍kamau hauti tibbaṃ padhānaṃ, tibbā tasmiṃ samayai aupak‍kamikā dukkhā tibbā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidiyaitha; yasmiṃ pana vau samayai na tibbau upak‍kamau hauti na tibbaṃ padhānaṃ, na tibbā tasmiṃ samayai aupak‍kamikā dukkhā tibbā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidiyaitha| aivaṃ santai āyasmantānaṃ nigaṇṭhānaṃ na kal‍lamassa vaiyyākaraṇāya – yaṃ kiñ‍cāyaṃ purisapuggalau paṭisaṃvaidaiti sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā, sabbaṃ taṃ pubbaikatahaitu| iti purāṇānaṃ kammānaṃ tapasā byantībhāvā, navānaṃ kammānaṃ akaraṇā, āyatiṃ anavassavau; āyatiṃ anavassavā kammakkhayau; kammakkhayā dukkhakkhayau; dukkhakkhayā vaidanākkhayau; vaidanākkhayā sabbaṃ dukkhaṃ nij‍jiṇṇaṃ bhavissatīti| sacai, āvusau nigaṇṭhā, yasmiṃ vau samayai tibbau upak‍kamau hauti tibbaṃ padhānaṃ, na tibbā tasmiṃ samayai aupak‍kamikā dukkhā tibbā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidiyaitha; yasmiṃ pana vau samayai na tibbau upak‍kamau hauti na tibbaṃ padhānaṃ, tibbā tasmiṃ samayai aupak‍kamikā dukkhā tibbā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidiyaitha padhānaṃ, tiṭṭhaiyyaiva tasmiṃ samayai… vaidanā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰); aivaṃ santai āyasmantānaṃ nigaṇṭhānaṃ kal‍lamassa vaiyyākaraṇāya – yaṃ kiñ‍cāyaṃ purisapuggalau paṭisaṃvaidaiti sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā, sabbaṃ taṃ pubbaikatahaitu| iti purāṇānaṃ kammānaṃ tapasā byantībhāvā, navānaṃ kammānaṃ akaraṇā, āyatiṃ anavassavau; āyatiṃ anavassavā kammakkhayau; kammakkhayā dukkhakkhayau; dukkhakkhayā vaidanākkhayau; vaidanākkhayā sabbaṃ dukkhaṃ nij‍jiṇṇaṃ bhavissatī’’ti|

‘‘‘yasmā ca khau, āvusau nigaṇṭhā, yasmiṃ vau samayai tibbau upak‍kamau hauti tibbaṃ padhānaṃ, tibbā tasmiṃ samayai aupak‍kamikā dukkhā tibbā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidiyaitha; yasmiṃ pana vau samayai na tibbau upak‍kamau hauti na tibbaṃ padhānaṃ, na tibbā tasmiṃ samayai aupak‍kamikā dukkhā tibbā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidiyaitha; tai tumhai sāmaṃyaiva aupak‍kamikā dukkhā  tibbā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidayamānā avij‍jā añ‍ñāṇā sammauhā vipac‍caitha  – yaṃ kiñ‍cāyaṃ purisapuggalau paṭisaṃvaidaiti sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā, sabbaṃ taṃ pubbaikatahaitu| iti purāṇānaṃ kammānaṃ  tapasā byantībhāvā, navānaṃ kammānaṃ akaraṇā, āyatiṃ anavassavau; āyatiṃ anavassavā kammakkhayau; kammakkhayā dukkhakkhayau; dukkhakkhayā vaidanākkhayau; vaidanākkhayā sabbaṃ dukkhaṃ nij‍jiṇṇaṃ bhavissatī’ti| aivaṃvādīpi aivaṃvādīsupi (ka॰) khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, nigaṇṭhaisu na kañ‍ci sahadhammikaṃ vādapaṭihāraṃ samanupassāmi|

7. ‘‘puna caparāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, tai nigaṇṭhai aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñathāvusau nigaṇṭhā, yamidaṃ kammaṃ diṭṭhadhammavaidanīyaṃ taṃ upak‍kamaina vā padhānaina vā samparāyavaidanīyaṃ hautūti labbhamaita’nti? ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’| ‘yaṃ panidaṃ kammaṃ samparāyavaidanīyaṃ taṃ upak‍kamaina vā padhānaina vā diṭṭhadhammavaidanīyaṃ hautūti labbhamaita’nti? ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’| ‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñathāvusau nigaṇṭhā, yamidaṃ kammaṃ sukhavaidanīyaṃ taṃ upak‍kamaina vā padhānaina vā dukkhavaidanīyaṃ hautūti labbhamaita’nti? ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’| ‘yaṃ panidaṃ kammaṃ dukkhavaidanīyaṃ taṃ upak‍kamaina vā padhānaina vā sukhavaidanīyaṃ hautūti labbhamaita’nti? ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’| ‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñathāvusau nigaṇṭhā, yamidaṃ kammaṃ paripak‍kavaidanīyaṃ taṃ upak‍kamaina vā padhānaina vā aparipak‍kavaidanīyaṃ hautūti labbhamaita’nti? ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’| ‘yaṃ panidaṃ kammaṃ aparipak‍kavaidanīyaṃ taṃ upak‍kamaina vā padhānaina vā paripak‍kavaidanīyaṃ hautūti labbhamaita’nti? ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’| ‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñathāvusau nigaṇṭhā, yamidaṃ kammaṃ  bahuvaidanīyaṃ taṃ upak‍kamaina vā padhānaina vā appavaidanīyaṃ  hautūti labbhamaita’nti? ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’| ‘yaṃ panidaṃ kammaṃ appavaidanīyaṃ taṃ upak‍kamaina vā padhānaina vā bahuvaidanīyaṃ hautūti labbhamaita’nti? ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’| ‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñathāvusau nigaṇṭhā, yamidaṃ kammaṃ savaidanīyaṃ taṃ upak‍kamaina vā padhānaina vā avaidanīyaṃ hautūti labbhamaita’nti? ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’| ‘yaṃ panidaṃ kammaṃ avaidanīyaṃ taṃ upak‍kamaina vā padhānaina vā savaidanīyaṃ hautūti labbhamaita’nti? ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’|

8. ‘‘iti kira, āvusau nigaṇṭhā, yamidaṃ kammaṃ diṭṭhadhammavaidanīyaṃ taṃ upak‍kamaina vā padhānaina vā samparāyavaidanīyaṃ hautūti alabbhamaitaṃ, yaṃ panidaṃ kammaṃ samparāyavaidanīyaṃ taṃ upak‍kamaina vā padhānaina vā diṭṭhadhammavaidanīyaṃ hautūti alabbhamaitaṃ, yamidaṃ kammaṃ sukhavaidanīyaṃ taṃ  upak‍kamaina vā padhānaina vā dukkhavaidanīyaṃ hautūti alabbhamaitaṃ, yamidaṃ kammaṃ dukkhavaidanīyaṃ  taṃ upak‍kamaina vā padhānaina vā sukhavaidanīyaṃ hautūti alabbhamaitaṃ, yamidaṃ kammaṃ paripak‍kavaidanīyaṃ taṃ upak‍kamaina vā padhānaina vā aparipak‍kavaidanīyaṃ hautūti alabbhamaitaṃ, yamidaṃ kammaṃ aparipak‍kavaidanīyaṃ taṃ upak‍kamaina vā padhānaina vā paripak‍kavaidanīyaṃ hautūti alabbhamaitaṃ, yamidaṃ kammaṃ bahuvaidanīyaṃ taṃ upak‍kamaina vā padhānaina vā appavaidanīyaṃ hautūti alabbhamaitaṃ, yamidaṃ kammaṃ appavaidanīyaṃ taṃ upak‍kamaina vā padhānaina vā bahuvaidanīyaṃ hautūti alabbhamaitaṃ, yamidaṃ kammaṃ savaidanīyaṃ taṃ upak‍kamaina vā padhānaina vā  avaidanīyaṃ hautūti alabbhamaitaṃ, yamidaṃ kammaṃ avaidanīyaṃ taṃ upak‍kamaina vā padhānaina vā savaidanīyaṃ hautūti alabbhamaitaṃ; aivaṃ santai āyasmantānaṃ nigaṇṭhānaṃ aphalau upak‍kamau  hauti, aphalaṃ padhānaṃ’’|

‘‘aivaṃvādī, bhikkhavai, nigaṇṭhā| aivaṃvādīnaṃ, bhikkhavai, nigaṇṭhānaṃ dasa sahadhammikā vādānuvādā gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchanti|

9. ‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, sattā pubbaikatahaitu sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidainti; addhā, bhikkhavai, nigaṇṭhā pubbai duk‍kaṭakammakārinau yaṃ aitarahi aivarūpā dukkhā tibbā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidiyanti| sacai, bhikkhavai, sattā issaranimmānahaitu sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidainti; addhā, bhikkhavai, nigaṇṭhā pāpakaina issaraina nimmitā yaṃ aitarahi aivarūpā dukkhā tibbā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidiyanti| sacai, bhikkhavai, sattā saṅgatibhāvahaitu sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidainti; addhā, bhikkhavai, nigaṇṭhā pāpasaṅgatikā yaṃ aitarahi aivarūpā dukkhā tibbā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidiyanti| sacai, bhikkhavai, sattā abhijātihaitu sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidainti; addhā, bhikkhavai, nigaṇṭhā pāpābhijātikā yaṃ aitarahi aivarūpā dukkhā tibbā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidiyanti| sacai, bhikkhavai, sattā diṭṭhadhammūpak‍kamahaitu sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidainti; addhā, bhikkhavai, nigaṇṭhā aivarūpā diṭṭhadhammūpak‍kamā yaṃ aitarahi aivarūpā dukkhā tibbā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidiyanti|

‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, sattā pubbaikatahaitu sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidainti, gārayhā nigaṇṭhā; nau cai sattā pubbaikatahaitu sukhadukkhaṃ  paṭisaṃvaidainti, gārayhā nigaṇṭhā| sacai, bhikkhavai, sattā issaranimmānahaitu  sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidainti, gārayhā nigaṇṭhā; nau cai sattā issaranimmānahaitu sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidainti, gārayhā nigaṇṭhā| sacai, bhikkhavai, sattā saṅgatibhāvahaitu  sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidainti, gārayhā nigaṇṭhā; nau cai sattā saṅgatibhāvahaitu sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidainti, gārayhā nigaṇṭhā| sacai, bhikkhavai, sattā abhijātihaitu sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidainti, gārayhā nigaṇṭhā; nau cai sattā abhijātihaitu sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidainti, gārayhā nigaṇṭhā| sacai, bhikkhavai, sattā diṭṭhadhammūpak‍kamahaitu sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidainti, gārayhā  nigaṇṭhā; nau cai sattā diṭṭhadhammūpak‍kamahaitu sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidainti, gārayhā nigaṇṭhā| aivaṃvādī, bhikkhavai, nigaṇṭhā| aivaṃvādīnaṃ, bhikkhavai, nigaṇṭhānaṃ imai dasa sahadhammikā vādānuvādā gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchanti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, aphalau upak‍kamau hauti, aphalaṃ padhānaṃ|

10. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, saphalau upak‍kamau hauti, saphalaṃ padhānaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na haiva anaddhabhūtaṃ attānaṃ dukkhaina addhabhāvaiti, dhammikañ‍ca sukhaṃ na paric‍cajati, tasmiñ‍ca sukhai anadhimucchitau hauti| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘imassa khau mai dukkhanidānassa saṅkhāraṃ padahatau saṅkhārappadhānā virāgau hauti, imassa pana mai dukkhanidānassa ajjhupaikkhatau upaikkhaṃ bhāvayatau virāgau hautī’ti| sau yassa hi khvāssa yassa khau panassa (sī॰), yassa khvāssa (pī॰)  dukkhanidānassa saṅkhāraṃ padahatau saṅkhārappadhānā virāgau hauti, saṅkhāraṃ tattha padahati| yassa panassa dukkhanidānassa ajjhupaikkhatau upaikkhaṃ bhāvayatau virāgau hauti, upaikkhaṃ tattha bhāvaiti| tassa tassa dukkhanidānassa saṅkhāraṃ padahatau saṅkhārappadhānā virāgau hauti – aivampissa taṃ dukkhaṃ nij‍jiṇṇaṃ hauti| tassa tassa dukkhanidānassa ajjhupaikkhatau upaikkhaṃ bhāvayatau virāgau hauti – aivampissa taṃ dukkhaṃ nij‍jiṇṇaṃ hauti|

11. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau itthiyā sārattau paṭibaddhacittau tibbacchandau tibbāpaikkhau| sau taṃ itthiṃ passaiyya añ‍ñaina purisaina saddhiṃ santiṭṭhantiṃ sal‍lapantiṃ sañ‍jagghantiṃ saṃhasantiṃ| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu tassa purisassa amuṃ itthiṃ disvā añ‍ñaina purisaina saddhiṃ santiṭṭhantiṃ sal‍lapantiṃ sañ‍jagghantiṃ saṃhasantiṃ uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ  saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassūpāyāsā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘amu hi, bhantai, purisau amussā itthiyā sārattau paṭibaddhacittau tibbacchandau tibbāpaikkhau| tasmā taṃ itthiṃ disvā añ‍ñaina purisaina saddhiṃ santiṭṭhantiṃ sal‍lapantiṃ sañ‍jagghantiṃ  saṃhasantiṃ uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassūpāyāsā’’ti| ‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, tassa purisassa aivamassa – ‘ahaṃ khau amussā itthiyā sārattau paṭibaddhacittau tibbacchandau tibbāpaikkhau| tassa mai amuṃ itthiṃ disvā añ‍ñaina purisaina saddhiṃ santiṭṭhantiṃ sal‍lapantiṃ sañ‍jagghantiṃ saṃhasantiṃ  uppaj‍janti saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassūpāyāsā| yaṃnūnāhaṃ yau mai amussā itthiyā chandarāgau taṃ pajahaiyya’nti| sau yau amussā itthiyā chandarāgau taṃ pajahaiyya| sau taṃ itthiṃ passaiyya aparaina samayaina añ‍ñaina purisaina saddhiṃ santiṭṭhantiṃ sal‍lapantiṃ sañ‍jagghantiṃ saṃhasantiṃ| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu tassa purisassa amuṃ itthiṃ disvā añ‍ñaina purisaina saddhiṃ santiṭṭhantiṃ sal‍lapantiṃ sañ‍jagghantiṃ saṃhasantiṃ uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassūpāyāsā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘amu hi, bhantai, purisau amussā itthiyā virāgau| tasmā taṃ itthiṃ disvā añ‍ñaina purisaina saddhiṃ santiṭṭhantiṃ sal‍lapantiṃ sañ‍jagghantiṃ saṃhasantiṃ na uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassūpāyāsā’’ti|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na haiva anaddhabhūtaṃ attānaṃ dukkhaina addhabhāvaiti, dhammikañ‍ca sukhaṃ na paric‍cajati, tasmiñ‍ca sukhai anadhimucchitau hauti| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘imassa khau mai dukkhanidānassa saṅkhāraṃ padahatau saṅkhārappadhānā virāgau hauti, imassa pana mai dukkhanidānassa ajjhupaikkhatau upaikkhaṃ bhāvayatau virāgau hautī’ti| sau yassa hi khvāssa dukkhanidānassa saṅkhāraṃ padahatau saṅkhārappadhānā virāgau hauti, saṅkhāraṃ tattha padahati; yassa panassa dukkhanidānassa ajjhupaikkhatau upaikkhaṃ bhāvayatau virāgau hauti, upaikkhaṃ tattha bhāvaiti| tassa tassa dukkhanidānassa saṅkhāraṃ padahatau  saṅkhārappadhānā virāgau hauti – aivampissa taṃ dukkhaṃ nij‍jiṇṇaṃ hauti| tassa tassa dukkhanidānassa ajjhupaikkhatau upaikkhaṃ bhāvayatau virāgau hauti – aivampissa taṃ dukkhaṃ nij‍jiṇṇaṃ hauti| aivampi, bhikkhavai, saphalau upak‍kamau hauti, saphalaṃ padhānaṃ|

12. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yathāsukhaṃ khau mai viharatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti; dukkhāya pana mai attānaṃ padahatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti| yaṃnūnāhaṃ dukkhāya attānaṃ padahaiyya’nti| sau dukkhāya attānaṃ padahati| tassa dukkhāya attānaṃ padahatau akusalā  dhammā  parihāyanti kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti| sau na aparaina samayaina dukkhāya attānaṃ padahati| taṃ kissa haitu? yassa hi sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu atthāya dukkhāya attānaṃ padahaiyya svāssa atthau abhinipphan‍nau hauti| tasmā na aparaina samayaina dukkhāya attānaṃ padahati| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, usukārau taijanaṃ dvīsu alātaisu ātāpaiti paritāpaiti ujuṃ karauti kammaniyaṃ| yatau khau, bhikkhavai, usukārassa taijanaṃ dvīsu alātaisu ātāpitaṃ hauti paritāpitaṃ ujuṃ kataṃ ujuṃ kataṃ hauti (sī॰) kammaniyaṃ, na sau taṃ aparaina samayaina usukārau taijanaṃ dvīsu alātaisu ātāpaiti paritāpaiti ujuṃ karauti kammaniyaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? yassa hi sau, bhikkhavai, atthāya usukārau taijanaṃ dvīsu alātaisu ātāpaiyya paritāpaiyya ujuṃ  karaiyya kammaniyaṃ svāssa atthau abhinipphan‍nau hauti| tasmā na aparaina samayaina usukārau taijanaṃ dvīsu alātaisu ātāpaiti paritāpaiti ujuṃ karauti kammaniyaṃ| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yathāsukhaṃ khau mai viharatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti; dukkhāya pana mai attānaṃ padahatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti| yaṃnūnāhaṃ dukkhāya attānaṃ padahaiyya’nti| sau dukkhāya attānaṃ padahati| tassa dukkhāya attānaṃ padahatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti| sau na aparaina samayaina dukkhāya  attānaṃ padahati| taṃ kissa haitu? yassa hi sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu atthāya dukkhāya attānaṃ padahaiyya svāssa atthau abhinipphan‍nau hauti| tasmā na aparaina samayaina dukkhāya attānaṃ padahati| aivampi, bhikkhavai, saphalau upak‍kamau hauti, saphalaṃ padhānaṃ|

13. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idha tathāgatau laukai uppaj‍jati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā| sau imaṃ laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ samārakaṃ sabrahmakaṃ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ pajaṃ sadaivamanussaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti| sau dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ  brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| taṃ dhammaṃ suṇāti gahapati vā gahapatiputtau  vā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vā kulai pac‍cājātau| sau taṃ dhammaṃ sutvā tathāgatai saddhaṃ paṭilabhati| sau taina saddhāpaṭilābhaina saman‍nāgatau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘sambādhau gharāvāsau rajāpathau, abbhaukāsau pabbaj‍jā| nayidaṃ sukaraṃ agāraṃ ajjhāvasatā aikantaparipuṇṇaṃ aikantaparisuddhaṃ saṅkhalikhitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carituṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ kaisamassuṃ  auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajaiyya’nti| sau aparaina samayaina appaṃ vā bhaugakkhandhaṃ pahāya mahantaṃ vā bhaugakkhandhaṃ pahāya, appaṃ vā ñātiparivaṭṭaṃ pahāya mahantaṃ vā ñātiparivaṭṭaṃ pahāya kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati|

14. ‘‘sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāsājīvasamāpan‍nau pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti nihitadaṇḍau nihitasatthau, laj‍jī dayāpan‍nau sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī viharati| adin‍nādānaṃ pahāya adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti din‍nādāyī din‍napāṭikaṅkhī, athainaina sucibhūtaina attanā viharati| abrahmacariyaṃ pahāya brahmacārī hauti ārācārī viratau maithunā gāmadhammā| musāvādaṃ pahāya musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti sac‍cavādī sac‍casandhau thaitau pac‍cayikau avisaṃvādakau laukassa| pisuṇaṃ vācaṃ pahāya pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti; itau sutvā na amutra akkhātā imaisaṃ bhaidāya, amutra vā sutvā na imaisaṃ akkhātā amūsaṃ bhaidāya – iti bhin‍nānaṃ  vā sandhātā sahitānaṃ vā anuppadātā samaggārāmau samaggaratau samagganandī samaggakaraṇiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti| pharusaṃ vācaṃ pahāya pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti; yā sā vācā nailā kaṇṇasukhā paimanīyā hadayaṅgamā paurī bahujanakantā bahujanamanāpā tathārūpiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti| samphappalāpaṃ pahāya samphappalāpā paṭiviratau hauti kālavādī bhūtavādī atthavādī dhammavādī vinayavādī, nidhānavatiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā kālaina sāpadaisaṃ pariyantavatiṃ atthasaṃhitaṃ| sau bījagāmabhūtagāmasamārambhā paṭiviratau hauti| aikabhattikau hauti rattūparatau viratau vikālabhaujanā| nac‍cagītavāditavisūkadassanā paṭiviratau hauti| mālāgandhavilaipanadhāraṇamaṇḍanavibhūsanaṭṭhānā  paṭiviratau hauti| uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanā paṭiviratau hauti| jātarūparajatapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| āmakadhañ‍ñapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| āmakamaṃsapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| itthikumārikapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| dāsidāsapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| ajaiḷakapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| kuk‍kuṭasūkarapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| hatthigavassavaḷavapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| khaittavatthupaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| dūtaiyyapahiṇagamanānuyaugā paṭiviratau hauti| kayavik‍kayā paṭiviratau hauti| tulākūṭakaṃsakūṭamānakūṭā paṭiviratau hauti| uk‍kauṭanavañ‍cananikatisāciyaugā sāviyaugā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) aittha sācisaddau kuṭilapariyāyau paṭiviratau hauti| chaidanavadhabandhanaviparāmausaālaupasahasākārā paṭiviratau hauti passa ma॰ ni॰ 1.293 cūḷahatthipadaupamai|

‘‘sau  santuṭṭhau hauti kāyaparihārikaina cīvaraina, kucchiparihārikaina piṇḍapātaina| sau yaina  yainaiva pak‍kamati samādāyaiva pak‍kamati| saiyyathāpi nāma pakkhī sakuṇau yaina yainaiva ḍaiti sapattabhārauva ḍaiti, aivamaiva bhikkhu santuṭṭhau hauti kāyaparihārikaina cīvaraina, kucchiparihārikaina piṇḍapātaina; sau yaina yainaiva pak‍kamati samādāyaiva pak‍kamati| sau iminā ariyaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau ajjhattaṃ anavaj‍jasukhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti|

15. ‘‘sau cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati, rakkhati cakkhundriyaṃ, cakkhundriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā…pai॰… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā…pai॰… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā…pai॰… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ manindriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati, rakkhati manindriyaṃ, manindriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sau iminā ariyaina indriyasaṃvaraina saman‍nāgatau ajjhattaṃ abyāsaikasukhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti|

‘‘sau  abhik‍kantai paṭik‍kantai sampajānakārī hauti, ālaukitai vilaukitai sampajānakārī hauti, samiñ‍jitai sammiñ‍jitai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pasāritai sampajānakārī hauti, saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇai sampajānakārī hauti, asitai pītai khāyitai sāyitai sampajānakārī hauti, uc‍cārapassāvakammai sampajānakārī hauti, gatai ṭhitai nisin‍nai suttai jāgaritai bhāsitai tuṇhībhāvai sampajānakārī hauti|

16. ‘‘sau iminā ca ariyaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau, (imāya ca ariyāya santuṭṭhiyā saman‍nāgatau,) passa ma॰ ni॰ 1.296 cūḷahatthipadaupamai iminā ca ariyaina indriyasaṃvaraina saman‍nāgatau, iminā ca ariyaina satisampajañ‍ñaina saman‍nāgatau vivittaṃ saināsanaṃ bhajati arañ‍ñaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ pabbataṃ kandaraṃ giriguhaṃ susānaṃ vanapatthaṃ abbhaukāsaṃ palālapuñ‍jaṃ| sau pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā, ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya, parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| sau abhijjhaṃ laukai pahāya vigatābhijjhaina caitasā viharati, abhijjhāya cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| byāpādapadausaṃ pahāya abyāpan‍nacittau viharati sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī, byāpādapadausā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| thinamiddhaṃ pahāya vigatathinamiddhau viharati ālaukasañ‍ñī satau sampajānau, thinamiddhā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ pahāya anuddhatau viharati ajjhattaṃ vūpasantacittau, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| vicikicchaṃ pahāya tiṇṇavicikicchau viharati akathaṃkathī kusalaisu dhammaisu, vicikicchāya cittaṃ parisaudhaiti|

‘‘sau imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| aivampi, bhikkhavai, saphalau upak‍kamau hauti, saphalaṃ padhānaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| aivampi, bhikkhavai, saphalau upak‍kamau hauti, saphalaṃ padhānaṃ|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati satau ca sampajānau, sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti| yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| aivampi, bhikkhavai, saphalau upak‍kamau hauti, saphalaṃ padhānaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā, pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā, adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| aivampi, bhikkhavai, saphalau upak‍kamau hauti, saphalaṃ padhānaṃ|

17. ‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ saiyyathīdaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau tissaupi jātiyau catassaupi jātiyau pañ‍capi jātiyau dasapi jātiyau vīsampi jātiyau tiṃsampi jātiyau cattālīsampi jātiyau pañ‍ñāsampi jātiyau jātisatampi  jātisahassampi jātisatasahassampi anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭakappai anaikaipi vivaṭṭakappai anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappai – ‘amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ; tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nau’ti| iti sākāraṃ  sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| aivampi, bhikkhavai, saphalau upak‍kamau hauti, saphalaṃ padhānaṃ|

18. ‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai sattānaṃ cutūpapātañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai, sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti  – ‘imai vata bhauntau sattā kāyaduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā manauduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ upavādakā micchādiṭṭhikā micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍nā| imai vā pana bhauntau sattā kāyasucaritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīsucaritaina saman‍nāgatā manausucaritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ anupavādakā sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ  saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā’ti| iti dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai, sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti| aivampi, bhikkhavai, saphalau upak‍kamau hauti, saphalaṃ padhānaṃ|

19. ‘‘sau  aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai āsavānaṃ khayañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; ‘imai āsavā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ āsavasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| tassa aivaṃ jānatau aivaṃ passatau kāmāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, bhavāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, avij‍jāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati| vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti| aivampi khau, bhikkhavai, saphalau upak‍kamau hauti, saphalaṃ padhānaṃ| aivaṃvādī, bhikkhavai, tathāgatā| aivaṃvādīnaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatānaṃ tathāgatau, aivaṃvādiṃ bhikkhavai tathāgataṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) dasa sahadhammikā pāsaṃsaṭṭhānā āgacchanti|

20. ‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, sattā pubbaikatahaitu sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidainti; addhā, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau pubbai  sukatakammakārī yaṃ aitarahi aivarūpā anāsavā sukhā vaidanā vaidaiti| sacai, bhikkhavai, sattā issaranimmānahaitu sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidainti; addhā, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau bhaddakaina issaraina  nimmitau yaṃ aitarahi aivarūpā anāsavā sukhā vaidanā vaidaiti| sacai, bhikkhavai, sattā saṅgatibhāvahaitu sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidainti; addhā, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau kalyāṇasaṅgatikau yaṃ aitarahi aivarūpā anāsavā sukhā vaidanā vaidaiti| sacai, bhikkhavai, sattā abhijātihaitu sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidainti; addhā, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau kalyāṇābhijātikau yaṃ aitarahi aivarūpā anāsavā sukhā vaidanā vaidaiti| sacai, bhikkhavai, sattā diṭṭhadhammūpak‍kamahaitu sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidainti; addhā, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau kalyāṇadiṭṭhadhammūpak‍kamau yaṃ aitarahi aivarūpā anāsavā sukhā vaidanā vaidaiti|

‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, sattā pubbaikatahaitu sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidainti, pāsaṃsau tathāgatau; nau cai sattā pubbaikatahaitu sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidainti, pāsaṃsau tathāgatau| sacai, bhikkhavai, sattā issaranimmānahaitu sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidainti, pāsaṃsau tathāgatau; nau cai sattā issaranimmānahaitu sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidainti, pāsaṃsau tathāgatau| sacai, bhikkhavai, sattā saṅgatibhāvahaitu sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidainti, pāsaṃsau tathāgatau; nau cai sattā saṅgatibhāvahaitu sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidainti, pāsaṃsau tathāgatau| sacai, bhikkhavai, sattā abhijātihaitu sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidainti, pāsaṃsau tathāgatau; nau cai sattā  abhijātihaitu sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidainti, pāsaṃsau tathāgatau| sacai, bhikkhavai, sattā diṭṭhadhammūpak‍kamahaitu sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidainti, pāsaṃsau tathāgatau; nau cai sattā diṭṭhadhammūpak‍kamahaitu sukhadukkhaṃ  paṭisaṃvaidainti, pāsaṃsau tathāgatau| aivaṃvādī, bhikkhavai, tathāgatā| aivaṃvādīnaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatānaṃ imai dasa sahadhammikā pāsaṃsaṭṭhānā āgacchantī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

daivadahasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. pañ‍cattayasuttaṃ pañ‍cāyatanasutta (ka॰)

21. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aparantakappikā aparantānudiṭṭhinau aparantaṃ ārabbha anaikavihitāni adhivuttipadāni adhimuttipadāni (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) abhivadanti| ‘sañ‍ñī attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā’ti – itthaikai abhivadanti; ‘asañ‍ñī attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā’ti – itthaikai abhivadanti; ‘naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñī attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā’ti – itthaikai abhivadanti; satau vā pana sattassa ucchaidaṃ vināsaṃ vibhavaṃ pañ‍ñapainti pañ‍ñāpainti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), diṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ vā panaikai abhivadanti| iti santaṃ vā attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā| iti imāni (ka॰) paraṃ maraṇā, satau vā pana sattassa ucchaidaṃ vināsaṃ vibhavaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, diṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ vā panaikai abhivadanti| iti imāni pañ‍ca paraṃ maraṇā| iti imāni (ka॰) hutvā tīṇi haunti, tīṇi hutvā pañ‍ca haunti – ayamuddaisau pañ‍cattayassa|

22. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ  pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā, rūpiṃ vā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā sañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā, arūpiṃ  vā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā sañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā, rūpiñ‍ca arūpiñ‍ca vā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā sañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā, naivarūpiṃ nārūpiṃ vā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā sañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā, aikattasañ‍ñiṃ vā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā sañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā, nānattasañ‍ñiṃ vā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā sañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ  pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā, parittasañ‍ñiṃ vā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā sañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā, appamāṇasañ‍ñiṃ vā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā sañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā, aitaṃ aivaṃ (ka॰) vā panaikaisaṃ panaitaisaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) upātivattataṃ viñ‍ñāṇakasiṇamaikai abhivadanti appamāṇaṃ ānaiñ‍jaṃ| tayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau abhijānāti pajānāti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) aṭṭhakathā aulaukaitabbā| yai khau tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā sañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā, rūpiṃ vā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā sañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā, arūpiṃ vā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā sañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā, rūpiñ‍ca arūpiñ‍ca vā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā sañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā, naivarūpiṃ nārūpiṃ vā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā sañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā, aikattasañ‍ñiṃ vā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā  sañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā, nānattasañ‍ñiṃ vā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā sañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā, parittasañ‍ñiṃ vā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā sañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā, appamāṇasañ‍ñiṃ vā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā sañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā maraṇāti (ka॰), yā vā panaitāsaṃ sañ‍ñānaṃ parisuddhā paramā aggā anuttariyā akkhāyati  – yadi rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ yadi arūpasañ‍ñānaṃ yadi aikattasañ‍ñānaṃ yadi nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ| ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanamaikai abhivadanti appamāṇaṃ ānaiñ‍jaṃ| ‘tayidaṃ saṅkhataṃ auḷārikaṃ atthi khau pana saṅkhārānaṃ niraudhau atthaita’nti – iti viditvā tassa nissaraṇadassāvī tathāgatau tadupātivattau|

23. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā asañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā, rūpiṃ vā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā asañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā, arūpiṃ vā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā asañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā, rūpiñ‍ca arūpiñ‍ca vā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā asañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā, naivarūpiṃ nārūpiṃ vā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā asañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā| tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā taisamaitai  paṭik‍kausanti| taṃ kissa haitu? sañ‍ñā raugau sañ‍ñā gaṇḍau sañ‍ñā sal‍laṃ, aitaṃ santaṃ aitaṃ paṇītaṃ yadidaṃ – ‘asañ‍ña’nti| tayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau abhijānāti yai khau tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā asañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā, rūpiṃ vā tai bhauntau  samaṇabrāhmaṇā asañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā, arūpiṃ vā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā asañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā, rūpiñ‍ca arūpiñ‍ca vā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā asañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā, naivarūpiṃ nārūpiṃ vā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā asañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā| yau hi kauci, bhikkhavai, samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahamañ‍ñatra rūpā, añ‍ñatra vaidanāya, añ‍ñatra sañ‍ñāya, añ‍ñatra  saṅkhāraihi, viñ‍ñāṇassa añ‍ñatra viñ‍ñāṇā (syā॰ kaṃ॰), añ‍ñatra viñ‍ñāṇaina (ka॰) āgatiṃ vā gatiṃ vā cutiṃ vā upapattiṃ vā vuddhiṃ vā virūḷhiṃ vā vaipul‍laṃ vā pañ‍ñapaissāmī’ti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| ‘tayidaṃ saṅkhataṃ auḷārikaṃ atthi khau pana saṅkhārānaṃ  niraudhau atthaita’nti – iti viditvā tassa nissaraṇadassāvī tathāgatau tadupātivattau|

24. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā, rūpiṃ vā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ  paraṃ maraṇā, arūpiṃ vā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā, rūpiñ‍ca arūpiñ‍ca vā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā, naivarūpiṃ nārūpiṃ vā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā| tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā taisamaitai paṭik‍kausanti, yaipi tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā asañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā taisamaitai paṭik‍kausanti| taṃ kissa haitu? sañ‍ñā raugau sañ‍ñā gaṇḍau sañ‍ñā sal‍laṃ, asañ‍ñā sammauhau, aitaṃ santaṃ aitaṃ paṇītaṃ yadidaṃ – ‘naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ña’nti| naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāti (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) aitantipadaṃ manasikātabbaṃ tayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau abhijānāti| yai khau tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā, rūpiṃ vā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā, arūpiṃ vā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ  pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā, rūpiñ‍ca arūpiñ‍ca vā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā, naivarūpiṃ nārūpiṃ vā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ  paraṃ maraṇā| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇabrāhmaṇā (sī॰ pī॰) diṭṭhasutamutaviñ‍ñātabbasaṅkhāramattaina aitassa āyatanassa upasampadaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, byasanañhaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, akkhāyati āyatanamakkhāyati (ka॰) aitassa āyatanassa upasampadāya| na haitaṃ, bhikkhavai, āyatanaṃ saṅkhārasamāpattipattabbamakkhāyati; saṅkhārāvasaisasamāpattipattabbamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, āyatanamakkhāyati| ‘tayidaṃ saṅkhataṃ auḷārikaṃ atthi khau pana saṅkhārānaṃ niraudhau atthaita’nti – iti viditvā tassa nissaraṇadassāvī tathāgatau tadupātivattau|

25. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā satau sattassa ucchaidaṃ vināsaṃ vibhavaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā taisamaitai paṭik‍kausanti, yaipi tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā asañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā taisamaitai paṭik‍kausanti, yaipi tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti araugaṃ paraṃ maraṇā taisamaitai paṭik‍kausanti| taṃ kissa haitu? sabbaipimai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā uddhaṃ saraṃ uddhaṃsarā (sī॰ pī॰), uddhaṃ parāmasanti (syā॰ kaṃ॰) āsattiṃyaiva abhivadanti – ‘iti paic‍ca  bhavissāma, iti paic‍ca bhavissāmā’ti| saiyyathāpi nāma vāṇijassa vāṇij‍jāya gacchatau aivaṃ hauti – ‘itau mai idaṃ bhavissati, iminā idaṃ lacchāmī’ti, aivamaivimai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā vāṇijūpamā mañ‍ñai paṭibhanti – ‘iti paic‍ca bhavissāma, iti paic‍ca bhavissāmā’ti| tayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau abhijānāti| yai khau tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā satau sattassa ucchaidaṃ vināsaṃ vibhavaṃ pañ‍ñapainti tai sak‍kāyabhayā sak‍kāyaparijaigucchā sak‍kāyañ‍ñaiva anuparidhāvanti anuparivattanti| saiyyathāpi nāma sā gaddulabaddhau daḷhai thambhai vā khilai khīlai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vā upanibaddhau, tamaiva thambhaṃ vā khilaṃ vā anuparidhāvati anuparivattati; aivamaivimai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā sak‍kāyabhayā sak‍kāyaparijaigucchā sak‍kāyañ‍ñaiva anuparidhāvanti anuparivattanti| ‘tayidaṃ saṅkhataṃ auḷārikaṃ atthi khau pana saṅkhārānaṃ niraudhau atthaita’nti – iti viditvā tassa nissaraṇadassāvī tathāgatau tadupātivattau|

26. ‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā aparantakappikā aparantānudiṭṭhinau aparantaṃ ārabbha anaikavihitāni adhivuttipadāni abhivadanti, sabbai tai imānaiva pañ‍cāyatanāni abhivadanti aitaisaṃ vā añ‍ñataraṃ|

27. ‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā pubbantakappikā pubbantānudiṭṭhinau pubbantaṃ ārabbha anaikavihitāni adhivuttipadāni abhivadanti| ‘sassatau attā ca laukau ca, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti – itthaikai abhivadanti, ‘asassatau attā ca laukau ca, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti – itthaikai abhivadanti, ‘sassatau ca asassatau ca attā ca laukau ca, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti – itthaikai abhivadanti, ‘naivasassatau  nāsassatau attā ca laukau ca, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti – itthaikai abhivadanti, ‘antavā attā ca laukau ca, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti – itthaikai abhivadanti, ‘anantavā attā ca laukau ca, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti – itthaikai abhivadanti, ‘antavā ca anantavā ca attā  ca laukau ca, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti – itthaikai abhivadanti, ‘naivantavā nānantavā attā ca laukau ca, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti – itthaikai abhivadanti, ‘aikattasañ‍ñī attā ca laukau ca, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti – itthaikai abhivadanti, ‘nānattasañ‍ñī attā ca laukau ca, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti – itthaikai abhivadanti, ‘parittasañ‍ñī attā ca laukau ca, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti – itthaikai abhivadanti, ‘appamāṇasañ‍ñī attā ca laukau ca, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti – itthaikai abhivadanti, ‘aikantasukhī attā ca laukau ca, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti – itthaikai abhivadanti, ‘aikantadukkhī attā ca laukau ca, idamaiva sac‍caṃ  maughamañ‍ña’nti – itthaikai abhivadanti, ‘sukhadukkhī attā ca laukau ca, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti – itthaikai abhivadanti, ‘adukkhamasukhī attā ca laukau ca, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti – itthaikai abhivadanti|

28. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘sassatau attā ca laukau ca, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti, taisaṃ vata añ‍ñatraiva saddhāya añ‍ñatra ruciyā añ‍ñatra anussavā añ‍ñatra ākāraparivitak‍kā añ‍ñatra diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā pac‍cattaṃyaiva ñāṇaṃ bhavissati parisuddhaṃ pariyaudātanti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| pac‍cattaṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, ñāṇai asati parisuddhai pariyaudātai yadapi yadipi (ka॰) tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā tattha ñāṇabhāgamattamaiva pariyaudapainti tadapi taisaṃ bhavataṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ upādānamakkhāyati| ‘tayidaṃ saṅkhataṃ auḷārikaṃ atthi khau pana saṅkhārānaṃ niraudhau atthaita’nti – iti viditvā tassa nissaraṇadassāvī tathāgatau tadupātivattau|

29. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘asassatau attā ca laukau ca, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti…pai॰… yathā sassatavārai, tathā vitthāraitabbaṃ sassatau ca asassatau ca attā ca laukau ca… naivasassatau nāsassatau attā ca laukau ca… antavā attā ca laukau ca… anantavā attā ca laukau ca… antavā ca anantavā ca attā ca laukau ca… naivantavā nānantavā attā ca laukau ca… aikattasañ‍ñī attā ca laukau ca… nānattasañ‍ñī attā ca laukau ca… parittasañ‍ñī attā ca laukau ca… appamāṇasañ‍ñī attā ca laukau ca… aikantasukhī attā ca laukau ca… aikantadukkhī attā ca laukau ca… sukhadukkhī attā ca laukau ca… adukkhamasukhī attā ca laukau ca, idamaiva  sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ñanti, taisaṃ vata añ‍ñatraiva  saddhāya añ‍ñatra ruciyā añ‍ñatra anussavā añ‍ñatra ākāraparivitak‍kā añ‍ñatra diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā pac‍cattaṃyaiva ñāṇaṃ bhavissati parisuddhaṃ pariyaudātanti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ  vij‍jati| pac‍cattaṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, ñāṇai asati parisuddhai pariyaudātai yadapi tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā tattha ñāṇabhāgamattamaiva pariyaudapainti tadapi taisaṃ bhavataṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ upādānamakkhāyati| ‘tayidaṃ saṅkhataṃ auḷārikaṃ atthi khau pana saṅkhārānaṃ niraudhau atthaita’nti – iti viditvā tassa nissaraṇadassāvī tathāgatau tadupātivattau|

30. ‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā pubbantānudiṭṭhīnañ‍ca paṭinissaggā, aparantānudiṭṭhīnañ‍ca paṭinissaggā, sabbasau kāmasaṃyaujanānaṃ anadhiṭṭhānā, pavivaikaṃ pītiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati – ‘aitaṃ santaṃ aitaṃ paṇītaṃ yadidaṃ pavivaikaṃ pītiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmī’ti| tassa sā pavivaikā pīti nirujjhati| pavivaikāya pītiyā niraudhā uppaj‍jati daumanassaṃ, daumanassassa niraudhā uppaj‍jati pavivaikā pīti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yaṃ chāyā jahati taṃ ātapau pharati, yaṃ ātapau jahati taṃ chāyā pharati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, pavivaikāya pītiyā niraudhā uppaj‍jati daumanassaṃ, daumanassassa niraudhā uppaj‍jati pavivaikā pīti| tayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau abhijānāti| ayaṃ khau bhavaṃ samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā pubbantānudiṭṭhīnañ‍ca paṭinissaggā, aparantānudiṭṭhīnañ‍ca paṭinissaggā, sabbasau kāmasaṃyaujanānaṃ anadhiṭṭhānā, pavivaikaṃ pītiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati – ‘aitaṃ santaṃ aitaṃ paṇītaṃ yadidaṃ pavivaikaṃ pītiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmī’ti| tassa sā pavivaikā pīti nirujjhati| pavivaikāya pītiyā niraudhā uppaj‍jati daumanassaṃ, daumanassassa niraudhā uppaj‍jati pavivaikā pīti| ‘tayidaṃ saṅkhataṃ auḷārikaṃ atthi khau pana saṅkhārānaṃ niraudhau atthaita’nti – iti viditvā tassa nissaraṇadassāvī tathāgatau tadupātivattau|

31. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā pubbantānudiṭṭhīnañ‍ca paṭinissaggā, aparantānudiṭṭhīnañ‍ca paṭinissaggā, sabbasau kāmasaṃyaujanānaṃ anadhiṭṭhānā, pavivaikāya pītiyā samatik‍kamā nirāmisaṃ sukhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati – ‘aitaṃ santaṃ aitaṃ paṇītaṃ yadidaṃ nirāmisaṃ sukhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmī’ti| tassa taṃ nirāmisaṃ sukhaṃ nirujjhati| nirāmisassa sukhassa niraudhā uppaj‍jati pavivaikā pīti, pavivaikāya pītiyā niraudhā uppaj‍jati nirāmisaṃ sukhaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yaṃ chāyā jahati taṃ ātapau pharati, yaṃ ātapau jahati  taṃ chāyā pharati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, nirāmisassa sukhassa niraudhā uppaj‍jati pavivaikā pīti, pavivaikāya pītiyā niraudhā uppaj‍jati nirāmisaṃ sukhaṃ| tayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau abhijānāti| ayaṃ khau bhavaṃ samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā pubbantānudiṭṭhīnañ‍ca paṭinissaggā, aparantānudiṭṭhīnañ‍ca paṭinissaggā, sabbasau kāmasaṃyaujanānaṃ anadhiṭṭhānā, pavivaikāya pītiyā samatik‍kamā, nirāmisaṃ sukhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati – ‘aitaṃ santaṃ aitaṃ paṇītaṃ yadidaṃ nirāmisaṃ sukhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmī’ti| tassa  taṃ nirāmisaṃ sukhaṃ nirujjhati| nirāmisassa sukhassa niraudhā uppaj‍jati pavivaikā pīti, pavivaikāya pītiyā niraudhā uppaj‍jati nirāmisaṃ sukhaṃ| ‘tayidaṃ saṅkhataṃ auḷārikaṃ atthi khau pana saṅkhārānaṃ niraudhau atthaita’nti – iti viditvā tassa nissaraṇadassāvī tathāgatau tadupātivattau|

32. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā pubbantānudiṭṭhīnañ‍ca paṭinissaggā, aparantānudiṭṭhīnañ‍ca paṭinissaggā, sabbasau kāmasaṃyaujanānaṃ anadhiṭṭhānā, pavivaikāya pītiyā samatik‍kamā, nirāmisassa sukhassa samatik‍kamā, adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati – ‘aitaṃ santaṃ aitaṃ paṇītaṃ yadidaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmī’ti| tassa sā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā nirujjhati| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya niraudhā uppaj‍jati nirāmisaṃ sukhaṃ, nirāmisassa sukhassa niraudhā uppaj‍jati adukkhamasukhā vaidanā| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yaṃ chāyā jahati taṃ ātapau pharati, yaṃ ātapau jahati taṃ chāyā pharati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya niraudhā uppaj‍jati nirāmisaṃ sukhaṃ, nirāmisassa sukhassa niraudhā uppaj‍jati adukkhamasukhā vaidanā| tayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau abhijānāti| ayaṃ khau bhavaṃ samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā pubbantānudiṭṭhīnañ‍ca paṭinissaggā, aparantānudiṭṭhīnañ‍ca paṭinissaggā, sabbasau kāmasaṃyaujanānaṃ anadhiṭṭhānā, pavivaikāya pītiyā samatik‍kamā, nirāmisassa sukhassa samatik‍kamā, adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati – ‘aitaṃ santaṃ aitaṃ paṇītaṃ yadidaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ  vaidanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmī’ti| tassa sā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā nirujjhati| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya niraudhā uppaj‍jati nirāmisaṃ sukhaṃ, nirāmisassa sukhassa niraudhā uppaj‍jati adukkhamasukhā vaidanā| ‘tayidaṃ saṅkhataṃ auḷārikaṃ atthi khau pana saṅkhārānaṃ niraudhau atthaita’nti – iti viditvā tassa nissaraṇadassāvī tathāgatau tadupātivattau|

33. ‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā pubbantānudiṭṭhīnañ‍ca paṭinissaggā, aparantānudiṭṭhīnañ‍ca paṭinissaggā, sabbasau kāmasaṃyaujanānaṃ anadhiṭṭhānā, pavivaikāya pītiyā samatik‍kamā, nirāmisassa sukhassa samatik‍kamā, adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya samatik‍kamā – ‘santauhamasmi, nibbutauhamasmi, anupādānauhamasmī’ti samanupassati| tayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau abhijānāti| ayaṃ khau bhavaṃ samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau  vā pubbantānudiṭṭhīnañ‍ca paṭinissaggā, aparantānudiṭṭhīnañ‍ca paṭinissaggā, sabbasau kāmasaṃyaujanānaṃ anadhiṭṭhānā, pavivaikāya pītiyā samatik‍kamā, nirāmisassa sukhassa samatik‍kamā, adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya samatik‍kamā – ‘santauhamasmi, nibbutauhamasmi, anupādānauhamasmī’ti samanupassati; addhā ayamāyasmā nibbānasappāyaṃyaiva  paṭipadaṃ abhivadati| atha ca panāyaṃ bhavaṃ samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā pubbantānudiṭṭhiṃ vā upādiyamānau upādiyati, aparantānudiṭṭhiṃ vā upādiyamānau upādiyati, kāmasaṃyaujanaṃ vā upādiyamānau upādiyati, pavivaikaṃ vā pītiṃ upādiyamānau upādiyati, nirāmisaṃ vā sukhaṃ upādiyamānau upādiyati, adukkhamasukhaṃ vā vaidanaṃ upādiyamānau upādiyati| yañ‍ca khau ayamāyasmā – ‘santauhamasmi, nibbutauhamasmi, anupādānauhamasmī’ti samanupassati tadapi imassa bhautau samaṇassa brāhmaṇassa upādānamakkhāyati| ‘tayidaṃ saṅkhataṃ auḷārikaṃ atthi khau pana saṅkhārānaṃ niraudhau atthaita’nti – iti viditvā tassa nissaraṇadassāvī tathāgatau tadupātivattau|

‘‘idaṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, tathāgataina anuttaraṃ santivarapadaṃ abhisambuddhaṃ yadidaṃ – chan‍naṃ phassāyatanānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ viditvā anupādāvimaukkhau| tayidaṃ bhikkhavai tathāgataina anuttaraṃ santivarapadaṃ abhisambuddhaṃ, yadidaṃ chan‍naṃ phassāyatanānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ viditvā anupādāvimaukkhauti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) anupādāvimaukkhau’’ti anupādāvimaukkhau| tayidaṃ bhikkhavai tathāgataina anuttaraṃ santivarapadaṃ abhisambuddhaṃ, yadidaṃ chan‍naṃ phassāyatanaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ viditvā anupādāvimaukkhauti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)|

idamavauca  bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

pañ‍cattayasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. kintisuttaṃ

34. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā pisinārāyaṃ kusinārāyaṃ (sī॰) viharati baliharaṇai vanasaṇḍai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘kinti vau, bhikkhavai, mayi hauti – ‘cīvarahaitu vā samaṇau gautamau dhammaṃ daisaiti, piṇḍapātahaitu vā samaṇau gautamau dhammaṃ daisaiti, saināsanahaitu vā samaṇau gautamau dhammaṃ daisaiti, itibhavābhavahaitu vā samaṇau gautamau dhammaṃ daisaitī’’’ti? ‘‘na khau nau, bhantai, bhagavati aivaṃ hauti – ‘cīvarahaitu vā samaṇau gautamau dhammaṃ daisaiti, piṇḍapātahaitu vā samaṇau gautamau dhammaṃ daisaiti, saināsanahaitu vā samaṇau gautamau dhammaṃ daisaiti, itibhavābhavahaitu vā samaṇau gautamau dhammaṃ daisaitī’’’ti|

‘‘na ca kira vau, bhikkhavai, mayi aivaṃ hauti – ‘cīvarahaitu vā samaṇau gautamau dhammaṃ daisaiti…pai॰… itibhavābhavahaitu vā samaṇau gautamau dhammaṃ daisaitī’ti; atha kinti carahi vau atha kinti vau (sī॰ pī॰), atha kiñ‍carahi vau (ka॰), bhikkhavai, mayi hautī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ khau nau, bhantai, bhagavati hauti – ‘anukampakau bhagavā hitaisī; anukampaṃ upādāya dhammaṃ daisaitī’’’ti| ‘‘aivañ‍ca  aivaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) kira vau, bhikkhavai, mayi hauti – ‘anukampakau bhagavā hitaisī; anukampaṃ upādāya dhammaṃ daisaitī’’’ti|

35. ‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, yai vau yai tai (ka॰) mayā dhammā abhiñ‍ñā daisitā, saiyyathidaṃ – cattārau satipaṭṭhānā cattārau sammappadhānā cattārau iddhipādā pañ‍cindriyāni pañ‍ca balāni satta baujjhaṅgā  ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, tattha sabbaihaiva samaggaihi sammaudamānaihi avivadamānaihi sikkhitabbaṃ| taisañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, samaggānaṃ sammaudamānānaṃ avivadamānānaṃ sikkhataṃ siyaṃsu siyuṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) saddanīti aulaukaitabbā dvai bhikkhū abhidhammai nānāvādā| tatra cai tumhākaṃ aivamassa – ‘imaisaṃ khau āyasmantānaṃ atthatau caiva nānaṃ byañ‍janatau ca nāna’nti, tattha yaṃ bhikkhuṃ suvacataraṃ subbacataraṃ (ka॰) mañ‍ñaiyyātha sau upasaṅkamitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘āyasmantānaṃ khau atthatau caiva nānaṃ, byañ‍janatau ca nānaṃ| tadamināpaitaṃ tadimināpaitaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) āyasmantau jānātha – yathā atthatau caiva nānaṃ, byañ‍janatau ca nānaṃ| māyasmantau vivādaṃ āpaj‍jitthā’ti| athāparaisaṃ aikataupakkhikānaṃ  bhikkhūnaṃ yaṃ bhikkhuṃ suvacataraṃ mañ‍ñaiyyātha sau upasaṅkamitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘āyasmantānaṃ khau atthatau caiva nānaṃ, byañ‍janatau ca nānaṃ| tadamināpaitaṃ āyasmantau jānātha – yathā atthatau  caiva nānaṃ, byañ‍janatau ca nānaṃ| māyasmantau vivādaṃ āpaj‍jitthā’ti| iti duggahitaṃ duggahitatau dhāraitabbaṃ, suggahitaṃ suggahitatau dhāraitabbaṃ| duggahitaṃ duggahitatau dhāraitvā suggahitaṃ suggahitatau dhāraitvā iti duggahitaṃ duggahitatau dhāraitabbaṃ, duggahitaṃ duggahitatau dhāraitvā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) anantaravārattayai pana idaṃ pāṭhanānattaṃ natthi yau dhammau yau vinayau sau bhāsitabbau|

36. ‘‘tatra  cai tumhākaṃ aivamassa – ‘imaisaṃ khau āyasmantānaṃ atthatau hi khau nānaṃ, byañ‍janatau samaitī’ti, tattha yaṃ bhikkhuṃ suvacataraṃ mañ‍ñaiyyātha sau upasaṅkamitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘āyasmantānaṃ khau atthatau hi nānaṃ, byañ‍janatau samaiti| tadamināpaitaṃ āyasmantau jānātha – yathā atthatau hi khau nānaṃ, byañ‍janatau samaiti| māyasmantau vivādaṃ āpaj‍jitthā’ti| athāparaisaṃ aikataupakkhikānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ yaṃ bhikkhuṃ suvacataraṃ mañ‍ñaiyyātha sau upasaṅkamitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘āyasmantānaṃ khau atthatau hi khau nānaṃ, byañ‍janatau samaiti| tadamināpaitaṃ āyasmantau jānātha – yathā atthatau hi khau nānaṃ, byañ‍janatau samaiti| māyasmantau vivādaṃ āpaj‍jitthā’ti| iti duggahitaṃ duggahitatau dhāraitabbaṃ, suggahitaṃ suggahitatau dhāraitabbaṃ| duggahitaṃ duggahitatau dhāraitvā suggahitaṃ suggahitatau dhāraitvā yau dhammau yau vinayau sau bhāsitabbau|

37. ‘‘tatra cai tumhākaṃ aivamassa – ‘imaisaṃ khau āyasmantānaṃ atthatau hi khau samaiti, byañ‍janatau nāna’nti, tattha yaṃ bhikkhuṃ suvacataraṃ mañ‍ñaiyyātha sau upasaṅkamitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘āyasmantānaṃ khau atthatau hi samaiti, byañ‍janatau nānaṃ| tadamināpaitaṃ āyasmantau jānātha – yathā atthatau hi khau samaiti, byañ‍janatau nānaṃ| appamattakaṃ khau panaitaṃ yadidaṃ – byañ‍janaṃ| māyasmantau appamattakai vivādaṃ āpaj‍jitthā’ti| athāparaisaṃ aikataupakkhikānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ  yaṃ bhikkhuṃ suvacataraṃ mañ‍ñaiyyātha sau upasaṅkamitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘āyasmantānaṃ khau atthatau hi samaiti, byañ‍janatau nānaṃ| tadamināpaitaṃ āyasmantau jānātha – yathā atthatau hi khau samaiti, byañ‍janatau nānaṃ| appamattakaṃ khau panaitaṃ yadidaṃ  – byañ‍janaṃ| māyasmantau appamattakai appamattakaihi (sī॰ pī॰) vivādaṃ āpaj‍jitthā’ti| iti suggahitaṃ suggahitatau dhāraitabbaṃ, duggahitaṃ duggahitatau dhāraitabbaṃ| suggahitaṃ suggahitatau dhāraitvā duggahitaṃ duggahitatau dhāraitvā yau dhammau yau vinayau sau bhāsitabbau|

38. ‘‘tatra  cai tumhākaṃ aivamassa – ‘imaisaṃ khau āyasmantānaṃ atthatau caiva samaiti byañ‍janatau ca samaitī’ti, tattha yaṃ bhikkhuṃ suvacataraṃ mañ‍ñaiyyātha sau upasaṅkamitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘āyasmantānaṃ khau atthatau caiva samaiti, byañ‍janatau ca samaiti| tadamināpaitaṃ āyasmantau jānātha – yathā atthatau caiva samaiti byañ‍janatau ca samaiti| māyasmantau vivādaṃ āpaj‍jitthā’ti| athāparaisaṃ aikataupakkhikānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ yaṃ bhikkhuṃ suvacataraṃ mañ‍ñaiyyātha sau upasaṅkamitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘āyasmantānaṃ khau atthatau caiva samaiti byañ‍janatau ca samaiti| tadamināpaitaṃ āyasmantau jānātha – yathā atthatau caiva samaiti byañ‍janatau ca samaiti| māyasmantau vivādaṃ  āpaj‍jitthā’ti| iti suggahitaṃ suggahitatau dhāraitabbaṃ| suggahitaṃ suggahitatau dhāraitvā yau dhammau yau vinayau sau bhāsitabbau|

39. ‘‘taisañ‍ca  vau, bhikkhavai, samaggānaṃ sammaudamānānaṃ avivadamānānaṃ sikkhataṃ siyā añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau āpatti siyā vītik‍kamau, tatra, bhikkhavai, na caudanāya taritabbaṃ cauditabbaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) turitabbaṃ (?)| puggalau upaparikkhitabbau – ‘iti mayhañ‍ca avihaisā bhavissati parassa ca puggalassa anupaghātau, parau hi puggalau ak‍kaudhanau anupanāhī adaḷhadiṭṭhī suppaṭinissaggī, sak‍kaumi cāhaṃ aitaṃ puggalaṃ akusalā vuṭṭhāpaitvā kusalai patiṭṭhāpaitu’nti| sacai, bhikkhavai, aivamassa, kal‍laṃ vacanāya|

‘‘sacai pana, bhikkhavai, aivamassa – ‘mayhaṃ khau avihaisā bhavissati parassa ca puggalassa upaghātau, parau hi puggalau kaudhanau upanāhī adaḷhadiṭṭhī suppaṭinissaggī, sak‍kaumi cāhaṃ aitaṃ puggalaṃ akusalā vuṭṭhāpaitvā kusalai patiṭṭhāpaituṃ| appamattakaṃ khau panaitaṃ yadidaṃ – parassa yadidaṃ mayhañ‍ca vihaisā bhavissati parassa ca (ka॰) puggalassa upaghātau| atha khau aitadaiva bahutaraṃ – svāhaṃ sak‍kaumi aitaṃ puggalaṃ akusalā vuṭṭhāpaitvā  kusalai patiṭṭhāpaitu’nti| sacai, bhikkhavai, aivamassa, kal‍laṃ vacanāya|

‘‘sacai pana, bhikkhavai, aivamassa – ‘mayhaṃ khau vihaisā bhavissati parassa ca puggalassa anupaghātau| parau hi puggalau ak‍kaudhanau anupanāhī daḷhadiṭṭhī duppaṭinissaggī, sak‍kaumi cāhaṃ aitaṃ puggalaṃ akusalā vuṭṭhāpaitvā kusalai patiṭṭhāpaituṃ| appamattakaṃ khau panaitaṃ yadidaṃ  – mayhaṃ vihaisā mayhañ‍ca vihaisā bhavissati parassa ca puggalassa upaghātau (ka॰)| atha khau aitadaiva bahutaraṃ – svāhaṃ sak‍kaumi aitaṃ puggalaṃ akusalā vuṭṭhāpaitvā kusalai patiṭṭhāpaitu’nti| sacai, bhikkhavai, aivamassa, kal‍laṃ vacanāya|

‘‘sacai pana, bhikkhavai, aivamassa – ‘mayhañ‍ca khau vihaisā bhavissati parassa ca puggalassa upaghātau| parau hi  puggalau kaudhanau upanāhī daḷhadiṭṭhī duppaṭinissaggī, sak‍kaumi cāhaṃ aitaṃ puggalaṃ akusalā vuṭṭhāpaitvā kusalai patiṭṭhāpaituṃ| appamattakaṃ khau panaitaṃ yadidaṃ – mayhañ‍ca vihaisā bhavissati parassa ca puggalassa upaghātau| atha khau aitadaiva bahutaraṃ – svāhaṃ sak‍kaumi aitaṃ puggalaṃ akusalā vuṭṭhāpaitvā kusalai patiṭṭhāpaitu’nti| sacai, bhikkhavai, aivamassa, kal‍laṃ vacanāya|

‘‘sacai pana, bhikkhavai, aivamassa – ‘mayhañ‍ca khau vihaisā bhavissati parassa ca puggalassa upaghātau| parau hi puggalau kaudhanau upanāhī daḷhadiṭṭhī duppaṭinissaggī, na cāhaṃ sak‍kaumi aitaṃ puggalaṃ akusalā vuṭṭhāpaitvā kusalai patiṭṭhāpaitu’nti| aivarūpai, bhikkhavai, puggalai upaikkhā nātimañ‍ñitabbā|

40. ‘‘taisañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, samaggānaṃ sammaudamānānaṃ avivadamānānaṃ sikkhataṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa vacīsaṃhārau vacīsaṅkhārau (sī॰ pī॰) uppaj‍jaiyya diṭṭhipaḷāsau diṭṭhipalāsau (sī॰ ka॰) caitasau āghātau appac‍cayau anabhiraddhi| tattha aikataupakkhikānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ yaṃ bhikkhuṃ suvacataraṃ mañ‍ñaiyyātha sau upasaṅkamitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘yaṃ nau, āvusau, amhākaṃ samaggānaṃ sammaudamānānaṃ avivadamānānaṃ sikkhataṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa vacīsaṃhārau uppan‍nau diṭṭhipaḷāsau caitasau āghātau appac‍cayau anabhiraddhi, taṃ jānamānau samaṇau garahaiyyā’ti samānau (sī॰ ka॰)| sammā byākaramānau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ byākaraiyya  – ‘yaṃ nau, āvusau, amhākaṃ  samaggānaṃ sammaudamānānaṃ avivadamānānaṃ sikkhataṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa vacīsaṃhārau uppan‍nau diṭṭhipaḷāsau caitasau āghātau appac‍cayau anabhiraddhi, taṃ jānamānau samaṇau garahaiyyāti| aitaṃ panāvusau, dhammaṃ appahāya nibbānaṃ sacchikaraiyyā’ti| sammā byākaramānau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘aitaṃ, āvusau, dhammaṃ appahāya na nibbānaṃ sacchikaraiyyā’ti|

‘‘athāparaisaṃ aikataupakkhikānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ yaṃ bhikkhuṃ suvacataraṃ mañ‍ñaiyyātha, sau upasaṅkamitvā  aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘yaṃ nau, āvusau, amhākaṃ samaggānaṃ sammaudamānānaṃ avivadamānānaṃ sikkhataṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa vacīsaṃhārau uppan‍nau diṭṭhipaḷāsau caitasau āghātau appac‍cayau anabhiraddhi, taṃ jānamānau samaṇau garahaiyyā’ti| sammā byākaramānau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘yaṃ nau, āvusau, amhākaṃ samaggānaṃ sammaudamānānaṃ avivadamānānaṃ sikkhataṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa vacīsaṃhārau uppan‍nau diṭṭhipaḷāsau caitasau āghātau appac‍cayau anabhiraddhi taṃ jānamānau samaṇau garahaiyyāti| aitaṃ panāvusau, dhammaṃ appahāya nibbānaṃ sacchikaraiyyā’ti| sammā byākaramānau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ byākaraiyya  – ‘aitaṃ khau, āvusau, dhammaṃ appahāya na nibbānaṃ sacchikaraiyyā’’’ti|

‘‘taṃ cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuṃ parai aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘āyasmatā nau aitai bhikkhū akusalā vuṭṭhāpaitvā kusalai patiṭṭhāpitā’ti? sammā byākaramānau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ byākaraiyya  – ‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃ, tassa mai bhagavā dhammaṃ daisaisi, tāhaṃ dhammaṃ sutvā taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhāsiṃ| taṃ tai bhikkhū dhammaṃ sutvā akusalā vuṭṭhahiṃsu, kusalai patiṭṭhahiṃsū’ti| aivaṃ byākaramānau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na caiva attānaṃ uk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākarauti, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānuvādau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchatī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

kintisuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. sāmagāmasuttaṃ

41. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati sāmagāmai| taina khau pana samayaina nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau nāthaputtau (sī॰ pī॰) pāvāyaṃ adhunākālaṅkatau kālakatau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) hauti| tassa kālaṅkiriyāya bhin‍nā nigaṇṭhā dvaidhikajātā dvaiḷhakajātā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) bhaṇḍanajātā kalahajātā vivādāpan‍nā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ mukhasattīhi vitudantā viharanti – ‘‘na tvaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānāsi, ahaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānāmi| kiṃ tvaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānissasi! micchāpaṭipan‍nau tvamasi, ahamasmi sammāpaṭipan‍nau| sahitaṃ mai, asahitaṃ tai| puraivacanīyaṃ pacchā avaca, pacchāvacanīyaṃ purai avaca| adhiciṇṇaṃ aviciṇṇaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) tai viparāvattaṃ| āraupitau tai vādau| niggahitausi, cara vādappamaukkhāya; nibbaiṭhaihi vā sacai pahausī’’ti| vadhauyaiva khau vadhauyaivaikau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) mañ‍ñai nigaṇṭhaisu nāṭaputtiyaisu vattati| yaipi nigaṇṭhassa nāṭaputtassa sāvakā gihī audātavasanā taipi nigaṇṭhaisu nāṭaputtiyaisu nibbin‍narūpā nibbindarūpā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) virattarūpā paṭivānarūpā yathā taṃ durakkhātai dhammavinayai duppavaiditai aniyyānikai anupasamasaṃvattanikai asammāsambuddhappavaiditai bhin‍nathūpai appaṭisaraṇai|

42. atha khau cundau samaṇuddaisau pāvāyaṃ vassaṃvuṭṭhau vassaṃvutthau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) yaina sāmagāmau yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau  khau cundau samaṇuddaisau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘nigaṇṭhau, bhantai, nāṭaputtau pāvāyaṃ adhunākālaṅkatau| tassa kālaṅkiriyāya bhin‍nā nigaṇṭhā dvaidhikajātā…pai॰… bhin‍nathūpai appaṭisaraṇai’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā ānandau cundaṃ samaṇuddaisaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘atthi khau idaṃ, āvusau cunda, kathāpābhataṃ bhagavantaṃ dassanāya| āyāma, āvusau cunda, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamissāma; upasaṅkamitvā aitamatthaṃ bhagavatau āraucaissāmā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau cundau samaṇuddaisau āyasmatau ānandassa pac‍cassausi|

atha  khau āyasmā ca ānandau cundau ca samaṇuddaisau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā  ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ayaṃ, bhantai, cundau samaṇuddaisau aivamāha – ‘nigaṇṭhau, bhantai, nāṭaputtau pāvāyaṃ adhunākālaṅkatau| tassa kālaṅkiriyāya bhin‍nā nigaṇṭhā dvaidhikajātā…pai॰… bhin‍nathūpai appaṭisaraṇai’ti| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ hauti – ‘māhaiva bhagavatau ac‍cayaina saṅghai vivādau uppaj‍ji; svāssa sau (sī॰ pī॰), svāyaṃ (ka॰) vivādau bahujanāhitāya bahujanāsukhāya bahunau janassa anatthāya ahitāya dukkhāya daivamanussāna’’’nti|

43. ‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, ānanda, yai vau mayā dhammā abhiñ‍ñā daisitā, saiyyathidaṃ – cattārau satipaṭṭhānā cattārau sammappadhānā cattārau iddhipādā pañ‍cindriyāni pañ‍ca balāni satta baujjhaṅgā ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, passasi nau tvaṃ, ānanda, imaisu dhammaisu dvaipi bhikkhū nānāvādai’’ti? ‘‘yai mai, bhantai, dhammā bhagavatā abhiñ‍ñā daisitā, saiyyathidaṃ – cattārau satipaṭṭhānā cattārau sammappadhānā cattārau iddhipādā pañ‍cindriyāni pañ‍ca balāni satta baujjhaṅgā ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, nāhaṃ passāmi imaisu dhammaisu dvaipi bhikkhū nānāvādai| yai ca khau santi ca khau (syā॰ kaṃ॰), santi ca (ka॰), bhantai, puggalā bhagavantaṃ patissayamānarūpā viharanti taipi bhagavatau ac‍cayaina saṅghai vivādaṃ janaiyyuṃ ajjhājīvai vā adhipātimaukkhai vā| svāssa saussa (sī॰ pī॰), svāyaṃ (ka॰) vivādau bahujanāhitāya bahujanāsukhāya bahunau janassa anatthāya ahitāya dukkhāya daivamanussāna’’nti| appamattakau sau, ānanda, vivādau yadidaṃ – ajjhājīvai vā adhipātimaukkhai vā| maggai vā hi, ānanda, paṭipadāya vā saṅghai vivādau uppaj‍jamānau uppaj‍jaiyya; svāssa vivādau bahujanāhitāya bahujanāsukhāya bahunau janassa anatthāya ahitāya dukkhāya daivamanussānaṃ|

44. ‘‘chayimāni, ānanda, vivādamūlāni| katamāni cha? idhānanda, bhikkhu kaudhanau hauti upanāhī| yau sau, ānanda, bhikkhu kaudhanau hauti upanāhī sau sattharipi agāravau viharati appatissau, dhammaipi  agāravau viharati appatissau, saṅghaipi  agāravau viharati appatissau, sikkhāyapi na paripūrakārī hauti| yau sau, ānanda, bhikkhu satthari agāravau viharati appatissau, dhammai… saṅghai agāravau viharati  appatissau, sikkhāya na paripūrakārī hauti, sau saṅghai vivādaṃ janaiti; yau hauti vivādau bahujanāhitāya bahujanāsukhāya, bahunau janassa anatthāya ahitāya dukkhāya daivamanussānaṃ| aivarūpañ‍cai tumhai, ānanda, vivādamūlaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā samanupassaiyyātha, tatra tumhai, ānanda, tassaiva pāpakassa vivādamūlassa pahānāya vāyamaiyyātha| aivarūpañ‍cai tumhai, ānanda, vivādamūlaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā na samanupassaiyyātha| tatra tumhai, ānanda, tassaiva pāpakassa  vivādamūlassa āyatiṃ anavassavāya paṭipaj‍jaiyyātha| aivamaitassa pāpakassa vivādamūlassa pahānaṃ hauti, aivamaitassa pāpakassa vivādamūlassa āyatiṃ anavassavau hauti|

45. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhu makkhī hauti paḷāsī…pai॰… issukī hauti maccharī…pai॰… saṭhau hauti māyāvī…pai॰… pāpicchau hauti micchādiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhī (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) …pai॰… sandiṭṭhiparāmāsī hauti ādhānaggāhī duppaṭinissaggī| yau sau, ānanda, bhikkhu sandiṭṭhiparāmāsī hauti ādhānaggāhī duppaṭinissaggī sau sattharipi agāravau viharati appatissau, dhammaipi agāravau viharati appatissau, saṅghaipi agāravau viharati appatissau, sikkhāyapi na paripūrakārī hauti| yau sau, ānanda, bhikkhu satthari agāravau viharati appatissau, dhammai… saṅghai… sikkhāya na paripūrakārī hauti sau  saṅghai vivādaṃ janaiti; yau hauti vivādau bahujanāhitāya bahujanāsukhāya, bahunau janassa anatthāya ahitāya dukkhāya daivamanussānaṃ| aivarūpañ‍cai tumhai, ānanda, vivādamūlaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā samanupassaiyyātha| tatra tumhai, ānanda, tassaiva pāpakassa vivādamūlassa pahānāya vāyamaiyyātha| aivarūpañ‍cai tumhai, ānanda, vivādamūlaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā na samanupassaiyyātha, tatra tumhai, ānanda, tassaiva pāpakassa vivādamūlassa āyatiṃ anavassavāya paṭipaj‍jaiyyātha| aivamaitassa pāpakassa vivādamūlassa pahānaṃ hauti, aivamaitassa pāpakassa vivādamūlassa āyatiṃ anavassavau hauti| imāni khau, ānanda, cha vivādamūlāni|

46. ‘‘cattārimāni, ānanda, adhikaraṇāni| katamāni cattāri? vivādādhikaraṇaṃ, anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ, kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ – imāni khau, ānanda, cattāri adhikaraṇāni| satta khau panimai, ānanda, adhikaraṇasamathā – uppan‍nuppan‍nānaṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ samathāya vūpasamāya sammukhāvinayau dātabbau, sativinayau dātabbau, amūḷhavinayau dātabbau, paṭiñ‍ñāya kāraitabbaṃ, yaibhuyyasikā, tassapāpiyasikā, tiṇavatthārakau|

47. ‘‘kathañ‍cānanda, sammukhāvinayau hauti? idhānanda, bhikkhū vivadanti dhammauti vā adhammauti vā vinayauti vā avinayauti vā| taihānanda, bhikkhūhi sabbaihaiva samaggaihi san‍nipatitabbaṃ| san‍nipatitvā dhammanaitti samanumaj‍jitabbā| dhammanaittiṃ samanumaj‍jitvā yathā tattha samaiti  tathā taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaitabbaṃ| aivaṃ khau, ānanda, sammukhāvinayau hauti; aivañ‍ca panidhaikac‍cānaṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ vūpasamau hauti yadidaṃ – sammukhāvinayaina|

48. ‘‘kathañ‍cānanda, yaibhuyyasikā hauti? tai cai, ānanda, bhikkhū na sak‍kaunti taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ tasmiṃ āvāsai vūpasamaituṃ| taihānanda, bhikkhūhi yasmiṃ āvāsai bahutarā bhikkhū sau āvāsau gantabbau| tattha sabbaihaiva samaggaihi san‍nipatitabbaṃ| san‍nipatitvā dhammanaitti samanumaj‍jitabbā| dhammanaittiṃ samanumaj‍jitvā yathā tattha samaiti tathā taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaitabbaṃ| aivaṃ khau, ānanda, yaibhuyyasikā hauti, aivañ‍ca panidhaikac‍cānaṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ vūpasamau hauti yadidaṃ – yaibhuyyasikāya|

49. ‘‘kathañ‍cānanda, sativinayau hauti? idhānanda, bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ aivarūpāya garukāya āpattiyā caudainti pārājikaina vā pārājikasāmantaina vā – ‘saratāyasmā aivarūpiṃ aivarūpaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) aivarūpāya-iti vuc‍camānavacanaina samaiti| vinayainapi saṃsandaitabbaṃ garukaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā pārājikaṃ vā pārājikasāmantaṃ vā’ti? sau aivamāha – ‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, sarāmi aivarūpiṃ garukaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā pārājikaṃ vā pārājikasāmantaṃ  vā’ti| tassa khau tassa khau aivaṃ (sabbattha), ānanda, bhikkhunau sativinayau dātabbau| aivaṃ khau, ānanda, sativinayau hauti, aivañ‍ca panidhaikac‍cānaṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ vūpasamau hauti yadidaṃ – sativinayaina|

50. ‘‘kathañ‍cānanda, amūḷhavinayau hauti? idhānanda, bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ aivarūpāya garukāya āpattiyā caudainti pārājikaina vā pārājikasāmantaina vā – ‘saratāyasmā aivarūpiṃ garukaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā pārājikaṃ vā pārājikasāmantaṃ vā’ti? (sau aivamāha – ‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, sarāmi aivarūpiṃ garukaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā pārājikaṃ vā pārājikasāmantaṃ vā’ti| tamainaṃ sau nibbaiṭhaintaṃ ativaiṭhaiti – ‘iṅghāyasmā sādhukamaiva jānāhi yadi sarasi aivarūpiṃ garukaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā pārājikaṃ vā pārājikasāmantaṃ vā’ti|) ( ) aitthantarai pāṭhau cūḷava॰ 237 natthi tassapāpiyasikāvāraiaivaitaina bhavitabbaṃ sau aivamāha – ‘ahaṃ khau, āvusau, ummādaṃ pāpuṇiṃ caitasau vipariyāsaṃ| taina mai ummattakaina bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ bhāsitaparikantaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| nāhaṃ taṃ sarāmi| mūḷhaina mai aitaṃ kata’nti| tassa khau tassa khau aivaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), ānanda, bhikkhunau amūḷhavinayau dātabbau| aivaṃ khau, ānanda, amūḷhavinayau hauti, aivañ‍ca panidhaikac‍cānaṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ vūpasamau hauti yadidaṃ – amūḷhavinayaina|

51. ‘‘kathañ‍cānanda, paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ hauti? idhānanda, bhikkhu cauditau vā acauditau vā āpattiṃ sarati, vivarati uttānīkarauti uttāniṃ karauti (ka॰)| taina, ānanda, bhikkhunā vuḍḍhataraṃ bhikkhuṃ vuḍḍhatarau bhikkhu (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ cīvaraṃ katvā pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ āpattiṃ āpan‍nau, taṃ paṭidaisaimī’ti| sau aivamāha – ‘passasī’ti? ‘āma passāmī’ti| ‘āyatiṃ  saṃvaraiyyāsī’ti| (‘saṃvarissāmī’ti|) ( ) vinayai natthi aivaṃ khau, ānanda, paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ hauti, aivañ‍ca panidhaikac‍cānaṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ vūpasamau hauti yadidaṃ – paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina|

52. ‘‘kathañ‍cānanda, tassapāpiyasikā hauti? idhānanda, bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ aivarūpāya garukāya āpattiyā caudaiti pārājikaina vā pārājikasāmantaina vā – ‘saratāyasmā aivarūpiṃ garukaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā pārājikaṃ vā pārājikasāmantaṃ vā’ti? sau aivamāha – ‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, sarāmi aivarūpiṃ garukaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā pārājikaṃ vā pārājikasāmantaṃ  vā’ti| tamainaṃ sau nibbaiṭhaintaṃ ativaiṭhaiti – ‘iṅghāyasmā sādhukamaiva jānāhi yadi sarasi aivarūpiṃ garukaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā pārājikaṃ vā pārājikasāmantaṃ vā’ti| sau aivamāha – ‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, sarāmi aivarūpiṃ garukaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā pārājikaṃ vā pārājikasāmantaṃ vā; sarāmi ca khau ahaṃ, āvusau, aivarūpiṃ appamattikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’ti| tamainaṃ sau nibbaiṭhaintaṃ ativaiṭhaiti – ‘iṅghāyasmā sādhukamaiva jānāhi yadi sarasi aivarūpiṃ garukaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā pārājikaṃ vā pārājikasāmantaṃ vā’ti? sau aivamāha – ‘imañhi nāmāhaṃ, āvusau, appamattikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā apuṭṭhau paṭijānissāmi| kiṃ panāhaṃ aivarūpiṃ garukaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā pārājikaṃ vā pārājikasāmantaṃ vā puṭṭhau napaṭijānissāmī’ti? sau aivamāha – ‘imañhi nāma tvaṃ, āvusau, appamattikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā apuṭṭhau napaṭijānissasi, kiṃ pana tvaṃ aivarūpiṃ garukaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā pārājikaṃ vā pārājikasāmantaṃ vā puṭṭhau apuṭṭhau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) paṭijānissasi? iṅghāyasmā sādhukamaiva jānāhi yadi sarasi aivarūpiṃ garukaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā pārājikaṃ vā pārājikasāmantaṃ vā’ti| sau aivamāha – ‘sarāmi khau ahaṃ, āvusau, aivarūpiṃ garukaṃ āpattiṃ  āpaj‍jitā pārājikaṃ vā pārājikasāmantaṃ vā| davā mai aitaṃ vuttaṃ, ravā mai aitaṃ vuttaṃ – nāhaṃ taṃ sarāmi aivarūpiṃ garukaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā pārājikaṃ vā pārājikasāmantaṃ vā’ti| aivaṃ khau, ānanda, tassapāpiyasikā hauti, aivañ‍ca panidhaikac‍cānaṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ vūpasamau hauti yadidaṃ – tassapāpiyasikāya|

53. ‘‘kathañ‍cānanda, tiṇavatthārakau hauti? idhānanda, bhikkhūnaṃ bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānaṃ viharataṃ bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ hauti bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| taihānanda, bhikkhūhi sabbaihaiva samaggaihi san‍nipatitabbaṃ| san‍nipatitvā aikataupakkhikānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ byattaina byattataraina (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) bhikkhunā uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ cīvaraṃ katvā añ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| idaṃ amhākaṃ bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānaṃ viharataṃ bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, ahaṃ yā caiva imaisaṃ āyasmantānaṃ āpatti yā ca attanau āpatti, imaisañ‍caiva āyasmantānaṃ atthāya attanau ca atthāya, saṅghamajjhai  tiṇavatthārakaina daisaiyyaṃ, ṭhapaitvā thul‍lavaj‍jaṃ ṭhapaitvā gihipaṭisaṃyutta’’’nti|

‘‘athāparaisaṃ aikataupakkhikānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ byattaina bhikkhunā uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ cīvaraṃ katvā añ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| idaṃ amhākaṃ bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānaṃ viharataṃ bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, ahaṃ yā caiva imaisaṃ āyasmantānaṃ āpatti yā ca attanau āpatti, imaisañ‍caiva āyasmantānaṃ atthāya attanau ca atthāya, saṅghamajjhai tiṇavatthārakaina daisaiyyaṃ, ṭhapaitvā thul‍lavaj‍jaṃ ṭhapaitvā gihipaṭisaṃyutta’’’nti|

‘‘aivaṃ khau, ānanda, tiṇavatthārakau hauti, aivañ‍ca panidhaikac‍cānaṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ vūpasamau hauti yadidaṃ – tiṇavatthārakaina|

54. ‘‘chayimai, ānanda, dhammā sāraṇīyā piyakaraṇā garukaraṇā saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattanti| katamai cha? idhānanda, bhikkhunau maittaṃ kāyakammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti sabrahmacārīsu āvi caiva rahau ca| ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau piyakaraṇau garukaraṇau saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhunau maittaṃ vacīkammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti sabrahmacārīsu āvi caiva rahau ca| ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau piyakaraṇau garukaraṇau  saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā  aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhunau maittaṃ manaukammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti sabrahmacārīsu āvi caiva rahau ca| ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau piyakaraṇau garukaraṇau saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhu – yai tai lābhā dhammikā dhammaladdhā antamasau pattapariyāpan‍namattampi tathārūpaihi lābhaihi – apaṭivibhattabhaugī hauti, sīlavantaihi sabrahmacārīhi sādhāraṇabhaugī| ayampi dhammau  sāraṇīyau piyakaraṇau garukaraṇau saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhu – yāni tāni sīlāni akhaṇḍāni acchiddāni asabalāni akammāsāni bhujissāni viñ‍ñuppasatthāni aparāmaṭṭhāni samādhisaṃvattanikāni tathārūpaisu sīlaisu – sīlasāmañ‍ñagatau viharati sabrahmacārīhi āvi caiva rahau ca| ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau piyakaraṇau garukaraṇau saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhu – yāyaṃ diṭṭhi ariyā niyyānikā niyyāti tak‍karassa sammā dukkhakkhayā tathārūpāya diṭṭhiyā – diṭṭhisāmañ‍ñagatau viharati sabrahmacārīhi āvi caiva rahau ca| ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau piyakaraṇau garukaraṇau saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā  aikībhāvāya saṃvattati| imai khau, ānanda, cha sāraṇīyā dhammā piyakaraṇā garukaraṇā saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattanti|
