‘‘imai cai tumhai, ānanda, cha sāraṇīyai dhammai samādāya vattaiyyātha, passatha nau tumhai, ānanda, taṃ vacanapathaṃ aṇuṃ vā thūlaṃ vā yaṃ tumhai nādhivāsaiyyāthā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘tasmātihānanda, imai cha sāraṇīyai dhammai samādāya vattatha| taṃ vau bhavissati dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

sāmagāmasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. sunakkhattasuttaṃ

55. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi  bhagavatau santikai añ‍ñā byākatā hauti – ‘‘‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāmā’’ti| assausi khau sunakkhattau licchaviputtau – ‘‘sambahulaihi kira bhikkhūhi bhagavatau santikai añ‍ñā byākatā hauti – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāmā’’ti| atha khau sunakkhattau licchaviputtau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sunakkhattau licchaviputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhantai – ‘sambahulaihi kira bhikkhūhi bhagavatau santikai añ‍ñā byākatā – khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāmā’’ti| ‘‘yai tai, bhantai, bhikkhū bhagavatau santikai añ‍ñaṃ byākaṃsu – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāmā’’ti, kac‍ci tai, bhantai, bhikkhū sammadaiva añ‍ñaṃ byākaṃsu udāhu santaitthaikac‍cai bhikkhū adhimānaina añ‍ñaṃ byākaṃsūti?

56. ‘‘yai tai, sunakkhatta, bhikkhū mama santikai añ‍ñaṃ byākaṃsu – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāmā’’ti| ‘‘santaitthaikac‍cai bhikkhū sammadaiva añ‍ñaṃ byākaṃsu, santi panidhaikac‍cai bhikkhū adhimānainapi adhimānaina (?) añ‍ñaṃ byākaṃsu| tatra, sunakkhatta, yai tai bhikkhū sammadaiva añ‍ñaṃ byākaṃsu taisaṃ taṃ tathaiva hauti; yai pana tai bhikkhū adhimānaina añ‍ñaṃ byākaṃsu tatra, sunakkhatta, tathāgatassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘dhammaṃ naisaṃ daisaissa’nti daisaiyyanti (pī॰ ka॰)| aivañ‍caittha, sunakkhatta, tathāgatassa hauti – ‘dhammaṃ naisaṃ daisaissa’nti| atha ca panidhaikac‍cai maughapurisā pañhaṃ abhisaṅkharitvā abhisaṅkharitvā tathāgataṃ upasaṅkamitvā pucchanti| tatra, sunakkhatta, yampi  tathāgatassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘dhammaṃ naisaṃ daisaissa’nti tassapi hauti añ‍ñathatta’’nti| ‘‘aitassa bhagavā kālau, aitassa sugata kālau, yaṃ bhagavā dhammaṃ daisaiyya| bhagavatau sutvā bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, sunakkhatta suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau sunakkhattau licchaviputtau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca –

57. ‘‘pañ‍ca  khau imai, sunakkhatta, kāmaguṇā| katamai pañ‍ca? cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā, sautaviñ‍ñaiyyā saddā…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyā gandhā… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā – imai khau, sunakkhatta, pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā|

58. ‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, sunakkhatta, vij‍jati yaṃ idhaikac‍cau purisapuggalau  laukāmisādhimuttau assa| laukāmisādhimuttassa khau, sunakkhatta, purisapuggalassa tappatirūpī caiva kathā saṇṭhāti, tadanudhammañ‍ca anuvitak‍kaiti, anuvicāraiti, tañ‍ca purisaṃ bhajati, taina ca vittiṃ āpaj‍jati; ānaiñ‍japaṭisaṃyuttāya ca pana kathāya kacchamānāya na sussūsati, na sautaṃ audahati, na añ‍ñā cittaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti upaṭṭhapaiti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), na ca taṃ purisaṃ bhajati, na ca taina vittiṃ āpaj‍jati| saiyyathāpi, sunakkhatta, purisau sakamhā gāmā vā nigamā vā ciravippavutthau assa| sau añ‍ñataraṃ purisaṃ passaiyya tamhā gāmā vā nigamā vā acirapak‍kantaṃ| sau taṃ purisaṃ tassa gāmassa vā nigamassa vā khaimatañ‍ca subhikkhatañ‍ca appābādhatañ‍ca pucchaiyya; tassa sau purisau tassa gāmassa vā nigamassa vā khaimatañ‍ca subhikkhatañ‍ca  appābādhatañ‍ca saṃsaiyya| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sunakkhatta, api nu sau purisau tassa purisassa sussūsaiyya, sautaṃ audahaiyya, añ‍ñā cittaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiyya, tañ‍ca purisaṃ bhajaiyya, taina ca vittiṃ āpaj‍jaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, sunakkhatta, ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ idhaikac‍cau purisapuggalau laukāmisādhimuttau assa| laukāmisādhimuttassa khau, sunakkhatta, purisapuggalassa tappatirūpī caiva kathā saṇṭhāti, tadanudhammañ‍ca anuvitak‍kaiti, anuvicāraiti, tañ‍ca purisaṃ bhajati, taina ca vittiṃ āpaj‍jati; ānaiñ‍japaṭisaṃyuttāya ca pana kathāya kacchamānāya na  sussūsati, na sautaṃ audahati, na añ‍ñā cittaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti, na ca taṃ purisaṃ bhajati, na ca taina vittiṃ āpaj‍jati| sau aivamassa vaiditabbau – ‘ānaiñ‍jasaṃyaujanaina hi khau visaṃyuttau ānaiñ‍jasaṃyaujanaina hi khau visaṃyuttau-iti pāṭhau sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu natthi, aṭṭhakathāsu pana tabbaṇṇanā dissatiyaiva laukāmisādhimuttau purisapuggalau’’’ti|

59. ‘‘ṭhānaṃ  khau panaitaṃ, sunakkhatta, vij‍jati yaṃ idhaikac‍cau purisapuggalau ānaiñ‍jādhimuttau  assa| ānaiñ‍jādhimuttassa khau, sunakkhatta, purisapuggalassa tappatirūpī caiva kathā saṇṭhāti, tadanudhammañ‍ca anuvitak‍kaiti, anuvicāraiti, tañ‍ca purisaṃ bhajati, taina ca vittiṃ āpaj‍jati; laukāmisapaṭisaṃyuttāya ca pana kathāya kacchamānāya na sussūsati, na sautaṃ audahati, na añ‍ñā cittaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti, na ca taṃ purisaṃ bhajati, na ca taina vittiṃ āpaj‍jati| saiyyathāpi, sunakkhatta, paṇḍupalāsau bandhanā pavuttau abhabbau haritattāya; aivamaiva khau, sunakkhatta, ānaiñ‍jādhimuttassa purisapuggalassa yai laukāmisasaṃyaujanai sai pavuttai| sau aivamassa vaiditabbau – ‘laukāmisasaṃyaujanaina hi khau visaṃyuttau ānaiñ‍jādhimuttau purisapuggalau’’’ti|

60. ‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, sunakkhatta, vij‍jati yaṃ idhaikac‍cau purisapuggalau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanādhimuttau assa| ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanādhimuttassa khau, sunakkhatta, purisapuggalassa tappatirūpī caiva kathā saṇṭhāti, tadanudhammañ‍ca anuvitak‍kaiti, anuvicāraiti, tañ‍ca purisaṃ bhajati, taina ca vittiṃ āpaj‍jati; ānaiñ‍japaṭisaṃyuttāya ca pana kathāya kacchamānāya na sussūsati, na sautaṃ audahati, na añ‍ñā cittaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti, na ca taṃ purisaṃ bhajati, na ca taina vittiṃ āpaj‍jati| saiyyathāpi, sunakkhatta, puthusilā dvaidhābhin‍nā appaṭisandhikā hauti; aivamaiva khau, sunakkhatta, ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanādhimuttassa purisapuggalassa yai ānaiñ‍jasaṃyaujanai sai bhin‍nai| sau aivamassa vaiditabbau – ‘ānaiñ‍jasaṃyaujanaina hi khau visaṃyuttau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanādhimuttau purisapuggalau’’’ti|

61. ‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, sunakkhatta, vij‍jati yaṃ idhaikac‍cau purisapuggalau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanādhimuttau assa| naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanādhimuttassa khau, sunakkhatta, purisapuggalassa tappatirūpī caiva kathā saṇṭhāti, tadanudhammañ‍ca anuvitak‍kaiti, anuvicāraiti, tañ‍ca purisaṃ bhajati, taina ca vittiṃ āpaj‍jati; ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanapaṭisaṃyuttāya ca pana kathāya kacchamānāya na sussūsati, na sautaṃ audahati, na añ‍ñā cittaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti, na ca taṃ purisaṃ bhajati, na ca taina vittiṃ āpaj‍jati| saiyyathāpi, sunakkhatta, purisau manuñ‍ñabhaujanaṃ bhuttāvī chaḍḍaiyya chaddaiyya (?)| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sunakkhatta, api  nu tassa purisassa tasmiṃ bhattai vantai (ka॰ sī॰), bhuttai (ka॰ sī॰ ka॰) puna bhauttukamyatā assā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘aduñhi, bhantai, bhattaṃ vantaṃ (sī॰) paṭikūlasammata’’nti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, sunakkhatta, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanādhimuttassa  purisapuggalassa yai ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasaṃyaujanai sai vantai| sau aivamassa vaiditabbau – ‘ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasaṃyaujanaina hi khau visaṃyuttau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanādhimuttau  purisapuggalau’ti|

62. ‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, sunakkhatta, vij‍jati yaṃ idhaikac‍cau purisapuggalau sammā nibbānādhimuttau assa| sammā nibbānādhimuttassa khau, sunakkhatta, purisapuggalassa tappatirūpī caiva kathā saṇṭhāti, tadanudhammañ‍ca anuvitak‍kaiti, anuvicāraiti, tañ‍ca purisaṃ bhajati, taina ca vittiṃ āpaj‍jati; naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanapaṭisaṃyuttāya ca pana kathāya  kacchamānāya na sussūsati, na sautaṃ audahati, na añ‍ñā cittaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti, na ca taṃ purisaṃ bhajati, na ca taina vittiṃ āpaj‍jati| saiyyathāpi, sunakkhatta, tālau matthakacchin‍nau abhabbau puna viruḷhiyā; aivamaiva khau, sunakkhatta, sammā nibbānādhimuttassa purisapuggalassa yai naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasaṃyaujanai sai ucchin‍namūlai tālāvatthukatai anabhāvaṃkatai anabhāvakatai (sī॰ pī॰), anabhāvaṅgatai (syā॰ kaṃ॰) āyatiṃ anuppādadhammai| sau aivamassa vaiditabbau – ‘naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasaṃyaujanaina hi khau visaṃyuttau sammā nibbānādhimuttau purisapuggalau’’’ti|

63. ‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, sunakkhatta, vij‍jati yaṃ idhaikac‍cassa bhikkhunau aivamassa – ‘taṇhā khau sal‍laṃ samaṇaina vuttaṃ, avij‍jāvisadausau, chandarāgabyāpādaina ruppati| taṃ mai taṇhāsal‍laṃ pahīnaṃ, apanītau avij‍jāvisadausau, sammā nibbānādhimuttauhamasmī’ti| aivaṃmāni aivaṃmānī (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰), aivamādi (syā॰ kaṃ॰) assa atathaṃ samānaṃ atthaṃ samānaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), atthasamānaṃ (sī॰)| sau yāni sammā nibbānādhimuttassa asappāyāni tāni anuyuñ‍jaiyya; asappāyaṃ cakkhunā rūpadassanaṃ anuyuñ‍jaiyya, asappāyaṃ sautaina  saddaṃ anuyuñ‍jaiyya, asappāyaṃ ghānaina gandhaṃ anuyuñ‍jaiyya, asappāyaṃ jivhāya rasaṃ anuyuñ‍jaiyya, asappāyaṃ kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ anuyuñ‍jaiyya, asappāyaṃ manasā dhammaṃ anuyuñ‍jaiyya| tassa asappāyaṃ cakkhunā rūpadassanaṃ anuyuttassa, asappāyaṃ sautaina saddaṃ anuyuttassa, asappāyaṃ ghānaina gandhaṃ anuyuttassa, asappāyaṃ jivhāya rasaṃ anuyuttassa, asappāyaṃ kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ anuyuttassa, asappāyaṃ manasā dhammaṃ anuyuttassa rāgau cittaṃ anuddhaṃsaiyya| sau rāgānuddhaṃsitaina cittaina maraṇaṃ vā nigacchaiyya maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, sunakkhatta, purisau sal‍laina viddhau assa savisaina gāḷhūpalaipanaina| tassa mittāmac‍cā  ñātisālauhitā bhisak‍kaṃ sal‍lakattaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiyyuṃ| tassa sau bhisak‍kau sal‍lakattau satthaina vaṇamukhaṃ parikantaiyya| satthaina vaṇamukhaṃ parikantitvā aisaniyā sal‍laṃ aisaiyya| aisaniyā sal‍laṃ aisitvā sal‍laṃ  abbuhaiyya, apanaiyya visadausaṃ saupādisaisaṃ| saupādisaisauti anupādisaisauti (sabbattha) ayaṃ hi tathāgatassa visayau jānamānau sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ambhau purisa, ubbhataṃ khau tai sal‍laṃ, apanītau visadausau saupādisaisau anupādisaisau (sabbattha) ayampi tathāgatassa visayau| analañ‍ca tai antarāyāya| sappāyāni caiva bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jaiyyāsi, mā tai asappāyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jatau vaṇau assāvī assa| kālaina kālañ‍ca vaṇaṃ dhauvaiyyāsi, kālaina kālaṃ vaṇamukhaṃ ālimpaiyyāsi, mā tai na kālaina kālaṃ vaṇaṃ dhauvatau na kālaina kālaṃ vaṇamukhaṃ  ālimpatau pubbalauhitaṃ vaṇamukhaṃ pariyaunandhi| mā ca vātātapai cārittaṃ anuyuñ‍ji, mā tai vātātapai cārittaṃ anuyuttassa rajausūkaṃ vaṇamukhaṃ anuddhaṃsaisi| vaṇānurakkhī ca, ambhau purisa, viharaiyyāsi vaṇasāraupī’ti vaṇassāraupīti (ka॰) vaṇa + saṃ + raupī = vaṇasāraupī-iti padavibhāgau| tassa aivamassa – ‘ubbhataṃ khau mai sal‍laṃ, apanītau visadausau anupādisaisau| analañ‍ca mai antarāyāyā’ti| sau asappāyāni caiva bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jaiyya| tassa asappāyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jatau vaṇau assāvī assa| na ca kālaina kālaṃ vaṇaṃ dhauvaiyya, na ca kālaina kālaṃ vaṇamukhaṃ ālimpaiyya| tassa na kālaina kālaṃ vaṇaṃ dhauvatau, na kālaina kālaṃ vaṇamukhaṃ ālimpatau pubbalauhitaṃ vaṇamukhaṃ pariyaunandhaiyya| vātātapai ca cārittaṃ anuyuñ‍jaiyya| tassa vātātapai cārittaṃ anuyuttassa rajausūkaṃ vaṇamukhaṃ  anuddhaṃsaiyya| na ca vaṇānurakkhī viharaiyya na vaṇasāraupī| tassa imissā ca asappāyakiriyāya, asuci visadausau apanītau saupādisaisau tadubhayaina vaṇau puthuttaṃ gacchaiyya| sau puthuttaṃ gataina vaṇaina maraṇaṃ vā nigacchaiyya maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, sunakkhatta, ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ idhaikac‍cassa bhikkhunau aivamassa – ‘taṇhā khau sal‍laṃ samaṇaina vuttaṃ, avij‍jāvisadausau chandarāgabyāpādaina ruppati| taṃ mai taṇhāsal‍laṃ pahīnaṃ, apanītau  avij‍jāvisadausau, sammā nibbānādhimuttauhamasmī’ti| aivaṃmāni assa atathaṃ samānaṃ| sau yāni sammā nibbānādhimuttassa asappāyāni tāni anuyuñ‍jaiyya, asappāyaṃ cakkhunā  rūpadassanaṃ anuyuñ‍jaiyya, asappāyaṃ sautaina saddaṃ anuyuñ‍jaiyya, asappāyaṃ ghānaina gandhaṃ anuyuñ‍jaiyya, asappāyaṃ jivhāya rasaṃ anuyuñ‍jaiyya, asappāyaṃ kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ anuyuñ‍jaiyya, asappāyaṃ manasā dhammaṃ anuyuñ‍jaiyya| tassa asappāyaṃ cakkhunā rūpadassanaṃ anuyuttassa, asappāyaṃ sautaina saddaṃ anuyuttassa, asappāyaṃ ghānaina  gandhaṃ anuyuttassa, asappāyaṃ jivhāya rasaṃ anuyuttassa, asappāyaṃ kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ anuyuttassa, asappāyaṃ manasā dhammaṃ anuyuttassa rāgau cittaṃ anuddhaṃsaiyya| sau rāgānuddhaṃsitaina cittaina maraṇaṃ vā nigacchaiyya maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ| maraṇañhaitaṃ, sunakkhatta, ariyassa vinayai yau sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati; maraṇamattañhaitaṃ, sunakkhatta, dukkhaṃ yaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ saṃkiliṭṭhaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati|

64. ‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, sunakkhatta, vij‍jati yaṃ idhaikac‍cassa bhikkhunau aivamassa – ‘taṇhā khau sal‍laṃ samaṇaina vuttaṃ, avij‍jāvisadausau chandarāgabyāpādaina ruppati| taṃ mai taṇhāsal‍laṃ pahīnaṃ, apanītau avij‍jāvisadausau, sammā nibbānādhimuttauhamasmī’ti| sammā nibbānādhimuttassaiva satau sau yāni sammā nibbānādhimuttassa asappāyāni tāni nānuyuñ‍jaiyya, asappāyaṃ cakkhunā rūpadassanaṃ nānuyuñ‍jaiyya, asappāyaṃ sautaina saddaṃ nānuyuñ‍jaiyya, asappāyaṃ ghānaina gandhaṃ nānuyuñ‍jaiyya, asappāyaṃ jivhāya rasaṃ nānuyuñ‍jaiyya, asappāyaṃ kāyaina  phauṭṭhabbaṃ nānuyuñ‍jaiyya, asappāyaṃ manasā dhammaṃ nānuyuñ‍jaiyya| tassa asappāyaṃ cakkhunā rūpadassanaṃ nānuyuttassa, asappāyaṃ sautaina saddaṃ nānuyuttassa, asappāyaṃ ghānaina gandhaṃ nānuyuttassa, asappāyaṃ jivhāya rasaṃ nānuyuttassa, asappāyaṃ  kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ nānuyuttassa, asappāyaṃ manasā dhammaṃ nānuyuttassa rāgau cittaṃ nānuddhaṃsaiyya| sau na rāgānuddhaṃsitaina  cittaina naiva maraṇaṃ vā nigacchaiyya na maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, sunakkhatta, purisau sal‍laina viddhau assa savisaina gāḷhūpalaipanaina| tassa mittāmac‍cā ñātisālauhitā bhisak‍kaṃ sal‍lakattaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiyyuṃ| tassa sau bhisak‍kau sal‍lakattau satthaina vaṇamukhaṃ parikantaiyya| satthaina vaṇamukhaṃ parikantitvā aisaniyā sal‍laṃ aisaiyya| aisaniyā sal‍laṃ aisitvā sal‍laṃ abbuhaiyya, apanaiyya visadausaṃ anupādisaisaṃ| anupādisaisauti jānamānau sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ambhau purisa, ubbhataṃ khau tai sal‍laṃ, apanītau visadausau anupādisaisau| analañ‍ca tai antarāyāya| sappāyāni caiva bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jaiyyāsi, mā tai asappāyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jatau vaṇau assāvī assa| kālaina kālañ‍ca vaṇaṃ dhauvaiyyāsi, kālaina kālaṃ vaṇamukhaṃ ālimpaiyyāsi| mā tai na kālaina kālaṃ vaṇaṃ dhauvatau na kālaina kālaṃ vaṇamukhaṃ ālimpatau pubbalauhitaṃ vaṇamukhaṃ pariyaunandhi| mā ca vātātapai cārittaṃ anuyuñ‍ji, mā tai vātātapai cārittaṃ anuyuttassa rajausūkaṃ vaṇamukhaṃ anuddhaṃsaisi| vaṇānurakkhī ca, ambhau purisa, viharaiyyāsi vaṇasāraupī’ti| tassa aivamassa – ‘ubbhataṃ khau mai sal‍laṃ, apanītau visadausau anupādisaisau| analañ‍ca mai antarāyāyā’ti| sau sappāyāni caiva bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jaiyya| tassa sappāyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jatau vaṇau na assāvī assa| kālaina kālañ‍ca vaṇaṃ dhauvaiyya, kālaina kālaṃ vaṇamukhaṃ ālimpaiyya| tassa kālaina kālaṃ vaṇaṃ dhauvatau kālaina kālaṃ vaṇamukhaṃ ālimpatau na pubbalauhitaṃ vaṇamukhaṃ pariyaunandhaiyya| na ca vātātapai cārittaṃ anuyuñ‍jaiyya| tassa vātātapai cārittaṃ ananuyuttassa rajausūkaṃ vaṇamukhaṃ nānuddhaṃsaiyya| vaṇānurakkhī ca viharaiyya vaṇasāraupī| tassa imissā ca sappāyakiriyāya asu ca asuci (sabbattha) saucāti tabbaṇṇanā manasikātabbā visadausau apanītau anupādisaisau tadubhayaina vaṇau viruhaiyya| sau ruḷhaina vaṇaina sañchavinā naiva maraṇaṃ vā nigacchaiyya na maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ|

‘‘aivamaiva  khau, sunakkhatta, ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ idhaikac‍cassa bhikkhunau aivamassa – ‘taṇhā khau sal‍laṃ samaṇaina vuttaṃ, avij‍jāvisadausau  chandarāgabyāpādaina ruppati| taṃ mai taṇhāsal‍laṃ pahīnaṃ, apanītau avij‍jāvisadausau, sammā nibbānādhimuttauhamasmī’ti| sammā nibbānādhimuttassaiva satau sau yāni sammā nibbānādhimuttassa asappāyāni tāni nānuyuñ‍jaiyya, asappāyaṃ cakkhunā rūpadassanaṃ nānuyuñ‍jaiyya, asappāyaṃ sautaina saddaṃ nānuyuñ‍jaiyya, asappāyaṃ ghānaina gandhaṃ nānuyuñ‍jaiyya, asappāyaṃ jivhāya rasaṃ nānuyuñ‍jaiyya, asappāyaṃ kāyaina  phauṭṭhabbaṃ nānuyuñ‍jaiyya, asappāyaṃ manasā dhammaṃ nānuyuñ‍jaiyya| tassa asappāyaṃ cakkhunā rūpadassanaṃ nānuyuttassa, asappāyaṃ sautaina saddaṃ nānuyuttassa, asappāyaṃ ghānaina gandhaṃ nānuyuttassa, asappāyaṃ jivhāya rasaṃ nānuyuttassa, asappāyaṃ kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ nānuyuttassa, asappāyaṃ manasā dhammaṃ nānuyuttassa, rāgau cittaṃ nānuddhaṃsaiyya| sau na rāgānuddhaṃsitaina cittaina naiva maraṇaṃ vā nigacchaiyya na maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ|

65. ‘‘upamā khau mai ayaṃ, sunakkhatta, katā atthassa viñ‍ñāpanāya| ayaṃyaivaittha atthau – vaṇauti khau, sunakkhatta, chan‍naitaṃ ajjhattikānaṃ āyatanānaṃ adhivacanaṃ; visadausauti khau, sunakkhatta, avij‍jāyaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ; sal‍lanti khau, sunakkhatta, taṇhāyaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ; aisanīti khau, sunakkhatta, satiyāyaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ; satthanti khau, sunakkhatta, ariyāyaitaṃ pañ‍ñāya  adhivacanaṃ; bhisak‍kau sal‍lakattauti khau, sunakkhatta, tathāgatassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa|

‘‘sau vata, sunakkhatta, bhikkhu chasu phassāyatanaisu saṃvutakārī ‘upadhi dukkhassa mūla’nti – iti viditvā nirupadhi upadhisaṅkhayai vimuttau upadhismiṃ vā kāyaṃ upasaṃharissati cittaṃ vā uppādaissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| saiyyathāpi, sunakkhatta, āpānīyakaṃsau vaṇṇasampan‍nau gandhasampan‍nau rasasampan‍nau; sau ca khau visaina saṃsaṭṭhau| atha purisau āgacchaiyya jīvitukāmau amaritukāmau sukhakāmau dukkhapaṭikūlau| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sunakkhatta, api nu sau purisau amuṃ āpānīyakaṃsaṃ  pivaiyya yaṃ jañ‍ñā – ‘imāhaṃ pivitvā maraṇaṃ vā nigacchāmi maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkha’’’nti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivamaiva  khau, sunakkhatta, sau vata bhikkhu chasu phassāyatanaisu saṃvutakārī ‘upadhi dukkhassa mūla’nti – iti viditvā nirupadhi upadhisaṅkhayai vimuttau  upadhismiṃ vā kāyaṃ upasaṃharissati cittaṃ vā uppādaissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| saiyyathāpi, sunakkhatta, āsīvisau āsivisau (ka॰) ghauravisau| atha purisau āgacchaiyya jīvitukāmau amaritukāmau sukhakāmau dukkhapaṭikūlau| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sunakkhatta, api nu sau purisau amussa āsīvisassa ghauravisassa hatthaṃ vā aṅguṭṭhaṃ vā daj‍jā yuñ‍jaiyya (ka॰) yaṃ jañ‍ñā – ‘imināhaṃ daṭṭhau maraṇaṃ vā nigacchāmi maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkha’’’nti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, sunakkhatta, sau vata bhikkhu chasu phassāyatanaisu saṃvutakārī ‘upadhi dukkhassa mūla’nti – iti viditvā nirupadhi upadhisaṅkhayai vimuttau upadhismiṃ vā kāyaṃ upasaṃharissati cittaṃ vā uppādaissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau sunakkhattau licchaviputtau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

sunakkhattasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. ānaiñ‍jasappāyasuttaṃ

66. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kurūsu viharati kammāsadhammaṃ nāma kurūnaṃ nigamau| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘anic‍cā, bhikkhavai, kāmā tucchā musā mausadhammā| māyākatamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bālalāpanaṃ| yai ca diṭṭhadhammikā kāmā, yai ca samparāyikā kāmā; yā ca diṭṭhadhammikā  kāmasañ‍ñā, yā ca samparāyikā kāmasañ‍ñā – ubhayamaitaṃ māradhaiyyaṃ, mārassaisa mārassaiva (ka॰) visayau, mārassaisa nivāpau, mārassaisa gaucarau| aitthaitai pāpakā akusalā mānasā abhijjhāpi byāpādāpi sārambhāpi saṃvattanti| taiva ariyasāvakassa idhamanusikkhatau antarāyāya sambhavanti| tatra, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yai ca diṭṭhadhammikā kāmā, yai ca samparāyikā kāmā; yā ca diṭṭhadhammikā kāmasañ‍ñā, yā ca samparāyikā kāmasañ‍ñā – ubhayamaitaṃ māradhaiyyaṃ, mārassaisa visayau, mārassaisa nivāpau, mārassaisa gaucarau| aitthaitai  pāpakā akusalā mānasā abhijjhāpi byāpādāpi sārambhāpi saṃvattanti, taiva ariyasāvakassa idhamanusikkhatau antarāyāya sambhavanti| yaṃnūnāhaṃ vipulaina mahaggataina caitasā viharaiyyaṃ abhibhuyya laukaṃ adhiṭṭhāya manasā| vipulaina hi mai mahaggataina caitasā viharatau abhibhuyya laukaṃ adhiṭṭhāya manasā  yai pāpakā akusalā mānasā abhijjhāpi byāpādāpi sārambhāpi tai na bhavissanti| taisaṃ pahānā aparittañ‍ca mai cittaṃ bhavissati appamāṇaṃ subhāvita’nti| tassa aivaṃpaṭipan‍nassa tabbahulavihārinau āyatanai cittaṃ pasīdati| sampasādai sati aitarahi vā ānaiñ‍jaṃ samāpaj‍jati pañ‍ñāya vā adhimuc‍cati kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā| ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ taṃsaṃvattanikaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ assa ānaiñ‍jūpagaṃ| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamā ānaiñ‍jasappāyā paṭipadā akkhāyati’’|

67. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yai ca diṭṭhadhammikā kāmā, yai ca samparāyikā kāmā; yā ca diṭṭhadhammikā kāmasañ‍ñā, yā ca samparāyikā kāmasañ‍ñā; yaṃ kiñ‍ci rūpaṃ (sabbaṃ rūpaṃ) ( ) natthi sī॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu cattāri ca mahābhūtāni, catun‍nañ‍ca mahābhūtānaṃ upādāyarūpa’nti| tassa aivaṃpaṭipan‍nassa tabbahulavihārinau āyatanai cittaṃ pasīdati| sampasādai sati aitarahi vā ānaiñ‍jaṃ samāpaj‍jati pañ‍ñāya vā adhimuc‍cati kāyassa  bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā| ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ taṃsaṃvattanikaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ assa ānaiñ‍jūpagaṃ| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyā ānaiñ‍jasappāyā paṭipadā akkhāyati|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yai ca diṭṭhadhammikā kāmā, yai ca samparāyikā kāmā; yā ca diṭṭhadhammikā kāmasañ‍ñā, yā ca samparāyikā kāmasañ‍ñā; yai ca diṭṭhadhammikā rūpā, yai ca samparāyikā rūpā; yā ca diṭṭhadhammikā rūpasañ‍ñā, yā ca samparāyikā rūpasañ‍ñā – ubhayamaitaṃ anic‍caṃ| yadanic‍caṃ taṃ nālaṃ abhinandituṃ, nālaṃ abhivadituṃ, nālaṃ ajjhausitu’nti| tassa aivaṃpaṭipan‍nassa tabbahulavihārinau āyatanai cittaṃ pasīdati| sampasādai sati aitarahi vā ānaiñ‍jaṃ samāpaj‍jati pañ‍ñāya vā adhimuc‍cati kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā| ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ taṃsaṃvattanikaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ assa ānaiñ‍jūpagaṃ| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyā ānaiñ‍jasappāyā paṭipadā akkhāyati|

68. ‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yai ca diṭṭhadhammikā kāmā, yai ca samparāyikā kāmā; yā ca diṭṭhadhammikā kāmasañ‍ñā, yā ca samparāyikā kāmasañ‍ñā; yai ca diṭṭhadhammikā rūpā, yai ca samparāyikā rūpā; yā ca diṭṭhadhammikā rūpasañ‍ñā, yā ca samparāyikā rūpasañ‍ñā; yā ca ānaiñ‍jasañ‍ñā – sabbā sañ‍ñā| yatthaitā aparisaisā nirujjhanti aitaṃ santaṃ aitaṃ paṇītaṃ – yadidaṃ ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatana’nti| tassa aivaṃpaṭipan‍nassa tabbahulavihārinau āyatanai cittaṃ pasīdati| sampasādai sati aitarahi vā ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samāpaj‍jati pañ‍ñāya vā adhimuc‍cati kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā| ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ taṃsaṃvattanikaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ assa ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanūpagaṃ| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamā ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasappāyā  paṭipadā akkhāyati|

69. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘suñ‍ñamidaṃ attaina vā attaniyaina vā’ti| tassa aivaṃpaṭipan‍nassa tabbahulavihārinau āyatanai cittaṃ pasīdati| sampasādai sati aitarahi vā ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samāpaj‍jati pañ‍ñāya vā adhimuc‍cati kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā| ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ taṃsaṃvattanikaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ assa ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanūpagaṃ| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyā ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasappāyā paṭipadā akkhāyati|

70. ‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘nāhaṃ k‍vacani k‍vacini (syā॰ kaṃ॰ sī॰ aṭṭha॰) kassaci kiñ‍canatasmiṃ kiñ‍canatasmi (?), na ca mama  k‍vacani kismiñ‍ci kiñ‍canaṃ natthī’ti| tassa aivaṃpaṭipan‍nassa tabbahulavihārinau āyatanai cittaṃ pasīdati| sampasādai sati aitarahi vā ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samāpaj‍jati pañ‍ñāya vā adhimuc‍cati kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā| ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ taṃsaṃvattanikaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ assa ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanūpagaṃ| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyā ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasappāyā paṭipadā akkhāyati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yai ca diṭṭhadhammikā kāmā, yai ca samparāyikā kāmā; yā ca diṭṭhadhammikā kāmasañ‍ñā, yā ca samparāyikā kāmasañ‍ñā; yai ca diṭṭhadhammikā rūpā, yai ca samparāyikā rūpā; yā ca diṭṭhadhammikā rūpasañ‍ñā, yā  ca samparāyikā rūpasañ‍ñā; yā ca ānaiñ‍jasañ‍ñā, yā ca ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñā – sabbā sañ‍ñā| yatthaitā aparisaisā nirujjhanti aitaṃ santaṃ aitaṃ paṇītaṃ – yadidaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatana’nti| tassa aivaṃpaṭipan‍nassa tabbahulavihārinau āyatanai cittaṃ pasīdati| sampasādai sati aitarahi vā naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samāpaj‍jati pañ‍ñāya vā adhimuc‍cati kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā| ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ taṃsaṃvattanikaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ assa naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanūpagaṃ| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasappāyā paṭipadā akkhāyatī’’ti|

71. aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idha, bhantai, bhikkhu aivaṃ paṭipan‍nau hauti – ‘nau cassa, nau ca mai siyā; na bhavissati, na mai bhavissati; yadatthi yaṃ, bhūtaṃ – taṃ pajahāmī’ti| aivaṃ upaikkhaṃ paṭilabhati| parinibbāyaiyya nu khau sau, bhantai, bhikkhu na vā parinibbāyaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘apaitthaikac‍cau, ānanda, bhikkhu parinibbāyaiyya, apaitthaikac‍cau bhikkhu na parinibbāyaiyyā’’ti| ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu kau pac‍cayau yainapaitthaikac‍cau bhikkhu parinibbāyaiyya, apaitthaikac‍cau bhikkhu na parinibbāyaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘idhānanda, bhikkhu aivaṃ paṭipan‍nau hauti – ‘nau cassa, nau ca mai siyā; na bhavissati, na mai bhavissati; yadatthi, yaṃ  bhūtaṃ – taṃ pajahāmī’ti| aivaṃ upaikkhaṃ paṭilabhati| sau taṃ upaikkhaṃ abhinandati, abhivadati, ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa  taṃ upaikkhaṃ abhinandatau abhivadatau ajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau tan‍nissitaṃ hauti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ tadupādānaṃ| saupādānau, ānanda, bhikkhu  na parinibbāyatī’’ti| ‘‘kahaṃ pana sau, bhantai, bhikkhu upādiyamānau upādiyatī’’ti? ‘‘naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ, ānandā’’ti| ‘‘upādānasaiṭṭhaṃ kira sau, bhantai, bhikkhu upādiyamānau upādiyatī’’ti? ‘‘upādānasaiṭṭhañhi sau, ānanda, bhikkhu upādiyamānau upādiyati| upādānasaiṭṭhañhaitaṃ, ānanda, yadidaṃ – naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ’’|

72. ‘‘idhānanda, bhikkhu aivaṃ paṭipan‍nau hauti – ‘nau cassa, nau ca mai siyā; na bhavissati, na mai bhavissati; yadatthi, yaṃ bhūtaṃ – taṃ pajahāmī’ti| aivaṃ upaikkhaṃ paṭilabhati| sau taṃ upaikkhaṃ nābhinandati, nābhivadati, na ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa taṃ upaikkhaṃ anabhinandatau anabhivadatau anajjhausāya  tiṭṭhatau na tan‍nissitaṃ hauti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ na tadupādānaṃ| anupādānau, ānanda, bhikkhu parinibbāyatī’’ti|

73. ‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai! nissāya nissāya kira nau, bhantai, bhagavatā aughassa nittharaṇā akkhātā| katamau pana, bhantai, ariyau vimaukkhau’’ti? ‘‘idhānanda, bhikkhu ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yai ca diṭṭhadhammikā kāmā, yai ca samparāyikā kāmā; yā ca diṭṭhadhammikā kāmasañ‍ñā, yā ca samparāyikā kāmasañ‍ñā; yai ca diṭṭhadhammikā rūpā, yai ca samparāyikā rūpā; yā ca diṭṭhadhammikā rūpasañ‍ñā, yā ca samparāyikā rūpasañ‍ñā; yā ca ānaiñ‍jasañ‍ñā, yā ca ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñā, yā ca naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñā – aisa sak‍kāyau yāvatā sak‍kāyau| aitaṃ amataṃ yadidaṃ anupādā cittassa vimaukkhau| iti, khau, ānanda, daisitā mayā ānaiñ‍jasappāyā paṭipadā, daisitā ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasappāyā paṭipadā, daisitā naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasappāyā paṭipadā, daisitā nissāya nissāya aughassa nittharaṇā, daisitau ariyau vimaukkhau| yaṃ khau, ānanda, satthārā karaṇīyaṃ sāvakānaṃ hitaisinā anukampakaina anukampaṃ upādāya, kataṃ  vau taṃ mayā| aitāni, ānanda, rukkhamūlāni, aitāni suñ‍ñāgārāni| jhāyathānanda, mā pamādattha, mā pacchā vippaṭisārinau ahuvattha| ayaṃ vau amhākaṃ anusāsanī’’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

ānaiñ‍jasappāyasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. gaṇakamauggal‍lānasuttaṃ

74. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati pubbārāmai migāramātupāsādai| atha khau gaṇakamauggal‍lānau gaṇakamauggalānau (ka॰) brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ  sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau gaṇakamauggal‍lānau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhau gautama, imassa migāramātupāsādassa dissati anupubbasikkhā anupubbakiriyā anupubbapaṭipadā yadidaṃ – yāva pacchimasaupānakaḷaivarā imaisampi hi, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇānaṃ dissati anupubbasikkhā anupubbakiriyā anupubbapaṭipadā yadidaṃ – ajjhainai imaisampi hi, bhau gautama, issāsānaṃ dissati anupubbasikkhā anupubbakiriyā anupubbapaṭipadā yadidaṃ – issatthai issattai (ka॰)| amhākampi hi, bhau gautama, gaṇakānaṃ gaṇanājīvānaṃ dissati anupubbasikkhā anupubbakiriyā anupubbapaṭipadā yadidaṃ – saṅkhānai| mayañhi, bhau gautama, antaivāsiṃ labhitvā paṭhamaṃ aivaṃ gaṇāpaima – ‘aikaṃ aikakaṃ, dvai dukā, tīṇi tikā, cattāri catuk‍kā, pañ‍ca pañ‍cakā, cha chak‍kā, satta sattakā, aṭṭha aṭṭhakā, nava navakā, dasa dasakā’ti; satampi mayaṃ, bhau gautama, gaṇāpaima, bhiyyaupi gaṇāpaima| sak‍kā nu khau, bhau gautama, imasmimpi dhammavinayai aivamaiva anupubbasikkhā anupubbakiriyā anupubbapaṭipadā pañ‍ñapaitu’’nti?

75. ‘‘sak‍kā, brāhmaṇa, imasmimpi dhammavinayai anupubbasikkhā anupubbakiriyā anupubbapaṭipadā pañ‍ñapaituṃ| saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, dakkhau assadammakau bhaddaṃ assājānīyaṃ labhitvā paṭhamainaiva mukhādhānai kāraṇaṃ kāraiti, atha uttariṃ kāraṇaṃ kāraiti; aivamaiva khau, brāhmaṇa, tathāgatau purisadammaṃ labhitvā paṭhamaṃ aivaṃ vinaiti – ‘aihi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, sīlavā hauhi, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharāhi ācāragaucarasampan‍nau aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhassu sikkhāpadaisū’’’ti|

‘‘yatau  khau, brāhmaṇa, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu, tamainaṃ tathāgatau uttariṃ vinaiti – ‘aihi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, indriyaisu guttadvārau hauhi, cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā mā nimittaggāhī hauhi mānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jāhi; rakkhāhi cakkhundriyaṃ, cakkhundriyai saṃvaraṃ  āpaj‍jāhi| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā…pai॰… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā…pai॰… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā…pai॰… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya mā nimittaggāhī hauhi mānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ manindriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jāhi; rakkhāhi manindriyaṃ, manindriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jāhī’’’ti|

‘‘yatau  khau, brāhmaṇa, bhikkhu indriyaisu guttadvārau hauti, tamainaṃ tathāgatau uttariṃ vinaiti – ‘aihi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhaujanai mattañ‍ñū hauhi| paṭisaṅkhā yaunisau āhāraṃ āhāraiyyāsi – naiva davāya na madāya na maṇḍanāya na vibhūsanāya, yāvadaiva imassa kāyassa ṭhitiyā yāpanāya vihiṃsūparatiyā brahmacariyānuggahāya – iti purāṇañ‍ca vaidanaṃ paṭihaṅkhāmi, navañ‍ca vaidanaṃ na uppādaissāmi, yātrā ca mai bhavissati anavaj‍jatā ca phāsuvihārau cā’’’ti|

‘‘yatau khau, brāhmaṇa, bhikkhu bhaujanai mattañ‍ñū hauti, tamainaṃ tathāgatau uttariṃ vinaiti – ‘aihi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, jāgariyaṃ anuyuttau viharāhi, divasaṃ caṅkamaina nisaj‍jāya āvaraṇīyaihi dhammaihi cittaṃ parisaudhaihi, rattiyā paṭhamaṃ yāmaṃ caṅkamaina nisaj‍jāya āvaraṇīyaihi dhammaihi cittaṃ parisaudhaihi, rattiyā majjhimaṃ yāmaṃ dakkhiṇaina passaina sīhasaiyyaṃ kappaiyyāsi pādai pādaṃ ac‍cādhāya satau sampajānau uṭṭhānasañ‍ñaṃ manasikaritvā, rattiyā pacchimaṃ yāmaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya caṅkamaina nisaj‍jāya āvaraṇīyaihi dhammaihi cittaṃ parisaudhaihī’’’ti|

‘‘yatau khau, brāhmaṇa, bhikkhu jāgariyaṃ anuyuttau hauti, tamainaṃ tathāgatau uttariṃ vinaiti – ‘aihi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, satisampajañ‍ñaina saman‍nāgatau hauhi, abhik‍kantai paṭik‍kantai sampajānakārī, ālaukitai vilaukitai sampajānakārī, samiñ‍jitai pasāritai sampajānakārī, saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇai sampajānakārī, asitai pītai khāyitai sāyitai sampajānakārī, uc‍cārapassāvakammai sampajānakārī, gatai ṭhitai nisin‍nai suttai jāgaritai bhāsitai tuṇhībhāvai sampajānakārī’’’ti|

‘‘yatau khau, brāhmaṇa, bhikkhu satisampajañ‍ñaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, tamainaṃ tathāgatau uttariṃ vinaiti – ‘aihi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, vivittaṃ saināsanaṃ bhajāhi  arañ‍ñaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ pabbataṃ kandaraṃ giriguhaṃ susānaṃ vanapatthaṃ abbhaukāsaṃ palālapuñ‍ja’nti| sau vivittaṃ saināsanaṃ bhajati arañ‍ñaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ pabbataṃ kandaraṃ giriguhaṃ susānaṃ vanappatthaṃ abbhaukāsaṃ palālapuñ‍jaṃ| sau pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā, ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya, parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| sau abhijjhaṃ laukai pahāya vigatābhijjhaina caitasā viharati, abhijjhāya cittaṃ parisaudhaiti; byāpādapadausaṃ pahāya abyāpan‍nacittau viharati sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī, byāpādapadausā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti; thinamiddhaṃ thīnamiddhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pahāya vigatathinamiddhau viharati ālaukasañ‍ñī satau sampajānau, thinamiddhā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti; uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ pahāya anuddhatau viharati ajjhattaṃ vūpasantacittau, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti; vicikicchaṃ pahāya tiṇṇavicikicchau viharati akathaṃkathī kusalaisu dhammaisu, vicikicchāya cittaṃ parisaudhaiti|

76. ‘‘sau  imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| vitak‍kavicārānaṃ  vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| pītiyā ca virāgā… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sukhassa ca pahānā… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati|

‘‘yai khau tai, brāhmaṇa, bhikkhū saikkhā saikhā (sabbattha) apattamānasā anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ patthayamānā viharanti  taisu mai ayaṃ aivarūpī anusāsanī hauti| yai pana tai bhikkhū arahantau khīṇāsavā vusitavantau katakaraṇīyā auhitabhārā anuppattasadatthā parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanā sammadañ‍ñā vimuttā taisaṃ imai dhammā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārāya caiva saṃvattanti, satisampajañ‍ñāya cā’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, gaṇakamauggal‍lānau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau bhautau gautamassa sāvakā bhautā gautamaina aivaṃ auvadīyamānā aivaṃ anusāsīyamānā sabbai ac‍cantaṃ niṭṭhaṃ nibbānaṃ ārādhainti udāhu aikac‍cai nārādhaintī’’ti? ‘‘appaikac‍cai khau, brāhmaṇa, mama sāvakā mayā  aivaṃ auvadīyamānā aivaṃ anusāsīyamānā ac‍cantaṃ niṭṭhaṃ nibbānaṃ ārādhainti, aikac‍cai nārādhaintī’’ti|

‘‘kau nu khau, bhau gautama, haitu kau pac‍cayau yaṃ tiṭṭhataiva nibbānaṃ, tiṭṭhati nibbānagāmī maggau, tiṭṭhati bhavaṃ gautamau samādapaitā; atha ca pana bhautau gautamassa sāvakā bhautā gautamaina aivaṃ auvadīyamānā aivaṃ anusāsīyamānā appaikac‍cai ac‍cantaṃ niṭṭhaṃ nibbānaṃ ārādhainti, aikac‍cai nārādhaintī’’ti?

77. ‘‘taina  hi, brāhmaṇa, taṃyaivaittha paṭipucchissāmi| yathā tai khamaiyya tathā naṃ byākaraiyyāsi| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, brāhmaṇa, kusalau tvaṃ rājagahagāmissa maggassā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau, kusalau ahaṃ rājagahagāmissa maggassā’’ti| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, brāhmaṇa, idha purisau āgacchaiyya rājagahaṃ gantukāmau| sau taṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘icchāmahaṃ, bhantai, rājagahaṃ gantuṃ; tassa mai rājagahassa maggaṃ upadisā’ti| tamainaṃ tvaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyāsi – ‘aihambhau aivaṃ bhau (sī॰ pī॰) purisa, ayaṃ maggau rājagahaṃ gacchati| taina muhuttaṃ gaccha, taina muhuttaṃ gantvā dakkhissasi amukaṃ nāma gāmaṃ, taina muhuttaṃ gaccha, taina muhuttaṃ gantvā dakkhissasi amukaṃ nāma nigamaṃ; taina muhuttaṃ gaccha, taina muhuttaṃ gantvā dakkhissasi rājagahassa ārāmarāmaṇaiyyakaṃ vanarāmaṇaiyyakaṃ bhūmirāmaṇaiyyakaṃ paukkharaṇīrāmaṇaiyyaka’nti| sau tayā aivaṃ auvadīyamānau aivaṃ anusāsīyamānau ummaggaṃ gahaitvā pacchāmukhau gacchaiyya| atha dutiyau purisau āgacchaiyya rājagahaṃ gantukāmau| sau taṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘icchāmahaṃ, bhantai, rājagahaṃ gantuṃ; tassa mai rājagahassa maggaṃ upadisā’ti| tamainaṃ tvaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyāsi – ‘aihambhau purisa, ayaṃ maggau rājagahaṃ gacchati| taina muhuttaṃ gaccha, taina muhuttaṃ gantvā dakkhissasi  amukaṃ nāma gāmaṃ; taina muhuttaṃ gaccha, taina muhuttaṃ gantvā dakkhissasi amukaṃ nāma nigamaṃ; taina muhuttaṃ gaccha, taina muhuttaṃ gantvā dakkhissasi rājagahassa ārāmarāmaṇaiyyakaṃ  vanarāmaṇaiyyakaṃ bhūmirāmaṇaiyyakaṃ paukkharaṇīrāmaṇaiyyaka’nti| sau tayā aivaṃ auvadīyamānau aivaṃ anusāsīyamānau sautthinā rājagahaṃ gacchaiyya| kau nu khau, brāhmaṇa, haitu kau pac‍cayau yaṃ tiṭṭhataiva rājagahaṃ, tiṭṭhati rājagahagāmī maggau, tiṭṭhasi tvaṃ samādapaitā; atha ca pana tayā aivaṃ auvadīyamānau aivaṃ anusāsīyamānau aikau purisau ummaggaṃ gahaitvā pacchāmukhau gacchaiyya, aikau sautthinā rājagahaṃ gacchaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘aittha  kyāhaṃ, bhau gautama, karaumi? maggakkhāyīhaṃ, bhau gautamā’’ti|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, brāhmaṇa, tiṭṭhataiva nibbānaṃ, tiṭṭhati nibbānagāmī maggau, tiṭṭhāmahaṃ samādapaitā; atha ca pana mama sāvakā mayā aivaṃ auvadīyamānā aivaṃ anusāsīyamānā appaikac‍cai ac‍cantaṃ niṭṭhaṃ nibbānaṃ ārādhainti, aikac‍cai nārādhainti| aittha kyāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, karaumi? maggakkhāyīhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, tathāgatau’’ti|

78. aivaṃ vuttai, gaṇakamauggal‍lānau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yaimai, bhau gautama, puggalā assaddhā jīvikatthā na saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā saṭhā māyāvinau kaitabinau kaiṭubhinau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) uddhatā un‍naḷā capalā mukharā vikiṇṇavācā indriyaisu aguttadvārā bhaujanai amattañ‍ñunau jāgariyaṃ ananuyuttā sāmañ‍ñai anapaikkhavantau sikkhāya na tibbagāravā bāhulikā bāhul‍likā (syā॰ kaṃ॰) sāthalikā auk‍kamanai pubbaṅgamā pavivaikai nikkhittadhurā kusītā hīnavīriyā muṭṭhassatinau asampajānā asamāhitā vibbhantacittā  duppañ‍ñā aiḷamūgā, na taihi bhavaṃ gautamau saddhiṃ saṃvasati’’|

‘‘yai pana tai kulaputtā saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā asaṭhā amāyāvinau akaitabinau anuddhatā anun‍naḷā acapalā amukharā avikiṇṇavācā indriyaisu guttadvārā bhaujanai mattañ‍ñunau jāgariyaṃ anuyuttā sāmañ‍ñai apaikkhavantau sikkhāya tibbagāravā nabāhulikā nasāthalikā auk‍kamanai nikkhittadhurā pavivaikai pubbaṅgamā āraddhavīriyā pahitattā upaṭṭhitassatinau sampajānā samāhitā aikaggacittā pañ‍ñavantau anaiḷamūgā, taihi bhavaṃ gautamau saddhiṃ saṃvasati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhau gautama, yai kaici mūlagandhā, kālānusāri taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati; yai kaici sāragandhā, lauhitacandanaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati; yai kaici pupphagandhā, vassikaṃ  taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati; aivamaiva bhautau gautamassa auvādau paramaj‍jadhammaisu|

‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama! saiyyathāpi, bhau gautama, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya – ‘cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantī’ti; aivamaivaṃ bhautā gautamaina anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

gaṇakamauggal‍lānasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. gaupakamauggal‍lānasuttaṃ

79. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā ānandau rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai aciraparinibbutai bhagavati| taina khau pana samayaina rājā māgadhau ajātasattu vaidaihiputtau rājagahaṃ paṭisaṅkhārāpaiti rañ‍ñau paj‍jautassa āsaṅkamānau| atha khau āyasmā ānandau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya rājagahaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| atha khau āyasmatau ānandassa aitadahausi – ‘‘atippagau khau tāva rājagahai piṇḍāya carituṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaina gaupakamauggal‍lānassa brāhmaṇassa kammantau, yaina gaupakamauggal‍lānau brāhmaṇau tainupasaṅkamaiyya’’nti|

atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina gaupakamauggal‍lānassa brāhmaṇassa kammantau, yaina gaupakamauggal‍lānau brāhmaṇau tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau gaupakamauggal‍lānau brāhmaṇau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitu khau bhavaṃ ānandau| svāgataṃ bhautau ānandassa| cirassaṃ khau bhavaṃ ānandau imaṃ pariyāyamakāsi yadidaṃ idhāgamanāya| nisīdatu bhavaṃ ānandau, idamāsanaṃ pañ‍ñatta’’nti| nisīdi khau āyasmā ānandau pañ‍ñattai āsanai| gaupakamauggal‍lānaupi  khau brāhmaṇau añ‍ñataraṃ nīcaṃ āsanaṃ gahaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nau khau gaupakamauggal‍lānau brāhmaṇau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘atthi nu khau, bhau ānanda, aikabhikkhupi taihi  dhammaihi sabbainasabbaṃ sabbathāsabbaṃ saman‍nāgatau yaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau sau bhavaṃ gautamau ahausi arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau’’ti? ‘‘natthi khau, brāhmaṇa, aikabhikkhupi taihi dhammaihi sabbainasabbaṃ sabbathāsabbaṃ saman‍nāgatau yaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau sau bhagavā ahausi arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau| sau hi, brāhmaṇa, bhagavā anuppan‍nassa maggassa uppādaitā, asañ‍jātassa maggassa sañ‍janaitā, anakkhātassa maggassa akkhātā, maggañ‍ñū, maggavidū, maggakauvidau; maggānugā ca pana aitarahi sāvakā viharanti pacchā saman‍nāgatā’’ti| ayañ‍ca hidaṃ āyasmatau ānandassa gaupakamauggal‍lānaina brāhmaṇaina saddhiṃ antarākathā vippakatā ahausi|

atha  khau vassakārau brāhmaṇau magadhamahāmattau rājagahai kammantai anusañ‍ñāyamānau yaina gaupakamauggal‍lānassa brāhmaṇassa kammantau, yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā ānandaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau vassakārau brāhmaṇau magadhamahāmattau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kāyanuttha, bhau ānanda, aitarahi kathāya san‍nisin‍nā, kā ca pana vau antarākathā vippakatā’’ti? ‘‘idha maṃ, brāhmaṇa, gaupakamauggal‍lānau  brāhmaṇau aivamāha – ‘atthi nu khau, bhau ānanda, aikabhikkhupi taihi dhammaihi sabbainasabbaṃ sabbathāsabbaṃ saman‍nāgatau yaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau sau bhavaṃ gautamau ahausi arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau’ti| aivaṃ vuttai ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, gaupakamauggal‍lānaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘natthi khau, brāhmaṇa, aikabhikkhupi taihi dhammaihi sabbainasabbaṃ sabbathāsabbaṃ saman‍nāgatau yaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau sau bhagavā ahausi arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau| sau hi, brāhmaṇa, bhagavā anuppan‍nassa maggassa uppādaitā, asañ‍jātassa  maggassa sañ‍janaitā, anakkhātassa maggassa akkhātā, maggañ‍ñū, maggavidū, maggakauvidau; maggānugā ca pana aitarahi sāvakā viharanti pacchā saman‍nāgatā’ti| ayaṃ khau nau, brāhmaṇa, gaupakamauggal‍lānaina brāhmaṇaina saddhiṃ antarākathā vippakatā| atha tvaṃ anuppattau’’ti|

80. ‘‘atthi nu khau, bhau ānanda, aikabhikkhupi taina bhautā gautamaina ṭhapitau – ‘ayaṃ vau mamac‍cayaina paṭisaraṇaṃ bhavissatī’ti, yaṃ tumhai aitarahi  paṭipādaiyyāthā’’ti paṭidhāvaiyyāthāti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)? ‘‘natthi khau, brāhmaṇa, aikabhikkhupi taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina ṭhapitau – ‘ayaṃ vau mamac‍cayaina paṭisaraṇaṃ bhavissatī’ti, yaṃ mayaṃ aitarahi paṭipādaiyyāmā’’ti| ‘‘atthi pana, bhau ānanda, aikabhikkhupi saṅghaina sammatau, sambahulaihi  thairaihi bhikkhūhi ṭhapitau – ‘ayaṃ nau bhagavatau ac‍cayaina paṭisaraṇaṃ bhavissatī’ti, yaṃ tumhai aitarahi paṭipādaiyyāthā’’ti? ‘‘natthi khau, brāhmaṇa, aikabhikkhupi saṅghaina sammatau, sambahulaihi thairaihi bhikkhūhi ṭhapitau – ‘ayaṃ nau bhagavatau ac‍cayaina paṭisaraṇaṃ bhavissatī’ti, yaṃ mayaṃ aitarahi paṭipādaiyyāmā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ appaṭisaraṇai ca pana, bhau ānanda, kau haitu sāmaggiyā’’ti? ‘‘na khau mayaṃ, brāhmaṇa, appaṭisaraṇā; sappaṭisaraṇā mayaṃ, brāhmaṇa; dhammappaṭisaraṇā’’ti|

‘‘‘atthi  nu khau, bhau ānanda, aikabhikkhupi taina bhautā gautamaina ṭhapitau – ayaṃ vau mamac‍cayaina paṭisaraṇaṃ bhavissatīti, yaṃ tumhai aitarahi paṭipādaiyyāthā’ti – iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘natthi khau, brāhmaṇa, aikabhikkhupi taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina ṭhapitau – ayaṃ vau mamac‍cayaina paṭisaraṇaṃ bhavissatīti, yaṃ mayaṃ aitarahi paṭipādaiyyāmā’ti vadaisi; ‘atthi pana, bhau ānanda, aikabhikkhupi saṅghaina sammatau, sambahulaihi thairaihi bhikkhūhi ṭhapitau – ayaṃ nau bhagavatau ac‍cayaina paṭisaraṇaṃ bhavissatīti, yaṃ tumhai aitarahi paṭipādaiyyāthā’ti – iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘natthi khau, brāhmaṇa, aikabhikkhupi saṅghaina sammatau, sambahulaihi thairaihi bhikkhūhi  ṭhapitau – ayaṃ nau bhagavatau ac‍cayaina paṭisaraṇaṃ bhavissatīti, yaṃ mayaṃ aitarahi paṭipādaiyyāmā’ti – vadaisi; ‘aivaṃ appaṭisaraṇai ca pana, bhau ānanda, kau haitu sāmaggiyā’ti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘na khau mayaṃ, brāhmaṇa, appaṭisaraṇā; sappaṭisaraṇā mayaṃ, brāhmaṇa; dhammappaṭisaraṇā’ti vadaisi| imassa pana, bhau ānanda, bhāsitassa kathaṃ atthau daṭṭhabbau’’ti?

81. ‘‘atthi khau, brāhmaṇa, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ, pātimaukkhaṃ uddiṭṭhaṃ| tai mayaṃ tadahupausathai yāvatikā aikaṃ gāmakhaittaṃ upanissāya viharāma tai sabbai  aikajjhaṃ san‍nipatāma; san‍nipatitvā yassa taṃ pavattati taṃ ajjhaisāma| tasmiṃ cai bhañ‍ñamānai hauti bhikkhussa āpatti hauti vītik‍kamau taṃ mayaṃ yathādhammaṃ yathānusiṭṭhaṃ kāraimāti|

‘‘na kira nau bhavantau kārainti; dhammau nau kāraiti’’| ‘‘atthi nu khau, bhau ānanda, aikabhikkhupi yaṃ tumhai aitarahi sak‍karautha garuṃ karautha garukarautha (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) mānaitha pūjaitha; sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā garukatvā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) upanissāya viharathā’’ti? ‘‘natthi khau, brāhmaṇa, aikabhikkhupi yaṃ mayaṃ aitarahi sak‍karauma garuṃ karauma mānaima pūjaima; sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharāmā’’ti|

‘‘‘atthi nu khau, bhau ānanda, aikabhikkhupi taina bhautā gautamaina ṭhapitau – ayaṃ vau mamac‍cayaina paṭisaraṇaṃ bhavissatīti yaṃ tumhai aitarahi paṭipādaiyyāthā’ti – iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘natthi khau, brāhmaṇa, aikabhikkhupi taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina ṭhapitau – ayaṃ vau mamac‍cayaina paṭisaraṇaṃ bhavissatīti yaṃ mayaṃ aitarahi paṭipādaiyyāmā’ti  vadaisi; ‘atthi pana, bhau ānanda, aikabhikkhupi saṅghaina  sammatau, sambahulaihi thairaihi bhikkhūhi ṭhapitau – ayaṃ nau bhagavatau ac‍cayaina paṭisaraṇaṃ bhavissatīti yaṃ tumhai aitarahi paṭipādaiyyāthā’ti  – iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘natthi khau, brāhmaṇa, aikabhikkhupi saṅghaina sammatau, sambahulaihi thairaihi bhikkhūhi ṭhapitau – ayaṃ nau bhagavatau ac‍cayaina paṭisaraṇaṃ bhavissatīti yaṃ mayaṃ aitarahi paṭipādaiyyāmā’ti vadaisi; ‘atthi nu khau, bhau ānanda, aikabhikkhupi yaṃ tumhai aitarahi sak‍karautha garuṃ karautha mānaitha pūjaitha; sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharathā’ti – iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘natthi khau, brāhmaṇa, aikabhikkhupi yaṃ mayaṃ aitarahi sak‍karauma garuṃ karauma mānaima pūjaima; sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharāmā’ti vadaisi| imassa pana, bhau ānanda, bhāsitassa kathaṃ atthau daṭṭhabbau’’ti?

82. ‘‘atthi khau, brāhmaṇa, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina dasa pasādanīyā dhammā akkhātā| yasmiṃ nau imai dhammā saṃvij‍janti taṃ mayaṃ aitarahi sak‍karauma garuṃ karauma mānaima pūjaima; sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharāma| katamai dasa?

‘‘idha, brāhmaṇa, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau, aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu|

‘‘bahussutau hauti sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau| yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā, majjhaikalyāṇā, pariyausānakalyāṇā, sātthaṃ, sabyañ‍janaṃ sātthā sabyañ‍janā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti tathārūpāssa  dhammā bahussutā haunti dhātā dhatā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā|

‘‘santuṭṭhau hauti ( ) (itarītaraihi) dī॰ ni॰ 3.345 cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraihi|

‘‘catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī|

‘‘anaikavihitaṃ  iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauti – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā hauti, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau hauti; āvibhāvaṃ tiraubhāvaṃ; tiraukuṭṭaṃ tiraukuḍḍaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) tiraupākāraṃ tiraupabbataṃ asaj‍jamānau gacchati, saiyyathāpi ākāsai; pathaviyāpi ummuj‍janimuj‍jaṃ karauti, saiyyathāpi udakai; udakaipi abhij‍jamānai gacchati, saiyyathāpi pathaviyaṃ; ākāsaipi pal‍laṅkaina  kamati, saiyyathāpi pakkhī sakuṇau; imaipi candimasūriyai aivaṃmahiddhikai aivaṃmahānubhāvai pāṇinā parimasati parāmasati (ka॰) parimaj‍jati, yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiti|

‘‘dibbāya sautadhātuyā visuddhāya atik‍kantamānusikāya ubhau saddai suṇāti – dibbai ca mānusai ca, yai dūrai santikai ca|

‘‘parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāti| sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘sarāgaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, vītarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘vītarāgaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, sadausaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘sadausaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, vītadausaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘vītadausaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, samauhaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘samauhaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, vītamauhaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘vītamauhaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, saṃkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘saṃkhittaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, vikkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘vikkhittaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, mahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ ‘mahaggataṃ  citta’nti pajānāti, amahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ ‘amahaggataṃ citta’nti pajānāti, sauttaraṃ vā cittaṃ ‘sauttaraṃ citta’nti pajānāti, anuttaraṃ vā cittaṃ ‘anuttaraṃ citta’nti pajānāti, samāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘samāhitaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, asamāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘asamāhitaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, vimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘vimuttaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘avimuttaṃ citta’nti pajānāti|

‘‘anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau tissaupi jātiyau catassaupi jātiyau pañ‍capi jātiyau dasapi jātiyau vīsampi jātiyau tiṃsampi jātiyau cattārīsampi jātiyau pañ‍ñāsampi jātiyau jātisatampi jātisahassampi jātisatasahassampi anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭakappai anaikaipi vivaṭṭakappai anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappai – ‘amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ; tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau  aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nau’ti| iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati|

‘‘dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai, sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti|

‘‘āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ  pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati|

‘‘imai khau, brāhmaṇa, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina dasa pasādanīyā dhammā akkhātā| yasmiṃ nau imai dhammā saṃvij‍janti taṃ mayaṃ aitarahi sak‍karauma garuṃ karauma mānaima pūjaima; sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharāmā’’ti|

83. aivaṃ  vuttai vassakārau brāhmaṇau magadhamahāmattau upanandaṃ saināpatiṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñati bhavaṃ saināpati mañ‍ñasi aivaṃ saināpati (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), mañ‍ñasi saināpati (sī॰), mañ‍ñasi bhavaṃ saināpati (ka॰) yadimai bhauntau sak‍kātabbaṃ sak‍karaunti, garuṃ kātabbaṃ garuṃ karaunti, mānaitabbaṃ mānainti, pūjaitabbaṃ pūjainti’’? ‘‘tagghimai taggha mai (ka॰) bhauntau sak‍kātabbaṃ sak‍karaunti, garuṃ kātabbaṃ garuṃ karaunti, mānaitabbaṃ mānainti, pūjaitabbaṃ pūjainti| imañ‍ca hi tai bhauntau na sak‍karaiyyuṃ na garuṃ karaiyyuṃ na mānaiyyuṃ na pūjaiyyuṃ; atha kiñ‍carahi tai bhauntau sak‍karaiyyuṃ garuṃ karaiyyuṃ mānaiyyuṃ pūjaiyyuṃ, sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā mānaitvā pūjaitvā upanissāya viharaiyyu’’nti? atha khau vassakārau brāhmaṇau magadhamahāmattau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kahaṃ pana bhavaṃ ānandau aitarahi viharatī’’ti? ‘‘vaiḷuvanai khauhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, aitarahi viharāmī’’ti| ‘‘kac‍ci pana, bhau ānanda, vaiḷuvanaṃ ramaṇīyañ‍caiva appasaddañ‍ca appanigghausañ‍ca vijanavātaṃ  manussarāhassaiyyakaṃ manussarāhasaiyyakaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) paṭisal‍lānasāruppa’’nti? ‘‘taggha, brāhmaṇa, vaiḷuvanaṃ ramaṇīyañ‍caiva appasaddañ‍ca appanigghausañ‍ca vijanavātaṃ manussarāhassaiyyakaṃ paṭisal‍lānasāruppaṃ, yathā taṃ tumhādisaihi rakkhakaihi gaupakaihī’’ti| ‘‘taggha, bhau ānanda, vaiḷuvanaṃ ramaṇīyañ‍caiva appasaddañ‍ca appanigghausañ‍ca vijanavātaṃ manussarāhassaiyyakaṃ paṭisal‍lānasāruppaṃ, yathā taṃ bhavantaihi jhāyīhi jhānasīlīhi| jhāyinau caiva bhavantau jhānasīlinau ca’’|

‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, bhau ānanda, samayaṃ sau bhavaṃ gautamau vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| atha khvāhaṃ, bhau ānanda, yaina mahāvanaṃ kūṭāgārasālā yaina sau bhavaṃ gautamau tainupasaṅkamiṃ| tatra ca pana sau tatra ca sau (sī॰ pī॰) bhavaṃ gautamau anaikapariyāyaina jhānakathaṃ kathaisi| jhāyī caiva sau bhavaṃ gautamau ahausi jhānasīlī ca| sabbañ‍ca pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau jhānaṃ vaṇṇaisī’’ti|

84. ‘‘na ca khau, brāhmaṇa, sau bhagavā sabbaṃ jhānaṃ vaṇṇaisi, napi sau bhagavā sabbaṃ jhānaṃ na vaṇṇaisīti| kathaṃ rūpañ‍ca, brāhmaṇa, sau bhagavā jhānaṃ na vaṇṇaisi? idha, brāhmaṇa, aikac‍cau kāmarāgapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati kāmarāgaparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca kāmarāgassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti; sau kāmarāgaṃyaiva antaraṃ karitvā jhāyati pajjhāyati nijjhāyati apajjhāyati| byāpādapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati byāpādaparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca byāpādassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti; sau byāpādaṃyaiva antaraṃ karitvā jhāyati pajjhāyati nijjhāyati apajjhāyati| thinamiddhapariyuṭṭhitaina  caitasā viharati thinamiddhaparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca thinamiddhassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti; sau thinamiddhaṃyaiva antaraṃ karitvā jhāyati pajjhāyati nijjhāyati apajjhāyati| uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍capariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti; sau uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃyaiva antaraṃ karitvā jhāyati pajjhāyati nijjhāyati apajjhāyati| vicikicchāpariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati vicikicchāparaitaina, uppan‍nāya ca vicikicchāya nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti; sau vicikicchaṃyaiva antaraṃ karitvā jhāyati pajjhāyati nijjhāyati apajjhāyati| aivarūpaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, sau bhagavā jhānaṃ na vaṇṇaisi|

‘‘kathaṃ rūpañ‍ca, brāhmaṇa, sau bhagavā jhānaṃ vaṇṇaisi? idha, brāhmaṇa, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…  catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| aivarūpaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, sau bhagavā jhānaṃ vaṇṇaisī’’ti|

‘‘gārayhaṃ kira, bhau ānanda, sau bhavaṃ gautamau jhānaṃ garahi, pāsaṃsaṃ pasaṃsi| handa, ca dāni mayaṃ, bhau ānanda, gacchāma; bahukic‍cā mayaṃ bahukaraṇīyā’’ti| ‘‘yassadāni tvaṃ, brāhmaṇa, kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’’ti| atha  khau  vassakārau brāhmaṇau magadhamahāmattau āyasmatau ānandassa bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi|

atha khau gaupakamauggal‍lānau brāhmaṇau acirapak‍kantai vassakārai brāhmaṇai magadhamahāmattai āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yaṃ nau mayaṃ bhavantaṃ ānandaṃ apucchimhā taṃ nau bhavaṃ ānandau na byākāsī’’ti| ‘‘nanu tai, brāhmaṇa, avaucumhā – ‘natthi khau, brāhmaṇa, aikabhikkhupi taihi dhammaihi sabbainasabbaṃ sabbathāsabbaṃ saman‍nāgatau yaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau sau bhagavā ahausi arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau| sau hi, brāhmaṇa, bhagavā anuppan‍nassa maggassa uppādaitā, asañ‍jātassa maggassa sañ‍janaitā, anakkhātassa maggassa akkhātā, maggañ‍ñū, maggavidū, maggakauvidau| maggānugā ca pana aitarahi sāvakā viharanti pacchā saman‍nāgatā’’’ti|

gaupakamauggal‍lānasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. mahāpuṇṇamasuttaṃ

85. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati pubbārāmai migāramātupāsādai| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā tadahupausathai pan‍narasai puṇṇāya puṇṇamāya rattiyā bhikkhusaṅghaparivutau abbhaukāsai nisin‍nau hauti| atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ cīvaraṃ katvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘pucchaiyyāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ kiñ‍cidaiva daisaṃ, sacai mai bhagavā aukāsaṃ karauti pañhassa vaiyyākaraṇāyā’’ti| ‘‘taina hi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, sakai āsanai nisīditvā puccha yadākaṅkhasī’’ti|

86. atha khau sau bhikkhu sakai āsanai nisīditvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘imai nu khau, bhantai, pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā, saiyyathidaṃ  – rūpupādānakkhandhau, vaidanupādānakkhandhau, sañ‍ñupādānakkhandhau, saṅkhārupādānakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau’’ti? ‘‘imai khau, bhikkhu, pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā, saiyyathidaṃ – rūpupādānakkhandhau, vaidanupādānakkhandhau, sañ‍ñupādānakkhandhau, saṅkhārupādānakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau’’ti|

‘‘sādhu, bhantai’’ti khau sau bhikkhu bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā bhagavantaṃ uttariṃ pañhaṃ pucchi – ‘‘imai pana, bhantai, pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā kiṃmūlakā’’ti? ‘‘imai khau, bhikkhu, pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā chandamūlakā’’ti| ‘‘taṃyaiva nu khau, bhantai, upādānaṃ tai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā, udāhu  añ‍ñatra pañ‍cahupādānakkhandhaihi upādāna’’nti? ‘‘na khau, bhikkhu, taṃyaiva upādānaṃ tai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā, nāpi añ‍ñatra pañ‍cahupādānakkhandhaihi upādānaṃ| yau khau, bhikkhu, pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu chandarāgau taṃ tattha upādāna’’nti|

‘‘siyā pana, bhantai, pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu chandarāgavaimattatā’’ti? ‘‘siyā bhikkhū’’ti bhagavā avauca ‘‘idha, bhikkhu, aikac‍cassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘aivaṃrūpau  siyaṃ anāgatamaddhānaṃ, aivaṃvaidanau siyaṃ anāgatamaddhānaṃ, aivaṃsañ‍ñau siyaṃ anāgatamaddhānaṃ, aivaṃsaṅkhārau siyaṃ anāgatamaddhānaṃ, aivaṃviñ‍ñāṇau siyaṃ anāgatamaddhāna’nti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, siyā pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu chandarāgavaimattatā’’ti|

‘‘kittāvatā pana, bhantai, khandhānaṃ khandhādhivacanaṃ hautī’’ti? ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci, bhikkhu, rūpaṃ – atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā, auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā, hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā, yaṃ dūrai santikai vā – ayaṃ rūpakkhandhau| yā  kāci vaidanā – atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nā ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā, auḷārikā vā sukhumā vā, hīnā vā paṇītā vā, yā dūrai santikai vā – ayaṃ vaidanākkhandhau| yā kāci sañ‍ñā – atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nā…pai॰… yā dūrai santikai vā – ayaṃ sañ‍ñākkhandhau| yai kaici saṅkhārā – atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nā ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā, auḷārikā vā sukhumā vā, hīnā vā paṇītā vā, yai dūrai santikai vā – ayaṃ saṅkhārakkhandhau| yaṃ kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ – atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā, auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā, hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā, yaṃ dūrai santikai vā – ayaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau| aittāvatā khau, bhikkhu, khandhānaṃ khandhādhivacanaṃ hautī’’ti|

‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu kau pac‍cayau rūpakkhandhassa pañ‍ñāpanāya? kau haitu kau pac‍cayau vaidanākkhandhassa pañ‍ñāpanāya? kau haitu kau pac‍cayau sañ‍ñākkhandhassa pañ‍ñāpanāya? kau  haitu kau pac‍cayau saṅkhārakkhandhassa pañ‍ñāpanāya? kau haitu kau pac‍cayau viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhassa pañ‍ñāpanāyā’’ti?

‘‘cattārau khau, bhikkhu, mahābhūtā haitu, cattārau mahābhūtā pac‍cayau rūpakkhandhassa pañ‍ñāpanāya| phassau haitu, phassau pac‍cayau vaidanākkhandhassa pañ‍ñāpanāya| phassau haitu, phassau pac‍cayau sañ‍ñākkhandhassa pañ‍ñāpanāya| phassau haitu, phassau pac‍cayau saṅkhārakkhandhassa pañ‍ñāpanāya| nāmarūpaṃ khau, bhikkhu, haitu, nāmarūpaṃ pac‍cayau viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhassa pañ‍ñāpanāyā’’ti|

87. ‘‘kathaṃ pana, bhantai, sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi hautī’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhikkhu, assutavā puthuj‍janau ariyānaṃ adassāvī ariyadhammassa akauvidau ariyadhammai avinītau  sappurisānaṃ adassāvī  sappurisadhammassa akauvidau sappurisadhammai avinītau rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ attani vā rūpaṃ rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ; vaidanaṃ attatau samanupassati vaidanāvantaṃ vā attānaṃ attani vā vaidanaṃ vaidanāya vā attānaṃ; sañ‍ñaṃ attatau samanupassati sañ‍ñāvantaṃ vā attānaṃ attani vā sañ‍ñaṃ sañ‍ñāya vā attānaṃ; saṅkhārai attatau samanupassati saṅkhāravantaṃ vā attānaṃ attani vā saṅkhārai saṅkhāraisu vā attānaṃ; viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ attani  vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi hautī’’ti|

‘‘kathaṃ pana, bhantai, sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi na hautī’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhikkhu, sutavā ariyasāvakau ariyānaṃ dassāvī ariyadhammassa kauvidau ariyadhammai suvinītau sappurisānaṃ dassāvī sappurisadhammassa kauvidau sappurisadhammai suvinītau na rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati na rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ na attani vā rūpaṃ na rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ; na vaidanaṃ attatau samanupassati na vaidanāvantaṃ vā attānaṃ na attani vā vaidanaṃ na vaidanāya vā attānaṃ; na sañ‍ñaṃ attatau samanupassati na sañ‍ñāvantaṃ vā attānaṃ na attani vā sañ‍ñaṃ na sañ‍ñāya vā attānaṃ; na saṅkhārai attatau samanupassati na saṅkhāravantaṃ vā attānaṃ na attani vā saṅkhārai na saṅkhāraisu vā attānaṃ; na viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati na viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ na attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ na viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi na hautī’’ti|

88. ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, rūpai assādau, kau ādīnavau, kiṃ nissaraṇaṃ? kau vaidanāya assādau, kau ādīnavau, kiṃ nissaraṇaṃ? kau sañ‍ñāya assādau, kau ādīnavau, kiṃ nissaraṇaṃ? kau saṅkhāraisu assādau, kau ādīnavau, kiṃ nissaraṇaṃ? kau viñ‍ñāṇai assādau, kau ādīnavau, kiṃ nissaraṇa’’nti? ‘‘yaṃ khau, bhikkhu, rūpaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ, ayaṃ rūpai assādau| yaṃ rūpaṃ anic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ  vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, ayaṃ rūpai ādīnavau| yau rūpai chandarāgavinayau chandarāgappahānaṃ, idaṃ rūpai nissaraṇaṃ| yaṃ khau yañ‍ca (syā॰ kaṃ॰), bhikkhu, vaidanaṃ paṭic‍ca… sañ‍ñaṃ  paṭic‍ca… saṅkhārai paṭic‍ca… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ, ayaṃ viñ‍ñāṇai assādau| yaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, ayaṃ viñ‍ñāṇai ādīnavau| yau viñ‍ñāṇai chandarāgavinayau chandarāgappahānaṃ, idaṃ viñ‍ñāṇai nissaraṇa’’nti|

89. ‘‘kathaṃ  pana, bhantai, jānatau kathaṃ passatau imasmiñ‍ca saviñ‍ñāṇakai kāyai bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaisu ahaṃkāramamaṃkāramānānusayā na hauntī’’ti? ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci, bhikkhu, rūpaṃ – atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā  paṇītaṃ vā  yaṃ dūrai santikai vā – sabbaṃ rūpaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti – aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| yā kāci vaidanā… yā kāci sañ‍ñā… yai kaici saṅkhārā… yaṃ kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ – atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā – sabbaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti – aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, jānatau aivaṃ passatau imasmiñ‍ca saviñ‍ñāṇakai kāyai bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaisu ahaṃkāramamaṃkāramānānusayā na hauntī’’ti|

90. atha khau añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘iti kira, bhau, rūpaṃ anattā, vaidanā anattā, sañ‍ñā anattā, saṅkhārā anattā, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā; anattakatāni kammāni kamattānaṃ kathamattānaṃ (saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.82) phusissantī’’ti? atha khau bhagavā tassa bhikkhunau caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, vij‍jati yaṃ idhaikac‍cau maughapurisau avidvā avij‍jāgatau taṇhādhipataiyyaina caitasā satthu sāsanaṃ atidhāvitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyya – ‘iti kira, bhau, rūpaṃ anattā, vaidanā anattā, sañ‍ñā anattā, saṅkhārā anattā, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā; anattakatāni kammāni kamattānaṃ phusissantī’ti| paṭivinītā paṭic‍ca vinītā (sī॰ pī॰), paṭipucchāmi vinītā (syā॰ kaṃ॰) khau mai tumhai, bhikkhavai, tatra tatra dhammaisu’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ  panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, yaṃ kiñ‍ci rūpaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā sabbaṃ rūpaṃ – ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| yā kāci vaidanā… yā kāci sañ‍ñā… yai kaici saṅkhārā… yaṃ kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā  auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā sabbaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ – ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau rūpasmimpi nibbindati, vaidanāyapi nibbindati, sañ‍ñāyapi nibbindati, saṅkhāraisupi nibbindati, viñ‍ñāṇasmimpi nibbindati; nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati, virāgā vimuc‍cati| vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti| imasmiñ‍ca pana vaiyyākaraṇasmiṃ bhañ‍ñamānai saṭṭhimattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ anupādāya āsavaihi cittāni vimuc‍ciṃsūti|

mahāpuṇṇamasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ navamaṃ|

10. cūḷapuṇṇamasuttaṃ

91. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati pubbārāmai migāramātupāsādai| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā tadahupausathai pan‍narasai puṇṇāya puṇṇamāya  rattiyā bhikkhusaṅghaparivutau abbhaukāsai nisin‍nau hauti| atha khau bhagavā tuṇhībhūtaṃ tuṇhībhūtaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ anuvilaukaitvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘jānaiyya nu khau, bhikkhavai, asappurisau asappurisaṃ – ‘asappurisau ayaṃ bhava’’’nti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sādhu, bhikkhavai; aṭṭhānamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, anavakāsau  yaṃ asappurisau asappurisaṃ jānaiyya – ‘asappurisau ayaṃ bhava’nti| jānaiyya pana, bhikkhavai, asappurisau sappurisaṃ – ‘sappurisau ayaṃ bhava’’’nti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sādhu, bhikkhavai; aitampi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhānaṃ anavakāsau yaṃ asappurisau sappurisaṃ jānaiyya – ‘sappurisau ayaṃ bhava’nti| asappurisau, bhikkhavai, assaddhammasaman‍nāgatau hauti, asappurisabhatti asappurisabhattī (sabbattha) hauti, asappurisacintī hauti, asappurisamantī hauti, asappurisavācau hauti, asappurisakammantau hauti, asappurisadiṭṭhi asappurisadiṭṭhī (sabbattha) hauti; asappurisadānaṃ daiti’’|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, asappurisau assaddhammasaman‍nāgatau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, asappurisau assaddhau hauti, ahirikau hauti, anauttappī hauti, appassutau hauti, kusītau hauti, muṭṭhassati hauti, duppañ‍ñau hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, asappurisau assaddhammasaman‍nāgatau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, asappurisau asappurisabhatti hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, asappurisassa yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā assaddhā ahirikā anauttappinau appassutā kusītā muṭṭhassatinau duppañ‍ñā tyāssa mittā haunti tai sahāyā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, asappurisau asappurisabhatti hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, asappurisau asappurisacintī hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, asappurisau  attabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, parabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, ubhayabyābādhāyapi caitaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, asappurisau asappurisacintī hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, asappurisau asappurisamantī hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, asappurisau attabyābādhāyapi mantaiti, parabyābādhāyapi mantaiti, ubhayabyābādhāyapi  mantaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, asappurisau asappurisamantī hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, asappurisau asappurisavācau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, asappurisau musāvādī hauti, pisuṇavācau hauti, pharusavācau hauti, samphappalāpī hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, asappurisau asappurisavācau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, asappurisau asappurisakammantau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, asappurisau pāṇātipātī hauti, adin‍nādāyī hauti, kāmaisumicchācārī hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, asappurisau asappurisakammantau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, asappurisau asappurisadiṭṭhi hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, asappurisau aivaṃdiṭṭhi aivaṃdiṭṭhī (sī॰ pī॰), aivaṃdiṭṭhikau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) hauti – ‘natthi din‍naṃ, natthi yiṭṭhaṃ, natthi hutaṃ, natthi sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ suk‍kaṭaduk‍kaṭānaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau, natthi ayaṃ laukau, natthi parau laukau, natthi mātā, natthi pitā, natthi sattā aupapātikā, natthi laukai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā samaggatā (ka॰) sammāpaṭipan‍nā, yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaintī’ti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, asappurisau asappurisadiṭṭhi hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, asappurisau asappurisadānaṃ daiti? idha, bhikkhavai, asappurisau asak‍kac‍caṃ dānaṃ daiti, asahatthā dānaṃ daiti, acittīkatvā dānaṃ daiti, apaviṭṭhaṃ dānaṃ daiti anāgamanadiṭṭhikau dānaṃ daiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, asappurisau asappurisadānaṃ daiti|

‘‘sau, bhikkhavai, asappurisau aivaṃ assaddhammasaman‍nāgatau, aivaṃ asappurisabhatti, aivaṃ asappurisacintī, aivaṃ asappurisamantī, aivaṃ asappurisavācau, aivaṃ asappurisakammantau, aivaṃ  asappurisadiṭṭhi; aivaṃ asappurisadānaṃ datvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā yā asappurisānaṃ gati  tattha upapaj‍jati| kā ca, bhikkhavai, asappurisānaṃ gati? nirayau vā tiracchānayauni vā|

92. ‘‘jānaiyya nu khau, bhikkhavai, sappurisau sappurisaṃ – ‘sappurisau ayaṃ bhava’’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sādhu, bhikkhavai; ṭhānamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, vij‍jati yaṃ sappurisau sappurisaṃ jānaiyya – ‘sappurisau ayaṃ bhava’nti| jānaiyya pana, bhikkhavai, sappurisau asappurisaṃ – ‘asappurisau ayaṃ bhava’’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sādhu, bhikkhavai; aitampi khau, bhikkhavai, ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ sappurisau asappurisaṃ jānaiyya – ‘asappurisau ayaṃ bhava’nti| sappurisau, bhikkhavai, saddhammasaman‍nāgatau hauti, sappurisabhatti hauti, sappurisacintī  hauti, sappurisamantī hauti, sappurisavācau hauti, sappurisakammantau hauti, sappurisadiṭṭhi hauti; sappurisadānaṃ daiti’’|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sappurisau saddhammasaman‍nāgatau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, sappurisau saddhau hauti, hirimā hauti, auttappī hauti, bahussutau hauti, āraddhavīriyau hauti, upaṭṭhitassati hauti, pañ‍ñavā hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sappurisau saddhammasaman‍nāgatau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sappurisau sappurisabhatti hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, sappurisassa yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhā hirimantau auttappinau bahussutā āraddhavīriyā upaṭṭhitassatinau pañ‍ñavantau tyāssa mittā haunti, tai sahāyā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sappurisau sappurisabhatti  hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sappurisau sappurisacintī hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, sappurisau naivattabyābādhāya caitaiti, na parabyābādhāya caitaiti, na ubhayabyābādhāya caitaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sappurisau sappurisacintī hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sappurisau sappurisamantī hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, sappurisau naivattabyābādhāya  mantaiti, na parabyābādhāya mantaiti, na ubhayabyābādhāya mantaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sappurisau sappurisamantī hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sappurisau sappurisavācau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, sappurisau musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, samphappalāpā paṭiviratau hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sappurisau sappurisavācau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sappurisau sappurisakammantau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, sappurisau pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau  hauti, kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sappurisau sappurisakammantau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sappurisau sappurisadiṭṭhi hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, sappurisau aivaṃdiṭṭhi hauti – ‘atthi din‍naṃ, atthi yiṭṭhaṃ, atthi hutaṃ, atthi sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau, atthi ayaṃ laukau, atthi parau laukau, atthi mātā, atthi pitā, atthi sattā aupapātikā, atthi laukai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā sammāpaṭipan‍nā yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaintī’ti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sappurisau sappurisadiṭṭhi hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sappurisau sappurisadānaṃ daiti? idha, bhikkhavai, sappurisau sak‍kac‍caṃ dānaṃ daiti, sahatthā dānaṃ daiti, cittīkatvā dānaṃ daiti, anapaviṭṭhaṃ dānaṃ daiti, āgamanadiṭṭhikau dānaṃ daiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sappurisau sappurisadānaṃ daiti|

‘‘sau, bhikkhavai, sappurisau aivaṃ saddhammasaman‍nāgatau, aivaṃ sappurisabhatti, aivaṃ sappurisacintī, aivaṃ sappurisamantī, aivaṃ sappurisavācau, aivaṃ sappurisakammantau, aivaṃ sappurisadiṭṭhi; aivaṃ sappurisadānaṃ datvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā yā sappurisānaṃ gati tattha upapaj‍jati| kā ca, bhikkhavai, sappurisānaṃ gati? daivamahattatā vā manussamahattatā vā’’ti|

idamavauca  bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

cūḷapuṇṇamasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dasamaṃ|

daivadahavaggau niṭṭhitau paṭhamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

daivadahaṃ pañ‍cattayaṃ, kinti-sāma-sunakkhattaṃ|

sappāya-gaṇa-gaupaka-mahāpuṇṇacūḷapuṇṇañ‍cāti||


2. anupadavaggau

1. anupadasuttaṃ

93. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘paṇḍitau, bhikkhavai, sāriputtau; mahāpañ‍ñau, bhikkhavai, sāriputtau; puthupañ‍ñau, bhikkhavai, sāriputtau; hāsapañ‍ñau hāsupañ‍ñau (sī॰ pī॰), bhikkhavai, sāriputtau; javanapañ‍ñau, bhikkhavai, sāriputtau; tikkhapañ‍ñau, bhikkhavai, sāriputtau; nibbaidhikapañ‍ñau, bhikkhavai, sāriputtau; sāriputtau, bhikkhavai, aḍḍhamāsaṃ anupadadhammavipassanaṃ vipassati| tatridaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāriputtassa anupadadhammavipassanāya hauti|

94. ‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, sāriputtau vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| yai ca paṭhamai jhānai paṭhamajjhānai (ka॰ sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) dhammā vitak‍kau ca vicārau ca pīti ca sukhañ‍ca cittaikaggatā ca, phassau vaidanā sañ‍ñā caitanā cittaṃ chandau adhimaukkhau vīriyaṃ sati upaikkhā manasikārau – tyāssa dhammā anupadavavatthitā haunti| tyāssa dhammā viditā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘aivaṃ  kiramai dhammā ahutvā sambhaunti, hutvā paṭivaintī’ti| sau taisu dhammaisu anupāyau anapāyau anissitau appaṭibaddhau appaṭibandhau (ka॰) vippamuttau visaṃyuttau vimariyādīkataina caitasā viharati| sau ‘atthi uttari nissaraṇa’nti pajānāti| tabbahulīkārā atthitvaivassa atthitaivassa (sī॰ pī॰) hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāriputtau vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ  caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ  avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| yai ca dutiyai jhānai dhammā – ajjhattaṃ sampasādau ca pīti ca sukhañ‍ca cittaikaggatā ca, phassau vaidanā sañ‍ñā caitanā  cittaṃ chandau adhimaukkhau vīriyaṃ sati upaikkhā manasikārau – tyāssa dhammā anupadavavatthitā haunti| tyāssa dhammā viditā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘aivaṃ kiramai dhammā ahutvā sambhaunti, hutvā paṭivaintī’ti| sau taisu dhammaisu anupāyau anapāyau anissitau appaṭibaddhau vippamuttau visaṃyuttau vimariyādīkataina caitasā viharati| sau ‘atthi uttari nissaraṇa’nti pajānāti| tabbahulīkārā atthitvaivassa hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāriputtau pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati satau ca sampajānau, sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti| yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| yai ca tatiyai jhānai dhammā – sukhañ‍ca  sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca cittaikaggatā ca, phassau vaidanā sañ‍ñā caitanā cittaṃ chandau adhimaukkhau vīriyaṃ sati upaikkhā manasikārau – tyāssa dhammā anupadavavatthitā haunti, tyāssa dhammā viditā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘aivaṃ kiramai dhammā ahutvā sambhaunti, hutvā paṭivaintī’ti| sau taisu dhammaisu anupāyau anapāyau anissitau appaṭibaddhau vippamuttau visaṃyuttau vimariyādīkataina caitasā viharati| sau ‘atthi uttari nissaraṇa’nti pajānāti| tabbahulīkārā atthitvaivassa hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāriputtau sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| yai ca catutthai jhānai dhammā – upaikkhā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā passaddhattā caitasau anābhaugau satipārisuddhi cittaikaggatā ca, phassau vaidanā sañ‍ñā caitanā cittaṃ chandau adhimaukkhau vīriyaṃ sati upaikkhā manasikārau – tyāssa dhammā anupadavavatthitā haunti| tyāssa dhammā viditā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā  abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘aivaṃ kiramai dhammā ahutvā sambhaunti, hutvā paṭivaintī’ti| sau taisu dhammaisu anupāyau anapāyau anissitau appaṭibaddhau vippamuttau visaṃyuttau vimariyādīkataina  caitasā viharati| sau ‘atthi uttari nissaraṇa’nti pajānāti| tabbahulīkārā atthitvaivassa hauti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāriputtau sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| yai ca ākāsānañ‍cāyatanai dhammā – ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñā ca cittaikaggatā ca phassau vaidanā sañ‍ñā caitanā cittaṃ chandau adhimaukkhau vīriyaṃ sati upaikkhā manasikārau – tyāssa dhammā anupadavavatthitā haunti| tyāssa dhammā viditā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘aivaṃ kiramai dhammā ahutvā sambhaunti, hutvā paṭivaintī’ti| sau taisu dhammaisu anupāyau anapāyau anissitau appaṭibaddhau vippamuttau visaṃyuttau vimariyādīkataina caitasā viharati| sau ‘atthi uttari nissaraṇa’nti pajānāti| tabbahulīkārā atthitvaivassa hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāriputtau sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| yai ca viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanai dhammā – viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñā ca cittaikaggatā ca, phassau vaidanā sañ‍ñā caitanā cittaṃ chandau adhimaukkhau vīriyaṃ sati upaikkhā manasikārau – tyāssa dhammā anupadavavatthitā haunti| tyāssa dhammā viditā uppaj‍janti, viditā  upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘aivaṃ kiramai dhammā ahutvā sambhaunti, hutvā paṭivaintī’ti| sau taisu dhammaisu anupāyau anapāyau anissitau appaṭibaddhau vippamuttau visaṃyuttau vimariyādīkataina caitasā viharati| sau ‘atthi uttari nissaraṇa’nti pajānāti| tabbahulīkārā atthitvaivassa hauti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāriputtau sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| yai ca ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanai dhammā – ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñā ca cittaikaggatā ca, phassau vaidanā sañ‍ñā caitanā cittaṃ chandau adhimaukkhau vīriyaṃ sati upaikkhā manasikārau – tyāssa dhammā anupadavavatthitā haunti| tyāssa dhammā viditā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘aivaṃ kiramai dhammā ahutvā sambhaunti, hutvā paṭivaintī’ti| sau taisu dhammaisu anupāyau anapāyau anissitau appaṭibaddhau vippamuttau  visaṃyuttau vimariyādīkataina caitasā viharati| sau ‘atthi uttari nissaraṇa’nti pajānāti| tabbahulīkārā atthitvaivassa hauti|

95. ‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāriputtau sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau tāya samāpattiyā satau vuṭṭhahati| sau tāya samāpattiyā satau vuṭṭhahitvā yai dhammā yai tai dhammā (sī॰) atītā niruddhā vipariṇatā tai dhammai samanupassati  – ‘aivaṃ kiramai dhammā ahutvā sambhaunti, hutvā paṭivaintī’ti| sau taisu dhammaisu anupāyau anapāyau anissitau appaṭibaddhau vippamuttau visaṃyuttau vimariyādīkataina caitasā viharati| sau ‘atthi uttari nissaraṇa’nti pajānāti| tabbahulīkārā atthitvaivassa hauti|

96. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāriputtau sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| pañ‍ñāya cassa disvā āsavā parikkhīṇā haunti| sau tāya samāpattiyā satau vuṭṭhahati| sau tāya samāpattiyā satau vuṭṭhahitvā yai dhammā atītā niruddhā vipariṇatā tai dhammai samanupassati – ‘aivaṃ kiramai dhammā ahutvā sambhaunti, hutvā paṭivaintī’ti| sau taisu dhammaisu anupāyau anapāyau anissitau appaṭibaddhau vippamuttau visaṃyuttau vimariyādīkataina caitasā viharati| sau ‘natthi uttari nissaraṇa’nti pajānāti| tabbahulīkārā natthitvaivassa hauti|

97. ‘‘yaṃ khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘vasippattau pāramippattau ariyasmiṃ sīlasmiṃ, vasippattau pāramippattau  ariyasmiṃ samādhismiṃ, vasippattau pāramippattau ariyāya pañ‍ñāya, vasippattau pāramippattau ariyāya vimuttiyā’ti, sāriputtamaiva taṃ sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘vasippattau pāramippattau ariyasmiṃ sīlasmiṃ, vasippattau pāramippattau ariyasmiṃ samādhismiṃ, vasippattau pāramippattau ariyāya pañ‍ñāya, vasippattau pāramippattau ariyāya vimuttiyā’ti| yaṃ khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘bhagavatau puttau aurasau mukhatau jātau dhammajau dhammanimmitau dhammadāyādau nau āmisadāyādau’ti, sāriputtamaiva taṃ sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘bhagavatau puttau aurasau mukhatau jātau dhammajau dhammanimmitau dhammadāyādau nau āmisadāyādau’ti| sāriputtau, bhikkhavai, tathāgataina anuttaraṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattitaṃ sammadaiva anuppavattaitī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

anupadasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. chabbisaudhanasuttaṃ

98. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –
