‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu añ‍ñaṃ byākarauti – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāmī’ti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau bhāsitaṃ naiva abhinanditabbaṃ nappaṭik‍kausitabbaṃ| anabhinanditvā appaṭik‍kausitvā pañhau pucchitabbau – ‘cattāraumai, āvusau, vauhārā taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina sammadakkhātā| katamai cattārau? diṭṭhai diṭṭhavāditā, sutai sutavāditā, mutai mutavāditā, viñ‍ñātai viñ‍ñātavāditā  – imai khau, āvusau, cattārau vauhārā taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina sammadakkhātā| kathaṃ jānatau panāyasmatau, kathaṃ passatau imaisu catūsu vauhāraisu anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimutta’nti? khīṇāsavassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau vusitavatau katakaraṇīyassa auhitabhārassa anuppattasadatthassa parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanassa sammadañ‍ñāvimuttassa ayamanudhammau hauti vaiyyākaraṇāya – ‘diṭṭhai khau ahaṃ, āvusau, anupāyau anapāyau anissitau  appaṭibaddhau vippamuttau visaṃyuttau vimariyādīkataina caitasā viharāmi| sutai khau ahaṃ, āvusau…pai॰… mutai khau ahaṃ, āvusau… viñ‍ñātai khau ahaṃ, āvusau, anupāyau anapāyau anissitau appaṭibaddhau vippamuttau visaṃyuttau vimariyādīkataina caitasā viharāmi| aivaṃ khau mai, āvusau, jānatau aivaṃ passatau imaisu catūsu vauhāraisu anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimutta’nti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ‘sādhū’ti bhāsitaṃ abhinanditabbaṃ anumauditabbaṃ| ‘sādhū’ti bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uttariṃ pañhau pucchitabbau|

99. ‘‘‘pañ‍cimai, āvusau, upādānakkhandhā taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina  sammadakkhātā| katamai pañ‍ca? saiyyathidaṃ – rūpupādānakkhandhau, vaidanupādānakkhandhau, sañ‍ñupādānakkhandhau, saṅkhārupādānakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau – imai khau, āvusau, pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina sammadakkhātā| kathaṃ jānatau panāyasmatau, kathaṃ passatau imaisu pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimutta’nti? khīṇāsavassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau vusitavatau katakaraṇīyassa auhitabhārassa anuppattasadatthassa parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanassa sammadañ‍ñāvimuttassa ayamanudhammau hauti vaiyyākaraṇāya – ‘rūpaṃ khau ahaṃ, āvusau, abalaṃ virāgunaṃ virāgaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), virāgutaṃ (ṭīkā) anassāsikanti viditvā yai rūpai upāyūpādānā upayūpādānā (ka॰) caitasau  adhiṭṭhānābhinivaisānusayā taisaṃ khayā virāgā niraudhā  cāgā paṭinissaggā vimuttaṃ mai cittanti pajānāmi| vaidanaṃ khau ahaṃ, āvusau…pai॰… sañ‍ñaṃ khau ahaṃ, āvusau… saṅkhārai khau ahaṃ, āvusau… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ khau ahaṃ, āvusau, abalaṃ virāgunaṃ anassāsikanti viditvā yai viñ‍ñāṇai upāyūpādānā caitasau adhiṭṭhānābhinivaisānusayā taisaṃ khayā virāgā niraudhā cāgā paṭinissaggā vimuttaṃ mai cittanti pajānāmi| aivaṃ khau mai, āvusau, jānatau aivaṃ passatau imaisu pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimutta’nti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ‘sādhū’ti bhāsitaṃ abhinanditabbaṃ, anumauditabbaṃ| ‘sādhū’ti bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uttariṃ pañhau pucchitabbau|

100. ‘‘‘chayimā, āvusau, dhātuyau taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina sammadakkhātā| katamā cha? pathavīdhātu, āpaudhātu, taijaudhātu, vāyaudhātu, ākāsadhātu, viñ‍ñāṇadhātu – imā khau, āvusau, cha dhātuyau taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina sammadakkhātā| kathaṃ jānatau panāyasmatau, kathaṃ passatau imāsu chasu dhātūsu anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimutta’nti? khīṇāsavassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau vusitavatau katakaraṇīyassa auhitabhārassa  anuppattasadatthassa parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanassa sammadañ‍ñāvimuttassa ayamanudhammau hauti vaiyyākaraṇāya – ‘pathavīdhātuṃ khau ahaṃ, āvusau, na attatau upagacchiṃ, na ca pathavīdhātunissitaṃ attānaṃ| yai ca pathavīdhātunissitā upāyūpādānā caitasau adhiṭṭhānābhinivaisānusayā taisaṃ  khayā virāgā niraudhā cāgā paṭinissaggā vimuttaṃ mai cittanti pajānāmi| āpaudhātuṃ khau ahaṃ, āvusau…pai॰…  taijaudhātuṃ khau ahaṃ, āvusau… vāyaudhātuṃ khau ahaṃ, āvusau… ākāsadhātuṃ khau ahaṃ, āvusau… viñ‍ñāṇadhātuṃ khau ahaṃ, āvusau, na attatau upagacchiṃ, na ca viñ‍ñāṇadhātunissitaṃ attānaṃ| yai ca viñ‍ñāṇadhātunissitā upāyūpādānā caitasau adhiṭṭhānābhinivaisānusayā taisaṃ khayā virāgā niraudhā cāgā paṭinissaggā vimuttaṃ mai cittanti pajānāmi| aivaṃ khau mai, āvusau, jānatau, aivaṃ passatau imāsu chasu dhātūsu anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimutta’nti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ‘sādhū’ti bhāsitaṃ  abhinanditabbaṃ, anumauditabbaṃ| ‘sādhū’ti bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uttariṃ pañhau pucchitabbau|

101. ‘‘‘cha khau panimāni, āvusau, ajjhattikabāhirāni ajjhattikāni bāhirāni (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) āyatanāni taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina sammadakkhātāni| katamāni cha? cakkhu caiva rūpā ca, sautañ‍ca saddā ca, ghānañ‍ca gandhā ca, jivhā ca rasā ca, kāyau ca phauṭṭhabbā ca, manau ca dhammā ca – imāni khau, āvusau, cha ajjhattikabāhirāni āyatanāni taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina sammadakkhātāni| kathaṃ jānatau panāyasmatau, kathaṃ passatau imaisu chasu ajjhattikabāhiraisu āyatanaisu  anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimutta’nti? khīṇāsavassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau vusitavatau katakaraṇīyassa auhitabhārassa  anuppattasadatthassa parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanassa sammadañ‍ñāvimuttassa ayamanudhammau hauti vaiyyākaraṇāya  – ‘cakkhusmiṃ, āvusau, rūpai cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇai cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbaisu dhammaisu yau chandau yau rāgau yā nandī nandi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) yā taṇhā yai ca upāyūpādānā caitasau adhiṭṭhānābhinivaisānusayā taisaṃ khayā virāgā niraudhā cāgā paṭinissaggā vimuttaṃ mai cittanti pajānāmi| sautasmiṃ, āvusau, saddai sautaviñ‍ñāṇai…pai॰… ghānasmiṃ, āvusau, gandhai ghānaviñ‍ñāṇai… jivhāya, āvusau, rasai jivhāviñ‍ñāṇai… kāyasmiṃ, āvusau, phauṭṭhabbai kāyaviñ‍ñāṇai… manasmiṃ, āvusau, dhammai manauviñ‍ñāṇai manauviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbaisu dhammaisu yau chandau yau rāgau yā nandī yā taṇhā yai ca upāyūpādānā caitasau adhiṭṭhānābhinivaisānusayā taisaṃ khayā virāgā niraudhā cāgā paṭinissaggā vimuttaṃ mai cittanti pajānāmi| aivaṃ khau mai, āvusau, jānatau aivaṃ passatau imaisu chasu ajjhattikabāhiraisu āyatanaisu anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimutta’nti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ‘sādhū’ti bhāsitaṃ abhinanditabbaṃ anumauditabbaṃ| ‘sādhū’ti bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uttariṃ pañhau pucchitabbau|

102. ‘‘‘kathaṃ jānatau panāyasmatau, kathaṃ passatau imasmiñ‍ca saviñ‍ñāṇakai kāyai bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaisu ahaṃkāramamaṃkāramānānusayā samūhatā’ti susamūhatāti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)? khīṇāsavassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau vusitavatau katakaraṇīyassa auhitabhārassa anuppattasadatthassa parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanassa sammadañ‍ñāvimuttassa ayamanudhammau hauti vaiyyākaraṇāya – ‘pubbai khau ahaṃ, āvusau, agāriyabhūtau samānau aviddasu ahausiṃ| tassa mai tathāgatau vā tathāgatasāvakau vā dhammaṃ daisaisi| tāhaṃ dhammaṃ sutvā tathāgatai saddhaṃ paṭilabhiṃ| sau taina saddhāpaṭilābhaina saman‍nāgatau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhiṃ – sambādhau gharāvāsau rajāpathau, abbhaukāsau pabbaj‍jā| nayidaṃ sukaraṃ agāraṃ ajjhāvasatā aikantaparipuṇṇaṃ aikantaparisuddhaṃ saṅkhalikhitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carituṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā  anagāriyaṃ pabbajaiyya’’’nti|

‘‘sau khau ahaṃ, āvusau, aparaina samayaina appaṃ vā bhaugakkhandhaṃ pahāya mahantaṃ vā bhaugakkhandhaṃ pahāya, appaṃ vā ñātiparivaṭṭaṃ pahāya mahantaṃ vā ñātiparivaṭṭaṃ pahāya kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajiṃ| sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāsājīvasamāpan‍nau pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau ahausiṃ nihitadaṇḍau nihitasatthau, laj‍jī dayāpan‍nau sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī vihāsiṃ| adin‍nādānaṃ pahāya adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau ahausiṃ din‍nādāyī din‍napāṭikaṅkhī, athainaina  sucibhūtaina attanā vihāsiṃ| abrahmacariyaṃ pahāya brahmacārī ahausiṃ ārācārī viratau maithunā gāmadhammā| musāvādaṃ pahāya musāvādā paṭiviratau ahausiṃ sac‍cavādī sac‍casandhau thaitau pac‍cayikau avisaṃvādakau  laukassa| pisuṇaṃ vācaṃ pahāya pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau ahausiṃ, itau sutvā na amutra akkhātā imaisaṃ bhaidāya, amutra vā sutvā na imaisaṃ akkhātā amūsaṃ bhaidāya; iti bhin‍nānaṃ vā sandhātā sahitānaṃ vā anuppadātā samaggārāmau samaggaratau samagganandī samaggakaraṇiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā ahausiṃ| pharusaṃ vācaṃ pahāya pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau ahausiṃ; yā sā vācā nailā kaṇṇasukhā paimanīyā  hadayaṅgamā paurī bahujanakantā bahujanamanāpā tathārūpiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā ahausiṃ| samphappalāpaṃ pahāya samphappalāpā paṭiviratau ahausiṃ; kālavādī bhūtavādī atthavādī dhammavādī vinayavādī nidhānavatiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā ahausiṃ kālaina sāpadaisaṃ pariyantavatiṃ atthasaṃhitaṃ|

‘‘sau bījagāmabhūtagāmasamārambhā paṭiviratau ahausiṃ, aikabhattikau ahausiṃ rattūparatau viratau vikālabhaujanā| nac‍cagītavāditavisūkadassanā paṭiviratau ahausiṃ| mālāgandhavilaipanadhāraṇamaṇḍanavibhūsanaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau ahausiṃ| uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanā paṭiviratau ahausiṃ| jātarūparajatapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau ahausiṃ, āmakadhañ‍ñapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau ahausiṃ, āmakamaṃsapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau ahausiṃ; itthikumārikapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau ahausiṃ, dāsidāsapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau ahausiṃ, ajaiḷakapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau ahausiṃ, kuk‍kuṭasūkarapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau ahausiṃ, hatthigavassavaḷavapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau ahausiṃ, khaittavatthupaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau ahausiṃ| dūtaiyyapahiṇagamanānuyaugā  paṭiviratau ahausiṃ, kayavik‍kayā paṭiviratau ahausiṃ, tulākūṭakaṃsakūṭamānakūṭā paṭiviratau ahausiṃ, uk‍kauṭanavañ‍cananikatisāciyaugā paṭiviratau ahausiṃ, chaidanavadhabandhanaviparāmausaālaupasahasākārā paṭiviratau ahausiṃ|

‘‘sau santuṭṭhau ahausiṃ kāyaparihārikaina cīvaraina, kucchiparihārikaina piṇḍapātaina| sau yaina yainaiva yaina yaina ca (ka॰) pak‍kamiṃ samādāyaiva pak‍kamiṃ| saiyyathāpi nāma pakkhī sakuṇau yaina yainaiva ḍaiti sapattabhārauva ḍaiti; aivamaiva khau ahaṃ, āvusau; santuṭṭhau ahausiṃ kāyaparihārikaina cīvaraina, kucchiparihārikaina piṇḍapātaina| sau yaina yainaiva pak‍kamiṃ samādāyaiva pak‍kamiṃ| sau iminā ariyaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau ajjhattaṃ anavaj‍jasukhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaisiṃ|

103. ‘‘sau  cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā na nimittaggāhī ahausiṃ nānubyañ‍janaggāhī; yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jiṃ; rakkhiṃ cakkhundriyaṃ, cakkhundriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jiṃ| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā…pai॰… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā…pai॰… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā…pai॰… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya na nimittaggāhī ahausiṃ nānubyañ‍janaggāhī; yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ manindriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jiṃ; rakkhiṃ manindriyaṃ, manindriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jiṃ| sau  iminā ariyaina indriyasaṃvaraina saman‍nāgatau ajjhattaṃ abyāsaikasukhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaisiṃ|

‘‘sau abhik‍kantai paṭik‍kantai sampajānakārī ahausiṃ, ālaukitai vilaukitai sampajānakārī ahausiṃ, samiñ‍jitai pasāritai sampajānakārī ahausiṃ, saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇai sampajānakārī ahausiṃ, asitai pītai khāyitai sāyitai sampajānakārī ahausiṃ, uc‍cārapassāvakammai sampajānakārī ahausiṃ, gatai ṭhitai nisin‍nai suttai jāgaritai bhāsitai tuṇhībhāvai sampajānakārī ahausiṃ|

‘‘sau  iminā ca ariyaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau, (imāya ca ariyāya santuṭṭhiyā saman‍nāgatau,) passa ma॰ ni॰ 1.296 cūḷahatthipadaupamai iminā ca ariyaina indriyasaṃvaraina saman‍nāgatau, iminā ca ariyaina satisampajañ‍ñaina saman‍nāgatau vivittaṃ saināsanaṃ bhajiṃ arañ‍ñaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ pabbataṃ kandaraṃ giriguhaṃ susānaṃ vanapatthaṃ abbhaukāsaṃ palālapuñ‍jaṃ| sau pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau nisīdiṃ pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā|

‘‘sau abhijjhaṃ laukai pahāya vigatābhijjhaina caitasā vihāsiṃ, abhijjhāya cittaṃ parisaudhaisiṃ| byāpādapadausaṃ pahāya abyāpan‍nacittau vihāsiṃ sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī, byāpādapadausā cittaṃ parisaudhaisiṃ| thinamiddhaṃ pahāya vigatathinamiddhau vihāsiṃ ālaukasañ‍ñī satau sampajānau, thinamiddhā cittaṃ parisaudhaisiṃ| uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ pahāya anuddhatau vihāsiṃ ajjhattaṃ, vūpasantacittau, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cā cittaṃ parisaudhaisiṃ| vicikicchaṃ pahāya tiṇṇavicikicchau vihāsiṃ akathaṃkathī kusalaisu dhammaisu, vicikicchāya cittaṃ parisaudhaisiṃ|

104. ‘‘sau  imai  pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja vihāsiṃ| vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja vihāsiṃ|

‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai āsavānaṃ khayañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaisiṃ| sau idaṃ dukkhanti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, ayaṃ dukkhasamudayauti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhauti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadāti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ; imai āsavāti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, ayaṃ āsavasamudayauti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, ayaṃ āsavaniraudhauti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, ayaṃ āsavaniraudhagāminī paṭipadāti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ| tassa mai aivaṃ jānatau aivaṃ  passatau kāmāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cittha, bhavāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cittha, avij‍jāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuccittha vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ ahausi| khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyāti abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ| aivaṃ khau mai, āvusau, jānatau aivaṃ passatau imasmiñ‍ca saviñ‍ñāṇakai kāyai bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaisu ahaṃkāramamaṃkāramānānusayā samūhatā’’ti| ‘‘tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ‘sādhū’ti bhāsitaṃ abhinanditabbaṃ anumauditabbaṃ| ‘sādhū’ti bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘lābhā nau, āvusau, suladdhaṃ  nau, āvusau, yai mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ tādisaṃ sabrahmacāriṃ samanupassāmā’’’ti passāmāti (sī॰)|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

chabbisaudhanasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. sappurisasuttaṃ

105. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘sappurisadhammañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi asappurisadhammañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asappurisadhammau? idha, bhikkhavai, asappurisau uc‍cākulā pabbajitau hauti| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ahaṃ khaumhi uc‍cākulā pabbajitau, imai panañ‍ñai bhikkhū na uc‍cākulā pabbajitā’ti| sau tāya uc‍cākulīnatāya attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, paraṃ vambhaiti| ayaṃ ayampi (sī॰ pī॰), bhikkhavai, asappurisadhammau| sappurisau ca khau, bhikkhavai, iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘na khau uc‍cākulīnatāya laubhadhammā vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, dausadhammā vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, mauhadhammā vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti| nau caipi uc‍cākulā pabbajitau hauti; sau ca hauti dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau anudhammacārī, sau tattha puj‍jau, sau tattha pāsaṃsau’ti| sau paṭipadaṃyaiva antaraṃ karitvā tāya uc‍cākulīnatāya naivattānuk‍kaṃsaiti na paraṃ vambhaiti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, sappurisadhammau|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, asappurisau mahākulā pabbajitau hauti…pai॰… yathā uc‍cākulavārai tathā vitthāraitabbaṃ mahābhaugakulā pabbajitau hauti…pai॰… uḷārabhaugakulā pabbajitau hauti| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ahaṃ khaumhi uḷārabhaugakulā pabbajitau, imai panañ‍ñai bhikkhū na uḷārabhaugakulā pabbajitā’ti| sau tāya uḷārabhaugatāya attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, asappurisadhammau| sappurisau ca khau, bhikkhavai, iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘na khau uḷārabhaugatāya laubhadhammā vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, dausadhammā vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, mauhadhammā vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti| nau caipi uḷārabhaugakulā pabbajitau hauti; sau  ca hauti dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau anudhammacārī, sau tattha puj‍jau, sau tattha pāsaṃsau’ti| sau paṭipadaṃyaiva antaraṃ karitvā tāya uḷārabhaugatāya naivattānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, sappurisadhammau|

106. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, asappurisau ñātau hauti yasassī| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ahaṃ khaumhi ñātau yasassī, imai panañ‍ñai bhikkhū appañ‍ñātā appaisakkhā’ti| sau taina ñattaina ñātaina (sī॰ ka॰), ñātattaina (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, asappurisadhammau| sappurisau ca khau, bhikkhavai, iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘na khau ñattaina laubhadhammā vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, dausadhammā vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, mauhadhammā vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti| nau caipi ñātau hauti yasassī; sau ca hauti dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau anudhammacārī, sau tattha puj‍jau, sau tattha pāsaṃsau’ti| sau paṭipadaṃyaiva antaraṃ karitvā taina ñattaina naivattānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, sappurisadhammau|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, asappurisau lābhī hauti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ahaṃ khaumhi  lābhī cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ, imai panañ‍ñai bhikkhū na lābhinau cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārāna’nti| sau taina lābhaina attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, asappurisadhammau| sappurisau ca khau, bhikkhavai, iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘na khau lābhaina laubhadhammā vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, dausadhammā vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, mauhadhammā vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti| nau caipi lābhī hauti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ; sau ca hauti dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau anudhammacārī, sau tattha puj‍jau, sau tattha pāsaṃsau’ti| sau paṭipadaṃyaiva antaraṃ karitvā taina lābhaina naivattānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, sappurisadhammau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, asappurisau bahussutau hauti| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ahaṃ khaumhi bahussutau, imai panañ‍ñai bhikkhū na bahussutā’ti| sau taina bāhusac‍caina attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, asappurisadhammau| sappurisau ca khau, bhikkhavai, iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘na khau bāhusac‍caina laubhadhammā vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, dausadhammā vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, mauhadhammā vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti| nau caipi bahussutau hauti; sau ca hauti dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau anudhammacārī, sau tattha puj‍jau, sau tattha pāsaṃsau’ti| sau paṭipadaṃyaiva antaraṃ karitvā taina bāhusac‍caina naivattānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, sappurisadhammau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, asappurisau vinayadharau hauti| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ahaṃ khaumhi vinayadharau, imai panañ‍ñai bhikkhū na vinayadharā’ti| sau taina vinayadharattaina attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, asappurisadhammau| sappurisau ca khau, bhikkhavai, iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘na khau vinayadharattaina laubhadhammā  vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, dausadhammā vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, mauhadhammā vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti| nau caipi vinayadharau hauti; sau ca hauti dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau anudhammacārī, sau tattha puj‍jau, sau tattha pāsaṃsau’ti| sau paṭipadaṃyaiva antaraṃ karitvā taina vinayadharattaina naivattānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, sappurisadhammau|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, asappurisau dhammakathikau hauti| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ahaṃ khaumhi dhammakathikau, imai panañ‍ñai bhikkhū na dhammakathikā’ti| sau taina dhammakathikattaina attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, asappurisadhammau| sappurisau ca khau, bhikkhavai, iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘na khau dhammakathikattaina laubhadhammā vā  parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, dausadhammā vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, mauhadhammā vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti| nau caipi dhammakathikau hauti; sau ca hauti dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau anudhammacārī, sau tattha puj‍jau, sau tattha pāsaṃsau’ti| sau paṭipadaṃyaiva antaraṃ karitvā taina dhammakathikattaina naivattānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, sappurisadhammau|

107. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, asappurisau ārañ‍ñikau hauti| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ahaṃ khaumhi ārañ‍ñikau imai panañ‍ñai bhikkhū na ārañ‍ñikā’ti| sau taina ārañ‍ñikattaina attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, asappurisadhammau| sappurisau ca khau, bhikkhavai, iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘na khau ārañ‍ñikattaina laubhadhammā vā  parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, dausadhammā vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, mauhadhammā vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti| nau caipi ārañ‍ñikau hauti; sau ca hauti dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau anudhammacārī, sau tattha puj‍jau, sau tattha pāsaṃsau’ti| sau paṭipadaṃyaiva antaraṃ karitvā taina ārañ‍ñikattaina naivattānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, sappurisadhammau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, asappurisau paṃsukūlikau hauti| sau  iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ahaṃ khaumhi paṃsukūlikau, imai panañ‍ñai bhikkhū na paṃsukūlikā’ti| sau taina paṃsukūlikattaina attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, asappurisadhammau| sappurisau ca khau, bhikkhavai, iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘na khau paṃsukūlikattaina laubhadhammā vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, dausadhammā vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, mauhadhammā vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti| nau caipi paṃsukūlikau hauti; sau ca hauti dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau anudhammacārī, sau tattha puj‍jau, sau tattha pāsaṃsau’ti| sau paṭipadaṃyaiva antaraṃ karitvā taina paṃsukūlikattaina naivattānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, sappurisadhammau|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, asappurisau piṇḍapātikau hauti| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ahaṃ khaumhi piṇḍapātikau, imai panañ‍ñai bhikkhū na piṇḍapātikā’ti| sau taina piṇḍapātikattaina attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, asappurisadhammau| sappurisau ca khau, bhikkhavai, iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘na khau piṇḍapātikattaina laubhadhammā vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, dausadhammā vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, mauhadhammā vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti| nau caipi piṇḍapātikau hauti; sau ca hauti dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau anudhammacārī, sau tattha puj‍jau, sau tattha pāsaṃsau’ti| sau paṭipadaṃyaiva antaraṃ karitvā taina piṇḍapātikattaina naivattānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, sappurisadhammau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, asappurisau rukkhamūlikau hauti| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ahaṃ khaumhi rukkhamūlikau, imai panañ‍ñai bhikkhū na  rukkhamūlikā’ti| sau taina rukkhamūlikattaina attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, asappurisadhammau| sappurisau ca khau, bhikkhavai, iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘na khau rukkhamūlikattaina  laubhadhammā vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, dausadhammā vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, mauhadhammā vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti| nau caipi rukkhamūlikau hauti; sau ca hauti dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau anudhammacārī, sau tattha puj‍jau, sau tattha pāsaṃsau’ti| sau paṭipadaṃyaiva  antaraṃ karitvā taina rukkhamūlikattaina naivattānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, sappurisadhammau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, asappurisau sausānikau hauti…pai॰… abbhaukāsikau hauti… naisaj‍jikau hauti…  yathāsanthatikau hauti… aikāsanikau hauti| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ahaṃ khaumhi aikāsanikau, imai panañ‍ñai bhikkhū na aikāsanikā’ti| sau taina aikāsanikattaina attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, asappurisadhammau| sappurisau ca khau, bhikkhavai, iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘na khau aikāsanikattaina laubhadhammā vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, dausadhammā vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, mauhadhammā vā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti| nau caipi aikāsanikau hauti; sau ca hauti dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau anudhammacārī, sau tattha puj‍jau, sau tattha pāsaṃsau’ti| sau paṭipadaṃyaiva antaraṃ karitvā  taina aikāsanikattaina naivattānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, sappurisadhammau|

108. ‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, asappurisau vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ahaṃ khaumhi paṭhamajjhānasamāpattiyā lābhī, imai panañ‍ñai bhikkhū paṭhamajjhānasamāpattiyā na lābhinau’ti| sau tāya paṭhamajjhānasamāpattiyā attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, asappurisadhammau| sappurisau ca khau, bhikkhavai, iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati  – ‘paṭhamajjhānasamāpattiyāpi khau atammayatā vuttā bhagavatā| yaina yaina hi mañ‍ñanti tatau taṃ hauti añ‍ñathā’ti| sau atammayatañ‍ñaiva  antaraṃ karitvā tāya paṭhamajjhānasamāpattiyā naivattānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, sappurisadhammau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, asappurisau vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ahaṃ khaumhi catutthajjhānasamāpattiyā lābhī, imai panañ‍ñai bhikkhū catutthajjhānasamāpattiyā na lābhinau’ti| sau tāya catutthajjhānasamāpattiyā attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, asappurisadhammau| sappurisau ca khau, bhikkhavai, iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘catutthajjhānasamāpattiyāpi khau atammayatā vuttā bhagavatā| yaina yaina hi mañ‍ñanti tatau taṃ hauti añ‍ñathā’ti| sau atammayatañ‍ñaiva antaraṃ karitvā tāya catutthajjhānasamāpattiyā naivattānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, sappurisadhammau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, asappurisau sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ahaṃ khaumhi ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyā lābhī, imai panañ‍ñai bhikkhū ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyā na lābhinau’ti| sau tāya ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyā attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, asappurisadhammau| sappurisau ca khau, bhikkhavai, iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati  – ‘ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyāpi khau atammayatā vuttā bhagavatā| yaina yaina hi mañ‍ñanti tatau taṃ hauti añ‍ñathā’ti| sau atammayatañ‍ñaiva antaraṃ karitvā tāya ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyā  naivattānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, sappurisadhammau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, asappurisau sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ahaṃ khaumhi viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyā  lābhī, imai panañ‍ñai bhikkhū  viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyā na lābhinau’ti| sau tāya viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyā attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, asappurisadhammau| sappurisau ca khau, bhikkhavai, iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyāpi khau atammayatā vuttā bhagavatā| yaina yaina hi mañ‍ñanti tatau taṃ hauti añ‍ñathā’ti| sau atammayatañ‍ñaiva antaraṃ karitvā tāya viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyā naivattānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, sappurisadhammau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, asappurisau sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ahaṃ khaumhi ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā lābhī, imai panañ‍ñai bhikkhū ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā na lābhinau’ti| sau tāya ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, asappurisadhammau| sappurisau ca khau, bhikkhavai, iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyāpi khau atammayatā vuttā bhagavatā| yaina yaina hi mañ‍ñanti tatau taṃ hauti añ‍ñathā’ti| sau atammayatañ‍ñaiva antaraṃ karitvā tāya ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā naivattānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, sappurisadhammau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, asappurisau sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma  naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ahaṃ khaumhi naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā lābhī, imai panañ‍ñai bhikkhū naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā na lābhinau’ti| sau tāya naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, asappurisadhammau| sappurisau ca khau, bhikkhavai, iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyāpi khau atammayatā vuttā bhagavatā| yaina yaina hi mañ‍ñanti tatau taṃ hauti añ‍ñathā’ti| sau atammayatañ‍ñaiva antaraṃ karitvā tāya naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā naivattānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, sappurisadhammau|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, sappurisau sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| pañ‍ñāya cassa disvā āsavā aikac‍cai āsavā (ka॰) parikkhīṇā haunti| ayaṃ ayaṃ khau (syā॰ kaṃ॰), bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na kiñ‍ci mañ‍ñati, na kuhiñ‍ci mañ‍ñati, na kainaci mañ‍ñatī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

sappurisasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. saivitabbāsaivitabbasuttaṃ

109. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘saivitabbāsaivitabbaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, dhammapariyāyaṃ daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘kāyasamācāraṃpāhaṃ pahaṃ (sabbattha), bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampi; tañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ kāyasamācāraṃ| vacīsamācāraṃpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampi; tañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ vacīsamācāraṃ| manausamācāraṃpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampi; tañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ manausamācāraṃ| cittuppādaṃpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampi; tañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ  cittuppādaṃ| sañ‍ñāpaṭilābhaṃpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampi; tañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ sañ‍ñāpaṭilābhaṃ| diṭṭhipaṭilābhaṃpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampi; tañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ diṭṭhipaṭilābhaṃ| attabhāvapaṭilābhaṃpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampi; tañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ attabhāvapaṭilābha’’nti|

aivaṃ  vuttai āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘imassa khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa, vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa, aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmi|

110. ‘‘‘kāyasamācāraṃpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampi; tañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ kāyasamācāra’nti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā| kiñ‍caitaṃ  paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? yathārūpaṃ, bhantai, kāyasamācāraṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti, aivarūpau kāyasamācārau na saivitabbau; yathārūpañ‍ca khau, bhantai, kāyasamācāraṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, aivarūpau kāyasamācārau saivitabbau|

111. ‘‘kathaṃrūpaṃ, bhantai, kāyasamācāraṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti? idha, bhantai, aikac‍cau pāṇātipātī hauti luddau lauhitapāṇi hatappahatai niviṭṭhau adayāpan‍nau pāṇabhūtaisu; adin‍nādāyī khau pana hauti, yaṃ taṃ parassa paravittūpakaraṇaṃ gāmagataṃ vā arañ‍ñagataṃ vā taṃ adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādātā hauti; kāmaisumicchācārī khau pana hauti, yā tā māturakkhitā piturakkhitā mātāpiturakkhitā bhāturakkhitā bhaginirakkhitā ñātirakkhitā gauttarakkhitā dhammarakkhitā sassāmikā saparidaṇḍā antamasau mālāguḷaparikkhittāpi tathārūpāsu cārittaṃ āpaj‍jitā hauti – aivarūpaṃ, bhantai, kāyasamācāraṃ  saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti|

‘‘kathaṃrūpaṃ, bhantai, kāyasamācāraṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti? idha, bhantai, aikac‍cau pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti nihitadaṇḍau nihitasatthau, laj‍jī dayāpan‍nau sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī viharati; adin‍nādānaṃ pahāya adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, yaṃ taṃ parassa paravittūpakaraṇaṃ  gāmagataṃ vā arañ‍ñagataṃ vā taṃ nādin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādātā hauti; kāmaisumicchācāraṃ pahāya kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti, yā tā māturakkhitā piturakkhitā mātāpiturakkhitā bhāturakkhitā bhaginirakkhitā ñātirakkhitā gauttarakkhitā dhammarakkhitā sassāmikā saparidaṇḍā antamasau mālāguḷaparikkhittāpi tathārūpāsu na cārittaṃ āpaj‍jitā hauti – aivarūpaṃ, bhantai, kāyasamācāraṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti| ‘kāyasamācāraṃpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampi; tañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ kāyasamācāra’nti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘vacīsamācāraṃpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampi; tañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ vacīsamācāra’nti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? yathārūpaṃ, bhantai, vacīsamācāraṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti, aivarūpau vacīsamācārau na saivitabbau; yathārūpañ‍ca khau, bhantai, vacīsamācāraṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti  aivarūpau vacīsamācārau saivitabbau|

112. ‘‘kathaṃrūpaṃ, bhantai, vacīsamācāraṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti? idha, bhantai, aikac‍cau musāvādī hauti, sabhāgatau sabhaggatau (bahūsu) vā parisāgatau parisaggatau (bahūsu) vā ñātimajjhagatau  vā pūgamajjhagatau vā rājakulamajjhagatau vā abhinītau sakkhipuṭṭhau – ‘aihambhau purisa, yaṃ jānāsi taṃ vadaihī’ti sau ajānaṃ vā āha – ‘jānāmī’ti, jānaṃ vā āha – ‘na jānāmī’ti; apassaṃ vā āha – ‘passāmī’ti, passaṃ vā āha – ‘na passāmī’ti – iti passa ma॰ ni॰ 1.440 sālaiyyakasuttai attahaitu vā parahaitu vā āmisakiñ‍cikkhahaitu kiñ‍cakkhahaitu (sī॰) vā sampajānamusā bhāsitā hauti; pisuṇavācau khau pana hauti, itau sutvā amutra akkhātā imaisaṃ bhaidāya, amutra vā sutvā imaisaṃ akkhātā amūsaṃ bhaidāya – iti samaggānaṃ vā bhaittā, bhin‍nānaṃ vā anuppadātā, vaggārāmau, vaggaratau, vagganandī, vaggakaraṇiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā  hauti; pharusavācau khau pana hauti, yā sā vācā kaṇḍakā kak‍kasā pharusā parakaṭukā parābhisaj‍janī kaudhasāmantā asamādhisaṃvattanikā, tathārūpiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti; samphappalāpī khau pana hauti akālavādī abhūtavādī anatthavādī adhammavādī avinayavādī, anidhānavatiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti akālaina anapadaisaṃ apariyantavatiṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ – aivarūpaṃ, bhantai, vacīsamācāraṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti|

‘‘kathaṃrūpaṃ, bhantai, vacīsamācāraṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti? idha, bhantai, aikac‍cau musāvādaṃ pahāya musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti sabhāgatau vā parisāgatau vā ñātimajjhagatau vā pūgamajjhagatau vā rājakulamajjhagatau vā abhinītau sakkhipuṭṭhau – ‘aihambhau purisa, yaṃ jānāsi taṃ vadaihī’ti sau ajānaṃ vā āha – ‘na jānāmī’ti, jānaṃ vā āha – ‘jānāmī’ti, apassaṃ vā āha – ‘na passāmī’ti, passaṃ vā āha – ‘passāmī’ti  – iti attahaitu vā parahaitu vā āmisakiñ‍cikkhahaitu vā na sampajānamusā bhāsitā hauti; pisuṇaṃ vācaṃ pahāya pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, itau sutvā na amutra akkhātā imaisaṃ bhaidāya, amutra  vā sutvā na imaisaṃ akkhātā amūsaṃ bhaidāya – iti bhin‍nānaṃ vā sandhātā sahitānaṃ vā anuppadātā samaggārāmau samaggaratau samagganandī samaggakaraṇiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti; pharusaṃ vācaṃ pahāya pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, yā sā vācā nailā kaṇṇasukhā paimanīyā hadayaṅgamā paurī bahujanakantā bahujanamanāpā tathārūpiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti; samphappalāpaṃ pahāya samphappalāpā paṭiviratau hauti kālavādī bhūtavādī atthavādī dhammavādī vinayavādī, nidhānavatiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti kālaina sāpadaisaṃ pariyantavatiṃ atthasaṃhitaṃ – aivarūpaṃ, bhantai, vacīsamācāraṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti| ‘vacīsamācāraṃpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampi; tañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ vacīsamācāra’nti  – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘manausamācāraṃpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampi; tañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ manausamācāra’nti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? yathārūpaṃ, bhantai, manausamācāraṃ saivatau akusalā  dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti aivarūpau manausamācārau na saivitabbau; yathārūpañ‍ca khau, bhantai, manausamācāraṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti aivarūpau manausamācārau saivitabbau|

113. ‘‘kathaṃrūpaṃ, bhantai, manausamācāraṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti? idha, bhantai, aikac‍cau abhijjhālu hauti, yaṃ taṃ parassa paravittūpakaraṇaṃ taṃ abhijjhātā hauti – ‘ahau vata yaṃ parassa taṃ mamassā’ti; byāpan‍nacittau khau pana hauti paduṭṭhamanasaṅkappau  – ‘imai sattā hañ‍ñantu vā vajjhantu vā ucchij‍jantu vā vinassantu vā mā vā ahaisu’nti – aivarūpaṃ, bhantai, manausamācāraṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti|

‘‘kathaṃrūpaṃ, bhantai, manausamācāraṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti? idha, bhantai, aikac‍cau anabhijjhālu hauti, yaṃ taṃ parassa paravittūpakaraṇaṃ taṃ nābhijjhātā hauti – ‘ahau vata yaṃ parassa taṃ mamassā’ti; abyāpan‍nacittau khau  pana hauti appaduṭṭhamanasaṅkappau  – ‘imai sattā avairā abyābajjhā abyāpajjhā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) anīghā sukhī attānaṃ pariharantū’ti – aivarūpaṃ, bhantai, manausamācāraṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti| ‘manausamācāraṃpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampi; tañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ manausamācāra’nti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

114. ‘‘‘cittuppādaṃpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampi; tañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ cittuppāda’nti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? yathārūpaṃ, bhantai, cittuppādaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti aivarūpau cittuppādau na saivitabbau; yathārūpañ‍ca khau, bhantai, cittuppādaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti aivarūpau cittuppādau saivitabbau|

‘‘kathaṃrūpaṃ, bhantai, cittuppādaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti? idha, bhantai, aikac‍cau abhijjhālu hauti, abhijjhāsahagataina  caitasā viharati; byāpādavā hauti, byāpādasahagataina caitasā viharati; vihaisavā hauti, vihaisāsahagataina caitasā viharati – aivarūpaṃ, bhantai, cittuppādaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti|

‘‘kathaṃrūpaṃ, bhantai, cittuppādaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā  dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti? idha, bhantai, aikac‍cau anabhijjhālu hauti, anabhijjhāsahagataina caitasā viharati; abyāpādavā hauti, abyāpādasahagataina caitasā viharati; avihaisavā hauti, avihaisāsahagataina caitasā viharati – aivarūpaṃ, bhantai, cittuppādaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti| ‘cittuppādaṃpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampi; tañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ cittuppāda’nti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

115. ‘‘‘sañ‍ñāpaṭilābhaṃpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampi; tañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ sañ‍ñāpaṭilābha’nti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā| kiñ‍caitaṃ  paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? yathārūpaṃ, bhantai, sañ‍ñāpaṭilābhaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti aivarūpau sañ‍ñāpaṭilābhau na saivitabbau; yathārūpañ‍ca khau, bhantai, sañ‍ñāpaṭilābhaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti aivarūpau sañ‍ñāpaṭilābhau saivitabbau|

‘‘kathaṃrūpaṃ, bhantai, sañ‍ñāpaṭilābhaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti? idha, bhantai, aikac‍cau abhijjhālu hauti, abhijjhāsahagatāya sañ‍ñāya viharati; byāpādavā hauti, byāpādasahagatāya sañ‍ñāya viharati; vihaisavā hauti, vihaisāsahagatāya sañ‍ñāya viharati – aivarūpaṃ, bhantai, sañ‍ñāpaṭilābhaṃ saivatau  akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti|

‘‘kathaṃrūpaṃ, bhantai, sañ‍ñāpaṭilābhaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti? idha, bhantai, aikac‍cau anabhijjhālu hauti, anabhijjhāsahagatāya sañ‍ñāya viharati; abyāpādavā hauti, abyāpādasahagatāya sañ‍ñāya viharati; avihaisavā hauti, avihaisāsahagatāya sañ‍ñāya viharati – aivarūpaṃ, bhantai, sañ‍ñāpaṭilābhaṃ saivatau  akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti| ‘sañ‍ñāpaṭilābhaṃpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampi; tañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ sañ‍ñāpaṭilābha’nti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

116. ‘‘‘diṭṭhipaṭilābhaṃpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampi; tañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ diṭṭhipaṭilābha’nti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? yathārūpaṃ, bhantai, diṭṭhipaṭilābhaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti aivarūpau diṭṭhipaṭilābhau na saivitabbau; yathārūpañ‍ca khau, bhantai, diṭṭhipaṭilābhaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti – aivarūpau diṭṭhipaṭilābhau saivitabbau|

‘‘kathaṃrūpaṃ, bhantai, diṭṭhipaṭilābhaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti? idha, bhantai, aikac‍cau aivaṃdiṭṭhikau hauti – ‘natthi din‍naṃ, natthi yiṭṭhaṃ, natthi hutaṃ, natthi sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ  kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau, natthi ayaṃ laukau, natthi parau laukau, natthi mātā, natthi pitā, natthi sattā aupapātikā, natthi laukai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā sammāpaṭipan‍nā yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaintī’ti – aivarūpaṃ, bhantai, diṭṭhipaṭilābhaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti|

‘‘kathaṃrūpaṃ, bhantai, diṭṭhipaṭilābhaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti? idha, bhantai, aikac‍cau aivaṃdiṭṭhikau hauti – ‘atthi din‍naṃ, atthi yiṭṭhaṃ, atthi hutaṃ, atthi sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau, atthi ayaṃ laukau, atthi parau laukau, atthi mātā, atthi pitā, atthi sattā aupapātikā, atthi laukai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā sammāpaṭipan‍nā yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaintī’ti – aivarūpaṃ, bhantai, diṭṭhipaṭilābhaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti| ‘diṭṭhipaṭilābhaṃpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampi; tañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ diṭṭhipaṭilābha’nti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

117. ‘‘‘attabhāvapaṭilābhaṃpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampi; tañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ attabhāvapaṭilābha’nti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? yathārūpaṃ, bhantai, attabhāvapaṭilābhaṃ  saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti – aivarūpau attabhāvapaṭilābhau na saivitabbau; yathārūpañ‍ca khau, bhantai, attabhāvapaṭilābhaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti – aivarūpau attabhāvapaṭilābhau saivitabbau|

‘‘kathaṃrūpaṃ, bhantai, attabhāvapaṭilābhaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti? sabyābajjhaṃ sabyāpajjhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰), bhantai, attabhāvapaṭilābhaṃ abhinibbattayatau apariniṭṭhitabhāvāya akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti; abyābajjhaṃ, bhantai, attabhāvapaṭilābhaṃ abhinibbattayatau pariniṭṭhitabhāvāya akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti| ‘attabhāvapaṭilābhaṃpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampi; tañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ attabhāvapaṭilābha’nti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘imassa khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa, vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa, aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmī’’ti|

118. ‘‘sādhu sādhu, sāriputta! sādhu khau tvaṃ, sāriputta, imassa mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa, vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa, aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāsi|

‘‘‘kāyasamācāraṃpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi  – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampi; tañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ kāyasamācāra’nti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ mayā| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? yathārūpaṃ, sāriputta, kāyasamācāraṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti aivarūpau kāyasamācārau na saivitabbau; yathārūpañ‍ca khau, sāriputta, kāyasamācāraṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti – aivarūpau kāyasamācārau saivitabbau|

‘‘kathaṃrūpaṃ, sāriputta, kāyasamācāraṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti? idha, sāriputta, aikac‍cau pāṇātipātī hauti  luddau lauhitapāṇi hatappahatai niviṭṭhau adayāpan‍nau pāṇabhūtaisu; adin‍nādāyī khau pana hauti, yaṃ taṃ parassa paravittūpakaraṇaṃ gāmagataṃ vā arañ‍ñagataṃ vā taṃ adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādātā hauti; kāmaisumicchācārī khau pana hauti, yā tā māturakkhitā piturakkhitā mātāpiturakkhitā bhāturakkhitā bhaginirakkhitā ñātirakkhitā gauttarakkhitā dhammarakkhitā sassāmikā saparidaṇḍā antamasau mālāguḷaparikkhittāpi tathārūpāsu cārittaṃ āpaj‍jitā hauti – aivarūpaṃ, sāriputta, kāyasamācāraṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti|

‘‘kathaṃrūpaṃ, sāriputta, kāyasamācāraṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti? idha, sāriputta, aikac‍cau pāṇātipātaṃ  pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti  nihitadaṇḍau nihitasatthau, laj‍jī dayāpan‍nau sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī viharati; adin‍nādānaṃ pahāya adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, yaṃ taṃ parassa paravittūpakaraṇaṃ gāmagataṃ vā arañ‍ñagataṃ vā taṃ nādin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādātā hauti; kāmaisumicchācāraṃ pahāya kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti, yā tā māturakkhitā piturakkhitā mātāpiturakkhitā bhāturakkhitā bhaginirakkhitā ñātirakkhitā gauttarakkhitā dhammarakkhitā sassāmikā saparidaṇḍā antamasau mālāguḷaparikkhittāpi tathārūpāsu na cārittaṃ āpaj‍jitā hauti – aivarūpaṃ, sāriputta, kāyasamācāraṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti| ‘kāyasamācāraṃpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampi; tañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ kāyasamācāra’nti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ mayā idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘vacīsamācāraṃpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi  …pai॰… manausamācāraṃpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi…pai॰… cittuppādaṃpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi…pai॰… sañ‍ñāpaṭilābhaṃpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi…pai॰… diṭṭhipaṭilābhaṃpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘‘attabhāvapaṭilābhaṃpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampi; tañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ attabhāvapaṭilābha’nti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ mayā| kiñ‍caitaṃ  paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? yathārūpaṃ, sāriputta, attabhāvapaṭilābhaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti aivarūpau attabhāvapaṭilābhau na saivitabbau; yathārūpañ‍ca khau, sāriputta, attabhāvapaṭilābhaṃ  saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti – aivarūpau attabhāvapaṭilābhau saivitabbau|

‘‘kathaṃrūpaṃ, sāriputta, attabhāvapaṭilābhaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti? sabyābajjhaṃ, sāriputta, attabhāvapaṭilābhaṃ abhinibbattayatau apariniṭṭhitabhāvāya akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti; abyābajjhaṃ, sāriputta, attabhāvapaṭilābhaṃ abhinibbattayatau pariniṭṭhitabhāvāya akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti| ‘attabhāvapaṭilābhaṃpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampi; tañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ attabhāvapaṭilābha’nti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ mayā idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ| imassa khau, sāriputta, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthau daṭṭhabbau|

119. ‘‘cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ rūpaṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampi; sautaviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ saddaṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi asaivitabbampi; ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ gandhaṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampi; jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ rasaṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampi; kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ  phauṭṭhabbaṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampi; manauviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ dhammaṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘imassa khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa, vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa, aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmi| ‘cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ rūpaṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta, duvidhaina  vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampī’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? yathārūpaṃ, bhantai, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ rūpaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti aivarūpaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ rūpaṃ na saivitabbaṃ; yathārūpañ‍ca khau, bhantai, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ rūpaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti aivarūpaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ rūpaṃ saivitabbaṃ| ‘cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ  rūpaṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampī’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘sautaviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ saddaṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta…pai॰… aivarūpau sautaviñ‍ñaiyyau saddau na saivitabbau… aivarūpau sautaviñ‍ñaiyyau saddau saivitabbau… aivarūpau ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyau gandhau na saivitabbau… aivarūpau  ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyau gandhau saivitabbau… aivarūpau jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyau rasau na saivitabbau… aivarūpau  jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyau rasau saivitabbau… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ phauṭṭhabbaṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta … aivarūpau kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyau phauṭṭhabbau na saivitabbau… aivarūpau kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyau phauṭṭhabbau saivitabbau|

‘‘‘manauviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ dhammaṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampī’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? yathārūpaṃ, bhantai, manauviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ dhammaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti aivarūpau  manauviñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau na saivitabbau; yathārūpañ‍ca khau, bhantai, manauviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ dhammaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti aivarūpau manauviñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau saivitabbau| ‘manauviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ dhammaṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampī’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ| imassa khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa, vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa, aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmī’’ti|

120. ‘‘sādhu sādhu, sāriputta! sādhu khau tvaṃ, sāriputta, imassa mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa, vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa, aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāsi| ‘cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ rūpaṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampī’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ mayā| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? yathārūpaṃ, sāriputta, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ rūpaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti aivarūpaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ rūpaṃ na saivitabbaṃ; yathārūpañ‍ca khau, sāriputta, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ rūpaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā  abhivaḍḍhanti aivarūpaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ rūpaṃ saivitabbaṃ| ‘cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ rūpaṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampī’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ mayā idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘sautaviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ  saddaṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta…pai॰… aivarūpau sautaviñ‍ñaiyyau saddau na saivitabbau… aivarūpau sautaviñ‍ñaiyyau saddau saivitabbau… aivarūpau ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyau gandhau na saivitabbau… aivarūpau ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyau gandhau saivitabbau… aivarūpau jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyau rasau na saivitabbau… aivarūpau jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyau rasau saivitabbau… aivarūpau kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyau phauṭṭhabbau na saivitabbau… aivarūpau kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyau phauṭṭhabbau saivitabbau|

‘‘manauviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ  dhammaṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta…pai॰… aivarūpau manauviñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau na saivitabbau… aivarūpau manauviñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau saivitabbau| ‘manauviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ dhammaṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampī’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ mayā idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ| imassa khau, sāriputta, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthau daṭṭhabbau|

121. ‘‘cīvaraṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampi…pai॰… piṇḍapātaṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta… saināsanaṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta… gāmaṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta… nigamaṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta… nagaraṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta… janapadaṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta… puggalaṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampī’’ti|

aivaṃ  vuttai, āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘imassa khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa, vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa, aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmi| ‘cīvaraṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampī’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? yathārūpaṃ, bhantai, cīvaraṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti aivarūpaṃ cīvaraṃ na saivitabbaṃ; yathārūpañ‍ca khau, bhantai, cīvaraṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti aivarūpaṃ cīvaraṃ saivitabbaṃ| ‘cīvaraṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampī’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘piṇḍapātaṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta…pai॰… aivarūpau piṇḍapātau na saivitabbau… aivarūpau piṇḍapātau  saivitabbau… saināsanaṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta…pai॰… aivarūpaṃ saināsanaṃ na saivitabbaṃ… aivarūpaṃ saināsanaṃ saivitabbaṃ… gāmaṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta  …pai॰… aivarūpau gāmau na saivitabbau… aivarūpau gāmau saivitabbau… aivarūpau nigamau na saivitabbau… aivarūpau nigamau saivitabbau… aivarūpaṃ nagaraṃ na saivitabbaṃ… aivarūpaṃ nagaraṃ saivitabbaṃ… aivarūpau janapadau na saivitabbau… aivarūpau janapadau saivitabbau|

‘‘‘puggalaṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampī’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? yathārūpaṃ, bhantai, puggalaṃ saivatau akusalā  dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti aivarūpau puggalau na saivitabbau; yathārūpañ‍ca khau, bhantai, puggalaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti aivarūpau puggalau saivitabbau| ‘puggalaṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampī’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttanti| imassa khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa, vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmī’’ti|

122. ‘‘sādhu sādhu, sāriputta! sādhu khau tvaṃ, sāriputta, imassa mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa, vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāsi| ‘cīvaraṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampī’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ mayā| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca  vuttaṃ? yathārūpaṃ, sāriputta, cīvaraṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti aivarūpaṃ cīvaraṃ na saivitabbaṃ; yathārūpañ‍ca khau, sāriputta, cīvaraṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti aivarūpaṃ cīvaraṃ saivitabbaṃ| ‘cīvaraṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampī’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ mayā idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ| (yathā paṭhamaṃ tathā vitthāraitabbaṃ) aivarūpau piṇḍapātau… aivarūpaṃ saināsanaṃ… aivarūpau gāmau… aivarūpau nigamau… aivarūpaṃ nagaraṃ… aivarūpau janapadau|

‘‘‘puggalaṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampī’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ mayā| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? yathārūpaṃ, sāriputta, puggalaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti aivarūpau puggalau na saivitabbau; yathārūpañ‍ca khau, sāriputta, puggalaṃ saivatau  akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti aivarūpau puggalau saivitabbau| ‘puggalaṃpāhaṃ, sāriputta, duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbampi, asaivitabbampī’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ mayā idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ| imassa khau, sāriputta, mayā  saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthau daṭṭhabbau|

123. ‘‘sabbaipi cai, sāriputta, khattiyā imassa mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānaiyyuṃ, sabbaisānampissa khattiyānaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya| sabbaipi cai, sāriputta, brāhmaṇā…pai॰… sabbaipi cai, sāriputta, vaissā… sabbaipi cai, sāriputta, suddā imassa mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānaiyyuṃ, sabbaisānampissa suddānaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya| sadaivakaupi cai, sāriputta, laukau samārakau sabrahmakau sassamaṇabrāhmaṇī pajā sadaivamanussā imassa mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānaiyya, sadaivakassapissa laukassa samārakassa sabrahmakassa sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’ti|

idamavauca  bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

saivitabbāsaivitabbasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. bahudhātukasuttaṃ

124. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘yāni kānici, bhikkhavai, bhayāni uppaj‍janti sabbāni tāni bālatau uppaj‍janti, nau paṇḍitatau; yai kaici upaddavā uppaj‍janti sabbai tai bālatau uppaj‍janti, nau paṇḍitatau; yai kaici upasaggā uppaj‍janti sabbai tai bālatau uppaj‍janti, nau paṇḍitatau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, naḷāgārā vā tiṇāgārā vā aggi muttau aggimuk‍kau (sī॰ pī॰) kūṭāgārānipi dahati ul‍littāvalittāni nivātāni phusitaggaḷāni pihitavātapānāni; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yāni kānici bhayāni uppaj‍janti sabbāni tāni bālatau uppaj‍janti, nau paṇḍitatau; yai kaici upaddavā uppaj‍janti sabbai tai bālatau uppaj‍janti, nau paṇḍitatau; yai kaici upasaggā uppaj‍janti sabbai tai bālatau uppaj‍janti, nau paṇḍitatau| iti khau, bhikkhavai, sappaṭibhayau bālau, appaṭibhayau paṇḍitau; saupaddavau bālau, anupaddavau paṇḍitau; saupasaggau bālau, anupasaggau paṇḍitau| natthi, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitatau bhayaṃ, natthi paṇḍitatau upaddavau, natthi paṇḍitatau upasaggau| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘paṇḍitā bhavissāma vīmaṃsakā’ti – aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti|

aivaṃ  vuttai, āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, paṇḍitau bhikkhu ‘vīmaṃsakau’ti alaṃ vacanāyā’’ti? ‘‘yatau khau, ānanda, bhikkhu dhātukusalau ca hauti, āyatanakusalau ca hauti, paṭic‍casamuppādakusalau ca hauti, ṭhānāṭhānakusalau ca hauti – aittāvatā khau, ānanda, paṇḍitau bhikkhu ‘vīmaṃsakau’ti alaṃ vacanāyā’’ti|

125. ‘‘kittāvatā pana, bhantai, ‘dhātukusalau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāyā’’ti? ‘‘aṭṭhārasa  khau imā, ānanda, dhātuyau – cakkhudhātu, rūpadhātu, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātu; sautadhātu, saddadhātu, sautaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu; ghānadhātu, gandhadhātu, ghānaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu; jivhādhātu, rasadhātu, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇadhātu; kāyadhātu, phauṭṭhabbadhātu, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu; manaudhātu, dhammadhātu, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu| imā khau, ānanda, aṭṭhārasa dhātuyau yatau jānāti passati – aittāvatāpi khau, ānanda, ‘dhātukusalau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāyā’’ti|

‘‘siyā pana, bhantai, añ‍ñaupi pariyāyau, yathā ‘dhātukusalau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāyā’’ti? ‘‘siyā, ānanda| chayimā, ānanda, dhātuyau – pathavīdhātu, āpaudhātu, taijaudhātu, vāyaudhātu, ākāsadhātu, viñ‍ñāṇadhātu| imā khau, ānanda, cha dhātuyau yatau jānāti  passati – aittāvatāpi khau, ānanda, ‘dhātukusalau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāyā’’ti|

‘‘siyā pana, bhantai, añ‍ñaupi pariyāyau, yathā ‘dhātukusalau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāyā’’ti? ‘‘siyā, ānanda| chayimā, ānanda, dhātuyau – sukhadhātu, dukkhadhātu, saumanassadhātu, daumanassadhātu, upaikkhādhātu, avij‍jādhātu| imā khau, ānanda, cha dhātuyau yatau jānāti passati – aittāvatāpi khau, ānanda, ‘dhātukusalau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāyā’’ti|

‘‘siyā pana, bhantai, añ‍ñaupi pariyāyau, yathā ‘dhātukusalau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāyā’’ti? ‘‘siyā, ānanda| chayimā, ānanda, dhātuyau – kāmadhātu, naikkhammadhātu, byāpādadhātu, abyāpādadhātu, vihiṃsādhātu, avihiṃsādhātu| imā khau, ānanda, cha dhātuyau yatau jānāti passati – aittāvatāpi khau, ānanda, ‘dhātukusalau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāyā’’ti|

‘‘siyā pana, bhantai, añ‍ñaupi pariyāyau, yathā ‘dhātukusalau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāyā’’ti? ‘‘siyā, ānanda| tissau imā, ānanda, dhātuyau – kāmadhātu, rūpadhātu, arūpadhātu| imā khau, ānanda, tissau dhātuyau yatau jānāti passati – aittāvatāpi khau, ānanda, ‘dhātukusalau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāyā’’ti|

‘‘siyā  pana, bhantai, añ‍ñaupi pariyāyau, yathā ‘dhātukusalau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāyā’’ti? ‘‘siyā, ānanda| dvai imā, ānanda, dhātuyau – saṅkhatādhātu, asaṅkhatādhātu| imā khau, ānanda, dvai dhātuyau yatau jānāti passati – aittāvatāpi khau, ānanda, ‘dhātukusalau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāyā’’ti|

126. ‘‘kittāvatā pana, bhantai, ‘āyatanakusalau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāyā’’ti? ‘‘cha khau panimāni, ānanda, ajjhattikabāhirāni āyatanāni – cakkhucaiva  rūpā ca sautañ‍ca saddā ca ghānañ‍ca gandhā ca jivhā ca rasā ca kāyau ca phauṭṭhabbā ca manau ca dhammā ca| imāni khau, ānanda, cha ajjhattikabāhirāni āyatanāni yatau jānāti passati – aittāvatā khau, ānanda, ‘āyatanakusalau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāyā’’ti|

‘‘kittāvatā  pana, bhantai, ‘paṭic‍casamuppādakusalau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāyā’’ti? ‘‘idhānanda, bhikkhu aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘imasmiṃ sati idaṃ hauti, imassuppādā idaṃ uppaj‍jati, imasmiṃ asati idaṃ na hauti, imassa niraudhā idaṃ nirujjhati, yadidaṃ – avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ, saḷāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ, upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassūpāyāsā sambhavanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti| avij‍jāyatvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau, saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhā nāmarūpaniraudhau, nāmarūpaniraudhā saḷāyatananiraudhau, saḷāyatananiraudhā phassaniraudhau, phassaniraudhā vaidanāniraudhau, vaidanāniraudhā taṇhāniraudhau, taṇhāniraudhā upādānaniraudhau, upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau, bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhau, jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassūpāyāsā  nirujjhanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti’| aittāvatā khau, ānanda, ‘paṭic‍casamuppādakusalau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāyā’’ti|

127. ‘‘kittāvatā pana, bhantai, ‘ṭhānāṭhānakusalau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāyā’’ti? ‘‘idhānanda, bhikkhu ‘aṭṭhānamaitaṃ anavakāsau yaṃ diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau kañ‍ci kiñ‍ci (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) saṅkhāraṃ nic‍catau upagacchaiyya, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti; ‘ṭhānañ‍ca khau aitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ puthuj‍janau kañ‍ci  saṅkhāraṃ nic‍catau upagacchaiyya, ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti; ‘aṭṭhānamaitaṃ anavakāsau yaṃ diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau kañ‍ci saṅkhāraṃ sukhatau upagacchaiyya, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti; ‘ṭhānañ‍ca khau aitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ puthuj‍janau kañ‍ci saṅkhāraṃ sukhatau upagacchaiyya, ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti| ‘aṭṭhānamaitaṃ anavakāsau yaṃ diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau kañ‍ci dhammaṃ attatau upagacchaiyya, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti, ‘ṭhānañ‍ca khau aitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ puthuj‍janau kañ‍ci dhammaṃ attatau upagacchaiyya, ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti|

128. ‘‘‘aṭṭhānamaitaṃ  anavakāsau yaṃ diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau mātaraṃ jīvitā vauraupaiyya, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti; ‘ṭhānañ‍ca khau aitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ puthuj‍janau mātaraṃ jīvitā vauraupaiyya, ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti| ‘aṭṭhānamaitaṃ anavakāsau yaṃ diṭṭhisampan‍nau  puggalau pitaraṃ jīvitā vauraupaiyya…pai॰…  arahantaṃ jīvitā vauraupaiyya, ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti; ‘aṭṭhānamaitaṃ anavakāsau yaṃ diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau duṭṭhacittau tathāgatassa lauhitaṃ uppādaiyya, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti; ‘ṭhānañ‍ca khau aitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ puthuj‍janau duṭṭhacittau tathāgatassa lauhitaṃ uppādaiyya, ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti| ‘aṭṭhānamaitaṃ anavakāsau yaṃ diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau saṅghaṃ bhindaiyya, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti; ‘ṭhānañ‍ca khau aitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ puthuj‍janau saṅghaṃ bhindaiyya, ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti| ‘aṭṭhānamaitaṃ anavakāsau yaṃ diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau añ‍ñaṃ satthāraṃ uddisaiyya, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti; ‘ṭhānañ‍ca khau aitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ puthuj‍janau añ‍ñaṃ satthāraṃ uddisaiyya, ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti|

129. ‘‘‘aṭṭhānamaitaṃ anavakāsau yaṃ aikissā laukadhātuyā dvai arahantau sammāsambuddhā apubbaṃ acarimaṃ uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti; ‘ṭhānañ‍ca khau aitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ aikissā laukadhātuyā aikau arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau uppaj‍jaiyya, ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti| ‘aṭṭhānamaitaṃ anavakāsau yaṃ aikissā laukadhātuyā dvai rājānau cak‍kavattinau apubbaṃ acarimaṃ uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti; ‘ṭhānañ‍ca khau aitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ aikissā laukadhātuyā aikau rājā cak‍kavattī uppaj‍jaiyya, ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti|

130. ‘‘‘aṭṭhānamaitaṃ  anavakāsau yaṃ itthī arahaṃ assa sammāsambuddhau, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti; ‘ṭhānañ‍ca khau aitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ purisau arahaṃ assa sammāsambuddhau, ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti| ‘aṭṭhānamaitaṃ anavakāsau yaṃ itthī rājā assa cak‍kavattī, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti; ‘ṭhānañ‍ca khau aitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ purisau rājā assa cak‍kavattī, ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti| ‘aṭṭhānamaitaṃ anavakāsau yaṃ itthī sak‍kattaṃ karaiyya … mārattaṃ karaiyya… brahmattaṃ karaiyya, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti; ‘ṭhānañ‍ca khau aitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ purisau sak‍kattaṃ  karaiyya… mārattaṃ karaiyya… brahmattaṃ karaiyya, ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti|

131. ‘‘‘aṭṭhānamaitaṃ anavakāsau yaṃ kāyaduc‍caritassa iṭṭhau kantau manāpau vipākau nibbattaiyya, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti; ‘ṭhānañ‍ca khau aitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ kāyaduc‍caritassa aniṭṭhau akantau amanāpau vipākau nibbattaiyya, ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti| ‘aṭṭhānamaitaṃ anavakāsau yaṃ vacīduc‍caritassa…pai॰… yaṃ manauduc‍caritassa iṭṭhau kantau manāpau vipākau nibbattaiyya, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti; ṭhānañ‍ca khau aitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ vacīduc‍caritassa…pai॰… yaṃ manauduc‍caritassa aniṭṭhau akantau amanāpau vipākau nibbattaiyya, ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatīti pajānāti| ‘aṭṭhānamaitaṃ anavakāsau yaṃ kāyasucaritassa aniṭṭhau akantau amanāpau vipākau nibbattaiyya, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti; ‘ṭhānañ‍ca khau aitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ kāyasucaritassa  iṭṭhau kantau manāpau vipākau nibbattaiyya, ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti| ‘aṭṭhānamaitaṃ anavakāsau yaṃ vacīsucaritassa…pai॰… yaṃ manausucaritassa aniṭṭhau akantau amanāpau vipākau nibbattaiyya, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti; ‘ṭhānañ‍ca khau aitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ vacīsucaritassa…pai॰… yaṃ manausucaritassa iṭṭhau kantau manāpau vipākau nibbattaiyya, ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti|

‘‘‘aṭṭhānamaitaṃ anavakāsau yaṃ kāyaduc‍caritasamaṅgī taṃnidānā tappac‍cayā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jaiyya, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti; ‘ṭhānañ‍ca khau aitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ kāyaduc‍caritasamaṅgī  taṃnidānā tappac‍cayā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jaiyya, ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti| ‘aṭṭhānamaitaṃ anavakāsau  yaṃ vacīduc‍caritasamaṅgī…pai॰… yaṃ manauduc‍caritasamaṅgī taṃnidānā tappac‍cayā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jaiyya, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti; ‘ṭhānañ‍ca khau aitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ vacīduc‍caritasamaṅgī…pai॰… yaṃ manauduc‍caritasamaṅgī taṃnidānā tappac‍cayā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jaiyya, ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti| ‘aṭṭhānamaitaṃ anavakāsau yaṃ kāyasucaritasamaṅgī taṃnidānā tappac‍cayā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ  vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jaiyya, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti; ‘ṭhānañ‍ca khau aitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ kāyasucaritasamaṅgī taṃnidānā tappac‍cayā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ  saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jaiyya, ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti| ‘aṭṭhānamaitaṃ anavakāsau yaṃ vacīsucaritasamaṅgī…pai॰… yaṃ manausucaritasamaṅgī taṃnidānā tappac‍cayā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jaiyya, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti; ‘ṭhānañ‍ca khau aitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ vacīsucaritasamaṅgī…pai॰… yaṃ manausucaritasamaṅgī taṃnidānā tappac‍cayā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jaiyya, ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’ti pajānāti| aittāvatā khau, ānanda, ‘ṭhānāṭhānakusalau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāyā’’ti|

132. aivaṃ vuttai āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai! kaunāmau ayaṃ, bhantai, dhammapariyāyau’’ti? ‘‘tasmātiha tvaṃ, ānanda, imaṃ dhammapariyāyaṃ ‘bahudhātukau’tipi naṃ dhāraihi, ‘catuparivaṭṭau’tipi naṃ dhāraihi, ‘dhammādāsau’tipi naṃ dhāraihi, ‘amatadundubhī’tipi dudrabhītipi (ka॰) naṃ dhāraihi, ‘anuttarau saṅgāmavijayau’tipi naṃ dhāraihī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

bahudhātukasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. isigilisuttaṃ

133. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati isigilismiṃ pabbatai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘passatha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, aitaṃ vaibhāraṃ pabbata’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aitassapi khau, bhikkhavai, vaibhārassa pabbatassa añ‍ñāva samañ‍ñā ahausi añ‍ñā pañ‍ñatti’’|

‘‘passatha  nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, aitaṃ paṇḍavaṃ pabbata’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aitassapi khau, bhikkhavai, paṇḍavassa pabbatassa añ‍ñāva samañ‍ñā ahausi añ‍ñā pañ‍ñatti’’|

‘‘passatha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, aitaṃ vaipul‍laṃ pabbata’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aitassapi khau, bhikkhavai, vaipul‍lassa pabbatassa añ‍ñāva samañ‍ñā ahausi añ‍ñā pañ‍ñatti’’|

‘‘passatha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, aitaṃ gijjhakūṭaṃ pabbata’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aitassapi khau, bhikkhavai, gijjhakūṭassa pabbatassa añ‍ñāva samañ‍ñā ahausi añ‍ñā pañ‍ñatti’’|

‘‘passatha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, imaṃ isigiliṃ pabbata’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘imassa khau pana, bhikkhavai, isigilissa pabbatassa aisāva samañ‍ñā ahausi aisā pañ‍ñatti’’|

‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca pac‍caikabuddhasatāni imasmiṃ isigilismiṃ pabbatai ciranivāsinau ahaisuṃ| tai imaṃ pabbataṃ pavisantā dissanti, paviṭṭhā na dissanti| tamainaṃ manussā disvā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘ayaṃ pabbatau imai isī isayau (ka॰) gilatī’ti; ‘isigili isigili’ tvaiva samañ‍ñā udapādi| ācikkhissāmi ācikkhissāmi vau (ka॰), bhikkhavai, pac‍caikabuddhānaṃ nāmāni; kittayissāmi, bhikkhavai, pac‍caikabuddhānaṃ nāmāni; daisaissāmi, bhikkhavai, pac‍caikabuddhānaṃ nāmāni| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

134. ‘‘ariṭṭhau nāma, bhikkhavai, pac‍caikasambuddhau pac‍caikabuddhau (ka॰ sī॰ pī॰) imasmiṃ isigilismiṃ pabbatai ciranivāsī ahausi; upariṭṭhau nāma, bhikkhavai, pac‍caikasambuddhau imasmiṃ isigilismiṃ pabbatai ciranivāsī ahausi; tagarasikhī taggarasikhī (ka॰) nāma, bhikkhavai, pac‍caikasambuddhau imasmiṃ isigilismiṃ pabbatai ciranivāsī ahausi; yasassī nāma, bhikkhavai, pac‍caikasambuddhau imasmiṃ isigilismiṃ pabbatai ciranivāsī ahausi; sudassanau nāma, bhikkhavai, pac‍caikasambuddhau imasmiṃ isigilismiṃ pabbatai ciranivāsī ahausi; piyadassī nāma, bhikkhavai, pac‍caikasambuddhau  imasmiṃ isigilismiṃ pabbatai ciranivāsī ahausi; gandhārau nāma, bhikkhavai, pac‍caikasambuddhau imasmiṃ isigilismiṃ pabbatai ciranivāsī ahausi; piṇḍaulau nāma, bhikkhavai, pac‍caikasambuddhau imasmiṃ isigilismiṃ pabbatai ciranivāsī ahausi; upāsabhau nāma, bhikkhavai, pac‍caikasambuddhau imasmiṃ isigilismiṃ pabbatai ciranivāsī ahausi; nītau nāma, bhikkhavai, pac‍caikasambuddhau imasmiṃ isigilismiṃ pabbatai ciranivāsī ahausi; tathau nāma, bhikkhavai, pac‍caikasambuddhau  imasmiṃ isigilismiṃ pabbatai ciranivāsī ahausi, sutavā nāma, bhikkhavai, pac‍caikasambuddhau imasmiṃ isigilismiṃ pabbatai ciranivāsī ahausi; bhāvitattau nāma, bhikkhavai, pac‍caikasambuddhau imasmiṃ isigilismiṃ pabbatai ciranivāsī ahausi|

135.

 ‘‘yai sattasārā anīghā nirāsā,

pac‍caikamaivajjhagamaṃsu baudhiṃ pac‍caikamaivajjhagamuṃ subaudhiṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)|

taisaṃ visal‍lāna naruttamānaṃ,

nāmāni mai kittayatau suṇātha||

‘‘ariṭṭhau upariṭṭhau tagarasikhī yasassī,

sudassanau piyadassī ca susambuddhau buddhau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)|

gandhārau piṇḍaulau upāsabhau ca,

nītau tathau sutavā bhāvitattau||

‘‘sumbhau  subhau matulau maithulau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) aṭṭhamau ca,

athassumaighau aṭṭhasumaidhau (ka॰) anīghau sudāṭhau|

pac‍caikabuddhā bhavanaittikhīṇā,

hiṅgū ca hiṅgau ca mahānubhāvā||

‘‘dvai jālinau muninau aṭṭhakau ca,

atha kausal‍lau buddhau athau subāhu|

upanaimisau naimisau santacittau,

sac‍cau tathau virajau paṇḍitau ca||

‘‘kāḷūpakāḷā  vijitau jitau ca,

aṅgau ca paṅgau ca guttijitau ca|

passi jahi upadhidukkhamūlaṃ passī jahī upadhiṃ dukkhamūlaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰),

aparājitau mārabalaṃ ajaisi||

‘‘satthā pavattā sarabhaṅgau laumahaṃsau,

uc‍caṅgamāyau asitau anāsavau|

manaumayau mānacchidau ca bandhumā,

tadādhimuttau vimalau ca kaitumā||

‘‘kaitumbharāgau ca mātaṅgau ariyau,

athac‍cutau ac‍cutagāmabyāmakau|

sumaṅgalau dabbilau supatiṭṭhitau,

asayhau khaimābhiratau ca sauratau||

‘‘duran‍nayau saṅghau athaupi uj‍jayau,

aparau muni sayhau anaumanik‍kamau|

ānandau  nandau upanandau dvādasa,

bhāradvājau antimadaihadhārī antimadaihadhāri (sī॰)||

‘‘baudhi mahānāmau athaupi uttarau,

kaisī sikhī sundarau dvārabhājau|

tissūpatissā  bhavabandhanacchidā,

upasikhi taṇhacchidau ca sikhari upasīdarī taṇhacchidau ca sīdarī (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)||

‘‘buddhau ahu maṅgalau vītarāgau,

usabhacchidā jāliniṃ dukkhamūlaṃ|

santaṃ padaṃ ajjhagamaupanītau,

upausathau sundarau sac‍canāmau||

‘‘jaitau jayantau padumau uppalau ca,

padumuttarau rakkhitau pabbatau ca|

mānatthaddhau  saubhitau vītarāgau,

kaṇhau ca buddhau suvimuttacittau||

‘‘aitai  ca añ‍ñai ca mahānubhāvā,

pac‍caikabuddhā bhavanaittikhīṇā|

tai sabbasaṅgātigatai mahaisī,

parinibbutai vandatha appamaiyyai’’ti||

isigilisuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. mahācattārīsakasuttaṃ

136. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘ariyaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, sammāsamādhiṃ daisaissāmi saupanisaṃ saparikkhāraṃ| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, ariyau sammāsamādhi saupanisau saparikkhārau? saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvācā, sammākammantau, sammāājīvau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati; yā khau, bhikkhavai, imaihi sattahaṅgaihi cittassa aikaggatā parikkhatā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyau sammāsamādhi saupanisau itipi, saparikkhārau itipi| tatra, bhikkhavai, sammādiṭṭhi pubbaṅgamā hauti| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sammādiṭṭhi pubbaṅgamā hauti? micchādiṭṭhiṃ ‘micchādiṭṭhī’ti pajānāti, sammādiṭṭhiṃ ‘sammādiṭṭhī’ti pajānāti – sāssa hauti sammādiṭṭhi|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, micchādiṭṭhi? ‘natthi din‍naṃ, natthi yiṭṭhaṃ, natthi hutaṃ, natthi sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau, natthi  ayaṃ laukau, natthi parau laukau, natthi mātā, natthi pitā, natthi sattā aupapātikā, natthi  laukai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā sammāpaṭipan‍nā yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaintī’ti – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, micchādiṭṭhi|

‘‘katamā  ca, bhikkhavai, sammādiṭṭhi? sammādiṭṭhiṃpahaṃ sammādiṭṭhimahaṃ (ka॰) aivaṃ sammāsaṅkappaṃpahaṃkyādīsupi, bhikkhavai, dvāyaṃ dvayaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) ṭīkā aulaukaitabbā vadāmi – atthi, bhikkhavai, sammādiṭṭhi sāsavā puñ‍ñabhāgiyā upadhivaipak‍kā; atthi, bhikkhavai, sammādiṭṭhi ariyā anāsavā laukuttarā maggaṅgā| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, sammādiṭṭhi sāsavā puñ‍ñabhāgiyā upadhivaipak‍kā? ‘atthi din‍naṃ, atthi yiṭṭhaṃ, atthi hutaṃ, atthi sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau, atthi ayaṃ laukau, atthi parau laukau, atthi mātā, atthi pitā, atthi sattā aupapātikā, atthi laukai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā sammāpaṭipan‍nā yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaintī’ti – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, sammādiṭṭhi sāsavā puñ‍ñabhāgiyā upadhivaipak‍kā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, sammādiṭṭhi ariyā anāsavā laukuttarā maggaṅgā? yā khau, bhikkhavai, ariyacittassa anāsavacittassa ariyamaggasamaṅginau ariyamaggaṃ bhāvayatau pañ‍ñā pañ‍ñindriyaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau sammādiṭṭhi maggaṅgaṃ maggaṅgā (sī॰ pī॰) – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sammādiṭṭhi ariyā anāsavā laukuttarā maggaṅgā| sau micchādiṭṭhiyā pahānāya  vāyamati, sammādiṭṭhiyā, upasampadāya, svāssa svāyaṃ (ka॰) hauti sammāvāyāmau| sau satau micchādiṭṭhiṃ pajahati, satau sammādiṭṭhiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, sāssa sāyaṃ (ka॰) hauti sammāsati| itiyimai itimai (sī॰), itissimai (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) tayau dhammā sammādiṭṭhiṃ anuparidhāvanti anuparivattanti, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati|

137. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, sammādiṭṭhi pubbaṅgamā hauti| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sammādiṭṭhi pubbaṅgamā hauti? micchāsaṅkappaṃ ‘micchāsaṅkappau’ti pajānāti, sammāsaṅkappaṃ ‘sammāsaṅkappau’ti pajānāti, sāssa hauti sammādiṭṭhi|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, micchāsaṅkappau? kāmasaṅkappau, byāpādasaṅkappau, vihiṃsāsaṅkappau – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, micchāsaṅkappau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sammāsaṅkappau? sammāsaṅkappaṃpahaṃ, bhikkhavai, dvāyaṃ vadāmi – atthi, bhikkhavai, sammāsaṅkappau sāsavau puñ‍ñabhāgiyau upadhivaipak‍kau; atthi, bhikkhavai, sammāsaṅkappau ariyau anāsavau laukuttarau maggaṅgau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sammāsaṅkappau sāsavau puñ‍ñabhāgiyau  upadhivaipak‍kau? naikkhammasaṅkappau, abyāpādasaṅkappau, avihiṃsāsaṅkappau – ‘ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, sammāsaṅkappau sāsavau puñ‍ñabhāgiyau upadhivaipak‍kau’’’|

‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, sammāsaṅkappau ariyau anāsavau laukuttarau  maggaṅgau? yau khau, bhikkhavai, ariyacittassa anāsavacittassa ariyamaggasamaṅginau ariyamaggaṃ bhāvayatau tak‍kau vitak‍kau saṅkappau appanā byappanā caitasau abhiniraupanā vacīsaṅkhārau – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, sammāsaṅkappau ariyau anāsavau laukuttarau maggaṅgau| sau micchāsaṅkappassa pahānāya vāyamati, sammāsaṅkappassa upasampadāya, svāssa hauti sammāvāyāmau| sau satau micchāsaṅkappaṃ pajahati, satau sammāsaṅkappaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; sāssa hauti sammāsati| itiyimai tayau dhammā sammāsaṅkappaṃ anuparidhāvanti anuparivattanti, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati|

138. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, sammādiṭṭhi pubbaṅgamā hauti| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sammādiṭṭhi pubbaṅgamā hauti? micchāvācaṃ ‘micchāvācā’ti pajānāti, sammāvācaṃ ‘sammāvācā’ti pajānāti; sāssa hauti sammādiṭṭhi| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, micchāvācā? musāvādau, pisuṇā vācā, pharusā vācā, samphappalāpau – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, micchāvācā| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, sammāvācā? sammāvācaṃpahaṃ, bhikkhavai, dvāyaṃ vadāmi – atthi, bhikkhavai, sammāvācā sāsavā puñ‍ñabhāgiyā upadhivaipak‍kā; atthi, bhikkhavai, sammāvācā ariyā anāsavā laukuttarā maggaṅgā| katamā  ca, bhikkhavai, sammāvācā sāsavā puñ‍ñabhāgiyā upadhivaipak‍kā? musāvādā vairamaṇī, pisuṇāya vācāya vairamaṇī, pharusāya vācāya vairamaṇī, samphappalāpā vairamaṇī – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, sammāvācā sāsavā puñ‍ñabhāgiyā upadhivaipak‍kā| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, sammāvācā ariyā anāsavā laukuttarā maggaṅgā? yā khau, bhikkhavai, ariyacittassa anāsavacittassa ariyamaggasamaṅginau ariyamaggaṃ bhāvayatau catūhi vacīduc‍caritaihi ārati virati paṭivirati vairamaṇī – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, sammāvācā ariyā anāsavā laukuttarā maggaṅgā| sau micchāvācāya pahānāya vāyamati, sammāvācāya upasampadāya; svāssa hauti sammāvāyāmau| sau satau micchāvācaṃ pajahati, satau sammāvācaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; sāssa hauti  sammāsati| itiyimai tayau dhammā sammāvācaṃ anuparidhāvanti anuparivattanti, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati|
