139. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, sammādiṭṭhi pubbaṅgamā hauti| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sammādiṭṭhi pubbaṅgamā hauti? micchākammantaṃ ‘micchākammantau’ti pajānāti, sammākammantaṃ ‘sammākammantau’ti pajānāti; sāssa hauti sammādiṭṭhi| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, micchākammantau? pāṇātipātau, adin‍nādānaṃ, kāmaisumicchācārau – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, micchākammantau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sammākammantau? sammākammantaṃpahaṃ, bhikkhavai, dvāyaṃ vadāmi – atthi, bhikkhavai, sammākammantau sāsavau puñ‍ñabhāgiyau upadhivaipak‍kau; atthi, bhikkhavai, sammākammantau ariyau anāsavau laukuttarau maggaṅgau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sammākammantau sāsavau puñ‍ñabhāgiyau upadhivaipak‍kau? pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī, adin‍nādānā vairamaṇī, kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇī – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, sammākammantau sāsavau puñ‍ñabhāgiyau upadhivaipak‍kau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sammākammantau ariyau anāsavau laukuttarau maggaṅgau? yā khau, bhikkhavai, ariyacittassa anāsavacittassa ariyamaggasamaṅginau ariyamaggaṃ bhāvayatau tīhi kāyaduc‍caritaihi ārati virati paṭivirati vairamaṇī – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, sammākammantau ariyau  anāsavau laukuttarau maggaṅgau| sau micchākammantassa pahānāya vāyamati, sammākammantassa upasampadāya; svāssa hauti sammāvāyāmau| sau satau micchākammantaṃ pajahati, satau sammākammantaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; sāssa hauti sammāsati| itiyimai tayau dhammā sammākammantaṃ anuparidhāvanti anuparivattanti, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati|

140. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, sammādiṭṭhi pubbaṅgamā hauti| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sammādiṭṭhi pubbaṅgamā hauti? micchāājīvaṃ ‘micchāājīvau’ti pajānāti, sammāājīvaṃ ‘sammāājīvau’ti pajānāti; sāssa  hauti sammādiṭṭhi| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, micchāājīvau? kuhanā, lapanā, naimittikatā, nippaisikatā, lābhaina lābhaṃ nijigīsanatā nijigiṃsanatā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, micchāājīvau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sammāājīvau? sammāājīvaṃpahaṃ, bhikkhavai, dvāyaṃ vadāmi – atthi, bhikkhavai, sammāājīvau sāsavau puñ‍ñabhāgiyau upadhivaipak‍kau; atthi, bhikkhavai, sammāājīvau ariyau anāsavau laukuttarau maggaṅgau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sammāājīvau sāsavau puñ‍ñabhāgiyau upadhivaipak‍kau? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau micchāājīvaṃ pahāya sammāājīvaina jīvikaṃ kappaiti – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, sammāājīvau sāsavau puñ‍ñabhāgiyau upadhivaipak‍kau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sammāājīvau ariyau anāsavau laukuttarau maggaṅgau? yā khau, bhikkhavai, ariyacittassa anāsavacittassa ariyamaggasamaṅginau ariyamaggaṃ bhāvayatau micchāājīvā ārati virati paṭivirati vairamaṇī – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, sammāājīvau ariyau anāsavau laukuttarau maggaṅgau| sau micchāājīvassa pahānāya vāyamati, sammāājīvassa upasampadāya; svāssa hauti sammāvāyāmau| sau satau micchāājīvaṃ pajahati, satau sammāājīvaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; sāssa hauti sammāsati| itiyimai tayau dhammā sammāājīvaṃ anuparidhāvanti  anuparivattanti, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati|

141. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, sammādiṭṭhi pubbaṅgamā hauti| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sammādiṭṭhi pubbaṅgamā hauti? sammādiṭṭhissa, bhikkhavai, sammāsaṅkappau pahauti, sammāsaṅkappassa sammāvācā pahauti, sammāvācassa sammākammantau pahauti, sammākammantassa sammāājīvau pahauti, sammāājīvassa sammāvāyāmau pahauti, sammāvāyāmassa sammāsati pahauti, sammāsatissa sammāsamādhi pahauti, sammāsamādhissa sammāñāṇaṃ pahauti, sammāñāṇassa sammāvimutti pahauti| iti khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatau saikkhau aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatā saikhā paṭipadā (sī॰), aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatau saikhau pāṭipadau (pī॰ ka॰) ( ) natthi sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu, dasaṅgasaman‍nāgatau arahā hauti| (tatrapi sammāñāṇaina anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā vigatā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti)|

142. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, sammādiṭṭhi pubbaṅgamā hauti| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sammādiṭṭhi pubbaṅgamā hauti? sammādiṭṭhissa, bhikkhavai, micchādiṭṭhi nij‍jiṇṇā hauti| yai ca micchādiṭṭhipac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti tai  cassa nij‍jiṇṇā haunti| sammādiṭṭhipac‍cayā anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti| sammāsaṅkappassa, bhikkhavai, micchāsaṅkappau nij‍jiṇṇau hauti…pai॰… sammāvācassa, bhikkhavai, micchāvācā nij‍jiṇṇā hauti… sammākammantassa, bhikkhavai, micchākammantau nij‍jiṇṇau hauti… sammāājīvassa, bhikkhavai, micchāājīvau nij‍jiṇṇau hauti… sammāvāyāmassa, bhikkhavai, micchāvāyāmau nij‍jiṇṇau hauti… sammāsatissa, bhikkhavai, micchāsati nij‍jiṇṇā hauti… sammāsamādhissa, bhikkhavai, micchāsamādhi nij‍jiṇṇau hauti… sammāñāṇassa, bhikkhavai, micchāñāṇaṃ nij‍jiṇṇaṃ hauti… sammāvimuttassa, bhikkhavai, micchāvimutti nij‍jiṇṇā hauti| yai ca micchāvimuttipac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti tai cassa nij‍jiṇṇā haunti| sammāvimuttipac‍cayā ca anaikai kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti|

‘‘iti khau, bhikkhavai, vīsati kusalapakkhā, vīsati akusalapakkhā – mahācattārīsakau  dhammapariyāyau pavattitau appaṭivattiyau samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā kainaci vā laukasmiṃ|

143. ‘‘yau hi kauci, bhikkhavai, samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā imaṃ mahācattārīsakaṃ dhammapariyāyaṃ garahitabbaṃ paṭik‍kausitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyya tassa diṭṭhaiva dhammai dasasahadhammikā vādānuvādā gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchanti – sammādiṭṭhiṃ cai bhavaṃ garahati, yai ca micchādiṭṭhī samaṇabrāhmaṇā tai bhautau puj‍jā, tai bhautau pāsaṃsā; sammāsaṅkappaṃ cai bhavaṃ garahati, yai ca micchāsaṅkappā samaṇabrāhmaṇā tai bhautau puj‍jā, tai bhautau pāsaṃsā; sammāvācaṃ cai bhavaṃ garahati…pai॰… sammākammantaṃ cai bhavaṃ garahati… sammāājīvaṃ cai bhavaṃ garahati… sammāvāyāmaṃ cai bhavaṃ garahati… sammāsatiṃ cai bhavaṃ garahati… sammāsamādhiṃ cai bhavaṃ garahati… sammāñāṇaṃ cai bhavaṃ garahati … sammāvimuttiṃ cai bhavaṃ garahati, yai ca micchāvimuttī samaṇabrāhmaṇā tai bhautau puj‍jā, tai bhautau pāsaṃsā| yau kauci, bhikkhavai, samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā imaṃ mahācattārīsakaṃ dhammapariyāyaṃ garahitabbaṃ paṭik‍kausitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyya tassa diṭṭhaiva dhammai imai dasasahadhammikā vādānuvādā gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchanti| yaipi tai, bhikkhavai, ahaisuṃ auk‍kalā vassabhañ‍ñā vayabhiñ‍ñā (ka॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.62; a॰ ni॰ 4.30 passitabbaṃ ahaituvādā akiriyavādā natthikavādā taipi mahācattārīsakaṃ dhammapariyāyaṃ na garahitabbaṃ  napaṭik‍kausitabbaṃ amañ‍ñiṃsu mañ‍ñaiyyuṃ (ka॰)| taṃ kissa haitu? nindābyārausaupārambhabhayā’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

mahācattārīsakasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. ānāpānassatisuttaṃ

144. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati pubbārāmai migāramātupāsādai sambahulaihi abhiñ‍ñātaihi abhiñ‍ñātaihi thairaihi sāvakaihi saddhiṃ – āyasmatā ca sāriputtaina āyasmatā ca mahāmauggal‍lānaina mahāmauggalānaina (ka॰) āyasmatā ca mahākassapaina āyasmatā ca mahākac‍cāyanaina āyasmatā ca mahākauṭṭhikaina āyasmatā ca mahākappinaina āyasmatā ca mahācundaina āyasmatā ca anuruddhaina  āyasmatā ca raivataina āyasmatā ca ānandaina, añ‍ñaihi ca abhiñ‍ñātaihi abhiñ‍ñātaihi thairaihi sāvakaihi saddhiṃ|

taina khau pana samayaina thairā bhikkhū navai bhikkhū auvadanti anusāsanti| appaikac‍cai thairā bhikkhū dasapi bhikkhū auvadanti anusāsanti, appaikac‍cai thairā bhikkhū vīsampi bhikkhū auvadanti anusāsanti, appaikac‍cai thairā bhikkhū tiṃsampi bhikkhū auvadanti anusāsanti, appaikac‍cai thairā bhikkhū cattārīsampi bhikkhū auvadanti anusāsanti| tai ca navā bhikkhū thairaihi bhikkhūhi auvadiyamānā anusāsiyamānā uḷāraṃ pubbaināparaṃ visaisaṃ jānanti pajānanti (syā॰ kaṃ॰), sañ‍jānanti (ka॰)|

145. taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā tadahupausathai pan‍narasai pavāraṇāya puṇṇāya puṇṇamāya rattiyā bhikkhusaṅghaparivutau abbhaukāsai nisin‍nau hauti| atha khau bhagavā tuṇhībhūtaṃ tuṇhībhūtaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ anuvilaukaitvā  bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āraddhausmi, bhikkhavai, imāya paṭipadāya; āraddhacittausmi, bhikkhavai, imāya paṭipadāya| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, bhiyyausaumattāya vīriyaṃ ārabhatha appattassa pattiyā, anadhigatassa adhigamāya, asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya| idhaivāhaṃ sāvatthiyaṃ kaumudiṃ cātumāsiniṃ āgamaissāmī’’ti| assausuṃ khau jānapadā bhikkhū – ‘‘bhagavā kira tatthaiva sāvatthiyaṃ kaumudiṃ cātumāsiniṃ āgamaissatī’’ti| tai jānapadā bhikkhū sāvatthiṃ sāvatthiyaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) ausaranti bhagavantaṃ dassanāya| tai ca khau thairā bhikkhū bhiyyausaumattāya navai bhikkhū auvadanti anusāsanti| appaikac‍cai thairā bhikkhū dasapi bhikkhū auvadanti anusāsanti, appaikac‍cai thairā bhikkhū vīsampi bhikkhū auvadanti anusāsanti, appaikac‍cai thairā bhikkhū tiṃsampi bhikkhū auvadanti anusāsanti, appaikac‍cai thairā bhikkhū cattārīsampi bhikkhū auvadanti anusāsanti| tai ca navā bhikkhū thairaihi bhikkhūhi auvadiyamānā anusāsiyamānā uḷāraṃ  pubbaināparaṃ visaisaṃ jānanti|

146. taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā tadahupausathai pan‍narasai kaumudiyā cātumāsiniyā puṇṇāya puṇṇamāya rattiyā bhikkhusaṅghaparivutau abbhaukāsai nisin‍nau hauti| atha khau bhagavā tuṇhībhūtaṃ tuṇhībhūtaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ anuvilaukaitvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘apalāpāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, parisā; nippalāpāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, parisā; suddhā sārai suddhasārai patiṭṭhitā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) patiṭṭhitā| tathārūpau ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhusaṅghau; tathārūpā ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, parisā yathārūpā  parisā āhunaiyyā pāhunaiyyā dakkhiṇaiyyā añ‍jalikaraṇīyā anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| tathārūpau ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhusaṅghau; tathārūpā ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, parisā yathārūpāya parisāya appaṃ din‍naṃ bahu hauti, bahu din‍naṃ bahutaraṃ| tathārūpau ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhusaṅghau; tathārūpā ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, parisā yathārūpā parisā dul‍labhā dassanāya laukassa| tathārūpau ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhusaṅghau; tathārūpā ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, parisā yathārūpaṃ parisaṃ alaṃ yaujanagaṇanāni dassanāya gantuṃ puṭausaināpi’’ puṭausaināpi, tathārūpau ayaṃ bhikkhavai bhikkhusaṃghau, tathārūpā ayaṃ parisā (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰)|

147. ‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū imasmiṃ bhikkhusaṅghai arahantau khīṇāsavā vusitavantau katakaraṇīyā auhitabhārā anuppattasadatthā parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanā sammadañ‍ñāvimuttā – aivarūpāpi, bhikkhavai, santi bhikkhū imasmiṃ bhikkhusaṅghai| santi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū imasmiṃ bhikkhusaṅghai pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikā tattha parinibbāyinau anāvattidhammā tasmā laukā – aivarūpāpi, bhikkhavai, santi bhikkhū imasmiṃ bhikkhusaṅghai| santi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū imasmiṃ bhikkhusaṅghai tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā rāgadausamauhānaṃ tanuttā sakadāgāminau sakidaiva sakiṃ daiva (ka॰) imaṃ laukaṃ āgantvā dukkhassantaṃ  karissanti – aivarūpāpi, bhikkhavai, santi bhikkhū imasmiṃ bhikkhusaṅghai| santi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū imasmiṃ bhikkhusaṅghai tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā sautāpan‍nā avinipātadhammā niyatā sambaudhiparāyanā – aivarūpāpi, bhikkhavai, santi bhikkhū imasmiṃ bhikkhusaṅghai|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū imasmiṃ bhikkhusaṅghai catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvanānuyaugamanuyuttā viharanti  – aivarūpāpi, bhikkhavai, santi bhikkhū imasmiṃ bhikkhusaṅghai| santi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū imasmiṃ bhikkhusaṅghai catun‍naṃ sammappadhānānaṃ bhāvanānuyaugamanuyuttā viharanti…pai॰… catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ… pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ… pañ‍can‍naṃ balānaṃ… sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ… ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa bhāvanānuyaugamanuyuttā viharanti – aivarūpāpi, bhikkhavai, santi bhikkhū imasmiṃ bhikkhusaṅghai| santi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū imasmiṃ bhikkhusaṅghai maittābhāvanānuyaugamanuyuttā viharanti… karuṇābhāvanānuyaugamanuyuttā  viharanti… muditābhāvanānuyaugamanuyuttā viharanti… upaikkhābhāvanānuyaugamanuyuttā viharanti… asubhabhāvanānuyaugamanuyuttā viharanti… anic‍casañ‍ñābhāvanānuyaugamanuyuttā viharanti – aivarūpāpi, bhikkhavai, santi bhikkhū imasmiṃ bhikkhusaṅghai| santi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū imasmiṃ bhikkhusaṅghai ānāpānassatibhāvanānuyaugamanuyuttā viharanti| ānāpānassati, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā| ānāpānassati, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā bahulīkatā cattārau satipaṭṭhānai paripūraiti| cattārau satipaṭṭhānā  bhāvitā bahulīkatā satta baujjhaṅgai paripūrainti| satta baujjhaṅgā bhāvitā bahulīkatā vij‍jāvimuttiṃ paripūrainti|

148. ‘‘kathaṃ bhāvitā ca, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassati kathaṃ bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ  kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| sau satauva assasati satauva satau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) passasati|

‘‘dīghaṃ vā assasantau ‘dīghaṃ assasāmī’ti pajānāti, dīghaṃ vā passasantau ‘dīghaṃ passasāmī’ti pajānāti; rassaṃ vā assasantau ‘rassaṃ assasāmī’ti pajānāti, rassaṃ vā passasantau ‘rassaṃ passasāmī’ti pajānāti; ‘sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ passasissāmī’ti sikkhati|

‘‘‘pītipaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘pītipaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘sukhapaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti  sikkhati, ‘sukhapaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘cittasaṅkhārapaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘cittasaṅkhārapaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’ti  sikkhati; ‘passambhayaṃ cittasaṅkhāraṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘passambhayaṃ cittasaṅkhāraṃ passasissāmī’ti sikkhati|

‘‘‘cittapaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘cittapaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘abhippamaudayaṃ cittaṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘abhippamaudayaṃ cittaṃ passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘samādahaṃ cittaṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘samādahaṃ cittaṃ passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ passasissāmī’ti sikkhati|

‘‘‘anic‍cānupassī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘anic‍cānupassī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘virāgānupassī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘virāgānupassī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘niraudhānupassī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘niraudhānupassī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘paṭinissaggānupassī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘paṭinissaggānupassī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati| aivaṃ bhāvitā khau, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassati aivaṃ bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā|

149. ‘‘kathaṃ  bhāvitā ca, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassati kathaṃ bahulīkatā cattārau satipaṭṭhānai paripūraiti? yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dīghaṃ vā assasantau ‘dīghaṃ assasāmī’ti pajānāti, dīghaṃ vā passasantau ‘dīghaṃ passasāmī’ti pajānāti; rassaṃ vā assasantau ‘rassaṃ assasāmī’ti pajānāti, rassaṃ vā passasantau ‘rassaṃ passasāmī’ti pajānāti; ‘sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; kāyai kāyānupassī, bhikkhavai, tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| kāyaisu kāyañ‍ñatarāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ vadāmi yadidaṃ – assāsapassāsā| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, kāyai  kāyānupassī tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ|

‘‘yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu  ‘pītipaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘pītipaṭisaṃvaidī  passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘sukhapaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘sukhapaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘cittasaṅkhārapaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘cittasaṅkhārapaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘passambhayaṃ cittasaṅkhāraṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘passambhayaṃ cittasaṅkhāraṃ passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī, bhikkhavai, tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| vaidanāsu vaidanāñ‍ñatarāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ vadāmi yadidaṃ – assāsapassāsānaṃ sādhukaṃ manasikāraṃ| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ|

‘‘yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘cittapaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘cittapaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘abhippamaudayaṃ cittaṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘abhippamaudayaṃ cittaṃ passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘samādahaṃ cittaṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘samādahaṃ cittaṃ passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘vimaucayaṃ  cittaṃ passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; cittai cittānupassī, bhikkhavai, tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, muṭṭhassatissa asampajānassa  ānāpānassatiṃ vadāmi| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, cittai cittānupassī tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ|

‘‘yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘anic‍cānupassī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘anic‍cānupassī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘virāgānupassī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘virāgānupassī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘niraudhānupassī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘niraudhānupassī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘paṭinissaggānupassī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘paṭinissaggānupassī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; dhammaisu dhammānupassī, bhikkhavai, tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| sau yaṃ taṃ abhijjhādaumanassānaṃ  pahānaṃ taṃ pañ‍ñāya disvā sādhukaṃ ajjhupaikkhitā hauti| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, dhammaisu dhammānupassī tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ|

‘‘aivaṃ  bhāvitā khau, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassati aivaṃ bahulīkatā cattārau satipaṭṭhānai paripūraiti|

150. ‘‘kathaṃ bhāvitā ca, bhikkhavai, cattārau satipaṭṭhānā kathaṃ bahulīkatā satta baujjhaṅgai paripūrainti? yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ, upaṭṭhitāssa tasmiṃ samayai sati hauti asammuṭṭhā appammuṭṭhā (syā॰ kaṃ॰)| yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau upaṭṭhitā sati hauti  asammuṭṭhā, satisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti| satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti, satisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati|

‘‘sau tathāsatau viharantau taṃ dhammaṃ pañ‍ñāya pavicinati pavicayati pavicarati (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) parivīmaṃsaṃ āpaj‍jati| yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu tathāsatau viharantau  taṃ dhammaṃ pañ‍ñāya pavicinati pavicayati parivīmaṃsaṃ āpaj‍jati, dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti, dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti, dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati|

‘‘tassa taṃ dhammaṃ pañ‍ñāya pavicinatau pavicayatau parivīmaṃsaṃ āpaj‍jatau āraddhaṃ hauti vīriyaṃ asal‍līnaṃ| yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau taṃ dhammaṃ pañ‍ñāya pavicinatau pavicayatau parivīmaṃsaṃ āpaj‍jatau āraddhaṃ hauti vīriyaṃ asal‍līnaṃ, vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti, vīriyasambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti, vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati|

‘‘āraddhavīriyassa uppaj‍jati pīti nirāmisā| yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau āraddhavīriyassa uppaj‍jati pīti  nirāmisā, pītisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti, pītisambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti, pītisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati|

‘‘pītimanassa kāyaupi passambhati, cittampi passambhati| yasmiṃ  samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau  pītimanassa kāyaupi passambhati, cittampi passambhati, passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti, passaddhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti, passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati|

‘‘passaddhakāyassa sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau passaddhakāyassa sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati, samādhisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti, samādhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti, samādhisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati|

‘‘sau tathāsamāhitaṃ cittaṃ sādhukaṃ ajjhupaikkhitā hauti| yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu tathāsamāhitaṃ cittaṃ sādhukaṃ ajjhupaikkhitā hauti, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati|

151. ‘‘yasmiṃ  samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ, upaṭṭhitāssa tasmiṃ samayai sati hauti asammuṭṭhā| yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau upaṭṭhitā sati hauti asammuṭṭhā, satisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti, satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti, satisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati|

‘‘sau  tathāsatau viharantau taṃ dhammaṃ pañ‍ñāya pavicinati pavicayati parivīmaṃsaṃ āpaj‍jati| yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu tathāsatau viharantau taṃ dhammaṃ pañ‍ñāya pavicinati pavicayati parivīmaṃsaṃ āpaj‍jati, dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti, dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti, dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati|

‘‘tassa  taṃ dhammaṃ pañ‍ñāya pavicinatau pavicayatau parivīmaṃsaṃ āpaj‍jatau āraddhaṃ hauti vīriyaṃ asal‍līnaṃ| yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau taṃ dhammaṃ pañ‍ñāya pavicinatau pavicayatau parivīmaṃsaṃ āpaj‍jatau āraddhaṃ hauti vīriyaṃ asal‍līnaṃ, vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti, vīriyasambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti, vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati|

‘‘āraddhavīriyassa uppaj‍jati pīti nirāmisā| yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau āraddhavīriyassa uppaj‍jati pīti nirāmisā, pītisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti, pītisambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti, pītisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati|

‘‘pītimanassa kāyaupi passambhati, cittampi passambhati| yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pītimanassa kāyaupi passambhati, cittampi passambhati, passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti, passaddhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai  bhikkhu bhāvaiti, passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati|

‘‘passaddhakāyassa  sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau passaddhakāyassa sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati, samādhisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti, samādhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti, samādhisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati|

‘‘sau tathāsamāhitaṃ cittaṃ sādhukaṃ ajjhupaikkhitā hauti| yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu tathāsamāhitaṃ cittaṃ sādhukaṃ ajjhupaikkhitā hauti, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati| aivaṃ bhāvitā khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau satipaṭṭhānā aivaṃ bahulīkatā satta sambaujjhaṅgai paripūrainti|

152. ‘‘kathaṃ  bhāvitā ca, bhikkhavai, satta baujjhaṅgā kathaṃ bahulīkatā vij‍jāvimuttiṃ paripūrainti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… vīriyasambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… pītisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… passaddhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… samādhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ bhāvitā khau, bhikkhavai, satta baujjhaṅgā aivaṃ bahulīkatā vij‍jāvimuttiṃ paripūraintī’’ti|

idamavauca  bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

ānāpānassatisuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. kāyagatāsatisuttaṃ

153. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau sambahulānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantānaṃ upaṭṭhānasālāyaṃ san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ ayamantarākathā  udapādi – ‘‘acchariyaṃ, āvusau, abbhutaṃ, āvusau! yāvañ‍cidaṃ taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina kāyagatāsati kāyagatā sati (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā vuttā mahānisaṃsā’’ti| ayañ‍ca hidaṃ taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ antarākathā vippakatā hauti, atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina upaṭṭhānasālā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘kāya nuttha, bhikkhavai, aitarahi kathāya san‍nisin‍nā, kā ca pana vau antarākathā vippakatā’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhantai, amhākaṃ pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantānaṃ upaṭṭhānasālāyaṃ san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ ayamantarākathā udapādi – ‘acchariyaṃ, āvusau, abbhutaṃ, āvusau! yāvañ‍cidaṃ taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina kāyagatāsati bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā vuttā mahānisaṃsā’ti| ayaṃ khau nau, bhantai, antarākathā vippakatā, atha bhagavā anuppattau’’ti|

154. ‘‘kathaṃ  bhāvitā ca, bhikkhavai, kāyagatāsati kathaṃ bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| sau satauva assasati satauva passasati; dīghaṃ vā assasantau ‘dīghaṃ assasāmī’ti pajānāti, dīghaṃ vā passasantau ‘dīghaṃ passasāmī’ti pajānāti; rassaṃ vā assasantau ‘rassaṃ assasāmī’ti pajānāti, rassaṃ vā passasantau ‘rassaṃ passasāmī’ti pajānāti; ‘sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ passasissāmī’ti sikkhati| tassa aivaṃ appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau yai gaihasitā gaihassitā (ṭīkā) sarasaṅkappā tai pahīyanti| taisaṃ pahānā ajjhattamaiva cittaṃ santiṭṭhati san‍nisīdati aikaudi hauti aikaudī hauti (sī॰), aikaudibhauti (syā॰ kaṃ॰) samādhiyati| aivaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyagatāsatiṃ kāyagataṃ satiṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) bhāvaiti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu gacchantau vā ‘gacchāmī’ti pajānāti, ṭhitau vā ‘ṭhitaumhī’ti pajānāti, nisin‍nau vā ‘nisin‍naumhī’ti pajānāti, sayānau vā ‘sayānaumhī’ti pajānāti| yathā yathā vā panassa kāyau paṇihitau hauti, tathā tathā naṃ pajānāti| tassa aivaṃ appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau yai gaihasitā sarasaṅkappā tai pahīyanti| taisaṃ pahānā ajjhattamaiva cittaṃ  santiṭṭhati san‍nisīdati aikaudi hauti samādhiyati| aivampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyagatāsatiṃ bhāvaiti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu abhik‍kantai paṭik‍kantai sampajānakārī hauti, ālaukitai vilaukitai sampajānakārī hauti, samiñ‍jitai pasāritai sampajānakārī hauti, saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇai sampajānakārī hauti, asitai pītai khāyitai sāyitai sampajānakārī hauti, uc‍cārapassāvakammai sampajānakārī hauti, gatai ṭhitai nisin‍nai suttai jāgaritai bhāsitai tuṇhībhāvai sampajānakārī hauti| tassa aivaṃ appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau yai gaihasitā sarasaṅkappā tai pahīyanti| taisaṃ pahānā ajjhattamaiva cittaṃ santiṭṭhati san‍nisīdati aikaudi hauti samādhiyati| aivampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyagatāsatiṃ bhāvaiti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imamaiva kāyaṃ uddhaṃ pādatalā adhau kaisamatthakā tacapariyantaṃ pūraṃ nānappakārassa asucinau pac‍cavaikkhati – ‘atthi imasmiṃ kāyai kaisā laumā nakhā dantā tacau maṃsaṃ nhāru nahāru (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) aṭṭhi aṭṭhimiñ‍jaṃ vak‍kaṃ hadayaṃ yakanaṃ kilaumakaṃ pihakaṃ papphāsaṃ antaṃ antaguṇaṃ udariyaṃ karīsaṃ pittaṃ saimhaṃ pubbau lauhitaṃ saidau maidau assu vasā khaiḷau siṅghāṇikā lasikā mutta’nti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, ubhataumukhā putauḷi mūtauḷī (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pūrā nānāvihitassa dhañ‍ñassa, saiyyathidaṃ – sālīnaṃ vīhīnaṃ muggānaṃ māsānaṃ tilānaṃ taṇḍulānaṃ, tamainaṃ  cakkhumā purisau muñ‍citvā pac‍cavaikkhaiyya – ‘imai sālī imai vīhī imai muggā imai māsā imai tilā imai taṇḍulā’ti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imamaiva kāyaṃ uddhaṃ pādatalā adhau kaisamatthakā tacapariyantaṃ pūraṃ  nānappakārassa asucinau pac‍cavaikkhati – ‘atthi imasmiṃ kāyai kaisā laumā nakhā dantā tacau maṃsaṃ nhāru aṭṭhi aṭṭhimiñ‍jaṃ vak‍kaṃ hadayaṃ yakanaṃ kilaumakaṃ pihakaṃ papphāsaṃ antaṃ antaguṇaṃ udariyaṃ karīsaṃ pittaṃ saimhaṃ pubbau lauhitaṃ saidau maidau assu vasā khaiḷau siṅghāṇikā lasikā mutta’nti| tassa aivaṃ appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau yai gaihasitā sarasaṅkappā tai pahīyanti| taisaṃ pahānā ajjhattamaiva cittaṃ santiṭṭhati san‍nisīdati aikaudi hauti samādhiyati| aivampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyagatāsatiṃ bhāvaiti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imamaiva kāyaṃ yathāṭhitaṃ yathāpaṇihitaṃ dhātusau pac‍cavaikkhati – ‘atthi imasmiṃ kāyai pathavīdhātu āpaudhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātū’ti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, dakkhau gaughātakau vā gaughātakantaivāsī vā gāviṃ vadhitvā catumahāpathai cātummahāpathai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) bilasau vibhajitvā paṭivibhajitvā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) nisin‍nau assa; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imamaiva kāyaṃ yathāṭhitaṃ yathāpaṇihitaṃ dhātusau pac‍cavaikkhati – ‘atthi imasmiṃ kāyai pathavīdhātu āpaudhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātū’ti| tassa aivaṃ appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau yai gaihasitā  sarasaṅkappā tai pahīyanti| taisaṃ pahānā ajjhattamaiva cittaṃ santiṭṭhati san‍nisīdati aikaudi hauti samādhiyati| aivampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyagatāsatiṃ bhāvaiti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saiyyathāpi passaiyya sarīraṃ sivathikāya sīvathikāya (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) chaḍḍitaṃ aikāhamataṃ vā dvīhamataṃ vā tīhamataṃ vā uddhumātakaṃ vinīlakaṃ vipubbakajātaṃ| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ upasaṃharati – ‘ayampi khau kāyau aivaṃdhammau aivaṃbhāvī aivaṃanatītau’ti aitaṃ anatītauti (sī॰)| tassa aivaṃ appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau yai gaihasitā sarasaṅkappā tai pahīyanti| taisaṃ pahānā ajjhattamaiva cittaṃ santiṭṭhati san‍nisīdati aikaudi hauti samādhiyati| aivampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyagatāsatiṃ bhāvaiti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saiyyathāpi passaiyya sarīraṃ sivathikāya chaḍḍitaṃ kākaihi vā khaj‍jamānaṃ kulalaihi vā khaj‍jamānaṃ gijjhaihi vā khaj‍jamānaṃ kaṅkaihi vā khaj‍jamānaṃ sunakhaihi vā khaj‍jamānaṃ byagghaihi vā khaj‍jamānaṃ dīpīhi vā khaj‍jamānaṃ siṅgālaihi vā gijjhaihi vā khaj‍jamānaṃ suvānaihi vā khaj‍jamānaṃ sigālaihi vā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) khaj‍jamānaṃ vividhaihi vā pāṇakajātaihi khaj‍jamānaṃ| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ upasaṃharati – ‘ayampi  khau kāyau aivaṃdhammau aivaṃbhāvī aivaṃanatītau’ti| tassa aivaṃ appamattassa…pai॰… aivampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyagatāsatiṃ bhāvaiti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saiyyathāpi passaiyya sarīraṃ sivathikāya chaḍḍitaṃ aṭṭhikasaṅkhalikaṃ samaṃsalauhitaṃ nhārusambandhaṃ…pai॰… aṭṭhikasaṅkhalikaṃ  nimmaṃsalauhitamakkhitaṃ nhārusambandhaṃ…pai॰… aṭṭhikasaṅkhalikaṃ apagatamaṃsalauhitaṃ nhārusambandhaṃ…pai॰… aṭṭhikāni apagatasambandhāni apagatanahārūsambandhāni (syā॰ kaṃ॰) disāvidisāvikkhittāni disāvidisāsu vikkhitāni (sī॰ pī॰) añ‍ñaina hatthaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina pādaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina gaupphakaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina gaupphakaṭṭhikanti idaṃ sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu natthi añ‍ñaina jaṅghaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina ūruṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina kaṭiṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina kaṭaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina piṭṭhikaṇḍakaṃ añ‍ñaina sīsakaṭāhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) añ‍ñaina phāsukaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina piṭṭhiṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina khandhaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina gīvaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina hanukaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina dantaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina sīsakaṭāhaṃ añ‍ñaina kaṭaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina piṭṭhikaṇḍakaṃ añ‍ñaina sīsakaṭāhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ upasaṃharati – ‘ayampi khau kāyau aivaṃdhammau aivaṃbhāvī aivaṃanatītau’ti| tassa aivaṃ appamattassa…pai॰… aivampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyagatāsatiṃ bhāvaiti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saiyyathāpi passaiyya sarīraṃ sivathikāya chaḍḍitaṃ – aṭṭhikāni saitāni saṅkhavaṇṇapaṭibhāgāni saṅkhavaṇṇūpanibhāni (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) …pai॰… aṭṭhikāni puñ‍jakitāni tairauvassikāni…pai॰… aṭṭhikāni pūtīni cuṇṇakajātāni| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ upasaṃharati – ‘ayampi khau kāyau aivaṃdhammau aivaṃbhāvī aivaṃanatītau’ti| tassa aivaṃ appamattassa…pai॰… aivampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyagatāsatiṃ bhāvaiti|

155. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ vivaikajaina pītisukhaina abhisandaiti  parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa vivaikajaina pītisukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, dakkhau nhāpakau nahāpakau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vā nhāpakantaivāsī vā kaṃsathālai nhānīyacuṇṇāni nahānīyacuṇṇāni (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) ākiritvā  udakaina paripphausakaṃ paripphausakaṃ san‍naiyya, sāyaṃ nhānīyapiṇḍi sāssa nahānīyapiṇḍī (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) snaihānugatā snaihaparaitā santarabāhirā phuṭā snaihaina na ca pagghariṇī; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imamaiva kāyaṃ vivaikajaina pītisukhaina  abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati; nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa  vivaikajaina pītisukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti| tassa aivaṃ appamattassa…pai॰… aivampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyagatāsatiṃ bhāvaiti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ samādhijaina pītisukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati; nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa samādhijaina pītisukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, udakarahadau gambhīrau ubbhidaudakau ubbhitaudakau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰)| tassa naivassa puratthimāya disāya udakassa āyamukhaṃ na pacchimāya disāya udakassa āyamukhaṃ na uttarāya disāya udakassa āyamukhaṃ na dakkhiṇāya disāya udakassa āyamukhaṃ; daivau ca na kālaina kālaṃ sammā dhāraṃ anuppavaicchaiyya; atha khau tamhāva udakarahadā sītā vāridhārā ubbhij‍jitvā tamaiva udakarahadaṃ sītaina vārinā abhisandaiyya parisandaiyya paripūraiyya parippharaiyya, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau udakarahadassa sītaina vārinā apphuṭaṃ assa; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imamaiva kāyaṃ samādhijaina pītisukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa samādhijaina  pītisukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti| tassa aivaṃ appamattassa…pai॰… aivampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyagatāsatiṃ bhāvaiti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ nippītikaina sukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa nippītikaina sukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, uppaliniyaṃ vā paduminiyaṃ vā puṇḍarīkiniyaṃ vā appaikac‍cāni uppalāni vā padumāni vā puṇḍarīkāni vā  udakai jātāni udakai saṃvaḍḍhāni udakānuggatāni antaunimuggapausīni, tāni yāva caggā yāva ca mūlā sītaina vārinā abhisan‍nāni parisan‍nāni abhisandāni parisandāni (ka॰) paripūrāni paripphuṭāni, nāssa na naisaṃ (?) kiñ‍ci sabbāvataṃ uppalānaṃ vā padumānaṃ vā puṇḍarīkānaṃ vā sītaina vārinā apphuṭaṃ assa; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imamaiva kāyaṃ nippītikaina sukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti  parippharati, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa nippītikaina sukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti| tassa aivaṃ appamattassa…pai॰… aivampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyagatāsatiṃ bhāvaiti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ parisuddhaina caitasā pariyaudātaina pharitvā nisin‍nau hauti; nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa parisuddhaina caitasā pariyaudātaina apphuṭaṃ hauti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau audātaina vatthaina sasīsaṃ pārupitvā nisin‍nau assa, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa audātaina vatthaina apphuṭaṃ assa; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imamaiva kāyaṃ parisuddhaina caitasā pariyaudātaina pharitvā nisin‍nau hauti, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa parisuddhaina caitasā pariyaudātaina apphuṭaṃ hauti| tassa aivaṃ appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau yai gaihasitā sarasaṅkappā tai pahīyanti| taisaṃ pahānā ajjhattamaiva cittaṃ santiṭṭhati, san‍nisīdati aikaudi hauti samādhiyati| aivampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyagatāsatiṃ bhāvaiti|

156. ‘‘yassa kassaci, bhikkhavai, kāyagatāsati bhāvitā bahulīkatā, antaugadhāvāssa antaugadhā tassa (sī॰ pī॰) kusalā dhammā yai kaici vij‍jābhāgiyā| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yassa kassaci mahāsamuddau caitasā phuṭau, antaugadhāvāssa kun‍nadiyau yā kāci samuddaṅgamā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yassa kassaci kāyagatāsati bhāvitā bahulīkatā, antaugadhāvāssa kusalā dhammā yai kaici vij‍jābhāgiyā|

‘‘yassa kassaci, bhikkhavai, kāyagatāsati abhāvitā abahulīkatā, labhati tassa mārau autāraṃ, labhati tassa mārau ārammaṇaṃ āramaṇaṃ (?)| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau garukaṃ silāguḷaṃ al‍lamattikāpuñ‍jai pakkhipaiyya| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu taṃ garukaṃ silāguḷaṃ al‍lamattikāpuñ‍jai labhaitha autāra’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivamaiva  khau, bhikkhavai, yassa kassaci kāyagatāsati abhāvitā abahulīkatā, labhati tassa mārau autāraṃ, labhati tassa mārau ārammaṇaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, sukkhaṃ kaṭṭhaṃ kauḷāpaṃ kauḷāpaṃ ārakā udakā thalai nikkhittaṃ (ka॰); atha purisau āgacchaiyya uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya – ‘aggiṃ abhinibbattaissāmi, taijau pātukarissāmī’ti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu sau purisau amuṃ sukkhaṃ kaṭṭhaṃ kauḷāpaṃ uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya abhimanthaintau abhimanthaintau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) aggiṃ abhinibbattaiyya, taijau pātukaraiyyā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yassa kassaci kāyagatāsati abhāvitā abahulīkatā, labhati tassa mārau autāraṃ, labhati tassa mārau ārammaṇaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, udakamaṇikau rittau tucchau ādhārai ṭhapitau; atha purisau āgacchaiyya udakabhāraṃ ādāya| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu sau purisau labhaitha udakassa nikkhaipana’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yassa kassaci kāyagatāsati abhāvitā abahulīkatā, labhati tassa mārau autāraṃ, labhati tassa mārau ārammaṇaṃ’’|

157. ‘‘yassa kassaci, bhikkhavai, kāyagatāsati bhāvitā bahulīkatā, na tassa labhati mārau autāraṃ, na tassa labhati mārau ārammaṇaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau lahukaṃ suttaguḷaṃ sabbasāramayai aggaḷaphalakai pakkhipaiyya| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu sau purisau taṃ lahukaṃ suttaguḷaṃ sabbasāramayai aggaḷaphalakai labhaitha autāra’’nti? ‘‘nau  haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yassa kassaci kāyagatāsati bhāvitā bahulīkatā, na tassa labhati mārau autāraṃ, na tassa labhati mārau ārammaṇaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, al‍laṃ kaṭṭhaṃ sasnaihaṃ sasnaihaṃ ārakā udakā thalai nikkhittaṃ (ka॰); atha purisau āgacchaiyya uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya – ‘aggiṃ abhinibbattaissāmi, taijau pātukarissāmī’ti| taṃ kiṃ  mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu sau purisau amuṃ al‍laṃ kaṭṭhaṃ sasnaihaṃ uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya abhimanthaintau aggiṃ abhinibbattaiyya, taijau pātukaraiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yassa kassaci kāyagatāsati bhāvitā bahulīkatā, na tassa labhati mārau autāraṃ, na tassa labhati mārau ārammaṇaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, udakamaṇikau pūrau udakassa samatittikau kākapaiyyau ādhārai ṭhapitau; atha purisau āgacchaiyya udakabhāraṃ ādāya| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu sau purisau labhaitha udakassa nikkhaipana’’nti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yassa kassaci kāyagatāsati bhāvitā bahulīkatā, na tassa labhati mārau autāraṃ, na tassa labhati mārau ārammaṇaṃ’’|

158. ‘‘yassa kassaci, bhikkhavai, kāyagatāsati bhāvitā bahulīkatā, sau yassa yassa abhiñ‍ñāsacchikaraṇīyassa dhammassa cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti abhiñ‍ñāsacchikiriyāya, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati satiāyatanai| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, udakamaṇikau pūrau udakassa samatittikau  kākapaiyyau ādhārai ṭhapitau| tamainaṃ balavā purisau yatau yatau āviñchaiyya, āgacchaiyya udaka’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yassa kassaci kāyagatāsati bhāvitā  bahulīkatā sau, yassa yassa abhiñ‍ñāsacchikaraṇīyassa dhammassa cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti abhiñ‍ñāsacchikiriyāya, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati satiāyatanai| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, samai bhūmibhāgai caturassā paukkharaṇī paukkhariṇī (sī॰) assa āḷibandhā pūrā udakassa samatittikā kākapaiyyā| tamainaṃ balavā purisau yatau yatau āḷiṃ muñ‍caiyya āgacchaiyya udaka’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yassa kassaci kāyagatāsati bhāvitā bahulīkatā, sau yassa yassa abhiñ‍ñāsacchikaraṇīyassa dhammassa cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti abhiñ‍ñāsacchikiriyāya, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati satiāyatanai| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, subhūmiyaṃ catumahāpathai ājañ‍ñarathau yuttau assa ṭhitau audhastapataudau aubhastapataudau (ka॰), ubhantarapaṭaudau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) ava + dhaṃsu + ta = audhasta-itipadavibhāgau; tamainaṃ dakkhau yauggācariyau assadammasārathi abhiruhitvā vāmaina hatthaina rasmiyau gahaitvā dakkhiṇaina hatthaina pataudaṃ gahaitvā yainicchakaṃ yadicchakaṃ sāraiyyāpi  pac‍cāsāraiyyāpi; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yassa kassaci kāyagatāsati bhāvitā bahulīkatā, sau yassa yassa abhiñ‍ñāsacchikaraṇīyassa dhammassa cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti abhiñ‍ñāsacchikiriyāya, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati satiāyatanai’’|

159. ‘‘kāyagatāya, bhikkhavai, satiyā āsaivitāya bhāvitāya bahulīkatāya yānīkatāya vatthukatāya anuṭṭhitāya paricitāya susamāraddhāya dasānisaṃsā pāṭikaṅkhā| aratiratisahau hauti, na ca taṃ arati sahati, uppan‍naṃ aratiṃ abhibhuyya viharati|

‘‘bhayabhairavasahau hauti, na ca taṃ bhayabhairavaṃ sahati, uppan‍naṃ bhayabhairavaṃ abhibhuyya viharati|

‘‘khamau hauti sītassa uṇhassa jighacchāya pipāsāya ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasarīsapasamphassānaṃ duruttānaṃ durāgatānaṃ vacanapathānaṃ, uppan‍nānaṃ sārīrikānaṃ vaidanānaṃ dukkhānaṃ tibbānaṃ kharānaṃ kaṭukānaṃ asātānaṃ amanāpānaṃ pāṇaharānaṃ adhivāsakajātikau hauti|

‘‘catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ  nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī|

‘‘sau anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍cānubhauti| aikaupi hutvā bahudhā hauti, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau hauti, āvibhāvaṃ…pai॰… yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiti|

‘‘dibbāya  sautadhātuyā visuddhāya atik‍kantamānusikāya ubhau saddai suṇāti dibbai ca mānusai ca, yai dūrai santikai ca…pai॰…|

‘‘parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāti| sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘sarāgaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, vītarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ…pai॰… sadausaṃ vā cittaṃ… vītadausaṃ vā cittaṃ… samauhaṃ vā cittaṃ… vītamauhaṃ vā cittaṃ… saṃkhittaṃ vā  cittaṃ… vikkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ… mahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ… amahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ… sauttaraṃ vā cittaṃ… anuttaraṃ vā cittaṃ… samāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ… asamāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ… vimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ… avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘avimuttaṃ citta’nti pajānāti|

‘‘sau anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi  jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati|

‘‘dibbaina  cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai, sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti|

‘‘āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati|

‘‘kāyagatāya, bhikkhavai, satiyā āsaivitāya bhāvitāya bahulīkatāya yānīkatāya vatthukatāya anuṭṭhitāya paricitāya susamāraddhāya imai dasānisaṃsā pāṭikaṅkhā’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

kāyagatāsatisuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ navamaṃ|

10. saṅkhārupapattisuttaṃ

160. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘saṅkhārupapattiṃ saṅkhārūpapattiṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰), saṅkhāruppattiṃ (sī॰ pī॰) vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi, taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

161. ‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhāya saman‍nāgatau hauti, sīlaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, sutaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, cāgaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā khattiyamahāsālānaṃ khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau taṃ cittaṃ dahati, taṃ cittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, taṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti| tassa tai saṅkhārā ca vihārā vihārau (sī॰ pī॰) ca aivaṃ bhāvitā aivaṃ bahulīkatā  tatrupapattiyā tatrūpapattiyā (syā॰ kaṃ॰), tatruppattiyā (sī॰ pī॰) saṃvattanti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā tatrupapattiyā saṃvattati|

162. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhāya saman‍nāgatau hauti, sīlaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, sutaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, cāgaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ…pai॰… gahapatimahāsālānaṃ  brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau taṃ cittaṃ dahati, taṃ cittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, taṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti| tassa tai saṅkhārā ca vihārā ca aivaṃ bhāvitā aivaṃ bahulīkatā tatrupapattiyā saṃvattanti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā tatrupapattiyā saṃvattati|

163. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhāya saman‍nāgatau hauti, sīlaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, sutaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, cāgaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau hauti| tassa sutaṃ hauti – ‘cātumahārājikā cātummahārājikā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) daivā dīghāyukā vaṇṇavantau sukhabahulā’ti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā cātumahārājikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau taṃ cittaṃ dahati, taṃ cittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, taṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti| tassa tai saṅkhārā ca vihārā ca aivaṃ bhāvitā aivaṃ bahulīkatā tatrupapattiyā saṃvattanti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā tatrupapattiyā saṃvattati|

164. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhāya saman‍nāgatau hauti, sīlaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, sutaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, cāgaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau hauti| tassa sutaṃ hauti – tāvatiṃsā daivā…pai॰… yāmā daivā… tusitā daivā… nimmānaratī daivā… paranimmitavasavattī daivā dīghāyukā vaṇṇavantau sukhabahulāti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau taṃ cittaṃ dahati, taṃ cittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, taṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti| tassa tai saṅkhārā ca vihārā ca aivaṃ  bhāvitā aivaṃ bahulīkatā tatrupapattiyā saṃvattanti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā tatrupapattiyā saṃvattati|

165. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhāya saman‍nāgatau  hauti, sīlaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, sutaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, cāgaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau hauti| tassa sutaṃ hauti – ‘sahassau brahmā dīghāyukau vaṇṇavā sukhabahulau’ti| sahassau, bhikkhavai, brahmā sahassilaukadhātuṃ sahassiṃ laukadhātuṃ (sī॰) pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā adhimuñ‍citvā (ka॰) viharati| yaipi tattha sattā upapan‍nā taipi pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, cakkhumā purisau aikaṃ āmaṇḍaṃ hatthai karitvā pac‍cavaikkhaiyya; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, sahassau brahmā sahassilaukadhātuṃ pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati| yaipi tattha sattā upapan‍nā taipi pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sahassassa brahmunau sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau taṃ cittaṃ dahati, taṃ cittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, taṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti| tassa tai saṅkhārā ca vihārā ca aivaṃ bhāvitā aivaṃ bahulīkatā tatrupapattiyā saṃvattanti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā tatrupapattiyā saṃvattati|

166. ‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhāya saman‍nāgatau hauti, sīlaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, sutaina… cāgaina… pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau hauti| tassa sutaṃ hauti – dvisahassau brahmā…pai॰… tisahassau brahmā… catusahassau brahmā… pañ‍casahassau brahmā dīghāyukau vaṇṇavā  sukhabahulauti| pañ‍casahassau, bhikkhavai, brahmā pañ‍casahassilaukadhātuṃ pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati| yaipi tattha sattā upapan‍nā taipi pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, cakkhumā purisau pañ‍ca āmaṇḍāni hatthai karitvā pac‍cavaikkhaiyya; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍casahassau brahmā pañ‍casahassilaukadhātuṃ pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati| yaipi tattha sattā upapan‍nā taipi pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā pañ‍casahassassa brahmunau sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau taṃ cittaṃ dahati, taṃ cittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, taṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti| tassa  tai saṅkhārā ca vihārā ca aivaṃ bhāvitā aivaṃ bahulīkatā tatrupapattiyā saṃvattanti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā tatrupapattiyā saṃvattati|

167. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhāya saman‍nāgatau hauti, sīlaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, sutaina… cāgaina… pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau hauti| tassa sutaṃ hauti – ‘dasasahassau  brahmā dīghāyukau vaṇṇavā sukhabahulau’ti| dasasahassau, bhikkhavai, brahmā dasasahassilaukadhātuṃ pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā  viharati| yaipi tattha sattā upapan‍nā taipi pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, maṇi vaiḷuriyau subhau jātimā aṭṭhaṃsau suparikammakatau paṇḍukambalai nikkhittau bhāsatai ca tapatai ca bhāsati ca tapati ca (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) viraucati ca; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, dasasahassau brahmā dasasahassilaukadhātuṃ pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati| yaipi tattha sattā upapan‍nā taipi pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā dasasahassassa brahmunau sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau taṃ cittaṃ dahati, taṃ cittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, taṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti| tassa tai saṅkhārā ca vihārā ca aivaṃ bhāvitā aivaṃ bahulīkatā tatrupapattiyā saṃvattanti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā tatrupapattiyā saṃvattati|

168. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhāya saman‍nāgatau hauti, sīlaina… sutaina… cāgaina… pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau hauti| tassa sutaṃ hauti – ‘satasahassau brahmā dīghāyukau vaṇṇavā sukhabahulau’ti| satasahassau, bhikkhavai, brahmā satasahassilaukadhātuṃ pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati| yaipi tattha sattā upapan‍nā taipi pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, nikkhaṃ jambaunadaṃ naikkhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) dakkhakammāraputtauk‍kāmukhasukusalasampahaṭṭhaṃ  paṇḍukambalai  nikkhittaṃ bhāsatai ca tapatai ca viraucati ca; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, satasahassau brahmā satasahassilaukadhātuṃ pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati| yaipi tattha sattā upapan‍nā taipi pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā satasahassassa brahmunau sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau taṃ cittaṃ dahati, taṃ cittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, taṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti| tassa tai saṅkhārā ca vihārā ca aivaṃ bhāvitā aivaṃ bahulīkatā tatrupapattiyā saṃvattanti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā tatrupapattiyā saṃvattati|

169. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhāya saman‍nāgatau hauti, sīlaina… sutaina… cāgaina… pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau hauti| tassa sutaṃ hauti – ābhā daivā…pai॰… parittābhā daivā… appamāṇābhā daivā… ābhassarā daivā dīghāyukā vaṇṇavantau sukhabahulāti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā ābhassarānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau taṃ cittaṃ dahati, taṃ cittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, taṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti| tassa tai saṅkhārā ca vihārā ca aivaṃ bhāvitā aivaṃ bahulīkatā tatrupapattiyā saṃvattanti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā tatrupapattiyā saṃvattati|

170. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhāya saman‍nāgatau hauti, sīlaina … sutaina… cāgaina… pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau hauti| tassa sutaṃ hauti – parittasubhā daivā…pai॰… appamāṇasubhā daivā… subhakiṇhā daivā dīghāyukā vaṇṇavantau sukhabahulāti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā subhakiṇhānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau taṃ cittaṃ dahati, taṃ cittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, taṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti| tassa tai saṅkhārā ca vihārā ca aivaṃ bhāvitā aivaṃ bahulīkatā tatrupapattiyā saṃvattanti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā tatrupapattiyā saṃvattati|

171. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhāya saman‍nāgatau hauti, sīlaina… sutaina… cāgaina… pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau hauti| tassa  sutaṃ hauti – vaihapphalā daivā…pai॰… avihā daivā… atappā daivā… sudassā daivā… sudassī daivā… akaniṭṭhā daivā dīghāyukā vaṇṇavantau sukhabahulāti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā  akaniṭṭhānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau taṃ cittaṃ dahati, taṃ cittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, taṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti| tassa tai saṅkhārā ca vihārā  ca aivaṃ bhāvitā aivaṃ bahulīkatā tatrupapattiyā saṃvattanti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā tatrupapattiyā saṃvattati|

172. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhāya saman‍nāgatau hauti, sīlaina… sutaina… cāgaina… pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau hauti| tassa sutaṃ hauti – ‘ākāsānañ‍cāyatanūpagā daivā dīghāyukā ciraṭṭhitikā sukhabahulā’ti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā ākāsānañ‍cāyatanūpagānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau taṃ cittaṃ dahati, taṃ cittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, taṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti| tassa tai saṅkhārā ca vihārā ca aivaṃ bhāvitā aivaṃ bahulīkatā tatrupapattiyā saṃvattanti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā tatrupapattiyā saṃvattati|

173. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhāya saman‍nāgatau hauti, sīlaina… sutaina… cāgaina… pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau hauti| tassa sutaṃ hauti – ‘viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanūpagā daivā dīghāyukā ciraṭṭhitikā sukhabahulā’ti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanūpagānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau taṃ cittaṃ dahati, taṃ cittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, taṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti| tassa tai saṅkhārā ca vihārā ca aivaṃ bhāvitā aivaṃ bahulīkatā tatrupapattiyā saṃvattanti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā tatrupapattiyā saṃvattati|

174. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhāya saman‍nāgatau hauti, sīlaina… sutaina… cāgaina… pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau hauti| tassa sutaṃ hauti – ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanūpagā daivā…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanūpagā daivā dīghāyukā ciraṭṭhitikā sukhabahulāti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanūpagānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau taṃ cittaṃ dahati, taṃ cittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, taṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti| tassa tai saṅkhārā ca vihārā ca aivaṃ bhāvitā aivaṃ bahulīkatā tatrupapattiyā saṃvattanti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā tatrupapattiyā saṃvattati|

175. ‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhāya saman‍nāgatau hauti, sīlaina… sutaina… cāgaina…  pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti  – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti| sau āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na katthaci upapaj‍jatī’’ti na katthaci upapaj‍jati, na kuhiñ‍ci upapaj‍jatīti (sī॰ pī॰), na katthaci upapaj‍jati, na kuhiñ‍ci upasampaj‍ja viharatīti| (ka॰)|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

saṅkhārupapattisuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dasamaṃ|

anupadavaggau niṭṭhitau dutiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

anupāda-saudhana-paurisadhammau, saivitabba-bahudhātu-vibhatti|

buddhassa kittināma-cattārīsaina, ānāpānau kāyagatau upapatti itau paraṃ syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰ pautthakaisu aivampi dissati –-§candakai vimalai parisuddhai, puṇṇasammaudiniraudhaattanau|§dandhā bahujanasaivitaṃ dhammavaraṃ, yaṃ anupadaṃ vaggavaraṃ dutiyāti||


3. suñ‍ñatavaggau

1. cūḷasuñ‍ñatasuttaṃ

176. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati pubbārāmai migāramātupāsādai| atha khau āyasmā ānandau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aikamidaṃ, bhantai, samayaṃ bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati nagarakaṃ nāma sakyānaṃ nigamau| tattha mai, bhantai, bhagavatau sammukhā sutaṃ, sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘suñ‍ñatāvihāraināhaṃ, ānanda, aitarahi bahulaṃ viharāmī’ti| kac‍ci maitaṃ, bhantai, sussutaṃ suggahitaṃ sumanasikataṃ sūpadhārita’’nti? ‘‘taggha tai aitaṃ, ānanda, sussutaṃ suggahitaṃ sumanasikataṃ sūpadhāritaṃ| pubbaipāhaṃ pubbaicāhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), ānanda, aitarahipi aitarahi ca (sabbattha) suñ‍ñatāvihāraina bahulaṃ viharāmi| saiyyathāpi, ānanda, ayaṃ migāramātupāsādau suñ‍ñau hatthigavassavaḷavaina, suñ‍ñau jātarūparajataina, suñ‍ñau itthipurisasan‍nipātaina atthi caividaṃ asuñ‍ñataṃ yadidaṃ – bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṭic‍ca aikattaṃ; aivamaiva khau, ānanda, bhikkhu amanasikaritvā gāmasañ‍ñaṃ, amanasikaritvā manussasañ‍ñaṃ, arañ‍ñasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca manasi karauti aikattaṃ| tassa arañ‍ñasañ‍ñāya cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati adhimuc‍cati| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘yai assu darathā gāmasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca taidha na santi, yai assu darathā manussasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca taidha na santi, atthi caivāyaṃ darathamattā yadidaṃ – arañ‍ñasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca aikatta’nti| sau ‘suñ‍ñamidaṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ gāmasañ‍ñāyā’ti pajānāti, ‘suñ‍ñamidaṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ manussasañ‍ñāyā’ti pajānāti, ‘atthi caividaṃ asuñ‍ñataṃ yadidaṃ – arañ‍ñasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca aikatta’nti| iti yañhi khau tattha na hauti taina taṃ suñ‍ñaṃ samanupassati, yaṃ pana tattha avasiṭṭhaṃ  hauti taṃ ‘santamidaṃ  atthī’’’ti pajānāti| aivampissa aisā, ānanda, yathābhuc‍cā avipal‍latthā parisuddhā suñ‍ñatāvak‍kanti bhavati|

177. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhu amanasikaritvā manussasañ‍ñaṃ, amanasikaritvā arañ‍ñasañ‍ñaṃ, pathavīsañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca manasi karauti aikattaṃ| tassa pathavīsañ‍ñāya  cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati adhimuc‍cati| saiyyathāpi, ānanda, āsabhacammaṃ saṅkusataina suvihataṃ vigatavalikaṃ; aivamaiva khau, ānanda, bhikkhu yaṃ imissā pathaviyā uk‍kūlavik‍kūlaṃ nadīviduggaṃ khāṇukaṇṭakaṭṭhānaṃ pabbatavisamaṃ taṃ sabbaṃ sabbaṃ (ka॰) amanasikaritvā pathavīsañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca manasi karauti aikattaṃ| tassa pathavīsañ‍ñāya cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati adhimuc‍cati| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘yai assu darathā manussasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca taidha na santi, yai assu darathā arañ‍ñasañ‍ñaṃ  paṭic‍ca taidha na santi, atthi caivāyaṃ darathamattā yadidaṃ – pathavīsañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca aikatta’nti| sau ‘suñ‍ñamidaṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ manussasañ‍ñāyā’ti pajānāti, ‘suñ‍ñamidaṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ arañ‍ñasañ‍ñāyā’ti pajānāti, ‘atthi caividaṃ asuñ‍ñataṃ yadidaṃ – pathavīsañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca aikatta’nti| iti yañhi khau tattha na hauti taina taṃ suñ‍ñaṃ samanupassati, yaṃ pana tattha avasiṭṭhaṃ hauti taṃ ‘santamidaṃ atthī’ti pajānāti| aivampissa aisā, ānanda, yathābhuc‍cā avipal‍latthā parisuddhā suñ‍ñatāvak‍kanti bhavati|

178. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhu amanasikaritvā arañ‍ñasañ‍ñaṃ, amanasikaritvā pathavīsañ‍ñaṃ, ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca manasi karauti aikattaṃ| tassa ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñāya cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati adhimuc‍cati| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘yai assu darathā arañ‍ñasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca taidha na santi, yai assu darathā pathavīsañ‍ñaṃ  paṭic‍ca taidha na santi, atthi caivāyaṃ darathamattā yadidaṃ – ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca aikatta’nti| sau ‘suñ‍ñamidaṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ arañ‍ñasañ‍ñāyā’ti pajānāti, ‘suñ‍ñamidaṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ pathavīsañ‍ñāyā’ti pajānāti, ‘atthi caividaṃ asuñ‍ñataṃ yadidaṃ – ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca aikatta’nti| iti yañhi khau tattha na hauti taina taṃ suñ‍ñaṃ samanupassati, yaṃ pana tattha avasiṭṭhaṃ hauti taṃ ‘santamidaṃ atthī’ti pajānāti| aivampissa aisā, ānanda, yathābhuc‍cā avipal‍latthā parisuddhā suñ‍ñatāvak‍kanti bhavati|

179. ‘‘puna  caparaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhu amanasikaritvā pathavīsañ‍ñaṃ, amanasikaritvā ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca manasi karauti aikattaṃ| tassa viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñāya cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati adhimuc‍cati| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘yai assu darathā pathavīsañ‍ñaṃ  paṭic‍ca taidha na santi, yai assu darathā ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca taidha na santi, atthi caivāyaṃ darathamattā yadidaṃ – viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca aikatta’nti| sau ‘suñ‍ñamidaṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ pathavīsañ‍ñāyā’ti pajānāti, ‘suñ‍ñamidaṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñāyā’ti pajānāti, ‘atthi caividaṃ asuñ‍ñataṃ yadidaṃ – viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca aikatta’nti| iti yañhi khau tattha na hauti taina taṃ suñ‍ñaṃ samanupassati, yaṃ pana tattha avasiṭṭhaṃ hauti taṃ ‘santamidaṃ atthī’ti pajānāti| aivampissa aisā, ānanda, yathābhuc‍cā avipal‍latthā parisuddhā suñ‍ñatāvak‍kanti bhavati|

180. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhu amanasikaritvā ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ, amanasikaritvā viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ, ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca manasi karauti aikattaṃ| tassa ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñāya cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati adhimuc‍cati| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘yai assu darathā ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca taidha na  santi, yai assu darathā viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca taidha na santi, atthi caivāyaṃ darathamattā yadidaṃ – ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca aikatta’nti| sau ‘suñ‍ñamidaṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñāyā’ti pajānāti, ‘suñ‍ñamidaṃ  sañ‍ñāgataṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñāyā’ti pajānāti, ‘atthi caividaṃ asuñ‍ñataṃ yadidaṃ – ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca aikatta’nti| iti yañhi khau tattha na hauti taina taṃ suñ‍ñaṃ samanupassati, yaṃ pana tattha avasiṭṭhaṃ hauti taṃ ‘santamidaṃ atthī’ti pajānāti| aivampissa aisā, ānanda, yathābhuc‍cā avipal‍latthā parisuddhā suñ‍ñatāvak‍kanti bhavati|

181. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda bhikkhu amanasikaritvā viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ, amanasikaritvā ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca manasi karauti aikattaṃ| tassa naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñāya cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati adhimuc‍cati| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘yai assu darathā viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca taidha na  santi, yai assu darathā ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca taidha na santi, atthi caivāyaṃ darathamattā yadidaṃ – naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca aikatta’nti| sau ‘suñ‍ñamidaṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñāyā’ti pajānāti, ‘suñ‍ñamidaṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñāyā’ti pajānāti, ‘atthi caividaṃ asuñ‍ñataṃ  yadidaṃ – naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca aikatta’nti| iti yañhi khau tattha na hauti taina taṃ suñ‍ñaṃ samanupassati, yaṃ pana tattha avasiṭṭhaṃ hauti taṃ ‘santamidaṃ atthī’ti pajānāti| aivampissa aisā, ānanda, yathābhuc‍cā avipal‍latthā parisuddhā suñ‍ñatāvak‍kanti bhavati|

182. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhu amanasikaritvā ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ, amanasikaritvā naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ, animittaṃ caitausamādhiṃ paṭic‍ca manasi karauti aikattaṃ| tassa animittai caitausamādhimhi cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati adhimuc‍cati| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘yai assu darathā ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca taidha na santi, yai assu darathā naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca taidha na santi, atthi caivāyaṃ darathamattā yadidaṃ – imamaiva kāyaṃ paṭic‍ca saḷāyatanikaṃ jīvitapac‍cayā’ti| sau ‘suñ‍ñamidaṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñāyā’ti pajānāti, ‘suñ‍ñamidaṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñāyā’ti pajānāti, ‘atthi caividaṃ asuñ‍ñataṃ yadidaṃ – imamaiva kāyaṃ paṭic‍ca saḷāyatanikaṃ jīvitapac‍cayā’ti| iti yañhi khau tattha na hauti taina taṃ suñ‍ñaṃ samanupassati, yaṃ pana tattha avasiṭṭhaṃ hauti taṃ ‘santamidaṃ atthī’ti pajānāti| aivampissa aisā, ānanda, yathābhuc‍cā avipal‍latthā parisuddhā suñ‍ñatāvak‍kanti bhavati|

183. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhu amanasikaritvā ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ, amanasikaritvā naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ, animittaṃ caitausamādhiṃ  paṭic‍ca manasi karauti aikattaṃ| tassa animittai caitausamādhimhi cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati adhimuc‍cati| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘ayampi khau animittau caitausamādhi abhisaṅkhatau abhisañ‍caitayitau’| ‘yaṃ khau pana kiñ‍ci abhisaṅkhataṃ abhisañ‍caitayitaṃ tadanic‍caṃ niraudhadhamma’nti pajānāti| tassa aivaṃ jānatau aivaṃ passatau kāmāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, bhavāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, avij‍jāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati| vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘yai assu darathā  kāmāsavaṃ paṭic‍ca taidha na santi, yai assu darathā bhavāsavaṃ paṭic‍ca taidha na santi, yai assu darathā avij‍jāsavaṃ paṭic‍ca taidha na santi, atthi caivāyaṃ darathamattā yadidaṃ – imamaiva kāyaṃ paṭic‍ca saḷāyatanikaṃ  jīvitapac‍cayā’ti| sau ‘suñ‍ñamidaṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ kāmāsavainā’ti pajānāti, ‘suñ‍ñamidaṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ bhavāsavainā’ti pajānāti, ‘suñ‍ñamidaṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ avij‍jāsavainā’ti pajānāti, ‘atthi caividaṃ asuñ‍ñataṃ yadidaṃ – imamaiva kāyaṃ paṭic‍ca saḷāyatanikaṃ jīvitapac‍cayā’ti| iti yañhi khau tattha na hauti taina taṃ suñ‍ñaṃ samanupassati, yaṃ pana tattha avasiṭṭhaṃ hauti taṃ ‘santamidaṃ atthī’ti pajānāti| aivampissa aisā, ānanda, yathābhuc‍cā avipal‍latthā  parisuddhā paramānuttarā suñ‍ñatāvak‍kanti bhavati|

184. ‘‘yaipi hi kaici, ānanda, atītamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā  vā parisuddhaṃ paramānuttaraṃ suñ‍ñataṃ upasampaj‍ja vihariṃsu, sabbai tai imaṃyaiva parisuddhaṃ paramānuttaraṃ suñ‍ñataṃ upasampaj‍ja vihariṃsu| yaipi yai (sī॰ pī॰) hi kaici, ānanda, anāgatamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā parisuddhaṃ paramānuttaraṃ suñ‍ñataṃ upasampaj‍ja viharissanti, sabbai tai imaṃyaiva parisuddhaṃ paramānuttaraṃ suñ‍ñataṃ upasampaj‍ja viharissanti| yaipi yai (sī॰ pī॰) hi kaici, ānanda, aitarahi samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā parisuddhaṃ paramānuttaraṃ suñ‍ñataṃ upasampaj‍ja viharanti, sabbai tai imaṃyaiva parisuddhaṃ paramānuttaraṃ suñ‍ñataṃ upasampaj‍ja viharanti| tasmātiha, ānanda, ‘parisuddhaṃ paramānuttaraṃ suñ‍ñataṃ upasampaj‍ja viharissāmā’ti viharissāmīti (pī॰ ka॰) – aivañhi vau tai (ka॰), ānanda, sikkhitabba’’nti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

cūḷasuñ‍ñatasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. mahāsuñ‍ñatasuttaṃ

185. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati kapilavatthusmiṃ nigraudhārāmai| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya kapilavatthuṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| kapilavatthusmiṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau yaina kāḷakhaimakassa sak‍kassa vihārau tainupasaṅkami divāvihārāya| taina khau pana samayaina kāḷakhaimakassa  sak‍kassa vihārai sambahulāni saināsanāni pañ‍ñattāni haunti| addasā khau bhagavā kāḷakhaimakassa sak‍kassa vihārai sambahulāni saināsanāni  pañ‍ñattāni| disvāna bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘sambahulāni khau kāḷakhaimakassa sak‍kassa vihārai saināsanāni pañ‍ñattāni| sambahulā nu khau idha bhikkhū viharantī’’ti|

186. taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā ānandau sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ ghaṭāya sak‍kassa vihārai cīvarakammaṃ karauti| atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina ghaṭāya sak‍kassa vihārau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘sambahulāni khau, ānanda, kāḷakhaimakassa  sak‍kassa vihārai saināsanāni pañ‍ñattāni| sambahulā nu khau aittha bhikkhū viharantī’’ti? ‘‘sambahulāni, bhantai, kāḷakhaimakassa sak‍kassa vihārai saināsanāni pañ‍ñattāni| sambahulā bhikkhū aittha viharanti| cīvarakārasamayau nau, bhantai, vattatī’’ti|

‘‘na khau, ānanda, bhikkhu saubhati saṅgaṇikārāmau saṅgaṇikaratau saṅgaṇikārāmataṃ anuyuttau gaṇārāmau gaṇaratau gaṇasammuditau| sau vatānanda, bhikkhu saṅgaṇikārāmau saṅgaṇikaratau saṅgaṇikārāmataṃ anuyuttau gaṇārāmau gaṇaratau gaṇasammuditau yaṃ taṃ naikkhammasukhaṃ pavivaikasukhaṃ upasamasukhaṃ sambaudhisukhaṃ sambaudhasukhaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), sambaudhasukhaṃ cittaikaggatāsukhaṃ (ka॰) upari araṇavibhaṅgasuttai pana sambaudhisukhantvaiva dissati tassa sukhassa nikāmalābhī bhavissati akicchalābhī akasiralābhīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| yau ca khau sau, ānanda, bhikkhu aikau gaṇasmā vūpakaṭṭhau viharati tassaitaṃ bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ yaṃ taṃ naikkhammasukhaṃ pavivaikasukhaṃ upasamasukhaṃ sambaudhisukhaṃ  tassa sukhassa nikāmalābhī bhavissati akicchalābhī akasiralābhīti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sau vatānanda, bhikkhu saṅgaṇikārāmau saṅgaṇikaratau saṅgaṇikārāmataṃ anuyuttau gaṇārāmau gaṇaratau gaṇasammuditau sāmāyikaṃ vā kantaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharissati asāmāyikaṃ vā akuppanti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| yau ca khau sau, ānanda, bhikkhu aikau gaṇasmā vūpakaṭṭhau  viharati tassaitaṃ bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ sāmāyikaṃ vā kantaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ  upasampaj‍ja viharissati asāmāyikaṃ vā akuppanti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘nāhaṃ, ānanda, aikaṃ rūpampi aikarūpampi (sī॰) samanupassāmi yattha rattassa yathābhiratassa rūpassa vipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā na uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassūpāyāsā|

187. ‘‘ayaṃ  khau panānanda, vihārau tathāgataina abhisambuddhau yadidaṃ – sabbanimittānaṃ amanasikārā ajjhattaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ upasampaj‍ja viharituṃ viharataṃ (ka॰ sī॰), viharati (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰)| tatra cai, ānanda, tathāgataṃ iminā vihāraina viharantaṃ bhavanti bhagavantaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) upasaṅkamitārau bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau upāsakā upāsikāyau rājānau rājamahāmattā titthiyā titthiyasāvakā| tatrānanda, tathāgatau vivaikanin‍nainaiva cittaina vivaikapauṇaina vivaikapabbhāraina vūpakaṭṭhaina naikkhammābhirataina byantībhūtaina sabbasau āsavaṭṭhānīyaihi dhammaihi añ‍ñadatthu uyyaujanikapaṭisaṃyuttaṃyaiva kathaṃ kattā hauti| tasmātihānanda, bhikkhu caipi ākaṅkhaiyya – ‘ajjhattaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti, tainānanda, bhikkhunā ajjhattamaiva cittaṃ saṇṭhapaitabbaṃ san‍nisādaitabbaṃ aikaudi kātabbaṃ samādahātabbaṃ|

188. ‘‘kathañ‍cānanda, bhikkhu ajjhattamaiva cittaṃ saṇṭhapaiti san‍nisādaiti aikaudiṃ karauti aikaudikarauti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) samādahati? idhānanda, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| aivaṃ khau, ānanda, bhikkhu ajjhattamaiva cittaṃ saṇṭhapaiti san‍nisādaiti aikaudiṃ karauti samādahati| sau  ajjhattaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ manasi karauti| tassa ajjhattaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ manasikarautau suñ‍ñatāya cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati| aivaṃ santamaitaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhu aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘ajjhattaṃ  suñ‍ñataṃ khau mai manasikarautau ajjhattaṃ suñ‍ñatāya cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍catī’ti| itiha tattha sampajānau hauti| sau bahiddhā suñ‍ñataṃ manasi karauti…pai॰… sau ajjhattabahiddhā suñ‍ñataṃ manasi karauti  …pai॰… sau ānaiñ‍jaṃ manasi karauti| tassa ānaiñ‍jaṃ manasikarautau ānaiñ‍jāya cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati| aivaṃ santamaitaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhu aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘ānaiñ‍jaṃ khau mai manasikarautau ānaiñ‍jāya cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍catī’ti| itiha tattha sampajānau hauti|

‘‘tainānanda, bhikkhunā tasmiṃyaiva purimasmiṃ samādhinimittai ajjhattamaiva cittaṃ saṇṭhapaitabbaṃ san‍nisādaitabbaṃ aikaudi kātabbaṃ samādahātabbaṃ| sau ajjhattaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ manasi karauti| tassa ajjhattaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ manasikarautau ajjhattaṃ suñ‍ñatāya cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuc‍cati| aivaṃ santamaitaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhu aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘ajjhattaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ khau mai manasikarautau ajjhattaṃ suñ‍ñatāya cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuc‍catī’ti| itiha tattha sampajānau hauti| sau bahiddhā suñ‍ñataṃ manasi karauti…pai॰… sau ajjhattabahiddhā suñ‍ñataṃ manasi karauti…pai॰… sau ānaiñ‍jaṃ manasi karauti| tassa ānaiñ‍jaṃ manasikarautau ānaiñ‍jāya cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuc‍cati| aivaṃ santamaitaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhu aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘ānaiñ‍jaṃ khau mai  manasikarautau ānaiñ‍jāya cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuc‍catī’ti| itiha tattha sampajānau hauti|

189. ‘‘tassa cai, ānanda, bhikkhunau iminā vihāraina viharatau caṅkamāya cittaṃ namati, sau caṅkamati – ‘aivaṃ maṃ caṅkamantaṃ nābhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavissantī’ti| itiha tattha sampajānau hauti| tassa cai, ānanda, bhikkhunau iminā vihāraina viharatau ṭhānāya cittaṃ namati, sau tiṭṭhati – ‘aivaṃ maṃ ṭhitaṃ nābhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavissantī’ti| itiha tattha sampajānau hauti| tassa cai, ānanda, bhikkhunau iminā vihāraina viharatau nisaj‍jāya cittaṃ namati, sau nisīdati – ‘aivaṃ maṃ nisin‍naṃ nābhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavissantī’ti| itiha tattha sampajānau hauti| tassa cai, ānanda, bhikkhunau iminā vihāraina viharatau sayanāya cittaṃ namati, sau sayati – ‘aivaṃ maṃ sayantaṃ nābhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavissantī’ti| itiha tattha sampajānau hauti|

‘‘tassa  cai, ānanda, bhikkhunau iminā vihāraina viharatau kathāya bhassāya (sī॰), bhāsāya (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) cittaṃ namati, sau – ‘yāyaṃ kathā hīnā gammā pauthuj‍janikā anariyā anatthasaṃhitā na nibbidāya na virāgāya na niraudhāya na upasamāya na abhiñ‍ñāya na sambaudhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati, saiyyathidaṃ – rājakathā caurakathā mahāmattakathā sainākathā bhayakathā yuddhakathā  an‍nakathā pānakathā vatthakathā sayanakathā mālākathā gandhakathā ñātikathā yānakathā gāmakathā nigamakathā nagarakathā janapadakathā itthikathā surākathā visikhākathā kumbhaṭṭhānakathā pubbapaitakathā nānattakathā laukakkhāyikā samuddakkhāyikā itibhavābhavakathā iti vā iti – aivarūpiṃ kathaṃ na kathaissāmī’ti| itiha tattha sampajānau hauti| yā ca khau ayaṃ, ānanda, kathā abhisal‍laikhikā caitauvinīvaraṇasappāyā caitauvicāraṇasappāyā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), caitauvivaraṇasappāyā (pī॰) aikantanibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati, saiyyathidaṃ – appicchakathā santuṭṭhikathā pavivaikakathā asaṃsaggakathā vīriyārambhakathā sīlakathā samādhikathā pañ‍ñākathā vimuttikathā vimuttiñāṇadassanakathā iti – ‘aivarūpiṃ kathaṃ kathaissāmī’ti| itiha tattha sampajānau hauti|

‘‘tassa cai, ānanda, bhikkhunau iminā vihāraina viharatau vitak‍kāya  cittaṃ namati, sau – ‘yai tai vitak‍kā hīnā gammā pauthuj‍janikā anariyā anatthasaṃhitā na nibbidāya na virāgāya na niraudhāya na upasamāya na abhiñ‍ñāya na sambaudhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattanti, saiyyathidaṃ – kāmavitak‍kau byāpādavitak‍kau vihiṃsāvitak‍kau iti aivarūpai vitak‍kai aivarūpaina vitak‍kaina (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) na vitak‍kaissāmī’ti| itiha tattha sampajānau hauti| yai ca khau imai, ānanda, vitak‍kā ariyā niyyānikā niyyanti tak‍karassa sammādukkhakkhayāya, saiyyathidaṃ – naikkhammavitak‍kau abyāpādavitak‍kau avihiṃsāvitak‍kau iti – ‘aivarūpai vitak‍kai aivarūpaina vitak‍kaina (ka॰) vitak‍kaissāmī’ti| itiha tattha sampajānau hauti|

190. ‘‘pañ‍ca  khau imai, ānanda, kāmaguṇā| katamai pañ‍ca? cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā, sautaviñ‍ñaiyyā  saddā… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyā gandhā… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā  rajanīyā – imai khau, ānanda, pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā yattha bhikkhunā abhikkhaṇaṃ sakaṃ cittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘atthi nu khau mai imaisu pañ‍casu kāmaguṇaisu añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vā āyatanai uppaj‍jati caitasau samudācārau’ti? sacai, ānanda, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘atthi khau mai imaisu pañ‍casu kāmaguṇaisu añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vā āyatanai uppaj‍jati caitasau samudācārau’ti, aivaṃ santamaitaṃ aivaṃ santaṃ (aṭṭha॰), ānanda, bhikkhu aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘yau khau imaisu pañ‍casu kāmaguṇaisu chandarāgau sau mai nappahīnau’ti| itiha tattha sampajānau hauti| sacai panānanda, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘natthi khau mai imaisu pañ‍casu kāmaguṇaisu añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vā āyatanai uppaj‍jati caitasau samudācārau’ti, aivaṃ santamaitaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhu aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘yau khau imaisu pañ‍casu kāmaguṇaisu chandarāgau sau mai pahīnau’ti| itiha tattha sampajānau hauti|

191. ‘‘pañ‍ca khau imai, ānanda, upādānakkhandhā yattha bhikkhunā udayabbayānupassinā vihātabbaṃ – ‘iti rūpaṃ iti rūpassa samudayau  iti rūpassa atthaṅgamau, iti vaidanā…  iti sañ‍ñā… iti saṅkhārā… iti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ iti viñ‍ñāṇassa samudayau iti viñ‍ñāṇassa atthaṅgamau’ti| tassa imaisu pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu udayabbayānupassinau viharatau yau pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu asmimānau sau pahīyati| aivaṃ santamaitaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhu aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘yau khau imaisu pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu asmimānau sau mai pahīnau’ti| itiha tattha sampajānau hauti| imai khau tai, ānanda, dhammā aikantakusalā kusalāyātikā dhammā aikantakusalāyatikā (sabbattha) aṭṭhakathāṭīkā aulaukaitabbā ariyā laukuttarā anavak‍kantā pāpimatā| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, ānanda, kaṃ atthavasaṃ sampassamānau arahati sāvakau satthāraṃ anubandhituṃ api paṇuj‍jamānau’’ti api panuj‍jamānaupīti (ka॰ sī॰), api payuj‍jamānauti (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)? ‘‘bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā bhagavaṃnaittikā bhagavaṃpaṭisaraṇā| sādhu vata, bhantai, bhagavantaṃyaiva paṭibhātu aitassa bhāsitassa atthau| bhagavatau sutvā bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti|

192. ‘‘na khau, ānanda, arahati sāvakau satthāraṃ anubandhituṃ, yadidaṃ suttaṃ gaiyyaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ tassa haitu vaiyyākaraṇassa haitu (ka॰)| taṃ kissa haitu? dīgharattassa dīgharattaṃ + assāti padacchaidau hi tai, ānanda, dhammā sutā dhātā vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā| yā ca khau ayaṃ, ānanda, kathā abhisal‍laikhikā caitauvinīvaraṇasappāyā aikantanibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya  nibbānāya saṃvattati, saiyyathidaṃ – appicchakathā santuṭṭhikathā pavivaikakathā asaṃsaggakathā vīriyārambhakathā sīlakathā samādhikathā  pañ‍ñākathā vimuttikathā vimuttiñāṇadassanakathā – aivarūpiyā khau, ānanda, kathāya haitu arahati sāvakau satthāraṃ anubandhituṃ api paṇuj‍jamānau|

‘‘aivaṃ santai khau, ānanda, ācariyūpaddavau hauti, aivaṃ santai antaivāsūpaddavau hauti, aivaṃ santai brahmacārūpaddavau hauti|

193. ‘‘kathañ‍cānanda, ācariyūpaddavau hauti? idhānanda, aikac‍cau satthā vivittaṃ saināsanaṃ bhajati arañ‍ñaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ pabbataṃ kandaraṃ giriguhaṃ susānaṃ vanapatthaṃ  abbhaukāsaṃ palālapuñ‍jaṃ| tassa tathāvūpakaṭṭhassa viharatau anvāvattanti anvāvaṭṭanti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) brāhmaṇagahapatikā naigamā caiva jānapadā ca| sau anvāvattantaisu brāhmaṇagahapatikaisu naigamaisu caiva jānapadaisu ca mucchaṃ nikāmayati mucchati kāmayati (sī॰ pī॰) aṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana na tathā dissati, gaidhaṃ āpaj‍jati, āvattati bāhul‍lāya| ayaṃ vuc‍catānanda, upaddavau upaddutau (sī॰ pī॰) ācariyau| ācariyūpaddavaina avadhiṃsu naṃ pāpakā akusalā dhammā saṃkilaisikā paunaubbhavikā paunaubhavikā (sī॰ pī॰) sadarā dukkhavipākā āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇiyā| aivaṃ khau, ānanda, ācariyūpaddavau hauti|

194. ‘‘kathañ‍cānanda, antaivāsūpaddavau hauti? tassaiva khau panānanda, satthu sāvakau tassa satthu vivaikamanubrūhayamānau vivittaṃ saināsanaṃ bhajati  arañ‍ñaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ pabbataṃ kandaraṃ giriguhaṃ susānaṃ vanapatthaṃ abbhaukāsaṃ palālapuñ‍jaṃ| tassa tathāvūpakaṭṭhassa viharatau anvāvattanti brāhmaṇagahapatikā naigamā caiva jānapadā ca| sau anvāvattantaisu  brāhmaṇagahapatikaisu naigamaisu caiva jānapadaisu ca mucchaṃ nikāmayati, gaidhaṃ āpaj‍jati, āvattati bāhul‍lāya| ayaṃ vuc‍catānanda, upaddavau antaivāsī| antaivāsūpaddavaina avadhiṃsu naṃ pāpakā akusalā dhammā saṃkilaisikā paunaubbhavikā sadarā dukkhavipākā āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇiyā| aivaṃ khau, ānanda, antaivāsūpaddavau hauti|

195. ‘‘kathañ‍cānanda, brahmacārūpaddavau hauti? idhānanda, tathāgatau laukai uppaj‍jati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ  buddhau bhagavā| sau vivittaṃ saināsanaṃ bhajati arañ‍ñaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ pabbataṃ kandaraṃ giriguhaṃ susānaṃ vanapatthaṃ abbhaukāsaṃ palālapuñ‍jaṃ| tassa tathāvūpakaṭṭhassa viharatau anvāvattanti brāhmaṇagahapatikā naigamā caiva jānapadā ca| sau anvāvattantaisu brāhmaṇagahapatikaisu naigamaisu caiva jānapadaisu ca na mucchaṃ nikāmayati, na gaidhaṃ āpaj‍jati, na āvattati  bāhul‍lāya| tassaiva khau panānanda, satthu sāvakau tassa satthu vivaikamanubrūhayamānau vivittaṃ saināsanaṃ bhajati arañ‍ñaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ pabbataṃ kandaraṃ giriguhaṃ susānaṃ vanapatthaṃ abbhaukāsaṃ palālapuñ‍jaṃ| tassa tathāvūpakaṭṭhassa viharatau anvāvattanti brāhmaṇagahapatikā naigamā caiva jānapadā ca| sau anvāvattantaisu brāhmaṇagahapatikaisu naigamaisu caiva jānapadaisu ca mucchaṃ nikāmayati, gaidhaṃ  āpaj‍jati, āvattati bāhul‍lāya| ayaṃ vuc‍catānanda, upaddavau brahmacārī| brahmacārūpaddavaina avadhiṃsu naṃ pāpakā akusalā dhammā saṃkilaisikā paunaubbhavikā sadarā dukkhavipākā āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇiyā| aivaṃ khau, ānanda, brahmacārūpaddavau hauti|

‘‘tatrānanda, yau caivāyaṃ ācariyūpaddavau, yau ca antaivāsūpaddavau ayaṃ taihi brahmacārūpaddavau dukkhavipākatarau caiva kaṭukavipākatarau ca, api ca vinipātāya saṃvattati|

196. ‘‘tasmātiha maṃ, ānanda, mittavatāya samudācaratha, mā sapattavatāya| taṃ vau bhavissati dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya|
