‘‘kathañ‍cānanda, satthāraṃ sāvakā sapattavatāya samudācaranti, nau mittavatāya? idhānanda, satthā sāvakānaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti anukampakau hitaisī anukampaṃ upādāya – ‘idaṃ vau hitāya, idaṃ vau sukhāyā’ti| tassa sāvakā na sussūsanti, na sautaṃ audahanti, na añ‍ñā cittaṃ  upaṭṭhapainti, vauk‍kamma ca satthusāsanā vattanti| aivaṃ khau, ānanda, satthāraṃ sāvakā sapattavatāya samudācaranti, nau mittavatāya|

‘‘kathañ‍cānanda, satthāraṃ sāvakā mittavatāya samudācaranti, nau sapattavatāya? idhānanda, satthā sāvakānaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti anukampakau hitaisī anukampaṃ upādāya – ‘idaṃ vau hitāya, idaṃ vau sukhāyā’ti| tassa sāvakā sussūsanti, sautaṃ audahanti, añ‍ñā cittaṃ upaṭṭhapainti, na  ca vauk‍kama satthusāsanā vattanti| aivaṃ khau, ānanda, satthāraṃ sāvakā mittavatāya samudācaranti, nau sapattavatāya|

‘‘tasmātiha  maṃ, ānanda, mittavatāya samudācaratha, mā sapattavatāya| taṃ vau bhavissati dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya| na vau ahaṃ, ānanda, tathā parak‍kamissāmi yathā kumbhakārau āmakai āmakamattai| niggayha niggayhāhaṃ, ānanda, vakkhāmi; pavayha pavayha, ānanda, vakkhāmi pavayha pavayha (sī॰ pī॰), paggayha paggayha ānanda vakkhāmi (ka॰)| yau sārau sau ṭhassatī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

mahāsuñ‍ñatasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. acchariyaabbhutasuttaṃ

197. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau sambahulānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantānaṃ upaṭṭhānasālāyaṃ san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ ayamantarākathā udapādi – ‘‘acchariyaṃ, āvusau, abbhutaṃ, āvusau, tathāgatassa mahiddhikatā mahānubhāvatā, yatra hi nāma tathāgatau  atītai buddhai parinibbutai chin‍napapañ‍cai chin‍navaṭumai pariyādin‍navaṭṭai sabbadukkhavītivattai jānissati anussarissati jānissati (ka॰) – ‘aivaṃjac‍cā tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ’ itipi, ‘aivaṃnāmā tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ’ itipi, ‘aivaṃgauttā tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ’ itipi, ‘aivaṃsīlā tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ’ itipi, ‘aivaṃdhammā tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ’ itipi, ‘aivaṃpañ‍ñā tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ’ itipi, ‘aivaṃvihārī tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ’ itipi, ‘aivaṃvimuttā tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ’ itipī’’ti! aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā ānandau tai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘acchariyā caiva, āvusau, tathāgatā acchariyadhammasaman‍nāgatā ca; abbhutā caiva, āvusau, tathāgatā abbhutadhammasaman‍nāgatā cā’’ti| ayañ‍ca  hidaṃ taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ antarākathā vippakatā hauti|

198. atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yainupaṭṭhānasālā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘kāya nuttha, bhikkhavai, aitarahi kathāya san‍nisin‍nā, kā ca pana vau antarākathā vippakatā’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhantai, amhākaṃ pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantānaṃ upaṭṭhānasālāyaṃ san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ ayamantarākathā udapādi – ‘acchariyaṃ, āvusau, abbhutaṃ, āvusau, tathāgatassa mahiddhikatā mahānubhāvatā, yatra hi nāma tathāgatau atītai buddhai parinibbutai chin‍napapañ‍cai chin‍navaṭumai pariyādin‍navaṭṭai sabbadukkhavītivattai jānissati – aivaṃjac‍cā tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ itipi, aivaṃnāmā… aivaṃgauttā… aivaṃsīlā… aivaṃdhammā.. aivaṃpañ‍ñā… aivaṃvihārī… aivaṃvimuttā tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ itipī’ti! aivaṃ vuttai, bhantai, āyasmā ānandau amhai aitadavauca – ‘acchariyā caiva, āvusau, tathāgatā acchariyadhammasaman‍nāgatā ca, abbhutā  caiva, āvusau, tathāgatā abbhutadhammasaman‍nāgatā cā’ti| ayaṃ khau nau, bhantai, antarākathā vippakatā; atha bhagavā anuppattau’’ti|

199. atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘tasmātiha taṃ, ānanda, bhiyyausaumattāya paṭibhantu tathāgatassa acchariyā abbhutadhammā’’ti abbhutā dhammāti (?)|

‘‘sammukhā  maitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau sutaṃ, sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘satau sampajānau, ānanda, baudhisattau tusitaṃ kāyaṃ upapaj‍jī’ti| yampi, bhantai, satau sampajānau baudhisattau tusitaṃ kāyaṃ upapaj‍ji idaṃpāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau acchariyaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ dhāraimi|

‘‘sammukhā maitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau sutaṃ, sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘satau sampajānau, ānanda, baudhisattau tusitai kāyai aṭṭhāsī’ti| yampi, bhantai, satau sampajānau baudhisattau tusitai kāyai aṭṭhāsi idaṃpāhaṃ idaṃpahaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), bhantai, bhagavatau acchariyaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ dhāraimi|

200. ‘‘sammukhā maitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau sutaṃ, sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘yāvatāyukaṃ, ānanda, baudhisattau tusitai kāyai aṭṭhāsī’ti| yampi, bhantai, yāvatāyukaṃ baudhisattau tusitai kāyai aṭṭhāsi idaṃpāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau acchariyaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ dhāraimi|

‘‘sammukhā maitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau sutaṃ, sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘satau sampajānau, ānanda, baudhisattau tusitā, kāyā cavitvā mātukucchiṃ auk‍kamī’ti| yampi, bhantai, satau sampajānau baudhisattau tusitā kāyā cavitvā mātukucchiṃ auk‍kami idaṃpāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau acchariyaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ dhāraimi|

201. ‘‘sammukhā maitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau sutaṃ, sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘yadā, ānanda, baudhisattau tusitā kāyā cavitvā mātukucchiṃ auk‍kamati, atha sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya appamāṇau uḷārau aubhāsau laukai pātubhavati atik‍kammaiva daivānaṃ daivānubhāvaṃ| yāpi tā laukantarikā aghā asaṃvutā andhakārā andhakāratimisā, yatthapimai candimasūriyā aivaṃmahiddhikā aivaṃmahānubhāvā  ābhāya nānubhaunti  tatthapi appamāṇau uḷārau aubhāsau laukai pātubhavati atik‍kammaiva daivānaṃ daivānubhāvaṃ| yaipi tattha sattā upapan‍nā taipi tainaubhāsaina añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ sañ‍jānanti – añ‍ñaipi kira, bhau, santi sattā idhūpapan‍nāti| ayañ‍ca dasasahassī laukadhātu saṅkampati sampakampati sampavaidhati  appamāṇau ca uḷārau aubhāsau laukai pātubhavati atik‍kammaiva daivānaṃ daivānubhāva’nti| yampi, bhantai…pai॰… idaṃpāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau acchariyaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ dhāraimi|

202. ‘‘sammukhā maitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau sutaṃ, sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘yadā, ānanda, baudhisattau mātukucchiṃ auk‍kantau hauti, cattārau daivaputtā catuddisaṃ ārakkhāya upagacchanti – mā naṃ baudhisattaṃ vā baudhisattamātaraṃ vā manussau vā amanussau vā kauci vā vihaiṭhaisī’ti| yampi, bhantai…pai॰… idaṃpāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau acchariyaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ dhāraimi|

203. ‘‘sammukhā maitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau sutaṃ, sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘yadā, ānanda, baudhisattau mātukucchiṃ auk‍kantau hauti, pakatiyā sīlavatī baudhisattamātā hauti viratā pāṇātipātā viratā adin‍nādānā viratā kāmaisumicchācārā viratā musāvādā viratā surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā’ti| yampi, bhantai…pai॰… idaṃpāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau acchariyaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ dhāraimi|

‘‘sammukhā  maitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau sutaṃ, sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘yadā, ānanda, baudhisattau mātukucchiṃ auk‍kantau hauti, na baudhisattamātu purisaisu mānasaṃ uppaj‍jati kāmaguṇūpasaṃhitaṃ, anatik‍kamanīyā ca baudhisattamātā hauti kainaci purisaina rattacittainā’ti| yampi, bhantai…pai॰… idaṃpāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau acchariyaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ dhāraimi|

‘‘sammukhā maitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau sutaṃ, sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘yadā, ānanda, baudhisattau mātukucchiṃ auk‍kantau hauti, lābhinī baudhisattamātā hauti pañ‍can‍naṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ| sā pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitā samaṅgībhūtā paricāraitī’ti| yampi, bhantai…pai॰… idaṃpāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau acchariyaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ dhāraimi|

204. ‘‘sammukhā  maitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau sutaṃ, sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘yadā, ānanda, baudhisattau mātukucchiṃ auk‍kantau hauti, na baudhisattamātu kaucidaiva ābādhau uppaj‍jati; sukhinī baudhisattamātā hauti akilantakāyā; baudhisattañ‍ca baudhisattamātā tiraukucchigataṃ  passati sabbaṅgapac‍caṅgaṃ ahīnindriyaṃ| saiyyathāpi, ānanda, maṇi vaiḷuriyau subhau jātimā aṭṭhaṃsau suparikammakatau| tatrāssa suttaṃ āvutaṃ nīlaṃ vā pītaṃ vā lauhitaṃ vā audātaṃ vā paṇḍusuttaṃ vā| tamainaṃ cakkhumā purisau hatthai karitvā pac‍cavaikkhaiyya – ayaṃ khau maṇi vaiḷuriyau subhau jātimā aṭṭhaṃsau suparikammakatau, tatridaṃ suttaṃ āvutaṃ nīlaṃ vā pītaṃ vā lauhitaṃ vā audātaṃ vā paṇḍusuttaṃ vāti| aivamaiva khau, ānanda, yadā baudhisattau mātukucchiṃ auk‍kantau hauti, na baudhisattamātu kaucidaiva ābādhau uppaj‍jati; sukhinī baudhisattamātā hauti akilantakāyā; baudhisattañ‍ca baudhisattamātā tiraukucchigataṃ passati sabbaṅgapac‍caṅgaṃ ahīnindriya’nti| yampi, bhantai…pai॰… idaṃpāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau acchariyaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ dhāraimi|

205. ‘‘sammukhā  maitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau sutaṃ, sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘sattāhajātai, ānanda, baudhisattai baudhisattamātā kālaṃ karauti, tusitaṃ kāyaṃ upapaj‍jatī’ti| yampi, bhantai…pai॰… idaṃpāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau acchariyaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ dhāraimi|

‘‘sammukhā maitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau sutaṃ, sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘yathā khau panānanda, añ‍ñā itthikā nava vā dasa vā māsai gabbhaṃ kucchinā pariharitvā vijāyanti, na haivaṃ baudhisattaṃ baudhisattamātā vijāyati| dasaiva māsāni baudhisattaṃ baudhisattamātā kucchinā pariharitvā vijāyatī’ti| yampi, bhantai…pai॰… idaṃpāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau acchariyaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ dhāraimi|

‘‘sammukhā maitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau sutaṃ, sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘yathā khau panānanda, añ‍ñā itthikā nisin‍nā vā nipan‍nā vā vijāyanti, na haivaṃ baudhisattaṃ baudhisattamātā vijāyati| ṭhitāva baudhisattaṃ baudhisattamātā vijāyatī’ti| yampi, bhantai…pai॰… idaṃpāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau acchariyaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ dhāraimi|

‘‘sammukhā  maitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau sutaṃ, sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘yadā, ānanda, baudhisattau mātukucchimhā nikkhamati, daivā naṃ paṭhamaṃ paṭiggaṇhanti pacchā manussā’ti| yampi, bhantai…pai॰… idaṃpāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau acchariyaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ dhāraimi|

206. ‘‘sammukhā  maitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau sutaṃ, sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘yadā, ānanda, baudhisattau mātukucchimhā nikkhamati, appattauva baudhisattau pathaviṃ hauti, cattārau naṃ daivaputtā paṭiggahaitvā mātu puratau ṭhapainti – attamanā, daivi, hauhi; mahaisakkhau tai puttau uppan‍nau’ti| yampi, bhantai…pai॰… idaṃpāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau acchariyaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ dhāraimi|

‘‘sammukhā maitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau sutaṃ, sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘yadā, ānanda, baudhisattau mātukucchimhā nikkhamati, visadauva nikkhamati amakkhitau udaina uddaina (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) amakkhitau saimhaina amakkhitau ruhiraina amakkhitau kainaci  asucinā suddhau visadau visuddhau (syā॰)| saiyyathāpi, ānanda, maṇiratanaṃ kāsikai vatthai nikkhittaṃ naiva maṇiratanaṃ kāsikaṃ vatthaṃ makkhaiti nāpi kāsikaṃ vatthaṃ maṇiratanaṃ makkhaiti| taṃ kissa haitu? ubhin‍naṃ suddhattā| aivamaiva khau, ānanda, yadā baudhisattau mātukucchimhā nikkhamati, visadauva nikkhamati amakkhitau udaina amakkhitau saimhaina amakkhitau ruhiraina amakkhitau kainaci asucinā suddhau visadau’ti| yampi, bhantai…pai॰… idaṃpāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau acchariyaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ dhāraimi|

‘‘sammukhā  maitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau sutaṃ, sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘yadā, ānanda, baudhisattau mātukucchimhā nikkhamati, dvai udakassa dhārā antalikkhā pātubhavanti – aikā sītassa, aikā uṇhassa; yaina baudhisattassa udakakic‍caṃ karaunti mātu cā’ti| yampi, bhantai…pai॰… idaṃpāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau acchariyaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ dhāraimi|

207. ‘‘sammukhā  maitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau sutaṃ, sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘sampatijātau, ānanda, baudhisattau samaihi pādaihi pathaviyaṃ patiṭṭhahitvā uttarābhimukhau sattapadavītihāraina gacchati, saitamhi chattai anudhāriyamānai, sabbā ca disā vilaukaiti, āsabhiñ‍ca vācaṃ bhāsati – aggauhamasmi laukassa, jaiṭṭhauhamasmi laukassa, saiṭṭhauhamasmi laukassa| ayamantimā jāti, natthi dāni punabbhavau’ti| yampi, bhantai…pai॰… idaṃpāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau acchariyaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ dhāraimi|

‘‘sammukhā maitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau sutaṃ, sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘yadā, ānanda, baudhisattau mātukucchimhā nikkhamati, atha sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya appamāṇau uḷārau aubhāsau laukai pātubhavati atik‍kammaiva daivānaṃ daivānubhāvaṃ| yāpi tā laukantarikā aghā asaṃvutā andhakārā andhakāratimisā yatthapimai candimasūriyā aivaṃmahiddhikā aivaṃmahānubhāvā ābhāya nānubhaunti tatthapi  appamāṇau uḷārau aubhāsau laukai pātubhavati atik‍kammaiva daivānaṃ daivānubhāvaṃ| yaipi tattha  sattā upapan‍nā taipi tainaubhāsaina añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ sañ‍jānanti – añ‍ñaipi kira, bhau, santi sattā idhūpapan‍nāti| ayañ‍ca dasasahassī laukadhātu saṅkampati sampakampati sampavaidhati, appamāṇau ca uḷārau aubhāsau laukai pātubhavati atik‍kammaiva daivānaṃ daivānubhāva’nti| yampi, bhantai…pai॰… idaṃpāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau acchariyaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ dhāraimī’’ti|

208. ‘‘tasmātiha tvaṃ, ānanda, idampi tathāgatassa acchariyaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ dhāraihi| idhānanda, tathāgatassa viditā vaidanā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti; viditā sañ‍ñā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti; viditā vitak‍kā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| idampi khau, tvaṃ, ānanda, tathāgatassa acchariyaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ dhāraihī’’ti| ‘‘yampi, bhantai, bhagavatau viditā vaidanā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti; viditā sañ‍ñā… viditā vitak‍kā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| idaṃpāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau acchariyaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ dhāraimī’’ti|

idamavauca āyasmā ānandau| samanuñ‍ñau satthā ahausi; attamanā ca tai bhikkhū āyasmatau ānandassa bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

acchariyaabbhutasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. bākulasuttaṃ

209. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā bākulau bak‍kulau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| atha khau acailakassapau āyasmatau  bākulassa purāṇagihisahāyau yaināyasmā  bākulau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā bākulaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau acailakassapau āyasmantaṃ bākulaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘kīvaciraṃ pabbajitausi, āvusau bākulā’’ti? ‘‘asīti mai, āvusau, vassāni pabbajitassā’’ti| ‘‘imaihi pana tai, āvusau bākula, asītiyā vassaihi katikkhattuṃ maithunau dhammau paṭisaivitau’’ti? ‘‘na khau maṃ, āvusau kassapa, aivaṃ pucchitabbaṃ – ‘imaihi pana tai, āvusau bākula, asītiyā vassaihi katikkhattuṃ maithunau dhammau paṭisaivitau’ti| aivañ‍ca khau maṃ, āvusau kassapa, pucchitabbaṃ – ‘imaihi pana tai, āvusau bākula, asītiyā vassaihi katikkhattuṃ kāmasañ‍ñā uppan‍napubbā’’’ti? ( ) (imaihi pana tai āvusau bak‍kula asītiyau vassaihi katikkhattuṃ kāmasañ‍ñā uppan‍napubbāti|) (sī॰ pī॰)

210. ‘‘asīti mai, āvusau, vassāni pabbajitassa nābhijānāmi kāmasañ‍ñaṃ uppan‍napubbaṃ| yaṃpāyasmā bākulau asītiyā vassaihi nābhijānāti kāmasañ‍ñaṃ uppan‍napubbaṃ idampi mayaṃ āyasmatau bākulassa acchariyaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ dhāraima|

‘‘asīti mai, āvusau, vassāni pabbajitassa nābhijānāmi byāpādasañ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… vihiṃsāsañ‍ñaṃ uppan‍napubbaṃ| yaṃpāyasmā bākulau asītiyā vassaihi nābhijānāti vihiṃsāsañ‍ñaṃ uppan‍napubbaṃ, idampi mayaṃ āyasmatau bākulassa acchariyaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ dhāraima|

‘‘asīti mai, āvusau, vassāni pabbajitassa nābhijānāmi kāmavitak‍kaṃ uppan‍napubbaṃ| yaṃpāyasmā bākulau asītiyā vassaihi nābhijānāti kāmavitak‍kaṃ uppan‍napubbaṃ, idampi mayaṃ āyasmatau bākulassa acchariyaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ dhāraima|

‘‘asīti  mai, āvusau, vassāni pabbajitassa nābhijānāmi byāpādavitak‍kaṃ…pai॰… vihiṃsāvitak‍kaṃ uppan‍napubbaṃ| yaṃpāyasmā bākulau asītiyā vassaihi  nābhijānāti vihiṃsāvitak‍kaṃ uppan‍napubbaṃ, idampi mayaṃ āyasmatau bākulassa acchariyaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ dhāraima|

211. ‘‘asīti  mai, āvusau, vassāni pabbajitassa nābhijānāmi gahapaticīvaraṃ sāditā| yaṃpāyasmā bākulau asītiyā vassaihi nābhijānāti gahapaticīvaraṃ sāditā, idampi mayaṃ āyasmatau bākulassa acchariyaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ dhāraima|

‘‘asīti mai, āvusau, vassāni pabbajitassa nābhijānāmi satthaina cīvaraṃ chinditā| yaṃpāyasmā bākulau asītiyā vassaihi nābhijānāti satthaina cīvaraṃ chinditā…pai॰… dhāraima|

‘‘asīti mai, āvusau, vassāni pabbajitassa nābhijānāmi sūciyā cīvaraṃ sibbitā…pai॰… nābhijānāmi rajanaina cīvaraṃ rajitā… nābhijānāmi kathinai kaṭhinai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) cīvaraṃ sibbitā… nābhijānāmi sabrahmacārīnaṃ cīvarakammai vicāritā sabrahmacārī cīvarakammai byāpāritā (sī॰ pī॰) … nābhijānāmi nimantanaṃ sāditā… nābhijānāmi aivarūpaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍napubbaṃ  – ‘ahau vata maṃ kauci nimantaiyyā’ti… nābhijānāmi antaragharai nisīditā… nābhijānāmi antaragharai bhuñ‍jitā… nābhijānāmi mātugāmassa anubyañ‍janasau nimittaṃ gahaitā… nābhijānāmi mātugāmassa dhammaṃ daisitā antamasau catuppadampi gāthaṃ… nābhijānāmi bhikkhunupassayaṃ upasaṅkamitā… nābhijānāmi bhikkhuniyā dhammaṃ daisitā… nābhijānāmi sikkhamānāya dhammaṃ daisitā… nābhijānāmi sāmaṇairiyā dhammaṃ daisitā… nābhijānāmi pabbājaitā… nābhijānāmi upasampādaitā… nābhijānāmi nissayaṃ dātā… nābhijānāmi sāmaṇairaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitā… nābhijānāmi jantāgharai nhāyitā… nābhijānāmi cuṇṇaina nhāyitā… nābhijānāmi sabrahmacārīgattaparikammai  vicāritā byāpāritā (sī॰ pī॰) … nābhijānāmi ābādhaṃ uppan‍napubbaṃ, antamasau gaddūhanamattampi… nābhijānāmi bhaisaj‍jaṃ upaharitā, antamasau haritakikhaṇḍampi… nābhijānāmi apassainakaṃ apassayitā… nābhijānāmi saiyyaṃ kappaitā| yaṃpāyasmā…pai॰… dhāraima|

‘‘asīti mai, āvusau, vassāni pabbajitassa nābhijānāmi gāmantasaināsanai vassaṃ upagantā| yaṃpāyasmā bākulau asītiyā vassaihi  nābhijānāti gāmantasaināsanai vassaṃ upagantā, idampi mayaṃ āyasmatau bākulassa acchariyaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ dhāraima|

‘‘sattāhamaiva khau  ahaṃ, āvusau, saraṇau raṭṭhapiṇḍaṃ bhuñ‍jiṃ; atha aṭṭhamiyaṃ añ‍ñā udapādi| yaṃpāyasmā bākulau sattāhamaiva saraṇau raṭṭhapiṇḍaṃ bhuñ‍ji; atha aṭṭhamiyaṃ añ‍ñā udapādi idampi mayaṃ āyasmatau bākulassa acchariyaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ dhāraima|

212. ‘‘labhaiyyāhaṃ, āvusau bākula, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai pabbaj‍jaṃ, labhaiyyaṃ upasampada’’nti| alattha khau acailakassapau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai pabbaj‍jaṃ, alattha upasampadaṃ| acirūpasampan‍nau panāyasmā kassapau aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau khau panāyasmā kassapau arahataṃ ahausi|

atha khau āyasmā bākulau aparaina samayaina avāpuraṇaṃ apāpuraṇaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) ādāya vihāraina vihāraṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivamāha – ‘‘abhik‍kamathāyasmantau, abhik‍kamathāyasmantau| aj‍ja mai parinibbānaṃ bhavissatī’’ti| ‘‘yaṃpāyasmā bākulau avāpuraṇaṃ ādāya vihāraina vihāraṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivamāha – ‘abhik‍kamathāyasmantau, abhik‍kamathāyasmantau; aj‍ja mai parinibbānaṃ bhavissatī’ti, idampi mayaṃ āyasmatau bākulassa acchariyaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ dhāraima’’|

āyasmā  bākulau majjhai bhikkhusaṅghassa nisin‍nakauva parinibbāyi| ‘‘yaṃpāyasmā bākulau majjhai bhikkhusaṅghassa nisin‍nakauva parinibbāyi, idampi mayaṃ āyasmatau bākulassa acchariyaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ dhāraimā’’ti|

bākulasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. dantabhūmisuttaṃ

213. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina aciravatau samaṇuddaisau arañ‍ñakuṭikāyaṃ viharati| atha khau jayasainau rājakumārau jaṅghāvihāraṃ anucaṅkamamānau anuvicaramānau yaina aciravatau samaṇuddaisau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā aciravataina samaṇuddaisaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau jayasainau rājakumārau aciravataṃ samaṇuddaisaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhau aggivaissana – ‘idha bhikkhu appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau phusaiyya cittassa aikaggata’nti| ‘aivamaitaṃ, rājakumāra, aivamaitaṃ, rājakumāra| idha bhikkhu appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau phusaiyya cittassa aikaggata’nti| ‘sādhu mai bhavaṃ aggivaissanau yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ daisaitū’ti| ‘na khau tai ahaṃ, rājakumāra, sak‍kaumi yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ daisaituṃ| ahañ‍ca hi tai, rājakumāra, yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiyyaṃ, tvañ‍ca mai bhāsitassa atthaṃ na ājānaiyyāsi; sau mamassa kilamathau, sā mamassa vihaisā’ti| ‘daisaitu  mai bhavaṃ aggivaissanau yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ| appaivanāmāhaṃ bhautau aggivaissanassa  bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānaiyya’nti| ‘daisaiyyaṃ khau tai ahaṃ, rājakumāra, yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ| sacai mai tvaṃ bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānaiyyāsi, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ; nau cai mai tvaṃ bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānaiyyāsi, yathāsakai tiṭṭhaiyyāsi, na maṃ tattha uttariṃ paṭipucchaiyyāsī’ti| ‘daisaitu mai bhavaṃ aggivaissanau yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ| sacai ahaṃ bhautau aggivaissanassa bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānissāmi ājānaiyyāmi (ka॰), ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ; nau cai ahaṃ bhautau aggivaissanassa bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānissāmi, yathāsakai tiṭṭhissāmi tiṭṭhaiyyāmi (ka॰), nāhaṃ tattha bhavantaṃ aggivaissanaṃ uttariṃ paṭipucchissāmī’’’ti|

214. atha  khau aciravatau samaṇuddaisau jayasainassa rājakumārassa yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ daisaisi| aivaṃ vuttai, jayasainau rājakumārau aciravataṃ samaṇuddaisaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘aṭṭhānamaitaṃ, bhau aggivaissana, anavakāsau yaṃ bhikkhu appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau phusaiyya cittassa aikaggata’’nti| atha khau jayasainau rājakumārau aciravatassa samaṇuddaisassa aṭṭhānatañ‍ca anavakāsatañ‍ca pavaidaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi|

atha khau aciravatau samaṇuddaisau acirapak‍kantai jayasainai rājakumārai yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau aciravatau samaṇuddaisau yāvatakau ahausi jayasainaina rājakumāraina saddhiṃ kathāsal‍lāpau taṃ sabbaṃ bhagavatau āraucaisi|

aivaṃ  vuttai, bhagavā aciravataṃ samaṇuddaisaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘taṃ kutaittha, aggivaissana, labbhā| yaṃ taṃ naikkhammaina ñātabbaṃ naikkhammaina daṭṭhabbaṃ naikkhammaina pattabbaṃ naikkhammaina sacchikātabbaṃ taṃ vata jayasainau rājakumārau kāmamajjhai vasantau kāmai paribhuñ‍jantau kāmavitak‍kaihi khaj‍jamānau kāmapariḷāhaina pariḍayhamānau kāmapariyaisanāya  ussukau ussuk‍kau (sabbattha) ñassati vā dakkhati vā sacchi vā karissatī’’ti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

215. ‘‘saiyyathāpissu, aggivaissana, dvai hatthidammā vā assadammā vā gaudammā vā sudantā suvinītā, dvai hatthidammā vā assadammā vā gaudammā vā adantā avinītā| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, aggivaissana, yai tai dvai hatthidammā vā assadammā vā gaudammā vā sudantā suvinītā, api nu tai dantāva dantakāraṇaṃ gacchaiyyuṃ, dantāva dantabhūmiṃ sampāpuṇaiyyu’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yai pana tai dvai hatthidammā vā assadammā vā gaudammā vā adantā avinītā, api nu tai adantāva dantakāraṇaṃ gacchaiyyuṃ, adantāva dantabhūmiṃ sampāpuṇaiyyuṃ, saiyyathāpi tai dvai hatthidammā vā assadammā vā gaudammā vā sudantā suvinītā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, aggivaissana, yaṃ taṃ naikkhammaina ñātabbaṃ naikkhammaina daṭṭhabbaṃ naikkhammaina pattabbaṃ naikkhammaina sacchikātabbaṃ taṃ vata jayasainau rājakumārau kāmamajjhai vasantau kāmai  paribhuñ‍jantau  kāmavitak‍kaihi khaj‍jamānau kāmapariḷāhaina pariḍayhamānau kāmapariyaisanāya ussukau ñassati vā dakkhati vā sacchi vā karissatī’’ti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

216. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, aggivaissana, gāmassa vā nigamassa vā avidūrai mahāpabbatau| tamainaṃ dvai sahāyakā tamhā gāmā vā nigamā vā nikkhamitvā hatthavilaṅghakaina yaina sau pabbatau tainupasaṅkamaiyyuṃ; upasaṅkamitvā aikau sahāyakau haiṭṭhā pabbatapādai tiṭṭhaiyya, aikau sahāyakau uparipabbataṃ ārauhaiyya| tamainaṃ haiṭṭhā pabbatapādai ṭhitau sahāyakau uparipabbatai ṭhitaṃ sahāyakaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘yaṃ, samma, kiṃ tvaṃ passasi uparipabbatai ṭhitau’ti? sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘passāmi khau ahaṃ, samma, uparipabbatai ṭhitau ārāmarāmaṇaiyyakaṃ vanarāmaṇaiyyakaṃ bhūmirāmaṇaiyyakaṃ paukkharaṇīrāmaṇaiyyaka’’’nti|

‘‘sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘aṭṭhānaṃ khau aitaṃ, samma, anavakāsau yaṃ tvaṃ uparipabbatai ṭhitau passaiyyāsi ārāmarāmaṇaiyyakaṃ vanarāmaṇaiyyakaṃ bhūmirāmaṇaiyyakaṃ paukkharaṇīrāmaṇaiyyaka’nti| tamainaṃ uparipabbatai ṭhitau sahāyakau haiṭṭhimapabbatapādaṃ aurauhitvā taṃ sahāyakaṃ bāhāyaṃ gahaitvā uparipabbataṃ āraupaitvā muhuttaṃ assāsaitvā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘yaṃ, samma, kiṃ tvaṃ passasi uparipabbatai ṭhitau’ti? sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘passāmi khau ahaṃ, samma, uparipabbatai ṭhitau ārāmarāmaṇaiyyakaṃ vanarāmaṇaiyyakaṃ bhūmirāmaṇaiyyakaṃ paukkharaṇīrāmaṇaiyyaka’’’nti|

‘‘sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘idānaiva khau tai, samma, bhāsitaṃ – mayaṃ aivaṃ ājānāma – aṭṭhānaṃ khau aitaṃ samma, anavakāsau yaṃ tvaṃ uparipabbatai ṭhitau passaiyyāsi ārāmarāmaṇaiyyakaṃ vanarāmaṇaiyyakaṃ bhūmirāmaṇaiyyakaṃ paukkharaṇīrāmaṇaiyyaka’nti| idānaiva ca pana tai bhāsitaṃ mayaṃ aivaṃ ājānāma – ‘passāmi khau ahaṃ, samma, uparipabbatai ṭhitau ārāmarāmaṇaiyyakaṃ vanarāmaṇaiyyakaṃ bhūmirāmaṇaiyyakaṃ paukkharaṇīrāmaṇaiyyaka’nti| sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘tathā hi panāhaṃ, samma, iminā mahatā pabbataina āvutau āvaṭau (sī॰ aṭṭha॰ pī॰), āvuṭau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) daṭṭhaiyyaṃ nāddasa’’’nti|

‘‘atau  mahantataraina, aggivaissana, avij‍jākhandhaina jayasainau rājakumārau āvutau nivutau nivuṭau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) auphuṭau auvutau (sī॰), auvuṭau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pariyaunaddhau| sau vata yaṃ taṃ naikkhammaina ñātabbaṃ naikkhammaina daṭṭhabbaṃ naikkhammaina pattabbaṃ naikkhammaina sacchikātabbaṃ taṃ vata jayasainau rājakumārau kāmamajjhai vasantau kāmai paribhuñ‍jantau kāmavitak‍kaihi khaj‍jamānau kāmapariḷāhaina pariḍayhamānau kāmapariyaisanāya ussukau ñassati vā dakkhati vā sacchi vā karissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| sacai khau taṃ, aggivaissana, jayasainassa rājakumārassa imā dvai upamā paṭibhāyaiyyuṃ paṭibhāsaiyyuṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), anacchariyaṃ tai jayasainau rājakumārau pasīdaiyya, pasan‍nau ca tai pasan‍nākāraṃ karaiyyā’’ti| ‘‘kutau pana maṃ, bhantai, jayasainassa rājakumārassa imā dvai upamā paṭibhāyissanti paṭibhāsissanti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) anacchariyā pubbai assutapubbā, saiyyathāpi  bhagavanta’’nti?

217. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, aggivaissana, rājā khattiyau muddhāvasittau nāgavanikaṃ āmantaiti – ‘aihi tvaṃ, samma nāgavanika, rañ‍ñau nāgaṃ abhiruhitvā nāgavanaṃ pavisitvā ārañ‍ñakaṃ nāgaṃ atipassitvā rañ‍ñau nāgassa gīvāyaṃ upanibandhāhī’ti| ‘aivaṃ, daivā’ti khau, aggivaissana, nāgavanikau rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhāvasittassa paṭissutvā rañ‍ñau nāgaṃ abhiruhitvā nāgavanaṃ pavisitvā ārañ‍ñakaṃ nāgaṃ atipassitvā rañ‍ñau nāgassa gīvāyaṃ upanibandhati| tamainaṃ rañ‍ñau nāgau abbhaukāsaṃ nīharati| aittāvatā khau, aggivaissana, ārañ‍ñakau nāgau abbhaukāsaṃ gatau hauti| aitthagaidhā aitagaidhā (sī॰ pī॰) hi, aggivaissana, ārañ‍ñakā nāgā yadidaṃ – nāgavanaṃ| tamainaṃ nāgavanikau rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhāvasittassa āraucaisi – ‘abbhaukāsagatau khau khau tai (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), daiva, ārañ‍ñakau nāgau’ti| atha khau aggivaissana, tamainaṃ rājā khattiyau muddhāvasittau hatthidamakaṃ āmantaisi – ‘aihi tvaṃ, samma hatthidamaka, ārañ‍ñakaṃ nāgaṃ damayāhi ārañ‍ñakānañ‍caiva sīlānaṃ abhinimmadanāya ārañ‍ñakānañ‍caiva sarasaṅkappānaṃ abhinimmadanāya ārañ‍ñakānañ‍caiva darathakilamathapariḷāhānaṃ abhinimmadanāya gāmantai abhiramāpanāya manussakantaisu sīlaisu samādapanāyā’’’ti samādāpanāyāti (?)|

‘‘‘aivaṃ, daivā’ti khau, aggivaissana, hatthidamakau rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhāvasittassa paṭissutvā mahantaṃ thambhaṃ pathaviyaṃ nikhaṇitvā ārañ‍ñakassa nāgassa gīvāyaṃ upanibandhati ārañ‍ñakānañ‍caiva  sīlānaṃ abhinimmadanāya ārañ‍ñakānañ‍caiva sarasaṅkappānaṃ abhinimmadanāya ārañ‍ñakānañ‍caiva darathakilamathapariḷāhānaṃ abhinimmadanāya gāmantai abhiramāpanāya manussakantaisu sīlaisu samādapanāya| tamainaṃ hatthidamakau yā sā vācā nailā kaṇṇasukhā paimanīyā hadayaṅgamā paurī bahujanakantā bahujanamanāpā tathārūpāhi vācāhi samudācarati| yatau khau, aggivaissana, ārañ‍ñakau nāgau hatthidamakassa yā sā vācā nailā kaṇṇasukhā paimanīyā hadayaṅgamā paurī bahujanakantā bahujanamanāpā  tathārūpāhi vācāhi samudācariyamānau sussūsati, sautaṃ audahati, añ‍ñā cittaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti; tamainaṃ hatthidamakau uttari tiṇaghāsaudakaṃ anuppavaicchati|

‘‘yatau  khau, aggivaissana, ārañ‍ñakau nāgau hatthidamakassa tiṇaghāsaudakaṃ paṭiggaṇhāti, tatra hatthidamakassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘jīvissati khau nu khau (sī॰ ka॰) dāni ārañ‍ñakau rañ‍ñau (sī॰ pī॰) nāgau’ti| tamainaṃ hatthidamakau uttari kāraṇaṃ kāraiti – ‘ādiya, bhau, nikkhipa, bhau’ti| yatau khau, aggivaissana, ārañ‍ñakau nāgau hatthidamakassa ādānanikkhaipai vacanakarau hauti auvādappaṭikarau, tamainaṃ hatthidamakau uttari kāraṇaṃ kāraiti – ‘abhik‍kama, bhau, paṭik‍kama, bhau’ti| yatau khau, aggivaissana, ārañ‍ñakau nāgau hatthidamakassa abhik‍kamapaṭik‍kamavacanakarau hauti auvādappaṭikarau, tamainaṃ hatthidamakau uttari kāraṇaṃ kāraiti – ‘uṭṭhaha, bhau, nisīda, bhau’ti| yatau khau, aggivaissana, ārañ‍ñakau nāgau hatthidamakassa uṭṭhānanisaj‍jāya  vacanakarau hauti auvādappaṭikarau, tamainaṃ hatthidamakau uttari ānaiñ‍jaṃ nāma kāraṇaṃ kāraiti, mahantassa phalakaṃ sauṇḍāya upanibandhati, taumarahatthau ca purisau uparigīvāya nisin‍nau hauti, samantatau ca taumarahatthā purisā parivāraitvā ṭhitā haunti, hatthidamakau ca dīghataumarayaṭṭhiṃ gahaitvā puratau ṭhitau hauti| sau ānaiñ‍jaṃ kāraṇaṃ kāriyamānau naiva purimai pādai caupaiti na pacchimai pādai caupaiti, na purimakāyaṃ caupaiti na pacchimakāyaṃ caupaiti, na sīsaṃ caupaiti, na kaṇṇai caupaiti, na dantai caupaiti, na naṅguṭṭhaṃ caupaiti, na sauṇḍaṃ caupaiti| sau hauti ārañ‍ñakau nāgau khamau sattippahārānaṃ asippahārānaṃ usuppahārānaṃ sarapattappahārānaṃ parasatthappahārānaṃ (sī॰), parasattuppahārānaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) bhairipaṇavavaṃsasaṅkhaḍiṇḍimanin‍nādasaddānaṃ bhairipaṇavasaṅkhatiṇavanin‍nādasaddānaṃ (pī॰) sabbavaṅkadausanihitanin‍nītakasāvau rājārahau rājabhauggau rañ‍ñau aṅgantaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

218. ‘‘aivamaiva  khau, aggivaissana, idha tathāgatau laukai uppaj‍jati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā| sau imaṃ laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ samārakaṃ sabrahmakaṃ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ pajaṃ sadaivamanussaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti| sau dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| taṃ dhammaṃ suṇāti gahapati  vā gahapatiputtau vā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vā kulai pac‍cājātau| sau taṃ dhammaṃ sutvā tathāgatai saddhaṃ paṭilabhati| sau taina saddhāpaṭilābhaina saman‍nāgatau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘sambādhau gharāvāsau rajāpathau, abbhaukāsau pabbaj‍jā| nayidaṃ sukaraṃ agāraṃ ajjhāvasatā aikantaparipuṇṇaṃ aikantaparisuddhaṃ saṅkhalikhitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carituṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajaiyya’nti|

‘‘sau  aparaina samayaina appaṃ vā bhaugakkhandhaṃ pahāya mahantaṃ vā bhaugakkhandhaṃ pahāya appaṃ vā ñātiparivaṭṭaṃ pahāya mahantaṃ vā ñātiparivaṭṭaṃ pahāya kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati| aittāvatā khau, aggivaissana, ariyasāvakau abbhaukāsagatau hauti| aitthagaidhā hi, aggivaissana, daivamanussā yadidaṃ – pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā| tamainaṃ tathāgatau uttariṃ vinaiti – ‘aihi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, sīlavā hauhi, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharāhi ācāragaucarasampan‍nau, aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhassu sikkhāpadaisū’’’ti|

‘‘yatau khau, aggivaissana, ariyasāvakau sīlavā hauti, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu  bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu, tamainaṃ tathāgatau uttariṃ vinaiti – ‘aihi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, indriyaisu guttadvārau hauhi, cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā mā nimittaggāhī…pai॰… (yathā gaṇakamauggal‍lānasuttantai, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbāni|) 

219. ‘‘sau  imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya  caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| saiyyathāpi, aggivaissana, hatthidamakau mahantaṃ thambhaṃ pathaviyaṃ nikhaṇitvā ārañ‍ñakassa nāgassa gīvāyaṃ upanibandhati ārañ‍ñakānañ‍caiva sīlānaṃ abhinimmadanāya ārañ‍ñakānañ‍caiva sarasaṅkappānaṃ abhinimmadanāya ārañ‍ñakānañ‍caiva darathakilamathapariḷāhānaṃ abhinimmadanāya gāmantai abhiramāpanāya manussakantaisu sīlaisu samādapanāya; aivamaiva khau, aggivaissana, ariyasāvakassa imai cattārau satipaṭṭhānā caitasau upanibandhanā haunti gaihasitānañ‍caiva sīlānaṃ abhinimmadanāya gaihasitānañ‍caiva sarasaṅkappānaṃ abhinimmadanāya gaihasitānañ‍caiva darathakilamathapariḷāhānaṃ abhinimmadanāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya|

220. ‘‘tamainaṃ tathāgatau uttariṃ vinaiti – ‘aihi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, kāyai kāyānupassī viharāhi, mā ca kāmūpasaṃhitaṃ vitak‍kaṃ vitak‍kaisi| vaidanāsu… cittai… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharāhi, mā ca kāmūpasaṃhitaṃ vitak‍kaṃ vitak‍kaisī’’’ti|

‘‘sau vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ  upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati|

221. ‘‘sau  aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai sattānaṃ cutūpapātañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai, sugatai duggatai…pai॰… yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti|

‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai āsavānaṃ khayañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; ‘imai āsavā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ āsavasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| tassa  aivaṃ jānatau aivaṃ passatau kāmāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, bhavāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, avij‍jāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati| vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti|

‘‘sau hauti bhikkhu khamau sītassa uṇhassa jighacchāya pipāsāya ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasarīsapasamphassānaṃ  duruttānaṃ durāgatānaṃ vacanapathānaṃ, uppan‍nānaṃ sārīrikānaṃ  vaidanānaṃ dukkhānaṃ tibbānaṃ kharānaṃ kaṭukānaṃ asātānaṃ amanāpānaṃ pāṇaharānaṃ adhivāsakajātikau hauti sabbarāgadausamauhanihitanin‍nītakasāvau āhunaiyyau pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa|

222. ‘‘mahal‍lakau caipi, aggivaissana, rañ‍ñau nāgau adantau avinītau kālaṅkarauti, ‘adantamaraṇaṃ adantaṃ maraṇaṃ (ka॰) mahal‍lakau rañ‍ñau nāgau kālaṅkatau’tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati; majjhimau caipi, aggivaissana, rañ‍ñau nāgau| daharau caipi, aggivaissana, rañ‍ñau nāgau adantau avinītau kālaṅkarauti, ‘adantamaraṇaṃ daharau rañ‍ñau nāgau kālaṅkatau’tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati; aivamaiva khau, aggivaissana, thairau caipi bhikkhu akhīṇāsavau kālaṅkarauti, ‘adantamaraṇaṃ thairau bhikkhu kālaṅkatau’tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati; majjhimau caipi, aggivaissana, bhikkhu| navau caipi, aggivaissana, bhikkhu akhīṇāsavau kālaṅkarauti, ‘adantamaraṇaṃ navau bhikkhu kālaṅkatau’tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

‘‘mahal‍lakau  caipi, aggivaissana, rañ‍ñau nāgau sudantau suvinītau kālaṅkarauti, ‘dantamaraṇaṃ mahal‍lakau rañ‍ñau nāgau kālaṅkatau’tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati; majjhimau caipi, aggivaissana, rañ‍ñau nāgau… daharau caipi, aggivaissana, rañ‍ñau nāgau sudantau suvinītau kālaṅkarauti, ‘dantamaraṇaṃ daharau rañ‍ñau nāgau kālaṅkatau’tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati; aivamaiva khau, aggivaissana, thairau caipi bhikkhu khīṇāsavau kālaṅkarauti, ‘dantamaraṇaṃ thairau bhikkhu kālaṅkatau’tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati; majjhimau caipi, aggivaissana, bhikkhu| navau caipi, aggivaissana, bhikkhu khīṇāsavau kālaṅkarauti, ‘dantamaraṇaṃ navau bhikkhu kālaṅkatau’tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchatī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau aciravatau samaṇuddaisau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

dantabhūmisuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. bhūmijasuttaṃ

223. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| atha khau āyasmā bhūmijau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina jayasainassa rājakumārassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau jayasainau rājakumārau yaināyasmā bhūmijau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā bhūmijaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau jayasainau rājakumārau āyasmantaṃ bhūmijaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘santi, bhau bhūmija, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā  aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘āsañ‍caipi karitvā brahmacariyaṃ caranti, abhabbā carati, abhabbau (sī॰ pī॰) aivamuparipi aikavacanainaiva dissati phalassa adhigamāya; anāsañ‍caipi āsañ‍ca anāsañ‍ca caipi (aṭṭha॰) karitvā brahmacariyaṃ caranti, abhabbā phalassa adhigamāya; āsañ‍ca anāsañ‍caipi karitvā brahmacariyaṃ caranti, abhabbā phalassa adhigamāya; naivāsaṃ nānāsañ‍caipi karitvā brahmacariyaṃ caranti, abhabbā phalassa adhigamāyā’ti| idha bhautau bhūmijassa satthā kiṃvādī kiṃvādī kiṃdiṭṭhī (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) kimakkhāyī’’ti? ‘‘na khau maitaṃ, rājakumāra, bhagavatau sammukhā sutaṃ, sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ| ṭhānañ‍ca khau aitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ bhagavā aivaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘āsañ‍caipi karitvā ayaunisau brahmacariyaṃ caranti, abhabbā phalassa adhigamāya; anāsañ‍caipi karitvā ayaunisau brahmacariyaṃ caranti, abhabbā phalassa adhigamāya; āsañ‍ca anāsañ‍caipi karitvā ayaunisau brahmacariyaṃ caranti, abhabbā phalassa adhigamāya; naivāsaṃ nānāsañ‍caipi karitvā ayaunisau brahmacariyaṃ caranti, abhabbā phalassa adhigamāya| āsañ‍caipi karitvā yaunisau brahmacariyaṃ caranti, bhabbā phalassa adhigamāya; anāsañ‍caipi karitvā yaunisau brahmacariyaṃ caranti, bhabbā phalassa adhigamāya; āsañ‍ca anāsañ‍caipi karitvā yaunisau brahmacariyaṃ caranti, bhabbā phalassa adhigamāya; naivāsaṃ nānāsañ‍caipi karitvā yaunisau brahmacariyaṃ caranti, bhabbā phalassa adhigamāyā’ti| na khau mai taṃ, rājakumāra, bhagavatau sammukhā sutaṃ, sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ| ṭhānañ‍ca khau aitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ bhagavā aivaṃ byākaraiyyā’’ti| ‘‘sacai khau bhautau bhūmijassa satthā aivaṃvādī aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhī (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) aivamakkhāyī, addhā bhautau bhūmijassa satthā sabbaisaṃyaiva puthusamaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ muddhānaṃ buddhānaṃ (ka॰) muddhānantimuddhaṃ, matthakanti atthau mañ‍ñai āhac‍ca tiṭṭhatī’’ti| atha khau jayasainau rājakumārau āyasmantaṃ bhūmijaṃ sakainaiva thālipākaina parivisi|

224. atha khau āyasmā bhūmijau pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā bhūmijau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhantai, pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina jayasainassa rājakumārassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā  pañ‍ñattai  āsanai nisīdiṃ| atha khau, bhantai, jayasainau rājakumārau yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā mayā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau, bhantai, jayasainau rājakumārau maṃ aitadavauca – ‘santi, bhau bhūmija, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – āsañ‍caipi karitvā brahmacariyaṃ caranti, abhabbā phalassa adhigamāya; anāsañ‍caipi karitvā…pai॰… āsañ‍ca anāsañ‍caipi karitvā brahmacariyaṃ caranti, abhabbā phalassa adhigamāya; naivāsaṃ nānāsañ‍caipi  karitvā brahmacariyaṃ caranti, abhabbā phalassa adhigamāyā’ti| ‘idha bhautau bhūmijassa satthā kiṃvādī kimakkhāyī’ti? aivaṃ vuttai ahaṃ, bhantai, jayasainaṃ rājakumāraṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘na khau mai taṃ, rājakumāra, bhagavatau sammukhā sutaṃ, sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ| ṭhānañ‍ca khau aitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ bhagavā aivaṃ byākaraiyya – āsañ‍caipi karitvā ayaunisau brahmacariyaṃ caranti, abhabbā phalassa adhigamāya; anāsañ‍caipi karitvā ayaunisau brahmacariyaṃ caranti, abhabbā phalassa adhigamāya; āsañ‍ca anāsañ‍caipi karitvā ayaunisau brahmacariyaṃ caranti, abhabbā phalassa adhigamāya; naivāsaṃ nānāsañ‍caipi karitvā ayaunisau brahmacariyaṃ caranti, abhabbā phalassa adhigamāya| āsañ‍caipi karitvā yaunisau brahmacariyaṃ caranti, bhabbā phalassa adhigamāya; anāsañ‍caipi karitvā…pai॰… āsañ‍ca anāsañ‍caipi karitvā…pai॰… naivāsaṃ nānāsañ‍caipi karitvā yaunisau brahmacariyaṃ caranti, bhabbā phalassa adhigamāyāti| na khau mai taṃ, rājakumāra, bhagavatau sammukhā sutaṃ, sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ| ṭhānañ‍ca khau aitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ bhagavā aivaṃ byākaraiyyā’ti| ‘sacai bhautau bhūmijassa satthā aivaṃvādī aivamakkhāyī, addhā bhautau bhūmijassa satthā sabbaisaṃyaiva puthusamaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ muddhānaṃ mañ‍ñai āhac‍ca tiṭṭhatī’ti| ‘kac‍cāhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ puṭṭhau aivaṃ byākaramānau vuttavādī caiva bhagavatau haumi, na ca bhagavantaṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhāmi, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākaraumi, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānuvādau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchatī’’’ti?

‘‘taggha  tvaṃ, bhūmija, aivaṃ puṭṭhau aivaṃ byākaramānau vuttavādī caiva mai hausi, na ca maṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhasi, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākarausi, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānuvādau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchati| yai hi kaici, bhūmija, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā micchādiṭṭhinau micchāsaṅkappā micchāvācā  micchākammantā micchāājīvā micchāvāyāmā micchāsatī micchāsamādhinau tai āsañ‍caipi karitvā brahmacariyaṃ caranti, abhabbā phalassa adhigamāya; anāsañ‍caipi karitvā brahmacariyaṃ caranti, abhabbā phalassa adhigamāya; āsañ‍ca anāsañ‍caipi karitvā brahmacariyaṃ caranti, abhabbā phalassa adhigamāya; naivāsaṃ nānāsañ‍caipi karitvā brahmacariyaṃ caranti, abhabbā phalassa adhigamāya| taṃ kissa haitu? ayauni  haisā, bhūmija, phalassa adhigamāya|

225. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhūmija, purisau tailatthikau tailagavaisī tailapariyaisanaṃ caramānau vālikaṃ dauṇiyā ākiritvā udakaina paripphausakaṃ paripphausakaṃ pīḷaiyya| āsañ‍caipi karitvā vālikaṃ dauṇiyā ākiritvā udakaina paripphausakaṃ paripphausakaṃ pīḷaiyya, abhabbau tailassa adhigamāya; anāsañ‍caipi karitvā vālikaṃ dauṇiyā ākiritvā udakaina paripphausakaṃ paripphausakaṃ pīḷaiyya, abhabbau tailassa adhigamāya; āsañ‍ca anāsañ‍caipi karitvā vālikaṃ dauṇiyā ākiritvā udakaina paripphausakaṃ paripphausakaṃ pīḷaiyya, abhabbau tailassa adhigamāya; naivāsaṃ nānāsañ‍caipi karitvā vālikaṃ dauṇiyā ākiritvā udakaina paripphausakaṃ paripphausakaṃ pīḷaiyya, abhabbau tailassa adhigamāya| taṃ kissa haitu? ayauni haisā, bhūmija, tailassa adhigamāya| aivamaiva khau, bhūmija, yai hi kaici samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā micchādiṭṭhinau micchāsaṅkappā micchāvācā micchākammantā micchāājīvā micchāvāyāmā micchāsatī micchāsamādhinau tai āsañ‍caipi karitvā brahmacariyaṃ caranti, abhabbā phalassa adhigamāya; anāsañ‍caipi karitvā brahmacariyaṃ caranti, abhabbā phalassa adhigamāya; āsañ‍ca anāsañ‍caipi karitvā brahmacariyaṃ caranti, abhabbā phalassa adhigamāya; naivāsaṃ nānāsañ‍caipi karitvā brahmacariyaṃ caranti, abhabbā phalassa adhigamāya| taṃ  kissa haitu? ayauni haisā, bhūmija, phalassa adhigamāya|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhūmija, purisau khīratthikau khīragavaisī khīrapariyaisanaṃ caramānau gāviṃ taruṇavacchaṃ visāṇatau āviñchaiyya  āviñ‍jaiyya (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| āsañ‍caipi karitvā gāviṃ taruṇavacchaṃ visāṇatau āviñchaiyya, abhabbau khīrassa adhigamāya; anāsañ‍caipi karitvā…pai॰… āsañ‍ca anāsañ‍caipi karitvā…pai॰… naivāsaṃ  nānāsañ‍caipi karitvā gāviṃ taruṇavacchaṃ visāṇatau āviñchaiyya, abhabbau khīrassa adhigamāya| taṃ kissa haitu? ayauni haisā, bhūmija, khīrassa adhigamāya| aivamaiva khau, bhūmija, yai hi kaici samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā micchādiṭṭhinau…pai॰… micchāsamādhinau tai āsañ‍caipi karitvā brahmacariyaṃ caranti, abhabbā phalassa adhigamāya; anāsañ‍caipi karitvā…pai॰… āsañ‍ca anāsañ‍caipi karitvā…pai॰… naivāsaṃ nānāsañ‍caipi karitvā brahmacariyaṃ caranti, abhabbā phalassa adhigamāya| taṃ kissa haitu? ayauni haisā, bhūmija, phalassa adhigamāya|

226. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhūmija, purisau navanītatthikau navanītagavaisī navanītapariyaisanaṃ caramānau udakaṃ kalasai āsiñ‍citvā matthaina manthaina (sī॰), mattaina (ka॰) āviñchaiyya| āsañ‍caipi karitvā udakaṃ kalasai āsiñ‍citvā matthaina āviñchaiyya, abhabbau navanītassa adhigamāya; anāsañ‍caipi karitvā…pai॰… āsañ‍ca anāsañ‍caipi karitvā…pai॰… naivāsaṃ nānāsañ‍caipi karitvā udakaṃ kalasai āsiñ‍citvā matthaina āviñchaiyya, abhabbau navanītassa adhigamāya| taṃ kissa haitu? ayauni haisā, bhūmija, navanītassa adhigamāya| aivamaiva khau, bhūmija, yai hi kaici samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā micchādiṭṭhinau…pai॰… micchāsamādhinau tai āsañ‍caipi karitvā brahmacariyaṃ caranti, abhabbā  phalassa adhigamāya; anāsañ‍caipi karitvā…pai॰… āsañ‍ca anāsañ‍caipi karitvā…pai॰… naivāsaṃ nānāsañ‍caipi karitvā brahmacariyaṃ caranti, abhabbā phalassa adhigamāya| taṃ kissa haitu? ayauni haisā, bhūmija, phalassa adhigamāya|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhūmija, purisau aggitthikau aggatthikau (sī॰) aggigavaisī aggipariyaisanaṃ caramānau al‍laṃ kaṭṭhaṃ sasnaihaṃ uttarāraṇiṃ  ādāya abhimanthaiyya abhimatthaiyya (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰)| āsañ‍caipi karitvā al‍laṃ kaṭṭhaṃ sasnaihaṃ uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya abhimanthaiyya, abhabbau aggissa adhigamāya; anāsañ‍caipi karitvā…pai॰… āsañ‍ca anāsañ‍caipi karitvā…pai॰… naivāsaṃ nānāsañ‍caipi karitvā al‍laṃ kaṭṭhaṃ sasnaihaṃ uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya abhimanthaiyya, abhabbau aggissa adhigamāya| taṃ kissa haitu? ayauni haisā, bhūmija, aggissa adhigamāya| aivamaiva khau, bhūmija, yai hi kaici samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā  micchādiṭṭhinau…pai॰… micchāsamādhinau tai āsañ‍caipi karitvā  brahmacariyaṃ caranti, abhabbā phalassa adhigamāya; anāsañ‍caipi karitvā…pai॰…āsañ‍ca anāsañ‍caipi karitvā…pai॰… naivāsaṃ nānāsañ‍caipi karitvā brahmacariyaṃ caranti, abhabbā phalassa adhigamāya| taṃ kissa haitu? ayauni haisā, bhūmija, phalassa adhigamāya| yai hi kaici, bhūmija, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā sammādiṭṭhinau sammāsaṅkappā sammāvācā sammākammantā sammāājīvā sammāvāyāmā sammāsatī sammāsamādhinau tai āsañ‍caipi karitvā brahmacariyaṃ  caranti, bhabbā phalassa adhigamāya; anāsañ‍caipi karitvā brahmacariyaṃ caranti, bhabbā phalassa adhigamāya; āsañ‍ca anāsañ‍caipi karitvā brahmacariyaṃ caranti, bhabbā phalassa adhigamāya; naivāsaṃ nānāsañ‍caipi karitvā brahmacariyaṃ caranti, bhabbā phalassa adhigamāya| taṃ kissa haitu? yauni haisā, bhūmija, phalassa adhigamāya|

227. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhūmija, purisau tailatthikau tailagavaisī tailapariyaisanaṃ caramānau tilapiṭṭhaṃ dauṇiyā ākiritvā udakaina paripphausakaṃ paripphausakaṃ pīḷaiyya| āsañ‍caipi karitvā tilapiṭṭhaṃ dauṇiyā ākiritvā udakaina paripphausakaṃ paripphausakaṃ pīḷaiyya, bhabbau tailassa adhigamāya; anāsañ‍caipi karitvā…pai॰… āsañ‍ca anāsañ‍caipi karitvā…pai॰… naivāsaṃ nānāsañ‍caipi karitvā tilapiṭṭhaṃ dauṇiyā ākiritvā udakaina paripphausakaṃ paripphausakaṃ pīḷaiyya, bhabbau tailassa adhigamāya| taṃ kissa haitu? yauni haisā, bhūmija, tailassa adhigamāya| aivamaiva khau, bhūmija, yai hi kaici samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā sammādiṭṭhinau…pai॰… sammāsamādhinau tai āsañ‍caipi karitvā brahmacariyaṃ caranti, bhabbā  phalassa adhigamāya; anāsañ‍caipi karitvā…pai॰… āsañ‍ca anāsañ‍caipi karitvā…pai॰… naivāsaṃ nānāsañ‍caipi karitvā brahmacariyaṃ caranti, bhabbā phalassa adhigamāya| taṃ kissa haitu? yauni haisā, bhūmija, phalassa adhigamāya|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhūmija, purisau khīratthikau khīragavaisī khīrapariyaisanaṃ caramānau  gāviṃ taruṇavacchaṃ thanatau āviñchaiyya| āsañ‍caipi karitvā gāviṃ taruṇavacchaṃ thanatau āviñchaiyya, bhabbau khīrassa adhigamāya; anāsañ‍caipi karitvā…pai॰… āsañ‍ca anāsañ‍caipi karitvā…pai॰… naivāsaṃ nānāsañ‍caipi karitvā gāviṃ taruṇavacchaṃ thanatau āviñchaiyya, bhabbau khīrassa adhigamāya| taṃ kissa haitu? yauni haisā, bhūmija, khīrassa  adhigamāya| aivamaiva  khau, bhūmija, yai hi kaici samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā sammādiṭṭhinau…pai॰… sammāsamādhinau tai āsañ‍caipi karitvā…pai॰… anāsañ‍caipi karitvā…pai॰… āsañ‍ca anāsañ‍caipi karitvā…pai॰… naivāsaṃ nānāsañ‍caipi karitvā brahmacariyaṃ caranti, bhabbā phalassa adhigamāya| taṃ kissa haitu? yauni haisā, bhūmija, phalassa adhigamāya|

228. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhūmija, purisau navanītatthikau navanītagavaisī navanītapariyaisanaṃ caramānau dadhiṃ kalasai āsiñ‍citvā matthaina āviñchaiyya| āsañ‍caipi karitvā dadhiṃ kalasai āsiñ‍citvā matthaina āviñchaiyya, bhabbau navanītassa adhigamāya; anāsañ‍caipi karitvā… āsañ‍ca anāsañ‍caipi karitvā… naivāsaṃ nānāsañ‍caipi karitvā dadhiṃ kalasai āsiñ‍citvā matthaina āviñchaiyya, bhabbau navanītassa adhigamāya| taṃ kissa haitu? yauni haisā, bhūmija, navanītassa adhigamāya| aivamaiva khau, bhūmija, yai hi kaici samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā sammādiṭṭhinau…pai॰… sammāsamādhinau tai āsañ‍caipi karitvā brahmacariyaṃ caranti, bhabbā phalassa adhigamāya; anāsañ‍caipi karitvā… āsañ‍ca anāsañ‍caipi karitvā … naivāsaṃ nānāsañ‍caipi karitvā brahmacariyaṃ caranti, bhabbā phalassa adhigamāya| taṃ kissa haitu? yauni haisā, bhūmija, phalassa adhigamāya|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhūmija, purisau aggitthikau aggigavaisī aggipariyaisanaṃ caramānau sukkhaṃ kaṭṭhaṃ kauḷāpaṃ uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya abhimanthaiyya; ( ) (bhabbau aggissa adhigamāya) (sabbattha) āsañ‍caipi karitvā… anāsañ‍caipi  karitvā.. āsañ‍ca anāsañ‍caipi karitvā… naivāsaṃ nānāsañ‍caipi karitvā sukkha kaṭṭhaṃ kauḷāpaṃ uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya abhimanthaiyya, bhabbau aggissa adhigamāya| taṃ kissa haitu? yauni haisā, bhūmija, aggissa adhigamāya| aivamaiva khau, bhūmija, yai hi kaici samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā sammādiṭṭhinau…pai॰… sammāsamādhinau tai āsañ‍caipi karitvā brahmacariyaṃ caranti, bhabbā phalassa adhigamāya; anāsañ‍caipi karitvā brahmacariyaṃ caranti, bhabbā phalassa adhigamāya; āsañ‍ca anāsañ‍caipi karitvā brahmacariyaṃ caranti, bhabbā phalassa adhigamāya; naivāsaṃ nānāsañ‍caipi karitvā brahmacariyaṃ caranti, bhabbā phalassa adhigamāya| taṃ kissa haitu? yauni haisā, bhūmija, phalassa adhigamāya|

‘‘sacai  khau taṃ, bhūmija, jayasainassa rājakumārassa imā catassau upamā paṭibhāyaiyyuṃ anacchariyaṃ tai jayasainau rājakumārau pasīdaiyya, pasan‍nau ca tai pasan‍nākāraṃ karaiyyā’’ti| ‘‘kutau pana maṃ, bhantai, jayasainassa  rājakumārassa imā catassau upamā paṭibhāyissanti anacchariyā pubbai assutapubbā, saiyyathāpi bhagavanta’’nti?

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā bhūmijau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

bhūmijasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. anuruddhasuttaṃ

229. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau pañ‍cakaṅgau thapati añ‍ñataraṃ purisaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, yaināyasmā anuruddhau tainupasaṅkama; upasaṅkamitvā mama vacanaina āyasmatau anuruddhassa  pādai sirasā vandāhi vandāhi, aivañ‍ca vadaihi (sī॰ pī॰) – ‘pañ‍cakaṅgau, bhantai, thapati āyasmatau anuruddhassa pādai sirasā vandatī’ti; aivañ‍ca vadaihi aivañ‍ca vadaiti (sī॰ pī॰) – ‘adhivāsaitu kira, bhantai, āyasmā anuruddhau pañ‍cakaṅgassa thapatissa svātanāya attacatutthau bhattaṃ; yaina ca kira, bhantai, āyasmā anuruddhau pagaivataraṃ āgacchaiyya; pañ‍cakaṅgau, bhantai, thapati pañ‍cakaṅgau thapati (sī॰ pī॰) bahukic‍cau bahukaraṇīyau rājakaraṇīyainā’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau sau purisau pañ‍cakaṅgassa thapatissa paṭissutvā yaināyasmā anuruddhau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau purisau āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘pañ‍cakaṅgau, bhantai, thapati āyasmatau anuruddhassa pādai sirasā vandati, aivañ‍ca vadaiti – ‘adhivāsaitu kira, bhantai, āyasmā anuruddhau pañ‍cakaṅgassa thapatissa svātanāya attacatutthau bhattaṃ; yaina ca kira, bhantai, āyasmā anuruddhau pagaivataraṃ āgacchaiyya; pañ‍cakaṅgau, bhantai, thapati bahukic‍cau bahukaraṇīyau rājakaraṇīyainā’’’ti| adhivāsaisi khau  āyasmā anuruddhau tuṇhībhāvaina|

230. atha  khau āyasmā anuruddhau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina pañ‍cakaṅgassa thapatissa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau pañ‍cakaṅgau thapati āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaisi sampavāraisi| atha khau pañ‍cakaṅgau thapati āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ añ‍ñataraṃ nīcaṃ āsanaṃ gahaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau pañ‍cakaṅgau thapati āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘idha maṃ, bhantai, thairā bhikkhū upasaṅkamitvā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘appamāṇaṃ, gahapati, caitauvimuttiṃ  bhāvaihī’ti appamāṇā gahapati caitauvimutti bhāvaitabbāti (ka॰)| aikac‍cai thairā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘mahaggataṃ, gahapati, caitauvimuttiṃ bhāvaihī’ti| yā cāyaṃ, bhantai, appamāṇā caitauvimutti yā ca mahaggatā caitauvimutti – imai dhammā nānatthā caiva nānābyañ‍janā  ca, udāhu aikatthā byañ‍janamaiva nāna’’nti? ‘‘taina hi, gahapati, taṃ yaivaittha paṭibhātu| apaṇṇakantai itau bhavissatī’’ti| ‘‘mayhaṃ khau, bhantai, aivaṃ hauti – ‘yā cāyaṃ appamāṇā caitauvimutti yā ca mahaggatā caitauvimutti imai dhammā aikatthā byañ‍janamaiva nāna’’’nti| ‘‘yā cāyaṃ, gahapati, appamāṇā caitauvimutti yā ca mahaggatā caitauvimutti imai dhammā nānatthā caiva nānābyañ‍janā ca| tadamināpaitaṃ, gahapati, pariyāyaina vaiditabbaṃ yathā imai dhammā nānatthā caiva nānābyañ‍janā ca’’|

‘‘katamā ca, gahapati, appamāṇā caitauvimutti? idha, gahapati, bhikkhu maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ tathā tatiyaṃ tathā catutthaṃ; iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ maittāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyābajjhaina pharitvā viharati| karuṇāsahagataina caitasā… muditāsahagataina caitasā… upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ tathā tatiyaṃ tathā catutthaṃ; iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyābajjhaina pharitvā viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, gahapati, appamāṇā caitauvimutti|

231. ‘‘katamā  ca, gahapati, mahaggatā caitauvimutti? idha, gahapati, bhikkhu yāvatā aikaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ mahaggatanti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, gahapati, mahaggatā caitauvimutti| idha pana, gahapati, bhikkhu yāvatā dvai vā tīṇi vā rukkhamūlāni mahaggatanti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati| ayampi ayaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) vuc‍cati, gahapati, mahaggatā caitauvimutti| idha pana, gahapati, bhikkhu yāvatā aikaṃ gāmakkhaittaṃ mahaggatanti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati| ayampi vuc‍cati, gahapati, mahaggatā caitauvimutti| idha pana, gahapati, bhikkhu yāvatā dvai  vā tīṇi vā gāmakkhaittāni mahaggatanti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati| ayampi vuc‍cati, gahapati, mahaggatā caitauvimutti| idha pana, gahapati, bhikkhu yāvatā aikaṃ mahāraj‍jaṃ mahaggatanti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati| ayampi vuc‍cati, gahapati, mahaggatā caitauvimutti| idha pana, gahapati, bhikkhu yāvatā dvai vā  tīṇi vā mahāraj‍jāni mahaggatanti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati| ayampi vuc‍cati, gahapati, mahaggatā caitauvimutti| idha pana, gahapati, bhikkhu yāvatā samuddapariyantaṃ pathaviṃ mahaggatanti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati| ayampi vuc‍cati, gahapati, mahaggatā caitauvimutti| iminā khau aitaṃ, gahapati, pariyāyaina vaiditabbaṃ yathā imai dhammā nānatthā caiva nānābyañ‍janā ca|

232. ‘‘catassau khau imā gahapati, bhavūpapattiyau| katamā catassau? idha, gahapati, aikac‍cau ‘parittābhā’ti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā parittābhānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| idha pana, gahapati, aikac‍cau ‘appamāṇābhā’ti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā appamāṇābhānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| idha pana, gahapati, aikac‍cau ‘saṃkiliṭṭhābhā’ti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā saṃkiliṭṭhābhānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| idha pana, gahapati, aikac‍cau ‘parisuddhābhā’ti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā  viharati| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā parisuddhābhānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| imā khau, gahapati, catassau bhavūpapattiyau|

‘‘hauti khau sau, gahapati, samayau, yā tā daivatā aikajjhaṃ san‍nipatanti, tāsaṃ aikajjhaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ vaṇṇanānattañhi khau pañ‍ñāyati nau ca ābhānānattaṃ| saiyyathāpi, gahapati, purisau sambahulāni tailappadīpāni aikaṃ gharaṃ pavaisaiyya| taisaṃ aikaṃ gharaṃ pavaisitānaṃ ac‍cinānattañhi khau pañ‍ñāyaitha, nau ca ābhānānattaṃ; aivamaiva khau, gahapati, hauti khau sau samayau, yā tā daivatā aikajjhaṃ san‍nipatanti  tāsaṃ aikajjhaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ vaṇṇanānattañhi khau pañ‍ñāyati, nau ca ābhānānattaṃ|

‘‘hauti khau sau, gahapati, samayau, yā tā daivatā tatau vipak‍kamanti, tāsaṃ tatau vipak‍kamantīnaṃ vaṇṇanānattañ‍caiva pañ‍ñāyati ābhānānattañ‍ca| saiyyathāpi, gahapati, purisau tāni sambahulāni tailappadīpāni tamhā gharā nīharaiyya| taisaṃ tatau nīhatānaṃ nīharantānaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) ac‍cinānattañ‍caiva pañ‍ñāyaitha ābhānānattañ‍ca; aivamaiva khau, gahapati, hauti khau sau samayau, yā  tā daivatā tatau vipak‍kamanti, tāsaṃ tatau vipak‍kamantīnaṃ vaṇṇanānattañ‍caiva pañ‍ñāyati ābhānānattañ‍ca|

‘‘na khau, gahapati, tāsaṃ daivatānaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘idaṃ amhākaṃ nic‍canti  vā dhuvanti vā sassata’nti vā, api ca yattha yatthaiva tā yā (ka॰) daivatā abhinivisanti tattha tatthaiva tā daivatā abhiramanti| saiyyathāpi, gahapati, makkhikānaṃ kājaina vā piṭakaina vā harīyamānānaṃ na aivaṃ hauti – ‘idaṃ amhākaṃ nic‍canti vā dhuvanti vā sassata’nti vā, api ca yattha yatthaiva tā yā (ka॰) makkhikā abhinivisanti tattha tatthaiva tā makkhikā abhiramanti; aivamaiva khau, gahapati, tāsaṃ daivatānaṃ na aivaṃ hauti – ‘idaṃ amhākaṃ nic‍canti vā dhuvanti vā sassata’nti vā, api ca yattha yatthaiva tā daivatā abhinivisanti tattha tatthaiva tā daivatā abhiramantī’’ti|

233. aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā sabhiyau kac‍cānau kac‍cāyanau (sī॰) āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu, bhantai anuruddha! atthi ca mai aittha uttariṃ paṭipucchitabbaṃ| yā tā, bhantai, daivatā ābhā sabbā tā parittābhā udāhu santaittha aikac‍cā daivatā appamāṇābhā’’ti? ‘‘tadaṅgaina khau, āvusau kac‍cāna, santaittha aikac‍cā daivatā parittābhā, santi panaittha aikac‍cā daivatā appamāṇābhā’’ti| ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai anuruddha, haitu kau pac‍cayau yaina tāsaṃ daivatānaṃ aikaṃ daivanikāyaṃ upapan‍nānaṃ  santaittha  aikac‍cā daivatā parittābhā, santi panaittha aikac‍cā daivatā appamāṇābhā’’ti?

‘‘taina hāvusau kac‍cāna, taṃyaivaittha paṭipucchissāmi| yathā tai khamaiyya tathā naṃ byākaraiyyāsi| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, āvusau kac‍cāna, yvāyaṃ bhikkhu yāvatā aikaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ ‘mahaggata’nti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati, yaucāyaṃ yaupāyaṃ (ka॰) bhikkhu yāvatā dvai vā tīṇi vā rukkhamūlāni ‘mahaggata’nti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati – imāsaṃ ubhin‍naṃ cittabhāvanānaṃ katamā cittabhāvanā mahaggatatarā’’ti? ‘‘yvāyaṃ, bhantai, bhikkhu yāvatā dvai vā tīṇi vā rukkhamūlāni ‘mahaggata’nti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati – ayaṃ imāsaṃ ubhin‍naṃ cittabhāvanānaṃ mahaggatatarā’’ti|

‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, āvusau kac‍cāna, yvāyaṃ bhikkhu yāvatā dvai vā tīṇi vā rukkhamūlāni ‘mahaggata’nti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati, yaucāyaṃ bhikkhu yāvatā aikaṃ gāmakkhaittaṃ ‘mahaggata’nti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati – imāsaṃ ubhin‍naṃ cittabhāvanānaṃ katamā cittabhāvanā mahaggatatarā’’ti? ‘‘yvāyaṃ, bhantai, bhikkhu yāvatā aikaṃ gāmakkhaittaṃ ‘mahaggata’nti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati – ayaṃ imāsaṃ ubhin‍naṃ cittabhāvanānaṃ mahaggatatarā’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, āvusau kac‍cāna, yvāyaṃ bhikkhu yāvatā aikaṃ gāmakkhaittaṃ ‘mahaggata’nti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati, yaucāyaṃ bhikkhu yāvatā dvai vā tīṇi vā gāmakkhaittāni ‘mahaggata’nti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati – imāsaṃ ubhin‍naṃ cittabhāvanānaṃ katamā cittabhāvanā mahaggatatarā’’ti? ‘‘yvāyaṃ, bhantai, bhikkhu yāvatā dvai vā tīṇi vā gāmakkhaittāni ‘mahaggata’nti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati  – ayaṃ imāsaṃ ubhin‍naṃ cittabhāvanānaṃ mahaggatatarā’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, āvusau kac‍cāna, yvāyaṃ bhikkhu yāvatā dvai vā tīṇi vā gāmakkhaittāni ‘mahaggata’nti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā  viharati, yaucāyaṃ bhikkhu yāvatā aikaṃ mahāraj‍jaṃ ‘mahaggata’nti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati – imāsaṃ ubhin‍naṃ cittabhāvanānaṃ katamā cittabhāvanā  mahaggatatarā’’ti? ‘‘yvāyaṃ, bhantai, bhikkhu yāvatā aikaṃ mahāraj‍jaṃ ‘mahaggata’nti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati – ayaṃ imāsaṃ ubhin‍naṃ cittabhāvanānaṃ mahaggatatarā’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, āvusau kac‍cāna, yvāyaṃ bhikkhu yāvatā aikaṃ mahāraj‍jaṃ ‘mahaggata’nti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati, yaucāyaṃ bhikkhu yāvatā dvai vā tīṇi vā mahāraj‍jāni ‘mahaggata’nti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati – imāsaṃ ubhin‍naṃ cittabhāvanānaṃ katamā cittabhāvanā mahaggatatarā’’ti? ‘‘yvāyaṃ, bhantai, bhikkhu yāvatā dvai vā tīṇi vā mahāraj‍jāni ‘mahaggata’nti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati – ayaṃ imāsaṃ ubhin‍naṃ cittabhāvanānaṃ mahaggatatarā’’ti|

‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, āvusau kac‍cāna, yvāyaṃ bhikkhu yāvatā dvai vā tīṇi vā mahāraj‍jāni ‘mahaggata’nti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati, yaucāyaṃ bhikkhu yāvatā samuddapariyantaṃ pathaviṃ ‘mahaggata’nti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati – imāsaṃ ubhin‍naṃ cittabhāvanānaṃ katamā cittabhāvanā mahaggatatarā’’ti? ‘‘yvāyaṃ, bhantai, bhikkhu yāvatā samuddapariyantaṃ  pathaviṃ ‘mahaggata’nti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati – ayaṃ imāsaṃ ubhin‍naṃ cittabhāvanānaṃ mahaggatatarā’’ti? ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau kac‍cāna, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina tāsaṃ daivatānaṃ aikaṃ daivanikāyaṃ upapan‍nānaṃ santaittha aikac‍cā daivatā parittābhā, santi panaittha aikac‍cā daivatā appamāṇābhā’’ti|

234. ‘‘sādhu, bhantai anuruddha! atthi ca mai aittha uttariṃ paṭipucchitabbaṃ| yāvatā yā tā (ka॰), bhantai, daivatā ābhā sabbā tā saṃkiliṭṭhābhā udāhu santaittha aikac‍cā daivatā parisuddhābhā’’ti? ‘‘tadaṅgaina  khau, āvusau kac‍cāna, santaittha aikac‍cā daivatā saṃkiliṭṭhābhā, santi panaittha aikac‍cā daivatā parisuddhābhā’’ti| ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, anuruddha, haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina tāsaṃ daivatānaṃ aikaṃ daivanikāyaṃ upapan‍nānaṃ santaittha aikac‍cā daivatā saṃkiliṭṭhābhā, santi panaittha aikac‍cā daivatā parisuddhābhā’’ti?

‘‘taina, hāvusau kac‍cāna, upamaṃ tai karissāmi| upamāyapidhaikac‍cai upamāyamidhaikac‍cai (ka॰) viñ‍ñū purisā bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānanti| saiyyathāpi, āvusau kac‍cāna, tailappadīpassa jhāyatau tailampi aparisuddhaṃ vaṭṭipi aparisuddhā| sau tailassapi aparisuddhattā vaṭṭiyāpi aparisuddhattā andhandhaṃ viya jhāyati; aivamaiva khau, āvusau kac‍cāna, idhaikac‍cau bhikkhu ‘saṃkiliṭṭhābhā’ti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati, tassa kāyaduṭṭhul‍lampi na suppaṭippassaddhaṃ hauti, thinamiddhampi na susamūhataṃ hauti, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍campi na suppaṭivinītaṃ hauti| sau kāyaduṭṭhul‍lassapi na suppaṭippassaddhattā thinamiddhassapi na susamūhatattā uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cassapi na suppaṭivinītattā andhandhaṃ viya jhāyati| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā saṃkiliṭṭhābhānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| saiyyathāpi, āvusau kac‍cāna, tailappadīpassa jhāyatau tailampi parisuddhaṃ vaṭṭipi parisuddhā| sau tailassapi parisuddhattā vaṭṭiyāpi parisuddhattā na andhandhaṃ viya jhāyati; aivamaiva khau, āvusau kac‍cāna, idhaikac‍cau bhikkhu ‘parisuddhābhā’ti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā viharati| tassa kāyaduṭṭhul‍lampi suppaṭippassaddhaṃ hauti, thinamiddhampi susamūhataṃ hauti, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍campi  suppaṭivinītaṃ hauti| sau kāyaduṭṭhul‍lassapi suppaṭippassaddhattā thinamiddhassapi susamūhatattā uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cassapi suppaṭivinītattā na andhandhaṃ viya jhāyati| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā parisuddhābhānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| ayaṃ  khau, āvusau kac‍cāna, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau yaina tāsaṃ daivatānaṃ aikaṃ daivanikāyaṃ upapan‍nānaṃ santaittha aikac‍cā daivatā saṃkiliṭṭhābhā, santi panaittha aikac‍cā daivatā parisuddhābhā’’ti|

235. aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā sabhiyau kac‍cānau āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu, bhantai anuruddha! na, bhantai, āyasmā anuruddhau aivamāha – ‘aivaṃ mai suta’nti vā ‘aivaṃ arahati bhavitu’nti vā; atha ca pana, bhantai, āyasmā anuruddhau ‘aivampi tā daivatā, itipi tā daivatā’tvaiva bhāsati| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ hauti – ‘addhā āyasmatā anuruddhaina tāhi daivatāhi saddhiṃ san‍nivutthapubbañ‍caiva sal‍lapitapubbañ‍ca sākacchā ca samāpaj‍jitapubbā’’’ti| ‘‘addhā khau ayaṃ, āvusau kac‍cāna, āsaj‍ja upanīya vācā bhāsitā, api ca tai ahaṃ byākarissāmi – ‘dīgharattaṃ khau mai, āvusau  kac‍cāna, tāhi daivatāhi saddhiṃ san‍nivutthapubbañ‍caiva sal‍lapitapubbañ‍ca sākacchā ca samāpaj‍jitapubbā’’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā sabhiyau kac‍cānau pañ‍cakaṅgaṃ thapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘lābhā tai, gahapati, suladdhaṃ tai, gahapati, yaṃ tvañ‍caiva taṃ kaṅkhādhammaṃ pahāsi pajahasi (ka॰), mayañ‍cimaṃ yampimaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) dhammapariyāyaṃ alatthamhā savanāyā’’ti|

anuruddhasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. upak‍kilaisasuttaṃ

236. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina kausambiyaṃ bhikkhū bhaṇḍanajātā kalahajātā vivādāpan‍nā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ mukhasattīhi vitudantā viharanti| atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idha, bhantai, kausambiyaṃ bhikkhū bhaṇḍanajātā kalahajātā vivādāpan‍nā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ mukhasattīhi vitudantā viharanti| sādhu, bhantai, bhagavā yaina tai bhikkhū tainupasaṅkamatu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina| atha khau bhagavā yaina tai bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā tai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘alaṃ, bhikkhavai, mā bhaṇḍanaṃ, mā kalahaṃ, mā viggahaṃ, mā vivāda’’nti|

aivaṃ vuttai, añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āgamaitu, bhantai! bhagavā dhammassāmī; appaussuk‍kau, bhantai, bhagavā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāraṃ anuyuttau viharatu; mayamaitaina bhaṇḍanaina kalahaina viggahaina vivādaina pañ‍ñāyissāmā’’ti| dutiyampi khau bhagavā tai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘alaṃ, bhikkhavai, mā bhaṇḍanaṃ, mā kalahaṃ, mā viggahaṃ, mā vivāda’’nti| dutiyampi khau sau  bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āgamaitu, bhantai! bhagavā dhammassāmī; appaussuk‍kau, bhantai, bhagavā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāraṃ anuyuttau viharatu; mayamaitaina bhaṇḍanaina kalahaina viggahaina vivādaina pañ‍ñāyissāmā’’ti| tatiyampi  khau bhagavā tai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘alaṃ, bhikkhavai, mā bhaṇḍanaṃ, mā kalahaṃ, mā viggahaṃ, mā vivāda’’nti| tatiyampi khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āgamaitu, bhantai, bhagavā dhammassāmī; appaussuk‍kau, bhantai, bhagavā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāraṃ anuyuttau viharatu; mayamaitaina bhaṇḍanaina kalahaina viggahaina vivādaina pañ‍ñāyissāmā’’ti|

atha  khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya kausambiṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| kausambiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau saināsanaṃ saṃsāmaitvā pattacīvaramādāya ṭhitakauva imā gāthā abhāsi –

237.

 ‘‘puthusaddau  samajanau, na bālau kauci mañ‍ñatha|

saṅghasmiṃ bhij‍jamānasmiṃ, nāñ‍ñaṃ bhiyyau amañ‍ñaruṃ||

‘‘parimuṭṭhā paṇḍitābhāsā, vācāgaucarabhāṇinau|

yāvicchanti mukhāyāmaṃ, yaina nītā na taṃ vidū||

‘‘ak‍kaucchi maṃ avadhi maṃ, ajini maṃ ahāsi mai|

yai ca taṃ upanayhanti, vairaṃ taisaṃ na sammati||

‘‘ak‍kaucchi maṃ avadhi maṃ, ajini maṃ ahāsi mai|

yai  ca taṃ nupanayhanti, vairaṃ taisūpasammati||

‘‘na hi vairaina vairāni, sammantīdha kudācanaṃ|

avairaina ca sammanti, aisa dhammau sanantanau||

‘‘parai ca na vijānanti, mayamaittha yamāmasai|

yai ca tattha vijānanti, tatau sammanti maidhagā||

‘‘aṭṭhicchin‍nā pāṇaharā, gavassadhanahārinau|

raṭṭhaṃ vilumpamānānaṃ, taisampi hauti saṅgati|

kasmā tumhākaṃ nau siyā||

‘‘sacai  labhaitha nipakaṃ sahāyaṃ,

saddhiṃ caraṃ sādhuvihāri dhīraṃ|

abhibhuyya sabbāni parissayāni,

caraiyya tainattamanau satīmā||

‘‘nau  cai labhaitha nipakaṃ sahāyaṃ,

saddhiṃ caraṃ sādhuvihāri dhīraṃ|

rājāva raṭṭhaṃ vijitaṃ pahāya,

aikau carai mātaṅgarañ‍ñaiva nāgau||

‘‘aikassa caritaṃ saiyyau, natthi bālai sahāyatā|

aikau carai na ca pāpāni kayirā,

appaussuk‍kau mātaṅgarañ‍ñaiva nāgau’’ti||

238. atha  khau bhagavā ṭhitakauva imā gāthā bhāsitvā yaina bālakalauṇakāragāmau bālakalauṇakagāmau (ka॰), tathā vinayaipi tainupasaṅkami| taina khau pana samayaina  āyasmā bhagu bālakalauṇakāragāmai viharati| addasā khau āyasmā bhagu bhagavantaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna āsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaisi udakañ‍ca pādānaṃ dhauvanaṃ udakañ‍ca pādānaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| nisīdi bhagavā pañ‍ñattai āsanai| nisaj‍ja pādai pakkhālaisi| āyasmāpi khau bhagu bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ bhaguṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘kac‍ci, bhikkhu, khamanīyaṃ, kac‍ci yāpanīyaṃ, kac‍ci piṇḍakaina na kilamasī’’ti? ‘‘khamanīyaṃ bhagavā, yāpanīyaṃ bhagavā, na cāhaṃ, bhantai, piṇḍakaina kilamāmī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ bhaguṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā yaina pācīnavaṃsadāyau tainupasaṅkami|

taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā ca anuruddhau āyasmā ca nandiyau bhaddiyau (ma॰ ni॰ 2.166 naḷakapānai āyasmā ca kimilau kimbilau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pācīnavaṃsadāyai viharanti| addasā khau dāyapālau bhagavantaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mā, mahāsamaṇa, aitaṃ dāyaṃ pāvisi| santaittha tayau kulaputtā  attakāmarūpā viharanti| mā taisaṃ aphāsumakāsī’’ti| assausi khau āyasmā anuruddhau dāyapālassa bhagavatā saddhiṃ mantayamānassa| sutvāna dāyapālaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mā, āvusau dāyapāla, bhagavantaṃ vāraisi| satthā nau bhagavā anuppattau’’ti|

239. atha  khau āyasmā anuruddhau yaināyasmā ca nandiyau yaināyasmā ca kimilau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantañ‍ca nandiyaṃ āyasmantañ‍ca kimilaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kamathāyasmantau, abhik‍kamathāyasmantau, satthā nau bhagavā anuppattau’’ti| atha khau āyasmā ca anuruddhau āyasmā ca nandiyau āyasmā ca kimilau bhagavantaṃ pac‍cuggantvā aikau bhagavatau pattacīvaraṃ paṭiggahaisi, aikau āsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaisi, aikau pādaudakaṃ upaṭṭhapaisi| nisīdi bhagavā pañ‍ñattai āsanai| nisaj‍ja pādai pakkhālaisi| taipi khau āyasmantau bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘kac‍ci vau, anuruddhā, khamanīyaṃ, kac‍ci yāpanīyaṃ, kac‍ci piṇḍakaina na kilamathā’’ti? ‘‘khamanīyaṃ  bhagavā, yāpanīyaṃ bhagavā, na ca mayaṃ, bhantai, piṇḍakaina kilamāmā’’ti| ‘‘kac‍ci pana vau, anuruddhā, samaggā sammaudamānā avivadamānā khīraudakībhūtā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ piyacakkhūhi sampassantā viharathā’’ti? ‘‘taggha mayaṃ, bhantai, samaggā sammaudamānā avivadamānā khīraudakībhūtā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ piyacakkhūhi sampassantā viharāmā’’ti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tumhai, anuruddhā, samaggā sammaudamānā avivadamānā khīraudakībhūtā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ piyacakkhūhi sampassantā viharathā’’ti? ‘‘idha mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ hauti – ‘lābhā vata mai, suladdhaṃ vata  mai yauhaṃ aivarūpaihi sabrahmacārīhi saddhiṃ viharāmī’ti| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, imaisu āyasmantaisu maittaṃ kāyakammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ āvi caiva rahau ca, maittaṃ vacīkammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ āvi caiva rahau ca, maittaṃ manaukammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ āvi caiva rahau ca| tassa, mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ hauti – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ sakaṃ cittaṃ nikkhipitvā imaisaṃyaiva āyasmantānaṃ cittassa vasaina vattaiyya’nti| sau khau ahaṃ, bhantai, sakaṃ cittaṃ nikkhipitvā imaisaṃyaiva āyasmantānaṃ cittassa vasaina vattāmi| nānā hi khau nau, bhantai, kāyā, aikañ‍ca pana mañ‍ñai citta’’nti|

āyasmāpi khau nandiyau…pai॰… āyasmāpi khau kimilau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mayhampi khau, bhantai, aivaṃ hauti – ‘lābhā vata mai, suladdhaṃ vata mai yauhaṃ aivarūpaihi sabrahmacārīhi  saddhiṃ viharāmī’ti| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, imaisu āyasmantaisu maittaṃ kāyakammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ āvi caiva rahau ca, maittaṃ vacīkammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ āvi caiva rahau ca, maittaṃ manaukammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ  āvi caiva rahau ca| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ hauti – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ sakaṃ cittaṃ nikkhipitvā imaisaṃyaiva āyasmantānaṃ cittassa vasaina vattaiyya’nti| sau khau ahaṃ, bhantai, sakaṃ cittaṃ nikkhipitvā imaisaṃyaiva āyasmantānaṃ cittassa vasaina vattāmi| nānā hi khau nau, bhantai, kāyā, aikañ‍ca pana mañ‍ñai cittanti| aivaṃ khau mayaṃ, bhantai, samaggā sammaudamānā avivadamānā khīraudakībhūtā  añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ piyacakkhūhi sampassantā viharāmā’’ti|

240. ‘‘sādhu, sādhu, anuruddhā! kac‍ci pana vau, anuruddhā, appamattā ātāpinau pahitattā viharathā’’ti? ‘‘taggha  mayaṃ, bhantai, appamattā ātāpinau pahitattā viharāmā’’ti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tumhai, anuruddhā, appamattā ātāpinau pahitattā viharathā’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhantai, amhākaṃ yau paṭhamaṃ gāmatau piṇḍāya paṭik‍kamati, sau āsanāni pañ‍ñapaiti, pānīyaṃ paribhaujanīyaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti, avak‍kārapātiṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti| yau pacchā gāmatau piṇḍāya paṭik‍kamati – sacai hauti bhuttāvasaisau, sacai ākaṅkhati, bhuñ‍jati; nau cai ākaṅkhati, appaharitai vā chaḍḍaiti apāṇakai vā udakai aupilāpaiti – sau āsanāni paṭisāmaiti, pānīyaṃ paribhaujanīyaṃ paṭisāmaiti, avak‍kārapātiṃ dhauvitvā paṭisāmaiti, bhattaggaṃ sammaj‍jati| yau passati pānīyaghaṭaṃ vā paribhaujanīyaghaṭaṃ vā vac‍caghaṭaṃ vā rittaṃ tucchaṃ sau upaṭṭhāpaiti| sacassa hauti avisayhaṃ, hatthavikāraina dutiyaṃ āmantaitvā hatthavilaṅghakaina upaṭṭhāpaima upaṭṭhapaiti (sī॰), na tvaiva mayaṃ, bhantai, tappac‍cayā vācaṃ bhindāma| pañ‍cāhikaṃ khau pana mayaṃ, bhantai, sabbarattiṃ dhammiyā kathāya san‍nisīdāma| aivaṃ khau mayaṃ, bhantai, appamattā ātāpinau pahitattā viharāmā’’ti|

241. ‘‘sādhu, sādhu, anuruddhā! atthi pana vau, anuruddhā, aivaṃ appamattānaṃ ātāpīnaṃ pahitattānaṃ viharataṃ uttarimanussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisau  adhigatau phāsuvihārau’’ti? ‘‘idha mayaṃ, bhantai, appamattā ātāpinau pahitattā viharantā aubhāsañ‍caiva sañ‍jānāma dassanañ‍ca rūpānaṃ| sau khau pana nau aubhāsau nacirassaiva antaradhāyati dassanañ‍ca rūpānaṃ; tañ‍ca nimittaṃ nappaṭivijjhāmā’’ti|

‘‘taṃ  khau pana vau, anuruddhā, nimittaṃ paṭivijjhitabbaṃ| ahampi sudaṃ, anuruddhā, pubbaiva sambaudhā anabhisambuddhau baudhisattauva samānau aubhāsañ‍caiva sañ‍jānāmi dassanañ‍ca rūpānaṃ| sau khau pana mai aubhāsau nacirassaiva  antaradhāyati dassanañ‍ca rūpānaṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, anuruddhā, aitadahausi – ‘kau nu khau haitu kau pac‍cayau yaina mai aubhāsau antaradhāyati dassanañ‍ca rūpāna’nti? tassa mayhaṃ, anuruddhā, aitadahausi – ‘vicikicchā khau mai udapādi, vicikicchādhikaraṇañ‍ca pana mai samādhi cavi| samādhimhi cutai aubhāsau antaradhāyati dassanañ‍ca rūpānaṃ| sauhaṃ tathā karissāmi yathā mai puna na vicikicchā uppaj‍jissatī’’’ti|

‘‘sau khau ahaṃ, anuruddhā, appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau aubhāsañ‍caiva sañ‍jānāmi dassanañ‍ca rūpānaṃ| sau khau pana mai aubhāsau nacirassaiva antaradhāyati dassanañ‍ca rūpānaṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, anuruddhā, aitadahausi – ‘kau nu khau haitu kau pac‍cayau yaina mai aubhāsau antaradhāyati dassanañ‍ca rūpāna’nti? tassa mayhaṃ, anuruddhā, aitadahausi – ‘amanasikārau khau mai udapādi, amanasikārādhikaraṇañ‍ca pana mai samādhi cavi| samādhimhi cutai aubhāsau  antaradhāyati dassanañ‍ca rūpānaṃ| sauhaṃ tathā karissāmi yathā mai puna na vicikicchā uppaj‍jissati na amanasikārau’’’ti|

‘‘sau khau ahaṃ, anuruddhā…pai॰… tassa mayhaṃ, anuruddhā, aitadahausi – ‘thinamiddhaṃ khau mai udapādi, thinamiddhādhikaraṇañ‍ca pana mai samādhi cavi| samādhimhi cutai aubhāsau antaradhāyati dassanañ‍ca rūpānaṃ| sauhaṃ tathā karissāmi yathā mai puna na vicikicchā uppaj‍jissati na amanasikārau na thinamiddha’’’nti|

‘‘sau khau ahaṃ, anuruddhā…pai॰… tassa mayhaṃ, anuruddhā, aitadahausi – ‘chambhitattaṃ khau mai udapādi, chambhitattādhikaraṇañ‍ca pana mai samādhi cavi| samādhimhi cutai aubhāsau antaradhāyati dassanañ‍ca rūpānaṃ| saiyyathāpi, anuruddhā, purisau addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau, tassa ubhataupassai vaṭṭakā vadhakā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) uppataiyyuṃ, tassa tataunidānaṃ chambhitattaṃ uppaj‍jaiyya; aivamaiva khau mai, anuruddhā, chambhitattaṃ udapādi, chambhitattādhikaraṇañ‍ca pana mai samādhi cavi| samādhimhi  cutai aubhāsau  antaradhāyati dassanañ‍ca rūpānaṃ| sauhaṃ tathā karissāmi  yathā mai puna na vicikicchā uppaj‍jissati na amanasikārau na thinamiddhaṃ na chambhitatta’’’nti|

‘‘sau khau ahaṃ, anuruddhā…pai॰… tassa mayhaṃ, anuruddhā, aitadahausi – ‘uppilaṃ ubbil‍laṃ (sī॰ pī॰), ubbilaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) khau mai udapādi, uppilādhikaraṇañ‍ca pana mai samādhi cavi| samādhimhi cutai aubhāsau antaradhāyati dassanañ‍ca rūpānaṃ| saiyyathāpi, anuruddhā, purisau aikaṃ nidhimukhaṃ gavaisantau sakidaiva pañ‍canidhimukhāni adhigacchaiyya, tassa tataunidānaṃ uppilaṃ uppaj‍jaiyya; aivamaiva khau mai, anuruddhā, uppilaṃ udapādi, uppilādhikaraṇañ‍ca pana mai samādhi cavi| samādhimhi cutai aubhāsau antaradhāyati dassanañ‍ca rūpānaṃ| sauhaṃ tathā karissāmi yathā mai puna na vicikicchā uppaj‍jissati, na amanasikārau, na thinamiddhaṃ, na chambhitattaṃ, na uppila’’’nti|

‘‘sau khau ahaṃ, anuruddhā…pai॰… tassa mayhaṃ, anuruddhā, aitadahausi – ‘duṭṭhul‍laṃ khau mai udapādi, duṭṭhul‍lādhikaraṇañ‍ca pana mai samādhi cavi| samādhimhi cutai aubhāsau antaradhāyati dassanañ‍ca rūpānaṃ| sauhaṃ tathā karissāmi yathā mai puna na vicikicchā uppaj‍jissati, na amanasikārau, na thinamiddhaṃ, na chambhitattaṃ, na uppilaṃ, na duṭṭhul‍la’’’nti|

‘‘sau khau ahaṃ, anuruddhā…pai॰… tassa mayhaṃ, anuruddhā, aitadahausi – ‘ac‍cāraddhavīriyaṃ khau mai udapādi, ac‍cāraddhavīriyādhikaraṇañ‍ca pana mai samādhi cavi| samādhimhi cutai aubhāsau antaradhāyati dassanañ‍ca rūpānaṃ| saiyyathāpi, anuruddhā, purisau ubhauhi hatthaihi vaṭṭakaṃ gāḷhaṃ gaṇhaiyya, sau tatthaiva patamaiyya matamaiyya (bahūsu) pa + taṃ + aiyya = patamaiyya-iti padavibhāgau; aivamaiva khau mai, anuruddhā, ac‍cāraddhavīriyaṃ udapādi, ac‍cāraddhavīriyādhikaraṇañ‍ca pana mai samādhi cavi| samādhimhi cutai aubhāsau antaradhāyati  dassanañ‍ca rūpānaṃ| sauhaṃ tathā karissāmi yathā mai puna na vicikicchā uppaj‍jissati, na amanasikārau, na thinamiddhaṃ, na chambhitattaṃ, na uppilaṃ, na duṭṭhul‍laṃ, na ac‍cāraddhavīriya’’’nti|

‘‘sau khau ahaṃ, anuruddhā…pai॰… tassa mayhaṃ, anuruddhā, aitadahausi – ‘atilīnavīriyaṃ khau mai udapādi, atilīnavīriyādhikaraṇañ‍ca pana mai  samādhi cavi| samādhimhi  cutai aubhāsau antaradhāyati dassanañ‍ca rūpānaṃ| saiyyathāpi, anuruddhā, purisau vaṭṭakaṃ sithilaṃ gaṇhaiyya, sau tassa hatthatau uppataiyya; aivamaiva khau mai, anuruddhā, atilīnavīriyaṃ udapādi, atilīnavīriyādhikaraṇañ‍ca pana mai samādhi cavi| samādhimhi cutai aubhāsau antaradhāyati dassanañ‍ca rūpānaṃ| sauhaṃ tathā karissāmi yathā mai puna na vicikicchā uppaj‍jissati, na amanasikārau, na thinamiddhaṃ, na chambhitattaṃ, na uppilaṃ, na duṭṭhul‍laṃ, na ac‍cāraddhavīriyaṃ, na atilīnavīriya’’’nti|

‘‘sau khau ahaṃ, anuruddhā…pai॰… tassa mayhaṃ, anuruddhā, aitadahausi – ‘abhijappā khau mai udapādi, abhijappādhikaraṇañ‍ca pana mai samādhi cavi| samādhimhi cutai aubhāsau antaradhāyati dassanañ‍ca rūpānaṃ| sauhaṃ tathā karissāmi yathā mai puna na vicikicchā uppaj‍jissati, na amanasikārau, na thinamiddhaṃ, na chambhitattaṃ, na uppilaṃ, na duṭṭhul‍laṃ, na ac‍cāraddhavīriyaṃ, na atilīnavīriyaṃ, na abhijappā’’’ti|

‘‘sau  khau ahaṃ, anuruddhā…pai॰… tassa mayhaṃ, anuruddhā, aitadahausi – ‘nānattasañ‍ñā khau mai udapādi, nānattasañ‍ñādhikaraṇañ‍ca pana mai samādhi cavi| samādhimhi cutai aubhāsau antaradhāyati dassanañ‍ca rūpānaṃ| sauhaṃ tathā karissāmi yathā mai puna na vicikicchā uppaj‍jissati, na amanasikārau, na thinamiddhaṃ, na chambhitattaṃ, na uppilaṃ, na duṭṭhul‍laṃ, na ac‍cāraddhavīriyaṃ, na atilīnavīriyaṃ, na abhijappā, na nānattasañ‍ñā’’’ti|

‘‘sau khau ahaṃ, anuruddhā, appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau aubhāsañ‍caiva sañ‍jānāmi dassanañ‍ca rūpānaṃ| sau khau pana mai aubhāsau nacirassaiva antaradhāyati dassanañ‍ca rūpānaṃ| tassa mayhaṃ anuruddhā aitadahausi – ‘kau nu khau haitu kau pac‍cayau yaina mai aubhāsau antaradhāyati dassanañ‍ca rūpāna’nti| tassa mayhaṃ, anuruddhā, aitadahausi – ‘atinijjhāyitattaṃ khau mai rūpānaṃ udapādi, atinijjhāyitattādhikaraṇañ‍ca pana mai rūpānaṃ samādhi cavi| samādhimhi cutai aubhāsau antaradhāyati dassanañ‍ca rūpānaṃ| sauhaṃ tathā karissāmi yathā mai puna na vicikicchā uppaj‍jissati, na amanasikārau, na thinamiddhaṃ, na chambhitattaṃ, na uppilaṃ, na duṭṭhul‍laṃ, na ac‍cāraddhavīriyaṃ, na atilīnavīriyaṃ, na abhijappā, na nānattasañ‍ñā, na atinijjhāyitattaṃ rūpāna’’’nti|
