242. ‘‘sau  khau ahaṃ, anuruddhā, ‘vicikicchā cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti  – iti viditvā vicikicchaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisaṃ pajahiṃ, ‘amanasikārau cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā amanasikāraṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisaṃ pajahiṃ, ‘thinamiddhaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā thinamiddhaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisaṃ pajahiṃ, ‘chambhitattaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā chambhitattaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisaṃ pajahiṃ, ‘uppilaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā uppilaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisaṃ pajahiṃ, ‘duṭṭhul‍laṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā duṭṭhul‍laṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisaṃ pajahiṃ, ‘ac‍cāraddhavīriyaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā ac‍cāraddhavīriyaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisaṃ pajahiṃ, ‘atilīnavīriyaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā atilīnavīriyaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisaṃ pajahiṃ, ‘abhijappā cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā abhijappaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisaṃ pajahiṃ, ‘nānattasañ‍ñā cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā nānattasañ‍ñaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisaṃ pajahiṃ, ‘atinijjhāyitattaṃ rūpānaṃ cittassa  upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā atinijjhāyitattaṃ rūpānaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisaṃ pajahiṃ|

243. ‘‘sau khau ahaṃ, anuruddhā, appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau aubhāsañhi khau sañ‍jānāmi, na ca rūpāni passāmi; rūpāni hi khau passāmi, na ca aubhāsaṃ sañ‍jānāmi – ‘kaivalampi rattiṃ, kaivalampi divaṃ divasaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), kaivalampi rattindivaṃ’ rattidivaṃ (ka॰)| tassa mayhaṃ, anuruddhā, aitadahausi – ‘kau nu khau haitu kau pac‍cayau yvāhaṃ aubhāsañhi khau sañ‍jānāmi na ca rūpāni passāmi; rūpāni hi khau khau tasmiṃ samayai (sī॰ ka॰) passāmi na ca aubhāsaṃ sañ‍jānāmi – kaivalampi rattiṃ, kaivalampi divaṃ, kaivalampi rattindiva’nti| tassa mayhaṃ, anuruddhā, aitadahausi  – ‘yasmiñhi khau ahaṃ samayai rūpanimittaṃ amanasikaritvā aubhāsanimittaṃ manasi karaumi, aubhāsañhi khau tasmiṃ samayai sañ‍jānāmi, na ca rūpāni passāmi| yasmiṃ panāhaṃ samayai aubhāsanimittaṃ amanasikaritvā rūpanimittaṃ manasi karaumi, rūpāni hi khau tasmiṃ samayai passāmi na ca aubhāsaṃ sañ‍jānāmi – kaivalampi rattiṃ, kaivalampi divaṃ, kaivalampi rattindiva’’’nti|

‘‘sau  khau ahaṃ, anuruddhā, appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau parittañ‍caiva aubhāsaṃ sañ‍jānāmi, parittāni ca rūpāni passāmi; appamāṇañ‍caiva aubhāsaṃ sañ‍jānāmi, appamāṇāni  ca rūpāni passāmi – kaivalampi rattiṃ, kaivalampi divaṃ, kaivalampi rattindivaṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, anuruddhā, aitadahausi – ‘kau nu khau haitu kau pac‍cayau yvāhaṃ parittañ‍caiva aubhāsaṃ sañ‍jānāmi, parittāni ca rūpāni passāmi; appamāṇañ‍caiva aubhāsaṃ sañ‍jānāmi, appamāṇāni ca rūpāni passāmi – kaivalampi rattiṃ, kaivalampi divaṃ, kaivalampi rattindiva’nti| tassa mayhaṃ, anuruddhā, aitadahausi – ‘yasmiṃ khau mai samayai parittau samādhi hauti, parittaṃ mai tasmiṃ samayai cakkhu hauti| sauhaṃ parittaina cakkhunā parittañ‍caiva aubhāsaṃ sañ‍jānāmi, parittāni ca rūpāni passāmi| yasmiṃ pana mai samayai appamāṇau samādhi hauti, appamāṇaṃ mai tasmiṃ samayai cakkhu hauti| sauhaṃ appamāṇaina cakkhunā appamāṇañ‍caiva aubhāsaṃ sañ‍jānāmi, appamāṇāni ca rūpāni passāmi – kaivalampi rattiṃ, kaivalampi divaṃ, kaivalampi rattindiva’’’nti|

244. yatau khau mai, anuruddhā, ‘vicikicchā cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā vicikicchā cittassa upak‍kilaisau pahīnau ahausi, ‘amanasikārau cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā amanasikārau cittassa upak‍kilaisau pahīnau ahausi, ‘thinamiddhaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā thinamiddhaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisau pahīnau ahausi, ‘chambhitattaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā chambhitattaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisau pahīnau ahausi, ‘uppilaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā uppilaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisau pahīnau ahausi, ‘duṭṭhul‍laṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā duṭṭhul‍laṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisau pahīnau ahausi, ‘ac‍cāraddhavīriyaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā ac‍cāraddhavīriyaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisau pahīnau ahausi, ‘atilīnavīriyaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā atilīnavīriyaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisau pahīnau ahausi, ‘abhijappā cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā abhijappā cittassa upak‍kilaisau pahīnau ahausi, ‘nānattasañ‍ñā cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā nānattasañ‍ñā cittassa upak‍kilaisau pahīnau ahausi, ‘atinijjhāyitattaṃ rūpānaṃ cittassa  upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā  atinijjhāyitattaṃ rūpānaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisau pahīnau ahausi|

245. ‘‘tassa  mayhaṃ, anuruddhā, aitadahausi – ‘yai khau mai cittassa upak‍kilaisā tai mai pahīnā| handa, dānāhaṃ tividhaina samādhiṃ bhāvaimī’ti bhāvaisinti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰)| sau khau ahaṃ, anuruddhā, savitak‍kampi savicāraṃ samādhiṃ bhāvaisiṃ bhāvaimi (ka॰), avitak‍kampi vicāramattaṃ samādhiṃ bhāvaisiṃ, avitak‍kampi avicāraṃ samādhiṃ bhāvaisiṃ, sappītikampi samādhiṃ bhāvaisiṃ, nippītikampi samādhiṃ bhāvaisiṃ, sātasahagatampi samādhiṃ bhāvaisiṃ, upaikkhāsahagatampi samādhiṃ bhāvaisiṃ| yatau khau mai, anuruddhā, savitak‍kaupi savicārau samādhi bhāvitau ahausi, avitak‍kaupi vicāramattau samādhi bhāvitau ahausi, avitak‍kaupi avicārau samādhi bhāvitau ahausi, sappītikaupi samādhi bhāvitau ahausi, nippītikaupi samādhi bhāvitau ahausi, sātasahagataupi samādhi bhāvitau ahausi, upaikkhāsahagataupi samādhi bhāvitau ahausi| ñāṇañ‍ca pana mai dassanaṃ udapādi, akuppā mai caitauvimutti| ayamantimā jāti, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā anuruddhau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

upak‍kilaisasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. bālapaṇḍitasuttaṃ

246. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, bālassa bālalakkhaṇāni bālanimittāni bālāpadānāni| katamāni tīṇi? idha, bhikkhavai, bālau duc‍cintitacintī ca hauti dubbhāsitabhāsī ca duk‍kaṭakammakārī ca| nau caitaṃ nau caidaṃ (saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.27-28), bhikkhavai, bālau  duc‍cintitacintī ca abhavissa dubbhāsitabhāsī ca duk‍kaṭakammakārī ca kaina naṃ na taina naṃ (ka॰), na naṃ (?) paṇḍitā jānaiyyuṃ – ‘bālau ayaṃ bhavaṃ asappurisau’ti? yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, bālau duc‍cintitacintī ca hauti dubbhāsitabhāsī ca duk‍kaṭakammakārī ca tasmā naṃ paṇḍitā jānanti – ‘bālau ayaṃ bhavaṃ asappurisau’ti| sa khau sau, bhikkhavai, bālau tividhaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| sacai, bhikkhavai, bālau sabhāyaṃ vā nisin‍nau hauti, rathikāya rathiyāya (bahūsu) vā nisin‍nau hauti, siṅghāṭakai vā nisin‍nau hauti; tatra cai janau taj‍jaṃ tassāruppaṃ kathaṃ mantaiti| sacai, bhikkhavai, bālau pāṇātipātī hauti, adin‍nādāyī hauti, kāmaisumicchācārī hauti, musāvādī hauti, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhāyī hauti, tatra, bhikkhavai, bālassa aivaṃ hauti  – ‘yaṃ khau janau taj‍jaṃ tassāruppaṃ kathaṃ mantaiti, saṃvij‍jantaiva tai saṃvij‍jantai tai ca (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) dhammā mayi, ahañ‍ca taisu dhammaisu sandissāmī’ti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, bālau paṭhamaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti|

247. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bālau passati rājānau cauraṃ āgucāriṃ gahaitvā vividhā kammakāraṇā kāraintai – kasāhipi tāḷaintai  vaittaihipi tāḷaintai addhadaṇḍakaihipi tāḷaintai hatthampi chindantai pādampi chindantai hatthapādampi chindantai kaṇṇampi chindantai nāsampi chindantai kaṇṇanāsampi chindantai bilaṅgathālikampi karauntai saṅkhamuṇḍikampi karauntai rāhumukhampi karauntai jautimālikampi karauntai hatthapaj‍jautikampi karauntai airakavattikampi karauntai cīrakavāsikampi karauntai aiṇaiyyakampi karauntai baḷisamaṃsikampi karauntai kahāpaṇikampi karauntai  khārāpatacchikampi khārāpaṭicchakampi (ka॰) karauntai palighaparivattikampi karauntai palālapīṭhakampi palālapiṭṭhakampi (pī॰) karauntai tattainapi tailaina ausiñ‍cantai sunakhaihipi khādāpaintai jīvantampi sūlai uttāsaintai asināpi sīsaṃ chindantai| tatra, bhikkhavai, bālassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘yathārūpānaṃ khau pāpakānaṃ kammānaṃ haitu rājānau cauraṃ āgucāriṃ gahaitvā vividhā kammakāraṇā kārainti – kasāhipi tāḷainti…pai॰… asināpi sīsaṃ chindanti; saṃvij‍jantaiva tai dhammā mayi, ahañ‍ca taisu dhammaisu sandissāmi| maṃ caipi rājānau sacai mampi (ka॰) jānaiyyuṃ, mampi rājānau gahaitvā  vividhā  kammakāraṇā kāraiyyuṃ – kasāhipi tāḷaiyyuṃ…pai॰… jīvantampi sūlai uttāsaiyyuṃ, asināpi sīsaṃ chindaiyyu’nti| idampi, bhikkhavai, bālau dutiyaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti|

248. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bālaṃ pīṭhasamārūḷhaṃ vā mañ‍casamārūḷhaṃ vā chamāyaṃ chamāya (sī॰ pī॰) vā saimānaṃ, yānissa pubbai pāpakāni kammāni katāni kāyaina duc‍caritāni vācāya duc‍caritāni manasā duc‍caritāni tānissa tamhi samayai aulambanti ajjhaulambanti abhippalambanti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahataṃ pabbatakūṭānaṃ chāyā sāyanhasamayaṃ pathaviyā aulambanti ajjhaulambanti abhippalambanti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bālaṃ pīṭhasamārūḷhaṃ vā mañ‍casamārūḷhaṃ vā chamāyaṃ vā saimānaṃ, yānissa pubbai pāpakāni  kammāni katāni kāyaina duc‍caritāni vācāya duc‍caritāni manasā duc‍caritāni tānissa tamhi samayai aulambanti ajjhaulambanti abhippalambanti| tatra, bhikkhavai, bālassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘akataṃ vata mai kalyāṇaṃ, akataṃ kusalaṃ, akataṃ bhīruttāṇaṃ; kataṃ pāpaṃ, kataṃ luddaṃ, kataṃ kibbisaṃ| yāvatā, bhau, akatakalyāṇānaṃ akatakusalānaṃ akatabhīruttāṇānaṃ katapāpānaṃ kataluddānaṃ katakibbisānaṃ gati taṃ gatiṃ paic‍ca gacchāmī’ti| sau saucati kilamati paridaivati urattāḷiṃ kandati sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| idampi, bhikkhavai, bālau tatiyaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti|

‘‘sa  khau sau, bhikkhavai, bālau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ caritvā vācāya duc‍caritaṃ caritvā manasā duc‍caritaṃ caritvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| yaṃ khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘aikantaṃ aniṭṭhaṃ aikantaṃ akantaṃ aikantaṃ amanāpa’nti, nirayamaiva taṃ sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘aikantaṃ aniṭṭhaṃ aikantaṃ  akantaṃ aikantaṃ amanāpa’nti| yāvañ‍cidaṃ, bhikkhavai, upamāpi upamāhipi (sī॰) na sukarā yāva dukkhā nirayā’’ti|

249. aivaṃ vuttai, añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sak‍kā pana, bhantai, upamaṃ kātu’’nti? ‘‘sak‍kā bhikkhū’’ti bhagavā avauca| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, cauraṃ āgucāriṃ gahaitvā rañ‍ñau dassaiyyuṃ – ‘ayaṃ khau, daiva, caurau āgucārī, imassa yaṃ icchasi taṃ daṇḍaṃ paṇaihī’ti| tamainaṃ rājā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘gacchatha, bhau, imaṃ purisaṃ pubbaṇhasamayaṃ sattisataina hanathā’ti| tamainaṃ pubbaṇhasamayaṃ sattisataina hanaiyyuṃ| atha rājā majjhanhikasamayaṃ majjhantikasamayaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), majjhantikaṃ samayaṃ (pī॰) aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ambhau, kathaṃ sau purisau’ti? ‘‘‘tathaiva, daiva, jīvatī’ti| tamainaṃ rājā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘gacchatha, bhau, taṃ purisaṃ majjhanhikasamayaṃ sattisataina hanathā’ti| tamainaṃ majjhanhikasamayaṃ sattisataina hanaiyyuṃ| atha rājā sāyanhasamayaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ambhau, kathaṃ sau purisau’ti? ‘tathaiva, daiva, jīvatī’ti| tamainaṃ rājā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘gacchatha, bhau, taṃ purisaṃ sāyanhasamayaṃ sattisataina hanathā’ti| tamainaṃ sāyanhasamayaṃ  sattisataina hanaiyyuṃ| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu sau purisau tīhi sattisataihi hañ‍ñamānau tataunidānaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidiyaithā’’ti? ‘‘aikissāpi, bhantai, sattiyā hañ‍ñamānau sau purisau tataunidānaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidiyaitha, kau pana vādau tīhi sattisataihī’’ti?

250. atha khau bhagavā parittaṃ pāṇimattaṃ pāsāṇaṃ gahaitvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamau nu khau mahantatarau – yau cāyaṃ mayā parittau pāṇimattau pāsāṇau gahitau, yau ca himavā pabbatarājā’’ti? ‘‘appamattakau ayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā parittau pāṇimattau pāsāṇau gahitau, himavantaṃ pabbatarājānaṃ upanidhāya saṅkhampi na upaiti, kalabhāgampi na upaiti, upanidhampi upanidhimpi (sī॰ pī॰) na upaiti’’| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yaṃ sau purisau tīhi sattisataihi hañ‍ñamānau tataunidānaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti taṃ nirayakassa dukkhassa upanidhāya saṅkhampi na upaiti, kalabhāgampi na upaiti, upanidhampi na upaiti’’|

‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, nirayapālā pañ‍cavidhabandhanaṃ nāma kammakāraṇaṃ karaunti – tattaṃ ayaukhilaṃ ayaukhīlaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) hatthai gamainti, tattaṃ ayaukhilaṃ dutiyai hatthai gamainti, tattaṃ ayaukhilaṃ pādai gamainti, tattaṃ ayaukhilaṃ dutiyai pādai gamainti, tattaṃ ayaukhilaṃ majjhai urasmiṃ gamainti| sau tattha  dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidaiti, na ca tāva kālaṃ karauti yāva na taṃ pāpakammaṃ byantīhauti byantihauti (pī॰ ka॰)| tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, nirayapālā  saṃvaisaitvā kuṭhārīhi kudhārīhi (ka॰) tacchanti| sau tattha dukkhā tibbā…pai॰… byantīhauti| tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, nirayapālā uddhaṃpādaṃ adhausiraṃ gahaitvā vāsīhi tacchanti| sau  tattha dukkhā tibbā…pai॰… byantīhauti| tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, nirayapālā rathai yaujaitvā ādittāya pathaviyā sampaj‍jalitāya sajautibhūtāya sañ‍jautibhūtāya (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) sāraintipi pac‍cāsāraintipi| sau tattha dukkhā tibbā…pai॰… byantīhauti| tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, nirayapālā mahantaṃ aṅgārapabbataṃ ādittaṃ sampaj‍jalitaṃ sajautibhūtaṃ āraupaintipi auraupaintipi| sau tattha dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidaiti, na ca tāva kālaṃ karauti yāva na taṃ pāpakammaṃ byantīhauti| tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, nirayapālā uddhaṃpādaṃ adhausiraṃ gahaitvā tattāya lauhakumbhiyā pakkhipanti ādittāya sampaj‍jalitāya sajautibhūtāya| sau tattha phaiṇuddaihakaṃ pac‍cati| sau tattha phaiṇuddaihakaṃ pac‍camānau sakimpi uddhaṃ gacchati, sakimpi adhau gacchati, sakimpi tiriyaṃ gacchati| sau tattha dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidaiti, na ca tāva kālaṅkarauti yāva na taṃ pāpakammaṃ byantīhauti| tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, nirayapālā nirayapālā punappunaṃ (ka॰) mahānirayai pakkhipanti| sau khau pana, bhikkhavai, mahānirayau –

‘‘catuk‍kaṇṇau catudvārau, vibhattau bhāgasau mitau|

ayaupākārapariyantau, ayasā paṭikuj‍jitau||

‘‘tassa ayaumayā bhūmi, jalitā taijasā yutā|

samantā yaujanasataṃ, pharitvā tiṭṭhati  sabbadā’’||

‘‘anaikapariyāyainapi khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, nirayakathaṃ kathaiyyaṃ; yāvañ‍cidaṃ, bhikkhavai, na sukarā akkhānaina pāpuṇituṃ yāva dukkhā nirayā|

251. ‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, tiracchānagatā pāṇā tiṇabhakkhā| tai al‍lānipi tiṇāni sukkhānipi tiṇāni dantul‍laihakaṃ khādanti| katamai ca, bhikkhavai, tiracchānagatā pāṇā tiṇabhakkhā? hatthī assā gauṇā gadrabhā ajā migā, yai vā panañ‍ñaipi kaici tiracchānagatā  pāṇā tiṇabhakkhā| sa khau sau, bhikkhavai, bālau idha pubbai rasādau idha pāpāni kammāni karitvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā taisaṃ sattānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati yai tai sattā tiṇabhakkhā|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, tiracchānagatā pāṇā gūthabhakkhā| tai dūratauva gūthagandhaṃ ghāyitvā dhāvanti – ‘aittha bhuñ‍jissāma, aittha bhuñ‍jissāmā’ti| saiyyathāpi  nāma brāhmaṇā āhutigandhaina dhāvanti – ‘aittha bhuñ‍jissāma, aittha bhuñ‍jissāmā’ti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, santi tiracchānagatā pāṇā gūthabhakkhā, tai dūratauva gūthagandhaṃ  ghāyitvā dhāvanti – ‘aittha bhuñ‍jissāma, aittha bhuñ‍jissāmā’ti| katamai ca, bhikkhavai, tiracchānagatā pāṇā gūthabhakkhā? kuk‍kuṭā sūkarā sauṇā siṅgālā, yai vā panañ‍ñaipi kaici tiracchānagatā pāṇā gūthabhakkhā| sa khau sau, bhikkhavai, bālau idha pubbai rasādau idha pāpāni kammāni karitvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā taisaṃ sattānaṃ sahabyataṃ  upapaj‍jati yai tai sattā gūthabhakkhā|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, tiracchānagatā pāṇā andhakārai jāyanti andhakārai jīyanti jiyyanti (ka॰) andhakārai mīyanti miyyanti (ka॰)| katamai ca, bhikkhavai, tiracchānagatā pāṇā andhakārai jāyanti andhakārai jīyanti andhakārai mīyanti? kīṭā puḷavā paṭaṅgā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) gaṇḍuppādā, yai vā panañ‍ñaipi kaici tiracchānagatā pāṇā andhakārai jāyanti andhakārai jīyanti andhakārai mīyanti| sa khau sau, bhikkhavai, bālau idha pubbai rasādau, idha pāpāni kammāni karitvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā taisaṃ sattānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati yai tai sattā andhakārai jāyanti andhakārai jīyanti andhakārai mīyanti|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, tiracchānagatā pāṇā udakasmiṃ jāyanti udakasmiṃ jīyanti udakasmiṃ mīyanti| katamai ca, bhikkhavai, tiracchānagatā pāṇā udakasmiṃ jāyanti udakasmiṃ jīyanti udakasmiṃ mīyanti? macchā kacchapā susumārā, yai vā panañ‍ñaipi kaici tiracchānagatā pāṇā udakasmiṃ jāyanti udakasmiṃ jīyanti udakasmiṃ mīyanti| sa khau sau, bhikkhavai, bālau idha pubbai rasādau idha pāpāni kammāni karitvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā taisaṃ sattānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati yai tai sattā udakasmiṃ jāyanti udakasmiṃ jīyanti udakasmiṃ mīyanti|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, tiracchānagatā pāṇā asucismiṃ jāyanti asucismiṃ  jīyanti asucismiṃ mīyanti| katamai ca, bhikkhavai, tiracchānagatā pāṇā asucismiṃ jāyanti asucismiṃ jīyanti asucismiṃ mīyanti? yai tai, bhikkhavai, sattā pūtimacchai vā jāyanti pūtimacchai vā jīyanti pūtimacchai vā mīyanti pūtikuṇapai vā…pai॰… pūtikummāsai vā… candanikāya vā… auligal‍lai  vā jāyanti, (yai vā panañ‍ñaipi kaici tiracchānagatā pāṇā asucismiṃ jāyanti asucismiṃ jīyanti asucismiṃ mīyanti|) ( ) natthi sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu sa khau sau, bhikkhavai, bālau  idha pubbai rasādau idha pāpāni kammāni karitvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā taisaṃ sattānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati yai tai sattā asucismiṃ jāyanti asucismiṃ jīyanti asucismiṃ mīyanti|

‘‘anaikapariyāyainapi khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, tiracchānayaunikathaṃ kathaiyyaṃ; yāvañ‍cidaṃ, bhikkhavai, na sukaraṃ akkhānaina pāpuṇituṃ yāva dukkhā tiracchānayauni|

252. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau aikacchiggalaṃ yugaṃ mahāsamuddai pakkhipaiyya| tamainaṃ puratthimau vātau pacchimaina saṃharaiyya, pacchimau vātau puratthimaina saṃharaiyya, uttarau vātau dakkhiṇaina saṃharaiyya, dakkhiṇau vātau uttaraina saṃharaiyya| tatrāssa kāṇau kacchapau, sau vassasatassa vassasatassa vassasatassa vassasahassassa vassasatasahassassa (sī॰), vassasatassa (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) ac‍cayaina sakiṃ ummuj‍jaiyya| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu sau kāṇau kacchapau amusmiṃ aikacchiggalai yugai gīvaṃ pavaisaiyyā’’ti? (‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|) ( ) natthi sī॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu ‘‘yadi pana yadi nūna (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), bhantai, kadāci karahaci  dīghassa addhunau ac‍cayainā’’ti| ‘‘khippataraṃ khau sau, bhikkhavai, kāṇau kacchapau amusmiṃ aikacchiggalai yugai gīvaṃ pavaisaiyya, atau dul‍labhatarāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, manussattaṃ vadāmi sakiṃ vinipātagataina bālaina| taṃ kissa haitu? na haittha, bhikkhavai, atthi dhammacariyā samacariyā kusalakiriyā puñ‍ñakiriyā| añ‍ñamañ‍ñakhādikā aittha, bhikkhavai, vattati dubbalakhādikā’’|

‘‘sa khau sau, bhikkhavai, bālau sacai kadāci karahaci dīghassa addhunau ac‍cayaina manussattaṃ āgacchati, yāni tāni nīcakulāni – caṇḍālakulaṃ vā naisādakulaṃ vā vainakulaṃ vaiṇakulaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) vā rathakārakulaṃ vā puk‍kusakulaṃ vā| tathārūpai kulai pac‍cājāyati daliddai appan‍napānabhaujanai kasiravuttikai, yattha kasiraina ghāsacchādau labbhati| sau ca hauti dubbaṇṇau duddasikau  aukauṭimakau  bavhābādhau bahvābādhau (ka॰) kāṇau vā kuṇī vā khuj‍jau vā pakkhahatau vā na lābhī an‍nassa pānassa vatthassa  yānassa mālāgandhavilaipanassa saiyyāvasathapadīpaiyyassa| sau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ carati vācāya duc‍caritaṃ carati manasā duc‍caritaṃ carati| sau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ caritvā vācāya duc‍caritaṃ caritvā manasā duc‍caritaṃ caritvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, akkhadhuttau paṭhamainaiva kaliggahaina puttampi jīyaitha, dārampi jīyaitha, sabbaṃ sāpataiyyampi jīyaitha, uttaripi adhibandhaṃ  anubandhaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), addhubandhaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) nigacchaiyya| appamattakau sau, bhikkhavai, kaliggahau yaṃ sau akkhadhuttau paṭhamainaiva kaliggahaina puttampi jīyaitha, dārampi jīyaitha, sabbaṃ sāpataiyyampi jīyaitha, uttaripi adhibandhaṃ nigacchaiyya| atha khau ayamaiva tatau mahantatarau kaliggahau yaṃ sau bālau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ caritvā vācāya duc‍caritaṃ caritvā manasā duc‍caritaṃ caritvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, kaivalā paripūrā kaivalaparipūrā (sī॰ pī॰) ma॰ ni॰ 1.244 pāḷiyā saṃsandaitabbā bālabhūmī’’ti|

253. ‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitassa paṇḍitalakkhaṇāni paṇḍitanimittāni paṇḍitāpadānāni| katamāni tīṇi? idha, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitau sucintitacintī ca hauti subhāsitabhāsī ca sukatakammakārī ca| nau caitaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitau sucintitacintī ca abhavissa subhāsitabhāsī ca sukatakammakārī ca, kaina naṃ na taina naṃ (ka॰), na naṃ (?) paṇḍitā jānaiyyuṃ – ‘paṇḍitau ayaṃ bhavaṃ sappurisau’ti? yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitau sucintitacintī ca hauti subhāsitabhāsī ca sukatakammakārī ca tasmā naṃ paṇḍitā jānanti – ‘paṇḍitau ayaṃ bhavaṃ sappurisau’ti| sa khau sau, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitau tividhaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| sacai, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitau sabhāyaṃ vā nisin‍nau hauti, rathikāya vā nisin‍nau hauti, siṅghāṭakai vā nisin‍nau hauti; tatra cai janau taj‍jaṃ tassāruppaṃ kathaṃ mantaiti| sacai, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitau pāṇātipātā  paṭiviratau hauti, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, kāmaisumicchācārā  paṭiviratau hauti, musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, surāmairayamaj‍jappamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau hauti; tatra, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘yaṃ khau janau taj‍jaṃ tassāruppaṃ kathaṃ mantaiti; saṃvij‍jantaiva tai dhammā mayi, ahañ‍ca taisu dhammaisu sandissāmī’ti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitau paṭhamaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti|

254. ‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitau passati rājānau cauraṃ āgucāriṃ gahaitvā vividhā kammakāraṇā kāraintai – kasāhipi tāḷaintai vaittaihipi tāḷaintai addhadaṇḍakaihipi tāḷaintai hatthampi chindantai pādampi chindantai hatthapādampi chindantai kaṇṇampi chindantai nāsampi chindantai kaṇṇanāsampi chindantai bilaṅgathālikampi karauntai saṅkhamuṇḍikampi karauntai rāhumukhampi karauntai jautimālikampi karauntai hatthapaj‍jautikampi karauntai airakavattikampi karauntai cīrakavāsikampi karauntai aiṇaiyyakampi karauntai balisamaṃsikampi karauntai kahāpaṇikampi karauntai khārāpatacchikampi karauntai palighaparivattikampi karauntai palālapīṭhakampi karauntai tattainapi tailaina ausiñ‍cantai sunakhaihipi khādāpaintai jīvantampi sūlai uttāsaintai asināpi sīsaṃ chindantai| tatra, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘yathārūpānaṃ khau pāpakānaṃ kammānaṃ haitu rājānau cauraṃ āgucāriṃ gahaitvā vividhā kammakāraṇā kārainti kasāhipi tāḷainti, vaittaihipi tāḷainti, addhadaṇḍakaihipi tāḷainti, hatthampi chindanti, pādampi chindanti, hatthapādampi chindanti, kaṇṇampi chindanti, nāsampi chindanti, kaṇṇanāsampi chindanti, bilaṅgathālikampi karaunti, saṅkhamuṇḍikampi karaunti, rāhumukhampi karaunti, jautimālikampi karaunti, hatthapaj‍jautikampi karaunti, airakavattikampi karaunti, cīrakavāsikampi karaunti, aiṇaiyyakampi karaunti, balisamaṃsikampi karaunti, kahāpaṇikampi karaunti, khārāpatacchikampi karaunti, palighaparivattikampi karaunti, palālapīṭhakampi karaunti, tattainapi tailaina ausiñ‍canti, sunakhaihipi khādāpainti, jīvantampi sūlai uttāsainti, asināpi sīsaṃ chindanti, na tai dhammā mayi saṃvij‍janti, ahañ‍ca na taisu dhammaisu sandissāmī’ti| idampi, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitau dutiyaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti|

255. ‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitaṃ pīṭhasamārūḷhaṃ vā mañ‍casamārūḷhaṃ vā chamāyaṃ vā saimānaṃ, yānissa pubbai kalyāṇāni kammāni katāni kāyaina sucaritāni vācāya sucaritāni manasā sucaritāni tānissa tamhi samayai aulambanti…pai॰… saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahataṃ pabbatakūṭānaṃ chāyā sāyanhasamayaṃ pathaviyā aulambanti ajjhaulambanti abhippalambanti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitaṃ pīṭhasamārūḷhaṃ vā mañ‍casamārūḷhaṃ vā chamāyaṃ vā saimānaṃ yānissa pubbai kalyāṇāni kammāni katāni kāyaina sucaritāni vācāya sucaritāni manasā sucaritāni tānissa tamhi samayai aulambanti ajjhaulambanti abhippalambanti| tatra, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitassa aivaṃ hauti  – ‘akataṃ vata mai pāpaṃ, akataṃ luddaṃ, akataṃ kibbisaṃ; kataṃ kalyāṇaṃ, kataṃ kusalaṃ, kataṃ bhīruttāṇaṃ| yāvatā, bhau, akatapāpānaṃ akataluddānaṃ akatakibbisānaṃ katakalyāṇānaṃ katakusalānaṃ katabhīruttāṇānaṃ  gati taṃ gatiṃ paic‍ca gacchāmī’ti| sau na saucati, na kilamati, na paridaivati, na urattāḷiṃ kandati, na sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| idampi, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitau tatiyaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti|

‘‘sa khau sau, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitau kāyaina sucaritaṃ caritvā vācāya sucaritaṃ caritvā manasā sucaritaṃ caritvā kāyassa bhaidā  paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| yaṃ khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘aikantaṃ iṭṭhaṃ aikantaṃ kantaṃ aikantaṃ manāpa’nti, saggamaiva taṃ sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘aikantaṃ iṭṭhaṃ aikantaṃ kantaṃ aikantaṃ manāpa’nti| yāvañ‍cidaṃ, bhikkhavai, upamāpi na sukarā yāva sukhā saggā’’ti|

256. aivaṃ vuttai, añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sak‍kā pana, bhantai, upamaṃ kātu’’nti? ‘‘sak‍kā bhikkhū’’ti bhagavā avauca| ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, rājā cak‍kavattī sattahi ratanaihi saman‍nāgatau catūhi ca iddhīhi tataunidānaṃ sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| katamaihi sattahi? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhāvasittassa tadahupausathai pan‍narasai sīsaṃnhātassa upausathikassa uparipāsādavaragatassa dibbaṃ  cak‍karatanaṃ pātubhavati sahassāraṃ sanaimikaṃ sanābhikaṃ sabbākāraparipūraṃ| taṃ disvāna rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhāvasittassa aivaṃ hauti aitadahausi (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) – ‘sutaṃ khau pana maitaṃ  yassa rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhāvasittassa tadahupausathai pan‍narasai sīsaṃnhātassa upausathikassa uparipāsādavaragatassa dibbaṃ cak‍karatanaṃ pātubhavati sahassāraṃ sanaimikaṃ sanābhikaṃ sabbākāraparipūraṃ, sau hauti rājā cak‍kavattīti| assaṃ nu khau ahaṃ rājā cak‍kavattī’’’ti?

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, rājā khattiyau muddhāvasittau vāmaina hatthaina bhiṅkāraṃ gahaitvā dakkhiṇaina hatthaina cak‍karatanaṃ abbhuk‍kirati – ‘pavattatu bhavaṃ cak‍karatanaṃ, abhivijinātu bhavaṃ cak‍karatana’nti| atha khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, cak‍karatanaṃ puratthimaṃ disaṃ pavattati| anvadaiva rājā cak‍kavattī saddhiṃ caturaṅginiyā saināya| yasmiṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, padaisai cak‍karatanaṃ patiṭṭhāti tattha rājā cak‍kavattī vāsaṃ upaiti saddhiṃ caturaṅginiyā saināya| yai khau pana, bhikkhavai, puratthimāya disāya paṭirājānau tai rājānaṃ cak‍kavattiṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘aihi khau, mahārāja! svāgataṃ tai, mahārāja svāgataṃ mahārāja (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)! sakaṃ tai, mahārāja! anusāsa, mahārājā’ti| rājā cak‍kavattī aivamāha – ‘pāṇau na hantabbau, adin‍naṃ nādātabbaṃ, kāmaisumicchā na caritabbā, musā na bhāsitabbā, maj‍jaṃ na pātabbaṃ, yathābhuttañ‍ca bhuñ‍jathā’ti| yai khau pana, bhikkhavai, puratthimāya disāya paṭirājānau tai rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa anuyantā anuyuttā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) bhavanti ahaisuṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰)|

257. ‘‘atha khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, cak‍karatanaṃ puratthimaṃ  samuddaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā ajjhaugahaitvā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pac‍cuttaritvā dakkhiṇaṃ disaṃ pavattati…pai॰… dakkhiṇaṃ samuddaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā pac‍cuttaritvā pacchimaṃ disaṃ pavattati… pacchimaṃ samuddaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā pac‍cuttaritvā uttaraṃ disaṃ pavattati anvadaiva rājā cak‍kavattī saddhiṃ caturaṅginiyā saināya| yasmiṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, padaisai cak‍karatanaṃ patiṭṭhāti tattha rājā cak‍kavattī vāsaṃ upaiti saddhiṃ caturaṅginiyā saināya|

‘‘yai khau pana, bhikkhavai, uttarāya disāya paṭirājānau tai rājānaṃ cak‍kavattiṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘aihi khau, mahārāja! svāgataṃ tai, mahārāja! sakaṃ tai, mahārāja! anusāsa, mahārājā’ti| rājā cak‍kavattī aivamāha – ‘pāṇau na hantabbau, adin‍naṃ nādātabbaṃ, kāmaisumicchā na  caritabbā, musā na bhāsitabbā, maj‍jaṃ na pātabbaṃ; yathābhuttañ‍ca bhuñ‍jathā’ti| yai khau pana, bhikkhavai, uttarāya disāya paṭirājānau tai rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa anuyantā bhavanti|

‘‘atha khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, cak‍karatanaṃ samuddapariyantaṃ pathaviṃ abhivijinitvā tamaiva rājadhāniṃ pac‍cāgantvā rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa antaipuradvārai akkhāhataṃ mañ‍ñai tiṭṭhati rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa antaipuradvāraṃ upasaubhayamānaṃ| rañ‍ñau, bhikkhavai, cak‍kavattissa aivarūpaṃ cak‍karatanaṃ pātubhavati|

258. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa hatthiratanaṃ pātubhavati – sabbasaitau sattappatiṭṭhau iddhimā vaihāsaṅgamau upausathau  nāma nāgarājā| taṃ disvāna rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa cittaṃ pasīdati – ‘bhaddakaṃ vata, bhau, hatthiyānaṃ, sacai damathaṃ upaiyyā’ti| atha khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, hatthiratanaṃ  saiyyathāpi nāma bhaddau hatthājānīyau dīgharattaṃ suparidantau aivamaiva damathaṃ upaiti| bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, rājā cak‍kavattī tamaiva hatthiratanaṃ vīmaṃsamānau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ  abhiruhitvā samuddapariyantaṃ pathaviṃ anusaṃyāyitvā tamaiva rājadhāniṃ pac‍cāgantvā pātarāsamakāsi| rañ‍ñau, bhikkhavai, cak‍kavattissa aivarūpaṃ hatthiratanaṃ pātubhavati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa assaratanaṃ pātubhavati – sabbasaitau kāḷasīsau muñ‍jakaisau iddhimā vaihāsaṅgamau valāhakau nāma assarājā| taṃ disvāna rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa cittaṃ pasīdati – ‘bhaddakaṃ vata, bhau, assayānaṃ, sacai damathaṃ upaiyyā’ti| atha khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, assaratanaṃ saiyyathāpi nāma bhaddau assājānīyau dīgharattaṃ suparidantau aivamaiva damathaṃ upaiti| bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, rājā cak‍kavattī tamaiva assaratanaṃ vīmaṃsamānau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ abhiruhitvā samuddapariyantaṃ pathaviṃ anusaṃyāyitvā tamaiva rājadhāniṃ pac‍cāgantvā pātarāsamakāsi| rañ‍ñau, bhikkhavai, cak‍kavattissa aivarūpaṃ assaratanaṃ pātubhavati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa maṇiratanaṃ pātubhavati| sau hauti maṇi vaiḷuriyau subhau jātimā aṭṭhaṃsau suparikammakatau| tassa khau pana, bhikkhavai, maṇiratanassa ābhā samantā yaujanaṃ phuṭā hauti| bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, rājā cak‍kavattī tamaiva maṇiratanaṃ vīmaṃsamānau caturaṅginiṃ  sainaṃ san‍nayhitvā maṇiṃ dhajaggaṃ āraupaitvā rattandhakāratimisāya pāyāsi| yai khau pana, bhikkhavai, samantā gāmā ahaisuṃ tai tainaubhāsaina kammantai payaujaisuṃ ‘divā’ti mañ‍ñamānā| rañ‍ñau, bhikkhavai, cak‍kavattissa aivarūpaṃ maṇiratanaṃ pātubhavati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa itthiratanaṃ pātubhavati| sā abhirūpā dassanīyā pāsādikā paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatā nātidīghā nātirassā nātikisā  nātithūlā nātikāḷikā nātikāḷī (sī॰ pī॰) nāc‍caudātā, atik‍kantā mānusaṃ vaṇṇaṃ, appattā dibbaṃ vaṇṇaṃ| tassa khau pana, bhikkhavai, itthiratanassa aivarūpau kāyasamphassau hauti saiyyathāpi nāma tūlapicunau vā kappāsapicunau vā| tassa khau pana, bhikkhavai, itthiratanassa sītai uṇhāni gattāni haunti, uṇhai sītāni gattāni haunti| tassa khau pana, bhikkhavai, itthiratanassa kāyatau candanagandhau vāyati, mukhatau uppalagandhau vāyati| taṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, itthiratanaṃ rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa pubbuṭṭhāyinī hauti pacchānipātinī kiṃkārapaṭissāvinī manāpacārinī piyavādinī| taṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, itthiratanaṃ rājānaṃ cak‍kavattiṃ  manasāpi nau aticarati, kutau pana kāyaina? rañ‍ñau, bhikkhavai, cak‍kavattissa aivarūpaṃ itthiratanaṃ  pātubhavati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa gahapatiratanaṃ pātubhavati| tassa kammavipākajaṃ dibbacakkhu pātubhavati, yaina nidhiṃ passati sassāmikampi assāmikampi| sau rājānaṃ cak‍kavattiṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivamāha – ‘appaussuk‍kau tvaṃ, daiva, hauhi| ahaṃ tai dhanaina dhanakaraṇīyaṃ dhanaina karaṇīyaṃ (ka॰) karissāmī’ti| bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, rājā cak‍kavattī tamaiva gahapatiratanaṃ vīmaṃsamānau nāvaṃ abhiruhitvā majjhai gaṅgāya nadiyā sautaṃ augāhitvā augahaitvā (sī॰ pī॰) gahapatiratanaṃ aitadavauca – ‘atthau mai, gahapati, hirañ‍ñasuvaṇṇainā’ti| ‘taina hi, mahārāja, aikaṃ tīraṃ nāvā upaitū’ti| ‘idhaiva mai, gahapati, atthau hirañ‍ñasuvaṇṇainā’ti| atha khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, gahapatiratanaṃ ubhauhi hatthaihi udakai aumasitvā pūraṃ hirañ‍ñasuvaṇṇassa kumbhiṃ uddharitvā rājānaṃ cak‍kavattiṃ aitadavauca – ‘alamaittāvatā, mahārāja! katamaittāvatā, mahārāja! pūjitamaittāvatā, mahārājā’ti| rājā cak‍kavattī aivamāha – ‘alamaittāvatā, gahapati! katamaittāvatā, gahapati! pūjitamaittāvatā, gahapatī’ti| rañ‍ñau, bhikkhavai, cak‍kavattissa aivarūpaṃ gahapatiratanaṃ pātubhavati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa pariṇāyakaratanaṃ  pātubhavati – paṇḍitau byattau maidhāvī paṭibalau rājānaṃ cak‍kavattiṃ upayāpaitabbaṃ upayāpaituṃ upaṭṭhapaitabbaṃ upaṭṭhapaituṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) apayāpaitabbaṃ apayāpaituṃ ṭhapaitabbaṃ ṭhapaituṃ| sau rājānaṃ cak‍kavattiṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivamāha – ‘appaussuk‍kau tvaṃ, daiva, hauhi| ahamanusāsissāmī’ti| rañ‍ñau, bhikkhavai, cak‍kavattissa aivarūpaṃ pariṇāyakaratanaṃ pātubhavati| rājā, bhikkhavai, cak‍kavattī imaihi sattahi ratanaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti|

259. ‘‘katamāhi catūhi iddhīhi? idha, bhikkhavai, rājā cak‍kavattī abhirūpau hauti dassanīyau pāsādikau paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatau ativiya añ‍ñaihi manussaihi| rājā, bhikkhavai, cak‍kavattī imāya paṭhamāya iddhiyā saman‍nāgatau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rājā cak‍kavattī dīghāyukau hauti ciraṭṭhitikau ativiya añ‍ñaihi  manussaihi| rājā, bhikkhavai, cak‍kavattī imāya dutiyāya iddhiyā saman‍nāgatau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rājā cak‍kavattī appābādhau hauti appātaṅkau samavaipākiniyā gahaṇiyā saman‍nāgatau nātisītāya nāc‍cuṇhāya ativiya añ‍ñaihi manussaihi| rājā, bhikkhavai, cak‍kavattī imāya tatiyāya iddhiyā saman‍nāgatau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rājā cak‍kavattī brāhmaṇagahapatikānaṃ piyau hauti manāpau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, pitā puttānaṃ piyau hauti manāpau, aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, rājā cak‍kavattī brāhmaṇagahapatikānaṃ piyau hauti manāpau| rañ‍ñaupi, bhikkhavai, cak‍kavattissa brāhmaṇagahapatikā piyā haunti manāpā| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, pitu puttā piyā haunti manāpā, aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñaupi cak‍kavattissa brāhmaṇagahapatikā  piyā haunti manāpā|

‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, rājā cak‍kavattī caturaṅginiyā saināya uyyānabhūmiṃ niyyāsi| atha khau, bhikkhavai, brāhmaṇagahapatikā rājānaṃ cak‍kavattiṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘ataramānau, daiva, yāhi yathā taṃ mayaṃ cirataraṃ passaiyyāmā’ti| rājāpi, bhikkhavai, cak‍kavattī sārathiṃ āmantaisi – ‘ataramānau, sārathi, paisaihi yathā maṃ brāhmaṇagahapatikā cirataraṃ passaiyyu’nti| rājā, bhikkhavai, cak‍kavattī imāya catutthāya iddhiyā saman‍nāgatau hauti| rājā, bhikkhavai, cak‍kavattī imāhi catūhi iddhīhi saman‍nāgatau hauti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu khau rājā cak‍kavattī imaihi sattahi ratanaihi saman‍nāgatau imāhi catūhi ca iddhīhi tataunidānaṃ sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidiyaithā’’ti? ‘‘aikamaikainapi, bhantai, ratanaina taina ratanaina (sī॰) saman‍nāgatau rājā cak‍kavattī tataunidānaṃ sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidiyaitha, kau pana vādau sattahi ratanaihi catūhi ca iddhīhī’’ti?

260. atha khau bhagavā parittaṃ pāṇimattaṃ pāsāṇaṃ gahaitvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamau nu khau mahantatarau – yau cāyaṃ mayā parittau pāṇimattau pāsāṇau  gahitau yau ca himavā pabbatarājā’’ti? ‘‘appamattakau ayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā parittau pāṇimattau pāsāṇau gahitau; himavantaṃ pabbatarājānaṃ upanidhāya saṅkhampi na upaiti; kalabhāgampi na upaiti; upanidhampi na upaitī’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yaṃ rājā cak‍kavattī sattahi ratanaihi saman‍nāgatau  catūhi ca iddhīhi tataunidānaṃ sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti taṃ dibbassa sukhassa upanidhāya saṅkhampi na upaiti; kalabhāgampi na upaiti; upanidhampi na upaiti’’|

‘‘sa khau sau, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitau sacai kadāci karahaci dīghassa addhunau ac‍cayaina manussattaṃ āgacchati, yāni tāni uc‍cākulāni – khattiyamahāsālakulaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālakulaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālakulaṃ vā tathārūpai kulai pac‍cājāyati aḍḍhai mahaddhanai mahābhaugai pahūtajātarūparajatai pahūtavittūpakaraṇai pahūtadhanadhañ‍ñai| sau ca hauti abhirūpau dassanīyau pāsādikau paramāya  vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatau, lābhī an‍nassa pānassa vatthassa yānassa mālāgandhavilaipanassa saiyyāvasathapadīpaiyyassa| sau kāyaina sucaritaṃ carati, vācāya sucaritaṃ carati, manasā sucaritaṃ carati| sau kāyaina  sucaritaṃ caritvā, vācāya sucaritaṃ caritvā, manasā sucaritaṃ caritvā, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, akkhadhuttau paṭhamainaiva kaṭaggahaina mahantaṃ bhaugakkhandhaṃ adhigacchaiyya; appamattakau sau, bhikkhavai, kaṭaggahau yaṃ sau akkhadhuttau paṭhamainaiva kaṭaggahaina mahantaṃ bhaugakkhandhaṃ adhigacchaiyya| atha khau ayamaiva tatau mahantatarau kaṭaggahau yaṃ sau paṇḍitau kāyaina sucaritaṃ caritvā, vācāya sucaritaṃ caritvā, manasā sucaritaṃ caritvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ  laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, kaivalā paripūrā paṇḍitabhūmī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

bālapaṇḍitasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ navamaṃ|

10. daivadūtasuttaṃ

261. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, dvai agārā sadvārā sandhidvārā (ka॰), tattha cakkhumā purisau majjhai ṭhitau passaiyya manussai gaihaṃ pavisantaipi nikkhamantaipi anucaṅkamantaipi anuvicarantaipi; aivamaiva khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passāmi cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai, sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāmi – ‘imai vata bhauntau sattā kāyasucaritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīsucaritaina saman‍nāgatā manausucaritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ anupavādakā  sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā; tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā| imai vā pana bhauntau sattā kāyasucaritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīsucaritaina saman‍nāgatā manausucaritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ  anupavādakā sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā; tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā manussaisu upapan‍nā| imai vata bhauntau sattā kāyaduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā manauduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ upavādakā micchādiṭṭhikā micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā; tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā paittivisayaṃ upapan‍nā| imai vā pana bhauntau sattā kāyaduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā manauduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ upavādakā micchādiṭṭhikā micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā; tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā tiracchānayauniṃ upapan‍nā| imai vā pana bhauntau sattā kāyaduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā manauduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ upavādakā micchādiṭṭhikā micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā; tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍nā’’’ti|

262. ‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, nirayapālā nānābāhāsu gahaitvā yamassa rañ‍ñau dassainti – ‘ayaṃ, daiva, purisau amattaiyyau apaittaiyyau asāmañ‍ñau abrāhmañ‍ñau, na kulai jaiṭṭhāpacāyī| imassa  daivau daṇḍaṃ paṇaitū’ti| tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā paṭhamaṃ daivadūtaṃ samanuyuñ‍jati samanugāhati samanubhāsati – ‘ambhau purisa, na tvaṃ addasa manussaisu paṭhamaṃ daivadūtaṃ pātubhūta’nti? sau aivamāha – ‘nāddasaṃ, bhantai’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā aivamāha – ‘ambhau purisa, na tvaṃ addasa manussaisu daharaṃ kumāraṃ mandaṃ uttānasaiyyakaṃ sakai muttakarīsai palipan‍naṃ saimāna’nti? sau aivamāha – ‘addasaṃ, bhantai’’’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā aivamāha – ‘ambhau purisa, tassa tai viñ‍ñussa satau mahal‍lakassa  na aitadahausi – ahampi khaumhi jātidhammau, jātiṃ anatītau| handāhaṃ kalyāṇaṃ karaumi kāyaina vācāya manasā’ti? sau aivamāha – ‘nāsakkhissaṃ, bhantai, pamādassaṃ, bhantai’’’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā aivamāha – ‘ambhau purisa, pamādavatāya na kalyāṇamakāsi kāyaina vācāya manasā| taggha tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, tathā karissanti yathā taṃ pamattaṃ| taṃ khau pana tai aitaṃ pāpakammaṃ pāpaṃ kammaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) naiva mātarā kataṃ na pitarā kataṃ  na bhātarā kataṃ na bhaginiyā kataṃ na mittāmac‍caihi kataṃ na ñātisālauhitaihi kataṃ na samaṇabrāhmaṇaihi kataṃ na daivatāhi kataṃ, tayāvaitaṃ pāpakammaṃ pāpaṃ kammaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) kataṃ, tvañ‍ñaivaitassa vipākaṃ paṭisaṃvaidissasī’’’ti|

263. ‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā paṭhamaṃ daivadūtaṃ samanuyuñ‍jitvā samanugāhitvā samanubhāsitvā dutiyaṃ daivadūtaṃ samanuyuñ‍jati samanugāhati samanubhāsati – ‘ambhau purisa, na tvaṃ addasa manussaisu dutiyaṃ daivadūtaṃ pātubhūta’nti? sau aivamāha – ‘nāddasaṃ, bhantai’’’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā aivamāha – ‘ambhau purisa, na tvaṃ addasa manussaisu itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā ( ) (āsītikaṃ vā nāvutikaṃ vā vassasatikaṃ vā jātiyā) (ka॰ sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) tikaṅguttaraipi jiṇṇaṃ gaupānasivaṅkaṃ bhauggaṃ daṇḍaparāyanaṃ pavaidhamānaṃ gacchantaṃ āturaṃ gatayaubbanaṃ khaṇḍadantaṃ palitakaisaṃ vilūnaṃ khalitasiraṃ khalitaṃsirau (sī॰), khalitaṃsiraṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) valinaṃ tilakāhatagatta’nti? sau aivamāha – ‘addasaṃ, bhantai’’’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā aivamāha – ‘ambhau purisa, tassa tai viñ‍ñussa satau  mahal‍lakassa na aitadahausi – ahampi khaumhi jarādhammau, jaraṃ anatītau| handāhaṃ kalyāṇaṃ karaumi kāyaina vācāya manasā’ti? sau aivamāha – ‘nāsakkhissaṃ, bhantai, pamādassaṃ, bhantai’’’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā aivamāha – ‘ambhau purisa, pamādavatāya na kalyāṇamakāsi kāyaina vācāya manasā| taggha tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, tathā karissanti yathā taṃ pamattaṃ| taṃ khau pana tai aitaṃ pāpakammaṃ naiva mātarā kataṃ na pitarā kataṃ na bhātarā kataṃ na bhaginiyā kataṃ na mittāmac‍caihi kataṃ na ñātisālauhitaihi kataṃ na  samaṇabrāhmaṇaihi kataṃ na daivatāhi kataṃ, tayāvaitaṃ pāpakammaṃ kataṃ, tvañ‍ñaivaitassa vipākaṃ paṭisaṃvaidissasī’’’ti|

264. ‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā dutiyaṃ daivadūtaṃ samanuyuñ‍jitvā samanugāhitvā samanubhāsitvā tatiyaṃ daivadūtaṃ samanuyuñ‍jati samanugāhati samanubhāsati – ‘ambhau  purisa, na tvaṃ addasa manussaisu tatiyaṃ daivadūtaṃ pātubhūta’nti? sau aivamāha – ‘nāddasaṃ, bhantai’’’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā aivamāha – ‘ambhau purisa, na tvaṃ addasa manussaisu itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā ābādhikaṃ dukkhitaṃ bāḷhagilānaṃ sakai muttakarīsai palipan‍naṃ saimānaṃ añ‍ñaihi vuṭṭhāpiyamānaṃ añ‍ñaihi saṃvaisiyamāna’nti? sau aivamāha – ‘addasaṃ, bhantai’’’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā aivamāha – ‘ambhau purisa, tassa tai viñ‍ñussa satau mahal‍lakassa na aitadahausi – ahampi khaumhi byādhidhammau, byādhiṃ anatītau| handāhaṃ kalyāṇaṃ karaumi kāyaina vācāya manasā’ti? sau aivamāha – ‘nāsakkhissaṃ, bhantai, pamādassaṃ, bhantai’’’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā aivamāha – ‘ambhau purisa, pamādavatāya na kalyāṇamakāsi kāyaina vācāya manasā| taggha tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, tathā karissanti yathā taṃ pamattaṃ| taṃ khau pana tai aitaṃ pāpakammaṃ naiva mātarā kataṃ na pitarā kataṃ na bhātarā kataṃ  na bhaginiyā kataṃ na mittāmac‍caihi kataṃ na ñātisālauhitaihi kataṃ na samaṇabrāhmaṇaihi kataṃ na daivatāhi kataṃ, tayāvaitaṃ pāpakammaṃ kataṃ, tvañ‍ñaivaitassa vipākaṃ paṭisaṃvaidissasī’’’ti|

265. ‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā tatiyaṃ daivadūtaṃ samanuyuñ‍jitvā samanugāhitvā samanubhāsitvā catutthaṃ daivadūtaṃ samanuyuñ‍jati samanugāhati samanubhāsati – ‘ambhau purisa, na tvaṃ addasa manussaisu catutthaṃ daivadūtaṃ pātubhūta’nti? sau aivamāha – ‘nāddasaṃ, bhantai’’’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā aivamāha – ‘ambhau purisa, na tvaṃ addasa manussaisu rājānau cauraṃ āgucāriṃ gahaitvā vividhā kammakāraṇā kāraintai – kasāhipi tāḷaintai vaittaihipi tāḷaintai addhadaṇḍakaihipi tāḷaintai hatthampi chindantai pādampi chindantai hatthapādampi chindantai kaṇṇampi chindantai  nāsampi chindantai kaṇṇanāsampi chindantai bilaṅgathālikampi karauntai saṅkhamuṇḍikampi karauntai rāhumukhampi karauntai jautimālikampi karauntai hatthapaj‍jautikampi karauntai  airakavattikampi karauntai cīrakavāsikampi karauntai aiṇaiyyakampi karauntai baḷisamaṃsikampi karauntai kahāpaṇikampi karauntai khārāpatacchikampi karauntai palighaparivattikampi karauntai palālapīṭhakampi karauntai tattainapi tailaina ausiñ‍cantai sunakhaihipi khādāpaintai jīvantampi sūlai uttāsaintai asināpi sīsaṃ chindantai’ti? sau aivamāha – ‘addasaṃ, bhantai’’’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā aivamāha – ‘ambhau purisa, tassa tai viñ‍ñussa satau mahal‍lakassa na aitadahausi – yai kira, bhau, pāpakāni kammāni karaunti tai diṭṭhaiva dhammai aivarūpā vividhā kammakāraṇā karīyanti, kimaṅgaṃ kimaṅga (sī॰ pī॰) pana parattha! handāhaṃ kalyāṇaṃ karaumi kāyaina vācāya manasā’ti? sau aivamāha – ‘nāsakkhissaṃ, bhantai, pamādassaṃ, bhantai’’’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā aivamāha – ‘ambhau purisa, pamādavatāya na kalyāṇamakāsi kāyaina vācāya manasā| taggha tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, tathā karissanti yathā taṃ pamattaṃ| taṃ khau pana tai aitaṃ pāpakammaṃ naiva mātarā kataṃ na pitarā kataṃ na bhātarā kataṃ na bhaginiyā kataṃ na mittāmac‍caihi kataṃ na ñātisālauhitaihi kataṃ na samaṇabrāhmaṇaihi kataṃ  na daivatāhi kataṃ, tayāvaitaṃ pāpakammaṃ kataṃ, tvañ‍ñaivaitassa vipākaṃ paṭisaṃvaidissasī’’’ti|

266. ‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā catutthaṃ daivadūtaṃ samanuyuñ‍jitvā samanugāhitvā samanubhāsitvā pañ‍camaṃ daivadūtaṃ samanuyuñ‍jati  samanugāhati samanubhāsati – ‘ambhau purisa, na tvaṃ addasa manussaisu pañ‍camaṃ daivadūtaṃ pātubhūta’nti? sau aivamāha – ‘nāddasaṃ, bhantai’’’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā aivamāha – ‘ambhau purisa, na tvaṃ addasa manussaisu itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā aikāhamataṃ vā dvīhamataṃ vā tīhamataṃ vā uddhumātakaṃ vinīlakaṃ vipubbakajāta’nti? sau aivamāha – ‘addasaṃ, bhantai’’’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā aivamāha – ‘ambhau purisa, tassa tai viñ‍ñussa satau mahal‍lakassa na aitadahausi – ahampi khaumhi maraṇadhammau, maraṇaṃ anatītau| handāhaṃ kalyāṇaṃ karaumi kāyaina vācāya manasā’ti? sau aivamāha – ‘nāsakkhissaṃ, bhantai, pamādassaṃ, bhantai’’’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā aivamāha – ‘ambhau purisa, pamādavatāya na kalyāṇamakāsi kāyaina vācāya manasā| taggha tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, tathā karissanti yathā taṃ pamattaṃ| taṃ khau pana tai aitaṃ pāpakammaṃ naiva mātarā kataṃ na pitarā kataṃ na bhātarā kataṃ na bhaginiyā kataṃ na mittāmac‍caihi kataṃ na ñātisālauhitaihi kataṃ na samaṇabrāhmaṇaihi kataṃ na daivatāhi kataṃ, tayāvaitaṃ pāpakammaṃ kataṃ, tvañ‍ñaivaitassa vipākaṃ paṭisaṃvaidissasī’’’ti|

267. ‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā pañ‍camaṃ daivadūtaṃ samanuyuñ‍jitvā samanugāhitvā samanubhāsitvā tuṇhī hauti| tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, nirayapālā pañ‍cavidhabandhanaṃ  nāma kammakāraṇaṃ karaunti – tattaṃ ayaukhilaṃ hatthai gamainti, tattaṃ ayaukhilaṃ dutiyai  hatthai gamainti, tattaṃ ayaukhilaṃ pādai gamainti, tattaṃ ayaukhilaṃ dutiyai pādai gamainti, tattaṃ ayaukhilaṃ majjhaiurasmiṃ  gamainti| sau tattha dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidaiti, na ca tāva kālaṃ karauti yāva na taṃ pāpakammaṃ byantīhauti| tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, nirayapālā saṃvaisaitvā kuṭhārīhi tacchanti…pai॰… tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, nirayapālā uddhaṃpādaṃ adhausiraṃ gahaitvā vāsīhi tacchanti…pai॰… tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, nirayapālā rathai yaujaitvā ādittāya pathaviyā sampaj‍jalitāya sajautibhūtāya sāraintipi, pac‍cāsāraintipi…pai॰… tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, nirayapālā mahantaṃ aṅgārapabbataṃ ādittaṃ sampaj‍jalitaṃ sajautibhūtaṃ āraupaintipi auraupaintipi…pai॰… tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, nirayapālā uddhaṃpādaṃ adhausiraṃ gahaitvā tattāya lauhakumbhiyā pakkhipanti ādittāya sampaj‍jalitāya sajautibhūtāya| sau tattha phaiṇuddaihakaṃ pac‍cati| sau tattha phaiṇuddaihakaṃ pac‍camānau sakimpi uddhaṃ gacchati, sakimpi adhau gacchati, sakimpi tiriyaṃ gacchati| sau tattha dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidaiti, na ca tāva kālaṅkarauti yāva na taṃ pāpakammaṃ byantīhauti| tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, nirayapālā mahānirayai pakkhipanti| sau khau pana, bhikkhavai, mahānirayau –

‘‘catuk‍kaṇṇau  catudvārau, vibhattau bhāgasau mitau|

ayaupākārapariyantau, ayasā paṭikuj‍jitau||

‘‘tassa ayaumayā bhūmi, jalitā taijasāyutā|

samantā yaujanasataṃ, pharitvā tiṭṭhati sabbadā’’||

268. ‘‘tassa khau pana, bhikkhavai, mahānirayassa puratthimāya bhittiyā ac‍ci uṭṭhahitvā pacchimāya bhittiyā paṭihañ‍ñati, pacchimāya bhittiyā ac‍ci uṭṭhahitvā puratthimāya bhittiyā  paṭihañ‍ñati, uttarāya bhittiyā ac‍ci uṭṭhahitvā dakkhiṇāya bhittiyā paṭihañ‍ñati, dakkhiṇāya bhittiyā ac‍ci uṭṭhahitvā uttarāya bhittiyā paṭihañ‍ñati, haiṭṭhā ac‍ci uṭṭhahitvā upari paṭihañ‍ñati, uparitau ac‍ci uṭṭhahitvā haiṭṭhā paṭihañ‍ñati| sau tattha dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidaiti, na ca tāva kālaṅkarauti yāva na taṃ pāpakammaṃ byantīhauti|

‘‘hauti  khau sau, bhikkhavai, samayau yaṃ kadāci karahaci dīghassa addhunau ac‍cayaina tassa mahānirayassa puratthimaṃ dvāraṃ apāpurīyati avāpurīyati (sī॰)| sau tattha sīghaina javaina dhāvati| tassa sīghaina javaina dhāvatau chavimpi ḍayhati, cammampi ḍayhati, maṃsampi ḍayhati, nhārumpi ḍayhati, aṭṭhīnipi sampadhūpāyanti, ubbhataṃ tādisamaiva hauti| yatau ca khau sau, bhikkhavai, bahusampattau hauti, atha taṃ dvāraṃ pidhīyati pithīyati (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| sau tattha dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidaiti, na ca tāva kālaṅkarauti yāva na taṃ pāpakammaṃ byantīhauti|

‘‘hauti khau sau, bhikkhavai, samayau yaṃ kadāci karahaci dīghassa addhunau ac‍cayaina tassa mahānirayassa pacchimaṃ dvāraṃ apāpurīyati…pai॰… uttaraṃ dvāraṃ apāpurīyati…pai॰… dakkhiṇaṃ dvāraṃ apāpurīyati| sau tattha sīghaina javaina dhāvati| tassa sīghaina javaina dhāvatau chavimpi ḍayhati, cammampi ḍayhati, maṃsampi ḍayhati, nhārumpi ḍayhati, aṭṭhīnipi sampadhūpāyanti, ubbhataṃ tādisamaiva hauti| yatau ca khau sau, bhikkhavai, bahusampattau hauti, atha taṃ dvāraṃ pidhīyati| sau tattha dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidaiti, na ca tāva kālaṅkarauti yāva na taṃ pāpakammaṃ byantīhauti|

‘‘hauti  khau sau, bhikkhavai, samayau yaṃ kadāci karahaci dīghassa addhunau ac‍cayaina tassa mahānirayassa puratthimaṃ dvāraṃ apāpurīyati| sau tattha sīghaina javaina dhāvati| tassa sīghaina javaina dhāvatau chavimpi ḍayhati, cammampi ḍayhati, maṃsampi ḍayhati, nhārumpi ḍayhati, aṭṭhīnipi sampadhūpāyanti, ubbhataṃ tādisamaiva hauti| sau taina dvāraina nikkhamati|

269. ‘‘tassa khau pana, bhikkhavai, mahānirayassa samanantarā  sahitamaiva mahantau gūthanirayau| sau tattha patati| tasmiṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, gūthanirayai sūcimukhā pāṇā chaviṃ chindanti, chaviṃ chaitvā cammaṃ chindanti, cammaṃ chaitvā maṃsaṃ chindanti, maṃsaṃ chaitvā nhāruṃ chindanti, nhāruṃ chaitvā aṭṭhiṃ chindanti, aṭṭhiṃ chaitvā aṭṭhimiñ‍jaṃ khādanti| sau tattha dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidaiti, na ca tāva kālaṅkarauti yāva na taṃ pāpakammaṃ byantīhauti|

‘‘tassa  khau pana, bhikkhavai, gūthanirayassa samanantarā sahitamaiva mahantau  kuk‍kulanirayau| sau tattha patati| sau tattha dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidaiti, na ca tāva kālaṅkarauti yāva na taṃ pāpakammaṃ byantīhauti|

‘‘tassa khau pana, bhikkhavai, kuk‍kulanirayassa samanantarā sahitamaiva mahantaṃ simbalivanaṃ uddhaṃ uc‍caṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰), ubbhatau (ka॰) yaujanamuggataṃ sauḷasaṅgulakaṇṭakaṃ sauḷasaṅgulakaṇḍakaṃ (sī॰) ādittaṃ sampaj‍jalitaṃ sajautibhūtaṃ| tattha āraupaintipi auraupaintipi| sau tattha dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidaiti, na ca tāva kālaṅkarauti yāva na taṃ pāpakammaṃ byantīhauti|

‘‘tassa khau pana, bhikkhavai, simbalivanassa samanantarā sahitamaiva mahantaṃ asipattavanaṃ| sau tattha pavisati| tassa vātairitāni pattāni patitāni hatthampi chindanti, pādampi chindanti, hatthapādampi chindanti, kaṇṇampi chindanti, nāsampi chindanti, kaṇṇanāsampi chindanti| sau tattha dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidaiti, na ca tāva kālaṅkarauti yāva na taṃ pāpakammaṃ byantīhauti|

‘‘tassa khau pana, bhikkhavai, asipattavanassa samanantarā sahitamaiva mahatī khāraudakā nadī khāraudikā nadī (sī॰)| sau tattha patati| sau tattha anusautampi vuyhati, paṭisautampi vuyhati, anusautapaṭisautampi vuyhati| sau tattha dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidaiti, na ca tāva kālaṅkarauti yāva na taṃ pāpakammaṃ byantīhauti|

270. ‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, nirayapālā balisaina uddharitvā  thalai patiṭṭhāpaitvā  aivamāhaṃsu – ‘ambhau purisa, kiṃ icchasī’ti? sau aivamāha – ‘jighacchitausmi, bhantai’ti| tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, nirayapālā tattaina ayausaṅkunā mukhaṃ vivaritvā ādittaina sampaj‍jalitaina sajautibhūtaina tattaṃ lauhaguḷaṃ mukhai pakkhipanti ādittaṃ sampaj‍jalitaṃ sajautibhūtaṃ| sau tassa taṃ tassa (ka॰), tassa (sī॰ pī॰) auṭṭhampi dahati ḍayhati (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), mukhampi dahati, kaṇṭhampi dahati, urampi udarampi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) dahati, antampi antaguṇampi ādāya adhaubhāgā nikkhamati| sau tattha dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidaiti, na ca tāva kālaṅkarauti yāva na taṃ pāpakammaṃ byantīhauti|

‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, nirayapālā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘ambhau purisa, kiṃ icchasī’ti? sau aivamāha – ‘pipāsitausmi, bhantai’ti| tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, nirayapālā tattaina ayausaṅkunā mukhaṃ vivaritvā ādittaina sampaj‍jalitaina sajautibhūtaina tattaṃ tambalauhaṃ mukhai āsiñ‍canti ādittaṃ sampaj‍jalitaṃ sajautibhūtaṃ| taṃ tassa aittha pana pāṭhabhaidau natthi auṭṭhampi dahati, mukhampi dahati, kaṇṭhampi dahati, urampi dahati, antampi antaguṇampi ādāya adhaubhāgā nikkhamati| sau tattha dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidaiti, na ca tāva kālaṅkarauti, yāva na taṃ pāpakammaṃ byantīhauti| tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, nirayapālā puna mahānirayai pakkhipanti|

‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamassa rañ‍ñau aitadahausi – ‘yai kira, bhau, laukai pāpakāni akusalāni kammāni karaunti tai aivarūpā vividhā kammakāraṇā karīyanti| ahau vatāhaṃ manussattaṃ labhaiyyaṃ| tathāgatau ca laukai uppaj‍jaiyya arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau| tañ‍cāhaṃ bhagavantaṃ payirupāsaiyyaṃ| sau ca mai bhagavā dhammaṃ daisaiyya| tassa cāhaṃ bhagavatau dhammaṃ ājānaiyya’nti| taṃ khau panāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, nāñ‍ñassa samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā sutvā vadāmi, api ca yadaiva sāmaṃ ñātaṃ sāmaṃ diṭṭhaṃ sāmaṃ viditaṃ tadaivāhaṃ vadāmī’’ti|

271. idamavauca  bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna idaṃ vatvā (sī॰ pī॰) aivamīdisaisu ṭhānaisu sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘cauditā daivadūtaihi, yai pamaj‍janti māṇavā|

tai dīgharattaṃ saucanti, hīnakāyūpagā narā||

‘‘yai ca khau daivadūtaihi, santau sappurisā idha|

cauditā nappamaj‍janti, ariyadhammai kudācanaṃ||

‘‘upādānai bhayaṃ disvā, jātimaraṇasambhavai|

anupādā vimuc‍canti, jātimaraṇasaṅkhayai||

‘‘tai  khaimappattā sukhinau, diṭṭhadhammābhinibbutā|

sabbavairabhayātītā, sabbadukkhaṃ sabbadukkhā (ka॰) upac‍cagu’’nti||

daivadūtasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dasamaṃ|

suñ‍ñatavaggau niṭṭhitau tatiyau|


tassuddānaṃ  –

dvidhāva suñ‍ñatā hauti, abbhutadhammabākulaṃ|

aciravatabhūmijanāmau, anuruddhupak‍kilaisaṃ|

bālapaṇḍitau daivadūtañ‍ca tai dasāti||


4. vibhaṅgavaggau

1. bhaddaikarattasuttaṃ

272. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘bhaddaikarattassa vau, bhikkhavai, uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍ca daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘atītaṃ nānvāgamaiyya, nappaṭikaṅkhai anāgataṃ|

yadatītaṃ pahīnaṃ taṃ, appattañ‍ca anāgataṃ||

‘‘pac‍cuppan‍nañ‍ca yau yaṃ (naittipāḷi) dhammaṃ, tattha tattha vipassati|

asaṃhīraṃ asaṃhiraṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) asaṃkuppaṃ, taṃ vidvā manubrūhayai||

‘‘aj‍jaiva kic‍camātappaṃ kic‍caṃ ātappaṃ (sī॰ ka॰), kau jañ‍ñā maraṇaṃ suvai|

na hi nau saṅgaraṃ taina, mahāsainaina mac‍cunā||

‘‘aivaṃ vihāriṃ ātāpiṃ, ahaurattamatanditaṃ|

taṃ vai bhaddaikarattauti, santau ācikkhatai muni’’ munīti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)||

273. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, atītaṃ anvāgamaiti? ‘aivaṃrūpau ahausiṃ atītamaddhāna’nti  tattha nandiṃ samanvānaiti, ‘aivaṃvaidanau ahausiṃ atītamaddhāna’nti tattha nandiṃ samanvānaiti, ‘aivaṃsañ‍ñau ahausiṃ atītamaddhāna’nti tattha nandiṃ samanvānaiti, ‘aivaṃsaṅkhārau ahausiṃ atītamaddhāna’nti tattha nandiṃ samanvānaiti, ‘aivaṃviñ‍ñāṇau ahausiṃ atītamaddhāna’nti tattha nandiṃ samanvānaiti – aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, atītaṃ anvāgamaiti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, atītaṃ nānvāgamaiti? ‘aivaṃrūpau ahausiṃ atītamaddhāna’nti tattha nandiṃ na samanvānaiti, ‘aivaṃvaidanau ahausiṃ atītamaddhāna’nti tattha nandiṃ na samanvānaiti, ‘aivaṃsañ‍ñau ahausiṃ atītamaddhāna’nti  tattha nandiṃ na samanvānaiti, ‘aivaṃsaṅkhārau ahausiṃ atītamaddhāna’nti tattha nandiṃ na samanvānaiti, ‘aivaṃviñ‍ñāṇau ahausiṃ atītamaddhāna’nti tattha nandiṃ na samanvānaiti – aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, atītaṃ nānvāgamaiti|

274. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, anāgataṃ paṭikaṅkhati? ‘aivaṃrūpau siyaṃ anāgatamaddhāna’nti tattha nandiṃ samanvānaiti, aivaṃvaidanau siyaṃ…pai॰… aivaṃsañ‍ñau siyaṃ… aivaṃsaṅkhārau siyaṃ… aivaṃviñ‍ñāṇau siyaṃ anāgatamaddhānanti tattha nandiṃ samanvānaiti – aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, anāgataṃ paṭikaṅkhati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, anāgataṃ nappaṭikaṅkhati? ‘aivaṃrūpau siyaṃ anāgatamaddhāna’nti tattha nandiṃ na samanvānaiti, aivaṃvaidanau siyaṃ … aivaṃsañ‍ñau siyaṃ… aivaṃsaṅkhārau siyaṃ… ‘aivaṃviñ‍ñāṇau siyaṃ anāgatamaddhāna’nti tattha nandiṃ na samanvānaiti – aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, anāgataṃ nappaṭikaṅkhati|

275. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, pac‍cuppan‍naisu dhammaisu saṃhīrati? idha, bhikkhavai, assutavā puthuj‍janau ariyānaṃ adassāvī ariyadhammassa akauvidau ariyadhammai avinītau sappurisānaṃ adassāvī sappurisadhammassa akauvidau sappurisadhammai avinītau rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ, attani vā rūpaṃ, rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ; vaidanaṃ…pai॰… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ  attatau samanupassati, viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ – aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pac‍cuppan‍naisu dhammaisu saṃhīrati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, pac‍cuppan‍naisu dhammaisu na saṃhīrati? idha, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau ariyānaṃ dassāvī ariyadhammassa kauvidau ariyadhammai suvinītau sappurisānaṃ dassāvī sappurisadhammassa kauvidau sappurisadhammai suvinītau na rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, na rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ, na attani vā rūpaṃ, na rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ; na vaidanaṃ… na sañ‍ñaṃ… na saṅkhārai… na viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati, na viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ, na attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, na viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ – aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pac‍cuppan‍naisu dhammaisu na saṃhīrati|

‘‘atītaṃ  nānvāgamaiyya, nappaṭikaṅkhai anāgataṃ|

yadatītaṃ pahīnaṃ taṃ, appattañ‍ca anāgataṃ||

‘‘pac‍cuppan‍nañ‍ca  yau dhammaṃ, tattha tattha vipassati|

asaṃhīraṃ asaṃkuppaṃ, taṃ vidvā manubrūhayai||

‘‘aj‍jaiva kic‍camātappaṃ, kau jañ‍ñā maraṇaṃ suvai|

na hi nau saṅgaraṃ taina, mahāsainaina mac‍cunā||

‘‘aivaṃ vihāriṃ ātāpiṃ, ahaurattamatanditaṃ|

taṃ vai bhaddaikarattauti, santau ācikkhatai munī’’ti||

‘‘‘bhaddaikarattassa vau, bhikkhavai, uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍ca daisaissāmī’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vutta’’nti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

bhaddaikarattasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. ānandabhaddaikarattasuttaṃ

276. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā ānandau upaṭṭhānasālāyaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaiti samādapaiti samuttaijaiti  sampahaṃsaiti, bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍ca bhāsati|

atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yainupaṭṭhānasālā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhikkhavai, upaṭṭhānasālāyaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi, bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍ca abhāsī’’ti? ‘‘āyasmā, bhantai, ānandau upaṭṭhānasālāyaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi, bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍ca abhāsī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhūnaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi, bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍ca abhāsī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhikkhūnaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisiṃ samādapaisiṃ samuttaijaisiṃ sampahaṃsaisiṃ, bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍ca abhāsiṃ –

‘‘atītaṃ  nānvāgamaiyya, nappaṭikaṅkhai anāgataṃ|

yadatītaṃ pahīnaṃ taṃ, appattañ‍ca anāgataṃ||

‘‘pac‍cuppan‍nañ‍ca yau dhammaṃ, tattha tattha vipassati|

asaṃhīraṃ asaṃkuppaṃ, taṃ vidvā manubrūhayai||

‘‘aj‍jaiva  kic‍camātappaṃ, kau jañ‍ñā maraṇaṃ suvai|

na hi nau saṅgaraṃ taina, mahāsainaina mac‍cunā||

‘‘aivaṃ vihāriṃ ātāpiṃ, ahaurattamatanditaṃ|

taṃ vai bhaddaikarattauti, santau ācikkhatai muni’’||

277. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, āvusau, atītaṃ anvāgamaiti? aivaṃrūpau ahausiṃ atītamaddhānanti tattha nandiṃ samanvānaiti, aivaṃvaidanau ahausiṃ atītamaddhānanti tattha nandiṃ samanvānaiti, aivaṃsañ‍ñau ahausiṃ atītamaddhānanti tattha nandiṃ samanvānaiti, aivaṃsaṅkhārau ahausiṃ atītamaddhānanti tattha nandiṃ samanvānaiti, aivaṃviñ‍ñāṇau ahausiṃ atītamaddhānanti tattha nandiṃ samanvānaiti – aivaṃ khau, āvusau, atītaṃ anvāgamaiti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, āvusau, atītaṃ nānvāgamaiti? aivaṃrūpau ahausiṃ atītamaddhānanti tattha nandiṃ na samanvānaiti, aivaṃvaidanau ahausiṃ atītamaddhānanti tattha nandiṃ na samanvānaiti, aivaṃsañ‍ñau ahausiṃ atītamaddhānanti tattha nandiṃ na samanvānaiti, aivaṃsaṅkhārau ahausiṃ atītamaddhānanti tattha nandiṃ na samanvānaiti, aivaṃviñ‍ñāṇau ahausiṃ  atītamaddhānanti tattha nandiṃ na samanvānaiti – aivaṃ khau, āvusau, atītaṃ nānvāgamaiti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, āvusau, anāgataṃ paṭikaṅkhati? aivaṃrūpau siyaṃ anāgatamaddhānanti tattha nandiṃ samanvānaiti, aivaṃvaidanau siyaṃ…pai॰… aivaṃsañ‍ñau siyaṃ… aivaṃsaṅkhārau  siyaṃ… aivaṃviñ‍ñāṇau siyaṃ anāgatamaddhānanti tattha nandiṃ samanvānaiti – aivaṃ khau, āvusau, anāgataṃ paṭikaṅkhati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, āvusau, anāgataṃ nappaṭikaṅkhati? aivaṃrūpau siyaṃ anāgatamaddhānanti tattha nandiṃ na samanvānaiti, aivaṃvaidanau siyaṃ…pai॰… aivaṃsañ‍ñau siyaṃ… aivaṃsaṅkhārau siyaṃ… aivaṃviñ‍ñāṇau siyaṃ anāgatamaddhānanti tattha nandiṃ na samanvānaiti – aivaṃ khau, āvusau, anāgataṃ nappaṭikaṅkhati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, āvusau, pac‍cuppan‍naisu dhammaisu saṃhīrati? idha, āvusau, assutavā puthuj‍janau ariyānaṃ adassāvī ariyadhammassa akauvidau ariyadhammai avinītau sappurisānaṃ adassāvī sappurisadhammassa  akauvidau sappurisadhammai avinītau rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ, attani vā rūpaṃ, rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ; vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati, viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ, attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ – aivaṃ khau, āvusau, pac‍cuppan‍naisu dhammaisu saṃhīrati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, āvusau, pac‍cuppan‍naisu dhammaisu na saṃhīrati? idha, āvusau, sutavā ariyasāvakau ariyānaṃ dassāvī ariyadhammassa kauvidau ariyadhammai suvinītau sappurisānaṃ dassāvī sappurisadhammassa kauvidau sappurisadhammai suvinītau na rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, na rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ, na attani vā rūpaṃ, na rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ; na vaidanaṃ… na sañ‍ñaṃ… na saṅkhārai… na viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati, na viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ, na attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, na viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ – aivaṃ khau, āvusau, pac‍cuppan‍naisu dhammaisu na saṃhīrati|

‘‘atītaṃ  nānvāgamaiyya, nappaṭikaṅkhai anāgataṃ|

yadatītaṃ pahīnaṃ taṃ, appattañ‍ca anāgataṃ||

‘‘pac‍cuppan‍nañ‍ca yau dhammaṃ, tattha tattha vipassati|

asaṃhīraṃ asaṃkuppaṃ, taṃ vidvā manubrūhayai||

‘‘aj‍jaiva kic‍camātappaṃ, kau jañ‍ñā maraṇaṃ suvai|

na hi nau saṅgaraṃ taina, mahāsainaina mac‍cunā||

‘‘aivaṃ vihāriṃ ātāpiṃ, ahaurattamatanditaṃ|

taṃ vai bhaddaikarattauti, santau ācikkhatai munī’’ti||

‘‘aivaṃ  khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhikkhūnaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisiṃ samādapaisiṃ samuttaijaisiṃ sampahaṃsaisiṃ, bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍ca abhāsi’’nti|

278. ‘‘sādhu, sādhu, ānanda! sādhu khau tvaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhūnaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi, bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍ca abhāsi –

‘‘atītaṃ nānvāgamaiyya…pai॰…

taṃ vai bhaddaikarattauti, santau ācikkhatai munī’’ti||

‘‘kathañ‍ca, ānanda, atītaṃ anvāgamaiti…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, ānanda, atītaṃ anvāgamaiti| kathañ‍ca, ānanda, atītaṃ nānvāgamaiti…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, ānanda, atītaṃ nānvāgamaiti| kathañ‍ca, ānanda, anāgataṃ paṭikaṅkhati…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, ānanda, anāgataṃ paṭikaṅkhati| kathañ‍ca, ānanda, anāgataṃ nappaṭikaṅkhati…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, ānanda, anāgataṃ nappaṭikaṅkhati| kathañ‍ca, ānanda, pac‍cuppan‍naisu dhammaisu saṃhīrati…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, ānanda, pac‍cuppan‍naisu dhammaisu saṃhīrati| kathañ‍ca, ānanda, pac‍cuppan‍naisu dhammaisu na saṃhīrati…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, ānanda, pac‍cuppan‍naisu dhammaisu na saṃhīrati|

‘‘atītaṃ nānvāgamaiyya…pai॰… 

taṃ vai bhaddaikarattauti, santau ācikkhatai munī’’ti||

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

ānandabhaddaikarattasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. mahākac‍cānabhaddaikarattasuttaṃ

279. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati tapaudārāmai| atha khau āyasmā samiddhi rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya yaina tapaudau tapaudā (sī॰) tainupasaṅkami gattāni parisiñ‍cituṃ| tapaudai gattāni  parisiñ‍citvā pac‍cuttaritvā aikacīvarau aṭṭhāsi gattāni pubbāpayamānau sukkhāpayamānau (ka॰)| atha khau añ‍ñatarā daivatā abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ tapaudaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaināyasmā samiddhi tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau sā daivatā āyasmantaṃ samiddhiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘dhāraisi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍cā’’ti? ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, dhāraimi bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍ca| tvaṃ panāvusau, dhāraisi bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍cā’’ti? ‘‘ahampi khau, bhikkhu, na dhāraimi bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍ca| dhāraisi pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhaddaikarattiyau gāthā’’ti? ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, dhāraimi bhaddaikarattiyau gāthāti| tvaṃ panāvusau, dhāraisi bhaddaikarattiyau gāthā’’ti? ‘‘ahampi khau, bhikkhu na dhāraimi bhaddaikarattiyau gāthāti| uggaṇhāhi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍ca; pariyāpuṇāhi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍ca; dhāraihi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍ca| atthasaṃhitau, bhikkhu, bhaddaikarattassa uddaisau  ca vibhaṅgau ca ādibrahmacariyakau’’ti| idamavauca sā daivatā; idaṃ vatvā tatthaivantaradhāyi|

280. atha khau āyasmā samiddhi tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā samiddhi bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhantai, rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya yaina tapaudau tainupasaṅkamiṃ gattāni parisiñ‍cituṃ| tapaudai  gattāni parisiñ‍citvā pac‍cuttaritvā aikacīvarau aṭṭhāsiṃ gattāni pubbāpayamānau| atha khau bhantai, añ‍ñatarā daivatā abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ tapaudaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ  ṭhitā khau sā daivatā maṃ aitadavauca – ‘dhāraisi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍cā’’’ti?

‘‘aivaṃ vuttai ahaṃ, bhantai, taṃ daivataṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, dhāraimi bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍ca| tvaṃ panāvusau, dhāraisi  bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍cā’ti? ‘ahampi khau, bhikkhu, na dhāraimi bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍ca| dhāraisi pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhaddaikarattiyau gāthā’ti? ‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, dhāraimi bhaddaikarattiyau gāthāti| tvaṃ panāvusau, dhāraisi bhaddaikarattiyau gāthā’ti? ‘ahampi khau, bhikkhu, na dhāraimi bhaddaikarattiyau gāthāti| uggaṇhāhi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhaddaikarattassa  uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍ca; pariyāpuṇāhi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍ca; dhāraihi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍ca| atthasaṃhitau, bhikkhu, bhaddaikarattassa uddaisau ca vibhaṅgau ca ādibrahmacariyakau’ti| idamavauca, bhantai, sā daivatā; idaṃ vatvā tatthaivantaradhāyi| sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍ca daisaitū’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhu, suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā samiddhi bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘atītaṃ nānvāgamaiyya, nappaṭikaṅkhai anāgataṃ|

yadatītaṃ pahīnaṃ taṃ, appattañ‍ca anāgataṃ||

‘‘pac‍cuppan‍nañ‍ca yau dhammaṃ, tattha tattha vipassati|

asaṃhīraṃ asaṃkuppaṃ, taṃ vidvā manubrūhayai||

‘‘aj‍jaiva kic‍camātappaṃ, kau jañ‍ñā maraṇaṃ suvai|

na hi nau saṅgaraṃ taina, mahāsainaina mac‍cunā||

‘‘aivaṃ vihāriṃ ātāpiṃ, ahaurattamatanditaṃ|

taṃ vai bhaddaikarattauti, santau ācikkhatai munī’’ti||

idamavauca bhagavā; idaṃ vatvāna sugatau uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ pāvisi| atha khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau, aitadahausi – ‘‘idaṃ khau nau, āvusau, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ  avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau –

‘‘atītaṃ nānvāgamaiyya, nappaṭikaṅkhai anāgataṃ|

yadatītaṃ pahīnaṃ taṃ, appattañ‍ca anāgataṃ||

‘‘pac‍cuppan‍nañ‍ca yau dhammaṃ, tattha tattha vipassati|

asaṃhīraṃ asaṃkuppaṃ, taṃ vidvā manubrūhayai||

‘‘aj‍jaiva  kic‍camātappaṃ, kau jañ‍ñā maraṇaṃ suvai|

na hi nau saṅgaraṃ taina, mahāsainaina mac‍cunā||

‘‘aivaṃ vihāriṃ ātāpiṃ, ahaurattamatanditaṃ|

taṃ vai bhaddaikarattauti, santau ācikkhatai munī’’ti||

‘‘kau nu khau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajaiyyā’’ti?

atha  khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā mahākac‍cānau satthu caiva saṃvaṇṇitau sambhāvitau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ; pahauti cāyasmā mahākac‍cānau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajituṃ| yaṃnūna mayaṃ yaināyasmā mahākac‍cānau tainupasaṅkamaiyyāma; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyāmā’’ti|

281. atha khau tai bhikkhū yaināyasmā mahākac‍cānau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā mahākac‍cānaina saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā  khau tai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ aitadavaucuṃ  – ‘‘idaṃ khau nau, āvusau kac‍cāna, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau –

‘‘atītaṃ nānvāgamaiyya…pai॰…

taṃ vai bhaddaikarattauti, santau ācikkhatai munī’’ti||

‘‘taisaṃ nau, āvusau kac‍cāna, amhākaṃ, acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau, aitadahausi – idaṃ khau nau, āvusau, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau –

‘‘atītaṃ nānvāgamaiyya…pai॰…

taṃ vai bhaddaikarattauti, santau ācikkhatai munī’’ti||

‘‘kau  nu khau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajaiyyāti? taisaṃ nau, āvusau kac‍cāna, amhākaṃ aitadahausi – ‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā mahākac‍cānau satthu caiva saṃvaṇṇitau sambhāvitau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ| pahauti cāyasmā mahākac‍cānau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajituṃ| yaṃnūna mayaṃ yaināyasmā mahākac‍cānau tainupasaṅkamaiyyāma; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyāmā’ti| vibhajatāyasmā mahākac‍cānau’’ti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, āvusau, purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ  caramānau mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau atik‍kammaiva  mūlaṃ atik‍kamma khandhaṃ sākhāpalāsai sāraṃ pariyaisitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyya; aivaṃ sampadamidaṃ āyasmantānaṃ satthari sammukhībhūtai taṃ bhagavantaṃ atisitvā amhai aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchitabbaṃ mañ‍ñatha mañ‍ñaitha (pī॰)| sau hāvusau, bhagavā jānaṃ jānāti, passaṃ passati, cakkhubhūtau ñāṇabhūtau dhammabhūtau brahmabhūtau vattā pavattā atthassa nin‍naitā amatassa dātā dhammassāmī tathāgatau| sau caiva panaitassa kālau ahausi yaṃ bhagavantaṃyaiva aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyātha, yathā vau bhagavā byākaraiyya tathā naṃ dhāraiyyāthā’’ti|

‘‘addhāvusau kac‍cāna, bhagavā jānaṃ jānāti, passaṃ passati, cakkhubhūtau ñāṇabhūtau dhammabhūtau brahmabhūtau vattā pavattā atthassa nin‍naitā amatassa dātā dhammassāmī tathāgatau| sau caiva panaitassa kālau ahausi yaṃ bhagavantaṃyaiva aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyāma; yathā nau bhagavā byākaraiyya tathā naṃ dhāraiyyāma| api cāyasmā mahākac‍cānau satthucaiva saṃvaṇṇitau sambhāvitau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ; pahauti cāyasmā mahākac‍cānau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajituṃ| vibhajatāyasmā mahākac‍cānau agaruṃ karitvā’’ti agarukaritvā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)|

‘‘taina hāvusau, suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau mahākac‍cānassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā  mahākac‍cānau aitadavauca –

‘‘yaṃ  khau nau, āvusau, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau –

‘‘atītaṃ nānvāgamaiyya…pai॰…

taṃ vai bhaddaikarattauti, santau ācikkhatai munī’’ti||

imassa khau ahaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmi –

282. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, āvusau, atītaṃ anvāgamaiti? iti mai cakkhu  ahausi atītamaddhānaṃ iti rūpāti – tattha chandarāgappaṭibaddhaṃ chandarāgappaṭibandhaṃ (ka॰) hauti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, chandarāgappaṭibaddhattā viñ‍ñāṇassa tadabhinandati, tadabhinandantau atītaṃ anvāgamaiti| iti mai sautaṃ ahausi atītamaddhānaṃ iti saddāti…pai॰… iti mai ghānaṃ ahausi atītamaddhānaṃ iti gandhāti… iti mai jivhā ahausi atītamaddhānaṃ iti rasāti… iti mai kāyau ahausi atītamaddhānaṃ iti phauṭṭhabbāti… iti mai manau ahausi atītamaddhānaṃ iti dhammāti – tattha chandarāgappaṭibaddhaṃ hauti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, chandarāgappaṭibaddhattā viñ‍ñāṇassa tadabhinandati, tadabhinandantau atītaṃ anvāgamaiti – aivaṃ khau, āvusau, atītaṃ anvāgamaiti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, āvusau, atītaṃ nānvāgamaiti? iti mai cakkhu ahausi atītamaddhānaṃ iti rūpāti – tattha na chandarāgappaṭibaddhaṃ hauti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, na chandarāgappaṭibaddhattā viñ‍ñāṇassa na tadabhinandati, na  tadabhinandantau atītaṃ nānvāgamaiti| iti mai sautaṃ ahausi atītamaddhānaṃ iti saddāti…pai॰… iti mai ghānaṃ ahausi atītamaddhānaṃ iti gandhāti… iti mai jivhā ahausi atītamaddhānaṃ iti rasāti… iti mai kāyau ahausi atītamaddhānaṃ iti phauṭṭhabbāti… iti mai manau ahausi atītamaddhānaṃ iti dhammāti – tattha na chandarāgappaṭibaddhaṃ hauti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, na chandarāgappaṭibaddhattā viñ‍ñāṇassa, na tadabhinandati, na tadabhinandantau atītaṃ nānvāgamaiti – aivaṃ khau, āvusau, atītaṃ nānvāgamaiti|

283. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, āvusau, anāgataṃ paṭikaṅkhati? iti mai cakkhu siyā anāgatamaddhānaṃ iti rūpāti – appaṭiladdhassa paṭilābhāya cittaṃ paṇidahati, caitasau paṇidhānapac‍cayā tadabhinandati, tadabhinandantau anāgataṃ paṭikaṅkhati| iti mai sautaṃ siyā anāgatamaddhānaṃ iti saddāti…pai॰… iti mai ghānaṃ siyā anāgatamaddhānaṃ iti gandhāti… iti mai jivhā siyā anāgatamaddhānaṃ iti rasāti… iti mai kāyau siyā anāgatamaddhānaṃ iti phauṭṭhabbāti… iti mai manau siyā anāgatamaddhānaṃ iti  dhammāti – appaṭiladdhassa paṭilābhāya cittaṃ paṇidahati, caitasau paṇidhānapac‍cayā tadabhinandati, tadabhinandantau anāgataṃ paṭikaṅkhati – aivaṃ khau, āvusau, anāgataṃ paṭikaṅkhati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, āvusau, anāgataṃ nappaṭikaṅkhati? iti mai cakkhu siyā anāgatamaddhānaṃ iti rūpāti – appaṭiladdhassa paṭilābhāya cittaṃ nappaṇidahati, caitasau appaṇidhānapac‍cayā na tadabhinandati, na tadabhinandantau anāgataṃ nappaṭikaṅkhati| iti mai sautaṃ siyā anāgatamaddhānaṃ iti saddāti…pai॰… iti mai ghānaṃ siyā anāgatamaddhānaṃ iti gandhāti… iti mai jivhā siyā anāgatamaddhānaṃ iti rasāti… iti mai kāyau siyā anāgatamaddhānaṃ iti phauṭṭhabbāti… iti mai manau siyā anāgatamaddhānaṃ iti dhammāti – appaṭiladdhassa paṭilābhāya cittaṃ nappaṇidahati, caitasau appaṇidhānapac‍cayā na tadabhinandati, na tadabhinandantau anāgataṃ nappaṭikaṅkhati – aivaṃ khau, āvusau, anāgataṃ nappaṭikaṅkhati|

284. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, āvusau, pac‍cuppan‍naisu dhammaisu saṃhīrati? yañ‍cāvusau, cakkhu yai ca rūpā  – ubhayamaitaṃ pac‍cuppan‍naṃ| tasmiṃ cai pac‍cuppan‍nai chandarāgappaṭibaddhaṃ hauti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, chandarāgappaṭibaddhattā viñ‍ñāṇassa tadabhinandati, tadabhinandantau pac‍cuppan‍naisu dhammaisu saṃhīrati| yañ‍cāvusau, sautaṃ yai ca saddā…pai॰… yañ‍cāvusau, ghānaṃ yai ca gandhā… yā cāvusau, jivhā yai ca rasā… yau cāvusau, kāyau yai ca phauṭṭhabbā… yau cāvusau, manau yai ca dhammā – ubhayamaitaṃ pac‍cuppan‍naṃ| tasmiṃ cai pac‍cuppan‍nai chandarāgappaṭibaddhaṃ hauti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, chandarāgappaṭibaddhattā viñ‍ñāṇassa tadabhinandati, tadabhinandantau pac‍cuppan‍naisu dhammaisu saṃhīrati – aivaṃ khau, āvusau, pac‍cuppan‍naisu dhammaisu saṃhīrati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, āvusau, pac‍cuppan‍naisu dhammaisu na saṃhīrati? yañ‍cāvusau, cakkhu yai ca rūpā – ubhayamaitaṃ pac‍cuppan‍naṃ| tasmiṃ cai pac‍cuppan‍nai na chandarāgappaṭibaddhaṃ hauti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, na chandarāgappaṭibaddhattā viñ‍ñāṇassa na tadabhinandati, na tadabhinandantau pac‍cuppan‍naisu dhammaisu na saṃhīrati| yañ‍cāvusau, sautaṃ yai ca saddā…pai॰… yañ‍cāvusau, ghānaṃ yai ca gandhā… yā cāvusau, jivhā yai ca rasā… yau cāvusau, kāyau yai ca phauṭṭhabbā… yau cāvusau, manau yai ca dhammā – ubhayamaitaṃ pac‍cuppan‍naṃ| tasmiṃ cai pac‍cuppan‍nai  na chandarāgappaṭibaddhaṃ hauti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, na chandarāgappaṭibaddhattā viñ‍ñāṇassa na tadabhinandati, na tadabhinandantau pac‍cuppan‍naisu dhammaisu na saṃhīrati – aivaṃ khau, āvusau, pac‍cuppan‍naisu dhammaisu na saṃhīrati|

285. ‘‘yaṃ khau nau, āvusau, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau –

‘‘atītaṃ nānvāgamaiyya…pai॰… 

taṃ vai bhaddaikarattauti, santau ācikkhatai munī’’ti||

‘‘imassa khau ahaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmi| ākaṅkhamānā ca pana tumhai āyasmantau bhagavantaṃyaiva upasaṅkamitvā aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyātha, yathā vau bhagavā byākarauti tathā naṃ dhāraiyyāthā’’ti|

atha  khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau mahākac‍cānassa bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘yaṃ khau nau, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau –

‘‘atītaṃ nānvāgamaiyya…pai॰…

taṃ vai bhaddaikarattauti, santau ācikkhatai munī’’ti||

taisaṃ nau, bhantai, amhākaṃ, acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau, aitadahausi – ‘‘idaṃ khau nau, āvusau, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau –

‘‘atītaṃ  nānvāgamaiyya, nappaṭikaṅkhai anāgataṃ|

yadatītaṃ pahīnaṃ taṃ, appattañ‍ca anāgataṃ||

‘‘pac‍cuppan‍nañ‍ca  yau dhammaṃ, tattha tattha vipassati|

asaṃhīraṃ asaṃkuppaṃ, taṃ vidvā manubrūhayai||

‘‘aj‍jaiva kic‍camātappaṃ, kau jañ‍ñā maraṇaṃ suvai|

na hi nau saṅgaraṃ taina, mahāsainaina mac‍cunā||

‘‘aivaṃ vihāriṃ ātāpiṃ, ahaurattamatanditaṃ|

taṃ vai bhaddaikarattauti, santau ācikkhatai munī’’ti||

‘‘‘kau  nu khau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina  atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajaiyyā’ti? taisaṃ nau, bhantai, amhākaṃ aitadahausi – ‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā mahākac‍cānau satthu caiva saṃvaṇṇitau sambhāvitau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ| pahauti cāyasmā mahākac‍cānau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajituṃ| yaṃnūna mayaṃ yaināyasmā mahākac‍cānau tainupasaṅkamaiyyāma; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyāmā’ti| atha khau mayaṃ, bhantai, yaināyasmā mahākac‍cānau tainupasaṅkamimha; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchimha| taisaṃ nau, bhantai, āyasmatā mahākac‍cānaina imaihi ākāraihi imaihi padaihi imaihi byañ‍janaihi atthau vibhattau’’ti|

‘‘paṇḍitau, bhikkhavai, mahākac‍cānau; mahāpañ‍ñau, bhikkhavai mahākac‍cānau| maṃ caipi tumhai, bhikkhavai, aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyātha, ahampi taṃ aivamaivaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ yathā taṃ mahākac‍cānaina byākataṃ| aisau, caivaitassa atthau| aivañ‍ca naṃ dhāraithā’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

mahākac‍cānabhaddaikarattasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. laumasakaṅgiyabhaddaikarattasuttaṃ
