286. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā laumasakaṅgiyau laumasakakaṅgiyau (ṭīkā) sak‍kaisu viharati kapilavatthusmiṃ nigraudhārāmai| atha khau candanau daivaputtau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇau kaivalakappaṃ nigraudhārāmaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaināyasmā laumasakaṅgiyau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau candanau daivaputtau āyasmantaṃ laumasakaṅgiyaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘dhāraisi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍cā’’ti? ‘‘na  khau ahaṃ, āvusau, dhāraimi bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍ca| tvaṃ panāvusau, dhāraisi bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍cā’’ti? ‘‘ahampi khau, bhikkhu, na dhāraimi bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍ca| dhāraisi pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhaddaikarattiyau gāthā’’ti? ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, dhāraimi bhaddaikarattiyau gāthā| tvaṃ panāvusau, dhāraisi bhaddaikarattiyau gāthā’’ti? ‘‘dhāraimi khau ahaṃ, bhikkhu, bhaddaikarattiyau gāthā’’ti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, āvusau, dhāraisi bhaddaikarattiyau gāthā’’ti? ‘‘aikamidaṃ, bhikkhu, samayaṃ bhagavā daivaisu tāvatiṃsaisu viharati pāricchattakamūlai paṇḍukambalasilāyaṃ| tatra bhagavā daivānaṃ tāvatiṃsānaṃ  bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍ca abhāsi –

‘‘atītaṃ nānvāgamaiyya, nappaṭikaṅkhai anāgataṃ|

yadatītaṃ pahīnaṃ taṃ, appattañ‍ca anāgataṃ||

‘‘pac‍cuppan‍nañ‍ca yau dhammaṃ, tattha tattha vipassati|

asaṃhīraṃ asaṃkuppaṃ, taṃ vidvā manubrūhayai||

‘‘aj‍jaiva kic‍camātappaṃ, kau jañ‍ñā maraṇaṃ suvai|

na hi nau saṅgaraṃ taina, mahāsainaina mac‍cunā||

‘‘aivaṃ  vihāriṃ ātāpiṃ, ahaurattamatanditaṃ|

taṃ vai bhaddaikarattauti, santau ācikkhatai munī’’ti||

‘‘aivaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhikkhu, dhāraimi bhaddaikarattiyau gāthā| uggaṇhāhi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍ca; pariyāpuṇāhi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍ca; dhāraihi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍ca| atthasaṃhitau, bhikkhu, bhaddaikarattassa uddaisau ca vibhaṅgau ca ādibrahmacariyakau’’ti| idamavauca candanau daivaputtau| idaṃ vatvā tatthaivantaradhāyi|

287. atha khau āyasmā laumasakaṅgiyau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina saināsanaṃ saṃsāmaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina sāvatthi taina  cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina sāvatthi jaitavanaṃ anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ  abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā laumasakaṅgiyau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, bhantai, samayaṃ sak‍kaisu viharāmi kapilavatthusmiṃ nigraudhārāmai| atha khau, bhantai, añ‍ñatarau daivaputtau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇau kaivalakappaṃ nigraudhārāmaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau, bhantai, sau daivaputtau maṃ aitadavauca – ‘dhāraisi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍cā’ti? aivaṃ vuttai ahaṃ, bhantai, taṃ daivaputtaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, dhāraimi bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍ca| tvaṃ panāvusau, dhāraisi bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍cā’ti? ‘ahampi khau, bhikkhu, na dhāraimi bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍ca| dhāraisi pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhaddaikarattiyau gāthā’ti? ‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, dhāraimi bhaddaikarattiyau gāthā| tvaṃ panāvusau, dhāraisi bhaddaikarattiyau gāthā’ti? ‘dhāraimi khau ahaṃ, bhikkhu, bhaddaikarattiyau gāthā’ti| ‘yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, āvusau, dhāraisi bhaddaikarattiyau gāthā’ti? aikamidaṃ, bhikkhu, samayaṃ bhagavā daivaisu tāvatiṃsaisu viharati pāricchattakamūlai paṇḍukambalasilāyaṃ| tatra khau bhagavā daivānaṃ tāvatiṃsānaṃ bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍ca abhāsi –

‘‘atītaṃ nānvāgamaiyya…pai॰…

taṃ vai bhaddaikarattauti, santau ācikkhatai munī’’ti||

‘‘aivaṃ  khau ahaṃ, bhikkhu, dhāraimi bhaddaikarattiyau gāthā| uggaṇhāhi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍ca; pariyāpuṇāhi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhaddaikarattassa  uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍ca; dhāraihi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍ca| atthasaṃhitau, bhikkhu, bhaddaikarattassa uddaisau ca vibhaṅgau ca ādibrahmacariyakau’ti| idamavauca, bhantai, sau daivaputtau; idaṃ vatvā tatthaivantaradhāyi| sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā bhaddaikarattassa uddaisañ‍ca vibhaṅgañ‍ca daisaitū’’ti|

288. ‘‘jānāsi pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, taṃ daivaputta’’nti? ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, bhantai, jānāmi taṃ daivaputta’’nti| ‘‘candanau nāma sau, bhikkhu, daivaputtau| candanau, bhikkhu, daivaputtau aṭṭhiṃ katvā aṭṭhikatvā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) manasikatvā sabbacaitasā sabbaṃ caitasau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), sabbaṃ caitasā (ka॰) saman‍nāharitvā auhitasautau dhammaṃ suṇāti| taina hi, bhikkhu, suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā laumasakaṅgiyau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘atītaṃ  nānvāgamaiyya, nappaṭikaṅkhai anāgataṃ|

yadatītaṃ pahīnaṃ taṃ, appattañ‍ca anāgataṃ||

‘‘pac‍cuppan‍nañ‍ca yau dhammaṃ, tattha tattha vipassati|

asaṃhīraṃ asaṃkuppaṃ, taṃ vidvā manubrūhayai||

‘‘aj‍jaiva kic‍camātappaṃ, kau jañ‍ñā maraṇaṃ suvai|

na  hi nau saṅgaraṃ taina, mahāsainaina mac‍cunā|

‘‘aivaṃ vihāriṃ ātāpiṃ, ahaurattamatanditaṃ|

taṃ vai bhaddaikarattauti, santau ācikkhatai muni’’||

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhu, atītaṃ anvāgamaiti…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, atītaṃ anvāgamaiti| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhu, atītaṃ nānvāgamaiti…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, atītaṃ nānvāgamaiti| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhu, anāgataṃ paṭikaṅkhati…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, anāgataṃ paṭikaṅkhati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhu, anāgataṃ nappaṭikaṅkhati…pai॰…  aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, anāgataṃ nappaṭikaṅkhati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhu, pac‍cuppan‍naisu dhammaisu saṃhīrati…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, pac‍cuppan‍naisu dhammaisu saṃhīrati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhu, pac‍cuppan‍naisu dhammaisu na saṃhīrati…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, pac‍cuppan‍naisu dhammaisu na saṃhīrati|

‘‘atītaṃ  nānvāgamaiyya, nappaṭikaṅkhai anāgataṃ|

yadatītaṃ pahīnaṃ taṃ, appattañ‍ca anāgataṃ||

‘‘pac‍cuppan‍nañ‍ca  yau dhammaṃ, tattha tattha vipassati|

asaṃhīraṃ asaṃkuppaṃ, taṃ vidvā manubrūhayai||

‘‘aj‍jaiva  kic‍camātappaṃ, kau jañ‍ñā maraṇaṃ suvai|

na hi nau saṅgaraṃ taina, mahāsainaina mac‍cunā||

‘‘aivaṃ vihāriṃ ātāpiṃ, ahaurattamatanditaṃ|

taṃ vai bhaddaikarattauti, santau ācikkhatai munī’’ti||

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā laumasakaṅgiyau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

laumasakaṅgiyabhaddaikarattasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. cūḷakammavibhaṅgasuttaṃ subhasuttantipi vuc‍cati

289. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai, anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau subhau māṇavau taudaiyyaputtau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau subhau māṇavau taudaiyyaputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘kau nu khau, bhau gautama, haitu kau pac‍cayau yaina manussānaṃyaiva sataṃ manussabhūtānaṃ dissanti hīnappaṇītatā? dissanti hi, bhau gautama, manussā appāyukā, dissanti dīghāyukā; dissanti bavhābādhā bahvābādhā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), dissanti appābādhā; dissanti dubbaṇṇā, dissanti vaṇṇavantau; dissanti appaisakkhā, dissanti mahaisakkhā; dissanti appabhaugā, dissanti mahābhaugā; dissanti nīcakulīnā, dissanti uc‍cākulīnā; dissanti duppañ‍ñā, dissanti pañ‍ñavantau pañ‍ñāvantau (sī॰ pī॰)| kau nu khau, bhau gautama, haitu kau pac‍cayau yaina manussānaṃyaiva sataṃ manussabhūtānaṃ dissanti hīnappaṇītatā’’ti?

‘‘kammassakā, māṇava, sattā kammadāyādā kammayaunī kammabandhū kammayauni kammabandhu (sī॰) kammappaṭisaraṇā| kammaṃ sattai vibhajati yadidaṃ – hīnappaṇītatāyāti| na khau ahaṃ imassa bhautau gautamassa saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmi| sādhu  mai bhavaṃ gautamau tathā dhammaṃ daisaitu yathā ahaṃ imassa bhautau gautamassa saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānaiyya’’nti|

290. ‘‘taina hi, māṇava, suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’ti khau subhau māṇavau taudaiyyaputtau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘idha, māṇava, aikac‍cau itthī vā purisau vā pāṇātipātī hauti luddau lauhitapāṇi hatapahatai niviṭṭhau adayāpan‍nau pāṇabhūtaisu sabbapāṇabhūtaisu (sī॰ ka॰)| sau taina kammaina aivaṃ samattaina aivaṃ samādin‍naina samādiṇṇaina (pī॰ ka॰) kāyassa  bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| nau cai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati, sacai manussattaṃ āgacchati yattha yattha pac‍cājāyati appāyukau hauti| appāyukasaṃvattanikā aisā, māṇava, paṭipadā yadidaṃ – pāṇātipātī hauti luddau lauhitapāṇi hatapahatai niviṭṭhau adayāpan‍nau pāṇabhūtaisu|

‘‘idha pana, māṇava, aikac‍cau itthī vā purisau vā pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti nihitadaṇḍau nihitasatthau, laj‍jī dayāpan‍nau sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī viharati| sau taina kammaina aivaṃ samattaina aivaṃ samādin‍naina kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| nau cai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati, sacai manussattaṃ āgacchati  yattha yattha pac‍cājāyati dīghāyukau hauti| dīghāyukasaṃvattanikā aisā, māṇava, paṭipadā yadidaṃ – pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau  hauti nihitadaṇḍau nihitasatthau, laj‍jī dayāpan‍nau sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī viharati|

291. ‘‘idha, māṇava, aikac‍cau itthī vā purisau vā sattānaṃ vihaiṭhakajātikau hauti, pāṇinā vā laiḍḍunā vā daṇḍaina vā satthaina vā| sau taina kammaina aivaṃ samattaina aivaṃ samādin‍naina kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| nau cai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati, sacai manussattaṃ āgacchati yattha yattha pac‍cājāyati bavhābādhau hauti| bavhābādhasaṃvattanikā aisā, māṇava, paṭipadā yadidaṃ – sattānaṃ vihaiṭhakajātikau hauti pāṇinā vā laiḍḍunā vā daṇḍaina vā satthaina vā|

‘‘idha pana, māṇava, aikac‍cau itthī vā purisau vā sattānaṃ avihaiṭhakajātikau hauti pāṇinā vā laiḍḍunā vā daṇḍaina vā satthaina vā| sau taina kammaina aivaṃ samattaina aivaṃ samādin‍naina kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| nau cai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati, sacai manussattaṃ āgacchati yattha yattha pac‍cājāyati appābādhau hauti| appābādhasaṃvattanikā aisā, māṇava, paṭipadā yadidaṃ  – sattānaṃ avihaiṭhakajātikau hauti pāṇinā vā laiḍḍunā vā daṇḍaina vā satthaina vā|

292. ‘‘idha, māṇava, aikac‍cau itthī vā purisau vā kaudhanau hauti upāyāsabahulau| appampi  vuttau samānau abhisaj‍jati kuppati byāpaj‍jati patiṭṭhīyati kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti| sau taina kammaina aivaṃ samattaina aivaṃ samādin‍naina kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| nau cai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati, sacai manussattaṃ āgacchati yattha yattha pac‍cājāyati dubbaṇṇau hauti| dubbaṇṇasaṃvattanikā aisā, māṇava, paṭipadā yadidaṃ – kaudhanau hauti upāyāsabahulau; appampi vuttau samānau abhisaj‍jati kuppati byāpaj‍jati patiṭṭhīyati kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti|

‘‘idha pana, māṇava, aikac‍cau itthī vā purisau vā ak‍kaudhanau hauti anupāyāsabahulau; bahumpi vuttau samānau nābhisaj‍jati na kuppati na byāpaj‍jati na patiṭṭhīyati na kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti| sau taina kammaina aivaṃ samattaina aivaṃ samādin‍naina kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| nau cai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā  sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati, sacai manussattaṃ āgacchati yattha yattha pac‍cājāyati  pāsādikau hauti| pāsādikasaṃvattanikā aisā, māṇava, paṭipadā yadidaṃ – ak‍kaudhanau hauti anupāyāsabahulau; bahumpi vuttau samānau nābhisaj‍jati na kuppati na byāpaj‍jati na patiṭṭhīyati na kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti|

293. ‘‘idha, māṇava, aikac‍cau itthī vā purisau vā issāmanakau hauti; paralābhasak‍kāragarukāramānanavandanapūjanāsu issati upadussati issaṃ bandhati| sau taina kammaina aivaṃ samattaina aivaṃ samādin‍naina kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| nau cai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati, sacai manussattaṃ āgacchati yattha yattha pac‍cājāyati appaisakkhau hauti| appaisakkhasaṃvattanikā aisā, māṇava, paṭipadā yadidaṃ – issāmanakau hauti; paralābhasak‍kāragarukāramānanavandanapūjanāsu issati upadussati issaṃ bandhati|

‘‘idha  pana, māṇava, aikac‍cau itthī vā purisau vā anissāmanakau hauti; paralābhasak‍kāragarukāramānanavandanapūjanāsu na issati na upadussati na issaṃ bandhati| sau taina kammaina aivaṃ samattaina aivaṃ samādin‍naina kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| nau  cai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati, sacai manussattaṃ āgacchati yattha yattha pac‍cājāyati mahaisakkhau hauti| mahaisakkhasaṃvattanikā aisā, māṇava, paṭipadā yadidaṃ – anissāmanakau hauti; paralābhasak‍kāragarukāramānanavandanapūjanāsu na issati na upadussati na issaṃ bandhati|

294. ‘‘idha, māṇava, aikac‍cau itthī vā purisau vā na dātā hauti samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā an‍naṃ pānaṃ vatthaṃ yānaṃ mālāgandhavilaipanaṃ saiyyāvasathapadīpaiyyaṃ| sau taina kammaina aivaṃ samattaina aivaṃ samādin‍naina kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| nau cai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati, sacai manussattaṃ āgacchati yattha yattha pac‍cājāyati appabhaugau hauti| appabhaugasaṃvattanikā aisā, māṇava, paṭipadā yadidaṃ  – na dātā hauti samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā an‍naṃ pānaṃ vatthaṃ yānaṃ mālāgandhavilaipanaṃ saiyyāvasathapadīpaiyyaṃ|

‘‘idha pana, māṇava, aikac‍cau itthī vā purisau vā dātā hauti samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā an‍naṃ pānaṃ vatthaṃ yānaṃ mālāgandhavilaipanaṃ saiyyāvasathapadīpaiyyaṃ| sau taina kammaina aivaṃ samattaina aivaṃ samādin‍naina kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| nau cai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati, sacai manussattaṃ āgacchati yattha yattha pac‍cājāyati mahābhaugau hauti| mahābhaugasaṃvattanikā aisā, māṇava, paṭipadā yadidaṃ – dātā hauti samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā an‍naṃ  pānaṃ vatthaṃ yānaṃ mālāgandhavilaipanaṃ saiyyāvasathapadīpaiyyaṃ|

295. ‘‘idha, māṇava, aikac‍cau itthī vā purisau vā thaddhau hauti atimānī – abhivādaitabbaṃ na abhivādaiti, pac‍cuṭṭhātabbaṃ na pac‍cuṭṭhaiti, āsanārahassa na āsanaṃ daiti, maggārahassa na maggaṃ daiti, sak‍kātabbaṃ na sak‍karauti, garukātabbaṃ na garukarauti, mānaitabbaṃ na mānaiti, pūjaitabbaṃ na pūjaiti| sau taina kammaina aivaṃ samattaina aivaṃ samādin‍naina kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| nau cai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati, sacai manussattaṃ āgacchati yattha yattha pac‍cājāyati nīcakulīnau hauti| nīcakulīnasaṃvattanikā aisā, māṇava, paṭipadā yadidaṃ – thaddhau hauti atimānī; abhivādaitabbaṃ na abhivādaiti, pac‍cuṭṭhātabbaṃ na pac‍cuṭṭhaiti, āsanārahassa na āsanaṃ daiti, maggārahassa na maggaṃ daiti, sak‍kātabbaṃ na sak‍karauti, garukātabbaṃ na garukarauti, mānaitabbaṃ na mānaiti, pūjaitabbaṃ na pūjaiti|

‘‘idha  pana, māṇava, aikac‍cau itthī vā purisau vā atthaddhau hauti anatimānī; abhivādaitabbaṃ abhivādaiti, pac‍cuṭṭhātabbaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhaiti, āsanārahassa āsanaṃ daiti, maggārahassa maggaṃ daiti, sak‍kātabbaṃ sak‍karauti, garukātabbaṃ garukarauti, mānaitabbaṃ mānaiti, pūjaitabbaṃ pūjaiti| sau taina kammaina aivaṃ samattaina aivaṃ samādin‍naina  kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| nau cai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati, sacai manussattaṃ  āgacchati yattha yattha pac‍cājāyati uc‍cākulīnau hauti| uc‍cākulīnasaṃvattanikā aisā, māṇava, paṭipadā yadidaṃ – atthaddhau hauti anatimānī; abhivādaitabbaṃ abhivādaiti, pac‍cuṭṭhātabbaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhaiti, āsanārahassa āsanaṃ daiti, maggārahassa maggaṃ daiti, sak‍kātabbaṃ sak‍karauti, garukātabbaṃ garukarauti, mānaitabbaṃ mānaiti, pūjaitabbaṃ pūjaiti|

296. ‘‘idha, māṇava, aikac‍cau itthī vā purisau vā samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā upasaṅkamitvā na paripucchitā hauti – ‘kiṃ, bhantai, kusalaṃ, kiṃ akusalaṃ; kiṃ sāvaj‍jaṃ, kiṃ anavaj‍jaṃ; kiṃ saivitabbaṃ, kiṃ na saivitabbaṃ; kiṃ mai karīyamānaṃ dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya hauti, kiṃ vā pana mai karīyamānaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya hautī’ti? sau taina kammaina aivaṃ samattaina aivaṃ samādin‍naina kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| nau cai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati, sacai manussattaṃ āgacchati yattha yattha pac‍cājāyati duppañ‍ñau hauti| duppañ‍ñasaṃvattanikā aisā, māṇava, paṭipadā yadidaṃ – samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā upasaṅkamitvā na paripucchitā hauti – ‘kiṃ, bhantai, kusalaṃ, kiṃ akusalaṃ; kiṃ sāvaj‍jaṃ, kiṃ anavaj‍jaṃ; kiṃ saivitabbaṃ, kiṃ na saivitabbaṃ; kiṃ mai karīyamānaṃ dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya hauti, kiṃ vā pana mai karīyamānaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya hautī’’’ti?

‘‘idha  pana, māṇava, aikac‍cau itthī vā purisau vā samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā upasaṅkamitvā paripucchitā hauti – ‘kiṃ, bhantai, kusalaṃ, kiṃ akusalaṃ; kiṃ sāvaj‍jaṃ, kiṃ anavaj‍jaṃ; kiṃ saivitabbaṃ, kiṃ na saivitabbaṃ; kiṃ mai karīyamānaṃ dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya hauti, kiṃ vā pana mai karīyamānaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya hautī’ti? sau taina kammaina aivaṃ samattaina aivaṃ samādin‍naina kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| nau  cai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati, sacai manussattaṃ āgacchati yattha yattha pac‍cājāyati mahāpañ‍ñau hauti| mahāpañ‍ñasaṃvattanikā aisā, māṇava, paṭipadā yadidaṃ – samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā upasaṅkamitvā paripucchitā hauti – ‘kiṃ, bhantai, kusalaṃ, kiṃ akusalaṃ; kiṃ sāvaj‍jaṃ, kiṃ anavaj‍jaṃ; kiṃ saivitabbaṃ, kiṃ na saivitabbaṃ; kiṃ mai karīyamānaṃ dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya hauti, kiṃ vā pana mai karīyamānaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya hautī’’’ti?

297. ‘‘iti khau, māṇava, appāyukasaṃvattanikā paṭipadā appāyukattaṃ upanaiti, dīghāyukasaṃvattanikā paṭipadā dīghāyukattaṃ upanaiti; bavhābādhasaṃvattanikā paṭipadā bavhābādhattaṃ upanaiti, appābādhasaṃvattanikā paṭipadā appābādhattaṃ upanaiti; dubbaṇṇasaṃvattanikā paṭipadā  dubbaṇṇattaṃ upanaiti, pāsādikasaṃvattanikā paṭipadā pāsādikattaṃ upanaiti; appaisakkhasaṃvattanikā paṭipadā appaisakkhattaṃ upanaiti, mahaisakkhasaṃvattanikā paṭipadā mahaisakkhattaṃ upanaiti; appabhaugasaṃvattanikā paṭipadā appabhaugattaṃ upanaiti, mahābhaugasaṃvattanikā paṭipadā mahābhaugattaṃ upanaiti; nīcakulīnasaṃvattanikā paṭipadā nīcakulīnattaṃ upanaiti, uc‍cākulīnasaṃvattanikā paṭipadā uc‍cākulīnattaṃ upanaiti; duppañ‍ñasaṃvattanikā paṭipadā duppañ‍ñattaṃ upanaiti, mahāpañ‍ñasaṃvattanikā paṭipadā mahāpañ‍ñattaṃ upanaiti| kammassakā, māṇava, sattā kammadāyādā kammayaunī kammabandhū kammappaṭisaraṇā| kammaṃ sattai vibhajati yadidaṃ – hīnappaṇītatāyā’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, subhau māṇavau taudaiyyaputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama! saiyyathāpi, bhau gautama, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya – ‘cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantī’ti; aivamaivaṃ bhautā gautamaina anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

cūḷakammavibhaṅgasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. mahākammavibhaṅgasuttaṃ

298. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā samiddhi arañ‍ñakuṭikāyaṃ  viharati| atha khau pautaliputtau paribbājakau jaṅghāvihāraṃ anucaṅkamamānau anuvicaramānau yaināyasmā samiddhi tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā samiddhinā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau pautaliputtau paribbājakau āyasmantaṃ samiddhiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sammukhā maitaṃ, āvusau samiddhi, samaṇassa gautamassa sutaṃ, sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘maughaṃ kāyakammaṃ maughaṃ vacīkammaṃ, manaukammamaiva sac‍ca’nti| atthi ca sā atthi caisā (sī॰ ka॰) samāpatti yaṃ samāpattiṃ samāpan‍nau na kiñ‍ci vaidiyatī’’ti? ‘‘mā haivaṃ, āvusau pautaliputta, avaca; (mā haivaṃ, āvusau pautaliputta, avaca;) ( ) syā॰ kaṃ॰ pautthakaisu natthi mā bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi| na hi sādhu bhagavatau abbhakkhānaṃ| na hi bhagavā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘maughaṃ kāyakammaṃ maughaṃ vacīkammaṃ, manaukammamaiva sac‍ca’nti| atthi ca khau atthi caiva khau (sī॰ ka॰) sā, āvusau, samāpatti yaṃ samāpattiṃ samāpan‍nau na kiñ‍ci vaidiyatī’’ti| ‘‘kīvaciraṃ pabbajitausi, āvusau samiddhī’’ti? ‘‘na ciraṃ, āvusau! tīṇi vassānī’’ti| ‘‘aittha dāni mayaṃ thairai bhikkhū kiṃ vakkhāma, yatra hi nāma aivaṃnavau bhikkhu navakaina bhikkhunā (ka॰) satthāraṃ parirakkhitabbaṃ mañ‍ñissati| sañ‍caitanikaṃ, āvusau samiddhi, kammaṃ  katvā kāyaina vācāya manasā kiṃ sau vaidiyatī’’ti? ‘‘sañ‍caitanikaṃ, āvusau pautaliputta, kammaṃ katvā kāyaina vācāya manasā dukkhaṃ sau vaidiyatī’’ti| atha khau pautaliputtau paribbājakau āyasmatau samiddhissa bhāsitaṃ naiva abhinandi nappaṭik‍kausi; anabhinanditvā appaṭik‍kausitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi|

299. atha khau āyasmā samiddhi acirapak‍kantai pautaliputtai paribbājakai yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  āyasmatā ānandaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā samiddhi yāvatakau ahausi pautaliputtaina paribbājakaina saddhiṃ kathāsal‍lāpau taṃ sabbaṃ āyasmatau ānandassa āraucaisi|

aivaṃ  vuttai, āyasmā ānandau āyasmantaṃ samiddhiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘atthi khau idaṃ, āvusau samiddhi, kathāpābhataṃ bhagavantaṃ dassanāya| āyāmāvusau  samiddhi, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamissāma; upasaṅkamitvā aitamatthaṃ bhagavatau āraucaissāma| yathā nau bhagavā byākarissati tathā naṃ dhāraissāmā’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau āyasmā samiddhi āyasmatau ānandassa pac‍cassausi|

atha khau āyasmā ca ānandau āyasmā ca samiddhi yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau yāvatakau ahausi āyasmatau samiddhissa pautaliputtaina  paribbājakaina saddhiṃ kathāsal‍lāpau taṃ sabbaṃ bhagavatau āraucaisi| aivaṃ vuttai, bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘dassanampi khau ahaṃ, ānanda, pautaliputtassa paribbājakassa nābhijānāmi, kutau panaivarūpaṃ kathāsal‍lāpaṃ? iminā ca, ānanda, samiddhinā maughapurisaina pautaliputtassa paribbājakassa vibhaj‍jabyākaraṇīyau pañhau aikaṃsaina byākatau’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā udāyī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sacai pana kiṃ pana (ka॰), bhantai, āyasmatā samiddhinā idaṃ sandhāya bhāsitaṃ – yaṃ kiñ‍ci vaidayitaṃ taṃ dukkhasmi’’nti|

300. atha khau aivaṃ vuttai (syā॰ kaṃ॰) bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘passasi nau tvaṃ, ānanda, imassa udāyissa maughapurisassa ummaṅgaṃ ummaggaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), umaṅgaṃ (ka॰)? añ‍ñāsiṃ khau ahaṃ, ānanda – ‘idānaivāyaṃ udāyī maughapurisau ummuj‍jamānau ayaunisau ummuj‍jissatī’ti| ādiṃyaiva ādisauva (sī॰ pī॰), ādiyaiva (ka॰), ānanda, pautaliputtaina paribbājakaina tissau vaidanā pucchitā| sacāyaṃ, ānanda, samiddhi maughapurisau pautaliputtassa  paribbājakassa aivaṃ puṭṭhau aivaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘sañ‍caitanikaṃ, āvusau pautaliputta, kammaṃ katvā kāyaina vācāya manasā sukhavaidanīyaṃ sukhaṃ sau vaidayati; sañ‍caitanikaṃ, āvusau pautaliputta, kammaṃ katvā kāyaina vācāya manasā dukkhavaidanīyaṃ dukkhaṃ sau vaidayati; sañ‍caitanikaṃ, āvusau pautaliputta, kammaṃ katvā kāyaina vācāya manasā adukkhamasukhavaidanīyaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ sau vaidayatī’ti| aivaṃ byākaramānau  khau, ānanda, samiddhi maughapurisau pautaliputtassa paribbājakassa sammā (byākaramānau) ( ) natthi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) byākaraiyya| api  ca, ānanda, kai ca kaici (ka॰) añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā bālā abyattā kai ca tathāgatassa mahākammavibhaṅgaṃ jānissanti? sacai tumhai, ānanda, suṇaiyyātha tathāgatassa mahākammavibhaṅgaṃ vibhajantassā’’ti|

‘‘aitassa, bhagavā, kālau, aitassa, sugata, kālau yaṃ bhagavā mahākammavibhaṅgaṃ vibhajaiyya| bhagavatau sutvā bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti| ‘‘taina hānanda, suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘cattāraumai, ānanda, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? idhānanda, aikac‍cau puggalau idha pāṇātipātī hauti, adin‍nādāyī hauti, kāmaisumicchācārī hauti, musāvādī hauti, pisuṇavācau hauti, pharusavācau hauti, samphappalāpī hauti, abhijjhālu hauti, byāpan‍nacittau hauti, micchādiṭṭhi hauti| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati|

‘‘idha panānanda, aikac‍cau puggalau idha pāṇātipātī hauti, adin‍nādāyī hauti, kāmaisumicchācārī hauti, musāvādī hauti, pisuṇavācau hauti, pharusavācau hauti, samphappalāpī hauti, abhijjhālu hauti, byāpan‍nacittau hauti, micchādiṭṭhi hauti| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati|

‘‘idhānanda, aikac‍cau  puggalau idha pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti, musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, pharusāya vācāya  paṭiviratau hauti, samphappalāpā paṭiviratau hauti, anabhijjhālu hauti, abyāpan‍nacittau hauti, sammādiṭṭhi hauti| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati|

‘‘idha panānanda, aikac‍cau puggalau idha pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti, musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, pisuṇāya vācāya  paṭiviratau hauti, pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, samphappalāpā paṭiviratau hauti, anabhijjhālu hauti, abyāpan‍nacittau hauti, sammādiṭṭhi hauti| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati|

301. ‘‘idhānanda, aikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā ātappamanvāya padhānamanvāya anuyaugamanvāya  appamādamanvāya sammāmanasikāramanvāya tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ phusati yathāsamāhitai cittai dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina amuṃ puggalaṃ passati – idha pāṇātipātiṃ adin‍nādāyiṃ kāmaisumicchācāriṃ musāvādiṃ pisuṇavācaṃ pharusavācaṃ samphappalāpiṃ abhijjhāluṃ byāpan‍nacittaṃ micchādiṭṭhiṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā passati apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍naṃ| sau aivamāha – ‘atthi kira, bhau, pāpakāni kammāni, atthi  duc‍caritassa vipākau| amāhaṃ apāhaṃ (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) amuṃ + ahaṃ = amāhaṃ-iti padavibhāgau puggalaṃ addasaṃ idha pāṇātipātiṃ adin‍nādāyiṃ…pai॰… micchādiṭṭhiṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā passāmi apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍na’nti| sau aivamāha – ‘yau kira, bhau, pāṇātipātī adin‍nādāyī…pai॰… micchādiṭṭhi, sabbau sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| yai aivaṃ jānanti, tai sammā jānanti; yai añ‍ñathā jānanti, micchā taisaṃ ñāṇa’nti micchā tai sañ‍jānanti (ka॰)| iti sau yadaiva tassa sāmaṃ ñātaṃ sāmaṃ diṭṭhaṃ sāmaṃ viditaṃ tadaiva tattha thāmasā parāmāsā parāmassa (sī॰ pī॰) abhinivissa vauharati – ‘idamaiva sac‍caṃ, maughamañ‍ña’’’nti|

‘‘idha panānanda, aikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā ātappamanvāya  padhānamanvāya anuyaugamanvāya appamādamanvāya sammāmanasikāramanvāya tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ phusati yathāsamāhitai cittai dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina amuṃ puggalaṃ passati – idha pāṇātipātiṃ adin‍nādāyiṃ…pai॰… micchādiṭṭhiṃ, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā passati sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍naṃ| sau aivamāha – ‘natthi kira, bhau, pāpakāni kammāni, natthi duc‍caritassa vipākau| amāhaṃ puggalaṃ addasaṃ – idha pāṇātipātiṃ adin‍nādāyiṃ…pai॰… micchādiṭṭhiṃ, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā passāmi sugatiṃ saggaṃ  laukaṃ upapan‍na’nti| sau aivamāha – ‘yau  kira, bhau, pāṇātipātī adin‍nādāyī…pai॰… micchādiṭṭhi, sabbau sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| yai aivaṃ jānanti tai sammā jānanti; yai añ‍ñathā jānanti, micchā taisaṃ ñāṇa’nti| iti sau yadaiva tassa sāmaṃ ñātaṃ sāmaṃ diṭṭhaṃ sāmaṃ viditaṃ tadaiva tattha thāmasā parāmāsā abhinivissa vauharati – ‘idamaiva sac‍caṃ, maughamañ‍ña’’’nti|

‘‘idhānanda, aikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā ātappamanvāya padhānamanvāya anuyaugamanvāya appamādamanvāya sammāmanasikāramanvāya tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ phusati yathāsamāhitai cittai dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina amuṃ puggalaṃ passati – idha  pāṇātipātā paṭivirataṃ adin‍nādānā paṭivirataṃ kāmaisumicchācārā paṭivirataṃ musāvādā paṭivirataṃ pisuṇāya vācāya paṭivirataṃ pharusāya vācāya paṭivirataṃ samphappalāpā paṭivirataṃ anabhijjhāluṃ abyāpan‍nacittaṃ sammādiṭṭhiṃ, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā passati sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍naṃ| sau aivamāha – ‘atthi kira, bhau, kalyāṇāni kammāni, atthi sucaritassa vipākau| amāhaṃ puggalaṃ addasaṃ – idha pāṇātipātā paṭivirataṃ adin‍nādānā paṭivirataṃ…pai॰… sammādiṭṭhiṃ, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā passāmi sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍na’nti| sau aivamāha – ‘yau kira, bhau, pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau  adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau…pai॰… sammādiṭṭhi sabbau sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| yai aivaṃ jānanti tai sammā jānanti; yai añ‍ñathā jānanti, micchā taisaṃ ñāṇa’nti| iti sau yadaiva tassa sāmaṃ ñātaṃ sāmaṃ diṭṭhaṃ sāmaṃ viditaṃ tadaiva tattha thāmasā parāmāsā abhinivissa vauharati – ‘idamaiva sac‍caṃ, maughamañ‍ña’’’nti|

‘‘idha panānanda, aikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā ātappamanvāya padhānamanvāya anuyaugamanvāya appamādamanvāya sammāmanasikāramanvāya tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ phusati yathāsamāhitai cittai dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina amuṃ puggalaṃ  passati – idha pāṇātipātā paṭivirataṃ…pai॰… sammādiṭṭhiṃ, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā passati apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍naṃ| sau aivamāha – ‘natthi kira, bhau kalyāṇāni kammāni, natthi sucaritassa vipākau| amāhaṃ  puggalaṃ addasaṃ – idha pāṇātipātā paṭivirataṃ adin‍nādānā paṭivirataṃ…pai॰… sammādiṭṭhiṃ, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā passāmi apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍na’nti| sau aivamāha – ‘yau kira, bhau, pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau…pai॰… sammādiṭṭhi, sabbau sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| yai aivaṃ jānanti tai sammā jānanti; yai añ‍ñathā jānanti, micchā  taisaṃ ñāṇa’nti| iti sau yadaiva tassa sāmaṃ ñātaṃ sāmaṃ diṭṭhaṃ sāmaṃ viditaṃ tadaiva tattha thāmasā parāmāsā abhinivissa vauharati – ‘idamaiva sac‍caṃ, maughamañ‍ña’’’nti|

302. ‘‘tatrānanda, yvāyaṃ samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā aivamāha – ‘atthi kira, bhau, pāpakāni kammāni, atthi duc‍caritassa vipākau’ti idamassa anujānāmi; yampi sau aivamāha – ‘amāhaṃ puggalaṃ addasaṃ – idha pāṇātipātiṃ adin‍nādāyiṃ…pai॰… micchādiṭṭhiṃ, kāyassa  bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā passāmi apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍na’nti idampissa anujānāmi; yañ‍ca khau sau aivamāha – ‘yau kira, bhau, pāṇātipātī adin‍nādāyī…pai॰… micchādiṭṭhi, sabbau sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jatī’ti idamassa nānujānāmi; yampi sau aivamāha – ‘yai aivaṃ jānanti tai sammā jānanti; yai añ‍ñathā jānanti, micchā taisaṃ ñāṇa’nti idampissa nānujānāmi; yampi sau yadaiva tassa sāmaṃ ñātaṃ sāmaṃ diṭṭhaṃ sāmaṃ viditaṃ tadaiva tattha thāmasā parāmāsā abhinivissa vauharati – ‘idamaiva sac‍caṃ, maughamañ‍ña’nti idampissa nānujānāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? añ‍ñathā hi, ānanda, tathāgatassa mahākammavibhaṅgai ñāṇaṃ hauti|

‘‘tatrānanda, yvāyaṃ samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā aivamāha – ‘natthi kira, bhau, pāpakāni kammāni, natthi duc‍caritassa vipākau’ti idamassa  nānujānāmi; yañ‍ca khau sau aivamāha – ‘amāhaṃ puggalaṃ addasaṃ – idha pāṇātipātiṃ adin‍nādāyiṃ…pai॰… micchādiṭṭhiṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā passāmi sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍na’nti idamassa anujānāmi; yañ‍ca khau sau aivamāha – ‘yau kira, bhau, pāṇātipātī adin‍nādāyī…pai॰… micchādiṭṭhi, sabbau sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jatī’ti idamassa  nānujānāmi; yampi sau aivamāha – ‘yai aivaṃ  jānanti tai sammā jānanti; yai añ‍ñathā jānanti, micchā taisaṃ ñāṇa’nti idampissa nānujānāmi; yampi sau yadaiva tassa sāmaṃ ñātaṃ sāmaṃ diṭṭhaṃ sāmaṃ viditaṃ tadaiva tattha thāmasā parāmāsā abhinivissa vauharati – ‘idamaiva sac‍caṃ, maughamañ‍ña’nti idampissa nānujānāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? añ‍ñathā hi, ānanda, tathāgatassa mahākammavibhaṅgai ñāṇaṃ hauti|

‘‘tatrānanda, yvāyaṃ samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā aivamāha – ‘atthi kira, bhau, kalyāṇāni kammāni, atthi sucaritassa vipākau’ti idamassa anujānāmi; yampi sau aivamāha – ‘amāhaṃ puggalaṃ addasaṃ – idha pāṇātipātā paṭivirataṃ adin‍nādānā paṭivirataṃ…pai॰… sammādiṭṭhiṃ, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā passāmi sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍na’nti idampissa anujānāmi; yañ‍ca khau sau aivamāha – ‘yau kira, bhau, pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau…pai॰… sammādiṭṭhi, sabbau sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ  saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jatī’ti idamassa nānujānāmi; yampi sau aivamāha – ‘yai aivaṃ jānanti tai  sammā jānanti; yai añ‍ñathā jānanti, micchā taisaṃ ñāṇa’nti idampissa nānujānāmi; yampi sau yadaiva tassa sāmaṃ ñātaṃ sāmaṃ diṭṭhaṃ sāmaṃ viditaṃ tadaiva tattha thāmasā parāmāsā abhinivissa vauharati – ‘idamaiva sac‍caṃ, maughamañ‍ña’nti idampissa nānujānāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? añ‍ñathā hi, ānanda, tathāgatassa mahākammavibhaṅgai ñāṇaṃ hauti|

‘‘tatrānanda, yvāyaṃ samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā aivamāha – ‘natthi kira, bhau, kalyāṇāni kammāni, natthi sucaritassa vipākau’ti idamassa nānujānāmi; yañ‍ca khau sau aivamāha – ‘amāhaṃ puggalaṃ addasaṃ – idha pāṇātipātā paṭivirataṃ adin‍nādānā paṭivirataṃ…pai॰… sammādiṭṭhiṃ, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā passāmi apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍na’nti idamassa anujānāmi; yañ‍ca khau sau aivamāha – ‘yau kira, bhau, pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau…pai॰… sammādiṭṭhi, sabbau sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jatī’ti idamassa nānujānāmi; yañ‍ca khau sau aivamāha – ‘yai  aivaṃ jānanti tai sammā jānanti; yai añ‍ñathā jānanti, micchā taisaṃ ñāṇa’nti idampissa nānujānāmi; yampi sau yadaiva tassa sāmaṃ ñātaṃ  sāmaṃ diṭṭhaṃ sāmaṃ viditaṃ tadaiva tattha thāmasā parāmāsā abhinivissa vauharati – ‘idamaiva  sac‍caṃ, maughamañ‍ña’nti idampissa nānujānāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? añ‍ñathā hi, ānanda, tathāgatassa mahākammavibhaṅgai ñāṇaṃ hauti|

303. ‘‘tatrānanda, yvāyaṃ puggalau idha pāṇātipātī adin‍nādāyī…pai॰… micchādiṭṭhi, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati, pubbai vāssa taṃ kataṃ hauti pāpakammaṃ dukkhavaidanīyaṃ, pacchā vāssa taṃ kataṃ hauti pāpakammaṃ dukkhavaidanīyaṃ, maraṇakālai vāssa hauti micchādiṭṭhi samattā samādin‍nā| taina sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| yañ‍ca khau sau idha pāṇātipātī hauti adin‍nādāyī hauti…pai॰… micchādiṭṭhi hauti tassa diṭṭhaiva dhammai vipākaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti upapaj‍ja vā upapaj‍jaṃ vā (sī॰ pī॰), upapaj‍ja vā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) upapaj‍jitvāti saṃvaṇṇanāya saṃsandaitabbā aparai vā pariyāyai|

‘‘tatrānanda, yvāyaṃ puggalau idha pāṇātipātī adin‍nādāyī…pai॰… micchādiṭṭhi kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati, pubbai vāssa taṃ kataṃ hauti kalyāṇakammaṃ sukhavaidanīyaṃ, pacchā vāssa taṃ kataṃ hauti kalyāṇakammaṃ sukhavaidanīyaṃ, maraṇakālai vāssa hauti sammādiṭṭhi  samattā samādin‍nā| taina sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| yañ‍ca khau sau idha pāṇātipātī hauti adin‍nādāyī hauti…pai॰… micchādiṭṭhi hauti tassa diṭṭhaiva dhammai vipākaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti upapaj‍ja vā aparai vā pariyāyai|

‘‘tatrānanda, yvāyaṃ puggalau idha pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau…pai॰… sammādiṭṭhi, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati, pubbai vāssa taṃ kataṃ hauti kalyāṇakammaṃ sukhavaidanīyaṃ, pacchā vāssa taṃ kataṃ hauti kalyāṇakammaṃ sukhavaidanīyaṃ, maraṇakālai vāssa hauti sammādiṭṭhi samattā samādin‍nā| taina sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| yañ‍ca khau sau idha pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti  adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti…pai॰… sammādiṭṭhi hauti, tassa diṭṭhaiva dhammai vipākaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti upapaj‍ja vā aparai vā pariyāyai|

‘‘tatrānanda, yvāyaṃ puggalau idha pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau…pai॰… sammādiṭṭhi, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati, pubbai vāssa taṃ kataṃ hauti pāpakammaṃ dukkhavaidanīyaṃ, pacchā vāssa taṃ kataṃ hauti pāpakammaṃ dukkhavaidanīyaṃ, maraṇakālai vāssa hauti micchādiṭṭhi samattā samādin‍nā| taina sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| yañ‍ca khau sau idha pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti…pai॰… sammādiṭṭhi hauti, tassa diṭṭhaiva dhammai vipākaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti upapaj‍ja vā aparai vā pariyāyai|

‘‘iti  khau, ānanda, atthi kammaṃ abhabbaṃ abhabbābhāsaṃ, atthi kammaṃ abhabbaṃ bhabbābhāsaṃ, atthi kammaṃ bhabbañ‍caiva bhabbābhāsañ‍ca, atthi kammaṃ bhabbaṃ abhabbābhāsa’’nti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

mahākammavibhaṅgasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. saḷāyatanavibhaṅgasuttaṃ

304. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘saḷāyatanavibhaṅgaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘‘cha  ajjhattikāni āyatanāni vaiditabbāni, cha bāhirāni āyatanāni vaiditabbāni, cha viñ‍ñāṇakāyā vaiditabbā, cha phassakāyā vaiditabbā, aṭṭhārasa manaupavicārā vaiditabbā, chattiṃsa sattapadā vaiditabbā, tatra idaṃ nissāya idaṃ pajahatha, tayau satipaṭṭhānā yadariyau saivati yadariyau  saivamānau satthā gaṇamanusāsitumarahati, sau vuc‍cati yauggācariyānaṃ yaugācariyānaṃ (ka॰) anuttarau purisadammasārathī’ti – ayamuddaisau saḷāyatanavibhaṅgassa|

305. ‘‘‘cha ajjhattikāni āyatanāni vaiditabbānī’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? ‘cakkhāyatanaṃ sautāyatanaṃ ghānāyatanaṃ jivhāyatanaṃ kāyāyatanaṃ manāyatanaṃ – cha ajjhattikāni āyatanāni vaiditabbānī’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘cha bāhirāni āyatanāni vaiditabbānī’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? ‘rūpāyatanaṃ saddāyatanaṃ gandhāyatanaṃ rasāyatanaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ – cha bāhirāni āyatanāni vaiditabbānī’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘cha viñ‍ñāṇakāyā vaiditabbā’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? ‘cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ – cha viñ‍ñāṇakāyā vaiditabbā’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘cha  phassakāyā vaiditabbā’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? ‘cakkhusamphassau sautasamphassau ghānasamphassau jivhāsamphassau kāyasamphassau manausamphassau – cha phassakāyā vaiditabbā’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘aṭṭhārasa manaupavicārā vaiditabbā’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? ‘cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā saumanassaṭṭhānīyaṃ rūpaṃ upavicarati, daumanassaṭṭhānīyaṃ rūpaṃ upavicarati, upaikkhāṭṭhānīyaṃ rūpaṃ upavicarati| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ  phusitvā… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya saumanassaṭṭhānīyaṃ dhammaṃ upavicarati, daumanassaṭṭhānīyaṃ dhammaṃ upavicarati, upaikkhāṭṭhānīyaṃ dhammaṃ upavicarati| iti cha saumanassūpavicārā, cha daumanassūpavicārā, cha upaikkhūpavicārā, aṭṭhārasa manaupavicārā vaiditabbā’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

306. ‘‘‘chattiṃsa  sattapadā vaiditabbā’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? cha gaihasitāni gaihassitāni (?) saumanassāni, cha naikkhammasitāni naikkhammassitāni (ṭīkā) saumanassāni, cha gaihasitāni daumanassāni, cha naikkhammasitāni daumanassāni, cha gaihasitā upaikkhā, cha naikkhammasitā upaikkhā| tattha katamāni cha gaihasitāni saumanassāni? cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyānaṃ rūpānaṃ iṭṭhānaṃ kantānaṃ manāpānaṃ manauramānaṃ laukāmisapaṭisaṃyuttānaṃ paṭilābhaṃ vā paṭilābhatau samanupassatau pubbai vā paṭiladdhapubbaṃ atītaṃ niruddhaṃ vipariṇataṃ samanussaratau uppaj‍jati saumanassaṃ| yaṃ aivarūpaṃ saumanassaṃ idaṃ vuc‍cati gaihasitaṃ saumanassaṃ| sautaviñ‍ñaiyyānaṃ saddānaṃ… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyānaṃ gandhānaṃ… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyānaṃ rasānaṃ… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyānaṃ phauṭṭhabbānaṃ… manauviñ‍ñaiyyānaṃ dhammānaṃ iṭṭhānaṃ kantānaṃ manāpānaṃ…pai॰… saumanassaṃ| yaṃ aivarūpaṃ saumanassaṃ idaṃ vuc‍cati gaihasitaṃ saumanassaṃ| imāni cha gaihasitāni saumanassāni|

‘‘tattha katamāni cha naikkhammasitāni saumanassāni? rūpānaṃtvaiva anic‍cataṃ viditvā vipariṇāmavirāganiraudhaṃ vipariṇāmaṃ virāgaṃ niraudhaṃ (ka॰), ‘pubbai caiva rūpā aitarahi ca sabbai tai rūpā anic‍cā dukkhā vipariṇāmadhammā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passatau uppaj‍jati saumanassaṃ| yaṃ  aivarūpaṃ saumanassaṃ idaṃ vuc‍cati naikkhammasitaṃ saumanassaṃ| saddānaṃtvaiva… gandhānaṃtvaiva… rasānaṃtvaiva… phauṭṭhabbānaṃtvaiva… dhammānaṃtvaiva  anic‍cataṃ viditvā vipariṇāmavirāganiraudhaṃ, ‘pubbai caiva dhammā aitarahi  ca sabbai tai dhammā anic‍cā dukkhā vipariṇāmadhammā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passatau uppaj‍jati saumanassaṃ| yaṃ aivarūpaṃ saumanassaṃ idaṃ vuc‍cati naikkhammasitaṃ saumanassaṃ| imāni cha naikkhammasitāni saumanassāni|

307. ‘‘tattha katamāni cha gaihasitāni daumanassāni? cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyānaṃ rūpānaṃ…pai॰… sautaviñ‍ñaiyyānaṃ saddānaṃ… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyānaṃ gandhānaṃ… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyānaṃ rasānaṃ… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyānaṃ phauṭṭhabbānaṃ… manauviñ‍ñaiyyānaṃ dhammānaṃ iṭṭhānaṃ kantānaṃ manāpānaṃ manauramānaṃ laukāmisapaṭisaṃyuttānaṃ appaṭilābhaṃ vā appaṭilābhatau samanupassatau pubbai vā appaṭiladdhapubbaṃ atītaṃ niruddhaṃ vipariṇataṃ samanussaratau uppaj‍jati daumanassaṃ| yaṃ aivarūpaṃ daumanassaṃ  idaṃ vuc‍cati gaihasitaṃ daumanassaṃ| imāni cha gaihasitāni daumanassāni|

‘‘tattha katamāni cha naikkhammasitāni daumanassāni? rūpānaṃtvaiva  anic‍cataṃ viditvā vipariṇāmavirāganiraudhaṃ, ‘pubbai caiva rūpā aitarahi ca sabbai tai rūpā anic‍cā dukkhā vipariṇāmadhammā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā anuttaraisu vimaukkhaisu pihaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti – ‘kudāssu kadāssu (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) nāmāhaṃ tadāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharissāmi yadariyā aitarahi āyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharantī’ti iti anuttaraisu vimaukkhaisu pihaṃ upaṭṭhāpayatau uppaj‍jati pihapac‍cayā daumanassaṃ| yaṃ aivarūpaṃ daumanassaṃ idaṃ vuc‍cati naikkhammasitaṃ daumanassaṃ| saddānaṃtvaiva…pai॰… gandhānaṃtvaiva… rasānaṃtvaiva… phauṭṭhabbānaṃtvaiva… dhammānaṃtvaiva anic‍cataṃ viditvā vipariṇāmavirāganiraudhaṃ, ‘pubbai caiva dhammā aitarahi ca sabbai tai dhammā anic‍cā dukkhā vipariṇāmadhammā’ti  aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā anuttaraisu vimaukkhaisu pihaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti – ‘kudāssu nāmāhaṃ tadāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharissāmi yadariyā aitarahi āyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharantī’ti iti anuttaraisu vimaukkhaisu pihaṃ upaṭṭhāpayatau uppaj‍jati pihapac‍cayā daumanassaṃ| yaṃ aivarūpaṃ daumanassaṃ idaṃ vuc‍cati naikkhammasitaṃ daumanassaṃ| imāni cha naikkhammasitāni daumanassāni|

308. ‘‘tattha katamā cha gaihasitā upaikkhā? cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā uppaj‍jati upaikkhā bālassa mūḷhassa ( ) (mandassa) (ka॰) puthuj‍janassa anaudhijinassa avipākajinassa anādīnavadassāvinau assutavatau puthuj‍janassa| yā aivarūpā upaikkhā, rūpaṃ sā nātivattati| tasmā sā sāyaṃ (ka॰) upaikkhā ‘gaihasitā’ti  vuc‍cati| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya uppaj‍jati upaikkhā bālassa mūḷhassa puthuj‍janassa anaudhijinassa avipākajinassa anādīnavadassāvinau assutavatau puthuj‍janassa| yā aivarūpā upaikkhā, dhammaṃ sā nātivattati| tasmā sā upaikkhā ‘gaihasitā’ti vuc‍cati| imā cha gaihasitā upaikkhā|

‘‘tattha katamā cha naikkhammasitā upaikkhā? rūpānaṃtvaiva anic‍cataṃ viditvā vipariṇāmavirāganiraudhaṃ, ‘pubbai caiva rūpā aitarahi ca sabbai tai rūpā  anic‍cā dukkhā vipariṇāmadhammā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passatau uppaj‍jati upaikkhā| yā aivarūpā upaikkhā, rūpaṃ sā ativattati| tasmā sā upaikkhā ‘naikkhammasitā’ti vuc‍cati| saddānaṃtvaiva… gandhānaṃtvaiva… rasānaṃtvaiva… phauṭṭhabbānaṃtvaiva… dhammānaṃtvaiva anic‍cataṃ viditvā vipariṇāmavirāganiraudhaṃ, ‘pubbai caiva dhammā aitarahi ca sabbai tai dhammā anic‍cā dukkhā vipariṇāmadhammā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passatau uppaj‍jati upaikkhā| yā aivarūpā upaikkhā, dhammaṃ sā ativattati| tasmā sā upaikkhā ‘naikkhammasitā’ti vuc‍cati| imā cha naikkhammasitā upaikkhā| ‘chattiṃsa sattapadā vaiditabbā’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

309. ‘‘tatra  idaṃ nissāya idaṃ pajahathā’’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ; kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? tatra, bhikkhavai, yāni cha naikkhammasitāni saumanassāni tāni nissāya tāni āgamma yāni cha gaihasitāni saumanassāni tāni pajahatha, tāni samatik‍kamatha| aivamaitaisaṃ pahānaṃ hauti, aivamaitaisaṃ samatik‍kamau hauti|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yāni cha naikkhammasitāni daumanassāni tāni nissāya tāni āgamma yāni cha gaihasitāni daumanassāni tāni pajahatha, tāni samatik‍kamatha| aivamaitaisaṃ pahānaṃ hauti, aivamaitaisaṃ samatik‍kamau hauti|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yā cha naikkhammasitā upaikkhā tā nissāya tā āgamma yā cha gaihasitā  upaikkhā tā pajahatha, tā samatik‍kamatha| aivamaitāsaṃ pahānaṃ hauti, aivamaitāsaṃ samatik‍kamau hauti|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yāni cha naikkhammasitāni saumanassāni tāni nissāya tāni āgamma yāni cha naikkhammasitāni daumanassāni tāni pajahatha, tāni samatik‍kamatha| aivamaitaisaṃ pahānaṃ hauti, aivamaitaisaṃ samatik‍kamau hauti|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yā cha naikkhammasitā upaikkhā tā nissāya tā āgamma yāni cha naikkhammasitāni saumanassāni tāni pajahatha, tāni samatik‍kamatha| aivamaitaisaṃ pahānaṃ hauti, aivamaitaisaṃ samatik‍kamau hauti|

310. ‘‘atthi, bhikkhavai, upaikkhā nānattā nānattasitā, atthi upaikkhā aikattā aikattasitā| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, upaikkhā nānattā nānattasitā? atthi, bhikkhavai, upaikkhā rūpaisu, atthi saddaisu, atthi gandhaisu, atthi rasaisu, atthi phauṭṭhabbaisu – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, upaikkhā nānattā nānattasitā| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, upaikkhā aikattā aikattasitā? atthi, bhikkhavai, upaikkhā ākāsānañ‍cāyatananissitā, atthi viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatananissitā, atthi ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatananissitā, atthi naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatananissitā – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, upaikkhā aikattā aikattasitā|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yāyaṃ upaikkhā aikattā aikattasitā taṃ nissāya taṃ āgamma yāyaṃ upaikkhā nānattā nānattasitā taṃ pajahatha, taṃ samatik‍kamatha| aivamaitissā pahānaṃ hauti, aivamaitissā samatik‍kamau hauti|

‘‘atammayataṃ, bhikkhavai, nissāya atammayataṃ āgamma yāyaṃ upaikkhā aikattā aikattasitā taṃ pajahatha, taṃ samatik‍kamatha| aivamaitissā pahānaṃ hauti, aivamaitissā samatik‍kamau hauti| ‘tatra idaṃ  nissāya idaṃ pajahathā’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

311. ‘‘‘tayau  satipaṭṭhānā yadariyau saivati, yadariyau saivamānau satthā gaṇamanusāsitumarahatī’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ; kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, satthā sāvakānaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti anukampakau hitaisī anukampaṃ upādāya – ‘idaṃ vau hitāya, idaṃ vau sukhāyā’ti| tassa sāvakā na sussūsanti, na sautaṃ audahanti, na añ‍ñā cittaṃ upaṭṭhapainti, vauk‍kamma ca satthusāsanā vattanti| tatra, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau na caiva anattamanau hauti, na ca anattamanataṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti, anavassutau ca viharati satau sampajānau| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ yadariyau saivati, yadariyau saivamānau satthā gaṇamanusāsitumarahati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, satthā sāvakānaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti anukampakau hitaisī anukampaṃ upādāya – ‘idaṃ vau hitāya, idaṃ vau sukhāyā’ti| tassa aikac‍cai sāvakā na sussūsanti, na sautaṃ audahanti, na añ‍ñā cittaṃ upaṭṭhapainti, vauk‍kamma ca satthusāsanā vattanti; aikac‍cai sāvakā sussūsanti, sautaṃ audahanti, añ‍ñā cittaṃ upaṭṭhapainti, na ca vauk‍kamma satthusāsanā vattanti| tatra, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau na caiva anattamanau hauti, na ca anattamanataṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; na ca attamanau hauti, na ca attamanataṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| anattamanatā ca attamanatā ca – tadubhayaṃ abhinivaj‍jaitvā upaikkhakau viharati satau sampajānau| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dutiyaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ yadariyau saivati, yadariyau saivamānau satthā gaṇamanusāsitumarahati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, satthā sāvakānaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti anukampakau hitaisī anukampaṃ upādāya – ‘idaṃ vau hitāya, idaṃ vau sukhāyā’ti| tassa sāvakā sussūsanti, sautaṃ audahanti, añ‍ñācittaṃ  upaṭṭhapainti, na ca vauk‍kamma satthusāsanā vattanti| tatra, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau attamanau caiva hauti, attamanatañ‍ca paṭisaṃvaidaiti, anavassutau ca viharati satau sampajānau| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, tatiyaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ yadariyau saivati, yadariyau saivamānau satthā gaṇamanusāsitumarahati| ‘tayau  satipaṭṭhānā yadariyau saivati, yadariyau saivamānau satthā gaṇamanusāsitumarahatī’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

312. ‘‘‘sau vuc‍cati yauggācariyānaṃ anuttarau purisadammasārathī’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ  vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? hatthidamakaina, bhikkhavai, hatthidammau sāritau aikaṃyaiva disaṃ dhāvati – puratthimaṃ vā pacchimaṃ vā uttaraṃ vā dakkhiṇaṃ vā| assadamakaina, bhikkhavai, assadammau sāritau aikañ‍ñaiva disaṃ dhāvati – puratthimaṃ vā pacchimaṃ vā uttaraṃ vā dakkhiṇaṃ vā| gaudamakaina, bhikkhavai, gaudammau sāritau aikaṃyaiva disaṃ dhāvati – puratthimaṃ vā pacchimaṃ vā uttaraṃ vā dakkhiṇaṃ vā| tathāgataina hi, bhikkhavai, arahatā sammāsambuddhaina purisadammau sāritau aṭṭha disā vidhāvati| rūpī rūpāni passati – ayaṃ aikā disā; ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī bahiddhā rūpāni passati – ayaṃ dutiyā disā; subhantvaiva adhimuttau hauti – ayaṃ tatiyā disā; sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ  upasampaj‍ja viharati – ayaṃ catutthī disā; sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati – ayaṃ pañ‍camī disā; sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja  viharati – ayaṃ chaṭṭhī disā; sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati – ayaṃ sattamī disā; sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati – ayaṃ aṭṭhamī disā| tathāgataina, bhikkhavai, arahatā sammāsambuddhaina purisadammau sāritau imā aṭṭha disā vidhāvati| ‘sau vuc‍cati yauggācariyānaṃ anuttarau purisadammasārathī’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vutta’’nti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

saḷāyatanavibhaṅgasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. uddaisavibhaṅgasuttaṃ

313. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘uddaisavibhaṅgaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘tathā tathā, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu upaparikkhaiyya yathā yathā yathā yathāssa (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) upaparikkhatau bahiddhā cassa viñ‍ñāṇaṃ avikkhittaṃ avisaṭaṃ, ajjhattaṃ asaṇṭhitaṃ anupādāya na paritassaiyya| bahiddhā, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇai avikkhittai avisaṭai sati ajjhattaṃ asaṇṭhitai anupādāya aparitassatau āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇadukkhasamudayasambhavau na hautī’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ pāvisi|

314. atha khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau, aitadahausi – ‘‘idaṃ khau nau, āvusau, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – ‘tathā tathā, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu upaparikkhaiyya yathā yathā upaparikkhatau bahiddhā cassa viñ‍ñāṇaṃ avikkhittaṃ avisaṭaṃ, ajjhattaṃ asaṇṭhitaṃ anupādāya na paritassaiyya| bahiddhā, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇai avikkhittai avisaṭai sati ajjhattaṃ asaṇṭhitai anupādāya aparitassatau āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇadukkhasamudayasambhavau na hautī’ti| kau nu khau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajaiyyā’’ti? atha khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā mahākac‍cānau satthu caiva saṃvaṇṇitau sambhāvitau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ; pahauti cāyasmā mahākac‍cānau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajituṃ| yaṃnūna mayaṃ yaināyasmā mahākac‍cānau  tainupasaṅkamaiyyāma; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyāmā’’ti|

atha  khau tai bhikkhū yaināyasmā mahākac‍cānau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā mahākac‍cānaina saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ aitadavaucuṃ –

‘‘idaṃ khau nau, āvusau kac‍cāna, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – ‘tathā tathā, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu upaparikkhaiyya yathā yathā upaparikkhatau bahiddhā cassa viñ‍ñāṇaṃ avikkhittaṃ avisaṭaṃ, ajjhattaṃ asaṇṭhitaṃ anupādāya na paritassaiyya| bahiddhā, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇai avikkhittai avisaṭai sati ajjhattaṃ asaṇṭhitai anupādāya aparitassatau āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇadukkhasamudayasambhavau na hautī’ti| taisaṃ nau, āvusau kac‍cāna, amhākaṃ, acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau, aitadahausi – ‘idaṃ khau nau, āvusau, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – tathā tathā, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu upaparikkhaiyya, yathā yathā upaparikkhatau bahiddhā cassa viñ‍ñāṇaṃ avikkhittaṃ avisaṭaṃ ajjhattaṃ asaṇṭhitaṃ anupādāya na paritassaiyya| bahiddhā, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇai avikkhittai  avisaṭai sati ajjhattaṃ asaṇṭhitai anupādāya aparitassatau āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇadukkhasamudayasambhavau na hautī’ti| kau nu khau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ  avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajaiyyā’’ti| ‘‘taisaṃ nau, āvusau kac‍cāna, amhākaṃ aitadahausi – ‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā mahākac‍cānau satthu caiva saṃvaṇṇitau, sambhāvitau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ| pahauti cāyasmā mahākac‍cānau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajituṃ| yaṃnūna mayaṃ yaināyasmā mahākac‍cānau tainupasaṅkamaiyyāma; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyāmā’ti – vibhajatāyasmā mahākac‍cānau’’ti|

315. ‘‘‘saiyyathāpi, āvusau, purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau atik‍kammaiva mūlaṃ  atik‍kamma khandhaṃ sākhāpalāsai sāraṃ pariyaisitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyya, aivaṃ sampadamidaṃ āyasmantānaṃ satthari sammukhībhūtai taṃ bhagavantaṃ atisitvā amhai aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchitabbaṃ mañ‍ñatha| sau hāvusau, bhagavā jānaṃ jānāti, passaṃ passati, cakkhubhūtau ñāṇabhūtau dhammabhūtau brahmabhūtau vattā pavattā atthassa nin‍naitā amatassa dātā dhammassāmī tathāgatau| sau caiva panaitassa kālau ahausi yaṃ bhagavantaṃyaiva aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyātha; yathā vau bhagavā byākaraiyya tathā naṃ dhāraiyyāthā’’’ti| ‘addhāvusau kac‍cāna, bhagavā  jānaṃ jānāti, passaṃ passati, cakkhubhūtau ñāṇabhūtau dhammabhūtau brahmabhūtau vattā pavattā atthassa nin‍naitā amatassa dātā dhammassāmī tathāgatau| sau caiva panaitassa kālau ahausi yaṃ bhagavantaṃyaiva aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyāma; yathā nau bhagavā byākaraiyya  tathā naṃ dhāraiyyāma| api cāyasmā mahākac‍cānau satthu caiva saṃvaṇṇitau sambhāvitau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ| pahauti cāyasmā mahākac‍cānau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajituṃ| vibhajatāyasmā mahākac‍cānau agaruṃ karitvā’ti| ‘taina hāvusau, suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’ti| ‘aivamāvusau’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau mahākac‍cānassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā mahākac‍cānau aitadavauca –

‘yaṃ khau nau, āvusau, bhagavā  saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – tathā tathā, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu  upaparikkhaiyya, yathā yathā upaparikkhatau bahiddhā cassa viñ‍ñāṇaṃ avikkhittaṃ avisaṭaṃ ajjhattaṃ asaṇṭhitaṃ anupādāya na paritassaiyya, bahiddhā, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇai avikkhittai avisaṭai sati ajjhattaṃ asaṇṭhitai anupādāya aparitassatau āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇadukkhasamudayasambhavau na hautī’ti| imassa khau ahaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmi|

316. ‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, bahiddhā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ vikkhittaṃ visaṭanti vuc‍cati? idhāvusau, bhikkhunau cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā rūpanimittānusāri viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti rūpanimittassādagadhitaṃ …gathitaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) rūpanimittassādavinibandhaṃ …vinibandhaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) rūpanimittassādasaṃyaujanasaṃyuttaṃ bahiddhā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ vikkhittaṃ visaṭanti vuc‍cati| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya dhammanimittānusārī viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti; dhammanimittassādagadhitaṃ dhammanimittassādavinibandhaṃ dhammanimittassādasaṃyaujanasaṃyuttaṃ bahiddhā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ vikkhittaṃ visaṭanti vuc‍cati| aivaṃ khau āvusau, bahiddhā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ vikkhittaṃ visaṭanti vuc‍cati|

317. ‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, bahiddhā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ avikkhittaṃ avisaṭanti vuc‍cati? idhāvusau, bhikkhunau cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā na rūpanimittānusāri viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti rūpanimittassādagadhitaṃ na  rūpanimittassādavinibandhaṃ na rūpanimittassādasaṃyaujanasaṃyuttaṃ bahiddhā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ avikkhittaṃ avisaṭanti vuc‍cati| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya na dhammanimittānusārī viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti na dhammanimittassādagadhitaṃ na dhammanimittassādavinibandhaṃ na dhammanimittassādasaṃyaujanasaṃyuttaṃ bahiddhā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ avikkhittaṃ avisaṭanti vuc‍cati| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, bahiddhā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ avikkhittaṃ avisaṭanti vuc‍cati|

318. ‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, ajjhattaṃ ajjhattaṃ cittaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) saṇṭhitanti vuc‍cati? idhāvusau, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| tassa vivaikajapītisukhānusāri viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti vivaikajapītisukhassādagadhitaṃ vivaikajapītisukhassādavinibandhaṃ  vivaikajapītisukhassādasaṃyaujanasaṃyuttaṃ ajjhattaṃ cittaṃ saṇṭhitanti vuc‍cati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| tassa samādhijapītisukhānusāri viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti samādhijapītisukhassādagadhitaṃ samādhijapītisukhassādavinibandhaṃ samādhijapītisukhassādasaṃyaujanasaṃyuttaṃ ajjhattaṃ cittaṃ saṇṭhitanti vuc‍cati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau  ca viharati satau ca sampajānau sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| tassa upaikkhānusāri viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti upaikkhāsukhassādagadhitaṃ upaikkhāsukhassādavinibandhaṃ upaikkhāsukhassādasaṃyaujanasaṃyuttaṃ ajjhattaṃ cittaṃ saṇṭhitanti vuc‍cati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| tassa adukkhamasukhānusāri viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti adukkhamasukhassādagadhitaṃ adukkhamasukhassādavinibandhaṃ  adukkhamasukhassādasaṃyaujanasaṃyuttaṃ ajjhattaṃ cittaṃ saṇṭhitanti vuc‍cati| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, ajjhattaṃ ajjhattaṃ cittaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) saṇṭhitanti vuc‍cati|

319. ‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, ajjhattaṃ ajjhattaṃ cittaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) asaṇṭhitanti vuc‍cati? idhāvusau, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| tassa na vivaikajapītisukhānusāri viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti na vivaikajapītisukhassādagadhitaṃ na vivaikajapītisukhassādavinibandhaṃ na vivaikajapītisukhassādasaṃyaujanasaṃyuttaṃ ajjhattaṃ cittaṃ asaṇṭhitanti vuc‍cati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| tassa na samādhijapītisukhānusāri viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti na samādhijapītisukhassādagadhitaṃ na samādhijapītisukhassādavinibandhaṃ na samādhijapītisukhassādasaṃyaujanasaṃyuttaṃ ajjhattaṃ cittaṃ asaṇṭhitanti vuc‍cati|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| tassa na upaikkhānusāri viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti na upaikkhāsukhassādagadhitaṃ na upaikkhāsukhassādavinibandhaṃ na upaikkhāsukhassādasaṃyaujanasaṃyuttaṃ ajjhattaṃ cittaṃ asaṇṭhitanti vuc‍cati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| tassa na adukkhamasukhānusāri viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti na adukkhamasukhassādagadhitaṃ na adukkhamasukhassādavinibandhaṃ na adukkhamasukhassādasaṃyaujanasaṃyuttaṃ ajjhattaṃ cittaṃ asaṇṭhitanti vuc‍cati| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, ajjhattaṃ ajjhattaṃ cittaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) asaṇṭhitanti vuc‍cati|

320. ‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, anupādā paritassanā hauti? idhāvusau, assutavā puthuj‍janau ariyānaṃ adassāvī ariyadhammassa akauvidau ariyadhammai avinītau sappurisānaṃ adassāvī sappurisadhammassa  akauvidau sappurisadhammai avinītau rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ attani vā rūpaṃ rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| tassa taṃ rūpaṃ vipariṇamati, añ‍ñathā hauti| tassa rūpavipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā rūpavipariṇāmānuparivatti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti| tassa rūpavipariṇāmānuparivattajā paritassanā dhammasamuppādā cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhanti| caitasau pariyādānā uttāsavā ca hauti vighātavā ca apaikkhavā ca anupādāya ca paritassati| vaidanaṃ  …pai॰… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ  attatau samanupassati viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| tassa taṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ vipariṇamati, añ‍ñathā hauti| tassa viñ‍ñāṇavipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā viñ‍ñāṇavipariṇāmānuparivatti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti| tassa viñ‍ñāṇavipariṇāmānuparivattajā paritassanā dhammasamuppādā cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhanti| caitasau pariyādānā uttāsavā ca hauti vighātavā ca apaikkhavā ca anupādāya ca paritassati| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, anupādā paritassanā hauti|

321. ‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, anupādānā aparitassanā hauti? idhāvusau, sutavā ariyasāvakau ariyānaṃ dassāvī ariyadhammassa kauvidau ariyadhammai  suvinītau sappurisānaṃ dassāvī sappurisadhammassa kauvidau sappurisadhammai suvinītau na rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati na rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ na attani vā rūpaṃ na rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| tassa taṃ rūpaṃ vipariṇamati, añ‍ñathā hauti| tassa rūpavipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā na ca rūpavipariṇāmānuparivatti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti| tassa na rūpavipariṇāmānuparivattajā paritassanā dhammasamuppādā cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhanti| caitasau pariyādānā na caivuttāsavā na ca uttāsavā (sī॰) hauti na ca vighātavā na ca apaikkhavā anupādāya ca na paritassati| na vaidanaṃ… na sañ‍ñaṃ… na saṅkhārai… na viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati na viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ na attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ na viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā  attānaṃ| tassa taṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ vipariṇamati, añ‍ñathā hauti| tassa viñ‍ñāṇavipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā na ca viñ‍ñāṇavipariṇāmānuparivatti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti| tassa na viñ‍ñāṇavipariṇāmānuparivattajā paritassanā dhammasamuppādā cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhanti| caitasau pariyādānā na caivuttāsavā hauti na ca vighātavā na ca apaikkhavā, anupādāya ca na paritassati| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, anupādā aparitassanā hauti|

‘‘yaṃ khau nau, āvusau, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā  uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – ‘tathā tathā, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu upaparikkhaiyya yathā yathā upaparikkhatau bahiddhā cassa viñ‍ñāṇaṃ avikkhittaṃ avisaṭaṃ, ajjhattaṃ asaṇṭhitaṃ anupādāya na paritassaiyya| bahiddhā, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇai avikkhittai avisaṭai sati ajjhattaṃ asaṇṭhitai anupādāya aparitassatau āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇadukkhasamudayasambhavau na hautī’ti| imassa khau ahaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ  ājānāmi| ākaṅkhamānā ca pana tumhai āyasmantau bhagavantaṃyaiva upasaṅkamitvā aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyātha; yathā vau bhagavā byākarauti tathā naṃ dhāraiyyāthā’’ti|

322. atha khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau mahākac‍cānassa bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā  bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ –

‘‘yaṃ  khau nau, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – ‘tathā tathā, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu upaparikkhaiyya yathā yathā upaparikkhatau bahiddhā cassa viñ‍ñāṇaṃ avikkhittaṃ avisaṭaṃ, ajjhattaṃ asaṇṭhitaṃ anupādāya na paritassaiyya| bahiddhā, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇai avikkhittai avisaṭai sati ajjhattaṃ asaṇṭhitai anupādāya aparitassatau āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇadukkhasamudayasambhavau na hautī’’’ti|

‘‘taisaṃ nau, bhantai, amhākaṃ, acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau, aitadahausi – ‘idaṃ khau nau, āvusau, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – tathā tathā, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu upaparikkhaiyya, yathā yathā upaparikkhatau bahiddhā cassa viñ‍ñāṇaṃ avikkhittaṃ avisaṭaṃ, ajjhattaṃ asaṇṭhitaṃ anupādāya na paritassaiyya| bahiddhā, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇai avikkhittai avisaṭai sati ajjhattaṃ asaṇṭhitai anupādāya aparitassatau āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇadukkhasamudayasambhavau na hautīti| kau nu khau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajaiyyā’ti? taisaṃ nau, bhantai, amhākaṃ aitadahausi – ‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā mahākac‍cānau  satthu caiva saṃvaṇṇitau sambhāvitau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ| pahauti cāyasmā mahākac‍cānau  imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajituṃ| yaṃnūna mayaṃ yaināyasmā mahākac‍cānau tainupasaṅkamaiyyāma; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyāmā’ti|

‘‘atha khau mayaṃ, bhantai, yaināyasmā mahākac‍cānau tainupasaṅkamimha; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchimha| taisaṃ nau, bhantai, āyasmatā mahākac‍cānaina imaihi ākāraihi imaihi padaihi imaihi byañ‍janaihi atthau vibhattau’’ti|

‘‘paṇḍitau, bhikkhavai, mahākac‍cānau; mahāpañ‍ñau, bhikkhavai, mahākac‍cānau| maṃ caipi tumhai, bhikkhavai, aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyātha, ahampi aivamaivaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ  yathā taṃ mahākac‍cānaina byākataṃ| aisau caivaitassa aisau caitassa (sī॰ pī॰), aisau caiva tassa (syā॰ kaṃ॰), aisauyaiva tassa (ka॰) atthau| aivañ‍ca naṃ dhāraiyyāthā’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

uddaisavibhaṅgasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. araṇavibhaṅgasuttaṃ

323. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘araṇavibhaṅgaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘na kāmasukhamanuyuñ‍jaiyya hīnaṃ gammaṃ pauthuj‍janikaṃ anariyaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ, na ca attakilamathānuyaugamanuyuñ‍jaiyya dukkhaṃ anariyaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ| aitai khau, bhikkhavai aitai khau (sī॰), aitai tai (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), ubhau antai anupagamma majjhimā paṭipadā tathāgataina abhisambuddhā, cakkhukaraṇī ñāṇakaraṇī upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati| ussādanañ‍ca jañ‍ñā, apasādanañ‍ca jañ‍ñā; ussādanañ‍ca ñatvā apasādanañ‍ca ñatvā naivussādaiyya, na apasādaiyya nāpasādaiyya (sī॰), dhammamaiva daisaiyya| sukhavinicchayaṃ jañ‍ñā; sukhavinicchayaṃ ñatvā ajjhattaṃ sukhamanuyuñ‍jaiyya| rahauvādaṃ na bhāsaiyya, sammukhā na khīṇaṃ nātikhīṇaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) bhaṇai| ataramānauva bhāsaiyya, nau taramānau| janapadaniruttiṃ nābhinivaisaiyya, samañ‍ñaṃ nātidhāvaiyyāti – ayamuddaisau araṇavibhaṅgassa|

324. ‘‘‘na  kāmasukhamanuyuñ‍jaiyya hīnaṃ gammaṃ pauthuj‍janikaṃ anariyaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ, na ca attakilamathānuyaugamanuyuñ‍jaiyya dukkhaṃ anariyaṃ anatthasaṃhita’nti  – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ; kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? yau kāmapaṭisandhisukhinau saumanassānuyaugau hīnau gammau pauthuj‍janikau anariyau anatthasaṃhitau, sadukkhau aisau dhammau saupaghātau saupāyāsau sapariḷāhau; micchāpaṭipadā| yau kāmapaṭisandhisukhinau saumanassānuyaugaṃ ananuyaugau  hīnaṃ gammaṃ pauthuj‍janikaṃ anariyaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ, adukkhau aisau dhammau anupaghātau anupāyāsau apariḷāhau; sammāpaṭipadā| yau attakilamathānuyaugau dukkhau anariyau anatthasaṃhitau, sadukkhau aisau dhammau saupaghātau saupāyāsau sapariḷāhau; micchāpaṭipadā| yau attakilamathānuyaugaṃ ananuyaugau dukkhaṃ anariyaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ, adukkhau aisau dhammau anupaghātau  anupāyāsau apariḷāhau; sammāpaṭipadā| ‘na kāmasukhamanuyuñ‍jaiyya hīnaṃ gammaṃ pauthuj‍janikaṃ anariyaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ, na ca attakilamathānuyaugaṃ anuyuñ‍jaiyya dukkhaṃ anariyaṃ anatthasaṃhita’nti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

325. ‘‘‘aitai khau ubhau antai anupagamma majjhimā paṭipadā tathāgataina abhisambuddhā, cakkhukaraṇī ñāṇakaraṇī upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattatī’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvācā, sammākammantau, sammāājīvau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi| ‘aitai khau ubhau antai anupagamma majjhimā paṭipadā tathāgataina abhisambuddhā, cakkhukaraṇī ñāṇakaraṇī upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattatī’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

326. ‘‘‘ussādanañ‍ca jañ‍ñā, apasādanañ‍ca jañ‍ñā; ussādanañ‍ca ñatvā apasādanañ‍ca ñatvā naivussādaiyya, na apasādaiyya, dhammamaiva daisaiyyā’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, ussādanā ca hauti apasādanā ca, nau ca dhammadaisanā? ‘yai kāmapaṭisandhisukhinau saumanassānuyaugaṃ anuyuttā hīnaṃ gammaṃ pauthuj‍janikaṃ anariyaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ, sabbai tai sadukkhā saupaghātā saupāyāsā sapariḷāhā micchāpaṭipan‍nā’ti – iti vadaṃ iti paraṃ (ka॰) itthaikai apasādaiti|

‘‘‘yai  kāmapaṭisandhisukhinau saumanassānuyaugaṃ ananuyuttā hīnaṃ gammaṃ pauthuj‍janikaṃ anariyaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ, sabbai tai adukkhā anupaghātā anupāyāsā apariḷāhā sammāpaṭipan‍nā’ti – iti vadaṃ itthaikai ussādaiti|

‘‘‘yai attakilamathānuyaugaṃ anuyuttā dukkhaṃ anariyaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ, sabbai tai sadukkhā  saupaghātā saupāyāsā sapariḷāhā micchāpaṭipan‍nā’ti – iti vadaṃ itthaikai apasādaiti|

‘‘‘yai attakilamathānuyaugaṃ ananuyuttā dukkhaṃ anariyaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ, sabbai tai adukkhā  anupaghātā anupāyāsā apariḷāhā sammāpaṭipan‍nā’ti  – iti vadaṃ itthaikai ussādaiti|

‘‘‘yaisaṃ kaisañ‍ci bhavasaṃyaujanaṃ appahīnaṃ, sabbai tai sadukkhā saupaghātā saupāyāsā sapariḷāhā micchāpaṭipan‍nā’ti – iti vadaṃ itthaikai apasādaiti|

‘‘‘yaisaṃ kaisañ‍ci bhavasaṃyaujanaṃ pahīnaṃ, sabbai tai adukkhā anupaghātā anupāyāsā apariḷāhā sammāpaṭipan‍nā’ti – iti vadaṃ itthaikai ussādaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, ussādanā ca hauti apasādanā ca, nau ca dhammadaisanā|

327. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, naivussādanā hauti na apasādanā, dhammadaisanā ca dhammadaisanāva (syā॰ kaṃ॰)? ‘yai kāmapaṭisandhisukhinau saumanassānuyaugaṃ anuyuttā hīnaṃ gammaṃ pauthuj‍janikaṃ anariyaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ, sabbai tai sadukkhā saupaghātā saupāyāsā sapariḷāhā micchāpaṭipan‍nā’ti – na aivamāha| ‘anuyaugau ca khau, sadukkhau aisau dhammau saupaghātau saupāyāsau sapariḷāhau; micchāpaṭipadā’ti – iti vadaṃ dhammamaiva daisaiti|

‘‘‘yai kāmapaṭisandhisukhinau saumanassānuyaugaṃ ananuyuttā hīnaṃ gammaṃ pauthuj‍janikaṃ anariyaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ, sabbai tai adukkhā anupaghātā anupāyāsā apariḷāhā sammāpaṭipan‍nā’ti – na aivamāha| ‘ananuyaugau ca khau, adukkhau aisau dhammau anupaghātau anupāyāsau apariḷāhau; sammāpaṭipadā’ti – iti vadaṃ dhammamaiva daisaiti|

‘‘‘yai  attakilamathānuyaugaṃ anuyuttā dukkhaṃ anariyaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ, sabbai tai sadukkhā saupaghātā saupāyāsā sapariḷāhā micchāpaṭipan‍nā’ti – na aivamāha| ‘anuyaugau ca khau, sadukkhau aisau dhammau saupaghātau saupāyāsau sapariḷāhau; micchāpaṭipadā’ti – iti vadaṃ dhammamaiva daisaiti|

‘‘‘yai attakilamathānuyaugaṃ ananuyuttā dukkhaṃ anariyaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ, sabbai tai adukkhā anupaghātā anupāyāsā apariḷāhā sammāpaṭipan‍nā’ti – na aivamāha| ‘ananuyaugau ca khau, adukkhau aisau dhammau anupaghātau anupāyāsau apariḷāhau; sammāpaṭipadā’ti – iti vadaṃ dhammamaiva daisaiti|

‘‘‘yaisaṃ  kaisañ‍ci bhavasaṃyaujanaṃ appahīnaṃ, sabbai tai sadukkhā saupaghātā saupāyāsā sapariḷāhā micchāpaṭipan‍nā’ti – na aivamāha| ‘bhavasaṃyaujanai ca khau appahīnai bhavaupi appahīnau hautī’ti – iti vadaṃ dhammamaiva daisaiti|

‘‘‘yaisaṃ kaisañ‍ci bhavasaṃyaujanaṃ pahīnaṃ, sabbai tai adukkhā anupaghātā anupāyāsā apariḷāhā sammāpaṭipan‍nā’ti – na aivamāha| ‘bhavasaṃyaujanai ca khau pahīnai bhavaupi pahīnau hautī’ti – iti vadaṃ dhammamaiva daisaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, naivussādanā hauti na apasādanā, dhammadaisanā ca| ‘ussādanañ‍ca jañ‍ñā, apasādanañ‍ca jañ‍ñā; ussādanañ‍ca ñatvā apasādanañ‍ca ñatvā naivussādaiyya, na apasādaiyya, dhammamaiva daisaiyyā’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

328. ‘‘‘sukhavinicchayaṃ jañ‍ñā; sukhavinicchayaṃ ñatvā ajjhattaṃ sukhamanuyuñ‍jaiyyā’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, kāmaguṇā| katamai pañ‍ca? cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā, sautaviñ‍ñaiyyā  saddā… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyā gandhā… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā – imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā| yaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, imai pañ‍ca kāmaguṇai paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ idaṃ vuc‍cati kāmasukhaṃ mīḷhasukhaṃ puthuj‍janasukhaṃ anariyasukhaṃ| ‘na āsaivitabbaṃ, na bhāvaitabbaṃ, na bahulīkātabbaṃ, bhāyitabbaṃ aitassa sukhassā’ti – vadāmi| idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ vuc‍cati naikkhammasukhaṃ pavivaikasukhaṃ upasamasukhaṃ sambaudhisukhaṃ| ‘āsaivitabbaṃ, bhāvaitabbaṃ, bahulīkātabbaṃ, na bhāyitabbaṃ aitassa sukhassā’ti – vadāmi| ‘sukhavinicchayaṃ jañ‍ñā; sukhavinicchayaṃ ñatvā ajjhattaṃ sukhamanuyuñ‍jaiyyā’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

329. ‘‘‘rahauvādaṃ na bhāsaiyya, sammukhā na khīṇaṃ bhaṇai’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? tatra, bhikkhavai, yaṃ jañ‍ñā rahauvādaṃ abhūtaṃ atacchaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ sasak‍kaṃ sampattaṃ (ka॰) taṃ rahauvādaṃ na bhāsaiyya| yampi jañ‍ñā rahauvādaṃ bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ tassapi sikkhaiyya avacanāya| yañ‍ca khau jañ‍ñā rahauvādaṃ  bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ atthasaṃhitaṃ tatra kālañ‍ñū assa tassa rahauvādassa vacanāya| tatra, bhikkhavai, yaṃ jañ‍ñā sammukhā khīṇavādaṃ abhūtaṃ atacchaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ sasak‍kaṃ taṃ sammukhā khīṇavādaṃ na bhāsaiyya| yampi jañ‍ñā sammukhā khīṇavādaṃ bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ tassapi sikkhaiyya avacanāya| yañ‍ca khau jañ‍ñā sammukhā khīṇavādaṃ bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ atthasaṃhitaṃ tatra kālañ‍ñū assa tassa sammukhā khīṇavādassa vacanāya| ‘rahauvādaṃ na bhāsaiyya, sammukhā na khīṇaṃ bhaṇai’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

330. ‘‘‘ataramānauva bhāsaiyya nau taramānau’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? tatra, bhikkhavai, taramānassa bhāsatau kāyaupi kilamati, cittampi upahañ‍ñati ūhañ‍ñati (sī॰), saraupi upahañ‍ñati ūhañ‍ñati (sī॰), kaṇṭhaupi āturīyati, avisaṭṭhampi hauti aviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ taramānassa bhāsitaṃ| tatra, bhikkhavai, ataramānassa bhāsatau kāyaupi na kilamati, cittampi na upahañ‍ñati, saraupi na upahañ‍ñati, kaṇṭhaupi na āturīyati, visaṭṭhampi hauti viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ ataramānassa bhāsitaṃ| ‘ataramānauva bhāsaiyya, nau taramānau’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

331. ‘‘‘janapadaniruttiṃ  nābhinivaisaiyya, samañ‍ñaṃ nātidhāvaiyyā’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, janapadaniruttiyā ca abhinivaisau hauti samañ‍ñāya ca atisārau? idha, bhikkhavai, tadaivaikac‍caisu janapadaisu ‘pātī’ti sañ‍jānanti, ‘patta’nti  sañ‍jānanti, ‘vitta’nti viṭṭhanti (syā॰ kaṃ॰) sañ‍jānanti, ‘sarāva’nti sañ‍jānanti ‘dhāraupa’nti harausanti (syā॰ kaṃ॰) sañ‍jānanti, ‘pauṇa’nti sañ‍jānanti, ‘pisīlava’nti pisīlanti (sī॰ pī॰), pipilanti (syā॰ kaṃ॰) sañ‍jānanti| iti yathā yathā naṃ taisu taisu janapadaisu sañ‍jānanti tathā tathā thāmasā parāmāsā parāmassa (sī॰) abhinivissa vauharati – ‘idamaiva sac‍caṃ, maughamañ‍ña’nti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, janapadaniruttiyā ca abhinivaisau hauti samañ‍ñāya ca atisārau|

332. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, janapadaniruttiyā ca anabhinivaisau hauti samañ‍ñāya ca anatisārau? idha, bhikkhavai, tadaivaikac‍caisu janapadaisu ‘pātī’ti sañ‍jānanti, ‘patta’nti sañ‍jānanti, ‘vitta’nti sañ‍jānanti, ‘sarāva’nti sañ‍jānanti, ‘dhāraupa’nti sañ‍jānanti, ‘pauṇa’nti sañ‍jānanti, ‘pisīlava’nti sañ‍jānanti| iti yathā yathā naṃ taisu taisu janapadaisu sañ‍jānanti ‘idaṃ kira mai idaṃ kira tai ca (ka॰) āyasmantau sandhāya vauharantī’ti tathā tathā vauharati aparāmasaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, janapadaniruttiyā ca anabhinivaisau hauti, samañ‍ñāya ca anatisārau| ‘janapadaniruttiṃ nābhinivaisaiyya samañ‍ñaṃ nātidhāvaiyyā’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

333. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yau kāmapaṭisandhisukhinau saumanassānuyaugau hīnau gammau pauthuj‍janikau anariyau anatthasaṃhitau, sadukkhau aisau dhammau saupaghātau saupāyāsau sapariḷāhau; micchāpaṭipadā| tasmā aisau  dhammau saraṇau| tatra, bhikkhavai, yau kāmapaṭisandhisukhinau saumanassānuyaugaṃ ananuyaugau hīnaṃ gammaṃ pauthuj‍janikaṃ anariyaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ, adukkhau aisau dhammau anupaghātau anupāyāsau apariḷāhau; sammāpaṭipadā| tasmā aisau dhammau araṇau|

334. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yau attakilamathānuyaugau dukkhau anariyau anatthasaṃhitau, sadukkhau aisau dhammau saupaghātau saupāyāsau  sapariḷāhau; micchāpaṭipadā| tasmā aisau dhammau saraṇau| tatra, bhikkhavai, yau attakilamathānuyaugaṃ ananuyaugau dukkhaṃ anariyaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ, adukkhau aisau dhammau anupaghātau anupāyāsau apariḷāhau; sammāpaṭipadā| tasmā aisau dhammau araṇau|

335. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yāyaṃ majjhimā paṭipadā tathāgataina abhisambuddhā, cakkhukaraṇī ñāṇakaraṇī upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati, adukkhau aisau dhammau anupaghātau anupāyāsau apariḷāhau; sammāpaṭipadā| tasmā aisau dhammau araṇau|

336. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yāyaṃ ussādanā ca apasādanā ca nau ca dhammadaisanā, sadukkhau aisau dhammau saupaghātau saupāyāsau sapariḷāhau; micchāpaṭipadā| tasmā aisau dhammau saraṇau| tatra, bhikkhavai, yāyaṃ naivussādanā ca na apasādanā ca dhammadaisanā ca, adukkhau aisau dhammau anupaghātau anupāyāsau apariḷāhau; sammāpaṭipadā| tasmā aisau dhammau araṇau|

337. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yamidaṃ kāmasukhaṃ mīḷhasukhaṃ pauthuj‍janasukhaṃ anariyasukhaṃ, sadukkhau aisau dhammau saupaghātau saupāyāsau sapariḷāhau; micchāpaṭipadā| tasmā aisau dhammau saraṇau| tatra, bhikkhavai, yamidaṃ naikkhammasukhaṃ pavivaikasukhaṃ upasamasukhaṃ sambaudhisukhaṃ, adukkhau aisau dhammau anupaghātau anupāyāsau apariḷāhau; sammāpaṭipadā| tasmā aisau dhammau araṇau|
