338. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ rahauvādau abhūtau atacchau anatthasaṃhitau, sadukkhau aisau dhammau saupaghātau saupāyāsau sapariḷāhau; micchāpaṭipadā| tasmā aisau dhammau saraṇau| tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ rahauvādau bhūtau tacchau anatthasaṃhitau, sadukkhau aisau dhammau saupaghātau saupāyāsau sapariḷāhau; micchāpaṭipadā| tasmā aisau dhammau saraṇau| tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ rahauvādau bhūtau tacchau atthasaṃhitau, adukkhau aisau dhammau anupaghātau anupāyāsau apariḷāhau; sammāpaṭipadā| tasmā aisau dhammau araṇau|

339. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ sammukhā khīṇavādau abhūtau atacchau anatthasaṃhitau, sadukkhau aisau dhammau saupaghātau saupāyāsau sapariḷāhau; micchāpaṭipadā| tasmā aisau dhammau saraṇau| tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ sammukhā khīṇavādau bhūtau tacchau anatthasaṃhitau, sadukkhau aisau dhammau saupaghātau saupāyāsau sapariḷāhau; micchāpaṭipadā| tasmā aisau dhammau saraṇau| tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ sammukhā khīṇavādau  bhūtau tacchau atthasaṃhitau, adukkhau aisau  dhammau anupaghātau anupāyāsau apariḷāhau; sammāpaṭipadā| tasmā aisau dhammau araṇau|

340. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yamidaṃ taramānassa bhāsitaṃ, sadukkhau aisau dhammau saupaghātau saupāyāsau sapariḷāhau; micchāpaṭipadā| tasmā aisau dhammau saraṇau| tatra, bhikkhavai, yamidaṃ ataramānassa bhāsitaṃ, adukkhau aisau dhammau anupaghātau anupāyāsau apariḷāhau; sammāpaṭipadā| tasmā aisau dhammau araṇau|

341. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ janapadaniruttiyā ca abhinivaisau samañ‍ñāya ca atisārau, sadukkhau aisau dhammau saupaghātau saupāyāsau sapariḷāhau; micchāpaṭipadā| tasmā aisau dhammau saraṇau| tatra bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ janapadaniruttiyā ca anabhinivaisau samañ‍ñāya ca anatisārau, adukkhau aisau dhammau anupaghātau anupāyāsau apariḷāhau; sammāpaṭipadā| tasmā aisau dhammau araṇau|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘saraṇañ‍ca dhammaṃ jānissāma, araṇañ‍ca dhammaṃ jānissāma; saraṇañ‍ca dhammaṃ ñatvā araṇañ‍ca dhammaṃ ñatvā araṇapaṭipadaṃ paṭipaj‍jissāmā’ti aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabbaṃ| subhūti ca pana, bhikkhavai, kulaputtau araṇapaṭipadaṃ paṭipan‍nau’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

araṇavibhaṅgasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ navamaṃ|

10. dhātuvibhaṅgasuttaṃ

342. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā magadhaisu cārikaṃ caramānau yaina rājagahaṃ tadavasari; yaina bhaggavau kumbhakārau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhaggavaṃ kumbhakāraṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sacai tai, bhaggava, agaru viharaimu āvaisanai viharāmāvaisanai (sī॰ pī॰), viharāma nivaisanai (syā॰ kaṃ॰), viharaimu nivaisanai (ka॰) aikaratta’’nti| ‘‘na khau mai, bhantai, garu| atthi caittha pabbajitau paṭhamaṃ vāsūpagatau| sacai sau anujānāti, viharatha vihara (sī॰ pī॰), bhantai, yathāsukha’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina puk‍kusāti nāma kulaputtau bhagavantaṃ uddissa saddhāya agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau| sau tasmiṃ kumbhakārāvaisanai kumbhakāranivaisanai (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) paṭhamaṃ vāsūpagatau hauti| atha khau bhagavā yaināyasmā puk‍kusāti tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ puk‍kusātiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sacai tai, bhikkhu, agaru viharaimu āvaisanai aikaratta’’nti| ‘‘urundaṃ, āvusau ūrūndaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), urūddhaṃ (ka॰) dī॰ ni॰ 2 sak‍kapañhasuttaṭīkā aulaukaitabbā, kumbhakārāvaisanaṃ| viharatāyasmā yathāsukha’’nti|

atha khau bhagavā kumbhakārāvaisanaṃ pavisitvā aikamantaṃ tiṇasanthārakaṃ tiṇasantharikaṃ (sī॰), tiṇasantharakaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) pañ‍ñāpaitvā nisīdi pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| atha khau bhagavā bahudaiva rattiṃ  nisaj‍jāya vītināmaisi| āyasmāpi khau puk‍kusāti bahudaiva rattiṃ nisaj‍jāya vītināmaisi|

atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘pāsādikaṃ khau ayaṃ kulaputtau iriyati| yaṃnūnāhaṃ pucchaiyya’’nti| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ puk‍kusātiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kaṃsi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, uddissa pabbajitau? kau vā tai satthā? kassa vā tvaṃ dhammaṃ raucaisī’’ti? ‘‘atthāvusau, samaṇau gautamau sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau| taṃ khau pana bhagavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| tāhaṃ bhagavantaṃ uddissa pabbajitau| sau ca mai bhagavā satthā| tassa cāhaṃ bhagavatau  dhammaṃ raucaimī’’ti| ‘‘kahaṃ pana, bhikkhu, aitarahi sau bhagavā viharati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau’’ti| ‘‘atthāvusau, uttaraisu janapadaisu sāvatthi nāma nagaraṃ| tattha sau bhagavā aitarahi viharati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau’’ti| ‘‘diṭṭhapubbau pana tai, bhikkhu, sau bhagavā; disvā ca pana jānaiyyāsī’’ti? ‘‘na  khau mai, āvusau, diṭṭhapubbau sau bhagavā; disvā cāhaṃ na jānaiyya’’nti|

atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘mamañ‍ca khvāyaṃ maṃ tvāyaṃ (sī॰), mamaṃ khvāyaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰), maṃ khvāyaṃ (pī॰) kulaputtau uddissa pabbajitau| yaṃnūnassāhaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiyya’’nti| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ puk‍kusātiṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘dhammaṃ tai, bhikkhu, daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau āyasmā puk‍kusāti bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā  aitadavauca –

343. ‘‘‘chadhāturau chaddhāturau (sī॰) ayaṃ, bhikkhu, purisau chaphassāyatanau aṭṭhārasamanaupavicārau caturādhiṭṭhānau; yattha ṭhitaṃ mañ‍ñassavā nappavattanti, mañ‍ñassavai khau pana nappavattamānai muni santauti vuc‍cati| pañ‍ñaṃ nappamaj‍jaiyya, sac‍camanurakkhaiyya, cāgamanubrūhaiyya, santimaiva sau sikkhaiyyā’ti – ayamuddaisau dhātuvibhaṅgassa chadhātuvibhaṅgassa (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)|

344. ‘‘‘chadhāturau ayaṃ, bhikkhu, purisau’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? (chayimā, bhikkhu, dhātuyau) ( ) natthi sī॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu – pathavīdhātu, āpaudhātu, taijaudhātu, vāyaudhātu, ākāsadhātu, viñ‍ñāṇadhātu| ‘chadhāturau ayaṃ, bhikkhu, purisau’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

345. ‘‘‘chaphassāyatanau ayaṃ, bhikkhu, purisau’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? cakkhusamphassāyatanaṃ, sautasamphassāyatanaṃ, ghānasamphassāyatanaṃ, jivhāsamphassāyatanaṃ, kāyasamphassāyatanaṃ, manausamphassāyatanaṃ| ‘chaphassāyatanau ayaṃ, bhikkhu, purisau’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

346. ‘‘‘aṭṭhārasamanaupavicārau  ayaṃ, bhikkhu, purisau’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā saumanassaṭṭhānīyaṃ rūpaṃ upavicarati, daumanassaṭṭhānīyaṃ  rūpaṃ upavicarati, upaikkhāṭṭhānīyaṃ rūpaṃ upavicarati; sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… ghānaina gandhaṃ  ghāyitvā… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya saumanassaṭṭhānīyaṃ dhammaṃ upavicarati, daumanassaṭṭhānīyaṃ dhammaṃ upavicarati, upaikkhāṭṭhānīyaṃ dhammaṃ upavicarati – iti cha saumanassupavicārā, cha daumanassupavicārā, cha upaikkhupavicārā| ‘aṭṭhārasamanaupavicārau ayaṃ, bhikkhu, purisau’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

347. ‘‘‘caturādhiṭṭhānau ayaṃ, bhikkhu, purisau’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānau, sac‍cādhiṭṭhānau, cāgādhiṭṭhānau, upasamādhiṭṭhānau| ‘caturādhiṭṭhānau ayaṃ, bhikkhu, purisau’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

348. ‘‘‘pañ‍ñaṃ nappamaj‍jaiyya, sac‍camanurakkhaiyya, cāgamanubrūhaiyya, santimaiva sau sikkhaiyyā’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? kathañ‍ca, bhikkhu, pañ‍ñaṃ nappamaj‍jati? chayimā, bhikkhu, dhātuyau – pathavīdhātu, āpaudhātu, taijaudhātu, vāyaudhātu, ākāsadhātu, viñ‍ñāṇadhātu|

349. ‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhu, pathavīdhātu? pathavīdhātu siyā ajjhattikā siyā bāhirā| katamā ca, bhikkhu, ajjhattikā pathavīdhātu? yaṃ ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ kakkhaḷaṃ kharigataṃ upādin‍naṃ upādiṇṇaṃ (pī॰ ka॰), saiyyathidaṃ – kaisā laumā nakhā dantā tacau maṃsaṃ nhāru aṭṭhi aṭṭhimiñ‍jaṃ aṭṭhimiñ‍jā (sī॰ pī॰) vak‍kaṃ hadayaṃ yakanaṃ kilaumakaṃ pihakaṃ papphāsaṃ antaṃ antaguṇaṃ udariyaṃ karīsaṃ, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi kiñ‍ci ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ kakkhaḷaṃ kharigataṃ upādin‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhu, ajjhattikā pathavīdhātu| yā caiva khau pana ajjhattikā pathavīdhātu yā ca bāhirā pathavīdhātu pathavīdhāturaivaisā| ‘taṃ naitaṃ mama naisauhamasmi na maisau attā’ti – aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya  daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā pathavīdhātuyā nibbindati, pathavīdhātuyā cittaṃ virājaiti|

350. ‘‘katamā  ca, bhikkhu, āpaudhātu? āpaudhātu siyā ajjhattikā  siyā bāhirā| katamā ca, bhikkhu, ajjhattikā āpaudhātu? yaṃ ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ āpau āpaugataṃ upādin‍naṃ saiyyathidaṃ – pittaṃ saimhaṃ pubbau lauhitaṃ saidau maidau assu vasā khaiḷau siṅghāṇikā lasikā muttaṃ, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi kiñ‍ci ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ āpau āpaugataṃ upādin‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhu, ajjhattikā āpaudhātu| yā caiva khau pana ajjhattikā āpaudhātu yā ca bāhirā āpaudhātu āpaudhāturaivaisā| ‘taṃ naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti – aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā āpaudhātuyā nibbindati, āpaudhātuyā cittaṃ virājaiti|

351. ‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhu, taijaudhātu? taijaudhātu siyā ajjhattikā siyā bāhirā| katamā ca, bhikkhu, ajjhattikā taijaudhātu? yaṃ ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ taijau taijaugataṃ upādin‍naṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – yaina ca santappati, yaina ca jīrīyati, yaina ca pariḍayhati, yaina ca asitapītakhāyitasāyitaṃ sammā pariṇāmaṃ gacchati, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi kiñ‍ci ajjhattaṃ  pac‍cattaṃ taijau taijaugataṃ upādin‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhu, ajjhattikā taijaudhātu| yā caiva khau pana ajjhattikā taijaudhātu yā ca bāhirā taijaudhātu taijaudhāturaivaisā| ‘taṃ naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti – aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā taijaudhātuyā nibbindati, taijaudhātuyā cittaṃ virājaiti|

352. ‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhu, vāyaudhātu? vāyaudhātu siyā ajjhattikā siyā bāhirā| katamā ca, bhikkhu, ajjhattikā vāyaudhātu? yaṃ ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ vāyau vāyaugataṃ upādin‍naṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – uddhaṅgamā vātā adhaugamā vātā kucchisayā vātā kauṭṭhāsayā kauṭṭhasayā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vātā aṅgamaṅgānusārinau vātā assāsau passāsau iti, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi kiñ‍ci ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ vāyau vāyaugataṃ upādin‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhu, ajjhattikā vāyaudhātu| yā caiva khau pana ajjhattikā vāyaudhātu yā ca bāhirā vāyaudhātu vāyaudhāturaivaisā| ‘taṃ naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti – aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā vāyaudhātuyā nibbindati, vāyaudhātuyā cittaṃ virājaiti|

353. ‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhu, ākāsadhātu? ākāsadhātu siyā ajjhattikā siyā bāhirā| katamā ca, bhikkhu, ajjhattikā ākāsadhātu? yaṃ ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ ākāsaṃ ākāsagataṃ upādin‍naṃ, saiyyathidaṃ  – kaṇṇacchiddaṃ nāsacchiddaṃ mukhadvāraṃ yaina ca asitapītakhāyitasāyitaṃ ajjhauharati, yattha ca asitapītakhāyitasāyitaṃ santiṭṭhati, yaina ca asitapītakhāyitasāyitaṃ adhaubhāgaṃ adhaubhāgā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) daivadūtasuttaina samaiti nikkhamati, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi kiñ‍ci ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ ākāsaṃ ākāsagataṃ aghaṃ aghagataṃ vivaraṃ vivaragataṃ asamphuṭṭhaṃ maṃsalauhitaihi upādin‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati bhikkhu ajjhattikā ākāsadhātu| yā caiva khau pana ajjhattikā ākāsadhātu yā ca bāhirā ākāsadhātu ākāsadhāturaivaisā| ‘taṃ naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti – aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā ākāsadhātuyā nibbindati, ākāsadhātuyā cittaṃ virājaiti|

354. ‘‘athāparaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃyaiva avasissati parisuddhaṃ pariyaudātaṃ| taina ca viñ‍ñāṇaina kiṃ taina viñ‍ñāṇaina kiñ‍ca (sī॰) vijānāti? ‘sukha’ntipi vijānāti, ‘dukkha’ntipi vijānāti, ‘adukkhamasukha’ntipi vijānāti| sukhavaidaniyaṃ, bhikkhu, phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhā vaidanā| sau sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’ti pajānāti| ‘tassaiva sukhavaidaniyassa phassassa niraudhā yaṃ taj‍jaṃ vaidayitaṃ sukhavaidaniyaṃ phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍nā sukhā vaidanā sā nirujjhati, sā vūpasammatī’ti pajānāti|

355. ‘‘dukkhavaidaniyaṃ, bhikkhu, phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dukkhā vaidanā| sau dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’ti pajānāti| ‘tassaiva dukkhavaidaniyassa phassassa niraudhā yaṃ taj‍jaṃ vaidayitaṃ dukkhavaidaniyaṃ phassaṃ paṭic‍ca  uppan‍nā dukkhā vaidanā sā nirujjhati, sā vūpasammatī’ti pajānāti|

356. ‘‘adukkhamasukhavaidaniyaṃ, bhikkhu, phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati adukkhamasukhā vaidanā| sau adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’ti  pajānāti| ‘tassaiva adukkhamasukhavaidaniyassa  phassassa niraudhā yaṃ taj‍jaṃ vaidayitaṃ adukkhamasukhavaidaniyaṃ phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍nā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā sā nirujjhati, sā vūpasammatī’ti pajānāti|

357. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, dvin‍naṃ kaṭṭhānaṃ saṅghaṭṭā samphassa (sī॰ pī॰), saṅghaṭā (syā॰ kaṃ॰) samaudhānā usmā jāyati, taijau abhinibbattati, taisaṃyaiva dvin‍naṃ kaṭṭhānaṃ nānābhāvā vikkhaipā yā taj‍jā usmā sā nirujjhati, sā vūpasammati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhu, sukhavaidaniyaṃ  phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhā vaidanā| sau sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’ti pajānāti| ‘tassaiva sukhavaidaniyassa phassassa niraudhā yaṃ taj‍jaṃ vaidayitaṃ sukhavaidaniyaṃ phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍nā sukhā vaidanā sā nirujjhati, sā vūpasammatī’ti pajānāti|

358. ‘‘dukkhavaidaniyaṃ, bhikkhu, phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dukkhā vaidanā| sau dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’ti pajānāti| ‘tassaiva dukkhavaidaniyassa phassassa niraudhā yaṃ taj‍jaṃ vaidayitaṃ dukkhavaidaniyaṃ phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍nā dukkhā vaidanā sā nirujjhati, sā vūpasammatī’ti pajānāti|

359. ‘‘adukkhamasukhavaidaniyaṃ, bhikkhu, phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati adukkhamasukhā vaidanā| sau adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’ti pajānāti| ‘tassaiva adukkhamasukhavaidaniyassa phassassa niraudhā yaṃ taj‍jaṃ vaidayitaṃ adukkhamasukhavaidaniyaṃ phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍nā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā sā nirujjhati, sā vūpasammatī’ti pajānāti|

360. ‘‘athāparaṃ upaikkhāyaiva avasissati parisuddhā pariyaudātā mudu ca kammañ‍ñā ca pabhassarā ca| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, dakkhau suvaṇṇakārau vā suvaṇṇakārantaivāsī vā uk‍kaṃ bandhaiyya, uk‍kaṃ bandhitvā uk‍kāmukhaṃ ālimpaiyya, uk‍kāmukhaṃ ālimpaitvā saṇḍāsaina jātarūpaṃ gahaitvā uk‍kāmukhai pakkhipaiyya, tamainaṃ kālaina kālaṃ abhidhamaiyya, kālaina kālaṃ udakaina paripphausaiyya, kālaina kālaṃ ajjhupaikkhaiyya, taṃ hauti jātarūpaṃ jātarūpaṃ dhantaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) sudhantaṃ niddhantaṃ nīhaṭaṃ nihataṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) nin‍nītakasāvaṃ nihatakasāvaṃ (ka॰) mudu ca kammañ‍ñañ‍ca pabhassarañ‍ca, yassā yassā ca piḷandhanavikatiyā ākaṅkhati – yadi paṭṭikāya pavaṭṭikāya (sī॰ syā॰) yadi  kuṇḍalāya yadi gīvaiyyakāya yadi suvaṇṇamālāya tañ‍cassa atthaṃ  anubhauti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhu, athāparaṃ upaikkhāyaiva avasissati parisuddhā pariyaudātā mudu ca kammañ‍ñā ca pabhassarā ca|

361. ‘‘sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘imañ‍cai ahaṃ upaikkhaṃ aivaṃ parisuddhaṃ aivaṃ pariyaudātaṃ ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasaṃharaiyyaṃ, tadanudhammañ‍ca cittaṃ bhāvaiyyaṃ| aivaṃ mai ayaṃ upaikkhā taṃnissitā  tadupādānā ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ tiṭṭhaiyya| imañ‍cai ahaṃ upaikkhaṃ aivaṃ parisuddhaṃ aivaṃ pariyaudātaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasaṃharaiyyaṃ, tadanudhammañ‍ca cittaṃ bhāvaiyyaṃ| aivaṃ mai ayaṃ upaikkhā taṃnissitā tadupādānā ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ tiṭṭhaiyya| imañ‍cai ahaṃ  upaikkhaṃ aivaṃ parisuddhaṃ aivaṃ pariyaudātaṃ ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasaṃharaiyyaṃ, tadanudhammañ‍ca cittaṃ bhāvaiyyaṃ| aivaṃ mai ayaṃ upaikkhā taṃnissitā tadupādānā ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ tiṭṭhaiyya| imañ‍cai ahaṃ upaikkhaṃ aivaṃ parisuddhaṃ aivaṃ pariyaudātaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasaṃharaiyyaṃ, tadanudhammañ‍ca cittaṃ bhāvaiyyaṃ| aivaṃ mai ayaṃ upaikkhā taṃnissitā tadupādānā ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ tiṭṭhaiyyā’’’ti|

362. ‘‘sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘imañ‍cai ahaṃ upaikkhaṃ aivaṃ parisuddhaṃ aivaṃ pariyaudātaṃ ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasaṃharaiyyaṃ, tadanudhammañ‍ca cittaṃ bhāvaiyyaṃ; saṅkhatamaitaṃ| imañ‍cai ahaṃ upaikkhaṃ aivaṃ parisuddhaṃ aivaṃ pariyaudātaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasaṃharaiyyaṃ, tadanudhammañ‍ca cittaṃ bhāvaiyyaṃ; saṅkhatamaitaṃ| imañ‍cai ahaṃ upaikkhaṃ aivaṃ parisuddhaṃ aivaṃ pariyaudātaṃ ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasaṃharaiyyaṃ, tadanudhammañ‍ca cittaṃ bhāvaiyyaṃ; saṅkhatamaitaṃ| imañ‍cai ahaṃ upaikkhaṃ aivaṃ parisuddhaṃ aivaṃ pariyaudātaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasaṃharaiyyaṃ, tadanudhammañ‍ca cittaṃ bhāvaiyyaṃ; saṅkhatamaita’’’nti|

‘‘sau naiva taṃ abhisaṅkharauti, na abhisañ‍caitayati bhavāya vā vibhavāya vā| sau anabhisaṅkharauntau anabhisañ‍caitayantau bhavāya vā vibhavāya vā na kiñ‍ci  laukai upādiyati, anupādiyaṃ na paritassati, aparitassaṃ pac‍cattaṃyaiva parinibbāyati| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti|

363. ‘‘sau sukhañ‍cai vaidanaṃ vaidaiti, ‘sā anic‍cā’ti pajānāti, ‘anajjhausitā’ti pajānāti, ‘anabhinanditā’ti pajānāti| dukkhañ‍cai vaidanaṃ vaidaiti, ‘sā anic‍cā’ti pajānāti, ‘anajjhausitā’ti  pajānāti, ‘anabhinanditā’ti pajānāti| adukkhamasukhañ‍cai vaidanaṃ vaidaiti, ‘sā anic‍cā’ti pajānāti, ‘anajjhausitā’ti pajānāti, ‘anabhinanditā’ti pajānāti|

364. ‘‘sau sukhañ‍cai vaidanaṃ vaidaiti, visaṃyuttau naṃ vaidaiti; dukkhañ‍cai vaidanaṃ vaidaiti, visaṃyuttau naṃ vaidaiti; adukkhamasukhañ‍cai vaidanaṃ vaidaiti, visaṃyuttau naṃ vaidaiti| sau kāyapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘kāyapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’ti pajānāti, jīvitapariyantikaṃ  vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘jīvitapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’ti pajānāti, ‘kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā uddhaṃ jīvitapariyādānā idhaiva sabbavaidayitāni anabhinanditāni sītībhavissantī’ti pajānāti|

365. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, tailañ‍ca paṭic‍ca vaṭṭiñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tailappadīpau jhāyati; tassaiva tailassa ca vaṭṭiyā ca pariyādānā añ‍ñassa ca anupahārā anupāhārā (sī॰ pī॰), anupādānā (ka॰) anāhārau nibbāyati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhu, kāyapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘kāyapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’ti pajānāti, jīvitapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘jīvitapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’ti pajānāti, ‘kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā uddhaṃ jīvitapariyādānā idhaiva sabbavaidayitāni  anabhinanditāni sītībhavissantī’ti pajānāti| tasmā aivaṃ saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu iminā paramaina pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānaina saman‍nāgatau hauti| aisā hi, bhikkhu, paramā ariyā pañ‍ñā yadidaṃ – sabbadukkhakkhayai ñāṇaṃ|

366. ‘‘tassa sā vimutti sac‍cai ṭhitā akuppā hauti| tañhi, bhikkhu, musā yaṃ mausadhammaṃ, taṃ sac‍caṃ yaṃ amausadhammaṃ nibbānaṃ| tasmā aivaṃ saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu iminā paramaina sac‍cādhiṭṭhānaina saman‍nāgatau hauti| aitañhi, bhikkhu, paramaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ yadidaṃ – amausadhammaṃ nibbānaṃ|

367. ‘‘tassaiva khau pana pubbai aviddasunau upadhī haunti samattā samādin‍nā| tyāssa pahīnā haunti ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| tasmā aivaṃ saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu  iminā paramaina cāgādhiṭṭhānaina saman‍nāgatau hauti| aisau hi, bhikkhu, paramau ariyau cāgau yadidaṃ – sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau|

368. ‘‘tassaiva khau pana pubbai aviddasunau abhijjhā hauti chandau sārāgau| svāssa  pahīnau hauti ucchin‍namūlau tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṃkatau āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau| tassaiva khau pana pubbai aviddasunau āghātau hauti byāpādau sampadausau| svāssa pahīnau hauti ucchin‍namūlau  tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṃkatau āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau| tassaiva khau pana pubbai aviddasunau avij‍jā hauti sammauhau| svāssa pahīnau hauti ucchin‍namūlau tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṃkatau āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau| tasmā aivaṃ  saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu iminā paramaina upasamādhiṭṭhānaina saman‍nāgatau hauti| aisau hi, bhikkhu, paramau ariyau upasamau yadidaṃ – rāgadausamauhānaṃ upasamau| ‘pañ‍ñaṃ nappamaj‍jaiyya, sac‍camanurakkhaiyya, cāgamanubrūhaiyya, santimaiva sau sikkhaiyyā’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

369. ‘‘‘yattha ṭhitaṃ mañ‍ñassavā nappavattanti, mañ‍ñassavai khau pana nappavattamānai muni santauti vuc‍catī’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? ‘asmī’ti, bhikkhu, mañ‍ñitamaitaṃ, ‘ayamahamasmī’ti mañ‍ñitamaitaṃ, ‘bhavissa’nti mañ‍ñitamaitaṃ, ‘na bhavissa’nti mañ‍ñitamaitaṃ, ‘rūpī bhavissa’nti mañ‍ñitamaitaṃ, ‘arūpī bhavissa’nti mañ‍ñitamaitaṃ, ‘sañ‍ñī bhavissa’nti mañ‍ñitamaitaṃ, ‘asañ‍ñī bhavissa’nti mañ‍ñitamaitaṃ, ‘naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñī bhavissa’nti mañ‍ñitamaitaṃ| mañ‍ñitaṃ, bhikkhu, raugau mañ‍ñitaṃ gaṇḍau mañ‍ñitaṃ sal‍laṃ| sabbamañ‍ñitānaṃ tvaiva, bhikkhu, samatik‍kamā muni santauti vuc‍cati| muni khau pana, bhikkhu, santau na jāyati, na jīyati, na mīyati, na kuppati, na pihaiti| tañhissa, bhikkhu, natthi yaina jāyaitha, ajāyamānau kiṃ jīyissati, ajīyamānau kiṃ mīyissati, amīyamānau kiṃ kuppissati, akuppamānau kissa kiṃ (ka॰) pihaissati? ‘yattha ṭhitaṃ mañ‍ñassavā nappavattanti, mañ‍ñassavai khau pana nappavattamānai muni santauti vuc‍catī’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ| imaṃ khau mai tvaṃ, bhikkhu, saṃkhittaina chadhātuvibhaṅgaṃ dhāraihī’’ti|

370. atha  khau āyasmā puk‍kusāti – ‘‘satthā kira mai anuppattau, sugatau kira mai anuppattau sammāsambuddhau kira mai anuppattau’’ti uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ cīvaraṃ katvā bhagavatau pādaisu sirasā nipatitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ac‍cayau maṃ, bhantai, ac‍cagamā yathābālaṃ yathāmūḷhaṃ yathāakusalaṃ, yauhaṃ bhagavantaṃ āvusauvādaina samudācaritabbaṃ amañ‍ñissaṃ| tassa mai, bhantai, bhagavā ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau paṭiggaṇhātu āyatiṃ saṃvarāyā’’ti| ‘‘taggha tvaṃ, bhikkhu, ac‍cayau ac‍cagamā yathābālaṃ yathāmūḷhaṃ yathāakusalaṃ, yaṃ maṃ tvaṃ āvusauvādaina  samudācaritabbaṃ amañ‍ñittha| yatau ca khau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau disvā yathādhammaṃ paṭikarausi, taṃ tai mayaṃ paṭiggaṇhāma| vuddhihaisā, bhikkhu, ariyassa vinayai yau ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau disvā yathādhammaṃ paṭikarauti, āyatiṃ saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jatī’’ti| ‘‘labhaiyyāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau santikai upasampada’’nti| ‘‘paripuṇṇaṃ pana tai, bhikkhu, pattacīvara’’nti? ‘‘na khau mai, bhantai, paripuṇṇaṃ pattacīvara’’nti| ‘‘na khau, bhikkhu, tathāgatā aparipuṇṇapattacīvaraṃ upasampādaintī’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā puk‍kusāti bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pattacīvarapariyaisanaṃ pak‍kāmi| atha khau āyasmantaṃ puk‍kusātiṃ pattacīvarapariyaisanaṃ carantaṃ vibbhantā gāvī bhantagāvī (sī॰ pī॰), gāvī (syā॰ kaṃ॰) jīvitā vauraupaisi| atha khau sambahulā  bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘yau sau, bhantai, puk‍kusāti nāma kulaputtau bhagavatā saṃkhittaina auvādaina auvaditau sau kālaṅkatau| tassa kā gati, kau abhisamparāyau’’ti? ‘‘paṇḍitau, bhikkhavai, puk‍kusāti kulaputtau pac‍capādi dhammassānudhammaṃ, na ca maṃ dhammādhikaraṇaṃ vihaisaisi vihaiṭhaisi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) vihaisaiti (ka॰)| puk‍kusāti, bhikkhavai, kulaputtau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikau tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

dhātuvibhaṅgasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dasamaṃ|

11. sac‍cavibhaṅgasuttaṃ

371. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā bārāṇasiyaṃ viharati isipatanai migadāyai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘tathāgataina, bhikkhavai, arahatā sammāsambuddhaina bārāṇasiyaṃ isipatanai migadāyai anuttaraṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattitaṃ appaṭivattiyaṃ samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā kainaci vā laukasmiṃ, yadidaṃ – catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ ācikkhanā daisanā pañ‍ñāpanā paṭṭhapanā vivaraṇā vibhajanā uttānīkammaṃ| katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? dukkhassa ariyasac‍cassa ācikkhanā daisanā pañ‍ñāpanā paṭṭhapanā vivaraṇā vibhajanā uttānīkammaṃ, dukkhasamudayassa ariyasac‍cassa ācikkhanā daisanā pañ‍ñāpanā paṭṭhapanā vivaraṇā vibhajanā uttānīkammaṃ, dukkhaniraudhassa ariyasac‍cassa ācikkhanā daisanā pañ‍ñāpanā paṭṭhapanā vivaraṇā vibhajanā uttānīkammaṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ariyasac‍cassa ācikkhanā daisanā pañ‍ñāpanā paṭṭhapanā vivaraṇā vibhajanā uttānīkammaṃ| tathāgataina, bhikkhavai, arahatā sammāsambuddhaina bārāṇasiyaṃ isipatanai migadāyai anuttaraṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattitaṃ appaṭivattiyaṃ samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā kainaci vā laukasmiṃ, yadidaṃ – imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ ācikkhanā daisanā pañ‍ñāpanā paṭṭhapanā vivaraṇā vibhajanā uttānīkammaṃ|

‘‘saivatha, bhikkhavai, sāriputtamauggal‍lānai; bhajatha, bhikkhavai, sāriputtamauggal‍lānai| paṇḍitā bhikkhū anuggāhakā sabrahmacārīnaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, janaitā janaitti (sī॰ pī॰), aivaṃ sāriputtau; saiyyathāpi jātassa āpādaitā, aivaṃ mauggal‍lānau| sāriputtau, bhikkhavai, sautāpattiphalai vinaiti, mauggal‍lānau uttamatthai| sāriputtau, bhikkhavai, pahauti cattāri ariyasac‍cāni vitthāraina ācikkhituṃ daisaituṃ pañ‍ñāpaituṃ paṭṭhapaituṃ vivarituṃ vibhajituṃ uttānīkātu’’nti| idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ pāvisi|

372. tatra  khau āyasmā sāriputtau acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau, bhikkhavai’’ti| ‘‘āvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū  āyasmatau sāriputtassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā sāriputtau aitadavauca –

‘‘tathāgataina, āvusau, arahatā sammāsambuddhaina bārāṇasiyaṃ isipatanai migadāyai anuttaraṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattitaṃ appaṭivattiyaṃ samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā kainaci vā laukasmiṃ, yadidaṃ – catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ ācikkhanā daisanā pañ‍ñāpanā paṭṭhapanā vivaraṇā vibhajanā uttānīkammaṃ| katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? dukkhassa ariyasac‍cassa ācikkhanā daisanā pañ‍ñāpanā paṭṭhapanā vivaraṇā vibhajanā uttānīkammaṃ, dukkhasamudayassa ariyasac‍cassa  ācikkhanā daisanā pañ‍ñāpanā paṭṭhapanā vivaraṇā vibhajanā uttānīkammaṃ, dukkhaniraudhassa ariyasac‍cassa ācikkhanā daisanā pañ‍ñāpanā paṭṭhapanā vivaraṇā vibhajanā uttānīkammaṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ariyasac‍cassa ācikkhanā daisanā pañ‍ñāpanā paṭṭhapanā vivaraṇā vibhajanā uttānīkammaṃ|

373. ‘‘katamañ‍cāvusau, dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ? jātipi dukkhā, jarāpi dukkhā, maraṇampi dukkhaṃ, saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsāpi dukkhā, yampicchaṃ na labhati tampi dukkhaṃ; saṃkhittaina pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā dukkhā|

‘‘katamā cāvusau, jāti? yā taisaṃ taisaṃ sattānaṃ tamhi tamhi sattanikāyai jāti sañ‍jāti auk‍kanti abhinibbatti khandhānaṃ pātubhāvau āyatanānaṃ paṭilābhau, ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau – ‘jāti’’’|

‘‘katamā cāvusau, jarā? yā taisaṃ taisaṃ sattānaṃ tamhi tamhi sattanikāyai jarā jīraṇatā khaṇḍic‍caṃ pālic‍caṃ valittacatā āyunau saṃhāni indriyānaṃ paripākau, ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau – ‘jarā’’’|

‘‘katamañ‍cāvusau, maraṇaṃ? yā taisaṃ taisaṃ sattānaṃ tamhā tamhā sattanikāyā cuti cavanatā  bhaidau antaradhānaṃ mac‍cu maraṇaṃ kālaṃkiriyā khandhānaṃ bhaidau kaḷaivarassa nikkhaipau jīvitindriyassupacchaidau, idaṃ vuc‍catāvusau – ‘maraṇaṃ’’’|

‘‘katamau cāvusau, saukau? yau khau, āvusau, añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina byasanaina saman‍nāgatassa añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina dukkhadhammaina phuṭṭhassa saukau saucanā saucitattaṃ antausaukau antauparisaukau, ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau  – ‘saukau’’’|

‘‘katamau  cāvusau, paridaivau? yau khau, āvusau, añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina byasanaina saman‍nāgatassa añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina dukkhadhammaina phuṭṭhassa ādaivau paridaivau ādaivanā  paridaivanā ādaivitattaṃ paridaivitattaṃ, ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau – ‘paridaivau’’’|

‘‘katamañ‍cāvusau, dukkhaṃ? yaṃ khau, āvusau, kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ kāyikaṃ asātaṃ kāyasamphassajaṃ dukkhaṃ asātaṃ vaidayitaṃ, idaṃ vuc‍catāvusau – ‘dukkhaṃ’’’|

‘‘katamañ‍cāvusau, daumanassaṃ? yaṃ khau, āvusau, caitasikaṃ dukkhaṃ caitasikaṃ asātaṃ manausamphassajaṃ dukkhaṃ asātaṃ vaidayitaṃ, idaṃ vuc‍catāvusau – ‘daumanassaṃ’’’|

‘‘katamau cāvusau, upāyāsau? yau khau, āvusau, añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina byasanaina saman‍nāgatassa añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina dukkhadhammaina phuṭṭhassa āyāsau upāyāsau āyāsitattaṃ upāyāsitattaṃ, ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau – ‘upāyāsau’’’|

‘‘katamañ‍cāvusau, yampicchaṃ na labhati tampi dukkhaṃ? jātidhammānaṃ, āvusau, sattānaṃ aivaṃ icchā uppaj‍jati – ‘ahau vata, mayaṃ na jātidhammā assāma; na ca, vata, nau jāti āgacchaiyyā’ti| na khau panaitaṃ icchāya pattabbaṃ| idampi – ‘yampicchaṃ na labhati tampi dukkhaṃ’| jarādhammānaṃ, āvusau, sattānaṃ…pai॰… byādhidhammānaṃ, āvusau, sattānaṃ… maraṇadhammānaṃ, āvusau, sattānaṃ… saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsadhammānaṃ, āvusau, sattānaṃ aivaṃ icchā uppaj‍jati – ‘ahau vata, mayaṃ na saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsadhammā assāma; na ca, vata, nau saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā āgacchaiyyu’nti| na khau panaitaṃ icchāya pattabbaṃ| idampi – ‘yampicchaṃ na labhati tampi dukkhaṃ’’’|

‘‘katamai cāvusau, saṃkhittaina pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā dukkhā? saiyyathidaṃ – rūpupādānakkhandhau, vaidanupādānakkhandhau, sañ‍ñupādānakkhandhau, saṅkhārupādānakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau| imai vuc‍cantāvusau – ‘saṃkhittaina pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā dukkhā’| idaṃ vuc‍catāvusau – ‘dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ’’’|

374. ‘‘katamañ‍cāvusau, dukkhasamudayaṃ dukkhasamudayau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) ariyasac‍caṃ? yāyaṃ taṇhā paunaubbhavikā paunaubhavikā (sī॰ pī॰) nandīrāgasahagatā nandirāgasahagatā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) tatratatrābhinandinī, saiyyathidaṃ – kāmataṇhā  bhavataṇhā vibhavataṇhā, idaṃ vuc‍catāvusau – ‘dukkhasamudayaṃ dukkhasamudayau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) ariyasac‍caṃ’’’|

‘‘katamañ‍cāvusau, dukkhaniraudhaṃ dukkhaniraudhau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) ariyasac‍caṃ? yau tassāyaiva taṇhāya asaisavirāganiraudhau cāgau paṭinissaggau mutti anālayau, idaṃ vuc‍catāvusau – ‘dukkhaniraudhaṃ dukkhaniraudhau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) ariyasac‍caṃ’’’|

375. ‘‘katamañ‍cāvusau, dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvācā, sammākammantau, sammāājīvau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi|

‘‘katamācāvusau, sammādiṭṭhi? yaṃ khau, āvusau, dukkhai ñāṇaṃ, dukkhasamudayai ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhai ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ñāṇaṃ, ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau – ‘sammādiṭṭhi’’’|

‘‘katamau cāvusau, sammāsaṅkappau? naikkhammasaṅkappau, abyāpādasaṅkappau, avihiṃsāsaṅkappau, ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau – ‘sammāsaṅkappau’’’|

‘‘katamā cāvusau, sammāvācā? musāvādā vairamaṇī, pisuṇāya vācāya vairamaṇī, pharusāya vācāya vairamaṇī, samphappalāpā vairamaṇī, ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau – ‘sammāvācā’’’|

‘‘katamau  cāvusau, sammākammantau? pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī, adin‍nādānā vairamaṇī, kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇī, ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau – ‘sammākammantau’’’|

‘‘katamau cāvusau, sammāājīvau? idhāvusau, ariyasāvakau micchāājīvaṃ pahāya sammāājīvaina jīvikaṃ kappaiti, ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau – ‘sammāājīvau’’’|

‘‘katamau cāvusau, sammāvāyāmau? idhāvusau, bhikkhu anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ  uppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā  asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau – ‘sammāvāyāmau’’’|

‘‘katamā cāvusau, sammāsati? idhāvusau, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharati…pai॰… cittai cittānupassī viharati… dhammaisu dhammānupassī  viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ, ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau – ‘sammāsati’’’|

‘‘katamau cāvusau, sammāsamādhi? idhāvusau, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…  viharati, ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau – ‘sammāsamādhi’| idaṃ vuc‍catāvusau – ‘dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ’’’|

‘‘tathāgataināvusau, arahatā sammāsambuddhaina bārāṇasiyaṃ isipatanai migadāyai anuttaraṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattitaṃ appaṭivattiyaṃ samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā kainaci vā laukasmiṃ, yadidaṃ – imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ ācikkhanā daisanā pañ‍ñāpanā paṭṭhapanā vivaraṇā vibhajanā uttānīkamma’’nti|

idamavauca āyasmā sāriputtau| attamanā tai bhikkhū āyasmatau sāriputtassa bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

sac‍cavibhaṅgasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ aikādasamaṃ|

12. dakkhiṇāvibhaṅgasuttaṃ

376. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati kapilavatthusmiṃ nigraudhārāmai| atha khau mahāpajāpati mahāpajāpatī (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) gautamī navaṃ dussayugaṃ ādāya yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā  aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau mahāpajāpati gautamī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idaṃ mai, bhantai, navaṃ dussayugaṃ bhagavantaṃ uddissa sāmaṃ kantaṃ sāmaṃ vāyitaṃ| taṃ mai, bhantai, bhagavā paṭiggaṇhātu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, bhagavā mahāpajāpatiṃ gautamiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘saṅghai, gautami, daihi| saṅghai tai din‍nai ahañ‍caiva pūjitau bhavissāmi saṅghau cā’’ti| dutiyampi khau mahāpajāpati gautamī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idaṃ mai, bhantai, navaṃ dussayugaṃ bhagavantaṃ uddissa sāmaṃ kantaṃ sāmaṃ vāyitaṃ| taṃ mai, bhantai, bhagavā paṭiggaṇhātu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’ti| dutiyampi khau bhagavā mahāpajāpatiṃ gautamiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘saṅghai, gautami, daihi| saṅghai tai din‍nai ahañ‍caiva pūjitau bhavissāmi saṅghau cā’’ti| tatiyampi khau mahāpajāpati gautamī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idaṃ mai, bhantai, navaṃ dussayugaṃ bhagavantaṃ uddissa sāmaṃ kantaṃ sāmaṃ vāyitaṃ| taṃ mai, bhantai, bhagavā paṭiggaṇhātu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’ti| tatiyampi khau bhagavā mahāpajāpatiṃ gautamiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘saṅghai, gautami, daihi| saṅghai tai din‍nai ahañ‍caiva pūjitau bhavissāmi saṅghau cā’’ti|

377. aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘paṭiggaṇhātu, bhantai, bhagavā mahāpajāpatiyā gautamiyā navaṃ dussayugaṃ| bahūpakārā bahukārā (syā॰ kaṃ॰), bhantai, mahāpajāpati gautamī bhagavatau mātucchā āpādikā pausikā khīrassa dāyikā; bhagavantaṃ janaittiyā kālaṅkatāya thañ‍ñaṃ pāyaisi| bhagavāpi, bhantai, bahūpakārau mahāpajāpatiyā gautamiyā| bhagavantaṃ, bhantai, āgamma mahāpajāpati gautamī buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gatā, dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gatā, saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gatā| bhagavantaṃ, bhantai, āgamma mahāpajāpati gautamī pāṇātipātā paṭiviratā adin‍nādānā paṭiviratā kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratā musāvādā paṭiviratā surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratā| bhagavantaṃ, bhantai, āgamma mahāpajāpati gautamī buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatā, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatā, saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatā ariyakantaihi  sīlaihi saman‍nāgatā| bhagavantaṃ, bhantai, āgamma mahāpajāpati gautamī dukkhai nik‍kaṅkhā, dukkhasamudayai nik‍kaṅkhā, dukkhaniraudhai nik‍kaṅkhā, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya nik‍kaṅkhā| bhagavāpi, bhantai, bahūpakārau mahāpajāpatiyā gautamiyā’’ti|

378. ‘‘aivamaitaṃ, ānanda| yaṃ hānanda, puggalau puggalaṃ āgamma buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau hauti, dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau hauti, saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau hauti, imassānanda, puggalassa iminā puggalaina na suppatikāraṃ vadāmi, yadidaṃ – abhivādana-paccuṭṭhāna-añjalikamma- sāmīcikamma-cīvara-piṇḍapāta-saināsana-gilānappaccayabhaisajjaparikkhārānuppadānaina|

‘‘yaṃ hānanda, puggalau puggalaṃ āgamma pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti, musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau hauti, imassānanda, puggalassa iminā puggalaina na suppatikāraṃ vadāmi, yadidaṃ – abhivādana-paccuṭṭhāna-añjalikamma-sāmīcikamma-cīvara-piṇḍapāta-saināsana-gilānappaccayabhaisajjaparikkhārānuppadānaina|

‘‘yaṃ hānanda, puggalau puggalaṃ āgamma buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, dhammai… saṅghai… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti, imassānanda, puggalassa iminā puggalaina na suppatikāraṃ vadāmi, yadidaṃ – abhivādana-paccuṭṭhāna-añjalikamma-sāmīcikamma-cīvara-piṇḍapāta-saināsana-gilānappaccayabhaisajjaparikkhārānuppadānaina|

‘‘yaṃ hānanda, puggalau puggalaṃ āgamma dukkhai nik‍kaṅkhau hauti, dukkhasamudayai nik‍kaṅkhau hauti, dukkhaniraudhai nik‍kaṅkhau hauti, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya nik‍kaṅkhau hauti, imassānanda, puggalassa iminā puggalaina na suppatikāraṃ vadāmi, yadidaṃ – abhivādana-paccuṭṭhāna-añjalikamma-sāmīcikamma-cīvara-piṇḍapāta-saināsana-gilānappaccayabhaisajjaparikkhārānuppadānaina|

379. ‘‘cuddasa khau panimānanda, pāṭipuggalikā dakkhiṇā| katamā cuddasa? tathāgatai  arahantai sammāsambuddhai dānaṃ daiti – ayaṃ paṭhamā pāṭipuggalikā dakkhiṇā| pac‍caikasambuddhai pac‍caikabuddhai (sī॰ pī॰) dānaṃ  daiti – ayaṃ dutiyā pāṭipuggalikā dakkhiṇā| tathāgatasāvakai arahantai dānaṃ daiti – ayaṃ tatiyā pāṭipuggalikā dakkhiṇā| arahattaphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nai dānaṃ daiti – ayaṃ catutthī pāṭipuggalikā dakkhiṇā| anāgāmissa dānaṃ daiti – ayaṃ pañ‍camī pāṭipuggalikā dakkhiṇā| anāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya  paṭipan‍nai dānaṃ daiti – ayaṃ chaṭṭhī pāṭipuggalikā dakkhiṇā| sakadāgāmissa dānaṃ daiti – ayaṃ sattamī pāṭipuggalikā  dakkhiṇā| sakadāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nai dānaṃ daiti – ayaṃ aṭṭhamī pāṭipuggalikā dakkhiṇā| sautāpan‍nai dānaṃ daiti – ayaṃ navamī pāṭipuggalikā dakkhiṇā| sautāpattiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nai dānaṃ daiti – ayaṃ dasamī pāṭipuggalikā dakkhiṇā| bāhirakai kāmaisu vītarāgai dānaṃ daiti – ayaṃ aikādasamī pāṭipuggalikā dakkhiṇā| puthuj‍janasīlavantai dānaṃ daiti – ayaṃ dvādasamī pāṭipuggalikā dakkhiṇā| puthuj‍janadussīlai dānaṃ daiti – ayaṃ tairasamī pāṭipuggalikā dakkhiṇā| tiracchānagatai dānaṃ daiti – ayaṃ cuddasamī pāṭipuggalikā dakkhiṇāti|

‘‘tatrānanda, tiracchānagatai dānaṃ datvā sataguṇā dakkhiṇā pāṭikaṅkhitabbā, puthuj‍janadussīlai dānaṃ datvā sahassaguṇā dakkhiṇā pāṭikaṅkhitabbā, puthuj‍janasīlavantai dānaṃ datvā satasahassaguṇā dakkhiṇā  pāṭikaṅkhitabbā, bāhirakai kāmaisu vītarāgai dānaṃ datvā kauṭisatasahassaguṇā dakkhiṇā pāṭikaṅkhitabbā, sautāpattiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nai dānaṃ datvā asaṅkhaiyyā appamaiyyā dakkhiṇā pāṭikaṅkhitabbā, kau pana vādau sautāpan‍nai, kau pana vādau sakadāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nai, kau pana vādau sakadāgāmissa, kau pana vādau anāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nai, kau pana vādau anāgāmissa, kau pana vādau arahattaphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nai, kau pana vādau arahantai, kau pana vādau pac‍caikasambuddhai, kau pana vādau tathāgatai arahantai sammāsambuddhai!

380. ‘‘satta khau panimānanda, saṅghagatā dakkhiṇā| katamā satta? buddhappamukhai ubhatausaṅghai dānaṃ daiti – ayaṃ paṭhamā saṅghagatā dakkhiṇā| tathāgatai parinibbutai ubhatausaṅghai dānaṃ daiti – ayaṃ dutiyā saṅghagatā dakkhiṇā| bhikkhusaṅghai dānaṃ daiti – ayaṃ tatiyā saṅghagatā dakkhiṇā| bhikkhunisaṅghai dānaṃ daiti – ayaṃ catutthī saṅghagatā dakkhiṇā| ‘aittakā mai bhikkhū ca bhikkhuniyau ca  saṅghatau uddissathā’ti dānaṃ daiti – ayaṃ pañ‍camī saṅghagatā dakkhiṇā| ‘aittakā  mai bhikkhū saṅghatau uddissathā’ti dānaṃ daiti – ayaṃ chaṭṭhī saṅghagatā dakkhiṇā| ‘aittakā mai bhikkhuniyau saṅghatau uddissathā’ti dānaṃ daiti – ayaṃ sattamī saṅghagatā dakkhiṇā|

‘‘bhavissanti  khau panānanda, anāgatamaddhānaṃ gautrabhunau kāsāvakaṇṭhā  dussīlā pāpadhammā| taisu dussīlaisu saṅghaṃ uddissa dānaṃ dassanti| tadāpāhaṃ, ānanda, saṅghagataṃ dakkhiṇaṃ asaṅkhaiyyaṃ appamaiyyaṃ vadāmi| na tvaivāhaṃ, ānanda, kainaci pariyāyaina saṅghagatāya dakkhiṇāya pāṭipuggalikaṃ dānaṃ mahapphalataraṃ vadāmi|

381. ‘‘catassau khau imā, ānanda, dakkhiṇā visuddhiyau| katamā catassau? atthānanda, dakkhiṇā dāyakatau visujjhati nau paṭiggāhakatau| atthānanda, dakkhiṇā paṭiggāhakatau visujjhati nau dāyakatau| atthānanda, dakkhiṇā naiva dāyakatau visujjhati nau paṭiggāhakatau| atthānanda, dakkhiṇā dāyakatau caiva visujjhati paṭiggāhakatau ca|

‘‘kathañ‍cānanda, dakkhiṇā dāyakatau visujjhati nau paṭiggāhakatau? idhānanda, dāyakau hauti sīlavā kalyāṇadhammau, paṭiggāhakā haunti dussīlā pāpadhammā – aivaṃ khau, ānanda, dakkhiṇā dāyakatau visujjhati nau paṭiggāhakatau|

‘‘kathañ‍cānanda, dakkhiṇā paṭiggāhakatau visujjhati nau dāyakatau? idhānanda, dāyakau hauti dussīlau pāpadhammau, paṭiggāhakā haunti sīlavantau sīlavantā (sī॰) kalyāṇadhammā – aivaṃ khau, ānanda, dakkhiṇā paṭiggāhakatau visujjhati nau dāyakatau|

‘‘kathañ‍cānanda, dakkhiṇā naiva dāyakatau visujjhati nau paṭiggāhakatau? idhānanda, dāyakau ca hauti dussīlau pāpadhammau, paṭiggāhakā  ca haunti dussīlā pāpadhammā – aivaṃ khau, ānanda, dakkhiṇā naiva dāyakatau visujjhati nau paṭiggāhakatau|

‘‘kathañ‍cānanda, dakkhiṇā dāyakatau caiva visujjhati paṭiggāhakatau ca? idhānanda, dāyakau ca hauti sīlavā kalyāṇadhammau, paṭiggāhakā ca haunti sīlavantau kalyāṇadhammā  – aivaṃ  khau, ānanda, dakkhiṇā dāyakatau caiva visujjhati paṭiggāhakatau ca| imā khau, ānanda, catassau dakkhiṇā visuddhiyau’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

382.

 ‘‘yau  sīlavā dussīlaisu dadāti dānaṃ,

dhammaina laddhaṃ laddhā (sī॰ pī॰) supasan‍nacittau|

abhisaddahaṃ kammaphalaṃ uḷāraṃ,

sā dakkhiṇā dāyakatau visujjhati||

‘‘yau dussīlau sīlavantaisu dadāti dānaṃ,

adhammaina laddhaṃ appasan‍nacittau|

anabhisaddahaṃ kammaphalaṃ uḷāraṃ,

sā dakkhiṇā paṭiggāhakatau visujjhati||

‘‘yau dussīlau dussīlaisu dadāti dānaṃ,

adhammaina laddhaṃ appasan‍nacittau|

anabhisaddahaṃ  kammaphalaṃ uḷāraṃ,

na taṃ dānaṃ vipulapphalanti brūmi||

‘‘yau sīlavā sīlavantaisu dadāti dānaṃ,

dhammaina laddhaṃ supasan‍nacittau|

abhisaddahaṃ kammaphalaṃ uḷāraṃ,

taṃ vai dānaṃ vipulapphalanti brūmi sā dakkhiṇā naivubhatau visujjhati (sī॰ pī॰)||

‘‘yau vītarāgau vītarāgaisu dadāti dānaṃ,

dhammaina laddhaṃ supasan‍nacittau|

abhisaddahaṃ  kammaphalaṃ uḷāraṃ,

taṃ vai dānaṃ āmisadānānamagga’’ taṃ vai dānaṃ vipulanti brūmi (sī॰) nti||

dakkhiṇāvibhaṅgasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dvādasamaṃ|

vibhaṅgavaggau niṭṭhitau catutthau|


tassuddānaṃ –

bhaddaikānandakac‍cāna, laumasakaṅgiyāsubhau|

mahākammasaḷāyatanavibhaṅgā, uddaisaaraṇā dhātu sac‍caṃ||

dakkhiṇāvibhaṅgasuttanti|


5. saḷāyatanavaggau

1. anāthapiṇḍikauvādasuttaṃ

383. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati ābādhikau hauti dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati añ‍ñataraṃ purisaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkama; upasaṅkamitvā mama vacanaina bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandāhi vandāhi aivañ‍ca vadaihi (sabbattha) añ‍ñasuttaisu pana natthi – ‘anāthapiṇḍikau, bhantai, gahapati ābādhikau dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| sau bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandatī’ti| yaina cāyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkama; upasaṅkamitvā mama vacanaina āyasmatau sāriputtassa pādai sirasā vandāhi vandāhi aivañ‍ca vadaihi (sabbattha) añ‍ñasuttaisu pana natthi – ‘anāthapiṇḍikau, bhantai, gahapati ābādhikau dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| sau āyasmatau sāriputtassa pādai sirasā vandatī’ti| aivañ‍ca vadaihi – ‘sādhu kira, bhantai, āyasmā sāriputtau yaina anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkamatu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’’ti|

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau sau purisau anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau purisau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘anāthapiṇḍikau, bhantai, gahapati ābādhikau dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| sau bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandatī’’ti| yaina cāyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nau khau sau purisau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘anāthapiṇḍikau, bhantai, gahapati ābādhikau dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| sau āyasmatau sāriputtassa pādai sirasā vandati; aivañ‍ca vadaiti – ‘sādhu kira, bhantai, āyasmā sāriputtau yaina anāthapiṇḍikassa  gahapatissa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkamatu anukampaṃ  upādāyā’’’ti| adhivāsaisi khau āyasmā sāriputtau tuṇhībhāvaina|

384. atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya āyasmatā ānandaina pacchāsamaṇaina yaina anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai  āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau āyasmā sāriputtau anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kac‍ci tai, gahapati, khamanīyaṃ, kac‍ci yāpanīyaṃ? kac‍ci tai dukkhā vaidanā paṭik‍kamanti, nau abhik‍kamanti; paṭik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, nau abhik‍kamau’’ti?

‘‘na mai, bhantai sāriputta, khamanīyaṃ na yāpanīyaṃ| bāḷhā mai dukkhā vaidanā abhik‍kamanti, nau paṭik‍kamanti; abhik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, nau paṭik‍kamau| saiyyathāpi, bhantai sāriputta, balavā purisau tiṇhaina sikharaina  muddhani muddhānaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) abhimatthaiyya abhimanthaiyya (sī॰ pī॰); aivamaiva khau mai, bhantai sāriputta, adhimattā vātā muddhani auhananti (syā॰ kaṃ॰) ūhananti adhimattā vātā sīlaṃ parikantanti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰)| na mai, bhantai sāriputta, khamanīyaṃ na yāpanīyaṃ| bāḷhā mai dukkhā vaidanā abhik‍kamanti, nau paṭik‍kamanti; abhik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, nau paṭik‍kamau| saiyyathāpi, bhantai sāriputta, balavā purisau daḷhaina varattakhaṇḍaina sīsai sīsavaiṭhaṃ dadaiyya; aivamaiva khau mai, bhantai sāriputta, adhimattā sīsai sīsavaidanā| na mai, bhantai sāriputta, khamanīyaṃ na yāpanīyaṃ| bāḷhā mai dukkhā vaidanā abhik‍kamanti, nau paṭik‍kamanti; abhik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, nau paṭik‍kamau| saiyyathāpi, bhantai sāriputta, dakkhau gaughātakau vā gaughātakantaivāsī vā tiṇhaina gauvikantanaina kucchiṃ parikantaiyya; aivamaiva khau mai, bhantai sāriputta, adhimattā vātā kucchiṃ parikantanti| na mai, bhantai sāriputta, khamanīyaṃ na yāpanīyaṃ| bāḷhā mai dukkhā vaidanā abhik‍kamanti, nau paṭik‍kamanti; abhik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, nau paṭik‍kamau| saiyyathāpi, bhantai sāriputta, dvai balavantau purisā dubbalataraṃ purisaṃ nānābāhāsu gahaitvā aṅgārakāsuyā santāpaiyyuṃ, samparitāpaiyyuṃ; aivamaiva khau mai, bhantai sāriputta, adhimattau kāyasmiṃ ḍāhau| na mai, bhantai sāriputta, khamanīyaṃ na yāpanīyaṃ| bāḷhā mai dukkhā vaidanā  abhik‍kamanti, nau paṭik‍kamanti; abhik‍kamausānaṃ  pañ‍ñāyati, nau paṭik‍kamau’’ti|

385. ‘‘tasmātiha  tai, gahapati, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘na cakkhuṃ upādiyissāmi, na ca mai cakkhunissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ bhavissatī’ti| aivañhi tai, gahapati, sikkhitabbaṃ|

‘‘tasmātiha tai, gahapati, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘na sautaṃ upādiyissāmi, na ca mai sautanissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ bhavissatī’ti| aivañhi tai, gahapati, sikkhitabbaṃ| tasmātiha tai, gahapati, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘na ghānaṃ upādiyissāmi, na ca mai ghānanissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ bhavissatī’ti| aivañhi tai, gahapati, sikkhitabbaṃ| tasmātiha tai, gahapati, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘na jivhaṃ upādiyissāmi, na ca mai jivhānissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ bhavissatī’ti| aivañhi tai, gahapati, sikkhitabbaṃ| tasmātiha tai, gahapati, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘na kāyaṃ upādiyissāmi, na ca mai kāyanissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ bhavissatī’ti| aivañhi tai, gahapati, sikkhitabbaṃ| tasmātiha tai, gahapati, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘na manaṃ upādiyissāmi, na ca mai manaunissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ bhavissatī’ti| aivañhi tai, gahapati, sikkhitabbaṃ|

‘‘tasmātiha tai, gahapati, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘na rūpaṃ upādiyissāmi, na ca mai rūpanissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ bhavissatī’ti| aivañhi tai, gahapati, sikkhitabbaṃ| tasmātiha tai, gahapati, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘na saddaṃ upādiyissāmi…pai॰… na gandhaṃ upādiyissāmi… na rasaṃ upādiyissāmi… na phauṭṭhabbaṃ upādiyissāmi… na dhammaṃ upādiyissāmi na  ca mai dhammanissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ bhavissatī’ti| aivañhi tai, gahapati, sikkhitabbaṃ|

‘‘tasmātiha tai, gahapati, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘na cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādiyissāmi, na ca mai cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇanissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ bhavissatī’ti| aivañhi tai, gahapati, sikkhitabbaṃ| tasmātiha tai, gahapati, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘na sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādiyissāmi… na ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādiyissāmi… na jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādiyissāmi… na kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādiyissāmi… na manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādiyissāmi na ca mai manauviñ‍ñāṇanissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ bhavissatī’ti| aivañhi tai, gahapati, sikkhitabbaṃ|

‘‘tasmātiha  tai, gahapati, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘na cakkhusamphassaṃ upādiyissāmi, na ca  mai cakkhusamphassanissitaṃ  viñ‍ñāṇaṃ bhavissatī’ti| aivañhi tai, gahapati, sikkhitabbaṃ| tasmātiha tai, gahapati, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘na sautasamphassaṃ upādiyissāmi… na ghānasamphassaṃ upādiyissāmi… na jivhāsamphassaṃ upādiyissāmi… na kāyasamphassaṃ upādiyissāmi… na manausamphassaṃ upādiyissāmi, na ca mai manausamphassanissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ bhavissatī’ti| aivañhi tai, gahapati, sikkhitabbaṃ|

‘‘tasmātiha tai, gahapati, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘na cakkhusamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ upādiyissāmi, na ca mai cakkhusamphassajāvaidanānissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ bhavissatī’ti| aivañhi tai, gahapati, sikkhitabbaṃ| tasmātiha tai, gahapati, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘na sautasamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ upādiyissāmi… na ghānasamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ upādiyissāmi… na jivhāsamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ upādiyissāmi… na kāyasamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ upādiyissāmi… na manausamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ upādiyissāmi, na ca mai manausamphassajāvaidanānissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ bhavissatī’ti| aivañhi tai, gahapati, sikkhitabbaṃ|

386. ‘‘tasmātiha tai, gahapati, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘na pathavīdhātuṃ upādiyissāmi, na ca mai pathavīdhātunissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ bhavissatī’ti| aivañhi tai, gahapati, sikkhitabbaṃ| tasmātiha tai, gahapati, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘na āpaudhātuṃ upādiyissāmi… na taijaudhātuṃ upādiyissāmi… na vāyaudhātuṃ upādiyissāmi… na ākāsadhātuṃ upādiyissāmi… na viñ‍ñāṇadhātuṃ upādiyissāmi, na ca mai viñ‍ñāṇadhātunissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ bhavissatī’ti| aivañhi tai, gahapati, sikkhitabbaṃ|

‘‘tasmātiha tai, gahapati, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘na rūpaṃ upādiyissāmi, na ca mai rūpanissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ bhavissatī’ti| aivañhi tai, gahapati, sikkhitabbaṃ| tasmātiha tai, gahapati, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘na vaidanaṃ upādiyissāmi… na sañ‍ñaṃ upādiyissāmi… na saṅkhārai upādiyissāmi… na viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādiyissāmi, na ca mai viñ‍ñāṇanissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ bhavissatī’ti| aivañhi tai, gahapati, sikkhitabbaṃ|

‘‘tasmātiha tai, gahapati, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘na ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ  upādiyissāmi, na ca mai ākāsānañ‍cāyatananissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ bhavissatī’ti| aivañhi tai, gahapati, sikkhitabbaṃ| tasmātiha tai, gahapati, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘na viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upādiyissāmi… na ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ  upādiyissāmi… na naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upādiyissāmi na ca mai naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatananissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ bhavissatī’ti| aivañhi tai, gahapati, sikkhitabbaṃ|

‘‘tasmātiha tai, gahapati, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘na idhalaukaṃ upādiyissāmi, na ca mai idhalaukanissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ bhavissatī’ti| aivañhi tai, gahapati, sikkhitabbaṃ| tasmātiha tai, gahapati, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘na paralaukaṃ upādiyissāmi, na ca mai paralaukanissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ bhavissatī’ti| aivañhi tai, gahapati, sikkhitabbaṃ| tasmātiha tai, gahapati, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘yampi mai diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ mutaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ pattaṃ pariyaisitaṃ anupariyaisitaṃ anucaritaṃ manasā tampi na upādiyissāmi, na ca mai taṃnissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ bhavissatī’ti| aivañhi tai, gahapati, sikkhitabba’’nti|

387. aivaṃ vuttai, anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati paraudi, assūni pavattaisi| atha khau āyasmā ānandau anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aulīyasi khau tvaṃ, gahapati, saṃsīdasi khau tvaṃ, gahapatī’’ti? ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhantai ānanda, aulīyāmi, napi saṃsīdāmi; api ca mai dīgharattaṃ satthā payirupāsitau manaubhāvanīyā ca bhikkhū; na ca mai aivarūpī dhammī  kathā sutapubbā’’ti| ‘‘na khau, gahapati, gihīnaṃ audātavasanānaṃ aivarūpī dhammī kathā paṭibhāti; pabbajitānaṃ khau, gahapati, aivarūpī dhammī kathā paṭibhātī’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, bhantai sāriputta, gihīnampi audātavasanānaṃ aivarūpī dhammī kathā paṭibhātu| santi hi, bhantai, kulaputtā apparajakkhajātikā, assavanatā dhammassa parihāyanti; bhavissanti dhammassa añ‍ñātārau’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā ca sāriputtau āyasmā ca ānandau anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ iminā auvādaina auvaditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamiṃsu| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati, acirapak‍kantai āyasmantai ca sāriputtai āyasmantai ca ānandai, kālamakāsi tusitaṃ kāyaṃ upapaj‍ji| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau daivaputtau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇau kaivalakappaṃ  jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ  ṭhitau khau anāthapiṇḍikau daivaputtau bhagavantaṃ gāthāhi ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘idañhi taṃ jaitavanaṃ, isisaṅghanisaivitaṃ|

āvutthaṃ dhammarājaina, pītisañ‍jananaṃ mama||

‘‘kammaṃ vij‍jā ca dhammau ca, sīlaṃ jīvitamuttamaṃ|

aitaina mac‍cā sujjhanti, na gauttaina dhanaina vā||

‘‘tasmā hi paṇḍitau pausau, sampassaṃ atthamattanau|

yaunisau vicinai dhammaṃ, aivaṃ tattha visujjhati||

‘‘sāriputtauva  pañ‍ñāya, sīlaina upasamaina|

yaupi pāraṅgatau bhikkhu, aitāvaparamau siyā’’ti||

idamavauca anāthapiṇḍikau daivaputtau| samanuñ‍ñau satthā ahausi| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau daivaputtau – ‘‘samanuñ‍ñau mai satthā’’ti bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyi|

388. atha khau bhagavā tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘imaṃ, bhikkhavai, rattiṃ añ‍ñatarau daivaputtau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇau kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau sau daivaputtau maṃ gāthāhi ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘idañhi taṃ jaitavanaṃ, isisaṅghanisaivitaṃ|

āvutthaṃ dhammarājaina, pītisañ‍jananaṃ mama||

‘‘kammaṃ vij‍jā ca dhammau ca, sīlaṃ jīvitamuttamaṃ|

aitaina mac‍cā sujjhanti, na gauttaina dhanaina vā||

‘‘tasmā  hi paṇḍitau pausau, sampassaṃ atthamattanau|

yaunisau vicinai dhammaṃ, aivaṃ tattha visujjhati||

‘‘sāriputtauva pañ‍ñāya, sīlaina upasamaina|

yaupi pāraṅgatau bhikkhu, aitāvaparamau siyā’’ti||

‘‘idamavauca, bhikkhavai, sau daivaputtau| ‘samanuñ‍ñau mai satthā’ti maṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyī’’ti|

aivaṃ  vuttai, āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sau hi nūna sau, bhantai, anāthapiṇḍikau daivaputtau bhavissati| anāthapiṇḍikau, bhantai, gahapati āyasmantai sāriputtai abhippasan‍nau ahausī’’ti| ‘‘sādhu, sādhu, ānanda! yāvatakaṃ khau, ānanda, tak‍kāya pattabbaṃ, anuppattaṃ taṃ tayā| anāthapiṇḍikau sau, ānanda, daivaputtau’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

anāthapiṇḍikauvādasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. chan‍nauvādasuttaṃ

389. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā ca sāriputtau āyasmā ca mahācundau āyasmā ca chan‍nau gijjhakūṭai pabbatai viharanti| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā chan‍nau ābādhikau hauti dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaināyasmā mahācundau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahācundaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āyāmāvusau cunda, yaināyasmā chan‍nau tainupasaṅkamissāma gilānapucchakā’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau āyasmā mahācundau āyasmatau sāriputtassa pac‍cassausi|

atha khau āyasmā ca sāriputtau āyasmā ca mahācundau yaināyasmā chan‍nau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā chan‍naina saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ  sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ chan‍naṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kac‍ci tai, āvusau chan‍na, khamanīyaṃ, kac‍ci yāpanīyaṃ? kac‍ci tai dukkhā vaidanā paṭik‍kamanti, nau abhik‍kamanti; paṭik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, nau abhik‍kamau’’ti?

‘‘na mai, āvusau sāriputta, khamanīyaṃ na yāpanīyaṃ| bāḷhā mai dukkhā vaidanā abhik‍kamanti, nau paṭik‍kamanti; abhik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, nau paṭik‍kamau| saiyyathāpi, āvusau sāriputta, balavā purisau  tiṇhaina sikharaina muddhani abhimatthaiyya; aivamaiva khau mai, āvusau sāriputta, adhimattā vātā muddhani ūhananti| na mai, āvusau sāriputta, khamanīyaṃ na yāpanīyaṃ| bāḷhā mai dukkhā vaidanā abhik‍kamanti, nau paṭik‍kamanti; abhik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati nau paṭik‍kamau| saiyyathāpi, āvusau sāriputta, balavā purisau daḷhaina varattakkhaṇḍaina sīsai sīsavaiṭhaṃ dadaiyya; aivamaiva khau mai, āvusau sāriputta, adhimattā sīsai sīsavaidanā| na mai, āvusau sāriputta, khamanīyaṃ na yāpanīyaṃ| bāḷhā mai dukkhā vaidanā abhik‍kamanti, nau paṭik‍kamanti; abhik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, nau paṭik‍kamau| saiyyathāpi, āvusau  sāriputta, dakkhau gaughātakau vā gaughātakantaivāsī vā tiṇhaina gauvikantanaina kucchiṃ parikantaiyya; aivamaiva khau mai, āvusau sāriputta, adhimattā vātā kucchiṃ parikantanti| na mai, āvusau sāriputta, khamanīyaṃ na yāpanīyaṃ| bāḷhā mai dukkhā vaidanā abhik‍kamanti, nau paṭik‍kamanti; abhik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, nau paṭik‍kamau| saiyyathāpi, āvusau sāriputta, dvai balavantau purisā dubbalataraṃ purisaṃ nānābāhāsu gahaitvā aṅgārakāsuyā santāpaiyyuṃ samparitāpaiyyuṃ; aivamaiva khau mai, āvusau sāriputta, adhimattau  kāyasmiṃ ḍāhau| na mai, āvusau sāriputta, khamanīyaṃ na yāpanīyaṃ| bāḷhā mai dukkhā vaidanā abhik‍kamanti, nau paṭik‍kamanti; abhik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, nau paṭik‍kamau| satthaṃ, āvusau sāriputta, āharissāmi, nāvakaṅkhāmi jīvita’’nti|

390. ‘‘māyasmā chan‍nau satthaṃ āharaisi| yāpaitāyasmā chan‍nau| yāpaintaṃ mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ chan‍naṃ icchāma| sacai āyasmatau chan‍nassa natthi sappāyāni bhaujanāni, ahaṃ āyasmatau chan‍nassa sappāyāni bhaujanāni pariyaisissāmi| sacai āyasmatau chan‍nassa natthi sappāyāni bhaisaj‍jāni, ahaṃ āyasmatau chan‍nassa sappāyāni bhaisaj‍jāni pariyaisissāmi| sacai āyasmatau chan‍nassa natthi patirūpā upaṭṭhākā, ahaṃ āyasmantaṃ chan‍naṃ upaṭṭhahissāmi| māyasmā chan‍nau satthaṃ āharaisi| yāpaitāyasmā chan‍nau| yāpaintaṃ mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ chan‍naṃ icchāmā’’ti|

‘‘napi mai, āvusau sāriputta, natthi sappāyāni bhaujanāni; napi mai natthi sappāyāni bhaisaj‍jāni; napi mai natthi patirūpā upaṭṭhākā; api cāvusau sāriputta, pariciṇṇau mai satthā dīgharattaṃ manāpainaiva nau amanāpaina| aitañhi, āvusau sāriputta, sāvakassa patirūpaṃ yaṃ satthāraṃ paricaraiyya manāpainaiva nau amanāpaina| ‘anupavaj‍jaṃ chan‍nau bhikkhu satthaṃ āharissatī’ti aivamaitaṃ aivamaiva khau tvaṃ (ka॰), āvusau sāriputta, dhāraihī’’ti| ‘‘pucchaiyyāma mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ chan‍naṃ kañ‍cidaiva daisaṃ, sacai āyasmā  chan‍nau aukāsaṃ karauti pañhassa vaiyyākaraṇāyā’’ti| ‘‘pucchāvusau sāriputta, sutvā vaidissāmī’’ti|

391. ‘‘cakkhuṃ, āvusau chan‍na, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbai dhammai ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau  mai attā’ti samanupassasi? sautaṃ, āvusau chan‍na, sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… ghānaṃ, āvusau chan‍na, ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… jivhaṃ, āvusau chan‍na, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ … kāyaṃ, āvusau chan‍na, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… manaṃ, āvusau chan‍na, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ manauviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbai dhammai ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassasī’’ti?

‘‘cakkhuṃ, āvusau sāriputta, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbai dhammai ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassāmi| sautaṃ, āvusau sāriputta…pai॰… ghānaṃ, āvusau sāriputta… jivhaṃ, āvusau sāriputta… kāyaṃ, āvusau sāriputta… manaṃ, āvusau sāriputta, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ manauviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbai dhammai ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassāmī’’ti|

392. ‘‘cakkhusmiṃ, āvusau chan‍na, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇai cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbaisu dhammaisu kiṃ disvā kiṃ abhiñ‍ñāya cakkhuṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbai dhammai ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassasi? sautasmiṃ, āvusau chan‍na, sautaviñ‍ñāṇai … ghānasmiṃ, āvusau chan‍na, ghānaviñ‍ñāṇai… jivhāya, āvusau chan‍na, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇai… kāyasmiṃ, āvusau chan‍na, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇai… manasmiṃ, āvusau chan‍na, manauviñ‍ñāṇai manauviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbaisu dhammaisu kiṃ disvā kiṃ abhiñ‍ñāya manaṃ manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ manauviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbai dhammai ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassasī’’ti?

‘‘cakkhusmiṃ, āvusau sāriputta, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇai cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbaisu dhammaisu niraudhaṃ disvā niraudhaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya cakkhuṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbai dhammai ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassāmi| sautasmiṃ, āvusau sāriputta, sautaviñ‍ñāṇai… ghānasmiṃ, āvusau sāriputta, ghānaviñ‍ñāṇai… jivhāya, āvusau sāriputta, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇai… kāyasmiṃ, āvusau sāriputta, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇai… manasmiṃ, āvusau sāriputta, manauviñ‍ñāṇai manauviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbaisu  dhammaisu niraudhaṃ disvā niraudhaṃ abhiñ‍ñā manaṃ manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ manauviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbai  dhammai ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassāmī’’ti|

393. aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā mahācundau āyasmantaṃ chan‍naṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘tasmātiha, āvusau chan‍na, idampi tassa bhagavatau sāsanaṃ vacanaṃ (sī॰), nic‍cakappaṃ manasi kātabbaṃ – ‘nissitassa calitaṃ, anissitassa calitaṃ  natthi| calitai asati passaddhi, passaddhiyā sati nati na hauti| natiyā asati āgatigati na hauti| āgatigatiyā asati cutūpapātau na hauti| cutūpapātai asati naividha na huraṃ na ubhayamantaraina| aisaivantau dukkhassā’’’ti| atha khau āyasmā ca sāriputtau āyasmā ca mahācundau āyasmantaṃ chan‍naṃ iminā auvādaina auvaditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamiṃsu|

394. atha khau āyasmā chan‍nau acirapak‍kantai āyasmantai ca sāriputtai āyasmantai ca mahācundai satthaṃ āharaisi| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āyasmatā, bhantai, chan‍naina satthaṃ āharitaṃ| tassa kā gati, kau abhisamparāyau’’ti? ‘‘nanu tai, sāriputta, chan‍naina bhikkhunā sammukhāyaiva anupavaj‍jatā byākatā’’ti? ‘‘atthi, bhantai, pubbajiraṃ pappajitañhitaṃ (ka॰), upavaj‍jitaṃ (ka॰), pubbavij‍janaṃ, pubbavijjhanaṃ, pubbaviciraṃ (saṃyuttakai) nāma vaj‍jigāmau| tatthāyasmatau chan‍nassa mittakulāni suhaj‍jakulāni upavaj‍jakulānī’’ti| ‘‘haunti pausanti (ka॰) haitai, sāriputta, chan‍nassa bhikkhunau mittakulāni suhaj‍jakulāni upavaj‍jakulāni| nāhaṃ, sāriputta, aittāvatā  ‘saupavaj‍jau’ti vadāmi| yau khau, sāriputta, imañ‍ca kāyaṃ nikkhipati añ‍ñañ‍ca kāyaṃ upādiyati tamahaṃ ‘saupavaj‍jau’ti vadāmi| taṃ chan‍nassa bhikkhunau natthi| ‘anupavaj‍jau chan‍nau bhikkhu satthaṃ āharaisī’ti aivamaitaṃ, sāriputta, dhāraihī’’ti|

idamavauca  bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

chan‍nauvādasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. puṇṇauvādasuttaṃ

395. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau āyasmā puṇṇau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā puṇṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina auvādaina auvadatu, yamahaṃ bhagavatau dhammaṃ sutvā aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharaiyya’’nti| ‘‘taina hi, puṇṇa, suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā puṇṇau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘santi khau, puṇṇa, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| taṃ cai bhikkhu abhinandati abhivadati ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa taṃ abhinandatau abhivadatau ajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau uppaj‍jati nandī nandi (syā॰ kaṃ॰)| ‘nandīsamudayā dukkhasamudayau, puṇṇā’ti vadāmi|

‘‘santi khau, puṇṇa, sautaviñ‍ñaiyyā saddā… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyā gandhā… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā… manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| taṃ cai bhikkhu abhinandati abhivadati ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa taṃ abhinandatau abhivadatau  ajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau uppaj‍jati nandī| ‘nandīsamudayā dukkhasamudayau, puṇṇā’ti vadāmi|

‘‘santi  ca khau, puṇṇa, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| taṃ cai bhikkhu nābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa taṃ anabhinandatau anabhivadatau anajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau nandī nirujjhati| ‘nandīniraudhā dukkhaniraudhau, puṇṇā’ti vadāmi|

‘‘santi  ca khau, puṇṇa, sautaviñ‍ñaiyyā saddā… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyā gandhā… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā… manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā  rajanīyā| taṃ cai bhikkhu nābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa taṃ anabhinandatau anabhivadatau anajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau nandī nirujjhati| ‘nandīniraudhā dukkhaniraudhau, puṇṇā’ti vadāmi|

‘‘iminā ca tvaṃ puṇṇa, mayā saṃkhittaina auvādaina auvaditau katarasmiṃ janapadai viharissasī’’ti? ‘‘imināhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina auvādaina auvaditau, atthi sunāparantau nāma janapadau, tatthāhaṃ viharissāmī’’ti|

396. ‘‘caṇḍā khau, puṇṇa, sunāparantakā manussā; pharusā khau, puṇṇa, sunāparantakā manussā| sacai taṃ, puṇṇa, sunāparantakā manussā ak‍kausissanti paribhāsissanti, tattha tai, puṇṇa, kinti bhavissatī’’ti? ‘‘sacai maṃ, bhantai, sunāparantakā manussā ak‍kausissanti paribhāsissanti, tattha mai aivaṃ bhavissati – ‘bhaddakā bhadrakā (ka॰) vatimai sunāparantakā  manussā, subhaddakā vatimai sunāparantakā manussā, yaṃ mai nayimai pāṇinā pahāraṃ daintī’ti| aivamaittha aivammaittha (?), bhagavā, bhavissati; aivamaittha, sugata, bhavissatī’’ti|

‘‘sacai pana tai, puṇṇa, sunāparantakā manussā pāṇinā pahāraṃ dassanti, tattha pana tai, puṇṇa, kinti bhavissatī’’ti? ‘‘sacai mai, bhantai, sunāparantakā manussā pāṇinā pahāraṃ dassanti, tattha mai aivaṃ bhavissati – ‘bhaddakā vatimai sunāparantakā manussā, subhaddakā vatimai sunāparantakā manussā, yaṃ mai nayimai laiḍḍunā pahāraṃ daintī’ti| aivamaittha, bhagavā, bhavissati; aivamaittha, sugata, bhavissatī’’ti|

‘‘sacai pana tai, puṇṇa, sunāparantakā manussā laiḍḍunā pahāraṃ dassanti, tattha pana tai, puṇṇa, kinti bhavissatī’’ti? ‘‘sacai mai, bhantai, sunāparantakā manussā  laiḍḍunā pahāraṃ dassanti, tattha mai aivaṃ bhavissati – ‘bhaddakā vatimai sunāparantakā manussā, subhaddakā vatimai sunāparantakā manussā, yaṃ mai nayimai daṇḍaina pahāraṃ daintī’ti| aivamaittha, bhagavā, bhavissati; aivamaittha, sugata, bhavissatī’’ti|

‘‘sacai  pana tai, puṇṇa, sunāparantakā manussā daṇḍaina pahāraṃ dassanti, tattha pana tai, puṇṇa, kinti bhavissatī’’ti? ‘‘sacai mai, bhantai, sunāparantakā manussā daṇḍaina pahāraṃ dassanti, tattha mai aivaṃ bhavissati – ‘bhaddakā vatimai sunāparantakā manussā, subhaddakā vatimai  sunāparantakā manussā, yaṃ mai nayimai satthaina pahāraṃ daintī’ti| aivamaittha, bhagavā, bhavissati; aivamaittha, sugata, bhavissatī’’ti|

‘‘sacai pana tai, puṇṇa, sunāparantakā manussā satthaina pahāraṃ dassanti, tattha pana tai, puṇṇa, kinti bhavissatī’’ti? ‘‘sacai mai, bhantai, sunāparantakā manussā satthaina pahāraṃ dassanti, tattha mai aivaṃ bhavissati – ‘bhaddakā vatimai sunāparantakā manussā, subhaddakā vatimai sunāparantakā manussā, yaṃ maṃ yaṃ mai (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) nayimai tiṇhaina satthaina jīvitā vauraupaintī’ti| aivamaittha, bhagavā, bhavissati; aivamaittha, sugata, bhavissatī’’ti|

‘‘sacai pana taṃ, puṇṇa, sunāparantakā manussā tiṇhaina satthaina jīvitā vauraupaissanti, tattha pana tai, puṇṇa, kinti bhavissatī’’ti? ‘‘sacai maṃ, bhantai, sunāparantakā manussā tiṇhaina satthaina jīvitā vauraupaissanti, tattha mai aivaṃ bhavissati – ‘santi khau bhagavatau sāvakā kāyai ca jīvitai ca aṭṭīyamānā harāyamānā jigucchamānā satthahārakaṃ pariyaisanti| taṃ mai idaṃ apariyiṭṭhaṃyaiva satthahārakaṃ laddha’nti| aivamaittha, bhagavā, bhavissati; aivamaittha, sugata, bhavissatī’’ti| ‘‘sādhu, sādhu, puṇṇa! sakkhissasi khau tvaṃ, puṇṇa, iminā damūpasamaina saman‍nāgatau sunāparantasmiṃ janapadai viharituṃ| yassadāni tvaṃ, puṇṇa, kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’’ti|

397. atha khau āyasmā puṇṇau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā  padakkhiṇaṃ katvā saināsanaṃ saṃsāmaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina sunāparantau janapadau taina  cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina sunāparantau janapadau tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ āyasmā puṇṇau sunāparantasmiṃ janapadai viharati| atha khau āyasmā puṇṇau tainaivantaravassaina pañ‍camattāni upāsakasatāni paṭivaidaisi paṭipādaisi (sī॰ pī॰), paṭidaisaisi (syā॰ kaṃ॰), tainaivantaravassaina pañ‍camattāni upāsikasatāni paṭivaidaisi, tainaivantaravassaina tissau vij‍jā sacchākāsi| atha khau āyasmā puṇṇau aparaina samayaina parinibbāyi|

atha  khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘yau sau, bhantai, puṇṇau nāma  kulaputtau bhagavatā saṃkhittaina auvādaina auvaditau sau kālaṅkatau| tassa kā gati, kau abhisamparāyau’’ti? ‘‘paṇḍitau, bhikkhavai, puṇṇau kulaputtau pac‍capādi sac‍cavādī dhammavādī (ka॰) dhammassānudhammaṃ, na ca maṃ dhammādhikaraṇaṃ vihaiṭhaisi| parinibbutau, bhikkhavai, puṇṇau kulaputtau’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

puṇṇauvādasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. nandakauvādasuttaṃ

398. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau mahāpajāpatigautamī pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhunisataihi saddhiṃ yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau mahāpajāpatigautamī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘auvadatu, bhantai, bhagavā bhikkhuniyau; anusāsatu, bhantai, bhagavā bhikkhuniyau; karautu, bhantai, bhagavā bhikkhunīnaṃ dhammiṃ katha’’nti dhammikathanti (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰)|

taina khau pana samayaina thairā bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau auvadanti pariyāyaina| āyasmā nandakau na icchati bhikkhuniyau auvadituṃ pariyāyaina| atha  khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘kassa nu khau, ānanda, aj‍ja pariyāyau bhikkhuniyau auvadituṃ pariyāyainā’’ti? ‘‘sabbaihaiva, bhantai, katau nandakassa bhantai (sī॰ pī॰) pariyāyau bhikkhuniyau auvadituṃ pariyāyaina| ayaṃ, bhantai, āyasmā nandakau na icchati bhikkhuniyau auvadituṃ pariyāyainā’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ nandakaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘auvada, nandaka, bhikkhuniyau; anusāsa, nandaka, bhikkhuniyau; karauhi tvaṃ, brāhmaṇa, bhikkhunīnaṃ dhammiṃ katha’’nti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā  nandakau bhagavatau paṭissutvā  pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau attadutiyau yaina rājakārāmau tainupasaṅkami| addasaṃsu khau tā bhikkhuniyau āyasmantaṃ nandakaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna āsanaṃ pañ‍ñāpaisuṃ, udakañ‍ca pādānaṃ upaṭṭhapaisuṃ| nisīdi khau āyasmā nandakau pañ‍ñattai āsanai| nisaj‍ja pādai pakkhālaisi| tāpi khau bhikkhuniyau āyasmantaṃ nandakaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tā bhikkhuniyau āyasmā nandakau aitadavauca – ‘‘paṭipucchakathā khau, bhaginiyau, bhavissati| tattha ājānantīhi – ‘ājānāmā’ tissa vacanīyaṃ, na ājānantīhi – ‘na ājānāmā’ tissa vacanīyaṃ| yassā vā panassa kaṅkhā vā vimati vā  ahamaiva tattha paṭipucchitabbau – ‘idaṃ, bhantai, kathaṃ; imassa k‍vatthau’’’ti? ‘‘aittakainapi mayaṃ, bhantai, ayyassa nandakassa attamanā abhiraddhā abhinandāma (syā॰ kaṃ॰) yaṃ nau ayyau nandakau pavāraitī’’ti|

399. ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhaginiyau, cakkhu nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhaginiyau, sautaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai…pai॰… ghānaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’… ‘‘jivhā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’… ‘‘kāyau nic‍cau vā anic‍cau vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cau, bhantai’’… ‘‘manau nic‍cau vā anic‍cau vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cau, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ  nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘pubbaiva nau aitaṃ, bhantai, yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhaṃ – ‘itipimai cha ajjhattikā āyatanā anic‍cā’’’ti| ‘‘sādhu, sādhu, bhaginiyau! aivañhaitaṃ, bhaginiyau, hauti ariyasāvakassa yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passatau’’|

400. ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhaginiyau, rūpā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhaginiyau, saddā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai…pai॰… gandhā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’… ‘‘rasā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’… ‘‘phauṭṭhabbā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’… ‘‘dhammā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘pubbaiva nau aitaṃ, bhantai, yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhaṃ – ‘itipimai cha bāhirā āyatanā anic‍cā’’’ti| ‘‘sādhu, sādhu, bhaginiyau! aivañhaitaṃ, bhaginiyau, hauti ariyasāvakassa yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passatau’’|

401. ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhaginiyau, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhaginiyau, sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’… ‘‘jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’… ‘‘kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’… ‘‘manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vāti’’? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau  mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘pubbaiva nau aitaṃ, bhantai, yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhaṃ – ‘itipimai cha viñ‍ñāṇakāyā anic‍cā’’’ti| ‘‘sādhu, sādhu, bhaginiyau! aivañhaitaṃ, bhaginiyau, hauti ariyasāvakassa yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passatau’’|

402. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhaginiyau, tailappadīpassa jhāyatau tailampi anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, vaṭṭipi anic‍cā vipariṇāmadhammā, ac‍cipi anic‍cā vipariṇāmadhammā, ābhāpi anic‍cā vipariṇāmadhammā| yau nu khau, bhaginiyau, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘amussa tailappadīpassa jhāyatau tailampi anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, vaṭṭipi anic‍cā vipariṇāmadhammā, ac‍cipi anic‍cā vipariṇāmadhammā; yā ca khvāssa ābhā sā nic‍cā dhuvā sassatā avipariṇāmadhammā’ti; sammā nu khau sau, bhaginiyau, vadamānau vadaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘amussa hi, bhantai, tailappadīpassa jhāyatau tailampi anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, vaṭṭipi anic‍cā vipariṇāmadhammā, ac‍cipi anic‍cā vipariṇāmadhammā; pagaivassa ābhā anic‍cā vipariṇāmadhammā’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhaginiyau, yau nu khau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘cha khaumai ajjhattikā āyatanā anic‍cā anic‍cā vipariṇāmadhammā (?); yañ‍ca khau cha ajjhattikai āyatanai paṭic‍ca paṭisaṃvaidaiti sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā taṃ nic‍caṃ dhuvaṃ sassataṃ avipariṇāmadhamma’nti; sammā  nu khau sau, bhaginiyau, vadamānau vadaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘taj‍jaṃ  taj‍jaṃ, bhantai, pac‍cayaṃ paṭic‍ca taj‍jā taj‍jā vaidanā uppaj‍janti| taj‍jassa  taj‍jassa pac‍cayassa niraudhā taj‍jā taj‍jā vaidanā nirujjhantī’’ti| ‘‘sādhu, sādhu, bhaginiyau! aivañhaitaṃ, bhaginiyau, hauti ariyasāvakassa yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passatau’’|

403. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhaginiyau, mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau mūlampi anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, khandhaupi anic‍cau vipariṇāmadhammau, sākhāpalāsampi anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, chāyāpi anic‍cā vipariṇāmadhammā| yau nu khau, bhaginiyau, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘amussa mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau mūlampi anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, khandhaupi anic‍cau vipariṇāmadhammau, sākhāpalāsampi anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, yā ca khvāssa chāyā sā nic‍cā dhuvā sassatā avipariṇāmadhammā’ti; sammā nu khau sau, bhaginiyau, vadamānau vadaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘amussa hi, bhantai, mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau mūlampi anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, khandhaupi anic‍cau vipariṇāmadhammau, sākhāpalāsampi anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ; pagaivassa chāyā anic‍cā vipariṇāmadhammā’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhaginiyau, yau nu khau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘cha khaumai bāhirā āyatanā  anic‍cā anic‍cā vipariṇāmadhammā (sī॰ pī॰)| yañ‍ca khau cha bāhirai āyatanai paṭic‍ca paṭisaṃvaidaiti sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā taṃ nic‍caṃ dhuvaṃ sassataṃ avipariṇāmadhamma’nti; sammā nu khau sau, bhaginiyau, vadamānau  vadaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘taj‍jaṃ taj‍jaṃ, bhantai, pac‍cayaṃ paṭic‍ca taj‍jā taj‍jā vaidanā uppaj‍janti| taj‍jassa taj‍jassa pac‍cayassa niraudhā taj‍jā taj‍jā vaidanā nirujjhantī’’ti| ‘‘sādhu, sādhu, bhaginiyau! aivañhaitaṃ, bhaginiyau, hauti ariyasāvakassa yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passatau’’|

404. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhaginiyau, dakkhau gaughātakau vā gaughātakantaivāsī vā gāviṃ vadhitvā tiṇhaina gauvikantanaina gāviṃ saṅkantaiyya anupahac‍ca antaraṃ maṃsakāyaṃ anupahac‍ca bāhiraṃ cammakāyaṃ| yaṃ yadaiva tattha antarā vilimaṃsaṃ vilimaṃ (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) antarā nhāru antarā bandhanaṃ taṃ tadaiva tiṇhaina gauvikantanaina  sañchindaiyya saṅkantaiyya sampakantaiyya samparikantaiyya| sañchinditvā saṅkantitvā sampakantitvā samparikantitvā vidhunitvā bāhiraṃ cammakāyaṃ tainaiva cammaina taṃ gāviṃ paṭicchādaitvā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘tathaivāyaṃ gāvī saṃyuttā imināva cammainā’ti; sammā nu khau  sau, bhaginiyau, vadamānau vadaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘amu hi, bhantai, dakkhau gaughātakau vā gaughātakantaivāsī vā gāviṃ vadhitvā tiṇhaina gauvikantanaina gāviṃ saṅkantaiyya anupahac‍ca antaraṃ maṃsakāyaṃ anupahac‍ca bāhiraṃ cammakāyaṃ| yaṃ yadaiva tattha antarā vilimaṃsaṃ antarā nhāru antarā bandhanaṃ taṃ tadaiva tiṇhaina gauvikantanaina sañchindaiyya saṅkantaiyya sampakantaiyya samparikantaiyya| sañchinditvā saṅkantitvā sampakantitvā samparikantitvā vidhunitvā bāhiraṃ cammakāyaṃ tainaiva  cammaina taṃ gāviṃ paṭicchādaitvā kiñ‍cāpi sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘tathaivāyaṃ gāvī saṃyuttā imināva cammainā’ti; atha khau sā gāvī visaṃyuttā tainaiva cammainā’’ti|

‘‘upamā khau mai ayaṃ, bhaginiyau, katā atthassa viñ‍ñāpanāya| ayamaivaittha atthau; ‘antarā maṃsakāyau’ti khau, bhaginiyau, chan‍naitaṃ ajjhattikānaṃ āyatanānaṃ adhivacanaṃ; ‘bāhirau cammakāyau’ti khau bhaginiyau, chan‍naitaṃ bāhirānaṃ āyatanānaṃ adhivacanaṃ; ‘antarā vilimaṃsaṃ, antarā nhāru, antarā bandhana’nti khau, bhaginiyau, nandīrāgassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ; ‘tiṇhaṃ  gauvikantana’nti khau, bhaginiyau, ariyāyaitaṃ pañ‍ñāya adhivacanaṃ; yāyaṃ ariyā pañ‍ñā antarā kilaisaṃ antarā saṃyaujanaṃ antarā bandhanaṃ sañchindati saṅkantati sampakantati samparikantati|

405. ‘‘satta khau panimai, bhaginiyau, baujjhaṅgā, yaisaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| katamai satta? idha, bhaginiyau, bhikkhu satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ, dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… vīriyasambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… pītisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… passaddhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… samādhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ  niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| imai khau, bhaginiyau, satta baujjhaṅgā, yaisaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’’ti|

406. atha  khau āyasmā nandakau tā bhikkhuniyau iminā auvādaina auvaditvā uyyaujaisi – ‘‘gacchatha, bhaginiyau; kālau’’ti| atha khau tā bhikkhuniyau āyasmatau nandakassa bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā āyasmantaṃ nandakaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau tā bhikkhuniyau bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘gacchatha, bhikkhuniyau; kālau’’ti| atha khau tā bhikkhuniyau bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kamiṃsu| atha khau bhagavā acirapak‍kantīsu tāsu bhikkhunīsu bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, tadahupausathai cātuddasai na hauti bahunaujanassa kaṅkhā vā vimati vā – ‘ūnau nu khau candau, puṇṇau nu khau candau’ti, atha khau ūnau candautvaiva hauti| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, tā bhikkhuniyau nandakassa dhammadaisanāya attamanā haunti nau ca khau paripuṇṇasaṅkappā’’ti|
