407. atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ nandakaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi tvaṃ, nandaka, svaipi tā bhikkhuniyau tainaivauvādaina auvadaiyyāsī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā nandakau  bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| atha khau āyasmā nandakau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau attadutiyau yaina rājakārāmau tainupasaṅkami| addasaṃsu khau tā bhikkhuniyau āyasmantaṃ nandakaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna āsanaṃ pañ‍ñāpaisuṃ, udakañ‍ca pādānaṃ upaṭṭhapaisuṃ| nisīdi khau āyasmā nandakau pañ‍ñattai āsanai| nisaj‍ja pādai pakkhālaisi| tāpi khau bhikkhuniyau āyasmantaṃ nandakaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tā bhikkhuniyau āyasmā nandakau aitadavauca – ‘‘paṭipucchakathā khau, bhaginiyau, bhavissati| tattha ājānantīhi ‘ājānāmā’ tissa vacanīyaṃ, na ājānantīhi ‘na ājānāmā’ tissa vacanīyaṃ| yassā vā panassa kaṅkhā vā vimati vā, ahamaiva tattha paṭipucchitabbau  – ‘idaṃ, bhantai, kathaṃ; imassa k‍vatthau’’’ti| ‘‘aittakainapi mayaṃ, bhantai, ayyassa nandakassa attamanā abhiraddhā yaṃ nau ayyau nandakau pavāraitī’’ti|

408. ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhaginiyau, cakkhu nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai  attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhaginiyau, sautaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai…pai॰… ghānaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai… jivhā… kāyau… manau nic‍cau vā anic‍cau vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cau, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘pubbaiva nau aitaṃ, bhantai, yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhaṃ – ‘itipimai cha ajjhattikā āyatanā anic‍cā’’’ti| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, bhaginiyau! aivañhaitaṃ, bhaginiyau, hauti ariyasāvakassa yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passatau’’|

409. ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhaginiyau, rūpā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau  mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhaginiyau, saddā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai…pai॰… gandhā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai… rasā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai… phauṭṭhabbā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai… dhammā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘pubbaiva nau aitaṃ, bhantai, yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhaṃ – ‘itipimai cha bāhirā āyatanā anic‍cā’’’ti| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, bhaginiyau! aivañhaitaṃ, bhaginiyau, hauti ariyasāvakassa yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passatau’’|

410. ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhaginiyau, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai…pai॰… sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai… ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai… jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ  vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘pubbaiva nau aitaṃ, bhantai, yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya  sudiṭṭhaṃ – ‘itipimai cha viñ‍ñāṇakāyā anic‍cā’’’ti| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, bhaginiyau! aivañhaitaṃ, bhaginiyau, hauti ariyasāvakassa yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passatau’’|

411. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhaginiyau, tailappadīpassa jhāyatau tailampi anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, vaṭṭipi anic‍cā vipariṇāmadhammā, ac‍cipi anic‍cā vipariṇāmadhammā, ābhāpi anic‍cā vipariṇāmadhammā| yau nu khau, bhaginiyau, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘amussa tailappadīpassa jhāyatau tailampi anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, vaṭṭipi anic‍cā vipariṇāmadhammā, ac‍cipi anic‍cā vipariṇāmadhammā; yā ca khvāssa ābhā sā nic‍cā dhuvā sassatā avipariṇāmadhammā’ti; sammā nu khau sau, bhaginiyau, vadamānau vadaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘amussa hi, bhantai, tailappadīpassa jhāyatau tailampi anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, vaṭṭipi anic‍cā vipariṇāmadhammā, ac‍cipi anic‍cā vipariṇāmadhammā; pagaivassa ābhā anic‍cā vipariṇāmadhammā’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhaginiyau, yau nu khau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘cha khaumai ajjhattikā āyatanā anic‍cā| yañ‍ca khau cha ajjhattikai āyatanai  paṭic‍ca paṭisaṃvaidaiti sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā taṃ nic‍caṃ dhuvaṃ sassataṃ avipariṇāmadhamma’nti; sammā nu khau sau, bhaginiyau, vadamānau vadaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘taj‍jaṃ taj‍jaṃ, bhantai, pac‍cayaṃ paṭic‍ca taj‍jā taj‍jā vaidanā uppaj‍janti| taj‍jassa taj‍jassa pac‍cayassa niraudhā taj‍jā taj‍jā vaidanā nirujjhantī’’ti| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, bhaginiyau! aivañhaitaṃ, bhaginiyau, hauti ariyasāvakassa yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passatau’’|

412. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhaginiyau, mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau mūlampi anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, khandhaupi anic‍cau vipariṇāmadhammau, sākhāpalāsampi anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, chāyāpi anic‍cā vipariṇāmadhammā| yau nu khau, bhaginiyau, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘amussa mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau mūlampi anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, khandhaupi anic‍cau vipariṇāmadhammau, sākhāpalāsampi anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ; yā ca khvāssa chāyā sā nic‍cā dhuvā sassatā avipariṇāmadhammā’ti; sammā nu khau sau bhaginiyau, vadamānau vadaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘amussa hi, bhantai, mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau mūlampi anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, khandhaupi anic‍cau vipariṇāmadhammau, sākhāpalāsampi anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ; pagaivassa chāyā anic‍cā vipariṇāmadhammā’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhaginiyau, yau nu khau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘cha khaumai  bāhirā āyatanā anic‍cā| yañ‍ca khau bāhirai āyatanai paṭic‍ca paṭisaṃvaidaiti sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā taṃ nic‍caṃ dhuvaṃ  sassataṃ avipariṇāmadhamma’nti; sammā nu khau sau, bhaginiyau, vadamānau vadaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘taj‍jaṃ taj‍jaṃ, bhantai, pac‍cayaṃ paṭic‍ca taj‍jā taj‍jā vaidanā uppaj‍janti| taj‍jassa taj‍jassa pac‍cayassa niraudhā taj‍jā taj‍jā vaidanā nirujjhantī’’ti| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, bhaginiyau! aivañhaitaṃ, bhaginiyau, hauti ariyasāvakassa yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passatau’’|

413. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhaginiyau, dakkhau gaughātakau vā gaughātakantaivāsī vā gāviṃ vadhitvā tiṇhaina gauvikantanaina gāviṃ saṅkantaiyya anupahac‍ca  antaraṃ maṃsakāyaṃ anupahac‍ca bāhiraṃ cammakāyaṃ| yaṃ yadaiva tattha antarā vilimaṃsaṃ antarā nhāru antarā bandhanaṃ taṃ tadaiva tiṇhaina gauvikantanaina sañchindaiyya saṅkantaiyya sampakantaiyya samparikantaiyya| sañchinditvā saṅkantitvā sampakantitvā samparikantitvā vidhunitvā bāhiraṃ cammakāyaṃ tainaiva cammaina taṃ gāviṃ paṭicchādaitvā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘tathaivāyaṃ gāvī saṃyuttā imināva cammainā’ti; sammā nu khau sau, bhaginiyau, vadamānau vadaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘amu hi, bhantai, dakkhau gaughātakau vā gaughātakantaivāsī vā gāviṃ vadhitvā tiṇhaina gauvikantanaina gāviṃ saṅkantaiyya anupahac‍ca antaraṃ maṃsakāyaṃ anupahac‍ca bāhiraṃ cammakāyaṃ| yaṃ yadaiva tattha antarā vilimaṃsaṃ antarā  nhāru antarā bandhanaṃ taṃ tadaiva tiṇhaina gauvikantanaina sañchindaiyya saṅkantaiyya sampakantaiyya samparikantaiyya| sañchinditvā saṅkantitvā sampakantitvā samparikantitvā vidhunitvā bāhiraṃ cammakāyaṃ tainaiva cammaina taṃ gāviṃ paṭicchādaitvā kiñ‍cāpi sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘tathaivāyaṃ gāvī saṃyuttā imināva cammainā’ti; atha khau sā gāvī visaṃyuttā tainaiva cammainā’’ti|

‘‘upamā khau mai ayaṃ, bhaginiyau, katā atthassa viñ‍ñāpanāya ayamaivaittha atthau| ‘antarā maṃsakāyau’ti khau, bhaginiyau, chan‍naitaṃ ajjhattikānaṃ āyatanānaṃ adhivacanaṃ; ‘bāhirau cammakāyau’ti khau, bhaginiyau, chan‍naitaṃ bāhirānaṃ āyatanānaṃ adhivacanaṃ; ‘antarā vilimaṃsaṃ antarā nhāru antarā bandhana’nti khau, bhaginiyau, nandīrāgassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ; ‘tiṇhaṃ gauvikantana’nti khau, bhaginiyau, ariyāyaitaṃ pañ‍ñāya adhivacanaṃ; yāyaṃ ariyā pañ‍ñā antarā kilaisaṃ antarā saṃyaujanaṃ antarā bandhanaṃ sañchindati saṅkantati sampakantati samparikantati|

414. ‘‘satta khau panimai, bhaginiyau, baujjhaṅgā, yaisaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja  viharati| katamai satta? idha, bhaginiyau, bhikkhu satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… vīriyasambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… pītisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… passaddhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ  bhāvaiti… samādhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ  bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| imai khau, bhaginiyau, satta baujjhaṅgā yaisaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’’ti|

415. atha khau āyasmā nandakau tā bhikkhuniyau iminā auvādaina auvaditvā uyyaujaisi – ‘‘gacchatha, bhaginiyau; kālau’’ti| atha khau tā bhikkhuniyau āyasmatau nandakassa bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā āyasmantaṃ nandakaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau tā bhikkhuniyau bhagavā aitadavauca –‘‘gacchatha, bhikkhuniyau; kālau’’ti| atha khau tā bhikkhuniyau bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kamiṃsu| atha khau bhagavā acirapak‍kantīsu tāsu bhikkhunīsu bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, tadahupausathai pan‍narasai na hauti bahunau janassa kaṅkhā vā vimati vā – ‘ūnau nu khau candau, puṇṇau nu khau candau’ti, atha khau puṇṇau candautvaiva hauti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, tā bhikkhuniyau nandakassa dhammadaisanāya attamanā caiva paripuṇṇasaṅkappā ca| tāsaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ bhikkhunisatānaṃ yā  pacchimitā bhikkhunī sā yā pacchimā bhikkhunī, sā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), yā pacchimikā, tā bhikkhuniyau (ka॰) sautāpan‍nā avinipātadhammā niyatā sambaudhiparāyanā’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

nandakauvādasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. cūḷarāhulauvādasuttaṃ

416. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau bhagavatau rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘paripak‍kā khau  rāhulassa vimuttiparipācanīyā dhammā| yaṃnūnāhaṃ rāhulaṃ uttariṃ āsavānaṃ khayai vinaiyya’’nti| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau āyasmantaṃ rāhulaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘gaṇhāhi, rāhula, nisīdanaṃ; yaina andhavanaṃ tainupasaṅkamissāma divāvihārāyā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā rāhulau bhagavatau paṭissutvā nisīdanaṃ ādāya bhagavantaṃ piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhi|

taina khau pana samayaina anaikāni daivatāsahassāni bhagavantaṃ anubandhāni haunti – ‘‘aj‍ja bhagavā āyasmantaṃ rāhulaṃ uttariṃ āsavānaṃ khayai vinaissatī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā andhavanaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| āyasmāpi khau rāhulau bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ rāhulaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

417. ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, rāhula, cakkhu nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai ’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, rāhula, rūpā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ  samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, rāhula, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, rāhula, cakkhusamphassau nic‍cau vā anic‍cau vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cau, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ, dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau  haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, rāhula, yamidaṃ yampidaṃ (sī॰ ka॰) cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati  vaidanāgataṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ saṅkhāragataṃ viñ‍ñāṇagataṃ tampi nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ, dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

418. ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi rāhula, sautaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai…pai॰… ghānaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai…pai॰… jivhā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai…pai॰… kāyau nic‍cau vā anic‍cau vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cau, bhantai…pai॰… manau nic‍cau vā anic‍cau vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cau, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ, dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ –‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi rāhula, dhammā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ, dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñasi rāhula, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ, dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi rāhula, manausamphassau nic‍cau vā anic‍cau vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cau, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ, dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, rāhula, yamidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidanāgataṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ saṅkhāragataṃ viñ‍ñāṇagataṃ, tampi nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ, dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ  panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

419. ‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, rāhula, sutavā ariyasāvakau cakkhusmiṃ cakkhusmimpi (syā॰ kaṃ॰) aivamitaraisupi nibbindati, rūpaisu nibbindati, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇai nibbindati, cakkhusamphassai nibbindati, yamidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidanāgataṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ saṅkhāragataṃ viñ‍ñāṇagataṃ tasmimpi nibbindati| sautasmiṃ  nibbindati, saddaisu nibbindati…pai॰…, ghānasmiṃ nibbindati, gandhaisu nibbindati… jivhāya nibbindati, rasaisu nibbindati… kāyasmiṃ nibbindati, phauṭṭhabbaisu nibbindati… manasmiṃ nibbindati, dhammaisu nibbindati, manauviñ‍ñāṇai nibbindati, manausamphassai nibbindati, yamidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā  uppaj‍jati vaidanāgataṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ saṅkhāragataṃ viñ‍ñāṇagataṃ tasmimpi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati, virāgā vimuc‍cati| vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā rāhulau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti| imasmiñ‍ca pana vaiyyākaraṇasmiṃ bhañ‍ñamānai āyasmatau rāhulassa anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍ci| tāsañ‍ca anaikānaṃ daivatāsahassānaṃ virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi – ‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhamma’’nti|

cūḷarāhulauvādasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. chachak‍kasuttaṃ

420. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘dhammaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaissāmi, yadidaṃ – cha chak‍kāni| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘cha ajjhattikāni āyatanāni vaiditabbāni, cha bāhirāni āyatanāni vaiditabbāni, cha viñ‍ñāṇakāyā vaiditabbā, cha phassakāyā vaiditabbā, cha vaidanākāyā vaiditabbā, cha taṇhākāyā vaiditabbā|

421. ‘‘‘cha ajjhattikāni āyatanāni vaiditabbānī’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ, sautāyatanaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ, jivhāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ| ‘cha ajjhattikāni āyatanāni vaiditabbānī’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ| idaṃ paṭhamaṃ chak‍kaṃ|

‘‘‘cha  bāhirāni āyatanāni vaiditabbānī’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? rūpāyatanaṃ, saddāyatanaṃ, gandhāyatanaṃ, rasāyatanaṃ, phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ, dhammāyatanaṃ| ‘cha bāhirāni āyatanāni vaiditabbānī’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ| idaṃ dutiyaṃ chak‍kaṃ|

‘‘‘cha viñ‍ñāṇakāyā vaiditabbā’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? cakkhuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca rūpai ca uppaj‍jati cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, sautañ‍ca paṭic‍ca saddai ca uppaj‍jati  sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, ghānañ‍ca paṭic‍ca gandhai ca uppaj‍jati ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, jivhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca rasai ca uppaj‍jati jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, kāyañ‍ca paṭic‍ca phauṭṭhabbai ca uppaj‍jati kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, manañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dhammai ca uppaj‍jati manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| ‘cha viñ‍ñāṇakāyā vaiditabbā’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ| idaṃ tatiyaṃ chak‍kaṃ|

‘‘‘cha phassakāyā vaiditabbā’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? cakkhuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca rūpai ca uppaj‍jati cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau; sautañ‍ca paṭic‍ca saddai ca uppaj‍jati sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau; ghānañ‍ca paṭic‍ca gandhai ca uppaj‍jati ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau; jivhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca rasai ca uppaj‍jati jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau; kāyañ‍ca paṭic‍ca phauṭṭhabbai ca uppaj‍jati kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau; manañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dhammai ca  uppaj‍jati manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau| ‘cha phassakāyā vaiditabbā’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ| idaṃ catutthaṃ chak‍kaṃ|

‘‘‘cha vaidanākāyā vaiditabbā’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? cakkhuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca rūpai ca uppaj‍jati cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā; sautañ‍ca paṭic‍ca saddai ca uppaj‍jati sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā; ghānañ‍ca paṭic‍ca gandhai ca uppaj‍jati ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā; jivhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca rasai ca uppaj‍jati jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā; kāyañ‍ca paṭic‍ca phauṭṭhabbai ca uppaj‍jati kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau, phassapac‍cayā  vaidanā; manañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dhammai  ca uppaj‍jati manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā| ‘cha vaidanākāyā vaiditabbā’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ  vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ| idaṃ pañ‍camaṃ chak‍kaṃ|

‘‘‘cha taṇhākāyā vaiditabbā’ti – iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? cakkhuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca rūpai ca uppaj‍jati cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā; sautañ‍ca paṭic‍ca saddai ca uppaj‍jati sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… ghānañ‍ca paṭic‍ca gandhai ca uppaj‍jati ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… jivhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca rasai ca uppaj‍jati  jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… kāyañ‍ca paṭic‍ca phauṭṭhabbai ca uppaj‍jati kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… manañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dhammai ca uppaj‍jati manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā| ‘cha taṇhākāyā vaiditabbā’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ| idaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ chak‍kaṃ|

422. ‘‘‘cakkhu attā’ti yau vadaiyya taṃ na upapaj‍jati| cakkhussa uppādaupi vayaupi pañ‍ñāyati| yassa khau pana uppādaupi vayaupi pañ‍ñāyati, ‘attā mai uppaj‍jati ca vaiti cā’ti ic‍cassa aivamāgataṃ hauti| tasmā taṃ na upapaj‍jati – ‘cakkhu attā’ti yau vadaiyya| iti cakkhu anattā|

‘‘‘rūpā attā’ti yau vadaiyya taṃ na upapaj‍jati| rūpānaṃ uppādaupi vayaupi pañ‍ñāyati| yassa khau pana uppādaupi vayaupi pañ‍ñāyati, ‘attā mai uppaj‍jati ca vaiti cā’ti ic‍cassa aivamāgataṃ hauti| tasmā taṃ na upapaj‍jati – ‘rūpā attā’ti yau vadaiyya| iti cakkhu anattā, rūpā anattā|

‘‘‘cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ attā’ti yau vadaiyya taṃ na upapaj‍jati| cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa uppādaupi vayaupi pañ‍ñāyati| yassa khau pana uppādaupi vayaupi pañ‍ñāyati, ‘attā mai uppaj‍jati ca vaiti cā’ti ic‍cassa aivamāgataṃ hauti| tasmā taṃ na upapaj‍jati – ‘cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ attā’ti yau vadaiyya| iti cakkhu anattā, rūpā anattā, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā|

‘‘‘cakkhusamphassau attā’ti yau vadaiyya taṃ na upapaj‍jati| cakkhusamphassassa uppādaupi vayaupi pañ‍ñāyati| yassa khau pana uppādaupi vayaupi pañ‍ñāyati, ‘attā mai uppaj‍jati ca vaiti cā’ti ic‍cassa aivamāgataṃ hauti| tasmā taṃ na upapaj‍jati – ‘cakkhusamphassau attā’ti yau  vadaiyya| iti cakkhu anattā, rūpā anattā, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā, cakkhusamphassau anattā|

‘‘‘vaidanā  attā’ti yau vadaiyya taṃ  na upapaj‍jati| vaidanāya uppādaupi vayaupi pañ‍ñāyati| yassa khau pana uppādaupi vayaupi pañ‍ñāyati, ‘attā mai uppaj‍jati ca vaiti cā’ti ic‍cassa aivamāgataṃ hauti| tasmā taṃ na upapaj‍jati – ‘vaidanā attā’ti yau vadaiyya| iti cakkhu anattā, rūpā anattā, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā, cakkhusamphassau anattā, vaidanā anattā|

‘‘‘taṇhā attā’ti yau vadaiyya taṃ na upapaj‍jati| taṇhāya uppādaupi vayaupi pañ‍ñāyati| yassa khau pana uppādaupi vayaupi pañ‍ñāyati, ‘attā mai uppaj‍jati ca vaiti cā’ti ic‍cassa aivamāgataṃ hauti| tasmā taṃ na upapaj‍jati – ‘taṇhā attā’ti yau vadaiyya| iti cakkhu anattā, rūpā anattā, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā, cakkhusamphassau anattā, vaidanā anattā, taṇhā anattā|

423. ‘‘‘sautaṃ attā’ti yau vadaiyya…pai॰… ‘ghānaṃ attā’ti yau vadaiyya… ‘jivhā attā’ti yau vadaiyya… ‘kāyau attā’ti yau vadaiyya… ‘manau attā’ti yau vadaiyya taṃ na upapaj‍jati| manassa uppādaupi vayaupi pañ‍ñāyati| yassa khau pana uppādaupi vayaupi  pañ‍ñāyati, ‘attā mai uppaj‍jati ca vaiti cā’ti ic‍cassa aivamāgataṃ hauti| tasmā taṃ na upapaj‍jati – ‘manau attā’ti yau vadaiyya| iti manau anattā|

‘‘‘dhammā attā’ti yau vadaiyya taṃ na upapaj‍jati| dhammānaṃ uppādaupi vayaupi pañ‍ñāyati| yassa khau pana uppādaupi vayaupi pañ‍ñāyati, ‘attā mai uppaj‍jati ca vaiti cā’ti ic‍cassa aivamāgataṃ hauti| tasmā taṃ na upapaj‍jati – ‘dhammā attā’ti yau vadaiyya| iti manau anattā, dhammā anattā|

‘‘‘manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ attā’ti yau vadaiyya taṃ na upapaj‍jati| manauviñ‍ñāṇassa uppādaupi vayaupi pañ‍ñāyati| yassa khau pana uppādaupi vayaupi pañ‍ñāyati, ‘attā mai uppaj‍jati ca vaiti cā’ti ic‍cassa aivamāgataṃ hauti| tasmā taṃ na upapaj‍jati – ‘manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ attā’ti  yau vadaiyya| iti manau anattā, dhammā anattā, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā|

‘‘‘manausamphassau  attā’ti yau vadaiyya taṃ na upapaj‍jati| manausamphassassa uppādaupi vayaupi pañ‍ñāyati| yassa khau pana uppādaupi vayaupi pañ‍ñāyati, ‘attā mai uppaj‍jati ca vaiti cā’ti ic‍cassa aivamāgataṃ hauti| tasmā taṃ na upapaj‍jati – ‘manausamphassau attā’ti yau vadaiyya| iti manau anattā, dhammā anattā, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā, manausamphassau anattā|

‘‘‘vaidanā attā’ti yau vadaiyya taṃ na upapaj‍jati| vaidanāya uppādaupi vayaupi pañ‍ñāyati| yassa khau  pana uppādaupi  vayaupi pañ‍ñāyati, ‘attā mai uppaj‍jati ca vaiti cā’ti ic‍cassa aivamāgataṃ hauti| tasmā taṃ na upapaj‍jati – ‘vaidanā attā’ti yau vadaiyya| iti manau anattā, dhammā anattā, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā, manausamphassau anattā, vaidanā anattā|

‘‘‘taṇhā attā’ti yau vadaiyya taṃ na upapaj‍jati| taṇhāya uppādaupi vayaupi pañ‍ñāyati| yassa khau pana uppādaupi vayaupi pañ‍ñāyati, ‘attā mai uppaj‍jati ca vaiti cā’ti ic‍cassa aivamāgataṃ hauti| tasmā taṃ na upapaj‍jati – ‘taṇhā attā’ti yau vadaiyya| iti manau anattā, dhammā anattā, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā, manausamphassau anattā, vaidanā anattā, taṇhā anattā|

424. ‘‘ayaṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, sak‍kāyasamudayagāminī paṭipadā – cakkhuṃ ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassati; rūpai ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassati; cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassati; cakkhusamphassaṃ ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassati; vaidanaṃ ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassati; taṇhaṃ ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassati; sautaṃ ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassati…pai॰… ghānaṃ ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassati…pai॰… jivhaṃ ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti  samanupassati…pai॰… kāyaṃ ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassati…pai॰… manaṃ ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassati, dhammai ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassati, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassati, manausamphassaṃ ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassati, vaidanaṃ ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassati, taṇhaṃ ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassati|

‘‘ayaṃ  khau pana, bhikkhavai, sak‍kāyaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā – cakkhuṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassati| rūpai ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassati| cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassati| cakkhusamphassaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassati| vaidanaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassati| taṇhaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassati| sautaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassati…pai॰… ghānaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassati… jivhaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassati… kāyaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassati… manaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassati| dhammai ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassati| manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassati| manausamphassaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassati| vaidanaṃ ‘naitaṃ  mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassati| taṇhaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassati|

425. ‘‘cakkhuñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca rūpai ca uppaj‍jati cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati  phassau, phassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ  vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā| sau sukhāya vaidanāya phuṭṭhau samānau abhinandati abhivadati ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa rāgānusayau anusaiti| dukkhāya vaidanāya phuṭṭhau samānau saucati kilamati paridaivati urattāḷiṃ  kandati sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| tassa paṭighānusayau anusaiti| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya phuṭṭhau samānau tassā vaidanāya samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| tassa avij‍jānusayau anusaiti| sau vata, bhikkhavai, sukhāya vaidanāya rāgānusayaṃ appahāya dukkhāya vaidanāya paṭighānusayaṃ appaṭivinaudaitvā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya avij‍jānusayaṃ asamūhanitvā avij‍jaṃ appahāya vij‍jaṃ anuppādaitvā diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau bhavissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sautañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca saddai ca uppaj‍jati sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… ghānañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca gandhai ca uppaj‍jati ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… jivhañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca rasai ca uppaj‍jati jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca phauṭṭhabbai ca uppaj‍jati kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… manañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca dhammai ca uppaj‍jati manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau, phassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā| sau sukhāya vaidanāya phuṭṭhau samānau abhinandati abhivadati ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa rāgānusayau anusaiti| dukkhāya vaidanāya phuṭṭhau samānau saucati kilamati paridaivati urattāḷiṃ  kandati sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| tassa paṭighānusayau anusaiti| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya phuṭṭhau samānau tassā vaidanāya samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| tassa avij‍jānusayau anusaiti| sau vata, bhikkhavai, sukhāya vaidanāya rāgānusayaṃ appahāya dukkhāya vaidanāya paṭighānusayaṃ appaṭivinaudaitvā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya avij‍jānusayaṃ asamūhanitvā avij‍jaṃ appahāya vij‍jaṃ anuppādaitvā diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau bhavissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

426. ‘‘cakkhuñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca rūpai ca uppaj‍jati cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau, phassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā| sau sukhāya vaidanāya phuṭṭhau samānau nābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa rāgānusayau nānusaiti| dukkhāya vaidanāya phuṭṭhau samānau na saucati na kilamati paridaivati na urattāḷiṃ kandati  na sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| tassa paṭighānusayau nānusaiti| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya phuṭṭhau samānau tassā  vaidanāya samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| tassa avij‍jānusayau nānusaiti| sau vata, bhikkhavai, sukhāya vaidanāya rāgānusayaṃ pahāya dukkhāya vaidanāya paṭighānusayaṃ paṭivinaudaitvā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya avij‍jānusayaṃ samūhanitvā avij‍jaṃ pahāya vij‍jaṃ uppādaitvā diṭṭhaiva dhammai  dukkhassantakarau bhavissatīti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sautañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca saddai ca uppaj‍jati sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰…|

‘‘ghānañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca gandhai ca uppaj‍jati ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰…|

‘‘jivhañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca rasai ca uppaj‍jati jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰…|

‘‘kāyañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca phauṭṭhabbai ca uppaj‍jati kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰…|

‘‘manañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca dhammai ca uppaj‍jati manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau, phassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā| sau sukhāya vaidanāya phuṭṭhau samānau nābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa rāgānusayau nānusaiti| dukkhāya vaidanāya phuṭṭhau samānau na saucati na kilamati na paridaivati na urattāḷiṃ kandati na sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| tassa paṭighānusayau nānusaiti| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya phuṭṭhau samānau tassā vaidanāya samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| tassa avij‍jānusayau nānusaiti| sau vata, bhikkhavai, sukhāya vaidanāya rāgānusayaṃ pahāya dukkhāya vaidanāya paṭighānusayaṃ paṭivinaudaitvā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya avij‍jānusayaṃ samūhanitvā avij‍jaṃ pahāya vij‍jaṃ uppādaitvā diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau bhavissatīti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati|

427. ‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau cakkhusmiṃ cakkhusmimpi (syā॰ kaṃ॰) aivamitaraisupi nibbindati, rūpaisu nibbindati, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇai nibbindati, cakkhusamphassai  nibbindati, vaidanāya nibbindati, taṇhāya nibbindati| sautasmiṃ nibbindati, saddaisu nibbindati…pai॰…  ghānasmiṃ nibbindati, gandhaisu nibbindati… jivhāya nibbindati, rasaisu nibbindati… kāyasmiṃ nibbindati, phauṭṭhabbaisu nibbindati… manasmiṃ nibbindati, dhammaisu nibbindati, manauviñ‍ñāṇai nibbindati, manausamphassai nibbindati, vaidanāya nibbindati, taṇhāya nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati, virāgā vimuc‍cati| vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ  hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti| imasmiṃ khau pana vaiyyākaraṇasmiṃ bhañ‍ñamānai saṭṭhimattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ anupādāya āsavaihi cittāni vimuc‍ciṃsūti|

chachak‍kasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. mahāsaḷāyatanikasuttaṃ

428. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘mahāsaḷāyatanikaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

429. ‘‘cakkhuṃ, bhikkhavai, ajānaṃ apassaṃ yathābhūtaṃ, rūpai ajānaṃ apassaṃ yathābhūtaṃ, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ ajānaṃ apassaṃ yathābhūtaṃ, cakkhusamphassaṃ ajānaṃ apassaṃ yathābhūtaṃ, yamidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi ajānaṃ apassaṃ yathābhūtaṃ, cakkhusmiṃ sāraj‍jati, rūpaisu sāraj‍jati, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇai sāraj‍jati, cakkhusamphassai sāraj‍jati, yamidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tasmimpi sāraj‍jati|

‘‘tassa sārattassa saṃyuttassa sammūḷhassa assādānupassinau viharatau āyatiṃ pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā upacayaṃ gacchanti| taṇhā cassa paunaubbhavikā nandīrāgasahagatā tatratatrābhinandinī, sā cassa pavaḍḍhati| tassa kāyikāpi darathā pavaḍḍhanti, caitasikāpi  darathā pavaḍḍhanti; kāyikāpi santāpā pavaḍḍhanti, caitasikāpi santāpā pavaḍḍhanti; kāyikāpi pariḷāhā pavaḍḍhanti, caitasikāpi pariḷāhā pavaḍḍhanti| sau kāyadukkhampi kāyikadukkhampi (syā॰ kaṃ॰), kāyikaṃ dukkhampi (ka॰) caitaudukkhampi paṭisaṃvaidaiti|

‘‘sautaṃ, bhikkhavai, ajānaṃ apassaṃ yathābhūtaṃ…pai॰… ghānaṃ, bhikkhavai, ajānaṃ apassaṃ yathābhūtaṃ…pai॰… jivhaṃ, bhikkhavai, ajānaṃ apassaṃ yathābhūtaṃ…pai॰… kāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, ajānaṃ apassaṃ yathābhūtaṃ…pai॰… manaṃ, bhikkhavai, ajānaṃ apassaṃ yathābhūtaṃ, dhammai, bhikkhavai, ajānaṃ apassaṃ yathābhūtaṃ, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, ajānaṃ apassaṃ yathābhūtaṃ, manausamphassaṃ, bhikkhavai, ajānaṃ apassaṃ yathābhūtaṃ, yamidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi ajānaṃ apassaṃ yathābhūtaṃ, manasmiṃ sāraj‍jati, dhammaisu sāraj‍jati, manauviñ‍ñāṇai sāraj‍jati, manausamphassai sāraj‍jati, yamidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tasmimpi sāraj‍jati|

‘‘tassa sārattassa saṃyuttassa sammūḷhassa assādānupassinau viharatau āyatiṃ pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā upacayaṃ gacchanti| taṇhā cassa paunaubbhavikā nandīrāgasahagatā tatratatrābhinandinī, sā cassa pavaḍḍhati| tassa kāyikāpi darathā pavaḍḍhanti, caitasikāpi darathā pavaḍḍhanti; kāyikāpi santāpā pavaḍḍhanti, caitasikāpi santāpā pavaḍḍhanti; kāyikāpi pariḷāhā pavaḍḍhanti, caitasikāpi pariḷāhā pavaḍḍhanti| sau kāyadukkhampi caitaudukkhampi paṭisaṃvaidaiti|

430. ‘‘cakkhuñ‍ca  khau, bhikkhavai, jānaṃ passaṃ yathābhūtaṃ, rūpai jānaṃ passaṃ yathābhūtaṃ, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ jānaṃ passaṃ yathābhūtaṃ, cakkhusamphassaṃ jānaṃ passaṃ yathābhūtaṃ, yamidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi jānaṃ passaṃ yathābhūtaṃ, cakkhusmiṃ na sāraj‍jati, rūpaisu na sāraj‍jati, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇai na sāraj‍jati, cakkhusamphassai na sāraj‍jati, yamidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tasmimpi na sāraj‍jati|

‘‘tassa asārattassa asaṃyuttassa asammūḷhassa ādīnavānupassinau viharatau āyatiṃ pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā apacayaṃ gacchanti| taṇhā cassa paunaubbhavikā nandīrāgasahagatā tatratatrābhinandinī, sā cassa pahīyati| tassa kāyikāpi darathā pahīyanti, caitasikāpi darathā pahīyanti; kāyikāpi santāpā pahīyanti, caitasikāpi santāpā pahīyanti; kāyikāpi pariḷāhā pahīyanti, caitasikāpi pariḷāhā pahīyanti| sau kāyasukhampi caitausukhampi paṭisaṃvaidaiti|

431. ‘‘yā  tathābhūtassa yathābhūtassa (sī॰ pī॰) diṭṭhi sāssa hauti sammādiṭṭhi; yau tathābhūtassa yathābhūtassa (sī॰ pī॰) saṅkappau svāssa hauti  sammāsaṅkappau; yau tathābhūtassa yathābhūtassa (sī॰ pī॰) vāyāmau svāssa hauti sammāvāyāmau; yā tathābhūtassa yathābhūtassa (sī॰ pī॰) sati sāssa hauti sammāsati; yau tathābhūtassa yathābhūtassa (sī॰ pī॰) samādhi svāssa hauti sammāsamādhi| pubbaiva khau panassa kāyakammaṃ vacīkammaṃ ājīvau suparisuddhau hauti| aivamassāyaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati|

‘‘tassa  aivaṃ imaṃ ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvayatau cattāraupi satipaṭṭhānā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, cattāraupi sammappadhānā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, cattāraupi iddhipādā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, pañ‍capi indriyāni bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, pañ‍capi balāni bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, sattapi baujjhaṅgā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti|

‘‘tassimai dvai dhammā yuganandhā yuganaddhā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) vattanti – samathau ca vipassanā ca| sau yai dhammā abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñaiyyā tai dhammai abhiñ‍ñā parijānāti| yai dhammā abhiñ‍ñā pahātabbā tai dhammai abhiñ‍ñā pajahati| yai dhammā abhiñ‍ñā bhāvaitabbā tai dhammai abhiñ‍ñā bhāvaiti| yai dhammā abhiñ‍ñā sacchikātabbā tai dhammai abhiñ‍ñā sacchikarauti|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, dhammā abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñaiyyā? ‘pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā’ tissa vacanīyaṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – rūpupādānakkhandhau, vaidanupādānakkhandhau, sañ‍ñupādānakkhandhau, saṅkhārupādānakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau| imai dhammā abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñaiyyā|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, dhammā abhiñ‍ñā pahātabbā? avij‍jā ca bhavataṇhā ca – imai dhammā abhiñ‍ñā pahātabbā|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, dhammā abhiñ‍ñā bhāvaitabbā? samathau ca vipassanā ca – imai dhammā abhiñ‍ñā bhāvaitabbā|

‘‘katamai, bhikkhavai, dhammā abhiñ‍ñā sacchikātabbā? vij‍jā ca vimutti ca – imai dhammā abhiñ‍ñā sacchikātabbā|

432. ‘‘sautaṃ, bhikkhavai, jānaṃ passaṃ yathābhūtaṃ…pai॰… ghānaṃ bhikkhavai, jānaṃ  passaṃ yathābhūtaṃ…pai॰… jivhaṃ, bhikkhavai, jānaṃ passaṃ yathābhūtaṃ… kāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, jānaṃ passaṃ yathābhūtaṃ… manaṃ, bhikkhavai, jānaṃ passaṃ yathābhūtaṃ, dhammai jānaṃ passaṃ yathābhūtaṃ, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ jānaṃ passaṃ yathābhūtaṃ, manausamphassaṃ jānaṃ passaṃ yathābhūtaṃ, yamidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi jānaṃ passaṃ yathābhūtaṃ, manasmiṃ na sāraj‍jati, dhammaisu na sāraj‍jati, manauviñ‍ñāṇai na sāraj‍jati, manausamphassai na sāraj‍jati, yamidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tasmimpi na sāraj‍jati|

‘‘tassa asārattassa asaṃyuttassa asammūḷhassa ādīnavānupassinau viharatau āyatiṃ pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā apacayaṃ gacchanti| taṇhā cassa paunaubbhavikā nandīrāgasahagatā tatratatrābhinandinī, sā cassa pahīyati| tassa kāyikāpi darathā pahīyanti, caitasikāpi darathā pahīyanti; kāyikāpi santāpā pahīyanti, caitasikāpi santāpā pahīyanti; kāyikāpi pariḷāhā pahīyanti, caitasikāpi pariḷāhā pahīyanti| sau kāyasukhampi caitausukhampi paṭisaṃvaidaiti|

433. ‘‘yā tathābhūtassa diṭṭhi sāssa hauti sammādiṭṭhi; yau tathābhūtassa saṅkappau svāssa hauti sammāsaṅkappau; yau tathābhūtassa vāyāmau svāssa hauti sammāvāyāmau; yā tathābhūtassa sati sāssa hauti sammāsati; yau tathābhūtassa samādhi svāssa hauti sammāsamādhi| pubbaiva khau panassa kāyakammaṃ vacīkammaṃ ājīvau suparisuddhau hauti| aivamassāyaṃ  ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati|

‘‘tassa aivaṃ imaṃ ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvayatau cattāraupi satipaṭṭhānā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, cattāraupi sammappadhānā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, cattāraupi iddhipādā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, pañ‍capi indriyāni bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, pañ‍capi balāni bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, sattapi baujjhaṅgā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti|

‘‘tassimai dvai dhammā yuganandhā vattanti – samathau ca vipassanā ca| sau yai dhammā abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñaiyyā tai dhammai abhiñ‍ñā parijānāti| yai  dhammā abhiñ‍ñā pahātabbā tai dhammai abhiñ‍ñā pajahati| yai dhammā abhiñ‍ñā bhāvaitabbā tai dhammai abhiñ‍ñā bhāvaiti| yai dhammā abhiñ‍ñā sacchikātabbā tai dhammai abhiñ‍ñā sacchikarauti|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, dhammā abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñaiyyā? ‘pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā’ tissa vacanīyaṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – rūpupādānakkhandhau, vaidanupādānakkhandhau, sañ‍ñupādānakkhandhau, saṅkhārupādānakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau| imai dhammā abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñaiyyā|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, dhammā abhiñ‍ñā pahātabbā? avij‍jā ca bhavataṇhā ca – imai dhammā abhiñ‍ñā pahātabbā|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, dhammā abhiñ‍ñā bhāvaitabbā? samathau ca vipassanā ca – imai dhammā abhiñ‍ñā bhāvaitabbā|

‘‘katamai  ca, bhikkhavai, dhammā abhiñ‍ñā sacchikātabbā? vij‍jā ca vimutti ca – imai dhammā abhiñ‍ñā sacchikātabbā’’ti|

idamavauca  bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

mahāsaḷāyatanikasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. nagaravindaiyyasuttaṃ

434. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausalaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ yaina nagaravindaṃ nāma kausalānaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ gāmau tadavasari| assausuṃ khau nagaravindaiyyakā nagaravindaiyyā (ka॰) brāhmaṇagahapatikā – ‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau kausalaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ  nagaravindaṃ anuppattau| taṃ khau pana bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| sau  imaṃ laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ samārakaṃ sabrahmakaṃ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ pajaṃ sadaivamanussaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti| sau dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| sādhu khau pana tathārūpānaṃ arahataṃ dassanaṃ hautī’’ti|

atha khau nagaravindaiyyakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā appaikac‍cai bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| appaikac‍cai bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu; sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| appaikac‍cai yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| appaikac‍cai bhagavatau santikai nāmagauttaṃ sāvaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| appaikac‍cai tuṇhībhūtā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nai khau nagaravindaiyyakai brāhmaṇagahapatikai bhagavā aitadavauca –

435. ‘‘sacai vau, gahapatayau, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘kathaṃbhūtā, gahapatayau, samaṇabrāhmaṇā na sak‍kātabbā na garukātabbā na mānaitabbā na pūjaitabbā’ti? aivaṃ puṭṭhā tumhai, gahapatayau, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ byākaraiyyātha – ‘yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaisu rūpaisu avītarāgā avītadausā avītamauhā, ajjhattaṃ avūpasantacittā, samavisamaṃ caranti kāyaina vācāya manasā, aivarūpā samaṇabrāhmaṇā na sak‍kātabbā na garukātabbā na mānaitabbā na pūjaitabbā| taṃ kissa haitu? mayampi hi cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaisu  rūpaisu avītarāgā avītadausā avītamauhā, ajjhattaṃ avūpasantacittā, samavisamaṃ carāma kāyaina vācāya manasā, taisaṃ nau samacariyampi haitaṃ uttari apassataṃ| tasmā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā na sak‍kātabbā na garukātabbā na mānaitabbā na pūjaitabbā| yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sautaviñ‍ñaiyyaisu saddaisu… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyaisu gandhaisu… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyaisu rasaisu… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyaisu phauṭṭhabbaisu… manauviñ‍ñaiyyaisu dhammaisu avītarāgā avītadausā avītamauhā, ajjhattaṃ avūpasantacittā, samavisamaṃ caranti kāyaina vācāya manasā, aivarūpā samaṇabrāhmaṇā na sak‍kātabbā na garukātabbā na mānaitabbā na pūjaitabbā| taṃ kissa haitu? mayampi hi manauviñ‍ñaiyyaisu dhammaisu  avītarāgā avītadausā avītamauhā, ajjhattaṃ  avūpasantacittā, samavisamaṃ carāma kāyaina vācāya manasā, taisaṃ nau samacariyampi haitaṃ uttari apassataṃ| tasmā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā na sak‍kātabbā na garukātabbā na mānaitabbā na pūjaitabbā’ti| aivaṃ puṭṭhā tumhai, gahapatayau, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ byākaraiyyātha|

436. ‘‘sacai pana vau, gahapatayau, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘kathaṃbhūtā, gahapatayau, samaṇabrāhmaṇā sak‍kātabbā garukātabbā mānaitabbā pūjaitabbā’ti? aivaṃ puṭṭhā tumhai, gahapatayau, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ byākaraiyyātha – ‘yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaisu rūpaisu vītarāgā vītadausā vītamauhā, ajjhattaṃ vūpasantacittā, samacariyaṃ caranti kāyaina vācāya manasā, aivarūpā samaṇabrāhmaṇā sak‍kātabbā garukātabbā mānaitabbā pūjaitabbā| taṃ kissa haitu? mayampi hi mayaṃ hi (?) cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaisu rūpaisu avītarāgā avītadausā avītamauhā, ajjhattaṃ avūpasantacittā, samavisamaṃ carāma kāyaina vācāya manasā, taisaṃ nau samacariyampi haitaṃ uttari passataṃ| tasmā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā sak‍kātabbā garukātabbā mānaitabbā pūjaitabbā| yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sautaviñ‍ñaiyyaisu saddaisu… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyaisu gandhaisu… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyaisu rasaisu… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyaisu phauṭṭhabbaisu… manauviñ‍ñaiyyaisu  dhammaisu vītarāgā vītadausā vītamauhā, ajjhattaṃ vūpasantacittā, samacariyaṃ caranti kāyaina vācāya manasā, aivarūpā samaṇabrāhmaṇā sak‍kātabbā garukātabbā mānaitabbā pūjaitabbā| taṃ kissa haitu? mayampi hi manauviñ‍ñaiyyaisu dhammaisu avītarāgā avītadausā avītamauhā ajjhattaṃ avūpasantacittā, samavisamaṃ carāma kāyaina vācāya manasā, taisaṃ nau samacariyampi haitaṃ uttari passataṃ| tasmā tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā sak‍kātabbā garukātabbā mānaitabbā pūjaitabbā’ti| aivaṃ puṭṭhā tumhai, gahapatayau, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ byākaraiyyātha|

437. ‘‘sacai pana vau sacai tai (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰), gahapatayau, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘kai panāyasmantānaṃ ākārā, kai anvayā, yaina tumhai āyasmantau  aivaṃ vadaitha? addhā tai āyasmantau vītarāgā  vā rāgavinayāya vā paṭipan‍nā, vītadausā vā dausavinayāya vā paṭipan‍nā, vītamauhā vā mauhavinayāya vā paṭipan‍nā’ti? aivaṃ puṭṭhā tumhai, gahapatayau, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ byākaraiyyātha – ‘tathā hi tai āyasmantau arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni paṭisaivanti| natthi khau pana tattha tathārūpā cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā yai disvā disvā abhiramaiyyuṃ, natthi khau pana tattha tathārūpā sautaviñ‍ñaiyyā saddā yai sutvā sutvā abhiramaiyyuṃ, natthi khau pana tattha tathārūpā ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyā gandhā yai ghāyitvā ghāyitvā abhiramaiyyuṃ, natthi khau pana tattha tathārūpā jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā yai sāyitvā sāyitvā abhiramaiyyuṃ, natthi khau pana tattha tathārūpā kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā yai phusitvā phusitvā abhiramaiyyuṃ| imai khau nau, āvusau, ākārā, imai anvayā, yaina mayaṃ yaina mayaṃ āyasmantau (sī॰ pī॰), yaina mayaṃ āyasmantai (syā॰ kaṃ॰) aivaṃ vadaima – addhā tai āyasmantau vītarāgā vā rāgavinayāya vā paṭipan‍nā, vītadausā vā dausavinayāya vā paṭipan‍nā, vītamauhā vā mauhavinayāya vā paṭipan‍nā’ti| aivaṃ puṭṭhā tumhai, gahapatayau, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ byākaraiyyāthā’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, nagaravindaiyyakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama! saiyyathāpi, bhau gautama, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya – ‘cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantī’ti; aivamaivaṃ bhautā gautamaina anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aitai mayaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāma dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakai nau bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitai saraṇaṃ gatai’’ti|

nagaravindaiyyasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. piṇḍapātapārisuddhisuttaṃ

438. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā  vuṭṭhitau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘vippasan‍nāni  khau tai, sāriputta, indriyāni, parisuddhau chavivaṇṇau pariyaudātau| katamaina khau tvaṃ, sāriputta, vihāraina aitarahi bahulaṃ viharasī’’ti? ‘‘suñ‍ñatāvihāraina khau ahaṃ, bhantai, aitarahi bahulaṃ viharāmī’’ti| ‘‘sādhu, sādhu, sāriputta! mahāpurisavihāraina kira tvaṃ, sāriputta, aitarahi bahulaṃ viharasi| mahāpurisavihārau aisau haisa (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), sāriputta, yadidaṃ – suñ‍ñatā| tasmātiha, sāriputta, bhikkhu sacai ākaṅkhaiyya – ‘suñ‍ñatāvihāraina bahulaṃ aitarahi bahulaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) viharaiyya’nti, taina, sāriputta, bhikkhunā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhitabbaṃ – ‘yaina cāhaṃ maggaina gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisiṃ, yasmiñ‍ca padaisai piṇḍāya acariṃ, yaina ca maggaina gāmatau piṇḍāya paṭik‍kamiṃ, atthi nu khau mai tattha cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaisu rūpaisu chandau vā rāgau vā dausau vā mauhau vā paṭighaṃ vāpi caitasau’ti? sacai, sāriputta, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘yaina cāhaṃ maggaina gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisiṃ, yasmiñ‍ca padaisai piṇḍāya acariṃ, yaina ca maggaina gāmatau piṇḍāya paṭik‍kamiṃ, atthi mai tattha cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaisu rūpaisu chandau vā rāgau vā dausau vā mauhau vā paṭighaṃ vāpi caitasau’ti, taina, sāriputta, bhikkhunā taisaṃyaiva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ| sacai pana, sāriputta, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘yaina cāhaṃ maggaina gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisiṃ, yasmiñ‍ca padaisai piṇḍāya acariṃ, yaina  ca maggaina gāmatau piṇḍāya paṭik‍kamiṃ, natthi mai tattha cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaisu rūpaisu chandau vā rāgau vā dausau vā mauhau vā paṭighaṃ vāpi caitasau’ti, taina, sāriputta, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu|

439. ‘‘puna  caparaṃ, sāriputta, bhikkhunā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhitabbaṃ – ‘yaina cāhaṃ maggaina gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisiṃ, yasmiñ‍ca padaisai piṇḍāya acariṃ, yaina ca maggaina gāmatau piṇḍāya paṭik‍kamiṃ, atthi nu khau mai tattha sautaviñ‍ñaiyyaisu saddaisu…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyaisu gandhaisu… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyaisu rasaisu … kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyaisu phauṭṭhabbaisu… manauviñ‍ñaiyyaisu dhammaisu chandau vā rāgau vā dausau vā mauhau vā paṭighaṃ vāpi caitasau’ti? sacai, sāriputta, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘yaina cāhaṃ maggaina gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisiṃ, yasmiñ‍ca padaisai piṇḍāya acariṃ, yaina ca maggaina gāmatau piṇḍāya paṭik‍kamiṃ, atthi mai tattha manauviñ‍ñaiyyaisu dhammaisu chandau vā rāgau vā dausau  vā mauhau vā paṭighaṃ vāpi caitasau’ti, taina, sāriputta, bhikkhunā taisaṃyaiva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ| sacai pana, sāriputta, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘yaina cāhaṃ maggaina gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisiṃ, yasmiñ‍ca padaisai piṇḍāya acariṃ, yaina ca maggaina gāmatau piṇḍāya paṭik‍kamiṃ, natthi mai tattha manauviñ‍ñaiyyaisu dhammaisu chandau vā rāgau vā dausau vā mauhau vā paṭighaṃ vāpi caitasau’ti, taina, sāriputta, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu|

440. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, sāriputta, bhikkhunā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhitabbaṃ – ‘pahīnā nu khau mai pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā’ti? sacai, sāriputta, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘appahīnā khau mai pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā’ti, taina, sāriputta, bhikkhunā pañ‍can‍naṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ| sacai pana, sāriputta, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘pahīnā khau mai pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā’ti, taina, sāriputta, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu|

441. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, sāriputta, bhikkhunā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhitabbaṃ – ‘pahīnā nu khau mai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā’ti? sacai, sāriputta, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘appahīnā khau mai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā’ti, taina, sāriputta, bhikkhunā pañ‍can‍naṃ nīvaraṇānaṃ pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ| sacai pana, sāriputta, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘pahīnā khau  mai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā’ti, taina, sāriputta, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu|

442. ‘‘puna  caparaṃ, sāriputta, bhikkhunā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhitabbaṃ – ‘pariñ‍ñātā nu khau mai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā’ti? sacai, sāriputta, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘apariñ‍ñātā khau mai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā’ti, taina, sāriputta, bhikkhunā  pañ‍can‍naṃ upādānakkhandhānaṃ pariñ‍ñāya vāyamitabbaṃ| sacai pana, sāriputta, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau  aivaṃ jānāti – ‘pariñ‍ñātā khau mai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā’ti, taina, sāriputta, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu|

443. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, sāriputta, bhikkhunā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhitabbaṃ – ‘bhāvitā nu khau mai cattārau satipaṭṭhānā’ti? sacai, sāriputta, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘abhāvitā khau mai cattārau satipaṭṭhānā’ti, taina, sāriputta, bhikkhunā catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvanāya vāyamitabbaṃ| sacai pana, sāriputta, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘bhāvitā khau mai cattārau satipaṭṭhānā’ti, taina, sāriputta, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu|

444. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, sāriputta, bhikkhunā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhitabbaṃ – ‘bhāvitā nu khau mai cattārau sammappadhānā’ti? sacai, sāriputta, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘abhāvitā khau mai cattārau sammappadhānā’ti, taina, sāriputta, bhikkhunā catun‍naṃ sammappadhānānaṃ bhāvanāya vāyamitabbaṃ| sacai pana, sāriputta, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘bhāvitā khau mai cattārau sammappadhānā’ti, taina, sāriputta, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu|

445. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, sāriputta, bhikkhunā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhitabbaṃ – ‘bhāvitā nu khau mai cattārau iddhipādā’ti? sacai, sāriputta, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘abhāvitā khau mai cattārau iddhipādā’ti, taina, sāriputta, bhikkhunā catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvanāya vāyamitabbaṃ| sacai pana, sāriputta, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘bhāvitā khau mai cattārau iddhipādā’ti, taina, sāriputta, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu|

446. ‘‘puna  caparaṃ, sāriputta, bhikkhunā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhitabbaṃ – ‘bhāvitāni nu khau mai pañ‍cindriyānī’ti? sacai, sāriputta, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘abhāvitāni khau mai pañ‍cindriyānī’ti, taina, sāriputta, bhikkhunā pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ bhāvanāya vāyamitabbaṃ| sacai pana, sāriputta, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau  aivaṃ jānāti – ‘bhāvitāni khau mai pañ‍cindriyānī’ti, taina, sāriputta, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu|

447. ‘‘puna  caparaṃ, sāriputta, bhikkhunā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhitabbaṃ – ‘bhāvitāni nu khau mai pañ‍ca balānī’ti? sacai, sāriputta, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘abhāvitāni khau mai pañ‍ca balānī’ti, taina, sāriputta, bhikkhunā pañ‍can‍naṃ balānaṃ bhāvanāya vāyamitabbaṃ| sacai pana, sāriputta, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘bhāvitāni khau mai pañ‍ca balānī’ti, taina, sāriputta, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu|

448. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, sāriputta, bhikkhunā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhitabbaṃ – ‘bhāvitā nu khau mai satta baujjhaṅgā’ti? sacai, sāriputta, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘abhāvitā khau mai satta baujjhaṅgā’ti, taina, sāriputta, bhikkhunā sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ bhāvanāya vāyamitabbaṃ| sacai pana, sāriputta, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘bhāvitā khau mai satta baujjhaṅgā’ti, taina, sāriputta, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu|

449. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, sāriputta, bhikkhunā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhitabbaṃ – ‘bhāvitau nu khau mai ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau’ti? sacai, sāriputta, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘abhāvitau khau mai ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau’ti, taina, sāriputta, bhikkhunā ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa bhāvanāya vāyamitabbaṃ| sacai pana, sāriputta, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘bhāvitau khau mai ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau  maggau’ti, taina, sāriputta, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu|

450. ‘‘puna  caparaṃ, sāriputta, bhikkhunā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhitabbaṃ – ‘bhāvitā nu khau mai samathau ca vipassanā cā’ti? sacai, sāriputta, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘abhāvitā khau mai samathau ca vipassanā cā’ti, taina, sāriputta, bhikkhunā samathavipassanānaṃ bhāvanāya vāyamitabbaṃ| sacai pana, sāriputta, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘bhāvitā khau  mai samathau ca vipassanā cā’ti, taina, sāriputta, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu|

451. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, sāriputta, bhikkhunā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhitabbaṃ – ‘sacchikatā nu khau mai vij‍jā ca vimutti cā’ti? sacai, sāriputta, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘asacchikatā khau mai vij‍jā ca vimutti cā’ti, taina, sāriputta, bhikkhunā vij‍jāya vimuttiyā sacchikiriyāya vāyamitabbaṃ| sacai pana, sāriputta, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘sacchikatā khau mai vij‍jā ca vimutti cā’ti, taina, sāriputta, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu|

452. ‘‘yai hi kaici, sāriputta, atītamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā piṇḍapātaṃ parisaudhaisuṃ, sabbai tai aivamaiva pac‍cavaikkhitvā  pac‍cavaikkhitvā piṇḍapātaṃ parisaudhaisuṃ| yaipi hi kaici, sāriputta, anāgatamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā piṇḍapātaṃ parisaudhaissanti, sabbai tai aivamaiva pac‍cavaikkhitvā pac‍cavaikkhitvā piṇḍapātaṃ parisaudhaissanti| yaipi hi kaici, sāriputta, aitarahi samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā piṇḍapātaṃ parisaudhainti, sabbai tai aivamaiva pac‍cavaikkhitvā pac‍cavaikkhitvā piṇḍapātaṃ parisaudhainti| tasmātiha, sāriputta vau sāriputta aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), ‘pac‍cavaikkhitvā pac‍cavaikkhitvā piṇḍapātaṃ parisaudhaissāmā’ti – aivañhi vau, sāriputta, sikkhitabba’’nti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

piṇḍapātapārisuddhisuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ navamaṃ|

10. indriyabhāvanāsuttaṃ

453. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā gajaṅgalāyaṃ kajaṅgalāyaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), kaj‍jaṅgalāyaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) viharati suvaiḷuvanai vaiḷuvanai (syā॰ kaṃ॰), mukhailuvanai (sī॰ pī॰)| atha khau uttarau māṇavau pārāsiviyantaivāsī pārāsariyantaivāsī (sī॰ pī॰), pārāsiriyantaivāsī (syā॰ kaṃ॰) yaina bhagavā  tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau uttaraṃ māṇavaṃ pārāsiviyantaivāsiṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘daisaiti, uttara, pārāsiviyau brāhmaṇau sāvakānaṃ indriyabhāvana’’nti? ‘‘daisaiti, bhau gautama, pārāsiviyau brāhmaṇau sāvakānaṃ indriyabhāvana’’nti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana, uttara, daisaiti pārāsiviyau brāhmaṇau sāvakānaṃ indriyabhāvana’’nti? ‘‘idha, bhau gautama, cakkhunā rūpaṃ na passati, sautaina saddaṃ na suṇāti – aivaṃ khau, bhau gautama, daisaiti pārāsiviyau brāhmaṇau sāvakānaṃ indriyabhāvana’’nti| ‘‘aivaṃ santai khau, uttara, andhau bhāvitindriyau bhavissati, badhirau bhāvitindriyau bhavissati; yathā pārāsiviyassa brāhmaṇassa vacanaṃ| andhau hi, uttara, cakkhunā rūpaṃ na passati, badhirau sautaina saddaṃ na suṇātī’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, uttarau māṇavau pārāsiviyantaivāsī tuṇhībhūtau maṅkubhūtau pattakkhandhau adhaumukhau pajjhāyantau appaṭibhānau nisīdi|

atha khau bhagavā uttaraṃ māṇavaṃ pārāsiviyantaivāsiṃ tuṇhībhūtaṃ  maṅkubhūtaṃ pattakkhandhaṃ adhaumukhaṃ pajjhāyantaṃ appaṭibhānaṃ viditvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘añ‍ñathā khau, ānanda, daisaiti pārāsiviyau brāhmaṇau sāvakānaṃ indriyabhāvanaṃ, añ‍ñathā ca panānanda, ariyassa vinayai anuttarā indriyabhāvanā hautī’’ti| ‘‘aitassa, bhagavā, kālau; aitassa, sugata, kālau yaṃ bhagavā ariyassa  vinayai anuttaraṃ indriyabhāvanaṃ daisaiyya| bhagavatau sutvā bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti| ‘‘tainahānanda, suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca –

454. ‘‘kathañ‍cānanda, ariyassa vinayai anuttarā indriyabhāvanā hauti? idhānanda, bhikkhunau cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā uppaj‍jati manāpaṃ, uppaj‍jati amanāpaṃ, uppaj‍jati manāpāmanāpaṃ| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘uppan‍naṃ khau mai idaṃ manāpaṃ, uppan‍naṃ amanāpaṃ, uppan‍naṃ manāpāmanāpaṃ| tañ‍ca khau saṅkhataṃ auḷārikaṃ paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ| aitaṃ santaṃ aitaṃ paṇītaṃ yadidaṃ – upaikkhā’ti| tassa taṃ uppan‍naṃ manāpaṃ uppan‍naṃ amanāpaṃ uppan‍naṃ manāpāmanāpaṃ nirujjhati; upaikkhā saṇṭhāti| saiyyathāpi, ānanda, cakkhumā purisau ummīlaitvā vā nimīlaiyya, nimīlaitvā vā ummīlaiyya; aivamaiva khau, ānanda, yassa kassaci aivaṃsīghaṃ aivaṃtuvaṭaṃ  aivaṃappakasiraina uppan‍naṃ manāpaṃ uppan‍naṃ amanāpaṃ uppan‍naṃ manāpāmanāpaṃ nirujjhati, upaikkhā saṇṭhāti – ayaṃ vuc‍catānanda, ariyassa  vinayai anuttarā indriyabhāvanā cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaisu rūpaisu|

455. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhunau sautaina saddaṃ sutvā uppaj‍jati manāpaṃ, uppaj‍jati amanāpaṃ, uppaj‍jati manāpāmanāpaṃ| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘uppan‍naṃ khau mai idaṃ manāpaṃ, uppan‍naṃ amanāpaṃ, uppan‍naṃ manāpāmanāpaṃ| tañ‍ca khau saṅkhataṃ auḷārikaṃ paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ| aitaṃ santaṃ aitaṃ paṇītaṃ yadidaṃ – upaikkhā’ti| tassa taṃ uppan‍naṃ manāpaṃ uppan‍naṃ amanāpaṃ uppan‍naṃ manāpāmanāpaṃ nirujjhati; upaikkhā saṇṭhāti| saiyyathāpi, ānanda, balavā purisau appakasirainaiva accharaṃ accharikaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) paharaiyya; aivamaiva khau, ānanda, yassa kassaci aivaṃsīghaṃ aivaṃtuvaṭaṃ aivaṃappakasiraina uppan‍naṃ manāpaṃ uppan‍naṃ amanāpaṃ uppan‍naṃ manāpāmanāpaṃ nirujjhati, upaikkhā saṇṭhāti – ayaṃ vuc‍catānanda, ariyassa vinayai anuttarā indriyabhāvanā sautaviñ‍ñaiyyaisu saddaisu|

456. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhunau ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā uppaj‍jati manāpaṃ, uppaj‍jati amanāpaṃ, uppaj‍jati manāpāmanāpaṃ| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘uppan‍naṃ khau mai idaṃ manāpaṃ, uppan‍naṃ amanāpaṃ, uppan‍naṃ manāpāmanāpaṃ| tañ‍ca khau saṅkhataṃ auḷārikaṃ paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ| aitaṃ santaṃ aitaṃ paṇītaṃ yadidaṃ – upaikkhā’ti| tassa taṃ uppan‍naṃ manāpaṃ uppan‍naṃ amanāpaṃ uppan‍naṃ manāpāmanāpaṃ nirujjhati; upaikkhā saṇṭhāti| saiyyathāpi, ānanda, īsakaṃpauṇai īsakapauṇai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), īsakaphaṇai (sī॰ aṭṭha॰), ‘‘majjhai uc‍caṃ hutvā’’ti ṭīkāya saṃsanditabbā padumapalāsai  paduminipattai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) udakaphusitāni pavattanti, na saṇṭhanti; aivamaiva khau, ānanda, yassa kassaci aivaṃsīghaṃ aivaṃtuvaṭaṃ aivaṃappakasiraina uppan‍naṃ manāpaṃ uppan‍naṃ amanāpaṃ uppan‍naṃ manāpāmanāpaṃ nirujjhati, upaikkhā saṇṭhāti – ayaṃ vuc‍catānanda, ariyassa vinayai anuttarā indriyabhāvanā ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyaisu gandhaisu|

457. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhunau jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā uppaj‍jati manāpaṃ, uppaj‍jati  amanāpaṃ, uppaj‍jati manāpāmanāpaṃ| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘uppan‍naṃ khau mai idaṃ manāpaṃ, uppan‍naṃ amanāpaṃ, uppan‍naṃ manāpāmanāpaṃ| tañ‍ca khau saṅkhataṃ auḷārikaṃ paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ| aitaṃ santaṃ aitaṃ paṇītaṃ yadidaṃ – upaikkhā’ti| tassa taṃ uppan‍naṃ manāpaṃ uppan‍naṃ amanāpaṃ uppan‍naṃ manāpāmanāpaṃ nirujjhati; upaikkhā saṇṭhāti| saiyyathāpi, ānanda, balavā purisau jivhaggai khaiḷapiṇḍaṃ saṃyūhitvā appakasiraina vamaiyya sandhamaiyya (ka॰); aivamaiva khau, ānanda, yassa kassaci aivaṃsīghaṃ aivaṃtuvaṭaṃ aivaṃappakasiraina uppan‍naṃ manāpaṃ uppan‍naṃ amanāpaṃ uppan‍naṃ manāpāmanāpaṃ nirujjhati, upaikkhā saṇṭhāti – ayaṃ vuc‍catānanda, ariyassa vinayai anuttarā indriyabhāvanā jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyaisu rasaisu|

458. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhunau kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā uppaj‍jati manāpaṃ, uppaj‍jati amanāpaṃ, uppaj‍jati manāpāmanāpaṃ| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘uppan‍naṃ khau mai idaṃ manāpaṃ, uppan‍naṃ amanāpaṃ, uppan‍naṃ  manāpāmanāpaṃ| tañ‍ca khau saṅkhataṃ auḷārikaṃ paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ| aitaṃ santaṃ aitaṃ paṇītaṃ yadidaṃ – upaikkhā’ti| tassa taṃ uppan‍naṃ manāpaṃ uppan‍naṃ amanāpaṃ uppan‍naṃ manāpāmanāpaṃ nirujjhati; upaikkhā saṇṭhāti| saiyyathāpi, ānanda, balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya; aivamaiva khau, ānanda, yassa kassaci aivaṃsīghaṃ aivaṃtuvaṭaṃ aivaṃappakasiraina uppan‍naṃ manāpaṃ uppan‍naṃ amanāpaṃ uppan‍naṃ manāpāmanāpaṃ nirujjhati, upaikkhā saṇṭhāti – ayaṃ vuc‍catānanda, ariyassa vinayai anuttarā indriyabhāvanā kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyaisu phauṭṭhabbaisu|

459. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhunau manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya uppaj‍jati manāpaṃ, uppaj‍jati amanāpaṃ, uppaj‍jati manāpāmanāpaṃ| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘uppan‍naṃ khau mai idaṃ manāpaṃ, uppan‍naṃ amanāpaṃ, uppan‍naṃ manāpāmanāpaṃ| tañ‍ca khau saṅkhataṃ auḷārikaṃ paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ| aitaṃ santaṃ aitaṃ paṇītaṃ yadidaṃ – upaikkhā’ti| tassa taṃ uppan‍naṃ manāpaṃ uppan‍naṃ amanāpaṃ uppan‍naṃ manāpāmanāpaṃ nirujjhati; upaikkhā saṇṭhāti| saiyyathāpi, ānanda, balavā purisau divasaṃsantattai divasasantaittai (sī॰) ayaukaṭāhai dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni nipātaiyya| dandhau, ānanda, udakaphusitānaṃ nipātau, atha khau naṃ khippamaiva parikkhayaṃ pariyādānaṃ gacchaiyya; aivamaiva khau, ānanda, yassa kassaci aivaṃsīghaṃ aivaṃtuvaṭaṃ aivaṃappakasiraina uppan‍naṃ manāpaṃ uppan‍naṃ  amanāpaṃ uppan‍naṃ manāpāmanāpaṃ nirujjhati, upaikkhā saṇṭhāti – ayaṃ vuc‍catānanda, ariyassa vinayai anuttarā indriyabhāvanā manauviñ‍ñaiyyaisu dhammaisu| aivaṃ khau, ānanda, ariyassa vinayai anuttarā indriyabhāvanā hauti|

460. ‘‘kathañ‍cānanda, saikhau hauti pāṭipadau? idhānanda, bhikkhunau cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā uppaj‍jati manāpaṃ, uppaj‍jati amanāpaṃ, uppaj‍jati manāpāmanāpaṃ| sau  taina uppan‍naina manāpaina uppan‍naina amanāpaina uppan‍naina manāpāmanāpaina aṭṭīyati harāyati jigucchati| sautaina saddaṃ  sutvā…pai॰… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā…, jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya uppaj‍jati manāpaṃ, uppaj‍jati amanāpaṃ, uppaj‍jati manāpāmanāpaṃ| sau taina uppan‍naina manāpaina uppan‍naina amanāpaina uppan‍naina manāpāmanāpaina aṭṭīyati harāyati jigucchati| aivaṃ khau, ānanda, saikhau hauti pāṭipadau|

461. ‘‘kathañ‍cānanda, ariyau hauti bhāvitindriyau? idhānanda, bhikkhunau cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā uppaj‍jati manāpaṃ, uppaj‍jati amanāpaṃ, uppaj‍jati manāpāmanāpaṃ| sau sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘paṭikūlai paṭik‍kūlai (sabbattha) appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya’nti, appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī tattha viharati| sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘appaṭikūlai paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya’nti, paṭikūlasañ‍ñī tattha viharati| sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘paṭikūlai ca appaṭikūlai ca appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī  viharaiyya’nti, appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī tattha viharati| sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘appaṭikūlai ca paṭikūlai ca paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya’nti, paṭikūlasañ‍ñī tattha viharati| sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘paṭikūlañ‍ca appaṭikūlañ‍ca tadubhayaṃ abhinivaj‍jaitvā upaikkhakau viharaiyyaṃ satau sampajānau’ti, upaikkhakau tattha viharati satau sampajānau|

462. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhunau sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya uppaj‍jati manāpaṃ, uppaj‍jati amanāpaṃ, uppaj‍jati manāpāmanāpaṃ| sau sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘paṭikūlai appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya’nti, appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī tattha viharati| sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘appaṭikūlai paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya’nti, paṭikūlasañ‍ñī tattha viharati| sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘paṭikūlai ca appaṭikūlai ca appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya’nti, appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī tattha viharati| sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘appaṭikūlai ca paṭikūlai ca paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya’nti, paṭikūlasañ‍ñī tattha viharati| sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘paṭikūlañ‍ca appaṭikūlañ‍ca tadubhayampi  abhinivaj‍jaitvā upaikkhakau viharaiyyaṃ satau sampajānau’ti, upaikkhakau tattha viharati satau sampajānau| aivaṃ khau, ānanda, ariyau hauti bhāvitindriyau|

463. ‘‘iti  khau, ānanda, daisitā mayā ariyassa vinayai anuttarā indriyabhāvanā, daisitau saikhau pāṭipadau, daisitau ariyau bhāvitindriyau| yaṃ khau, ānanda, satthārā karaṇīyaṃ sāvakānaṃ hitaisinā anukampakaina anukampaṃ upādāya, kataṃ vau taṃ mayā| aitāni, ānanda, rukkhamūlāni, aitāni suñ‍ñāgārāni, jhāyathānanda, mā pamādattha, mā pacchā vippaṭisārinau ahuvattha| ayaṃ vau amhākaṃ anusāsanī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

indriyabhāvanāsuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dasamaṃ|

saḷāyatanavaggau niṭṭhitau pañ‍camau|


tassuddānaṃ –

anāthapiṇḍikau chan‍nau, puṇṇau nandakarāhulā|

chachak‍kaṃ saḷāyatanikaṃ, nagaravindaiyyasuddhikā|

indriyabhāvanā cāpi, vaggau auvādapañ‍camauti||

idaṃ vaggānamuddānaṃ –

daivadahaunupadau ca, suñ‍ñatau ca vibhaṅgakau|

saḷāyatanauti vaggā, uparipaṇṇāsakai ṭhitāti||

uparipaṇṇāsakaṃ samattaṃ|

tīhi paṇṇāsakaihi paṭimaṇḍitau sakalau

majjhimanikāyau samattau|
